## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [DeepLatent: Think with Images via Parallel Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00562)
*Dongchen Lu, Zhimo Li, Mao Shu, Huo Cao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (80.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeepLatent 提出了一种并行潜空间视觉推理框架，通过 LatentFormer 在连续潜空间中并行生成视觉状态，并设计了连续空间强化学习算法优化潜表征质量，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"用图像思考"范式中的工具辅助方法延迟高且操作受限，而潜推理方法自回归生成隐式视觉状态但性能不足且潜 token 无法捕获有效视觉信息。作者希望设计一种高效且有效的并行潜空间视觉推理方法。

Method: 提出 DeepLatent 框架，包含：(1) LatentFormer，使用可学习 2D token 在原始图像特征上并行生成上下文条件化的连续潜状态；(2) 连续空间强化学习算法，直接在 embedding 空间中优化潜调制参数；(3) 先通过知识蒸馏训练，再使用连续空间 RL 微调；(4) 构建了 DeepLatent-180K 大规模潜视觉推理数据集。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上取得 SOTA 性能，验证了并行潜推理框架相比自回归潜推理方法和工具辅助方法的优势。

Conclusion: DeepLatent 证明了并行连续潜空间视觉推理的有效性，连续空间 RL 算法能显著提升潜表征质量，为"用图像思考"范式提供了新的高效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DeepLatent，一种在连续潜空间中进行并行视觉推理的框架，使用可学习的 2D token 生成上下文条件化的连续潜状态，属于连续多模态推理与潜空间思维方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal latent reasoning with parallel generation and RL optimization, a top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous latent visual reasoning, parallel LatentFormer, continuous-space RL for latent quality, state-of-the-art on multiple benchmarks, aligns with continuous language/multimodal model direction

Abstract: The emerging paradigm of "thinking with images" embeds visual states into intermediate reasoning steps, defining a new frontier for Vision-Language Models. Existing approaches diverge along two lines. Tool-assisted methods apply explicit visual operations but suffer from high latency and restricted manipulation types. Latent reasoning methods autoregressively produce implicit visual states, but underperform tool-assisted methods, and their latent tokens fail to capture effective visual information. In this work, we propose DeepLatent, a parallel framework for latent visual reasoning. First, we introduce LatentFormer. It uses learnable 2D tokens to generate context-conditioned latent states in parallel, anchoring every visual update directly in the original image features. Second, we design a continuous-space reinforcement learning algorithm. It optimizes latent modulation parameters directly in the embedding space, significantly improving latent representation quality. The framework is trained via knowledge distillation followed by this continuous-space RL algorithm. Furthermore, we contribute DeepLatent-180K, a large-scale dataset tailored for latent visual reasoning. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that DeepLatent achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Visual Memory: Mechanistic Diagnostics of Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01287)
*Garvin Guo, Yu Chen, Xiang Wang, Shuai Li, Xinpei Zhao, Huaxing Liu, Shuai Dong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (77.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对潜在视觉推理（latent visual reasoning）方法进行机制诊断，将潜在 token 分解为潜在槽（latent slots）、边界标记（boundary markers）和格式（format）三个可测试组件。实验表明，增益并非来自视觉记忆编码的潜在槽，而是来自边界标记、格式以及注意力模式。不同训练监督方式下，即使准确率相同，模型依赖的机制也可能截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在视觉推理方法通过插入连续潜在 token 取得了显著性能提升，通常归因于这些 token 编码了视觉证据。但近期分析发现这些 token 与图像关联松散且对答案贡献有限，且现有分析将潜在 token 视为单一整体，掩盖了增益的真正来源。本文旨在分解潜在 token 并诊断其工作机制。

Method: 将潜在 token 分解为三个组件：潜在槽（latent slots）、边界标记（boundary markers）和格式（format）。开发了一种 SOTA 方法作为探针，在六种方法-阶段设置和四个感知密集型基准上进行系统实验，逐一检验视觉记忆假说的预测。

Result: 潜在槽未能通过视觉记忆假说的任何预测；仅保留边界标记即可在多个设置中保持 78-100% 的增益；模型在潜在位置对图像的关注比在答案位置更窄。不同训练监督方式下，即使准确率相同，机制也可能显著不同。

Conclusion: 潜在视觉推理的增益主要来自边界标记、格式和注意力模式，而非潜在槽。评估潜在视觉推理不仅需要关注准确率，还需要关注模型实际依赖的机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 latent visual reasoning 方法，通过在多模态语言模型中插入连续 latent token 进行推理，属于连续多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the core mechanism of continuous latent tokens in multimodal reasoning, a top-priority subtopic, with diagnostic insights that challenge common assumptions. Signals: latent visual reasoning mechanism diagnosis, continuous latent token decomposition, boundary markers vs. visual memory, training supervision affects reasoning mechanism, high relevance to continuous multimodal model priority

Abstract: Recent latent visual reasoning methods achieve substantial gains by inserting continuous latent tokens into multimodal language models. These gains are commonly attributed to the tokens encoding visual evidence; recent analyses, however, reveal a paradox: the tokens are loosely tied to the image and contribute little to the answer. Critically, these analyses treat latent tokens as a single unit, obscuring the true source of the gains. We therefore decompose latent tokens into three testable components: latent slots, boundary markers, and format, and develop a state-of-the-art method as a probe under favorable conditions. Across six method-stage settings and four perception-heavy benchmarks, latent slots fail every prediction of the visual-memory account. Strikingly, retaining only the boundary markers preserves 78 to 100% of the gain in several settings, while the model attends to the image more narrowly at latent positions than at answer positions. The gain therefore comes from boundary markers, format, and this attention pattern, not from latent slots. How each method engages this mechanism depends on its training supervision: at matched accuracy, mechanisms can still differ markedly. Latent visual reasoning thus needs evaluation not only by accuracy but by what the model actually relies on.

</details>

## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

### [3] [DLLM-JEPA: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00091)
*Sangdae Nam*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DLLM-JEPA 将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）与掩码扩散语言模型结合，消除了 LLM-JEPA 对显式多视图数据和两次梯度前向传播的需求，训练 FLOPs 降低 33%，在多个任务上显著提升准确率，并展现出"双重优势"特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-JEPA 将 JEPA 迁移到自回归语言模型，但因果注意力机制导致两个严重问题：需要显式的多视图数据（如文本-代码对），且每步需要两次带梯度的前向传播，训练成本高。作者希望消除这些开销，同时提升扩散语言模型的性能。

Method: DLLM-JEPA 将 JEPA 与掩码扩散语言模型配对。利用扩散模型的双向注意力机制，通过不同的掩码率从同一输入中产生两个语义不同的视图（无需显式数据对），且仅需一次带梯度的前向传播。训练时，一个视图（低掩码率）作为上下文，预测另一个视图（高掩码率）的潜表征，通过 JEPA 损失进行自监督学习。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B 上 GSM8K 提升 +18.7 pp，Dream-7B 上 GSM8K 提升 +11.4 pp，Spider、NL-RX-SYNTH、Django 等任务上也有持续正向增益。LLaDA-8B Wide-t 配置下，GSM8K 从 65.2 提升至 67.1，同时保持 MMLU 准确率不变，并降低 Wikitext 困惑度。训练 FLOPs 相比 LLM-JEPA 降低 33%。层探分析揭示了中间层的几何-功能漂移解耦机制。

Conclusion: DLLM-JEPA 是一种高效且有效的扩散语言模型自监督训练方法，消除了 LLM-JEPA 的两大开销，同时显著提升下游任务性能。其"双重优势"（提升任务准确率的同时保持甚至改善语言建模能力）和层级的几何-功能漂移解耦现象为未来扩散语言模型的预训练提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DLLM-JEPA，将 JEPA 与掩码扩散语言模型（masked-diffusion language model）结合，属于连续语言模型范畴。论文核心是扩散语言模型上的自监督表征学习，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model training efficiency and performance, a top priority subtopic. Signals: 33% FLOPs reduction, +18.7 pp GSM8K on LLaDA-8B, dual-win property, eliminates multi-view data requirement, layer-wise drift dissociation mechanism

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have reshaped self-supervised representation learning in vision. The recent LLM-JEPA ported JEPA to autoregressive language models but inherited two steep costs from the causal-attention substrate: it demands explicit multi-view data (e.g., text-code pairs), and it requires two gradient-carrying forward passes per step. We introduce DLLM-JEPA, which pairs JEPA with masked-diffusion language models to eliminate both costs at once. The bidirectional attention of diffusion models yields two semantically distinct views of the same input via different masking rates -- no explicit pairs needed -- and supports a single gradient-carrying forward pass, cutting training FLOPs by 33% relative to LLM-JEPA. DLLM-JEPA improves over diffusion-only fine-tuning in every (task, architecture) combination we evaluate: up to +18.7 pp on LLaDA-8B GSM8K and +11.4 pp on Dream-7B GSM8K, with consistent positive gains on Spider, NL-RX-SYNTH, and Django. Beyond accuracy, DLLM-JEPA exhibits a dual-win property: on LLaDA-8B with the Wide-t configuration, it simultaneously raises GSM8K accuracy (67.1 vs. 65.2, +1.8 pp), drives held-out Wikitext loss below the pre-trained base, and preserves MMLU accuracy at base level across three fine-tuning seeds -- whereas an L2-to-base parameter anchor matches baseline accuracy with no task gain. Layer-wise probing reveals the mechanism: a geometric-functional drift dissociation in which the fine-tuned backbone moves further from the pre-trained weights than the baseline yet forgets less on held-out Wikitext, with the amplification concentrated in middle transformer layers. The pattern appears on Dream-7B as well, indicating the phenomenon is not specific to a single backbone.

</details>


### [4] [EPIC: Efficient and Parallel Inference under CFG Constraints for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00722)
*Hyundong Jin, Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EPIC 框架，通过词法记忆化、Earley 解析替代确定性自动机、以及松弛兼容子集选择，显著加速扩散语言模型在上下文无关文法约束下的并行解码，推理时间降低最高 67.5%，额外开销减少最高 90.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型相比自回归模型具有并行解码的优势，但现有 CFG 约束解码方法因顺序有效性检查导致速度慢 4 倍，且严重削弱了并行解码的优势。需要一种高效方法在保持 CFG 约束的同时恢复并行解码效率。

Method: EPIC 框架包含三个关键技术：(1) 词法记忆化（Lexing Memoization）——缓存 token 的词法分析结果避免重复计算；(2) 基于 Earley 解析的有效性验证——替代确定性自动机，利用 Earley 解析器增量式验证，减少验证开销；(3) 松弛兼容子集选择（Relaxed Compatible Subset Selection）——允许多个兼容 token 同时提交，而非逐个检查，从而恢复并行解码能力。

Result: 在三个基准测试上使用四个模型进行实验，EPIC 相比现有 CFG 约束解码方法减少推理时间最高 67.5%，额外开销降低最高 90.5%，同时保持生成质量。

Conclusion: EPIC 有效解决了扩散语言模型在 CFG 约束下解码效率低下的问题，通过组合优化策略大幅恢复并行解码优势，为扩散语言模型的实际应用提供了可行的约束解码方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（diffusion language model）在 CFG 约束下的高效并行解码，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow），该子主题涵盖扩散语言模型与连续语言建模。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for continuous language models under grammar constraints, a key priority for your daily deep read. Signals: diffusion language model, CFG-constrained decoding, efficient inference, parallel decoding, Earley parsing

Abstract: Controlling language model outputs is essential for ensuring structural validity, reliability, and downstream usability, and diffusion language models are no exception. Recent advances in diffusion language model decoding have extended output control beyond regular constraints to context-free grammar (CFG) constraints. Existing methods, however, can be up to four times slower than unconstrained decoding. More importantly, they substantially diminish one of the key advantages of diffusion language models over autoregressive models, namely parallel decoding. This slowdown arises because sequential validity checking introduces significant overhead during parallel generation. We propose an efficient CFG-constrained decoding framework, EPIC, that addresses this limitation. Our method improves decoding efficiency by combining lexing memoization, validation using Earley-style parsing instead of deterministic automata, and relaxed compatible subset selection for parallel commit. It reduces repeated lexing and validation overhead while allowing multiple compatible tokens to be committed together. Experiments on three benchmarks using four models show that our method reduces inference time by up to 67.5% and decreases the additional overhead by up to 90.5% compared with existing CFG-constrained decoding methods. Our implementation is available at .

</details>


### [5] [Geodesic Flow Matching for Denoising High-Dimensional Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00248)
*Karim Habashy, Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出标准 Flow Matching 假设欧几里得几何，无法处理 Spatial Semantic Pointers (SSPs) 的环面流形约束，导致线性插值破坏相位和幅度结构。作者提出 Geodesic Flow Matching，利用黎曼传输动力学将去噪流限制在 SSP 环面流形上，在脉冲神经 SLAM 系统中验证了该方法，实现了 72% 的跟踪误差降低和 40% 的神经效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Flow Matching 假设平坦欧几里得几何，但 SSPs 将变量映射到连续环面流形上，欧几里得线性插值会"穿过"流形内部，破坏解码所需的相位和幅度结构，导致去噪效果不佳。

Method: 提出 Geodesic Flow Matching (GFM)，将黎曼传输动力学适配到 SSP 环面流形上，严格限制去噪流在流形上运行，而非在欧几里得空间中线性插值。

Result: 在脉冲神经 SLAM 系统中验证，GFM 相比竞争基线实现了 72% 的跟踪误差降低和 40% 的神经效率提升。

Conclusion: 流形感知的去噪方法（Geodesic Flow Matching）能有效保持 SSP 表征的结构完整性，显著提升连续分布式表征在神经符号系统中的鲁棒性和效率。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Vector Symbolic Algebras (VSAs) 和 Spatial Semantic Pointers (SSPs) 在高维连续流形上的去噪问题，提出 Geodesic Flow Matching 方法将去噪流限制在 SSP 环面流形上。这与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 子主题高度相关，因为 SSPs 是连续分布式表征的一种形式，Flow Matching 是连续语言建模的核心技术，且论文关注流形几何约束下的连续表征去噪。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model theory by solving geometric constraints in SSP manifolds, a core priority subtopic. Signals: Geodesic Flow Matching, SSP toroidal manifold, Riemannian transport dynamics, 72% tracking error reduction, 40% neural efficiency gain

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Algebras (VSAs) enable robust neurosymbolic reasoning by encoding symbolic information into high-dimensional distributed representations. For continuous domains, Spatial Semantic Pointers (SSPs) extend this framework by mapping variables onto continuous toroidal manifolds. However, standard approaches like Flow Matching assume a flat Euclidean geometry, which fails to account for the geometric constraints imposed on valid SSP states. We demonstrate that this assumption fails for SSPs: Euclidean linear interpolants ``cut through" the manifold's interior, destroying the phase and magnitude structure required for accurate decoding. To resolve this, we employ Geodesic Flow Matching, adapting Riemannian transport dynamics to strictly restrict the denoising flow to the SSP toroidal manifold. We validate this approach in a Spiking Neural SLAM system, showing that manifold-aware cleanup stabilizes path integration against drift. The method achieves a 72\% reduction in tracking error and enables a 40\% increase in neural efficiency compared to competitive baselines. Code is available at .

</details>


### [6] [DSL-LLaDA: Scaling Continuous Denoising to 8B Masked Diffusion LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01024)
*Longxuan Yu, Yunshu Wu, Yu Fu, Siheng Xiong, Rob Brekelmans, Hui Liu, Yue Dong, Greg Ver Steeg*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: DSL-LLaDA 通过对预训练的 LLaDA-8B 模型进行仅 1000 步的连续预训练（使用离散随机定位 DSL），将离散掩码扩散语言模型适配为连续 embedding 空间去噪模型，解决了少步解码中长度与质量的权衡问题，在零样本摘要任务上取得最佳 ROUGE-1 分数，并展现出选择性噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散掩码扩散语言模型在少步解码时面临长度与质量的权衡：固定步数下，要么生成短而高质量的输出，要么生成长但重复的文本。连续去噪可以通过在 embedding 空间中联合演化所有位置来规避这一权衡，但从头构建大规模连续去噪语言模型仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 从 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 出发，使用离散随机定位（DSL）方法进行仅 1000 步的继续预训练，将原本的二元掩码替换为连续的逐 token 高斯噪声作为软掩码。适配后的模型支持连续推理，在 embedding 空间中联合演化所有位置，并将硬 token 决策推迟到最后一步。

Result: 在低步数预算（≤16 次前向传播）的零样本摘要任务上，DSL-LLaDA-SDE 在所有四个基准上取得最佳 ROUGE-1 分数，且很大程度上避免了迭代去掩码的提前终止/重复权衡问题。该适配还带来了选择性噪声鲁棒性：模型能纠正被破坏的 token 同时保留干净的 token。使用相同计算量的标准掩码扩散训练对照实验未展现出这两种行为。

Conclusion: 通过轻量适配（仅 1000 步继续预训练），可以将预训练的离散掩码扩散语言模型转化为连续去噪语言模型，有效解决少步解码中的长度-质量权衡问题，并带来额外的噪声鲁棒性。这为大规模连续语言模型的构建提供了一条实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究连续去噪语言模型（continuous denoising LM），将预训练的离散掩码扩散语言模型（LLaDA）适配为连续 embedding 空间去噪模型（DSL-LLaDA），属于连续语言模型范畴，与 canonical subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model scaling with minimal compute, matching top priority subtopic and boosting autoregressive/video generation via efficient diffusion. Signals: continuous language model scaling, 8B masked diffusion LM adaptation, few-step decoding length-quality tradeoff solved, zero-shot summarization SOTA, selective noise robustness

Abstract: Discrete Masked diffusion language models generate text by iterative parallel decoding, but few-step decoding suffers from a tradeoff between length and quality: with a fixed step budget, standard methods can generate a short, high-quality output, or they can produce long but repetitive text. Continuous denoising can sidestep this tradeoff by evolving all positions jointly in embedding space, but building such a model from scratch at scale remains an open problem. We show that a pretrained masked DLM can instead be lightly adapted to support continuous embedding-space denoising. Starting from LLaDA-8B-Instruct, we continue-pretrain for only 1,000 steps with Discrete Stochastic Localization (DSL), replacing binary masking with continuous per-token Gaussian noise as a soft mask. The adapted model supports continuous inference that evolves all positions jointly in embedding space and defers hard token commitment to the final step. On zero-shot summarization at low step budgets (<=16 forward passes), DSL-LLaDA-SDE achieves the best ROUGE-1 on all four benchmarks and largely avoids the premature-termination / repetition tradeoff of iterative unmasking. The same adaptation also yields selective noisy-state robustness: the model corrects corrupted tokens while preserving clean ones. Control experiments using standard masked diffusion training with the same compute demonstrate neither behavior.

</details>


### [7] [Revise, Don't Freeze: Sampler-Matched Training for Self-Correcting Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01026)
*Longxuan Yu, Shaorong Zhang, Yu Fu, Hui Liu, Yue Dong, Greg Ver Steeg*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 D3IM（一种无需参数的采样器）和 SCOPE（轻量级后训练方法），用于改进掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的自我修正能力。D3IM 允许模型在去噪过程中直接修正已暴露的可见 token，而 SCOPE 通过模拟 D3IM 的采样过程来缓解模型的"保留偏差"（preservation bias）。在 LLaDA-8B 上，该方法在数学和代码任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在去噪过程中一旦 token 被揭示（unmasked）就将其固定，无法在后续步骤中修正。现有方法要么引入额外的启发式或学习机制来修正已提交的 token，要么将其重新掩码后再预测，但缺乏一种无需辅助模块的原则性采样器来直接修正可见 token。

Method: 1) D3IM（Denoising via Direct Inversion and Iterative Modification）：一种无需参数的采样器，推导为 corrector 风格的逆向更新，允许可见 token 到可见 token 的直接修正，无需额外模块或辅助前向传播。2) SCOPE（Self-Conditioned On Prediction Errors）：一种轻量级后训练过程，通过模拟 D3IM 的采样过程来缓解模型的"保留偏差"（即模型倾向于复现自己错误的已提交 token 而非修正它们）。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B 上使用 64 步去噪时，SCOPE+D3IM 相比原始 LLaDA-8B 的标准 unmasking 方法：GSM8K 提升 +13.0（达 68.3%），MATH-500 提升 +4.8（达 23.6%），HumanEval 提升 +15.3（达 29.3%），MBPP 提升 +10.4（达 30.8%）。在数学和 HumanEval 任务上，随着去噪步数增加，性能提升更加显著。

Conclusion: 本文证明了掩码扩散语言模型可以通过原则性的采样器设计（D3IM）和轻量级后训练（SCOPE）实现有效的自我修正，无需引入额外模块。该方法在数学推理和代码生成任务上表现优异，为扩散语言模型的推理能力提升提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的采样器改进，属于连续语言模型范畴。论文提出的 D3IM 采样器和 SCOPE 训练方法针对 LLaDA 这类扩散语言模型，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model sampling with a principled revision method, highly aligned with your priority subtopic on continuous language models. Signals: continuous language model, masked diffusion language model, sampler improvement, self-correction, LLaDA-8B

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) re-predict every position at each denoising step, but standard samplers commit tokens once revealed, leaving this revision capability unused. Existing approaches either add heuristic or learned mechanisms to revise committed tokens, or remask them back to [MASK] before re-predicting; a principled sampler that directly revises visible tokens without auxiliary modules remains underexplored. We introduce D3IM, a parameter-free sampler derived as a corrector-style reverse update that permits direct visible-to-visible revision without additional modules or auxiliary passes. D3IM also reveals a model-side obstacle we term preservation bias: the model tends to reproduce its own wrong committed tokens rather than correct them. We address this with SCOPE (Self-Conditioned On Prediction Errors), a lightweight post-training procedure that simulates D3IM's sampling process. On LLaDA-8B at 64 denoising steps, SCOPE+D3IM improves over the original LLaDA-8B with standard unmasking by +13.0 on GSM8K (68.3%), +4.8 on MATH-500 (23.6%), +15.3 on HumanEval (29.3%), and +10.4 on MBPP (30.8%), with gains that increase as more denoising steps are used on math and HumanEval.

</details>


### [8] [Geometric Latent Reasoning Induces Shorter Generations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02248)
*Shashi Kumar, Yacouba Kaloga, Petr Motlicek, Ina Kodrasi, Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Geometric Latent Reasoning (GLR)，将语言模型的推理过程建模为 token embedding 空间中的几何路径逼近问题，通过轻量级 transition head 预测连续方向更新，用连续 latent 步骤替代早期显式推理 token，在数学推理基准上显著缩短生成长度而不牺牲准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过生成冗长的显式推理 token 来解决复杂问题，这导致推理成本高、对长度敏感且受限于离散自然语言。连续 latent 推理虽提供了替代方案，但如何为中间 latent 状态设计有用的结构仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 将 latent 推理形式化为预训练 token embedding 空间中的几何路径逼近问题。使用轻量级 transition head 预测 embedding 空间中的迭代方向更新，以文本思维链轨迹为锚点，学习逼近离散推理轨迹，同时允许偏离精确 token embedding 的连续偏差。

Result: 在 Qwen3 模型上的数学推理基准评估中，GLR 在没有显式长度目标的情况下，显著缩短了生成长度。通过用连续 latent 步骤替换早期显式推理，模型通常能用更少的总生成步骤得到正确答案。

Conclusion: 连续轨迹可作为紧凑的中间推理状态，揭示了 latent 计算预算、输出长度和准确性之间的新权衡。几何 latent 推理能够诱导更短的生成，为高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Geometric Latent Reasoning (GLR)，在预训练 token embedding 空间中用连续 latent 路径替代离散思维链推理，属于连续语言模型与语言流（continuous language flow）范畴，与 ELF、CoLa 等连续语言建模思想高度一致，因此复用 canonical subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent reasoning in LLMs, a core priority subtopic, with a novel geometric formulation that reduces generation length. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, geometric path approximation, shorter generations, token embedding space, transition head

Abstract: Large language models solve complex problems by generating lengthy chains of explicit reasoning tokens. While effective, this makes reasoning expensive, length-sensitive, and constrained to (discrete) natural language. While latent reasoning offers a continuous alternative, determining useful structures for intermediate latent states is an open challenge. In this paper, we formulate latent reasoning as a geometric path-approximation problem within the model's pretrained token-embedding space. We introduce Geometric Latent Reasoning (GLR), which uses a lightweight transition head to predict iterative direction updates in embedding space. Using textual chain-of-thought traces as anchors, GLR learns to approximate discrete reasoning trajectories while permitting continuous deviations from exact token embeddings. Evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks using Qwen3 models reveal an emergent phenomenon: geometric latent reasoning induces substantially shorter generations without an explicit length objective. By replacing early explicit reasoning with continuous latent steps, models often reach correct answers using substantially fewer total generation steps. These findings suggest that continuous trajectories act as compact intermediate reasoning states, exposing a new tradeoff between latent computation budget, output length, and accuracy.

</details>


### [9] [SimSD: Simple Speculative Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02544)
*Junxia Cui, Haotian Ye, Runchu Tian, Hongcan Guo, Jinya Jiang, Haoru Li, Chaojie Ren, Yiming Huang, Kaijie Zhu, Zhongkai Yu, Kun Zhou, Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SimSD 提出了一种简单有效的推测解码算法，使扩散语言模型(dLLM)能够像自回归模型一样进行 token 级推测验证，通过引入参考 token 和注意力掩码策略，在保持 dLLM 并行解码优势的同时实现最高 7.46 倍的解码吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(dLLM)作为自回归(AR)模型的有前途替代方案，支持并行或块状解码实现更快推理。然而，dLLM 的掩码语言建模公式与标准 token 级推测解码不兼容——AR 模型中的因果掩码保留了时间有效的 token 级上下文，使目标模型能在单次前向传播中验证多个草稿 token；而 dLLM 依赖掩码 token 和双向注意力，导致有效上下文在去噪步骤间变化，无法直接进行 token 级推测验证。因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: SimSD 采用即插即用的掩码策略，为 dLLM 配备时间有效的 token 级上下文以进行推测解码。具体地，从草稿模型预测中显式引入参考 token，并设计注意力掩码来调节它们与当前步骤 token 的交互，使 dLLM 能在单次前向传播中计算草稿 token 的有效 logits。该方法无需训练，可灵活与 KV cache、块状解码等其他加速技术集成。

Result: 在 SDAR 系列 dLLM 上的四个基准测试中，SimSD 实现了最高 7.46 倍的解码吞吐量提升，同时保持甚至提高了平均生成质量。

Conclusion: SimSD 成功将推测解码引入扩散语言模型，恢复了因果掩码在 AR 模型中提供的关键验证能力，同时保留了 dLLM 的并行解码优势。该方法训练免费且可与其他加速技术灵活集成，为扩散语言模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型(dLLM)的推测解码加速方法，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"(continuous_language_flow)，该子主题涵盖扩散语言模型与连续语言建模。同时论文涉及生成加速，与 multimodal_generation 方向的 efficient_generation 子主题也相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model inference efficiency via speculative decoding, a top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, speculative decoding, diffusion language model, efficient inference, training-free acceleration

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) LLMs, offering faster inference through parallel or blockwise decoding. However, their masked language modeling formulation remains incompatible with standard token-level speculative decoding, one of the most effective acceleration techniques for AR models. In AR decoding, the causal mask preserves temporally valid token-level contexts, enabling a target model to verify multiple drafted tokens in a single forward pass. In contrast, dLLMs rely on mask tokens and bidirectional attention, causing the effective context to change across denoising steps and preventing direct token-level speculative verification. To bridge this gap, we propose a simple but effective speculative decoding algorithm for diffusion language models, named SimSD, which mainly adopts a plug-and-play masking strategy that equips dLLMs with temporally valid token-level contexts for speculative decoding. Our method explicitly introduces reference tokens from draft-model predictions and designs an attention mask that regulates their interaction with current-step tokens, allowing dLLMs to compute valid logits for drafted tokens in a single forward pass. This restores the key verification ability provided by causal masking in AR models while preserving the parallel decoding advantages of dLLMs. The proposed method is training-free and can be flexibly integrated with other acceleration techniques such as KV cache and blockwise decoding. Experiments on SDAR-family dLLMs across four benchmarks show that our method achieves up to 7.46x higher decoding throughput while maintaining and even improving average generation quality.

</details>


### [10] [FLARE: Diffusion for Hybrid Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01774)
*Yuchen Zhu, Jing Shi, Chongjian Ge, Hao Tan, Yiran Xu, Wanrong Zhu, Jason Kuen, Koustava Goswami, Rajiv Jain, Yongxin Chen, Molei Tao, Jiuxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: FLARE 是一个系统性的转换框架，将混合注意力 LLM 转换为同时支持自回归解码和扩散式并行去噪的统一模型，通过 token-equal AR-and-diffusion 目标、硬件感知内核和统一推理，在保持种子检查点能力的同时实现高效并行生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 LLM 的顺序解码是低延迟部署的关键瓶颈。现有工作沿两条路线推进：高效架构降低单次调用成本（混合注意力），以及并行生成减少解码步数（扩散语言模型）。但将两者结合面临挑战：AR 到 dLLM 的转换常丢失种子检查点能力，且混合注意力的循环状态和掩码约束使扩散训练和服务复杂化。FLARE 旨在系统性地解决这一结合问题。

Method: FLARE 提出一个系统转换框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) token-equal AR-and-diffusion 联合训练目标，使同一检查点同时支持 AR 和扩散两种解码模式；(2) 硬件感知内核，高效处理混合注意力架构下的扩散训练和服务；(3) 统一推理框架，支持 AR 式验证解码和扩散式并行去噪的灵活切换。论文还分析了转移数据质量是能力保持的首要决定因素。

Result: FLARE 在多个模型规模上与领先的开源 dLLM 具有竞争力，并在单 GPU 并发服务中相比开源 dLLM 基线实现了一致的吞吐量提升。实验表明，转移数据质量比损失函数形式和注意力掩码设计对能力保持更为关键。

Conclusion: FLARE 成功将混合注意力 LLM 转换为支持 AR 和扩散双模式解码的统一模型。研究进一步指出，实用 dLLM 不仅受限于解码算法，还受限于转移数据质量和当前块扩散目标的训练低效性，需要数据、目标、架构和推理系统的联合设计。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FLARE 框架，将扩散语言模型（dLLM）与混合注意力架构结合，实现 AR 式验证解码和扩散式并行去噪的统一。核心属于连续语言模型中的扩散语言模型方向，与 [CANONICAL] 的"连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度匹配。同时涉及生成模型加速（扩散并行解码），因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 下的 efficient_generation。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model and diffusion language model research, a top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, hybrid attention, parallel generation, efficient inference

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models (LLMs) have achieved broad practical success, but sequential decoding remains a key bottleneck for low-latency deployment. Recent efficient-inference work has progressed along two axes: reducing the cost of each model invocation through efficient architectures, and reducing serial decoding steps through parallel generation. Hybrid attention backbones address the former, while diffusion language models (dLLMs) pursue the latter via iterative parallel denoising. Combining these advantages remains challenging: AR-to-dLLM conversion often fails to preserve seed-checkpoint capability, and hybrid-attention recurrent states and masking constraints make diffusion training and serving nontrivial. We present FLARE, a systematic conversion framework for hybrid-attention LLMs. Our analysis identifies transfer data quality as the primary determinant of capability preservation, outweighing loss formulation and attention-mask design. The resulting framework combines a token-equal AR-and-diffusion objective, hardware-aware kernels, and unified inference, enabling one checkpoint to support both AR-style verified decoding and diffusion-style parallel denoising. Starting from strong AR checkpoints with limited post-training data, FLARE is competitive with leading open-source dLLMs across model scales and delivers consistent throughput gains over open-source dLLM baselines in single-GPU concurrent serving. Our results further suggest that practical dLLMs are limited not only by decoding algorithms, but also by transfer data quality and the training inefficiency of current block-diffusion objectives, motivating joint design of data, objectives, architectures, and inference systems.

</details>

### [11] [VLAMotor: Test-Guided Enhancement of Vision-Language-Action Models via Agent-BasedData Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00053)
*Zeqin Liao, Peifan Ren, Zixu Gao, Hongyu Gong, Lianyu Hu, Wenbing Tang, Yuhong Nan, Zibin Zheng, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLAMotor 提出首个面向 VLA 模型增强的分析框架，通过距离感知模型测试暴露失败模式，并利用基于智能体的数据合成将失败案例转化为成功轨迹用于模型微调，在仿真和真实机器人操作任务上显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型依赖数据驱动范式，受训练数据覆盖范围限制，在部署后容易在边缘配置下失败。现有研究大多止步于失败检测，缺乏利用发现的失败进行模型修复的机制。

Method: VLAMotor 包含两个核心模块：(1) 距离感知模型测试——基于输入与训练样本的距离估计不确定性，结合不确定性排序与冗余消除构建紧凑测试集以暴露多样化失败；(2) 基于智能体的数据合成——将失败轨迹抽象为结构化语义表示，规划参数化的修复技能序列，通过逆运动学和运动执行生成可执行的成功轨迹，自动标注后用于微调原始 VLA 模型。

Result: 在四个代表性机器人操作任务上，VLAMotor 生成的仿真测试用例中有 92.33% 触发了 VLA 失败，测试覆盖率比现有最优工具提升 18.93%。通过微调，VLA 模型整体成功率提升 49.25%。在真实硬件部署时，仿真增强模型相比原始模型成功率提升 57.50%。

Conclusion: VLAMotor 提供了一种低成本、有效的 VLA 模型增强方向，通过系统性地暴露失败并利用合成数据进行修复，显著提升了 VLA 模型在仿真和真实场景中的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在机器人操作任务中的测试增强，通过距离感知测试暴露失败模式，并利用智能体数据合成生成成功轨迹进行微调。这属于具身智能中机器人动作规划与控制的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong robotics VLA framework but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: VLA model enhancement, agent-based data synthesis, failure-driven fine-tuning, robotic manipulation, simulation-to-real transfer

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models follow a data-driven paradigm and are constrained by the coverage of training data, making them prone to failure on edge-case configurations after deployment. To mitigate such risks, it is essential to expose high-quality failure modes and convert the resulting failures into supervisory data for model enhancement. Existing studies largely stop at failure detection and lack a mechanism for leveraging discovered failures for model repair. We propose VLAMotor, the first analysis framework for VLA enhancement, which integrates distance-aware model testing for failure exposure and agent-based data synthesis for model finetunning. First, VLAMotor estimates input uncertainty based on the distance to training samples, and combines uncertainty ranking with redundancy elimination to build compact test sets that expose diverse failures. Then, VLAMotor abstracts failure trajectories into structured semantic representations, and plans parameterized repair-skill sequences, which are then realized as executable trajectories through inverse kinematics and motion execution. The resulting successful trajectories are automatically labeled and used to fine-tune the original VLA model, yielding an enhanced VLA model. Evaluation on four representative robotic manipulation tasks shows that 92.33% of the in-simulation test cases generated by VLAMotor trigger VLA failures, and VLAMotor improves test coverage over the state-of-the-art tool by 18.93%. By fine-tuning VLA models with synthetic data derived from failed test cases, VLAMotor further enhances the overall success rate of VLA models by 49.25%. When deployed on real hardware, the simulation-enhanced models improve the success rate over the original VLA models by 57.50%, demonstrating an effective and low-cost direction for VLA enhancement.

</details>

Result: 系统梳理了四类方法的代表性工作，分析了各自的优势与局限，并总结了三大开放挑战：非结构化视频到训练就绪片段的组织、跨具身与视角差异的动作映射、以及更贴近真实部署的评测协议设计。

Abstract: Recent progress in generalizable embodied control has been driven by large-scale pretraining of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, most existing approaches rely on large collections of robot demonstrations, which are costly to obtain and tightly coupled to specific embodiments. Human videos, by contrast, are abundant and capture rich interactions, providing diverse semantic and physical cues for real-world manipulation. Yet, embodiment differences and the frequent absence of task-aligned annotations make their direct use in VLA models challenging. This survey provides a unified view of how human videos are transformed into effective knowledge for VLA models. We categorize existing approaches into four classes based on the action-related information they derive: (i) latent action representations that encode inter-frame changes; (ii) predictive world models that forecast future frames; (iii) explicit 2D supervision that extracts image-plane cues; and (iv) explicit 3D reconstruction that recovers geometry or motion. Beyond this taxonomy, we highlight three key open challenges in this area: structuring unstructured videos into training-ready episodes, grounding video-derived supervision into robot-executable actions under embodiment and viewpoint heterogeneity, and designing evaluation protocols that better predict real-world deployment performance and transfer efficiency, thereby informing future research directions. A curated list of papers and resources is available at .

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应动力学编排（ADO）框架，通过在线反事实推演持续评估模型库中各动力学模型的预测误差，在模型预测控制中动态选择最适合当前导航上下文的模型，在保证实时性的同时显著降低预测误差，并在真实越野地面机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型预测控制（MPC）在自主导航中面临模型精度与实时效率的根本性权衡：高保真动力学模型预测准确但计算开销大，降低控制频率；轻量模型计算快但安全关键场景下预测错误可能导致灾难性后果（如车辆侧翻）。现有方法通常固定使用单一模型，无法适应不同地形和工况。

Method: 提出自适应动力学编排（ADO）框架，维护一个涵盖不同精度-效率权衡的动力学模型库。通过在线反事实推演（counterfactual rollouts），将已执行的控制动作回放至模型库中的所有模型，利用残差误差持续更新地形条件下的性能估计。基于这些实时估计，在每次控制循环中动态选择最适合当前导航上下文的动力学模型。

Result: 在真实越野地面机器人上的实验表明，ADO 相比固定低延迟基线显著降低了建模误差，同时接近最高保真模型的精度但无需承担其计算开销，在复杂地形中实现了更可靠、更有效的导航。

Conclusion: ADO 通过在线自适应模型选择有效解决了 MPC 中精度与效率的权衡问题，为自主导航提供了一种实用的动态模型编排方案，未来可扩展至更多类型的动力学模型和更复杂的机器人平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自适应动力学编排（ADO）框架，用于模型预测控制（MPC）中动态选择最合适的动力学模型，以平衡精度与效率。论文面向自主导航中的机器人控制与规划，属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on MPC for ground robots, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Model predictive control, adaptive dynamics selection, real-world robot validation, offline-road navigation, accuracy-efficiency trade-off

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) for autonomous navigation faces a fundamental trade-off between model accuracy and real-time efficiency. High-fidelity dynamics models can accurately predict complex vehicle-terrain interactions during trajectory rollouts, but incur significant computational cost, increasing inference latency and reducing control frequency. Conversely, lightweight models enable fast updates and dense sampling, yet may produce erroneous predictions under safety-critical conditions, potentially leading to catastrophic failures such as vehicle rollover. To address this trade-off, we propose Adaptive Dynamics Orchestration (ADO), a framework that dynamically selects the most appropriate dynamics model for the current navigation context. ADO maintains a library of models spanning diverse accuracy-efficiency profiles and continuously refines terrain-conditioned performance estimates using residual errors from online counterfactual rollouts, where executed control actions are replayed across the model library to assess predictive discrepancy. These estimates guide model selection in real time, balancing computational efficiency and predictive accuracy. Real-world experiments on an off-road ground robot demonstrate that ADO significantly reduces modeling error compared to a fixed low-latency baseline, while approaching the accuracy of the highest-fidelity model without incurring its computational cost, resulting in more reliable and effective navigation in challenging terrain.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性物理AI系统可以输出状态滚动、动作块和潜在计划，但低RMSE并不保证提案在物理上是可执行的。缺乏一个在执行前评估预测提案物理可行性的接口。

Method: 将物理可接受性形式化为预测-控制接口，包含运动学条件、动力学条件和直接到组合视界条件三个层级。在解码后的提案被执行前，使用这些条件评估其是否满足指定的物理包络，并在拒绝时给出组件级原因。

Result: 在Hugging Face LeRobot PushT数据集上，单步预测RMSE和标准化动力学残差达到AUC 0.982和0.972，纯运动学条件AUC 0.592，完整门控AUC 0.957。在基于重放的干预实验中，残差过滤器和完整物理可接受性门控阻止了87-89%的无效提案，同时保持平均进度接近0.998。

Conclusion: 物理可接受性条件能够有效识别物理上不可执行的预测提案，为预测性物理AI系统提供了一个实用的验证接口，有助于提升机器人系统的安全性和可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究预测性物理AI系统的物理可执行性（physical admissibility），提出运动学、动力学等条件来评估机器人动作提案是否物理可行，属于机器人规划与控制中的世界模型验证问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel physical admissibility filter for robot action proposals, but is tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: Robotics world model validation, Physical admissibility interface, Action proposal filtering, LeRobot PushT benchmark, Not generative world model

Abstract: Predictive Physical AI systems output state rollouts, action chunks, and latent plans, yet a low root-mean-square error (RMSE) does not imply that a particular proposal is physically executable. We formulate physical admissibility as a prediction-control interface: before execution, a decoded proposal is treated as candidate dynamics and evaluated using kinematic, dynamic, and direct-to-composed horizon conditions. Passing is not a certificate of task success; rejection identifies violation of the specified physical envelope and gives a component-level reason. On Hugging Face LeRobot PushT, controlled falsification shows that one-step prediction-RMSE and standardized dynamics residuals reach area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) 0.982 and 0.972, kinematic-only conditions reach AUC 0.592, and the full gate reaches AUC 0.957 with condition-level attribution. In replay-based intervention experiments, residual-based filters and the full physical-admissibility gate prevent 87-$89% of invalid proposals while preserving mean progress near 0.998.

</details>

*Barak Or*

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于Physical AI系统中"静默失败"问题的文献综述，指出现有具身基础模型、世界模型和自主系统在将多模态观测映射为物理动作时，可能产生看似合理但实际危险的错误动作，而现有安全机制（内容审核、经典机器人安全、不确定性估计等）均无法提供完整的运行时动作授权边界。论文提出了静默物理动作失败的定义、运行时护栏功能分类法以及评估要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI系统（如机器人基础模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、基于世界模型的自主系统）越来越多地将多模态观测和语言指令转化为物理动作，但黑盒模型可能在看似自信、合理且语义对齐的情况下发出物理上危险的动作。这种"静默失败"无法被传统AI内容审核或经典机器人安全机制完全捕获，现有模型能力与安全机制沿着分离的技术轨道发展，缺乏统一的运行时授权边界。

Method: 本文采用文献综述方法，系统梳理了具身基础模型、世界模型、机器人仿真、具身安全基准、安全控制、运行时保证、不确定性估计、验证和护栏评估等多个技术流派的现有工作，识别出各流派在运行时动作授权方面的共同缺口，并在此基础上提出了有界问题形式化、静默物理动作失败的定义、运行时护栏功能分类法以及护栏评估要求。

Result: 论文发现没有任何一个被调查的技术流派能提供完整的黑盒Physical AI模型与物理执行之间的运行时授权边界。基于此，论文构建了：1）有界问题形式化；2）静默物理动作失败的定义；3）运行时护栏功能的分类体系；4）护栏作为Physical AI保障机制的评估要求。

Conclusion: Physical AI系统的安全性需要一种新的运行时动作授权机制——"护栏"（guardrails），以弥补现有模型能力与安全机制之间的鸿沟。论文提出的分类法和评估要求为未来构建可验证的运行时安全保障提供了框架性指导，强调了在具身智能和世界模型部署中安全机制与能力发展必须同步推进。

Classification reason: 该论文综述了Physical AI系统中运行时动作授权（runtime action authorization）的安全问题，重点关注具身基础模型、世界模型、机器人仿真、安全控制等。这与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度相关，该子主题涵盖面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型，且论文中明确讨论了world-model-based autonomous systems和embodied foundation models的安全保障机制。

Importance reason: This paper is a safety-focused literature review on runtime action authorization, which is tangential to your core interests in generative models and continuous representations. Signals: Safety-focused review, Not generative model, Robotics safety, Runtime guardrails, Silent failures

Abstract: Physical AI systems increasingly map multimodal observations, language instructions, and learned world representations into physically consequential actions. Robotics foundation models, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based autonomous systems can condition decisions that move vehicles, robots, drones, and industrial machines. This transition exposes a safety problem that is not fully captured by conventional AI content moderation or by classical robot safety alone: a black-box model may issue a physically consequential action while appearing confident, plausible, and semantically aligned. The resulting failure can be silent, arising from sensor drift, occlusion, state-estimation error, distribution shift, hallucinated affordances, or invalid physical assumptions before downstream hardware controllers detect a violation. Across embodied foundation models, world models, robotics simulation, embodied safety benchmarks, safe control, runtime assurance, uncertainty estimation, verification, and guardrail evaluation, model capability and safety mechanisms have advanced along largely separate technical tracks. A recurring gap synthesized here is that no single stream surveyed in this review supplies a complete runtime authorization boundary between black-box Physical AI models and physical execution. The resulting analysis develops a bounded problem formulation, a definition of silent physical-action failure, a taxonomy of runtime guardrail functions, and evaluation requirements for comparing guardrails as Physical AI assurance mechanisms.

</details>

Conclusion: PEACE框架通过解耦规划与执行、引入世界模型和约束层，为无人机提供了一种更安全、可解释且高效的自主控制方案，减少了LLM调用开销并增强了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used to drive autonomous systems, yet existing approaches either keep the model in a tight control loop, raising latency and hallucination risk, or compile natural language into opaque end-to-end policies that are hard to explain, constraint and require domain-specific datasets and fine-tuning. We propose a planner-executor agent for PX4-based drones that decouples high-level mission planning from low-level control. A large language model performs single-pass task planning, while execution is handled through a structured ROS 2 tool-calling interface bridged to MAVLink. The system constructs a world model by combining modular 2D detectors (e.g., YOLO or vision-language models) with a pinhole depth projection module for 3D object localization. A constraint enforcement layer enforces altitude limits and horizontal geofencing, and bounded replanning enables recovery from execution-time action failures. We position our approach within three common design patterns for foundation-model-based robotics systems and demonstrate its feasibility in PX4 software-in-the-loop simulations in Gazebo. Results highlight improved explainability, constraint enforcement, and reduced LLM calls compared to tightly coupled LLM control. ### [17] [World Models for Robotic Manipulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00113)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作依赖于在执行前预测动作如何改变物体、接触和场景几何的能力。世界模型提供了这种能力，但该术语涵盖范围广泛（潜动力学模型、动作条件视频生成器、3D/4D场景预测器、物理模拟器、VLA系统中的预测模块等），导致文献碎片化，关键设计选择不清晰。本文旨在通过系统综述厘清该领域。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，操作性地将世界模型定义为动作条件预测系统，将其与感知模块、逆模型、策略、奖励和价值函数区分开。通过三个问题（预测什么表征、如何连接动作、何时使用预测）组织文献，将现有工作分为五种表征家族，建立功能分类法（集成预测-动作模型 vs. 显式预测规划器），并梳理了预训练、后训练和推理适应中的基础设施角色。

Result: 综述了34个机器人操作数据集，总结了预测保真度、任务性能和模拟器可靠性的评估协议，揭示了世界模型正从任务特定的动力学预测器演变为机器人学习的预测基础设施。

Conclusion: 世界模型在机器人操作中正变得越来越重要，但存在接触建模、幻觉控制、动作对齐和闭环基准测试等开放挑战。本文为未来研究提供了系统化的分类框架和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文是机器人操作领域世界模型的综述，核心关注具身智能中的动作条件预测、规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world models for robotics, a high-weight direction, with strong overlap in priority subtopics like world model and neural simulator. Signals: world model survey, robotic manipulation, action-conditioned prediction, predictive infrastructure, contact modeling challenge

Abstract: Robotic manipulation depends on the ability to anticipate how actions reshape objects, contacts, and scene geometry before execution. Learned world models provide this capability by predicting task-relevant future evolution under robot intervention, yet the term now spans latent dynamics models, action-conditioned video generators, three- and four-dimensional scene predictors, physics-informed simulators, and predictive modules inside vision-language-action systems. This breadth has fragmented the literature and obscured the design choices that matter for manipulation. We survey world models for robotic manipulation through three questions: what future representation is predicted, how prediction is connected to action, and when prediction is used in the robot-learning pipeline. We operationally define a world model as an action-conditioned predictive system and distinguish it from perception modules, inverse models, policies, rewards, and value functions. We then organize existing work into five representation families, develop a functional taxonomy that separates integrated prediction-action models from explicit predictive planners, and characterize infrastructure roles including synthetic experience generation, candidate filtering, search-based evaluation, learned environments, and outcome verification. We further map these roles across pretraining, post-training, and inference adaptation, review 34 manipulation datasets, and synthesize evaluation protocols for predictive fidelity, task performance, and simulator reliability. This survey shows that world models are evolving from task-specific dynamics predictors into predictive infrastructure for robot learning, while exposing open challenges in contact modeling, hallucination control, action alignment, and benchmarking under closed-loop use.

</details>

*Yusuke Sano, Takeshi Itoga*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Completion at the Boundary (CaB) 方法，解决 VLA 智能体在复合指令（如"先做A，再做B"）执行中何时切换以及如何平稳切换的问题。CaB 通过预测边界阶段 token（Before/Hit/After）保留两侧边界证据，CaB-When 用于判断切换时机，CaB-How 用于生成边界稳定的动作控制，在低校准（单次全局阈值设定）约束下提升了 Minecraft VLA 基准上的复合任务执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 智能体缺乏判断指令是否完成的操作接口，尤其在短复合指令（"做A，然后做B"）中，切换时机不当会导致下游任务级联失败。现有方法需要大量校准或在线学习，不适用于开放指令空间。

Method: 提出 Completion at the Boundary (CaB) 框架，包含：(1) Boundary-Phase Tokens（Before/Hit/After）作为事件局部的完成对象，保留两侧边界证据；(2) CaB-When 将完成对象转化为最小可审计的切换决策；(3) CaB-How 复用完成对象来条件化动作生成，实现边界稳定的切换控制。采用干预感知的 E1/E2 协议进行评测。

Result: 在 Minecraft VLA 基准上，CaB 在匹配容量和可部署性约束下，相比基线方法显著提升了复合指令执行质量和切换平稳性。

Conclusion: CaB 通过保留两侧边界证据的完成对象设计，在低校准约束下实现了有效的完成检测与切换控制，为 VLA 智能体的可部署操作接口提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）智能体在复合指令执行中的完成检测与切换控制问题，属于具身智能体在开放指令空间中的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA switching control in robotics, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLA agent, composite instruction, switching control, Minecraft benchmark, low-calibration deployment

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) agents can execute natural-language instructions, yet deployed systems still lack an operational interface: deciding when the instruction is complete. This gap is acute in short composites ("do A, then B"), where mistimed handoffs cascade into downstream failures. Completion is inherently closed-loop because switching is an intervention that changes the instruction context and thus future actions and observations. We study completion under a deployable low-calibration regime motivated by open-ended instruction spaces, enforcing no test-time relearning and a single globally calibrated switching rule selected once on development set and reused unchanged on test set. Under this constraint, collapsing asymmetric boundary evidence into a single scalar can be brittle under polarity shifts across tasks. We propose Completion at the Boundary (CaB), which predicts an event-local completion object in the form of Boundary-Phase Tokens (Before/Hit/After), retaining two-sided boundary evidence under this discipline. CaB-When converts this completion object into a minimal, auditable switching decision (when), while CaB-How reuses the same completion object to condition action generation for boundary-stable control through handoffs (how). Using an intervention-aware E1/E2 protocol, we show that CaB improves composite execution and handoff quality on a first-person Minecraft VLA benchmark under matched capacity and deployability constraints.

</details>


### [19] [Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00229)
*Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出 Continuous Reasoning for VLA，用共享高斯潜变量作为连续推理介质替代自然语言推理，通过自验证目标训练，在 LIBERO-PRO 和真实机器人上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言作为推理介质在 VLM 中有效，但与连续控制的时间粒度不匹配——一个推理步跨越多个动作块，与当前动作耦合弱。因此需要一种更适合 VLA 的推理介质：可跨实例共享、可通过下游动作改进验证、与时间扩展控制结构对齐。

Method: 模型首先预测一组结构化连续思维（continuous thoughts）作为共享高斯潜变量接口，然后将其作为共享上下文用于分块动作生成。训练采用自验证目标：指数移动平均（EMA）教师模型必须成功利用学生的推理结果来预测目标动作，从而确保推理的可共享性和可验证性。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上提升了鲁棒性；在真实机器人上，TX-G2（AgiBot G2 兼容变体）上平均子任务成功率比 π0.5 提高 40.4%，HSR 上提高 26.3%。

Conclusion: VLA 中的推理不在于增加额外 token，而在于构建可共享、可验证的内部动作语言。连续推理介质比自然语言更适合机器人控制场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略的连续推理方法，核心是机器人动作规划与控制，属于具身智能领域。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。同时论文涉及连续潜空间推理（continuous reasoning as shared Gaussian latent），与连续语言模型方向也有交叉。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent reasoning for VLA, a core priority subtopic, with strong empirical gains on real robots. Signals: continuous reasoning, shared Gaussian latent, self-verification objective, VLA policy, LIBERO-PRO

Abstract: Natural language is a powerful reasoning medium for language and vision-language models, but it is mismatched to the granularity of continuous control. Text and explicit subgoals operate at task-level granularity, whereas vision-language-action (VLA) policies must choose actions at a much finer temporal scale; a single reasoning step can therefore span many action chunks while remaining only weakly coupled to the action needed now. This suggests a different question for VLA: what should play the role of language? We argue that a useful VLA reasoning medium must be shareable across model instances, verifiable through downstream action improvement, and aligned with temporally extended control structure. Based on this view, we propose Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action. Our model first predicts continuous reasoning in the form of a structured set of continuous thoughts, then reuses them as shared context for chunk-structured action generation. Better action prediction alone does not certify good reasoning: if the same internal medium cannot be shared across model instances and independently verified through improved downstream control, the added latent may simply become a model-private shortcut that helps on seen behaviors without supporting generalizable control. We therefore instantiate continuous reasoning as a shared Gaussian latent interface and train it with a self-verification objective in which an exponential-moving-average teacher must successfully consume the student's reasoning when predicting target actions. Empirically, Continuous Reasoning improves LIBERO-PRO robustness and performs strongly on real robots, raising mean subtask success over {\pi}0.5 by 40.4% on TX-G2, an AgiBot G2-compatible variant, and 26.3% on HSR. This suggests that reasoning in VLA is less about extra tokens than about a shareable, verifiable internal language for action.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于机器人持久地图的信念一致性更新算子，通过逐类校准的提交门和逐事件冲突丢弃窗口，将基础模型（VLM）的语义断言与几何感知通道进行一致性融合，显著提升了地图语义标注的精度和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人持久地图系统将几何感知（高精度但类别有限）与基础模型语义通道（覆盖广但未校准可靠性）简单融合为逐元素后验投票，缺乏对基础模型逐类可靠性的校准机制，也无法检测两个通道之间的实时矛盾，导致地图语义标注质量不可控。

Method: 提出一个更新算子，包含两个协作机制：(1) 逐类校准提交门（per-class calibrated commit gate），对基础模型的每类语义输出进行可靠性校准；(2) 逐事件冲突丢弃窗口（per-event conflict-drop window），当几何通道与基础模型断言在空间上矛盾时，拒绝提交该基础模型断言。在KITTI-360和ScanNet上使用oracle几何通道（全景真值）和现成语义分割器（Mask2Former）进行评估。

Result: 该算子生成的提交地图精度显著更高：KITTI上车类提交精度99.7%（对比仅校准算子的43.9%），平均每类IoU 0.522（对比0.180）；相比整体式组合VLM提示，在更高精度下保留了更多组合真阳性。该框架在oracle和现成分割器几何通道上均达到部署级质量，且对基础模型替换具有不变性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模几何与语义通道之间的信念一致性，可以大幅提升机器人持久地图中语义标注的可靠性和精度，为多源感知融合提供了一种实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人持久地图中几何感知与基础模型语义通道的一致性融合，属于机器人/具身智能领域的世界模型构建问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic mapping and VLM calibration, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: VLM calibration, persistent robotic maps, geometric perception fusion, belief consistency, not generative

Abstract: Persistent maps used by autonomous robots increasingly fuse a geometric perception stack whose assertions are well-characterized with a foundation-model channel that produces semantic claims without calibrated reliability about the same scene. Contemporary mapping systems integrate the two channels by treating the foundation-model channel as an additional voter into a per-element posterior, uncalibrated for its own per-class reliability and without machinery to flag when the two channels contradict each other at a given moment. We propose an update operator with two cooperating mechanisms: a per-class calibrated commit gate, and a per-event conflict-drop window that refuses to commit foundation-model claims contradicted by the geometric channel at the moment of the claim. We evaluate on KITTI-360 and ScanNet, with an oracle geometric channel (panoptic ground truth) and an off-the-shelf online semantic segmenter (Mask2Former) to demonstrate real-world performance. The operator produces substantially more accurate committed maps (KITTI is car commit precision 99.7% vs. 43.9% for the calibration-only operator; mean per-class IoU 0.522 vs. 0.180), retains more compositional true positives at higher precision than a monolithic compositional VLM prompt. The framework operates at deployment quality across both oracle and off-the-shelf-segmenter geometric channels, and is invariant under foundation-model substitution.

</details>


### [21] [PaCo-VLA: Passivity-Shielded Compliance Prior for Contact-Rich Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00515)
*Haofan Cao, Zhaoyang Li, Zhichao You, Liang Guo, Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, eess.SY

TL;DR: PaCo-VLA 提出了一种被动性屏蔽的柔顺先验框架，将 VLA 模型的输出重新解释为任务级柔顺提议（语义绑定、任务阶段、导纳调度），而非直接的低层电机指令。一个高频的被动性屏蔽层通过能量-储层记账和边界检查来监管这些提议，防止无效或未经验证的模型预测绕过接触物理。在连接器插入任务中，该方法在无被动性违规的情况下实现了优于未屏蔽 VLA 基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务既需要高层语义推理，又需要对高频接触动力学的安全调控。现有的 VLA 模型虽然提供了前所未有的语义泛化能力，但其低频率输出在力敏感任务中缺乏直接控制所需的可靠性，存在语义到控制之间的鸿沟。

Method: PaCo-VLA 将 VLA 网络输出视为任务级柔顺提议（语义绑定、任务阶段、导纳调度），而非直接电机命令。引入一个高频的、与提议无关的被动性屏蔽层，通过能量-储层（energy-tank）记账和边界检查来监管这些提议，防止无效、过时或未经验证的模型预测绕过低层接触物理。这种解耦架构还支持因果评估，将语义贡献与几何捷径分离。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的连接器插入实验中，PaCo-VLA 相比未屏蔽的 VLA 基线实现了更高的精度，即使在对抗性柔顺偏移下也能保持零被动性违规。

Conclusion: 该框架在导纳端口建立了可证明的采样-被动运行时契约，为在接触丰富领域部署基础模型提供了运行时接口，弥合了语义推理与安全物理交互之间的鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PaCo-VLA，一种面向接触丰富操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，核心是结合被动性屏蔽（passivity shield）的柔顺先验，用于机器人操作中的安全控制。这属于具身智能、机器人规划与控制的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics contact-rich manipulation with passivity shielding, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Robotics, Contact-rich manipulation, Passivity shield, VLA model, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation demands both high-level semantic reasoning and the safe regulation of high-frequency contact dynamics. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide unprecedented semantic generalization, their low-rate outputs lack the reliability required for direct plant authority in force-sensitive tasks. To bridge this semantic-to-control gap, we introduce PaCo-VLA, a passivity-shielded compliance prior that recasts the VLA interface. Rather than trusting VLAs with direct motor commands, PaCo-VLA treats network outputs as task-level compliance proposals: semantic bindings, task stages, and admittance schedules. A high-frequency, proposal-independent passivity shield governs these proposals through energy-tank accounting and boundary checks, preventing invalid, stale, or unverified model predictions from bypassing low-level contact physics. This decoupled architecture also enables causal evaluation, isolating semantic contributions from geometric shortcuts. Extensive simulated and real-world connector-insertion experiments demonstrate that PaCo-VLA achieves superior precision over unshielded VLA baselines, sustaining zero passivity violations even under adversarial compliance shifts. This framework establishes a provably sampled-passive runtime contract at the admittance port and provides a runtime interface for deploying foundation models in contact-rich domains.

</details>


### [22] [DriveAnchor: Progressive Anchor-based Flow Learning for Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00519)
*Limin Yan, Haoyun Tang, Yutao Qiu, Hongqing Liu, Haoyu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: DriveAnchor 提出三阶段自动驾驶规划框架：演示流预训练构建轨迹词汇表替代高斯先验，引导流后训练通过能量场实现可控性，奖励精炼微调使用零阶强化学习对齐碰撞避免目标。在约200万场景上近距碰撞率降低89%，推理仅2.06ms，已通过实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法在行为多样性、可控性和安全性之间存在权衡，难以同时满足三者。扩散模型虽能生成多样化轨迹但缺乏可控性，且安全对齐需要复杂的 ODE/SDE 转换和似然计算。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 演示流预训练——用最远点采样构建2398个轨迹形状的词汇表替代无结构高斯先验；(2) 引导流后训练——联合训练能量场模块与流匹配，仅依赖静态道路几何将锚点重定位到用户指定的走廊多边形；(3) 奖励精炼微调——利用流匹配模型在单步模式下的确定性前馈特性，将奖励优化转化为锚点空间中的方向搜索，使用零阶强化学习。

Result: 在约200万保留驾驶场景上，近距碰撞率降低89%，平均奖励提升32%，且不降低模仿精度。在 NVIDIA Drive Orin 上推理仅需2.06ms。已通过实车测试验证。

Conclusion: DriveAnchor 通过渐进式锚点流学习，在行为多样性、可控性和安全性之间取得了良好平衡，且计算高效，具备实际部署潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DriveAnchor，一个面向自动驾驶规划的三阶段框架，涉及轨迹生成、流匹配（flow matching）和强化学习对齐，属于具身智能中的规划控制与动作条件生成，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving planning, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model, autonomous driving, flow matching, reinforcement learning, real-world validation

Abstract: We present DriveAnchor, a three-stage framework for autonomous driving planning that achieves behavioral diversity, controllability, and safety in a composable pipeline. Demonstration Flow Pretraining replaces the unstructured Gaussian prior with a vocabulary of 2,398 trajectory shapes constructed by farthest-point sampling, structurally grounding behavioral diversity in vocabulary coverage. Guided Flow Post-training jointly post-trains an Energy Field module with flow matching (FM), conditioning the Energy Field on static road geometry alone, to relocate anchors toward user-specified corridor polygons before flow generation, adding controllability without differentiable guidance; after Stage 2, new corridor presets require only Energy Field updates, not FM retraining. Reward-Refined Flow Fine-tuning applies zeroth-order reinforcement learning to align each anchor's output with collision-avoidance objectives: because the flow-matching model is a deterministic feedforward network in single-step mode, each anchor uniquely determines the output trajectory, reducing reward optimization to a direction search in anchor space without log-likelihood computation or ODE-to-SDE conversion. Evaluated on approximately 2 million held-out driving scenarios, DriveAnchor reduces near-range collision rates by 89% and improves mean reward by 32% without degradation in imitation accuracy, with 2.06 ms inference on NVIDIA Drive Orin. DriveAnchor has been validated through real-world vehicle testing, confirming its practicality for production deployment.

</details>

Abstract: Embodied world models have emerged as a promising paradigm in robotics by predicting how robot actions affect the surrounding scene. However, the rollout inference remains computationally expensive in pixel space, as long-horizon manipulation videos typically have to be generated frame by frame. This cost cannot be easily reduced by indiscriminately dropping frames, since downstream policies rely on complete preservation of sparse task-relevant events such as approach, contact, grasp, and release. To address this challenge, we propose Sparse Keyframe Interpolation Paradigm (SKIP), an event-preserving sparse-to-dense framework that avoids dense frame-by-frame generation. SKIP first identifies task-relevant keyframes by leveraging robot-aware multimodal features. It then synthesizes only these keyframes with a sparse video diffusion model. A learned gap predictor and an action-conditioned interpolator subsequently reconstruct the missing intervals according to the robot actions. On LIBERO, SKIP generates dense rollouts $4.16\times$ faster than a dense baseline while improving visual fidelity and reducing aggregate FVD by $89.0\%$. Importantly, SKIP-generated videos are effective policy-training data. Even when they fully replace real demonstrations, $\pi_{0.5}$ success drops only $1.3$ pp in LIBERO simulation and $6.7$ pp on the real robot, whereas fully dense frame-by-frame generation collapses by $48$ to $58$ pp.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出价值引导扩散规划（Value-Guided Diffusion Planning）框架：1）每个机器人使用基于单智能体运动数据训练的扩散模型独立生成候选轨迹；2）通过多智能体强化学习（MARL）训练一个集中式价值函数，评估多机器人联合轨迹的回报；3）在反向扩散过程中，利用价值函数的梯度对去噪分布进行指数倾斜（exponential tilting）引导，使生成轨迹偏向更高多智能体回报的方向，从而实现交互感知的轨迹生成。

Result: 在四机器人迷宫仿真环境中，该方法将智能体间干扰率从55.4%降低到41.8%，同时保持了分散式轨迹生成的可扩展性。实验表明，MARL价值引导能有效将协调引入分散式生成规划器，无需完全联合的多机器人模型。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过MARL价值函数引导扩散模型的去噪过程，可以在不牺牲分散式生成可扩展性的前提下实现多机器人协调运动规划。该方法避免了集中式联合规划的扩展瓶颈，为大规模多机器人系统的运动规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Coordinating multiple robots in shared environments requires generating feasible trajectories for each agent while accounting for interactions among agents. Centralized planning approaches become difficult to scale as the number of robots increases, while decentralized approaches that allow each agent to plan independently do not inherently account for inter-agent interactions. This paper presents a framework for coordinated multi-robot motion planning that combines decentralized generative trajectory planning with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL)-based coordination. Each robot independently generates candidate trajectories using a diffusion model trained on single-agent motion data, leveraging the generative model's ability to produce feasible and diverse trajectories. To reduce conflicts between agents, a centralized value function trained via MARL guides the reverse diffusion process through gradient-based steering, enabling interaction-aware trajectory generation without centralized joint planning or retraining of the generative model. This guidance follows an exponential tilting formulation, in which the value function biases the denoising distribution toward trajectories with higher expected multi-agent return. The framework is evaluated in a simulated maze environment with four mobile robots. Experimental results show that the proposed value-guided diffusion planning reduces the inter-agent interference rate from 55.4% to 41.8%, demonstrating that coordination can be effectively achieved while preserving the scalability of decentralized trajectory generation. These results suggest that MARL-based value guidance can effectively introduce coordination into decentralized generative planners without requiring a fully joint multi-robot model.

</details>

Abstract: World models, internal simulators that learn the structure and dynamics of an environment, have emerged as a central paradigm in the pursuit of artificial general intelligence, enabling agents to predict, plan, and reason within learned representations. Despite rapid progress across reinforcement learning, robotics, autonomous driving, and video generation, the field lacks a unified framework integrating its diverse architectural choices, training methods, reasoning mechanisms, and application settings. This survey addresses that gap with a multi-axis taxonomy organized along four dimensions: (i) architecture, encompassing representation format, dynamics formulation, input modality, learning paradigm, and downstream application; (ii) methodological family, including state-space and recurrent approaches, transformer-based models, diffusion-based generators, physics-informed networks, and language-augmented multimodal systems; (iii) reasoning strategy, covering imagination-based planning, latent policy learning, counterfactual reasoning, and planning under uncertainty; and (iv) application domain, spanning robotics, autonomous driving, video prediction, multimodal agents, reinforcement learning, scientific modeling, medical imaging, educational measurement, and business and finance. Tracing the field from early cognitive-science foundations to milestone systems such as PlaNet, the Dreamer family, MuZero, Sora, Cosmos, and Genie, we examine how these dimensions interact and highlight the recent convergence of chain-of-thought reasoning with world-model imagination. We review evaluation protocols and benchmarks, identify persistent challenges such as compounding prediction errors, sim-to-real transfer, and fragmented evaluation, and outline future directions toward unified multimodal world models, foundation-scale interactive simulators, and safe deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and embodied navigation by bridging 2D VLM understanding with 3D spatial reasoning via a hierarchical semantic-geometric map. Signals: world model, zero-shot navigation, hierarchical semantic-geometric map, VLM spatial reasoning, embodied agent

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables embodied agents to reach target locations in unseen environments by following language instructions. Despite recent progress with vision-language models (VLMs), a critical semantic-geometric gap remains: while VLMs excel at language and 2D visual understanding, they struggle with 3D spatial reasoning and fail to capture the causal dynamics between actions and spatial transitions, resulting in unreliable navigation, particularly in zero-shot settings. To bridge this gap, we propose a Hierarchical Semantic-Geometric Map (HSGM) that transforms 3D geometric information into a structured representation compatible with VLMs, effectively linking them to the physical world. Specifically, HSGM is represented as a multi-channel top-down map organized into three levels: (1) geometric level that records navigable regions and obstacles, (2) semantic level that represents objects and their relations, and (3) decision level that supports high-level task reasoning and goal selection. During navigation, the VLM acts as a high-level semantic planner, interpreting the spatial layout encoded in the HSGM to select geometrically valid waypoints, while low-level, collision-free movements between waypoints are executed by a classical path-planning algorithm, fully decoupling semantic reasoning from action execution. Additionally, complex instructions are decomposed into subtasks to alleviate the problem of progress forgetting or hallucinating in long-horizon navigation. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks demonstrate that our zero-shot framework achieves state-of-the-art performance and even outperforms several supervised methods. Code is available at .

</details>

Abstract: Vision-Language Action (VLA) models continue to face challenges such as slow inference speed and difficulty performing fine-grained motion adjustments, limiting their widespread adoption in industry. While the Real-Time Action Chunking (RTAC) algorithm has been proposed to address these bottlenecks, bridging the gap between the algorithm provided in pseudocode to a stable, real-world deployment on a low-cost robotic arm remains a challenge. In this work, we present a complete system-level implementation of RTAC tailored for a low-cost robotic manipulation system. We advance beyond the original high-level pseudocode by optimizing the threading implementation for the policy inference and control pipeline, reducing end-to-end latency and improving responsiveness without modifying the underlying policy. We evaluate this system on tasks involving the manipulation of agricultural produce, specifically garlic bulbs and walnuts. Experimental results demonstrate that our custom threading implementation significantly improves control stability and speed compared to the base implementation of RTAC.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable progress for mobile manipulation, but their performance on long-horizon tasks remains poor. These tasks are especially challenging because (1) progress toward high-level goals must be maintained across extended sequences of spatially distributed subtasks, and (2) early execution errors compound rapidly over the task horizon. These challenges persist despite finetuning on large human teleoperated mobile manipulation data, indicating that more data alone may not resolve the problem. To address these challenges, we propose MPVI: Motion Planner / VLA Interleaving, a framework that integrates model-based motion planning with VLAs to improve robustness without further training. The proposed integration enables localization and navigation to distant or occluded target objects through cluttered scenes using open-vocabulary object detection, frontier exploration and motion planning. However, such integration is non-trivial, requiring reliable switching between modules; we show one way forward via VLM-based completion checking with proprioceptive triggers. We evaluate our approach on the BEHAVIOR-1K benchmark and demonstrate 113% improvement in task progress over a top end-to-end VLA baseline. Additional details are available at the project page: .

</details>

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时域和精细机器人操作任务上，τ₀-WM 展现出优于其他相关基线的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 τ₀-WM，一个面向机器人操作的统一视频-动作世界模型，集成了策略学习、视频预测和动作评估，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Integrates video diffusion world model with action prediction, directly relevant to world model and video generation priorities. Signals: world model, video diffusion, robotic manipulation, test-time computation, action-conditioned video simulator

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires models that generate executable actions while anticipating and evaluating their future consequences before physical execution. We present $\tau_0$-World Model ($\tau_0$-WM), a unified video-action world model that integrates policy learning, video prediction, and action evaluation within a single future-predictive framework. Built on a shared video diffusion backbone, $\tau_0$-WM provides two complementary interfaces. First, a video action model jointly predicts future visual latents and continuous action chunks from multi-view observations, language instructions, and robot state. Second, an action-conditioned video simulator rolls out candidate action chunks into multi-view futures and predicts dense task-progress scores. The model is trained on approximately $27{,}300$ hours of real-robot teleoperation, UMI-style interaction, egocentric human videos, and rollout or failure trajectories using modality-specific supervision masks. At inference time, $\tau_0$-WM uses test-time computation to sample action candidates, rank them with re-denoising consistency, and invoke simulator-based rectification for low-quality candidates. On challenging long-horizon and fine-grained robotic manipulation tasks, $\tau_0$-WM shows superior performance over other relevant baselines.

</details>

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to learn how to execute tasks via observation. However, real-world environments like homes and offices are often severely partially observed due to their large spatial scales. In addition, many tasks involve executing a series of subtasks requiring autonomous robots to reason over extended time horizons. To address these challenges, we propose using scene graphs as an explicit and structured memory mechanism in imitation learning. By maintaining a dynamic scene graph that captures object-centric relationships and their evolution over time, our method allows the agent to retain relevant historical context during task execution to efficiently reason over incrementally accrued scene information. Our experiments on simulated mobile manipulation and real-world tabletop manipulation demonstrate that our approach substantially improves policy performance, particularly in settings that demand long-term reasoning and robust generalization under partial observability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的诊断框架，通过行为 rollout 分析和稀疏自编码器特征分析两个视角，系统比较 WAM（世界-动作模型）和 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略在机器人操作中的行为差异和内部表征特性。在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin2.0 上评估了7种策略，发现仅靠任务成功率会掩盖关键差异：WAM 能改善目标级行为和选择性，但收益依赖架构且推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对 WAM 是否真正带来行为上有意义的改进的系统性理解，仅靠任务成功率无法揭示 WAM 与 VLA 在行为动态和内部表征上的本质差异。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的诊断框架，包含两个互补视角：(1) 行为 rollout 分析——测量动作动态一致性、目标物体进展、干扰物扰动和运行时成本；(2) 基于稀疏自编码器的特征分析——将内部表征分类为记忆型、反应型或预测型，揭示模型是否编码了面向未来的结构。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin2.0 上评估了7种策略（包括直接 VLA、联合 WAM、顺序 WAM 和辅助 WAM）。结果表明：WAM 通常能改善目标级行为和选择性，但收益依赖具体架构且推理成本更高；顺序 WAM 展现出最清晰的预测结构，而辅助 WAM 和联合 WAM 分别压缩或纠缠了未来信息。

Conclusion: 任务成功率本身会掩盖 WAM 与 VLA 之间的关键差异。未来 WAM 设计应保留行为上可用的未来表征，以实现高效操作。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 WAM（World-Action Model）与 VLA（Vision-Language-Action）策略在机器人操作中的行为与表征差异，核心关注世界模型的未来预测能力对机器人行为的影响，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Provides behavioral and representational diagnostics for world models in robotics, but is outside the user's core priority subtopics. Signals: World model diagnostics, Behavioral analysis, Sparse autoencoder, Robotics manipulation, WAM vs VLA comparison

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies and World-Action Models (WAM) represent two increasingly important paradigms for robotic manipulation. However, it remains unclear whether future prediction in WAMs leads to behaviorally meaningful improvements beyond final task success. In this paper, we ask whether WAMs merely add future prediction, or whether they change robot behavior and internal representations in ways that are actionable for control. We introduce a model-agnostic diagnostic framework that compares WAMs and VLAs through two complementary lenses: behavioral rollout analysis and sparse-autoencoder-based feature analysis. The behavioral protocol measures action dynamics consistency, target-object progress, distractor disturbance, and runtime cost. The feature-space protocol characterizes internal representations as memorized, reactive, or predictive, revealing whether models encode future-oriented structure. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, we evaluate 7 policies spanning direct VLAs and joint, sequential, and auxiliary WAMs. Our results show that success alone hides key differences: WAMs often improve object-level behavior and target selectivity, but their gains depend on architecture and incur higher inference cost. Sequential WAMs show the clearest predictive structure, while auxiliary and joint WAMs respectively compress or entangle future information. These findings suggest future directions for WAMs design to preserve behaviorally actionable future representations for efficient manipulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出 Implicit Drifting Policy (IDP)，一种一步式模仿学习框架，通过隐式提取条件专家几何结构来约束动作流形，无需显式估计漂移场，在2D/3D/真实机器人操作任务上实现高效且保持动作有效性的生成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散或流匹配的生成式动作策略在行为克隆中表现优异，但其迭代采样过程延迟高，难以满足高频机器人控制需求。现有一步式方法虽缓解了延迟问题，但丢弃了中间轨迹演化带来的动作校正机制。直接恢复该机制需要显式估计训练时的漂移场，但由于条件演示稀疏性，这在数学上是不适定的。

Method: IDP 从观测相似专家动作的局部变化中提取条件专家几何结构，并将其与全局参考几何结构对比，分离出条件特定约束。该局部几何结构自适应地加权一个标量势目标，结合专家近端终端评估，在训练过程中直接对一步生成器施加流形约束，无需显式向量场估计。

Result: 在2D、3D和真实机器人操作任务上的广泛评估表明，IDP 有效维持了对有效动作流形的遵循，优于显式漂移方法，并与强一步基线方法性能相当。

Conclusion: IDP 通过隐式几何约束实现了高效的一步生成式动作策略，在保持低延迟的同时恢复了类似漂移的动作校正能力，为高频机器人控制中的生成式策略提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种面向机器人行为克隆的一步生成式动作策略（Implicit Drifting Policy），核心是解决扩散/流匹配策略迭代采样延迟问题，同时保持动作流形约束。这属于机器人动作规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics control and imitation learning, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: one-step imitation learning, implicit drifting policy, action manifold constraints, robotics manipulation, flow matching for action generation

Abstract: Generative action policies based on diffusion or flow matching excel in behavior cloning, yet their iterative sampling is prohibitive for high-frequency robot control. While recent one-step formulations alleviate this latency, they inevitably discard the intermediate trajectory evolution that provides crucial action correction. Directly recovering this mechanism by explicitly estimating a training-time drifting field is mathematically ill-posed due to extreme conditional demonstration sparsity. We introduce Implicit Drifting Policy (IDP), a one-step imitation learning framework that brings the training-time correction of Drifting into policy learning without explicit vector field estimation. IDP extracts a conditional expert geometry from the local variation of observation-similar expert actions, and compares it against a global reference geometry to isolate condition-specific constraints. This local geometric structure adaptively weights a scalar potential objective. Combined with an expert-proximal terminal evaluation, IDP directly enforces manifold constraints on the one-step generator during training. Extensive evaluations across 2D, 3D, and real-world manipulation tasks show IDP effectively maintains adherence to valid action manifolds, improving upon explicit drifting methods and achieving competitive performance with strong one-step baselines.

</details>
