<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的测试时神经干预方法使用固定系数，属于开环控制，无法适应具身控制中随时间变化的任务状态和概念误差，容易导致过校正、振荡和任务成功率下降。

Method: 将表征与调控解耦：沿运动对齐的残差方向进行干预，同时使用反馈控制器（PID或RL）在线调整干预强度，实现闭环自适应控制。

Result: 在四个LIBERO任务套件上，CTRL-STEER比固定系数基线实现了更稳定的概念调控和更好的调控-任务成功权衡，且无需修改或重新训练基础模型。

Conclusion: 闭环神经激活控制能有效提升VLA模型在具身任务中的稳定性和成功率，为可调控的机器人策略提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CTRL-STEER，一种面向VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的闭环神经激活控制框架，用于具身机器人策略的在线概念调控。论文在LIBERO任务套件上使用OpenVLA策略进行实验，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制范畴，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied VLA control rather than core continuous language/multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: closed-loop control, vision-language-action, neural activation steering, PID controller, reinforcement learning controller

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can be steered at test time by intervening on semantically meaningful internal directions, but existing methods use a fixed steering coefficient, effectively operating in open loop. This is poorly suited to embodied control, where task state and concept error evolve over time, often causing overcorrection, oscillation, and reduced task success, especially for temporal behaviors such as speed and smoothness. We propose CTRL-STEER, a closed-loop framework that replaces static intervention strength with adaptive, time-varying control signals. The key idea is to decouple representation from regulation: rather than assuming temporal concepts are directly controlled by individual neurons, we steer along motion-aligned residual directions while a feedback controller adjusts intervention magnitude online. We instantiate this framework with both PID and reinforcement learning based controllers. Experiments with a fine-tuned OpenVLA policy on four LIBERO task suites show that CTRL-STEER achieves more stable concept regulation and a better steering-task success trade-off than fixed-coefficient baselines, without modifying or retraining the base model.

</details>

*Renhao Zhang, Haotian Fu, Mingxi Jia, George Konidaris, Yilun Du, Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 提出参数化扩散策略（PDP），通过学习一个行为流形将扩散策略从随机多样性生成转变为精确可控的行为引导工具，支持策略间的平滑插值和新约束下的零样本适应，在仿真和真实机器人任务上显著优于标准扩散策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散策略虽然能生成多样化的行为，但缺乏对生成行为的精确控制能力，难以在保持多样性的同时实现策略间的平滑插值或适应新约束。现有方法通常需要重新训练或微调模型权重，效率低下。

Method: PDP 构建一个低维连续参数嵌入的行为流形，使得流形中潜表示之间的距离反映物理轨迹之间的语义相似性。扩散策略以该流形中的参数为条件，从而将扩散过程从随机多样性生成转变为可优化的行为引导工具。通过在该流形上进行优化，可以实现已知策略间的平滑插值以及对新约束的高效适应，无需更新策略权重。

Result: 在多个复杂多模态基准任务上的仿真和真实机器人实验中，PDP 相比标准扩散策略显著提升了适应性能，特别是在需要合成新行为的场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: PDP 通过参数化行为流形将扩散策略从随机生成转变为精确可控的工具，为机器人行为学习提供了一种高效、可插值、可优化的新范式，在无需重新训练的情况下即可适应新约束。

Classification reason: 该论文提出参数化扩散策略（PDP），将扩散策略（diffusion policy）与低维连续参数嵌入的行为流形相结合，实现机器人动作的平滑插值与高效适应。这属于具身智能中面向机器人动作规划与控制的扩散策略方法，与 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel parameterized diffusion policy for controllable behavior steering, directly relevant to world models and efficient inference. Signals: world model, diffusion policy, controllable generation, efficient inference, zero-shot adaptation

Abstract: We propose Parameterized Diffusion Policy (PDP), a framework for learning diffusion policies conditioned on low-dimensional, continuous parameters embedded in a learned behavior manifold. By constructing this manifold so that distances between latent representations reflect the semantic similarity between physical trajectories, we transform diffusion from a mechanism for stochastic diversity into a precise and optimizable tool for behavior steering. Our approach enables smooth interpolation between known strategies and efficient adaptation to novel constraints without updating policy weights. We demonstrate that PDP significantly improves adaptation performance on complex multimodal benchmarks in both simulated and real-robot experiments compared to standard diffusion policies, particularly in scenarios requiring the synthesis of novel behaviors.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Generalized Action Manifold (GAM) 框架，通过时间不变性（弧长参数化解耦空间路径与时间动态）和几何不变性（Schema-Affine-Factorization 将轨迹映射到规范世界线）两个正交维度实现动作的广义协变性，并集成到 VLA 架构中，使稀疏演示能够稠密填充连续有效的动作流形，在具身智能任务中展现出优越的迁移和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法通过回归绝对坐标来学习动作，这违反了广义协变性原理，将内在的任务几何与刚性的执行模式混为一谈，导致策略被绑定到特定的运动风格和固定速度上，难以从有限数据中实现鲁棒泛化。

Method: 提出 Generalized Action Manifold (GAM) 框架，通过两个正交维度的不变性来强制执行广义协变性：(1) 时间不变性——使用弧长参数化器将空间路径几何与时间动态解耦，确保对速度变化的鲁棒性；(2) 几何不变性——通过 Schema-Affine-Factorization 机制将轨迹映射到姿态归一化坐标系中的规范"世界线"，区分不变几何模式与仿射调制。将 GAM 集成到结构化的 VLA 架构中。

Result: 实验结果表明，GAM 在具身智能任务上实现了优越的迁移能力和鲁棒性，显著优于忽略几何结构的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过时空解耦和广义协变性约束，GAM 框架能够从稀疏演示中学习到泛化能力更强的动作表征，为具身智能中的动作建模提供了新的几何视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Generalized Action Manifold (GAM) 框架，通过时空解耦实现动作流形的广义协变性，并集成到 VLA 架构中用于具身智能的动作建模与泛化。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，关注机器人/具身智能中的动作表征、规划与泛化。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous representation priorities via geometric invariance and manifold learning for embodied action. Signals: world_model, continuous manifold, spatio-temporal decoupling, embodied VLA, geometric invariance

Abstract: Achieving robust generalization from limited data is a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Prevailing methods fail by regressing absolute coordinates, which violates the principle of general covariance. Fundamentally, this conflates the intrinsic task geometry with rigid execution patterns, binding policies to specific motion styles and fixed speeds. To resolve this, we propose the Generalized Action Manifold (GAM) framework that enforces general covariance through structural disentanglement. Specifically, GAM realizes the manifold by enforcing invariance across two orthogonal dimensions: (1) Temporal Invariance, utilizing an Arc-Length Parameterizer to orthogonalize the spatial path geometry from temporal dynamics, ensuring robustness to velocity variations; (2) Geometric Invariance, where a Schema-Affine-Factorization mechanism maps trajectories to canonical ``world lines'' in a pose-normalized coordinate frame. This distinguishes invariant geometric schemas from affine modulations, ensuring spatial generalizability. By integrating GAM within a structured Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architecture, we enable sparse demonstrations to densely populate a continuous, valid action manifold. Empirical results demonstrate that GAM enables superior transfer and robustness capabilities, outperforming geometry-agnostic baselines.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Crazyflow，一个基于 JAX 的 GPU 加速、可微分的无人机模拟器，支持大规模并行仿真、梯度策略学习、采样避障以及在线飞行中强化学习。这属于面向机器人/具身智能的仿真器与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: High-speed differentiable drone simulator enabling in-flight learning and large-scale swarm simulation, directly relevant to world models and efficient inference. Signals: GPU-accelerated differentiable simulator, 4000-drone swarm simulation, in-flight RL in 0.38 seconds, sub-centimeter tracking accuracy, JAX-based

Abstract: High-quality, large-scale synthetic data from simulations is becoming a cornerstone for pushing the capabilities of robot algorithms. While aerial robotics simulators have evolved to support specialized needs such as fidelity, differentiability, and swarms independently, a unified platform that can synthesize data across all these domains is missing. In this work, we propose Crazyflow, a simulator designed to push the limits of aerial-robotics algorithm development, from model-based to data-driven methods, gradient-based to sampling-based approaches, and single-agent to multi-agent systems. Compared to existing state-of-the-art drone simulators, it achieves speeds more than an order of magnitude faster for a single drone and can simulate thousands of swarms of 4000 drones each. Real-world experiments show Crazyflow supports both analytical-gradient-based policy learning, achieving sub-centimeter trajectory tracking accuracy without domain randomization, and sampling-based obstacle avoidance at speeds exceeding half a billion steps per second. Breaking the traditional train-then-deploy paradigm, we show that its unprecedented speed even enables in-flight reinforcement learning; we demonstrate this by throwing a physical drone into the air and training a recovery policy from scratch in 0.38 seconds, successfully stabilizing the drone. Crazyflow supports multiple levels of simulation abstraction, is directly compatible with all open-source Crazyflie models, and enables rapid reconfiguration across custom drone platforms and applications by providing a light-weight system identification pipeline. By pushing accuracy, speed, and differentiability simultaneously, Crazyflow serves as an open-source resource for synthetic data generation, with emerging capabilities for large-scale parallelization for online, in-execution learning and optimization, opening the door to novel algorithm development.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于感知的安全滤波器对所有障碍物使用相同的距离安全裕度，或仅在控制器下游调整语义信息，未能将语义风险编码到空间表示中，导致高风险物体（如行人）与低风险物体（如树木）被同等对待。

Method: 1) 基于基础模型的SLAM前端从单目RGB视频重建稠密3D几何；2) 逐帧语义分割提供像素级类别标签并融合到重建几何中；3) 将几何-语义表示转换为ESDF，根据语义标签对不同类别区域施加不同的膨胀半径；4) 语义感知ESDF为CBF控制器提供局部距离值和空间导数，类别相关增益进一步调节控制器响应。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中实现10-20Hz的在线运行，在遥操作和自主导航中均展现出语义感知的安全行为，高风险物体被赋予更大的安全空间。

Conclusion: 将语义风险嵌入距离场能够有效提升CBF安全控制器的语义感知能力，且计算效率满足在线实时运行需求，为单目视觉安全控制提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于单目视觉的在线安全控制框架，将语义风险嵌入距离场用于CBF控制，属于机器人安全导航与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics safety control, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Semantic risk in ESDF, CBF-based safe control, monocular perception-to-control, online navigation, world model application but not generative

Abstract: We propose an online monocular perception-to-control framework that embeds semantic risk into the distance field used by Control Barrier Function (CBF)-based safe navigation and teleoperation. Many perception-based safety filters assign the same distance-based safety margin to all mapped obstacles or use semantics only as a downstream controller adjustment, rather than encoding semantic risk in the spatial representation. Our framework instead reasons online about obstacle geometry and class-dependent risk by embedding semantic information directly into the Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF). This design encodes semantic risk before control optimization, so high-risk objects exert a larger spatial influence in the safety field while retaining efficient ESDF queries at runtime. Specifically, a foundation-model-based SLAM front end reconstructs dense 3-D geometry from monocular RGB video, while per-frame semantic segmentation provides pixel-level class labels that are fused into the reconstructed geometry. The resulting geometric-semantic representation is then converted into an ESDF, where semantic labels identify safety-relevant regions and impose class-dependent inflation before field computation. The semantic-aware ESDF provides the local distance values and spatial derivatives required by the CBF controller, while class-dependent gains further regulate the controller response. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate online operation at 10--20 Hz and semantic-aware safe behavior in both teleoperation and autonomous navigation.

</details>

Method: 对经典 Open-Closed List (OCL) 搜索进行改进，集成两个学习组件：(1) 生成模型，从中间状态快速 rollout 生成完整轨迹；(2) 启发式模型，对候选推理路径进行优先级排序。引入新的探索控制机制，在 OCL 框架内协调两个学习模型。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for AI planning, yet their performance remains constrained by the training data distribution. One approach is to improve generated solutions during inference by scaling test-time compute. A more efficient alternative is to optimize the inference process itself. In this paper, we show that a modified version of a classical Open-Closed List (OCL) search provides just such an efficient inference procedure. Our algorithm synergizes two learned components: a generative model that performs fast rollouts from intermediate states and a heuristic model that prioritizes among candidate reasoning paths. Key contributions include novel exploration control mechanisms and integration of learned models within the OCL framework. Across multiple combinatorial planning domains, our approach outperforms both neurosymbolic search baselines and classical solvers in computational efficiency and solution quality.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal representation priorities via object-centric Gaussian splatting and action-conditional dynamics. Signals: world model, object-centric Gaussian splatting, action-conditional dynamics, spatio-temporal transformer, rigid body motion prediction

Abstract: World models enable intelligent agents to predict the consequences of their actions on the environment. In this paper, we propose Multi Rigid Object Gaussian World Model (MRO-GWM), a novel model that learns action-conditional dynamics of rigid objects in 3D. By representing the scene by object-centric Gaussians, we can represent arbitrary object shapes and multi-object scenes. We develop a novel spatio-temporal transformer architecture that predicts future rigid body motion from a history of object Gaussians and future actions. Objects are represented by their Gaussians in a canonical frame, which allows for describing object motion as rigid body transformation. Our model is trained on reconstructions from multiple viewpoints, which requires the model to handle partial observations of objects due to occlusions. We analyze prediction performance of our approach on synthetic datasets composed of typical household objects with multi-object dynamics and interactions by a robot end effector. We also evaluate our model in model-predictive control for non-prehensile manipulation in simulation.

</details>

*Shalfun Li, Victor Yao, Charles Yang, Truth Qu, Regis Cheng, Ryan Yu, Howard Lu, Newton Von, Vincent Chen, Yohann Tang, Maeve Zhang, Ellie Ma, Gody Li, Sage Yang, Lorien Shu, J.W. TL;DR: WALL-WM 提出了一种以语义事件为基本单元的世界动作模型（World Action Model），通过事件级 VLA 预训练和事件级数据生态（事件描述、聚类平衡采样）解决固定长度动作块与语言/视觉/动作之间的粒度不匹配问题，支持事件模式和统一模式两种推理方式，在大规模真实世界泛化评测中达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常从多模态或视频基础模型初始化，然后基于固定长度的动作块进行优化，导致语言（语义目标）、视觉（连续场景动态）和动作（控制级时间尺度）三者之间的粒度不匹配，VLA 训练退化为短视的相关性拟合。

Method: WALL-WM 将监督和数据组织围绕语义事件展开：(1) 事件级 VLA 预训练，以语义连贯的动作事件作为原子学习单元；(2) 构建事件级数据生态，包括事件描述和聚类平衡采样；(3) 支持两种推理模式：事件模式（消费下一事件描述，生成可变长度执行块）和统一模式（使用 VLM + Staircase Decoding 条件化固定长度块推理）；(4) 采用 Muon 优化器进行大规模预训练。

Result: WALL-WM 在语言、场景和任务上展现出广泛的泛化能力，在大规模真实世界泛化评测中取得了 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: WALL-WM 通过事件驱动的 VLA 预训练有效解决了动作块粒度不匹配问题，为通用世界动作模型提供了一种可扩展的规模化方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WALL-WM，一种面向机器人动作规划的世界动作模型（World Action Model），将视频-动作学习组织为事件驱动的视觉-语言-动作预训练，属于具身智能中的世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal reasoning with event-grounded VLA pretraining, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world action model, event-grounded VLA pretraining, continuous multimodal representation, variable-length execution, state-of-the-art real-world generalization

Abstract: WALL-WM is a World Action Model that shifts video-action learning from chunk-centric optimization to event-grounded Vision-Language-Action pretraining, using semantically coherent action events as the atomic unit of learning. Existing WAMs commonly initialize from multimodal or video foundation models and then optimize fixed-length action chunks conditioned directly on the current observation and instruction. Although convenient, this chunk-centric formulation creates a fundamental granularity mismatch. Language describes semantic goals and events, vision evolves through continuous scene dynamics, and actions operate at control-level timescales; forcing all three into the same fixed-length prediction window turns VLA training into short-horizon correlation fitting. WALL-WM addresses this mismatch by organizing both supervision and data around semantic events. Specifically, it pairs event-grounded VLA pretraining with a data ecosystem built from event-level captions and cluster-balanced sampling, enabling scalable learning over diverse behaviors, scenes, and task structures. From the same event-pretrained backbone, WALL-WM supports two complementary inference modes. The event mode consumes next-event descriptions and enables variable-length execution chunks, while the unified mode uses a VLM with Staircase Decoding to condition conventional fixed-length chunk inference while preserving a gradient-continuous VLA path. Together with Muon-optimizer-based large-scale pretraining infrastructure, WALL-WM provides a practical scale-up recipe for general-purpose WAMs. Experiments show that WALL-WM generalizes broadly across language, scenes, and tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance in large-scale real-world generalization evaluation.

</details>

*Arusa Kanwal, Pablo Valle, Shaukat Ali, Aitor Arrieta*

TL;DR: 本文提出 FATE-VLA，一种面向 VLA 模型的故障感知测试生成方法，通过多样性驱动的探索与代理模型引导测试向高风险区域集中，在四个 SOTA VLA 模型上发现更多且更多样化的故障模式，揭示了静态基准测试的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型作为通用机器人策略日益普及，但其评估仍依赖静态基准随机采样任务场景。在高维具身空间中，故障是稀疏且聚类的，静态基准无法充分暴露模型的鲁棒性风险。

Method: 将 VLA 评估重新定义为主动故障发现问题，提出结合多样性驱动探索与从观测执行中学习的代理模型的测试生成方法，引导测试向高风险且多样化的场景区域进行。

Result: 在四个 SOTA VLA 模型上，相比基线方法最多多发现 29.7% 的故障，且故障模式更多样化。例如 GR00T-N1.6 的成功率从 64.4% 降至 34.7%。

Conclusion: VLA 评估应从被动测量转向自适应的故障寻找测试生成，以在部署前暴露模型弱点的结构。

Classification reason: 该论文关注 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型作为通用机器人策略的评估与测试，属于具身智能/机器人领域。虽然论文侧重测试生成而非模型本身，但其核心对象是 VLA 机器人策略，与"具身世界模型与规划控制"方向紧密相关，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA testing methodology, not on core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: VLA evaluation, failure-aware testing, embodied robotics, diversity-driven exploration, surrogate model

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly used as generalist robot policies, yet their evaluation still relies largely on static benchmarks that randomly sample task scenes. In high-dimensional embodied spaces, failures are sparse and clustered, so static benchmarking can underestimate robustness risks. We reframe VLA evaluation as an active failure-discovery problem and propose a failure-aware test-generation approach that combines diversity-driven exploration with surrogate models learned from observed executions. The method steers testing toward high-risk yet diverse scene regions. Across four state-of-the-art VLA models, it uncovers substantially more failures (up to +29.7 % over selected baselines) while revealing more diverse failure modes. This mean that, for instance, in the case of GR00T-N1.6, success rate dropped from 64.4% to 34.7%. More broadly, our findings call for a shift in VLA evaluation: from passive measurement on fixed task suites to adaptive, failure-seeking test generation that exposes the structure of model weaknesses before deployment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型假设场景在观测与执行之间是静止的，当物体运动时，从观测到动作执行的延迟导致抓取失败。作者希望在不重新训练大模型的前提下，赋予 VLA 模型对动态场景的预测能力，弥合感知-执行延迟带来的性能差距。

Method: AHEAD 包含三个核心组件：(1) 运动感知潜空间世界模型，在 VLA 编码器的 patch token 特征空间中，基于光流估计的逐 token 速度与加速度进行未来预测；(2) 语言-运动显著性掩码，结合语言指令注意力与运动显著性，聚焦任务相关区域；(3) 自适应预测视界，滚动预测直到不确定性超过阈值。整个包装器仅 4.9M 参数，冻结 7B OpenVLA 的编码器与动作解码器。

Result: 在 20 个动态仿真场景中，AHEAD 达到 79-97% 成功率（最强基线 31-58%）。在物理 UFactory xArm 7 机器人上，传送带与滚球任务 29-30/30，球拍拦截 23/30，抛射物接取 19/30（所有基线 0/30）。

Conclusion: AHEAD 通过轻量级潜空间世界模型有效弥合了 VLA 模型在动态场景中的感知-执行延迟差距，以极小参数量代价大幅提升动态操控成功率，验证了"先预测后执行"范式在具身智能中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AHEAD，一个为 VLA 模型设计的预测-执行包装器，通过一个轻量级潜空间世界模型预测未来 patch token，用于动态场景中的机器人操控。这直接属于"具身世界模型与规划控制"（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题，因为其核心是面向机器人操控的潜空间世界模型，用于动作规划与动态环境适应。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, with a novel latent-space predictive approach for dynamic VLA manipulation. Signals: world model, efficient inference (4.9M params), latent-space prediction, motion-aware, adaptive horizon

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generalize across static manipulation but fail when objects move during task execution. They map the current observation to an action and assume the scene is stationary between observation and execution, so at any non-trivial object speed the resulting latency exceeds the time available to grasp. We close this gap with AHEAD (Anticipatory Horizon Extrapolation with Adaptive Dynamics), a predict-then-act wrapper that augments a frozen VLA with a motion-aware latent world model. A small world model trained on manipulation video forecasts future patch tokens in the VLA's feature space, conditioned on per-token velocity and acceleration from optical flow. A language-and-motion saliency mask concentrates prediction on task-relevant patches, and the model rolls forward for an adaptive horizon, halting when prediction uncertainty crosses a threshold. The frozen action decoder then receives the predicted future tokens in place of the current ones. AHEAD adds 4.9M parameters to a frozen 7B OpenVLA and reaches 79 to 97% success across 20 dynamic simulation scenarios where the strongest baseline reaches 31 to 58%. On a physical UFactory xArm 7, AHEAD succeeds on 29/30 to 30/30 on three conveyor and rolling-ball tasks, 23/30 on paddle interception, and 19/30 on projectile catching where every baseline scores 0/30.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可供性理解方法要么只定位任务相关区域而不指定可执行动作，要么预测动作但可扩展性有限，缺乏一个既能理解交互位置和方式、又能跨环境/物体/任务泛化的统一基础模型。

Method: 提出AFUN模型，输入RGB-D图像和语言任务描述，输出任务条件功能掩码（where）和3D接触后运动曲线（how）。构建大规模标准化数据管道，将异构数据（机器人、人类演示、仿真、真实扫描）统一为包含语言描述、掩码和物体中心3D运动标签的共享可供性标注格式。

Result: 在4个基准的8个测试集上，可供性分割mIoU/cIoU提升+23.9/+26.3；接触点预测命中率提升12.7-61.3%；3D运动预测在所有三个测试集上最优；可直接部署于真实机器人操作无需微调或任务特定启发式。

Conclusion: AFUN向可供性基础模型迈出了重要一步，通过统一数据管道和联合预测交互位置与运动方式，实现了跨环境、物体和任务的泛化能力，可直接用于真实机器人操作。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个可供性基础模型（affordance foundation model），从RGB-D观测和语言任务描述预测功能掩码和3D运动曲线，直接服务于机器人操作与规划，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。

Abstract: Affordance understanding bridges visual perception and physical action, serving as an explainable interface for robot manipulation in open and unstructured real-world environments. Yet, building an affordance foundation model that not only understands where and how the interaction should happen, but also generalizes across diverse environments, objects, and tasks, remains a long-standing research challenge. Existing methods typically address only part of this challenge, either localizing task-relevant regions without specifying executable motion, or predicting motion but with limited scalability. In this paper, we present ourmodel, a step towards an affordance foundation model for functionality understanding. From a single RGB-D observation and a language task description, ourmodel predicts a task-conditional functional mask (where to interact) and a 3D post-contact motion curve (how to interact). To support open-world generalization, we build a large-scale standardized data pipeline that converts heterogeneous robot, human, simulation, and real-world scan data into a shared affordance schema with language, masks, and object-centric 3D motion labels. We evaluate ourmodel from three aspects: for affordance segmentation, ourmodel outperforms all baselines by a large margin across 8 test sets from 4 benchmarks, improving mean gIoU/cIoU by +23.9/+26.3; for contact-point prediction, it predicts substantially more accurate points, with a 12.7--61.3% hit-rate gain over the best baseline; and for 3D motion, it achieves the best performance on all three test sets. ourmodel can be deployed for real-world robot manipulation without finetuning for robot embodiment or using task-specific heuristics, demonstrating the ability to adapt to open-world affordance tasks. Project page:

*Junjie Ye, Rong Xue, Basile Van Hoorick, Runhao Li, Harshitha Rajaprakash, Pavel Tokmakov, Muhammad Zubair Irshad, Vitor Guizilini, Yue Wang*

TL;DR: RoboDream 是一个具身中心的世界模型，通过将机器人运动轨迹渲染与环境场景合成解耦，实现大规模、多样化的机器人演示数据生成，支持轨迹复用（retrieval & rebirth）和无道具遥操作（prop-free teleoperation），实验证明生成数据能有效提升下游策略性能并减少真实数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人遥操作数据采集成本高昂且耗时，限制了机器人学习的规模化。现有视频扩散模型方法多局限于表面视觉增强，或存在具身幻觉导致物理不可行的运动。因此需要一种可泛化的数据合成方法，能够在新场景、新物体、新视角下生成逼真的机器人演示数据。

Method: 提出 RoboDream，一种具身中心的世界模型。核心思想是将轨迹执行与环境合成解耦：首先渲染机器人运动轨迹（基于显式场景和物体先验），然后以渲染的运动为锚点，利用视频扩散模型合成逼真的场景和物体外观。该方法支持两种数据扩展能力：(1) retrieval & rebirth——将已有轨迹复用到全新上下文中；(2) prop-free teleoperation——操作员在空中执行动作，模型后续"幻觉"出目标物体和场景。

Result: 在多种真实机器人操作任务上，使用 RoboDream 生成的数据训练下游策略，一致提升了策略性能，并显著减少了真实数据的需求量。实验验证了生成数据的物理可行性和视觉逼真度。

Conclusion: RoboDream 通过解耦轨迹执行与环境合成，提供了一种可扩展的机器人数据合成范式，能够有效缓解真实数据稀缺问题，为大规模机器人学习提供了可行的数据扩展路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RoboDream，一个面向机器人数据合成的具身中心世界模型，通过将轨迹执行与环境合成解耦，实现新物体、新场景、新视角下的逼真演示数据生成。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，属于该方向的核心工作。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video generation priorities, with a novel decoupling approach for scalable robot data synthesis. Signals: world model, video generation, scalable data synthesis, trajectory reuse, prop-free teleoperation

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires large-scale, diverse demonstrations, yet real-world data collection via teleoperation remains prohibitively expensive and time-consuming. While video diffusion models offer a promising avenue for data scaling, existing generative approaches are often limited to superficial visual augmentation, or suffer from embodiment hallucinations that yield physically infeasible motions. We present a generalizable embodiment-centric world model that achieves scalable data generation by synthesizing photorealistic demonstrations with novel objects, in novel scenes, and from novel viewpoints. Our approach anchors generation to rendered robot motion while conditioning on explicit scene and object priors, effectively decoupling trajectory execution from environment synthesis. This formulation has the potential to unlock two powerful data scaling capabilities: (1) retrieval and rebirth, which repurposes existing trajectories into entirely new contexts without new motion data; and (2) prop-free teleoperation, where operators manipulate empty air and the model hallucinates the target objects and scene afterwards, eliminating reset time. We demonstrate with real-world experiments that our generated data consistently improves downstream policy performance and significantly reduces real-world data requirements across diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>

*Ruoxuan Zhang, Qiaoqiao Wan, Zhengguang Wang, Chenghao Yu, Hongxia Xie, Jianlong Fu, Wen-Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindClaw 是一个面向具身智能体的闭环心理状态推理与精准干预框架，通过多源输入、信念记忆、认知触发技能、心理推理与动作生成模块，使智能体在实时环境中判断何时需要推理并仅在必要时提供帮助，优于直接使用 VLM 的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ToM（心理理论）基准主要评估离线问答或最终动作预测，未能测试具身智能体在动态环境中实时更新信念、判断何时需要推理、以及仅在有用时进行干预的能力。因此需要一种闭环的具身心理状态推理框架。

Method: MindClaw 框架包含五个模块：多源输入（视觉、语言、状态）、信念记忆（维护对他人信念的持续更新）、具身认知触发技能（判断何时需要推理）、心理推理（基于 ToM 推断他人意图与目标）、动作生成（输出帮助行为或保持沉默）。采用闭环设计，智能体在环境中持续感知并决定是否干预。

Result: 实验表明，直接使用 VLM 的基线方法在任务感知和干预校准方面表现不佳，而 MindClaw 在整体性能上达到最优，验证了触发技能优化对于闭环具身 ToM 辅助的重要性。

Conclusion: MindClaw 证明了在具身环境中引入闭环心理状态推理与精准干预的有效性，触发技能的设计是关键组件，为未来具身智能体的社会性交互与辅助提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MindClaw 框架，面向具身智能体的心理理论（ToM）推理与精准干预，涉及具身环境中的信念记忆、认知触发、动作生成与闭环控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong on embodied world model reasoning but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: embodied mental-state reasoning, closed-loop intervention, theory of mind, belief memory, cognitive trigger skill

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) enables an agent to reason about another actor's beliefs, goals, and intentions, which is essential for human-centered embodied assistance. Existing ToM benchmarks have advanced text and multimodal mental-state recognition, but they mostly evaluate offline question answering or final action prediction. They do not fully test whether an embodied agent can stay connected to a changing environment, update actor-specific beliefs, decide when reasoning is needed, and intervene only when help is useful. Building on MindPower, we extend robot-centric ToM reasoning to a real-time closed-loop setting and introduce MindClaw, a framework for embodied mental-state reasoning with precision intervention. MindClaw connects multi-source inputs, belief memory, an embodied cognitive trigger skill, mental reasoning, and action generation, allowing the agent to output helpful actions at the right time while remaining silent when intervention is unnecessary. Experiments show that direct VLM baselines struggle with task awareness and intervention calibration, while MindClaw achieves the best overall performance, demonstrating the importance of trigger-skill optimization for closed-loop embodied ToM assistance.

</details>

*Md Mainul Abrar, Xun Jia, Yujie Chi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的放疗计划制定缺乏物理直觉且收敛困难，而纯DRL方法在复杂临床场景中泛化性不足。本文旨在结合DRL的优化能力与LLM的推理能力，构建一个可泛化的自主放疗计划框架。

Method: 提出M2M框架：1) DRL智能体在放疗计划参数空间中学习最优参数分布知识；2) 通过上下文学习（in-context learning）将DRL学到的参数分布知识传递给LLM智能体；3) LLM智能体在DRL引导的物理有效参数空间内进行自主迭代规划，逐步优化治疗计划参数。

Result: 在前列腺癌（基础与复杂配置）和肝癌三种场景下，引导的LLM智能体始终达到最优规划分数，同时显著减少迭代次数。最终参数配置展示了智能体学习到目标的分层优先级，恢复了参数调优与剂量学结果之间的因果逻辑关系。

Conclusion: 该M2M框架通过桥接DRL的专门优化与LLM的适应性推理，建立了可泛化的自主放疗计划制定基础，在不同患者解剖结构、治疗部位和初始计划质量下均保持高质量规划，有望在临床实践中落地。

Classification reason: 该论文使用DRL智能体提取放疗计划参数分布知识，通过LLM智能体进行自主迭代规划，属于具身/机器人领域的规划控制任务，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on medical radiotherapy planning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords like clinical. Signals: medical domain, negative keyword clinical, no continuous language model, no video generation, no world model for video

Abstract: In this work, we propose a prototype machine-to-machine (M2M) knowledge-guided Large Language Model (LLM) framework for automated radiotherapy treatment planning. In the proposed paradigm, Treatment Planning Parameter (TPP) distribution knowledge discovered by a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agent is transferred to an LLM agent through in-context learning, enabling autonomous iterative planning without human intervention. While standard LLM-based planning often lacks physical intuition and struggles with convergence, the integration of DRL-derived guidance constrains the agent to a physically valid parameter space. Experimental evaluations are performed across three diverse planning scenarios: basic prostate cases, complex prostate configurations with increased organ-at-risk (OAR) constraints, and liver cases. The evaluation results demonstrate that the guided LLM agent consistently achieves optimal planning scores while significantly reducing the number of iterations compared to unguided planning. Analysis of the final TPP configurations reveals that the agent successfully learns a hierarchical priority of objectives, effectively restoring a logical "cause-and-effect" relationship between parameter tuning and dosimetric outcomes. Crucially, this prototype framework exhibits robust generalizability, maintaining high planning quality regardless of specific patient anatomy, treatment site, or initial plan quality. By bridging the specialized optimization of DRL with the adaptive reasoning of LLMs, this M2M framework establishes a scalable foundation towards generalizable autonomous treatment planning, ultimately benefiting clinical practice in realistic environments.

</details>


### [47] [Goal2Pixel: Grounding Goals to Pixels for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01621)
*Muyi Bao, Yuxin Cai, Hang Xu, Zongtai Li, Jinxi He, Jingfan Tang, Chen Lv, Ji Zhang, Yaqi Xie, Wenshan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (67.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Goal2Pixel 提出一种纯像素范式，将 VLN-CE 重新定义为可导航像素定位问题，通过图像平面作为统一空间接口，预测可见可导航像素并反投影为 3D 航点，同时引入可见性感知的关键帧记忆和坐标感知辅助损失，在 R2R-CE 上以更少的 VLM 推理调用次数达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM 的 VLN-CE 方法将导航建模为低层动作预测，存在接口模糊、受限于短视运动基元、需要频繁查询 VLM 导致效率低下等问题。作者希望提出一种更高效、更自然的导航范式。

Method: Goal2Pixel 将导航任务转化为像素定位问题：模型预测图像中一个可见的可导航像素，将其反投影为 3D 航点用于前向导航；对于非前向动作（左转、右转、停止），在图像平面边缘附加指令区域进行解释。同时提出可见性感知的关键帧记忆用于长程导航历史表示，并引入语义嵌入和坐标感知辅助损失来适配预训练 VLM。

Result: 在 R2R-CE Val-Unseen 上达到 54.1% SR 和 52.5% SPL，每个 episode 仅需 7.75 次 VLM 推理调用，比直接动作预测方法（46.62 次调用，32.9% SR）减少约 6 倍调用次数，同时性能更优。

Conclusion: Goal2Pixel 证明了纯像素级导航范式在 VLN-CE 中的有效性，通过将 VLM 推理与机器人运动解耦为统一的图像空间接口，显著提升了效率与性能，为视觉语言导航提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言导航（VLN-CE），将导航任务转化为可导航像素定位，属于具身智能中的规划与控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel pixel-based paradigm for VLN-CE reduces VLM calls while achieving SOTA, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, efficient inference, vision-language navigation, pixel grounding, keyframe memory

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE). Yet most VLM-based methods cast navigation as low-level action prediction, an interface that is ambiguous, tied to short-horizon motion primitives, and inefficient due to repeated VLM querying. We propose Goal2Pixel, a pure pixel-based paradigm that reformulates VLN-CE as navigable pixel grounding. Rather than predicting actions, Goal2Pixel uses the image plane as a unified spatial interface between VLM reasoning and robot motion: the model predicts a visible navigable pixel to the agent, which is back-projected into a 3D waypoint for forward navigation. For non-forward actions, we append auxiliary directive regions to the image plane, where the left/right/bottom regions are interpreted as turning left, turning right, and stopping, respectively. To enable long-horizon navigation, we propose a visibility-aware keyframe memory for compact and informative history representation. To adapt pretrained VLMs to navigable pixel grounding, we introduce semantic embeddings and coordinate-aware auxiliary losses. Goal2Pixel achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance while requiring fewer VLM inference calls than prior methods. On R2R-CE Val-Unseen it achieves 54.1% SR and 52.5% SPL with just 7.75 VLM calls per episode, 6x fewer than the 46.62 required by direct action prediction at 32.9% SR. The same trend holds on Page: .

Conclusion: HomeFlow 证明了通过可验证的模拟环境、程序化场景生成和树搜索轨迹合成，可以有效构建高质量的训练数据飞轮，显著提升智能家居智能体的任务执行能力。该方法为具身智能体在复杂物理环境中的训练提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond text-only interaction toward physical-world control, with smart homes as a representative domain. Real domestic interaction requires understanding ambiguous intents, operating in dynamic environments, and performing multi-turn reasoning. However, existing methods struggle to generate high-quality training data for smart home agents. We propose HomeFlow, a verifiable data flywheel for this domain. HomeFlow uses HomeEnv as a unified simulation environment and HomeMaker to procedurally generate diverse home settings. Subsequently, Blueprint compiles open-ended user intents into executable state-based success conditions, while MCTS-Flow synthesizes diverse, verifiable multi-turn trajectories through environment-guided tree search. We then optimize the agents via supervised fine-tuning and step-wise RLVE, which facilitates iterative improvement through authentic physical feedback. We further construct SmartHome-Bench to evaluate the agent across various smart home tasks. On this benchmark, HomeFlow-RL-4B and HomeFlow-RL-8B achieve task success rates of 84.60% and 87.03%. It is worth noting that HomeFlow-RL-8B even surpasses the leading GPT-5.5 by 1.23 percentage points.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 信息论任务表征学习：提取行为策略不变的任务定义潜变量，缓解上下文分布偏移；2) Transformer随机世界模型：对时序任务信息进行建模；3) 保守值惩罚：对基于世界模型的想象轨迹施加保守值惩罚，防止策略利用模型不准确性。

Result: 在多个离线元强化学习基准上，该方法在分布外和稀疏奖励设置下均优于现有最先进方法，展现出更好的稳定性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 行为策略不变的任务表征学习结合Transformer世界模型与保守值惩罚，能有效解决离线元强化学习中的分布偏移问题，实现鲁棒的跨环境泛化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于Transformer随机世界模型的离线元强化学习框架，面向动作规划与策略泛化，属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型方法，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on offline meta-RL with world models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model, offline meta-RL, Transformer, task representation, distribution shift

Abstract: Offline meta-reinforcement learning leverages static datasets to enable agents to generalize to unseen environments by combining offline efficiency with meta-learning adaptability, yet it faces key challenges from context and policy distribution shifts. These issues hinder agents from adapting to online environments, and are further exacerbated under sparse-reward settings. As a result, agents often become trapped in an inherent pattern dilemma, failing to achieve robust generalization. In this work, we propose a novel framework that integrates information-theoretic task representation learning with a Transformer-based stochastic world model. Our approach extracts task-defining latent variables that are invariant to behavior policy, thereby effectively mitigating the context distribution shift. To further handle policy shift and model exploitation, we apply a conservative value penalty to imagination-based rollouts, preventing the policy from exploiting model inaccuracies while maintaining robust adaptation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, with superior stability and generalization under out-of-distribution and sparse-reward settings.

</details>

*Shayan Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: TERRA 提出一种任务嵌入的推理与表征架构，形式化研究动作条件潜世界模型在跨领域（如驾驶场景、机器人工作空间、金融订单簿）结构化状态之间的迁移问题，定义了基于 lax bisimulation discrepancy 和 Gromov-Wasserstein 距离的跨域对应关系，并推导了 Lipschitz 预测器下的迁移误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然动作条件潜世界模型在单个领域（如驾驶、机器人、金融）中已有验证，但跨领域迁移问题尚未被形式化研究。作者希望回答：一个在某个结构化状态域中学到的表征或预测器，在结构类似但领域无关的另一个域中能迁移多少？

Method: 将每个域建模为分级潜网格上的受控马尔可夫过程，将实例分解为薄域适配器和共享的域不变核心；通过近似马尔可夫决策过程同态定义跨域对应关系，用 lax bisimulation discrepancy 和 Gromov-Wasserstein 距离度量迁移质量；在 Lipschitz 预测器假设下推导迁移误差界，并通过 bisimulation 度量的 Lipschitz 值性质将潜误差与决策遗憾联系起来。

Result: 本文为纯理论研究提案，未呈现实证结果。主要理论贡献包括：形式化跨域迁移问题、提出结构化状态迁移假设（Structured-State Transfer Hypothesis）、推导了随预测步长几何增长的迁移误差界、建立了潜误差与决策遗憾之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: TERRA 将广泛存在的直觉（跨域迁移）转化为可检验的理论框架，为动作条件潜世界模型的跨域迁移提供了形式化基础。论文以预注册实验方案结束，提出了可证伪的假设，为未来实证验证铺平道路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种动作条件潜空间预测架构（action-conditioned latent predictive architecture），研究跨领域结构化状态转移问题，核心涉及动作条件潜世界模型、离散动作分词化、联合嵌入预测等，属于世界模型方向。虽然论文不限于具身智能，但其核心是动作条件潜世界模型与跨域转移理论，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]中"动作规划和控制的世界模型"主题高度吻合，故复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes theoretical transfer bounds for latent world models but lacks empirical results and is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: Theoretical framework, cross-domain transfer, no empirical results, world model theory, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: A single action-conditioned latent predictive architecture can in principle be trained on the structured state of a driving scene, a robot workspace, or a financial order book. The ingredients for doing so within any one domain already exist and are individually validated: masked-latent prediction, action-conditioned latent world models, discrete action tokenization, and joint-embedding prediction on voxelized state. What is not established, and what TERRA addresses, is the transfer question: when does a representation or predictor learned in one structured-state domain carry over to a structurally analogous but otherwise unrelated domain, and by how much. We give this question a formal treatment. We model each domain as a controlled Markov process on a graded latent grid, factor any instantiation into thin domain adapters and a shared domain-invariant core, and identify a cross-domain correspondence with an approximate Markov decision process homomorphism whose quality is measured by a lax bisimulation discrepancy and, for domains lacking a shared coordinate system, by a Gromov-Wasserstein distance between their action-conditioned transition operators. Under a Lipschitz predictor we derive a transfer bound that separates source-model error from structural mismatch, grows geometrically in the prediction horizon, and is certified from below by the Gromov-Wasserstein distance; we then connect latent error to decision regret through the Lipschitz value property of bisimulation metrics. The resulting Structured-State Transfer Hypothesis is stated as a falsifiable claim with a preregistered experimental program, centered on a transfer test from driving scenes to order books, including conditions under which it is refuted. We present no empirical results: this is a research proposal that converts a widely repeated intuition into testable theory.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal-Plan-Bench基准和Causal-Plan-1M数据集，揭示当前具身视觉语言规划模型本质上是基于语言先验的token预测而非物理因果推理。基于Qwen3-VL-8B训练的Causal Planner通过因果训练数据实现了从33.22到45.28的显著提升，并发现了因果缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身视觉语言规划基准偏向语言next-token预测而非物理的next-state推理，模型依赖统计语言先验而非因果依赖关系，导致物理规划退化为浅层序列建模。需要从语言驱动的token预测转向物理驱动的因果推理。

Method: 构建Causal-Plan-Bench高保真诊断基准（覆盖四个因果维度），以及Causal-Plan-1M百万级因果推理轨迹数据集（通过四阶段标注流水线从第一人称视频生成）。基于Qwen3-VL-8B训练Causal Planner模型，采用因果训练配方。

Result: Gemini 3 Pro在基准上仅达38.18分，表明领先模型仍缺乏物理智能。Causal Planner实现强域内性能和跨基准泛化，发现因果缩放定律：因果训练数据扩展至百万级带来36.3%的相对提升（33.22→45.28）。

Conclusion: 本文工作为将智能体从浅层token预测器转变为物理因果推理者提供了具体步骤，证明了因果训练数据规模与物理规划能力之间存在缩放定律。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦具身智能中的物理规划与因果推理，属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题。同时，论文涉及视觉语言模型在具身规划中的评测，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly challenges token prediction in embodied planning, aligning with world model and causal reasoning priorities. Signals: world model, causal reasoning, embodied planning, benchmark, scaling law

Abstract: Current benchmarks for embodied vision-language planning often favor linguistic next-token prediction over physically grounded next-state reasoning. This rewards models that mimic statistical language priors rather than track causal dependencies, reducing physical planning to shallow sequence modeling. We argue that reliable physical autonomy requires a shift from linguistically grounded token prediction toward physically grounded causal reasoning. To this end, we introduce Causal-Plan-Bench, a high-fidelity diagnostic suite curated through multi-stage verification to evaluate embodied planning across four causal dimensions. We also construct Causal-Plan-1M, a million-scale corpus of explicit reasoning traces produced by a four-stage annotation pipeline over egocentric videos. Extensive evaluation shows that leading models still struggle to demonstrate genuine physical agency, with Gemini 3 Pro reaching only 38.18 on our benchmark. In contrast, our training recipe enables Causal Planner, built on Qwen3-VL-8B, to internalize physical logic for more accurate next-state estimation. The model achieves strong in-domain performance and cross-benchmark generalization, and reveals a Causal Scaling Law: scaling causal training data to one million instances yields a 36.3% relative gain, from 33.22 to 45.28. Overall, our work provides a concrete step toward turning agents from superficial token predictors into physically grounded causal reasoners.

</details>


### [52] [SafeMCP: Proactive Power Regulation for LLM Agent Defense via Environment-Grounded Look-Ahead Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01991)
*Lichao Wang, Zhaoxing Ren, Tianzhuo Yang, Jiaming Ji, Chi Harold Liu, Yaodong Yang, Juntao Dai*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (80.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeMCP 是一个服务端防御插件，通过内部世界模型进行前瞻推理，对 LLM Agent 的工具获取实施两级防御（主动工具过滤与即时干预），在保障任务效用的同时有效缓解 Agent 的权力寻求风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 通过 MCP 协议在复杂环境中操作时，其动作空间扩展带来了不安全的能力和权力寻求风险。微小的错误或幻觉可能被放大为灾难性失败，现有防御方法难以在安全与效用之间取得平衡。

Method: SafeMCP 采用内部世界模型进行前瞻推理，实现两级防御：1）主动工具过滤，约束危险权力扩展；2）即时干预作为故障安全。训练采用三阶段流程：环境动态接地、安全策略初始化、基于双重可验证奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在 PowerSeeking Bench、ToolEmu 和 AgentHarm 基准上，SafeMCP 实现了安全均衡，有效缓解风险的同时保持了 Agent 的效用。

Conclusion: SafeMCP 通过环境接地的前瞻推理，为 LLM Agent 提供了一种有效的服务端安全防御方案，在安全与效用之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SafeMCP，利用内部世界模型进行前瞻推理（look-ahead reasoning）来约束 LLM Agent 的工具获取与动作空间，属于世界模型驱动的安全规划与控制。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关，因为该 subtopic 涵盖面向 agent 的规划、控制与安全策略。

Importance reason: Strong world model defense for LLM agents, but tangential to your core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: world model for safety, proactive tool filtering, RL with dual rewards, LLM agent defense, power-seeking risk mitigation

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly leverage the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to operate in complex environments, the expansion of their action spaces offers agents unsafe capabilities and underscores the risk of power-seeking. While broad action space and greater environment influence are essential for task fulfillment, they create a fragile risk surface where minor errors or hallucinations are magnified into catastrophic failures. In response, we propose SafeMCP, a {server-side} defense plugin that constrains tool acquisition via predictive reasoning regarding future safety risks. SafeMCP utilizes an internal world model for look-ahead reasoning to implement a two-tier defense: proactive tool filtering to constrain hazardous power expansion and immediate intervention as a fail-safe. To train SafeMCP, we introduce a three-stage pipeline comprising environmental dynamic grounding, safe policy initialization, and reinforcement learning (RL) with dual verifiable rewards. Experiments on PowerSeeking Bench, ToolEmu, and AgentHarm show that SafeMCP achieves a safe equilibrium, effectively mitigating risks while preserving agent utility.

</details>


### [53] [Where to Look: Can Foundation Models Reach a Target Viewpoint Through Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01247)
*Liyang Li, Muzhi Zhu, Zhiyue Zhao, Hengyu Zhao, Ke Liu, Linhao Zhong, Hao Chen, Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出目标视角复现（TVR）任务——智能体在3D环境中通过主动探索调整视角以匹配目标图像，并构建TVRBench室内仿真基准。实验表明最强开源/闭源模型成功率仅7.8%/12.0%，瓶颈在于多轮视觉历史利用和身体平移动作。通过统一后训练框架（SFT+GRPO），9B模型可达51.4%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型的空间智能主要研究被动理解预采集的观测数据，而人类可以通过主动的头部和身体运动复现目标视角。本文旨在弥合这一差距，研究基础模型能否通过主动探索达到目标视角。

Method: 1) 定义TVR任务：智能体在3D环境中通过连续动作调整视角，使其观察与目标图像匹配。2) 构建TVRBench室内仿真基准。3) 提出统一后训练框架，包含专家轨迹SFT、理由监督CoT-SFT、离线单轮GRPO和在线多轮GRPO（基于实时模拟器rollout）。

Result: 最强开源模型（GPT-4o）成功率7.8%，闭源模型（Gemini 2.0 Flash）成功率12.0%。通过视觉-动作SFT，9B模型提升至50.8%；多轮GRPO进一步优化多房间场景，达到51.4%成功率。CoT监督和单轮GRPO在闭环场景中表现退化。

Conclusion: TVRBench可作为衡量和训练基础模型在3D环境中主动感知与行动的测试平台。视觉-动作SFT是主要增益来源，多轮GRPO提供针对性优化，而CoT和单轮GRPO不适合闭环控制场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究智能体在3D环境中通过主动探索（动作控制）调整视角以匹配目标图像，属于具身智能中的主动感知与规划控制问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解（目标图像与当前观察的匹配），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and embodied spatial reasoning, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model, embodied exploration, spatial intelligence, active perception, TVRBench benchmark

Abstract: Humans can reproduce the viewpoint specified by a target image through active head and body motion, yet spatial intelligence in foundation models has largely been studied as passive understanding of pre-collected observations. We introduce Target Viewpoint Reproduction (TVR) -- an active task where an agent adjusts its viewpoint in a 3D environment until its observation matches a given target image -- and TVRBench, an indoor-simulation benchmark spanning scene scale and target-view visual richness. TVR is far from solved: on the evaluation split, the strongest open-source and closed-source models reach only 7.8% and 12.0% success. Fine-grained analysis identifies two consistent bottlenecks: off-the-shelf models struggle with multi-turn visual history, and performance drops sharply when viewpoint reproduction requires body translation rather than in-place rotation, exposing a gap in mapping spatial discrepancies to embodied movement. To study reducing this gap, we build a unified TVR post-training framework covering expert-trajectory SFT, rationale-supervised CoT-SFT, offline Single-turn GRPO, and on-policy Multi-turn GRPO from live simulator rollouts. Visual-action SFT supplies the main gain, raising a 9B open-source model to 50.8% success; Multi-turn GRPO provides targeted multi-room refinement and reaches 51.4% overall, while CoT supervision and Single-turn GRPO degrade closed-loop performance. These results establish TVRBench as a testbed for measuring and training foundation models that actively perceive and act in 3D environments. Our code, data, and models are available at .

</details>

*Youwei Liu, Jian Wang, Hanlin Wang, Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: COMAP 提出了一种文本世界模型与 LLM 智能体策略共同演化的框架，通过闭环交互使世界模型适应智能体不断变化的策略分布，并在具身任务规划、Web 导航和工具使用等基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本世界模型通常在训练后固定不变，无法适应智能体策略演化带来的状态-动作分布偏移；同时，智能体改进方法往往依赖外部奖励或验证器，限制了其在真实交互环境中的适用性。

Method: COMAP 框架包含两个核心组件：1）文本世界模型在每个决策步骤预测候选动作的未来状态反馈；2）智能体通过估计反馈的可靠性进行未来感知反思，并据此优化动作。产生的 on-policy 轨迹通过自蒸馏更新世界模型，使其更好地匹配智能体演化的交互分布，形成闭环共同演化。

Result: 在具身任务规划、Web 导航和工具使用基准上，COMAP 一致优于竞争基线，例如使用 Qwen3-4B 时相对提升 16.75%。分析表明共同演化循环随时间提高了世界模型的预测准确性，并实现了更有效的长程决策。

Conclusion: COMAP 证明了文本世界模型与智能体策略的共同演化能够有效提升世界模型的预测准确性和智能体的长程决策能力，为无需外部奖励的智能体学习提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 COMAP 框架，通过闭环交互共同演化文本世界模型与智能体策略，用于具身任务规划、Web 导航和工具使用等场景，属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制子主题，与 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes co-evolving world models with agent policies, directly relevant to world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, LLM agent, co-evolution, self-distillation, embodied planning

Abstract: Equipping language agents with world models enables them to anticipate environment dynamics and evaluate candidate actions before execution. However, existing textual world models are typically fixed after training, preventing them from adapting to the on-policy state-action distributions induced by an evolving agent. Meanwhile, agent-improvement methods often rely on external rewards or verifiers, limiting their applicability in realistic interactive environments. In this paper, we propose COMAP, a novel framework that co-evolves textual world models and agent policies through closed-loop interaction. At each decision step, the world model predicts future state feedback for candidate actions, and the agent performs future-aware reflection by estimating the reliability of this feedback and refining its action accordingly. The resulting on-policy trajectories are then used to update the world model via self-distillation, allowing it to better match the agent's evolving interaction distribution. Across embodied task planning, Web navigation, and tool-use benchmarks, COMAP consistently outperforms competitive baselines, e.g., +16.75% relative improvement with Qwen3-4B. Further analyses show that the co-evolutionary loop improves the world model's prediction accuracy over time and leads to more effective long-horizon decision-making. Our code is available at: .

</details>

*Baoqi Gao, Ruize Han, Miao Wang, Song Wang*

TL;DR: 本文提出 IMWM，将直觉模型与世界模型结合用于潜空间规划。直觉模型从演示中学习识别有前景的动作，通过检索初始化、混合代价和可靠性门控三个轻量组件与世界模型协作，在四个像素级目标到达任务上显著优于纯世界模型规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使拥有完美的世界模型（用真实环境动力学替代学习的前向预测器），有限预算的基于采样的规划器仍然会在某些任务上失败，说明瓶颈在于搜索而非世界模型精度。因此需要额外的直觉模型来引导搜索。

Method: IMWM 包含一个世界模型和一个直觉模型。直觉模型从专家演示中训练，用于识别有前景的动作。两者通过三个组件协作：(i) 检索初始化——从检索到的演示初始化规划器的动作提议；(ii) 混合代价——将直觉得分与世界模型 rollout 代价结合；(iii) 可靠性门控——根据场景调整规划器对直觉的信任程度。

Result: 在 Two-Room、Reacher、Push-T 和 OGBench-Cube 四个像素级目标到达任务上，IMWM 在所有任务上均优于纯世界模型规划器，最大提升在 Two-Room（99.2%，+11.5个百分点）和 OGBench-Cube（94.7%，+28.5个百分点）。

Conclusion: 直觉模型可以有效弥补世界模型在有限预算规划中的搜索瓶颈，两者互补协作能显著提升潜空间规划的成功率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将直觉模型（Intuition Model）与世界模型（World Model）结合用于潜空间规划（latent planning），核心是面向像素级控制任务的规划与决策，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Combines world models with intuition models for latent planning, but focuses on embodied control rather than the user's priority subtopics. Signals: world model, latent planning, intuition model, demonstration retrieval, pixel-based control

Abstract: Planning with a learned latent world model is a promising route to control from raw pixels, but a strong world model alone is not enough. We show this experimentally: even with a perfect world model (operationalized by replacing the learned forward predictor with an idealized rollout of the true environment dynamics), a finite-budget sample-based planner still fails on some tasks, indicating that the bottleneck can lie in search rather than in world-model accuracy. Motivated by this gap, we propose IMWM (Intuition Model + World Model), which pairs the world model with an intuition model trained from demonstrations to recognize promising actions. The two models collaborate through three lightweight components: (i) Retrieval Initialization, which initializes the planner's action proposal from a retrieved demonstration; (ii) Hybrid Cost, which combines the intuition score with the world-model rollout cost; and (iii) a Reliability Gate, which adjusts how much the planner trusts intuition in each setting. Across four pixel-based goal-reaching tasks (Two-Room, Reacher, Push-T, and OGBench-Cube), IMWM has higher mean success than the world-model-only planner on all four, with the largest gains on Two-Room (99.2%, +11.5 percentage points) and OGBench-Cube (94.7%, +28.5 percentage points).

</details>

*Kui Wu, Beiyu Guo, Hao Chen, ShuHang Xu, Yuling Li, Yongdan Zeng, Zhoujun Li, Yizhou Wang, Fangwei Zhong*

TL;DR: RescueBench 是一个面向搜救场景的具身智能体诊断基准，将任务分解为多模态探索、目标救援、记忆引导返回和最终交接四个阶段，包含五个难度级别。实验表明现有方法在最高难度下无法完成任务，自主探索是主要失败原因，空间记忆是次要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立评估具身智能体的探索、交互、记忆等能力，忽略了这些能力在实际搜救工作流中组合时失败如何累积传播的问题。作者希望构建一个能诊断各阶段失败传播的基准。

Method: 将搜救任务形式化为四阶段流水线：多模态探索（寻找线索）、目标救援（交互操作）、记忆引导返回（利用空间记忆回到起点）、最终交接。设计了五个渐进难度级别，并构建了自动化的场景生成与标注管线。评估了7个基线方法、一个oracle参考和人类玩家。

Result: 所有基线方法在最高难度下均无法完成完整任务。阶段级诊断表明自主探索是主要失败模式，空间记忆是第二个独立瓶颈。当前拓扑视觉语言导航和基于地图的方法无法解决这些限制。

Conclusion: RescueBench 揭示了现有具身智能体在组合式搜救任务中的严重不足，自主探索和空间记忆是两大关键瓶颈，为未来研究提供了明确的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RescueBench，一个面向具身智能体在搜救场景中的诊断性基准，涉及多模态探索、目标救援、空间记忆与长时序规划，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Strong diagnostic benchmark for embodied world models in realistic SAR, but only tangentially touches user's priority subtopics. Signals: world model benchmark, embodied agent diagnosis, exploration bottleneck, spatial memory failure, multi-stage pipeline

Abstract: Search-and-rescue (SAR) requires embodied agents to explore unfamiliar environments under multimodal uncertainty, perform multi-stage interactions, and retrieve spatial memory over long horizons. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear how failures compound when they must be composed in realistic workflows. We introduce RescueBench, a photo-realistic diagnostic benchmark that instantiates SAR as a four-stage pipeline: multimodal exploration, target rescue, memory-guided return, and final handoff. By combining sequential task composition with stage-level evaluation, RescueBench enables analysis of how exploration and memory failures propagate through embodied rescue workflows. It contains five progressive difficulty levels that vary in environmental complexity, clue ambiguity, and spatial hierarchy, along with an automatic episode generation and annotation pipeline for scalable evaluation and training. We evaluate seven baselines, an oracle reference, and human players, showing that no baselines complete the full task at the greatest difficulty. Stage-level diagnosis identifies autonomous exploration as the dominant failure mode and spatial memory as a second, independent bottleneck, suggesting that these limitations are not resolved by current topological visual-language navigation or map-based methods. Code is available in

### [57] [Multimodal Action Diffusion for Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02105)
*Jorge Daniel Rodríguez-Vidal, Diego Porres, Gabriel Villalonga Pineda, Antonio M. López Peña*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (62.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Action Diffusion Transformer (ADT)，一种基于扩散 Transformer 的端到端自动驾驶方法，直接预测油门、转向和刹车等多模态动作分布，而非中间轨迹点。ADT 通过生成 K 个候选动作并在推理时用最近邻匹配选择最优动作，在 Bench2Drive 基准上达到 SOTA，同时延迟降低 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统大多预测中间轨迹路点，依赖手工控制器和 GPS 进行最终控制。直接预测控制信号（油门、转向、刹车）的研究不足，且动作多模态性在其中的作用尚未被充分理解。作者认为超越确定性单动作输出不仅是建模选择，更是提升驾驶性能、表征质量和训练稳定性的关键。

Method: 提出 Action Diffusion Transformer (ADT)，一种无锚点的扩散 Transformer，使用 MSE 损失训练，原生建模合理驾驶动作的多模态分布。ADT 生成 K 个动作候选，推理时通过最近邻匹配 (NNM) 选择最优动作。模型直接输出控制信号（油门、转向、刹车），无需中间轨迹预测和手工控制器。

Result: ADT 在闭环 Bench2Drive 基准上超越此前 SOTA，同时延迟降低 10 倍。实验表明动作多模态性在学习表征和行为一致性方面带来可衡量的收益，确定性架构无法复现这些效果。

Conclusion: 表达性多模态动作建模对于鲁棒的端到端自动驾驶既是实际高效的，也是概念上必不可少的。直接控制信号预测结合扩散多模态建模是一种有前景的自动驾驶范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究端到端自动驾驶中的多模态动作扩散建模，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model application in autonomous driving but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, diffusion transformer, end-to-end autonomous driving, action multimodality, Bench2Drive SOTA

Abstract: End-to-End Autonomous Driving (E2E-AD) systems have largely converged on predicting intermediate trajectory waypoints, delegating final control to hand-crafted controllers with GPS access. Direct control-signal prediction (outputting throttle, steer and brake in an end-to-end fashion) remains underexplored, and critically, the role of action multimodality in such systems is not well understood. We argue that moving beyond deterministic, single-action outputs is not merely a modelling choice, but a key driver of driving performance, representational quality, and training stability. To validate this, we introduce the Action Diffusion Transformer (ADT), an anchor-free diffusion transformer trained with a MSE objective that natively models the multimodal distribution of plausible driving actions. Rather than committing to a single deterministic command, ADT generates K action candidates and selects the most suitable one at inference via Nearest Neighbour Matching (NNM). Beyond strong benchmark numbers, we show that action multimodality yields measurable benefits in learned representations and behavioral consistency, effects that deterministic architectures cannot replicate. ADT surpasses previous state-of-the-art on the challenging closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark while achieving ten times lower latency, demonstrating that expressive, multimodal action modelling is both practically efficient and conceptually essential for robust end-to-end driving.

</details>


### [58] [Policy and World Modeling Co-Training for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02388)
*Ning Lu, Baijiong Lin, Shengcai Liu, Jiahao Wu, Haoze Lv, Yanbin Wei, Lingting Zhu, Shengju Qian, Xin Wang, Ying-Cong Chen, Qi Wang, Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (86.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PaW（Policy and World modeling co-training）框架，在强化学习训练 LLM 智能体时，利用策略 rollout 中的 (动作, 下一观测) 对作为世界模型监督信号，通过动作熵数据选择、噪声容忍损失和奖励自适应平衡三个组件，在不改变推理范式的情况下提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能教会 LLM 智能体哪些动作带来高奖励，但对动作如何影响环境缺乏监督。世界模型可以弥补这一不足，但现有方法需要独立模拟器、额外训练阶段或推理时额外计算。作者观察到 on-policy RL rollout 中天然包含 (动作, 下一观测) 对，因此希望利用这些数据作为世界模型监督信号。

Method: 提出 PaW 框架，在 RL 训练过程中为策略添加辅助的世界模型监督。包含三个组件：(1) 基于动作熵的 WM 数据选择，筛选信息量大的 transition；(2) 噪声容忍的 WM 损失，应对 rollout 中的噪声观测；(3) 奖励自适应的损失平衡，动态调整 RL 损失与 WM 损失的权重。不改变推理范式。

Result: 在三个智能体任务基准（ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld）上的实验表明，PaW 在多种模型和 RL 算法上均一致优于强 RL 基线，验证了标准 RL rollout 可作为语言智能体训练中世界模型监督的实用来源。

Conclusion: 标准 RL rollout 中天然包含的世界模型监督信号可以被有效利用来提升语言智能体的性能，PaW 框架提供了一种简单、高效且无需额外模拟器或推理开销的协同训练方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PaW 框架，在强化学习训练语言智能体时引入世界模型辅助监督，通过策略与世界模型协同训练提升智能体对环境的理解与决策能力。这属于面向语言智能体的世界建模与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical co-training method for world models in LLM agents without extra inference cost, relevant to world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, LLM agent, reinforcement learning, co-training, efficient inference

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves large language model (LLM) agents by teaching them which actions lead to high rewards, but provides little supervision on what those actions do to the environment. World modeling (WM) can fill this gap, yet existing approaches often require separate simulators, extra training stages, or additional inference-time computation. We observe that on-policy RL rollouts already contain the needed signal: each transition pairs an action with its resulting next observation. Based on this observation, we propose PaW, a Policy and World modeling co-training framework that adds auxiliary WM supervision to the same policy during RL, without changing the inference paradigm. To make auxiliary WM supervision informative and stable, PaW introduces three components: action-entropy-based WM data selection, noise-tolerant WM loss, and reward-adaptive loss balancing. Experiments on three agentic task benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong RL baselines across models and RL algorithms. These results suggest that standard RL rollouts are a practical source of WM supervision for language-agent training.

</details>

### [59] [Coarse-to-Fine Compositional Diffusion for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00837)
*Byoungwoo Park, Utkarsh A. Mishra, Jaemoo Choi, Juho Lee, Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoFi 是一种推理时组合扩散采样方法，通过先对齐局部去噪估计形成全局粗结构骨架，再将骨架扩散到中间噪声水平并用局部先验去噪恢复细节，从而在长程机器人规划、全景图像生成和长视频生成中同时提升全局一致性和局部质量，且减少 2-8 倍去噪步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型擅长生成结构化数据，但难以直接处理超出训练规模的长程任务。现有组合生成方法通过拼接局部计划实现长程输出，但主要依赖相邻局部计划间的一致性约束，缺乏对全局结构的显式建模，导致局部兼容但全局不合理的输出。现有改进方法（如反复传播局部一致性信号或推理时优化）在局部计划数量或维度增加时计算成本过高。

Method: CoFi 分为两个阶段：(1) 粗结构形成阶段——将多个局部去噪估计对齐到共享的粗结构上，捕获长程任务级排列；(2) 细结构恢复阶段——将粗结构扩散到中间噪声水平，再用相同的预训练局部先验去噪，在保留粗结构全局一致性的同时恢复局部细节。整个过程在推理时完成，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在长程机器人规划、全景图像生成和长视频生成三个任务上，CoFi 在全局一致性和局部样本质量上均优于现有组合扩散基线，同时所需去噪评估次数减少 2-8 倍。

Conclusion: CoFi 通过粗到细的两阶段推理策略，有效解决了组合扩散中全局结构缺失的问题，在多个长程生成任务上实现了更优的性能和更高的效率，为长程规划与生成提供了一种实用的推理时方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CoFi，一种粗到细的组合扩散方法，用于长程规划（long-horizon planning），涵盖机器人规划、全景图像生成和长视频生成。核心是使用预训练的短程先验通过组合扩散生成全局一致的长时间序列输出，属于世界模型中的可交互与物理世界建模方向（interactive_physical_world），因为其聚焦于长程动作规划与生成式时序建模。同时，该方法也涉及多模态生成（全景图、长视频），因此匹配 multimodal_generation。

Importance reason: Directly advances long-horizon planning and video generation via a novel coarse-to-fine diffusion sampler, aligning with world model and video generation priorities. Signals: Coarse-to-fine compositional diffusion, Long-horizon planning, Video generation, World model, Inference-time efficiency

Abstract: Diffusion models provide strong priors for generating structured data, but many tasks require outputs beyond the scale on which these models are typically trained. Compositional generation addresses this by composing overlapping local plans from a pretrained short-horizon prior into a long-horizon output. However, standard composition primarily enforces agreement between neighboring local plans, yielding local consistency without directly specifying the global structure of the full composition. As a result, locally compatible plans may still form an implausible route, task sequence, or temporal evolution. Existing methods improve global coherence by repeatedly propagating local consistency signals or by adding inference-time optimization, but these procedures become expensive as the number or dimensionality of local plans increases. We propose Coarse-to-Fine Compositional Diffusion (CoFi), an inference-time sampler that separates global structure formation from local detail refinement. CoFi first aligns local denoised estimates around a shared coarse structure, producing a global scaffold that captures the long-range task-level arrangement. It then diffuses this scaffold to an intermediate noise level and denoises it with the same pretrained local prior, restoring local fine structure while preserving the scaffold-induced global coherence. Across long-horizon robotic planning, panoramic image generation, and long video generation, CoFi not only improves both global coherence and local sample quality over prior compositional baselines, but also requires 2-8x fewer denoiser evaluations.

</details>

*Yuanyuan Wang, Wenjie Wang, Kun Zhang, Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, stat.ML

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度证明了在仅使用编码器（无解码器重建）的管道中，可以从原始视频像素唯一恢复二阶线性ODE的物理参数，给出了不同阻尼状态下最小数据需求的特征刻画，并提出了方差地板正则化器防止潜空间坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在视觉真实感与物理理解之间存在鸿沟，现有方法通常依赖像素级重建来学习物理动态，计算开销大且可解释性差。本文旨在回答：能否仅通过编码器从视频中唯一地恢复连续时间物理定律的参数？

Method: 理论分析：证明水平集斜率覆盖条件可确保学习到的潜空间与真实物理状态局部仿射，从而实现参数精确恢复。刻画了不同阻尼状态下最小数据需求（欠阻尼系统只需单个视频片段，其他状态需三个不同轨迹）。提出方差地板正则化器（variance-floor regularizer）稳定无解码器目标函数，防止潜空间坍塌。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上验证了理论结果，证明无需像素重建即可从视频中可靠估计可解释的物理常数（如阻尼比、固有频率），同时保证了物理正确性和透明性。

Conclusion: 本文首次从理论上刻画了从视频中识别物理ODE参数的最小数据条件，证明了无解码器管道的可行性，为构建物理可解释且计算高效的视频世界模型提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从视频中识别连续时间物理定律（二阶线性ODE）的结构可辨识性，属于物理世界建模的核心问题，与[interactive_physical_world]（可交互与物理世界建模）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model identifiability from video, a core user priority, with rigorous theory and minimal data requirements. Signals: world model, video-based physical parameter recovery, identifiability theory, encoder-only pipeline, minimal trajectory conditions

Abstract: Bridging the gap between visual realism and physical understanding is a core challenge for video-based world models. We study the structural identifiability of continuous-time physical laws from raw pixels, focusing on whether an encoder-only pipeline can uniquely recover the parameters of second-order linear ODEs. We prove that a level-set slope-coverage condition ensures the learned latent space is locally affine to the true physical state, enabling exact parameter recovery. Our theory provides the first characterization of minimal data requirements across damping regimes, establishing that underdamped systems are identifiable from a single video clip, whereas other regimes require three diverse trajectories. We further introduce a variance-floor regularizer to stabilize the decoder-free objective and prevent latent collapse. Validated on synthetic and real-world data, our approach demonstrates that interpretable physical constants can be reliably estimated from video without the need for compute-intensive pixel reconstruction, ensuring both physical correctness and transparency. Code is available at .

</details>

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，从应用场景、世界状态演化、场景模态三个维度归纳研究趋势，深入分析动作条件可控性、长时序交互记忆、实时响应性三大技术挑战，并对比四个应用领域的评测基准与指标。

Result: 总结了交互式世界建模的现有技术框架与分类体系，梳理了各应用领域（开放世界探索、游戏引擎、自动驾驶、机器人）的代表性工作与评测基准，指出了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 交互式世界建模是通向下一代通用世界模型的关键方向，未来需要在物理一致性、长时序记忆、实时交互、多模态融合等方面进一步突破。

Classification reason: 该论文是一篇关于交互式世界模型的综述，核心关注动作条件视频/3D生成、可交互性、物理世界状态演化等主题，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Comprehensive survey on interactive world modeling, directly aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model survey, interactive video generation, action-conditioned controllability, long-horizon memory, real-time responsiveness

Abstract: With rapid development of large language models and diffusion-based content generation, world modeling has attracted increasing research attention, benefiting various downstream domains such as game engines, embodied AI, autonomous driving, etc. Through explicitly incorporating user actions into world state transition, recent literature empowers world modeling with interactivity in an action-conditioned video or 3D generation paradigm, further enhancing controllability over world evolutions and facilitating users to freely traverse, manipulate, navigate, and personalize the state evolution. In this paper, we aim to systematically review recent research trends, technical developments, evaluation benchmarks, and also propose future potential directions in interactive world modeling. Specifically, we first summarize recent efforts and trends in terms of application scenarios, world state evolution, and scene modality. Afterwards, we delve into three crucial technical challenges, including action-conditioned controllability, long-horizon interactions and memory, and action-following responsiveness for real-time interactivity. Furthermore, we also thoroughly compare existing benchmarks and metrics in four specific application fields: open-world exploration, game engine, autonomous driving, and robotics. Finally, we discuss several promising future directions in achieving next-generation interactive world modeling. The corresponding repository is publicly available at: .

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用 JEPA（Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture）框架，在潜空间中进行状态预测。关键创新是块旋转预测器（block-rotation predictor），它将模10运算的循环群结构显式编码到潜空间预测中，使模型能够零样本泛化到未见过的模运算操作。

Result: 基于 ResNet 的 JEPA 块旋转模型在零样本和 rollout 准确率上均达到 99.46%，远超标准监督基线和普通 JEPA 模型，后者在未见运算上几乎无法泛化。

Conclusion: 当模型架构与问题的底层结构（如循环群）相匹配时，潜空间世界模型能够学习符号变换规则并实现强零样本泛化，为构建更具抽象推理能力的神经架构提供了启示。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BRo-JEPA，一种在潜空间学习模运算的 JEPA 风格世界模型，属于世界模型方向。其核心是学习状态转换规则（动作条件动力学），与「可交互与物理世界建模」子主题（interactive_physical_world）中的环境动力学建模高度吻合。同时，论文在连续潜空间中进行预测，也与连续语言/多模态方向相关，但主要贡献是世界模型中的潜空间动力学学习。

Importance reason: Focuses on abstract algebraic rule learning in latent world models, which is tangentially related to world models but not directly applicable to the user's priority subtopics like video generation or continuous multimodal models. Signals: JEPA latent world model, modular arithmetic zero-shot generalization, block-rotation predictor, MNIST digit states, not video or multimodal generation

Abstract: Can neural networks learn abstract algebraic rules, or do they merely memorize training patterns? We investigate this using MNIST digits as states and modular arithmetic operations as actions in a JEPA-style latent world model. Standard supervised baselines and JEPA models with additive operation embeddings fit seen operations but fail to extrapolate reliably to unseen ones. To bridge this gap, we introduce a block-rotation predictor that imposes the circular structure of modulo-10 arithmetic in latent space. This enables strong zero-shot generalization, with the best ResNet-based JEPA block-rotation model achieving 99.46\% zero-shot and 99.46\% rollout accuracy. Our results suggest that latent world models can learn symbolic transformation rules when architecture matches the structure of the problem. Our code can be \href{ }{accessed here}.

</details>


### [63] [PhyScene3D: Physically Consistent Interactive 3D Tabletop Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01649)
*Weixing Chen, Zhuoqian Feng, Yang Liu, Yexin Zhang, Yifan Wen, Yinghong Liao, Weichao Qiu, Guanbin Li, Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 值得关注 (67.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhyScene3D 提出一种物理一致的交互式3D桌面场景生成框架，通过认知拓扑推理链（CTRC）将场景合成分解为锚点条件化的序列过程，并引入物理感知去噪对齐（PADA）结合可微SDF与测试时优化，将生成场景投影到物理可行流形上，在语义准确性和物理有效性上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D桌面场景生成方法存在误差传播或过拟合到包含广泛物理违规的噪声监督信号的问题，生成的场景往往物理不一致、存在碰撞，无法直接用于物理模拟器中的机器人交互学习。

Method: 提出 PhyScene3D 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 认知拓扑推理链（CTRC），将场景合成分解为锚点条件化的序列生成过程，采用3D AABB（轴对齐包围盒）放置方案施加强结构归纳偏置；(2) 物理感知去噪对齐（PADA），集成可微符号距离场（SDF）与测试时优化（TTO），将生成场景投影到物理可行流形上同时保持语义意图。

Result: 在多个指标上超越现有最先进方法，场景级碰撞率相比人工标注训练数据降低40%，在语义准确性和物理有效性方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: PhyScene3D 通过将场景生成建模为类人构造过程，结合结构化归纳偏置与物理感知优化，有效解决了物理一致3D桌面场景生成这一基础但未充分探索的问题，为交互式和通用机器人学习提供了可直接加载到物理模拟器的场景数据。

Classification reason: 该论文关注物理一致的3D桌面场景生成，生成可直接加载到物理模拟器中的交互式场景，属于世界模型方向中的可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）[CANONICAL]。同时其生成性质也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Strong physical world modeling for interactive 3D scenes, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, interactive 3D scene generation, physical consistency, cognitive topological reasoning, physics-aware denoising

Abstract: Generating physically consistent 3D tabletop scenes is a fundamental yet underexplored problem for interactive and generalist robotic learning. The challenge stems from dense object hierarchies and irregular affordances. Here, an interactive scene denotes a physically valid, collision-free environment directly loadable into physics simulators. Existing methods, ranging from decoupled symbolic solvers to end-to-end regression models, often suffer from error propagation or overfitting to noisy supervision containing widespread physical violations. To address these limitations, we introduce PhyScene3D, a framework that reformulates generation as a Human-Mimetic Constructive Process. The proposed Cognitive Topological Reasoning Chain (CTRC) factorizes scene synthesis into a sequential, anchor-conditioned process. It employs a 3D AABB-based placement scheme that imposes a strong structural inductive bias. To address imperfect supervision and physical infeasibility, we introduce Physics-Aware Denoising Alignment (PADA). It integrates a differentiable Signed Distance Field (SDF) with Test-Time Optimization (TTO) to project generated scenes onto a physics-feasible manifold while preserving semantic intent. Experiments demonstrate that PhyScene3D outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in both semantic accuracy and physical validity, achieving a 40% reduction in scene-wise collision rate relative to the human-annotated training data.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理动力学推断方法在物理一致性和长期外推方面存在不足，缺乏系统比较不同范式（符号推理 vs. 数据驱动）在物理仿真任务上的能力边界。

Method: 构建了2D MPM物理仿真数据集（含可变形体、流体、动力学物体、发射器四类现象），分别采用代码生成方法（基于LLM生成MPM仿真代码）和视频扩散方法（基于扩散模型生成未来帧），并通过控制物理侧信息的输入量来系统评估两种方法的优劣。

Result: 代码生成方法能自动合成MPM仿真代码，但难以从纯视觉输入准确推断物理参数；视频扩散方法能更好地从视觉输入识别几何属性，但生成的外推结果在物理上不可靠（违反物理规律）。代码生成在时间和物理稳定性上优于视频扩散。

Conclusion: 两种方法各有优劣：代码生成擅长保持物理一致性和长期稳定性但视觉感知弱，视频扩散擅长视觉感知但物理一致性差。未来工作可探索两者结合以取长补短。

Classification reason: 该论文使用MPM物理仿真数据集研究从视频推断物理动力学并外推，属于物理世界建模与神经仿真器范畴，与[interactive_physical_world]（可交互与物理世界建模）高度匹配。同时论文也涉及视频扩散方法，因此也匹配multimodal_generation方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on physical world modeling via MPM simulations, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, physical dynamics inference, code generation vs video diffusion, MPM simulation dataset, limited to 2D

Abstract: To study the ability to infer physical dynamics from videos and extrapolate them forward in time, we assemble a dataset of 2D Material Point Method (MPM) physical simulations covering rich physical phenomena such as deformable objects, fluids, kinetic objects, and emitters. We study code generation and video diffusion approaches on this dataset, identifying their strengths and weaknesses by varying the amount of physically relevant side information. The code generation model, beyond giving a working demonstration of automatic synthesis of MPM simulations, reveals that such an approach struggles with inferring physical parameters from visual input, but relative to video diffusion, produces physically and temporally stable extrapolations forward in time, while the video diffusion model more strongly identifies geometric properties from visual input but produces physically implausible extrapolations.

</details>
