### [65] [ImagineUAV: Aerial Vision-Language Navigation via World-Action Modeling and Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01205)
*Xuchen Liu, Jiawei Huang, Shihao Xia, Bingxi Liu, Jinqiang Cui, Jiankun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.97) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ImagineUAV 提出了一种基于想象驱动的无人机视觉语言导航框架，通过级联世界-动作建模：先使用潜视频扩散模型生成指令条件化的未来观测，再通过动作提取器推断6-DoF运动，最后由运动动力学规划器优化为无碰撞轨迹。仅1.3B参数即在基准和真实飞行中超越先前VLN和VLA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在无人机VLN任务中因几何不一致和动力学不匹配而表现脆弱，直接回归动作缺乏对环境的显式建模。作者希望引入"想象"机制，让模型先预测环境演化再推导动作，从而提升鲁棒性和物理可行性。

Method: 提出ImagineUAV框架，包含三个级联模块：(1) 潜视频扩散模型，以指令和当前观测为条件生成未来帧序列；(2) 动作提取器，从生成的未来观测中推断6-DoF运动；(3) 运动动力学规划器，将动作估计优化为无碰撞轨迹。此外设计了步蒸馏推理管线以实现实时执行。

Result: 在多个VLN基准和真实无人机飞行实验中，ImagineUAV以1.3B参数超越先前VLN和VLA基线方法，验证了想象驱动导航的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 想象驱动的级联世界-动作建模能够有效解决VLA模型在无人机导航中的几何与动力学问题，为具身导航提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用潜视频扩散模型生成指令条件化的未来观测（想象环境演化），并从中提取6-DoF动作，属于动作条件视频生成与未来预测，与[视频世界模型]子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation for embodied navigation, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, video diffusion, continuous visual token, latent flow, efficient inference

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) for UAVs demands grounding free-form instructions into 6-DoF flight under partial observability. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at semantic reasoning, they suffer from brittleness due to geometric inconsistency and dynamics mismatch. To address this, we propose ImagineUAV, an imagination-driven framework leveraging cascaded world-action modeling. Instead of direct regression, ImagineUAV employs a latent video diffusion model to generate instruction-conditioned future observations, explicitly imagining environmental evolution, from which 6-DoF motions are inferred via an action extractor. A kinodynamic planner then refines these estimates into collision-free trajectories. Additionally, a step-distilled inference pipeline ensures real-time execution. With only 1.3B parameters, ImagineUAV outperforms prior VLN and VLA baselines on benchmarks and real-world flights, validating the practicality of imagination-driven aerial navigation.

</details>


### [66] [StressDream: Steering Video World Models for Robust Policy Evaluation and Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00267)
*Junwon Seo, Sushant Veer, Ran Tian, Wenhao Ding, Apoorva Sharma, Karen Leung, Edward Schmerling, Marco Pavone, Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StressDream 提出一种推理时优化扩散视频世界模型初始噪声的方法，利用 VLM 语义目标与合理性约束，将生成想象引导至高风险但合理的未来场景（如任务失败），从而支持鲁棒的策略评估与改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在策略评估中通常依赖名义上的想象（nominal imaginations），这容易遗漏高风险但罕见的关键结果。现有方法需要大量采样才能覆盖这些高影响事件，效率低下。因此需要一种能在推理时高效地将想象引导至高风险合理场景的方法。

Method: StressDream 通过优化扩散式世界模型的初始噪声来引导生成。包含两个互补目标：(1) 语义目标——利用视觉语言模型（VLM）对生成视频进行推理，提供信息丰富的梯度；(2) 合理性目标——防止优化后的噪声偏离分布导致不合理的想象。两者结合实现文本指定的高风险场景引导。

Result: 在自动驾驶和机器人操作任务上，使用 SOTA 视频世界模型进行实验，StressDream 能有效将想象引导至文本指定的高风险但合理的场景（如任务失败），从而识别出可能导致不良后果的动作，实现鲁棒策略评估与改进。

Conclusion: StressDream 提供了一种推理时引导视频世界模型想象的有效方法，通过语义与合理性双重目标优化噪声，在不牺牲合理性的前提下聚焦高风险场景，为基于世界模型的策略评估与改进提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 StressDream，通过优化扩散式视频世界模型的初始噪声，将想象引导至高风险但合理的未来场景，用于策略评估与改进。核心是视频世界模型（video world model）的动作条件视频生成与未来预测，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "视频世界模型" (video_world_model)。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models for policy evaluation, a top-priority subtopic, with a novel inference-time steering method using VLM guidance. Signals: world model, video diffusion, inference-time optimization, VLM-guided steering, autonomous driving

Abstract: Video world models (WMs) have shown promise for policy evaluation and improvement by imagining realistic future observations conditioned on ego-robot actions. While WMs can model distributions over futures, policy evaluation and improvement typically rely on nominal imaginations, which can miss high-impact outcomes of robot actions unless prohibitively many samples are drawn. To enable robust policy evaluation and improvement over WM imaginations, we propose StressDream, which steers imaginations toward high-impact yet plausible outcomes specified at inference time by optimizing the initial noise of diffusion-based WMs. However, optimizing high-dimensional noise is challenging: the optimization must reason about nuanced, scene-dependent target events in generated videos while avoiding out-of-distribution (OOD) noise that yields implausible imaginations. We address this with two complementary objectives: a semantic objective with a Vision-Language Model that provides informative gradients by reasoning about the generated video, and a plausibility objective that prevents the optimized noise from drifting OOD. With state-of-the-art video world models for autonomous driving and robotic manipulation, we show that StressDream effectively steers imaginations toward high-impact yet plausible outcomes specified by text at inference time, such as task failures, enabling robust policy evaluation and improvement by identifying actions whose plausible futures include undesirable outcomes. Video results are available at .

</details>


### [67] [Physical Object Understanding with a Physically Controllable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00439)
*Rahul Venkatesh, Klemen Kotar, Lilian Naing Chen, Wanhee Lee, Gia Ancone, Seungwoo Kim, Luca Thomas Wheeler, Jared Watrous, Honglin Chen, Daniel Bear, Stefan Stojanov, Daniel LK Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的概率世界模型，通过自回归序列建模从原始视频中学习物理场景结构。该模型能够生成多个合理的未来状态，从中发现物体及其关节子部件，在3D中操控物体，并计算物体间的物理关系（如Visual Jenga）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能的核心挑战是从原始视频中学习场景的物理结构——区域如何形成物体以及支配它们交互的物理定律。现有世界模型架构缺乏从部分观测中推断世界分布状态的能力，无法支持对任意视觉变量（如外观和动力学）的条件概率估计。

Method: 提出一类新的概率世界模型，通过自回归序列建模进行高效训练。模型支持对任意视觉变量（如外观和动力学）在给定其他变量条件下的概率估计。通过顺序推理生成多个合理的未来世界状态，然后分析这些未来状态中的运动相关性来提取物体及其关节子部件。

Result: 模型成功捕获了物体运动的物理规律，能够生成多个合理的未来状态；通过运动相关性分析发现了物体和关节子部件；能够在3D中操控已发现的物体；能够计算物体间的物理关系，实现了Visual Jenga等应用。

Conclusion: 自回归序列建模可以高效训练概率世界模型，使其具备从部分观测中推断分布状态的能力，并从中涌现出丰富的物体理解能力，包括物体发现、3D操控和物理关系计算。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一类新的概率世界模型，通过自回归序列建模从原始视频中学习物理结构（物体形成及其交互规律），能够生成多个合理的未来状态、发现物体及其子部件、在3D中操控物体，并计算物体间的物理关系。这属于视频世界模型的核心范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型" (video_world_model)。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous visual representation learning via autoregressive sequence modeling, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, autoregressive sequence modeling, physical object understanding, 3D manipulation, Visual Jenga

Abstract: A central challenge in visual intelligence is learning the physical structure of scenes from raw videos: how regions form objects and the laws that govern their interactions. Solving these tasks requires world models capable of inferring distributional states of the world from partial observations - capabilities that current architectures do not provide. We introduce a new class of probabilistic world models that support estimation of the probability of any visual variable, such as appearance and dynamics, conditioned on any other variables. Here, we identify that these models can be trained efficiently with autoregressive sequence modeling, yielding world models from which rich object understanding emerges. First, we demonstrate that our model captures the physical laws governing how objects move by generating multiple plausible future states of the world through sequential inference. Then, by analyzing motion correlations across these futures, we extract objects and articulated object subparts. Having discovered these objects, we show that our world model can manipulate them in 3D. Finally, we demonstrate how physical relationships between objects can be computed from the world model, enabling applications such as Visual Jenga.

</details>


### [68] [OptiWorld: Optimal Control for Video World Generation under Physical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00499)
*Yu Yuan, Jianhao Yuan, Xijun Wang, Daiqing Li, Liu He, Lu Ling, Stanley H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OptiWorld 将经典最优控制引入视频生成世界模型，在推理时提取紧凑世界状态、在物理约束下规划最优轨迹，并据此生成视频，实现目标条件图像到视频生成、视频动力学编辑和反事实生成等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成世界模型主要生成看似合理的运动，但无法主动控制或优化底层动力学，导致生成视频中物体轨迹可能不安全、不光滑、低效或物理不一致。

Method: OptiWorld 在推理时首先从视频生成模型中提取紧凑的任务相关世界状态，然后在物理约束下规划最优轨迹（将规划建模为连续流形上的几何问题，将3D几何和任务相关物理约束转化为统一的规划几何），最后基于该轨迹条件生成视频。

Result: 在目标条件图像到视频生成、视频动力学编辑和反事实生成等多个任务上展示了生成具有更优动力学特性的视频的能力。

Conclusion: 通过将最优控制层引入视频生成世界模型，OptiWorld 能够生成符合物理约束且动力学更优的视频，为可控视频世界生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将最优控制引入视频生成世界模型，在推理时规划最优轨迹并生成符合物理约束的视频，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴。同时涉及视频生成，因此也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with optimal control for physically consistent generation, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, video generation, optimal control, physical constraints, goal-conditioned generation

Abstract: Video generation models are becoming a scalable form of world models, but they mainly generate plausible motion rather than proactively control or optimize the underlying dynamics. As a result, an object in the generated video may follow trajectories that are unsafe, not smooth, inefficient, or physically inconsistent. In this work, we propose \textbf{OptiWorld}, a framework that brings classical optimal control into video generation at inference time. OptiWorld first extracts a compact, task-relevant world state, then plans an optimal trajectory under physical constraints, and finally renders the video conditioned on this trajectory. We formulate planning as a geometric problem on a continuous manifold, which converts 3D geometry and task-dependent physical constraints into a unified planning geometry. By adding this optimal-control layer, OptiWorld generates videos with preferable dynamics, demonstrating strong potential in multiple tasks including goal-conditioned image-to-video generation, video dynamics editing, and counterfactual generation.

</details>


### [69] [RoboTrustBench: Benchmarking the Trustworthiness of Video World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01600)
*Huiqiong Li, Jiayu Wang, Zhiting Mei, Anirudha Majumdar, Jingjing Chen, Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RoboTrustBench 是一个针对机器人操作视频世界模型可信度的评测基准，包含 1,207 个专家验证的指令-图像对和六维评估协议。评测发现当前视频世界模型虽能生成视觉连贯的视频，但在约束推理、反事实理解、物理交互和抑制不安全指令方面表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型评测大多只关注有效、可行、安全的指令场景，缺乏对模型在约束敏感、反事实、对抗等复杂场景下可信度的系统评估。为填补这一空白，作者构建了 RoboTrustBench 基准。

Method: 基于真实 DROID 机器人操作数据构建 1,207 个专家验证的指令-图像对，设计 Normal、Constraint-Sensitive、Counterfactual、Adversarial 四种场景，提出六维评估协议（含 13 个细粒度标准），结合人类评估和 MLLM 评估对七个代表性视频世界模型进行评测。

Result: 当前视频世界模型在视觉连贯性上表现良好，但在约束推理、反事实理解、物理交互和不安全指令抑制方面存在显著不足，表明视觉质量和表面指令遵循不足以支撑可信的机器人视频世界建模。

Conclusion: RoboTrustBench 揭示了现有视频世界模型在可信度方面的关键短板，为未来构建更安全、更可靠的机器人视频世界模型提供了评测基础和改进方向。

Classification reason: 论文提出 RoboTrustBench，用于评测视频世界模型在机器人操作中的可信度（包括正常、约束敏感、反事实、对抗场景）。核心关注视频世界模型的评测，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）高度匹配。同时涉及生成模型评测，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向下的 generation_benchmark。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model trustworthiness, a critical gap for video world models in robotics, aligning with high-priority world_model and video generation world model subtopics. Signals: world model trustworthiness, robotic manipulation benchmark, constraint reasoning, counterfactual understanding, unsafe-instruction suppression

Abstract: Video world models are increasingly used in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate them under valid, feasible, and safe instructions. We introduce RoboTrustBench, a benchmark for evaluating the trustworthiness of video world models under four scenarios: Normal, Constraint-Sensitive, Counterfactual, and Adversarial. Built from real-world DROID episodes, RoboTrustBench contains 1,207 expert-validated instruction-image pairs and a six-dimensional evaluation protocol with 13 fine-grained criteria. Evaluating seven representative video world models with human and MLLM assessment, we find that current models often generate visually coherent videos, but struggle with constraint reasoning, counterfactual grounding, physical interaction, and unsafe-instruction suppression. These results show that visual quality and surface-level instruction following are insufficient for trustworthy robotic video world modeling.

</details>


### [70] [MBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark on Memory Capability for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00793)
*Shengjun Zhang, Zhang Zhang, Simin Huang, Zhenyu Tang, Hanyang Wang, Chensheng Dai, Min Chen, Yifan Li, Yuxin Li, Yingjie Chen, Hao Liu, Chen Li, Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MBench 是一个专门评估视频世界模型记忆能力的综合基准，将记忆能力分解为实体一致性、环境一致性和因果一致性三个层次共12个可量化子维度，基于真实长视频和规则/VLM评估，揭示了现有视频世界模型在长时状态保持方面的系统性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型基准主要关注视觉质量、运动连贯性和文本-视频对齐，但忽略了世界模型的核心能力——记忆（即在长时间跨度内维持稳定且合理的内部状态）。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 将视频世界模型的记忆能力系统分解为三个层次维度：实体一致性（物体身份、属性、数量等）、环境一致性（场景布局、光照、天气等）和因果一致性（物理规律、动作结果等），进一步细化为12个可量化子维度。基于精心筛选的真实长视频构建基准，采用基于规则的量化指标和VLM进行客观评估。

Result: 对主流视频世界模型的广泛评估揭示了现有方法在长时状态保持方面的关键系统性局限，表明当前模型在长时间跨度内维持实体、环境和因果一致性方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: MBench 为视频世界模型的记忆能力提供了标准化评估基准，明确了该领域需要进一步研究的方向，即提升模型在长时间跨度内的状态保持与一致性能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MBench 基准，专门评估视频世界模型的长时记忆能力（实体一致性、环境一致性、因果一致性），属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题。同时，该基准涉及视频生成评测，也与多模态生成方向下的生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）子主题相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model memory consistency, a critical gap for video world models, aligning with top user priority. Signals: world model benchmark, memory capability, entity consistency, environment consistency, causal consistency

Abstract: Recent advancements in video-based world models have demonstrated an unprecedented ability to synthesize high-fidelity visual sequences. However, a fundamental gap persists between visually plausible video generation and the functional requirements of a world model, particularly in maintaining a stable and reasonable internal state over extended temporal horizons. While existing benchmarks primarily emphasize visual quality, motion coherence, and text-video alignment, they largely overlook memory, the core capability of a world model to preserve consistency across long-term horizons and complex interactions. To address this gap, we present \textbf{MBench}, a comprehensive benchmark dedicated to quantifying and evaluating the memory capability of video world models. We systematically decompose the memory capability of video world models into three hierarchical and complementary core dimensions: entity consistency, environment consistency, and causal consistency, which are further refined into 12 quantifiable sub-dimensions for comprehensive characterization of long-term memory. Our benchmark is built upon rigorously curated real-captured long videos, and evaluated by rule-based quantitative matrices and VLM to enable objective and comprehensive consistency assessment. Extensive evaluations of mainstream state-of-the-art video world models reveal critical systemic limitations of existing methods in long-term state retention, providing a standardized benchmark and clear research direction to advance the field.

</details>


### [71] [Unified Driving Tokens: Representation- and Geometry-Guided Discrete Tokenizer for Driving World Models and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01935)
*Ziyang Yao, Zeyu Zhu, YunCheng Jiang, Zibin Guo, Huijing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表示引导与几何增强的离散视觉tokenizer（Unified Driving Tokens），通过联合监督（特征解码对齐DINO特征空间、RGB重建、深度与相对位姿监督）学习紧凑离散token，用于自动驾驶世界模型和规划。在NAVSIM上展示了更好的重建保真度、表示一致性、规划性能和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散视觉tokenizer主要针对图像生成优化，侧重于像素级重建，导致学到的token在自动驾驶决策任务中存在"易生成但难解码"的鸿沟。需要一种既能保持高质量重建又能提供对驾驶决策有用的表示的tokenizer。

Method: 提出表示引导与几何增强的离散tokenizer：(1) 通过特征解码对齐冻结的DINOv2特征空间；(2) 使用感知损失和对抗损失进行RGB重建保持外观；(3) 添加相邻帧深度和相对位姿监督注入几何状态线索；(4) 采用多码本量化（MCQ）稳定联合训练目标。使用轻量级规划读取头和GPT风格的next-token世界模型评估token质量。

Result: 在NAVSIM数据集上，该方法在重建保真度和表示一致性上优于基线；在固定解码器下取得有竞争力的规划性能；在相同设置下生成质量更好。

Conclusion: 通过联合特征对齐、几何监督和多码本量化，可以学习到既保持重建质量又对驾驶决策有用的离散视觉token，为自动驾驶世界模型和规划提供了更好的token表示基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶的离散视觉tokenizer，用于token-based世界模型和规划，属于视频世界模型（动作条件视频生成与未来预测）方向。同时其离散tokenizer设计也涉及多模态生成中的自回归视觉生成，但主要贡献在世界模型。

Importance reason: Strong alignment with world model and continuous representation priorities, with novel geometry-guided discrete tokenizer for driving. Signals: world model, discrete tokenizer, autonomous driving, representation-guided, geometry-enhanced

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens should provide a compact representation for both token-based world modeling and planning in autonomous driving. However, most tokenizers are inherited from image generation and are optimized mainly for pixel reconstruction, which may leave a gap between what is easy to generate and what is useful to decode for driving decisions. We present a representation-guided and geometry-enhanced tokenizer that learns discrete tokens under joint supervision. The tokenizer aligns its discrete bottleneck with a frozen DINO feature space through feature decoding, while preserving appearance via RGB reconstruction with perceptual and adversarial losses. To inject geometric state-related cues, we add adjacent-frame depth and relative-pose supervision during training and stabilize joint objectives with multi-codebook quantization. We evaluate the same learned tokens with a lightweight planning readout and a GPT-style next-token world model. Experiments on NAVSIM show improved reconstruction fidelity and representation consistency, competitive planning performance under a fixed decoder, and better generative quality under matched settings.

</details>


### [72] [Geometry-Aware Implicit Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02436)
*Zhengxuan Wei, Xu Guo, Xinghui Li, Xunzhi Xiang, Min Wei, Yiran Zhu, Qiulin Wang, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Xiangwang Hou, Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (91.37) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GIM-World 提出了一种几何感知隐式记忆框架，通过轻量级 Transformer 编码器将变长历史压缩为固定大小的记忆 token，并利用相机可查询的几何头在训练时从冻结基础模型中蒸馏 3D 场景结构，推理时丢弃几何教师，仅保留轻量记忆模块，在 MIND 数据集上实现了优于显式和隐式记忆基线的长程几何与视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在长程 rollout 中面临记忆问题：当观测超出模型原生上下文窗口后，模型需要记住历史信息。显式记忆（保留帧或在线 3D 重建）存在启发式检索错误、冗余外观存储或重建伪影等问题；隐式记忆将历史压缩为紧凑状态，但现有设计缺乏对跨视角场景几何的显式约束。因此，作者希望设计一种几何感知的隐式记忆机制，在保持轻量的同时更好地编码场景几何结构。

Method: GIM-World 包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级 Transformer 编码器，将变长历史观测压缩为固定大小的记忆 token；(2) 相机可查询的几何头（geometry head），在训练时从冻结的 3D 基础模型中蒸馏场景几何结构到记忆 token 中，使记忆具备几何感知能力；(3) 信息引导的剪枝规则（information-guided pruning），在历史增长时控制编码成本。推理时丢弃几何教师模型，仅保留轻量记忆模块。

Result: 在 MIND 数据集上的实验表明，GIM-World 在长程几何一致性和视觉一致性方面均优于显式记忆基线和隐式记忆基线。消融实验验证了几何蒸馏和剪枝策略的有效性。

Conclusion: GIM-World 通过几何感知隐式记忆有效提升了视频世界模型的长程 rollout 质量，证明了在隐式记忆中显式编码场景几何结构的价值，且推理时无需额外开销，为视频世界模型的记忆设计提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GIM-World，一种面向视频世界模型的几何感知隐式记忆框架，旨在提升长程 rollout 中的几何与视觉一致性。核心贡献在视频世界模型的记忆机制，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video generation priorities, with a novel geometry-aware implicit memory approach that boosts long-horizon consistency. Signals: world model, video world model, implicit memory, geometry-aware, long-horizon consistency

Abstract: Video world models aim to simulate controllable visual environments, but long-horizon rollouts depend on what the model remembers after observations leave its native context window. Explicit memories retain frames or online 3D reconstructions, which can suffer from heuristic retrieval errors, redundant appearance storage, or reconstruction artifacts. Implicit memories compress history into a compact state, but existing designs are not explicitly constrained to encode cross-view scene geometry. We propose GIM-World, a geometry-aware implicit memory framework for video world models. A lightweight transformer encoder compresses variable-length history into fixed-size memory tokens, a camera-queryable geometry head distills 3D scene structure from a frozen foundation model into the memory during training, and an information-guided pruning rule keeps encoding cost bounded as history grows. The geometry teacher is discarded at inference, leaving a lightweight memory module. Experiments on MIND show that GIM-World better preserves long-horizon geometric and visual consistency than both explicit- and implicit-memory baselines.

</details>


### [73] [From Zero to Hero: Training-Free Custom Concept Spawning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02575)
*Kiymet Akdemir, Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPAWN，一种无需训练的零样本方法，通过替换自回归世界模型上下文记忆中的锚定帧（参考帧的第一个 slot），在短注入窗口内插入外部概念潜变量，使概念通过模型自身记忆机制自然传播到后续生成帧中，实现用户指定视觉概念（如角色、建筑等）在交互式视频世界中的可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归世界模型仅能基于文本提示或单张参考帧生成世界，当用户导航到参考帧不可见的区域时，模型只能依赖先验知识填充，用户无法指定新区域中应出现什么内容。这种缺乏可控场景组合能力的限制阻碍了世界模型在游戏、交互式叙事和模拟等应用中的使用。

Method: SPAWN 利用图像到视频骨干网络的一个结构特性：上下文记忆的第一个 slot 固定为参考帧，作为每个生成块的锚定。方法通过三个步骤实现概念注入：(1) 将参考帧的锚定 latent 替换为外部概念图像（或文本描述经 T2I 模型生成的概念图像）的 latent；(2) 在短注入窗口（如 4 帧）内保持替换，让概念通过模型自注意力机制传播；(3) 恢复原始锚定 latent，概念已通过模型记忆自然传播到后续 rollout 中。该方法无需任何训练或微调。

Result: 实验表明 SPAWN 能够成功将细粒度实体（角色、道具）和大尺度元素（建筑、地标）注入世界模型生成的视频中，生成的概念在光照、尺度、透视方面与场景保持一致，同时保持身份一致性和时间连贯性。与基线方法（如直接替换、混合注入等）相比，SPAWN 在概念保真度和场景一致性上均表现更优。

Conclusion: SPAWN 证明了在现有自回归世界模型中无需训练即可实现可控概念生成，为世界模型在游戏、交互式叙事和模拟等应用中的场景可控组合提供了可行方案，开辟了无需额外训练即可扩展世界模型能力的新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种无需训练的方法，在自回归世界模型的视频 rollout 中注入用户指定的视觉概念（concept spawning），属于视频世界模型中的可控生成与交互式视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video world model generation with a training-free method, aligning with high-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, autoregressive video generation, controllable generation, training-free, concept spawning

Abstract: Autoregressive world models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for interactive video generation, allowing users to navigate dynamically generated environments through actions. These models are typically conditioned on a text prompt and/or a single reference frame, from which the entire world is generated. Yet the moment the user navigates beyond what is visible in that frame, the unseen regions are populated by the base model's priors, with no mechanism for the user to specify what should appear and where. This is a fundamental limitation for applications such as gaming, interactive storytelling, and simulation, where controllable scene composition is essential. We refer to this missing capability as concept spawning; introducing a user-specified visual concept into a world model, analogous to spawning in a game engine. We introduce SPAWN (Swapping Pinned Anchor with Windowed iNjection), a training-free method for concept spawning. SPAWN exploits a structural property of image-to-video backbones: the first slot of the context memory is pinned to the reference frame and acts as a foundational anchor for every generated chunk. By swapping this anchor with an external concept latent over a short injection window and letting the original anchor return, we cause the concept to propagate naturally through the rollout via the model's own memory. SPAWN supports concepts from fine-grained entities such as characters and props to large-scale elements such as buildings and landmarks, and accepts either a concept image or a text description as input. Experiments show that SPAWN integrates concepts with consistent lighting, scale, and perspective while preserving identity and temporal coherence, demonstrating that controllable concept spawning is achievable in existing autoregressive world models without any training.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 尽管NeRF和3DGS等新兴方法可直接生成带纹理的资产，多边形网格仍是建模、动画、视觉特效和游戏管线中的核心表示，因此神经3D网格纹理化仍然是活跃且重要的研究方向，需要系统性的综述来梳理该领域的发展脉络。

Result: 提供了一个结构化的神经3D网格纹理化研究全景图，涵盖纹理合成、纹理迁移和纹理补全三大任务，以及基于GAN、扩散模型等不同范式的技术路线。

Conclusion: 神经3D网格纹理化领域已取得显著进展，但仍面临纹理一致性、多视图协调、高质量数据集缺乏等挑战，未来可向更高效、更可控的生成方向进一步发展。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于神经3D网格纹理化的综述，涵盖纹理合成、迁移和补全，属于3D生成与重建范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）完美匹配，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: A comprehensive survey on 3D mesh texturing, but it is a tangential area not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Survey paper, 3D mesh texturing, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched, Negative keywords not triggered

Abstract: Texturing 3D meshes plays a vital role in determining the visual realism of digital objects and scenes. Although recent generative 3D approaches based on Neural Radiance Fields and Gaussian Splatting can produce textured assets directly, polygonal meshes remain the core representation across modeling, animation, visual effects, and gaming pipelines. Neural 3D mesh texturing therefore continues to be an essential and active area of research. In this survey, we present a comprehensive review of recent advances in neural 3D mesh texturing, covering methods for texture synthesis, transfer, and completion. We first summarize key foundations in mesh geometry, texture mapping, differentiable rendering, and neural generative models, and then organize the literature into a unified taxonomy spanning early GAN-based methods to modern diffusion-based pipelines. We further analyze common architectures and supervision strategies, review datasets and evaluation protocols, and discuss emerging applications, practical/commercial systems, and open challenges. Together, these insights provide a structured perspective on the current landscape and help guide future developments in learning-based 3D mesh texturing.

</details>


### [75] [HiGS: A Hierarchical Rendering Architecture for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00352)
*Dawid Pająk, Martin Bisson, Rodolfo Lima*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (61.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HiGS 提出层次化分块渲染架构，将 3DGS 中的空间划分与光栅化解耦到不同尺度（粗宏块划分 + 细渲染块光栅化），实现高达 15.8x 的加速，同时保持精确的前后 alpha 混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 将空间划分和光栅化绑定到同一 tile 大小，但两者对 tile 尺寸的需求相反：划分在大 tile 下更高效，光栅化在小 tile 下更高效。这种单尺度绑定导致少数密集 tile 成为帧时间瓶颈，需要解耦两者以提升渲染效率。

Method: HiGS 引入层次化分块架构：在粗粒度宏块（macro-tile）上进行高斯划分和深度排序，在细粒度渲染块（render tile）上进行光栅化。光栅化工作按每个宏块中的高斯数量比例分配，使密集区域分散到多个并行单元而非串行处理。

Result: 在多个测试场景中，HiGS 比原始 3DGS 快 15.8 倍，优于所有对比的现有光栅化器，同时保持精确的前后 alpha 混合渲染质量。

Conclusion: 通过解耦空间划分与光栅化的尺度，层次化渲染架构能显著提升 3DGS 的实时渲染性能，为 3D 高斯泼溅的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种层次化渲染架构 HiGS，用于加速 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的实时新视角合成，属于 3D 场景渲染与重建方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS rendering efficiency, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, real-time rendering, hierarchical tiling, rasterization acceleration, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the standard for real-time novel view synthesis on commodity GPUs. Its pipeline ties spatial partitioning and rasterization to one tile size, yet the two pull in opposite directions: partitioning, which bins and depth-sorts gaussians, grows cheaper with larger tiles, while rasterization gets cheaper with smaller ones. Prior acceleration work reduces the cost of individual stages but keeps both locked to that single scale, where a few dense tiles dominate frame time. We present Hierarchically Tiled Gaussian Splatting (HiGS), which gives each its own scale: partitioning runs over coarse macro-tiles, while rasterization runs over the fine render tiles within them. Rasterization work is then issued in proportion to the gaussians in each macro-tile rather than per tile, so dense regions spread across many parallel units instead of serializing through one. Across tested scenes, HiGS renders up to 15.8x faster than the original 3DGS and outperforms every other rasterizer we evaluate, while preserving exact front-to-back alpha compositing.

</details>


### [76] [Real-Time Physics Simulation with Dynamic Mesh-Gaussian Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00444)
*Adrian Ramlal, John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双表示框架，将固定拓扑网格用于物理碰撞检测、高斯泼溅用于渲染，实现实时物理仿真。实验表明，通过后处理将变拓扑高质量重建（如DG-Mesh）转换为固定拓扑会导致65-80%的几何退化，说明高质量重建与物理兼容拓扑是本质上不同的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D重建集成到物理仿真中需要固定拓扑网格以实现高效碰撞检测，但现有方法（如DG-Mesh）生成的网格拓扑变化大、优化目标是几何质量而非物理兼容性。如何在不牺牲重建保真度的前提下实现物理仿真集成是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出双表示框架：固定拓扑网格用于物理仿真（碰撞检测），高斯泼溅（3DGS）用于高质量渲染。通过运行时顶点缓冲区更新实现4.65倍加速。评估了两种拓扑转换策略——时序对应追踪和模板投影映射，并与原生固定拓扑方法（MaGS）在DG-Mesh数据集上对比。

Result: 两种转换策略均导致65-80%的几何退化，效果不如MaGS，尽管DG-Mesh初始质量更高。双表示框架相比变拓扑基线实现了4.65倍加速，支持实时物理仿真。

Conclusion: 高质量重建与物理兼容拓扑是本质上不同的目标，无法通过后处理转换调和。未来应开发物理感知的重建方法。该框架可与任何固定拓扑方法结合实现实时仿真。

Classification reason: 该论文提出结合固定拓扑网格（Mesh）与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的双表示框架，用于动态3D重建的物理仿真。核心涉及3D高斯泼溅、网格重建与神经渲染，属于3D生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D physics simulation and mesh topology, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous language/multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: 3D reconstruction, physics simulation, mesh topology, Gaussian splatting, real-time rendering

Abstract: Integrating dynamic 3D reconstructions into physics simulation requires fixed mesh topology for efficient collision detection, but state-of-the-art methods like DG-Mesh produce varying topology optimized for geometric quality. We investigate whether topology conversion can enable physics integration while preserving reconstruction fidelity. We propose a dual-representation framework combining fixed-topology meshes for physics with Gaussian splatting for rendering, achieving 4.65$\times$ speedup over varying-topology baselines through runtime vertex buffer updates. We evaluate two conversion strategies, temporal correspondence tracking and template-based projection, against native fixed-topology methods (MaGS) on the DG-Mesh dataset. Our evaluation reveals that both conversion approaches incur 65-80% geometric degradation, producing results inferior to MaGS despite DG-Mesh's superior initial quality. This demonstrates that high-quality reconstruction and physics-compatible topology represent fundamentally distinct objectives that cannot be reconciled through post-processing. Our findings inform future development of physics-aware reconstruction methods and our framework enables real-time simulation with any fixed-topology approach.

</details>


### [77] [Optimizing 3D Gaussian Splatting via Point Cloud Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00450)
*Adrian Ramlal, Yan Song Hu, John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种点云上采样方法（线性插值、三角插值、样条曲面重建、移动最小二乘曲面拟合、Voronoi 点生成）以及一种深度引导的点提升方法，用于优化 3D Gaussian Splatting 的初始化种子点质量，在 Mip-NeRF360 和 Replica 数据集上验证了不同方法在不同场景类型下的效果差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 的重建质量高度依赖于初始种子点的质量，而现有方法通常使用 SfM 点云直接初始化，在纹理稀疏区域或复杂几何场景中效果不佳。本文旨在探索不同的点云上采样策略以改善 3DGS 的初始化，从而提升重建质量。

Method: 提出了五种点云上采样方法（线性插值、三角插值、样条曲面重建、移动最小二乘曲面拟合、Voronoi 点生成）和一种深度引导的点提升方法。深度引导方法利用深度图保持与 SfM 重建的几何一致性，在纹理缺失区域添加几何感知的点。在 Mip-NeRF360 和 Replica 数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 不同上采样策略在不同场景中表现各异：曲面重建方法在有机、细节丰富的场景中表现更好，而简单插值方法更适合分段平滑几何场景。深度引导方法在纹理缺失区域添加几何感知点方面展现出潜力。

Conclusion: 点云上采样可以有效改善 3DGS 的初始化质量，但不同方法适用于不同场景类型。本文为根据场景特征和计算约束选择合适的上采样方法提供了初步的实用指南，推进了对点云初始化如何影响 3DGS 质量的理解。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的初始化优化，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS initialization optimization, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, point cloud upsampling, depth-guided point lifting, Mip-NeRF360, Replica dataset

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a technique for creating and rendering 3D scenes, however its performance depends heavily on the quality of initial seed points. To improve 3DGS initialization, this study presents and evaluates several point cloud upsampling approaches: linear interpolation, triangular interpolation, spline-based surface reconstruction, moving least squares surface fitting, and Voronoi-based point generation. Additionally, this research introduces a depth-guided point lifting method that leverages depth maps to maintain geometric consistency with Structure-from-Motion (SfM) reconstructions. Through extensive experiments on the Mip-NeRF360 and Replica datasets, the proposed methods demonstrate improvements in reconstruction quality across diverse scene types. Results indicate that different upsampling strategies excel in different scenarios: surface reconstruction methods perform better with organic, detailed scenes, while simpler interpolation approaches are more suited for scenes dominated by piecewise-smooth geometries. In comparison, the depth-guided approach shows promise for adding geometry-aware points across the entire scene, importantly in texture-less regions. These findings, which provide preliminary practical guidelines for selecting appropriate upsampling methods based on scene characteristics and computational constraints, advances the understanding of how point cloud initialization affects 3DGS quality.

</details>


### [78] [Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Brain MRI Synthesis with Modality and Metadata Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00689)
*Muhammad Nabi Yasinzai, Remika Mito, Mangor Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (41.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM)，结合小波融合VAE (WF-VAE) 潜空间压缩器与条件3D U-Net扩散模型，通过显式的模态和元数据条件控制，实现多模态脑部MRI的合成，在分布对齐指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态MRI数据中不同模态覆盖不均，且跨站点、扫描仪和采集协议的异质性以及元数据稀疏问题限制了大规模神经影像数据集的应用。现有MRI合成方法多针对窄模态集或同质队列，难以推广到大规模混合数据集。

Method: 提出Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM)：1) 使用Wavelet-Fusion VAE (WF-VAE) 将3D MRI体素压缩到潜空间；2) 在潜空间中训练条件3D U-Net扩散模型，通过显式的模态标签和元数据（如年龄、性别、站点等）进行条件控制；3) 利用小波变换融合实现高效潜空间压缩。

Result: WFDM在分布对齐指标上优于现有合成MRI生成器，能够生成高质量的3D多模态脑部MRI，有效缓解模态覆盖不均问题。

Conclusion: WFDM通过小波融合潜空间压缩与显式条件控制，为大规模异质神经影像数据集中的模态不平衡问题提供了有效的合成数据增强方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于小波融合VAE和条件扩散模型的3D脑部MRI合成方法，属于3D医学图像生成。虽然现有[3D 生成与重建]主要面向物体/场景，但3D体素生成（如MRI合成）在广义上属于3D生成范畴，且该方向是现有最接近的CANONICAL subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical MRI synthesis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes a negative keyword (medical diagnosis). Signals: medical imaging, brain MRI synthesis, wavelet fusion, latent diffusion, modality conditioning

Abstract: Multimodal MRI provides complementary information for neuroimaging analysis, where different imaging modalities capture distinct anatomical, tissue, and pathological features that support the development and evaluation of downstream AI applications. Although large-scale structural MRI resources are increasingly available, their modality coverage is often uneven across public and pooled neuroimaging datasets. This uneven modality coverage is further complicated by heterogeneity across sites, scanners, and acquisition protocols, as well as demographic and clinical variables that are often sparse, inconsistently recorded, or unavailable across studies. Synthetic MRI generation can help address this imbalance by synthesizing target-modality volumes for dataset augmentation and controlled synthetic cohort creation. However, many existing MRI synthesis approaches are trained on narrow modality sets or relatively homogeneous cohorts, limiting their applicability to large pooled neuroimaging resources where modality availability, acquisition protocols, and metadata coverage vary substantially across datasets. Diffusion models have become an attractive approach for MRI synthesis because of their strong sample fidelity and diversity, but sampling directly in 3D voxel space is computationally expensive and slow at inference. Latent diffusion improves practicality by synthesizing MRI in a learned, 3D latent space, although generation quality depends on the autoencoder's reconstruction fidelity and the resulting latent distribution. Our approach combines a Wavelet-Fusion variational autoencoder (WF-VAE) latent compressor with a conditional 3D U-Net diffusion model trained in the learned latent space using explicit modality and metadata conditioning. Our proposed Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM) achieved the strongest distributional alignment among the evaluated synthetic MRI generators.

</details>

Importance reason: Strong technical contribution in controllable 3D medical CT generation, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: multimodal controllable generation, diffusion transformer, 3D CT generation, text and segmentation prompts, FID improvement 24%

Abstract: Generative models for volumetric medical images have found many applications in medical imaging, ranging from data augmentation to serving as priors for inverse problems. For these applications, generating high-resolution 3D images with strong controllability is essential but remains highly challenging. Existing approaches typically control generation either through radiology reports used as text prompts or through full image segmentation. While text-based prompting is flexible, it provides limited spatial control over the location, shape, and boundary of abnormalities. In contrast, segmentation-based methods receive precise spatial guidance but are restrictive in requiring full-organ annotations. In this work, we propose a flexible multimodal framework for controllable volumetric image generation that supports input from radiology reports and segmentation prompts (both optional). Our approach allows users to provide segmentation of a specific anatomy or abnormality without requiring full-organ annotations. The semantic meaning of the segmentation mask is specified through an accompanying text description, resulting in a highly flexible and scalable conditioning mechanism. We develop a memory-efficient architecture based on a modified diffusion transformer that jointly processes image and segmentation tokens. The model further incorporates gated attention to effectively attend to long radiology reports. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art perceptual and semantic scores (e.g., 24% relative improvement in mean FID), generates high-resolution anatomically consistent CT volumes, and improves data efficiency when used for data augmentation. Radiologists' evaluation further confirms strong alignment between generated and real medical images.

</details>

Method: 提出 DeblurNVS 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 几何潜扩散恢复阶段，从模糊输入中恢复中间几何表征（如多视角几何线索）；(2) 目标视角合成阶段，将恢复的表征与目标相机信息结合，通过扩散模型合成清晰的新视角 RGB 图像。同时构建了基于 DL3DV-10K 的运动模糊 NVS 数据集用于训练。

Result: 在合成运动模糊基准上优于现有基线方法，并能泛化到真实运动模糊场景，生成感知上更清晰、结构更稳定的新视角，同时避免了昂贵的逐场景优化。

Conclusion: DeblurNVS 有效解决了从稀疏运动模糊图像进行新视角合成的难题，通过几何潜扩散实现了无需逐场景优化的高质量泛化合成，为实际场景中的 NVS 提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从稀疏运动模糊图像进行新视角合成（NVS），属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel view synthesis from blur is a niche application, not aligning with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D generation, motion blur, novel view synthesis, diffusion model, sparse views

Abstract: Novel view synthesis (NVS) is a fundamental problem in computer vision and graphics. Recent advances in neural radiance fields (NeRF), 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), and generative view synthesis have substantially improved its quality. Yet most methods still rely on clean observations, where image structures and cross-view geometric cues are well preserved. Motion blur breaks this assumption by corrupting local details and weakening multi-view correspondences. Such blur commonly arises from camera shake, scene motion, or finite exposure in practical capture. Blur-aware NVS methods address this degradation by modeling image formation, but their reliance on costly per-scene optimization limits efficient and generalizable sparse-view synthesis. To address this, we propose DeblurNVS, a novel framework for synthesizing high-fidelity novel views directly from sparse motion-blurred images, without requiring per-scene optimization. DeblurNVS restores the intermediate geometric representations needed for multi-view reasoning, enabling blurred inputs to recover reliable structure and correspondence cues. The restored representations are then combined with target camera information to synthesize the target-view representation and reconstruct a sharp RGB novel view. To enable the large-scale training, we construct a motion-blurred NVS dataset from DL3DV-10K using interpolation-based finite-exposure blur synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeblurNVS outperforms existing baselines on synthetic motion-blur benchmarks and generalizes to real motion-blurred scenes, producing perceptually sharper and structurally more stable novel views while avoiding costly per-scene optimization. Project page: .

</details>


### [81] [Splatshot: 3D Face Avatar Generation from a Single Unconstrained Photo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01493)
*Hao Liang, Zhixuan Ge, Soumendu Majee, Joanna Li, Ashok Veeraraghavan, Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatShot 提出一种无需训练的方法，将3DGS显式几何表示与扩散模型2D先验在去噪过程中直接耦合，通过每步3D反馈循环从单张非约束照片生成具有多视角一致性和身份保真度的3D人脸头像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3DGS模型在分布外输入上退化，而预训练扩散模型虽能生成高保真图像但缺乏多视角一致性。现有方法无法同时保证几何一致性和照片真实感，亟需一种能结合两者优势的框架。

Method: 给定基础3DGS人脸模型和单张参考图像，SplatShot在去噪过程中联合处理所有目标视角。每步从含噪潜变量预测干净图像，将多视角预测结果重新拟合3DGS，然后将3DGS重渲染与2D预测之间的光度差异反向传播到噪声估计中，引导采样轨迹朝向严格3D一致且身份保真的输出。

Result: 在多种非约束真实图像上的实验表明，SplatShot在身份保持、照片真实感和多视角一致性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式3D表示与2D扩散先验在去噪过程中直接耦合是一种有效的训练-free范式，能够从单张非约束照片生成高质量的3D人脸头像，兼具几何一致性和照片真实感。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张照片生成3D人脸头像，核心方法基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)与扩散模型结合，属于3D生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel training-free coupling of 3DGS and diffusion for 3D face avatars, but outside user's priority subtopics and directions. Signals: 3D face avatar, single image reconstruction, training-free, 3DGS + diffusion, multi-view consistency

Abstract: Reconstructing a photorealistic 3D face avatar from a single unconstrained photograph is challenging: feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) models degrade on out-of-distribution inputs, while pretrained diffusion models produce high-fidelity images but lack multi-view consistency. We observe that these paradigms are fundamentally complementary: explicit 3D representations guarantee geometric consistency, whereas 2D diffusion priors ensure photorealism. Building on this, we propose SplatShot, a training-free framework that couples these representations directly within the denoising process. Given a base 3DGS face model and a single reference image, we jointly denoise all target views using a per-step 3D feedback loop. At each timestep, we predict clean images from the noisy latents, refit the 3DGS to these multi-view predictions, and back-propagate the photometric discrepancy between the 3DGS re-renderings and 2D predictions into the noise estimate. This steers the sampling trajectory toward strictly 3D-coherent, identity-faithful outputs. Experiments on diverse in-the-wild images demonstrate that SplatShot produces 3D avatars with superior identity preservation, photorealism, and multi-view consistency.

</details>

*Yibin Zhao, Yihan Pan, Jun Nan, Wenli Yang, Liwei Chen, Jianjun Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅方法大多需要精确相机参数和逐场景优化，前馈方法使用像素对齐的高斯原语常出现伪影和非均匀原语问题。本文旨在设计一种无需逐场景优化、能利用预训练视觉基础模型的前馈3D重建方法。

Method: VG²GT 采用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）提取特征，引入多尺度可微分体素模块增强几何理解，从体素特征中直接分裂和回归高斯原语参数。训练时通过随机实体体积渲染监督深度图，保持VFM完全冻结，实现几何精确的高斯场景重建。

Result: 在DTU、Replica、TAT和ScanNet数据集上，VG²GT 在3D重建和新颖视角合成任务中均优于当前最先进方法，同时显著降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: VG²GT 通过体素-高斯泼溅与冻结视觉基础模型的结合，实现了高效、几何精确的前馈3D场景重建，且可无缝集成到任何基于patch特征的VFM中，为3D重建提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于体素-高斯泼溅的3D场景重建与新颖视角合成方法，属于3D生成与重建方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel voxel-Gaussian method for 3D reconstruction, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive/diffusion generation focus. Signals: 3D reconstruction, Gaussian splatting, visual foundation model, voxel module, depth supervision

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has shown strong potential for 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. However, most existing methods require accurate camera parameters and per-scene optimization, while feed-forward methods with pixel-aligned Gaussian primitives often suffer from artifacts and non-uniform primitives. In this paper, we propose $\text{VG}^2$GT, a Voxel-Gaussian Splatting Visual Geometry-Grounded Transformer. $\text{VG}^2$GT leverages a frozen pretrained visual foundation model (VFM), incorporates a multi-scale differentiable voxel module to enhance geometric understanding, and directly splits and regresses Gaussian primitive parameters from voxel features. During training, depth maps are supervised through stochastic solid volume rendering, enabling geometrically accurate Gaussian scene reconstruction while keeping the visual foundation model fully frozen. This design enables $\text{VG}^2$GT to be seamlessly plugged into any patch-feature-based VFM, while substantially reducing the required training cost. $\text{VG}^2$GT outperforms current state-of-the-art methods on widely used DTU, Replica, TAT, and ScanNet datasets.

</details>

*Can Zhang, Gim Hee Lee*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级物体铰接估计方法依赖密集监督、多帧输入或CAD模板，难以解耦几何与铰接结构，也无法恢复显式关节参数。本文旨在从单张3D观测中自监督地完成这一任务。

Method: 1) 使用SE(3)等变向量神经元自编码器消除全局姿态，将不同实例对齐到共享规范空间；2) 设计关节感知混合蒙皮模块建模部件运动；3) 通过观测形状与规范形状之间的循环重建以及跨空间对齐（可学习规范模板解耦共享类别几何与实例残差形状）来学习表征。

Result: 在合成和真实铰接物体数据集上，SCAPO 恢复了一致的部件结构和准确的关节参数，在所有自监督基线中表现最优。

Conclusion: SCAPO 证明了无需监督即可从单张3D观测中有效估计类别级铰接物体姿态与关节参数，为自监督3D物体理解提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单张RGB-D观测中估计类别级铰接物体的姿态、关节参数与部件分割，属于3D物体重建与理解范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D articulated pose estimation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Self-supervised 3D pose estimation, SE(3)-equivariant autoencoder, category-level articulation, single RGB-D observation, no direct link to user's priority topics

Abstract: Existing methods for category-level object articulation from a single 3D observation often rely on dense supervision, multi-frame inputs, or CAD templates, and still struggle to disentangle geometry from articulation or to recover explicit joint parameters. We propose SCAPO, a self-supervised framework that estimates canonical geometry, rigid part segmentation, and joint pivots, axes, and articulation states from a single RGB-D observation without ground-truth labels or category-specific models. Our SCAPO first uses an SE(3)-equivariant vector-neuron autoencoder to factor out global pose and align diverse instances into a shared canonical space. On this aligned shape, a joint-aware blend-skinning module is then designed to model part motion. We learn this representation through cycle reconstruction between observed and canonical shapes and cross-space alignment with a learnable canonical template that decouples shared category geometry from instance-specific residual shape. Experiments on synthetic and real articulated-object datasets show that our SCAPO recovers consistent part structure and accurate articulation parameters and outperforms all self-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Fast and Lightweight Novel View Synthesis with Differentiable Multiplane Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02068)
*Kaidi Zhang, Guanxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速轻量的新视角合成方法，基于可微 Multiplane Image (MPI) 表示，利用视觉基础模型预测的点图进行几何初始化，并引入一步扩散模型处理空洞和伪影，在保持竞争力的同时比 3DGS 方法快 30.7%、模型大小仅为其 14.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NeRF 和 3DGS 等方法在渲染速度、模型大小和稀疏视角下表现不佳，且前馈式 3DGS 重建会产生大量高斯体，难以部署在移动设备上。作者希望探索一种更轻量、快速且能在稀疏视角下有效工作的新视角合成方案。

Method: 重新审视 Multiplane Image (MPI) 表示，使用紧凑的平面层集合表示场景；利用视觉基础模型（如 DPT）预测的点图进行可靠的几何初始化；通过可微优化对 MPI 进行端到端优化；引入一步扩散模型（one-step diffusion）参与 MPI 的可微优化和渲染后处理，以填补稀疏初始化产生的空洞和伪影。

Result: 在正面视角场景下，该方法相比代表性 3DGS 方法速度快 30.7%，模型大小仅为其 14.8%，同时合成质量具有竞争力。在稀疏视角条件下也能取得较好效果。

Conclusion: 可微 MPI 结合视觉基础模型几何初始化与一步扩散增强，是一种高效、轻量且适用于稀疏视角的新视角合成方案，为移动端部署提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于可微 Multiplane Image (MPI) 的新视角合成方法，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel view synthesis with MPI is not a priority subtopic, and the method lacks strong connection to continuous language models or video generation. Signals: fast lightweight novel view synthesis, differentiable MPI, one-step diffusion, sparse-view, 3DGS comparison

Abstract: Recently, novel view synthesis has witnessed remarkable progress, with mainstream methods such as Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) delivering impressive results. However, these approaches often struggle to balance rendering speed and model size, and their optimization-based training can be highly time-consuming. Furthermore, they typically rely on dense observations, often failing to produce satisfactory results under sparse-view conditions. Although feed-forward reconstruction significantly reduces the optimization time of 3DGS, its pixel-aligned formulation generates millions of Gaussians from a single image, severely limiting its practical deployment on mobile devices. To address these limitations, we revisit the Multiplane Image(MPI) representation, which represents scenes using a compact set of planar layers for efficient novel view synthesis. Leveraging recent advances in visual foundation models, we utilize predicted point maps for reliable geometric initialization, followed by differentiable optimization. To address the issues of holes and artifacts in sparsely initialized MPI, we introduce one-step diffusion, which participates in both the differentiable optimization of MPI and the postprocessing of rendering results. Compared with a representative GS-based method, our approach is 30.7% faster and uses only 14.8% of its model size, while achieving competitive synthesis quality on front-view scenarios

</details>


### [85] [VEDAL: Variational Error-Driven Asynchronous Learning for 3D Gaussian Splatting Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02346)
*Aoduo Li, Jiancheng Li, Huan Ye, Hongjian Xu, Shiting Wu, Xiujun Zhang, Zimeng Li, Xuhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (64.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VEDAL 提出一种基于变分自由能最小化的 3DGS 剪枝框架，通过预测误差门控机制和变分不确定性头实现异步剪枝，在 Mip-NeRF 360 等数据集上达到 5.2x 压缩率，PSNR 仅下降 0.31 dB，保持 185 FPS 实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 虽然在新视角合成上效果优异且能实时渲染，但由于需要数百万个高斯原语，内存消耗过大。现有剪枝方法依赖启发式重要性分数或同步批量更新，导致压缩效果次优且训练不稳定。因此需要一种更 principled 的剪枝框架。

Method: 将高斯剪枝建模为变分自由能最小化问题，提出：(1) 预测误差门控机制，基于每个高斯的重建不确定性异步触发剪枝；(2) 变分不确定性头，将剪枝决策建模为具有可学习先验的潜变量。自由能目标函数通过信息论视角自然平衡重建保真度与模型复杂度。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks&Temples 和 Deep Blending 数据集上，VEDAL 实现 5.2x 压缩率，PSNR 仅下降 0.31 dB；相比 PUP 3D-GS 在更高压缩率下 PSNR 提升 +0.05 dB，相比 LightGaussian 在相当质量下 PSNR 提升 +0.35 dB，同时保持 185 FPS 实时渲染。

Conclusion: VEDAL 通过变分自由能框架为 3DGS 剪枝提供了一种理论驱动的解决方案，在压缩率和渲染质量之间取得了更好的平衡，验证了将剪枝视为概率推断问题的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的剪枝压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖 3DGS、NeRF 等神经渲染技术。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS pruning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3DGS compression, variational free energy, asynchronous pruning, real-time rendering, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves remarkable novel view synthesis quality with real-time rendering, yet suffers from excessive memory consumption due to millions of Gaussian primitives. Existing pruning methods rely on heuristic importance scores or synchronous batch updates, leading to suboptimal compression and training instability. We propose VEDAL, a principled framework that formulates Gaussian pruning as variational free energy minimization. Our approach introduces (1) a prediction-error gating mechanism that asynchronously activates pruning based on per-Gaussian reconstruction uncertainty, and (2) a variational uncertainty head that models pruning decisions as latent variables with learnable priors. The free energy objective naturally balances reconstruction fidelity against model complexity through an information-theoretic lens. Extensive experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks&Temples, and Deep Blending demonstrate that VEDAL achieves 5.2x compression with only 0.31 dB PSNR drop, outperforming PUP 3D-GS by +0.05 dB at a higher compression ratio and LightGaussian by +0.35 dB at comparable quality, while maintaining real-time rendering at 185 FPS.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 人体虚拟人方法在真实感和泛化性上难以兼得，主要受限于高质量多样化 3D 人体数据的稀缺性。本文旨在构建一个既逼真又通用的快速 3D 人体建模方案。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展数据管线：利用现有可绑定资产进行多样化姿态动画，并结合多视角人体捕捉数据进行拟合，生成 10 万资产的大规模训练数据。2) 采用前馈式 token 条件化建模框架：输入图像和估计的简化 SMPL 网格，两者编码为紧凑 token 表示，通过交叉注意力融合作为条件信号，构建 triplane 3D 虚拟人表征，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在多个基准上，HumanNOVA 在定量和定性指标上均优于现有方法，对多样化输入图像条件（不同姿态、遮挡、光照等）表现出良好的鲁棒性，推理时间小于 1 秒。

Conclusion: HumanNOVA 通过大规模数据生成管线与前馈式 token 条件化架构，实现了从单张图像快速生成逼真且通用的 3D 人体虚拟人，为 3D 人体建模提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张RGB图像快速生成逼真的3D人体虚拟人，属于3D生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 3D avatar generation but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal language models or video generation. Signals: 3D human avatar, single image to 3D, feed-forward triplane, 100k asset pipeline, fast inference <1s

Abstract: In this paper, we present HumanNOVA, a photorealistic, universal, and rapid model for generating 3D human avatars from a single RGB image. Achieving both photorealism and generalization is challenging due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality 3D human data. To address this, we build a scalable data generation pipeline that follows two strategies. The first one is to leverage existing rigged assets and animate them with extensive poses from daily life. The second strategy is to utilize existing multi-camera captures of humans and employ fitting to generate more diverse views for training. These two strategies enable us to scale up to 100k assets, significantly enhancing both the quantity and the diversity of data for robust model training. In terms of the architecture, HumanNOVA adopts a feed-forward, token-conditioned avatar modeling framework that allows fast inference in less than one second and requires no test-time optimization. Given an input image and an estimated simplified human mesh (SMPL) without detailed geometry or appearance, the model first encodes both inputs into compact token representations. These tokens then act as conditioning signals and are fused through cross-attention to construct a triplane-based 3D avatar representation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach, both quantitatively and qualitatively, as well as its robustness under diverse input image conditions. Project page at .

</details>

### [87] [(HB-ARFM) History-Bootstrapped Flow Matching for Inverse Boiling Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00349)
*Xianwei Zou, Sheikh Md Shakeel Hassan, Arthur Feeney, Aparna Chandramowlishwaran*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CE

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出历史引导自回归流匹配（HB-ARFM）方法，用于从部分观测中重建时空场。通过观测历史引导初始重建，再自回归地传播重建结果，解决了部分观测下逆问题的非马尔可夫后验难题。在沸腾动力学重建任务中，HB-ARFM 能从界面几何与运动恢复完整的速度场和温度场，优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分观测重建时空场是科学推理中的基本问题（如从卫星数据推断大气状态、从成像恢复流体状态）。当观测不完整时，逆问题本质上是病态的：即使底层 PDE 动力学在全状态上是马尔可夫的，部分观测算子也会导致非马尔可夫后验，无法从单个时间步解决。现有方法难以处理这种观测稀疏性带来的歧义性。

Method: 提出历史引导自回归流匹配（HB-ARFM）：(1) 利用观测历史通过条件流匹配引导初始重建，减少歧义；(2) 将同一条件传输模型自回归地应用于后续时间步，同时以新观测和过去预测为条件，将重建向前传播。该方法结合了流匹配的生成能力和自回归的时序传播机制。

Result: 在沸腾动力学重建的两个逆任务（不同观测稀疏度）上，HB-ARFM 能够生成物理和时间上有效的重建结果，而其他模型在这些任务上失败。实验表明，历史引导有效缓解了部分观测带来的歧义性，自回归传播保证了时序一致性。

Conclusion: HB-ARFM 为部分观测下的时空逆问题重建提供了一种有效的生成式框架，结合历史引导流匹配与自回归传播，能够处理非马尔可夫后验并产生物理一致的重建。该方法在科学计算和工程应用中具有广泛潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从部分观测（界面几何与运动）重建沸腾动力学中的完整速度场和温度场，属于动态物理场的时空重建问题。这与 [CANONICAL] 的 "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）方向相关，因为涉及动态场景/场的重建与生成。虽然更偏向科学计算中的逆问题重建，但核心是动态时空场的生成式重建，与现有 subtopic 合理匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on scientific inverse reconstruction for boiling dynamics, which is far from the user's priority subtopics in continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: flow matching for inverse reconstruction, autoregressive propagation, scientific simulation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Reconstructing spatiotemporal fields from partial observations is fundamental to scientific inference, from inferring atmospheric states from satellite data to recovering fluid states from imaging. When observations are incomplete, the inverse problem is fundamentally ill-posed: even when the underlying PDE dynamics are Markovian in the full state, partial observation operators induce a non-Markovian posterior that cannot be resolved from a single timestep. We propose a history-bootstrapped autoregressive flow matching (HB-ARFM) for spatiotemporal inverse reconstruction under partial observability. Observation history bootstraps the initial reconstruction via conditional flow matching, reducing ambiguities. The same conditional transport model is then applied autoregressively, conditioning on both new observations and past predictions to propagate the reconstruction forward in time. We evaluate the method on boiling dynamics reconstruction, recovering full velocity and temperature fields from interface geometry and motion. Across two inverse tasks with varying observation sparsity, HB-ARFM produces physically and temporally valid reconstructions where other models fail.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Static Gaussians: An Empirical Investigation of Architectural Paradigms for Dynamic 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00452)
*Adrian Ramlal, John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对动态3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法进行了系统分析，将其分为结构引导范式（使用变形场、规范空间等辅助表示）和高斯中心范式（通过连续函数或4D表示将动态直接编码到基元中），并在D-NeRF基准上评估了代表性方法，揭示了重建质量/紧凑性与渲染速度之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景重建是3D视觉的重要方向，3DGS方法在静态场景中取得了显著成功，但动态场景下不同方法的设计范式（结构引导 vs 高斯中心）之间的权衡尚未被系统理解，缺乏全面的实证比较来指导未来研究。

Method: 将动态3DGS方法分为两类：1）结构引导方法——使用辅助表示（变形场、规范空间、网格）建模时序变化；2）高斯中心方法——通过连续函数或4D表示将动态直接编码到高斯基元中。在D-NeRF基准上对两类方法的代表性工作进行定量评估，比较重建保真度、模型大小和渲染速度。

Result: 结构引导方法在重建保真度和模型紧凑性上更优；高斯中心方法渲染速度显著更高，可实现实时性能，但质量波动较大且存储开销可能更大。

Conclusion: 动态3DGS存在重建质量/紧凑性与渲染速度之间的根本权衡，结构引导方法适合质量优先场景，高斯中心方法适合实时应用，为未来动态场景重建研究提供了方向性指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态3D场景重建，属于多模态生成方向下的4D与动态场景生成（dynamic_4d_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，直接对应动态3DGS方法分析。

Importance reason: Systematic analysis of dynamic 3DGS paradigms, but tangential to user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Dynamic 3D scene reconstruction, 3D Gaussian Splatting, structure-guided vs gaussian-centric, reconstruction quality vs rendering speed trade-off, D-NeRF benchmark

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a compelling approach for representing evolving environments, yet understanding trade-offs between methodologies remains crucial. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of dynamic 3DGS methods, categorizing them into two paradigms: structure-guided methods employing auxiliary representations (deformation fields, canonical spaces, grids) to model temporal changes, and gaussian-centric methods encoding dynamics directly into primitives via continuous functions or 4D representations. We evaluate representative methods from both paradigms on the D-NeRF benchmark. Our findings reveal that structure-guided methods achieve superior reconstruction fidelity and compact model sizes, while gaussian-centric approaches demonstrate significantly higher rendering speeds enabling real-time performance, though with greater quality variability and potentially substantial storage overhead. This analysis highlights a fundamental trade-off between reconstruction quality/compactness versus rendering speed, providing insights to guide future research and application development in dynamic scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [89] [Not All Points Are Equal: Uncertainty-Aware 4D LiDAR Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02510)
*Xiang Xu, Alan Liang, Youquan Liu, Xian Sun, Linfeng Li, Lingdong Kong, Ziwei Liu, Qingshan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: U4D 提出了一种不确定性感知的 4D LiDAR 场景合成框架，通过 Shannon 熵从预训练分割器导出逐点不确定性图，采用"从难到易"的两阶段扩散策略（先无条件生成高熵区域，再条件补全剩余区域），并设计 MoST 模块保持跨帧时空一致性，在 nuScenes 和 SemanticKITTI 上取得了 SOTA 场景保真度和时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D LiDAR 场景生成方法对所有空间区域采用均匀建模能力，忽略了单次扫描中不同区域的感知难度差异巨大——远处表面、遮挡边界和小物体具有远高于良好观测结构的不确定性。作者希望利用空间不确定性来引导生成过程，优先处理高不确定性区域。

Method: U4D 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 从预训练分割器计算 Shannon 熵得到逐点不确定性图；(2) 两阶段扩散生成——先无条件扩散阶段合成高熵区域（精确几何），再条件补全阶段利用已生成结构作为先验填充剩余区域；(3) MoST（Mixture of Spatio-Temporal）模块动态平衡空间细节与时序连续性，维持跨帧一致性。

Result: 在 nuScenes 和 SemanticKITTI 数据集上的实验表明，U4D 在场景保真度、时序一致性和下游任务性能上均达到 SOTA，优于现有 4D LiDAR 生成方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模空间不确定性并采用"从难到易"的生成调度，可以显著提升 4D LiDAR 场景合成的质量，为具身 AI 提供更逼真的 4D 世界。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 U4D 框架，从 LiDAR 序列生成 4D 场景（动态 3D 场景），属于多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）。同时，4D 场景合成也涉及世界模型方向中的空间 3D/4D 世界建模，因此匹配 world_model 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on 4D LiDAR scene synthesis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 4D LiDAR scene synthesis, uncertainty-aware diffusion, "hard-to-easy" generation, MoST for temporal consistency, nuScenes/SemanticKITTI benchmarks

Abstract: Constructing faithful 4D worlds from LiDAR-acquired sequences is crucial for embodied AI, yet current generative frameworks apply uniform modeling capacity across all spatial regions. This ignores that perceptual difficulty varies dramatically within a single scan: distant surfaces, occluded boundaries, and small-scale objects carry far higher uncertainty than well-observed structures. We present U4D, a new framework that explicitly leverages spatial uncertainty to guide LiDAR scene generation in a "hard-to-easy" schedule. U4D derives per-point uncertainty maps via Shannon Entropy from a pretrained segmentor, then applies an unconditional diffusion stage to synthesize high-entropy areas with precise geometry, followed by a conditional completion stage that fills in the remaining regions using these structures as priors. A MoST (Mixture of Spatio-Temporal) block further maintains cross-frame coherence by dynamically balancing spatial detail and temporal continuity. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and SemanticKITTI demonstrate state-of-the-art scene fidelity, temporal consistency, and downstream performance.

</details>


### [90] [Learning Neural Deformation Representation for 4D Dynamic Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01021)
*Gyojin Han, Jiwan Hur, Jaehyun Choi, Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的神经变形表示，结合条件神经有符号距离场，设计了解耦形状与运动潜空间的 4D 表示架构，并基于扩散模型实现 4D 动态形状的无条件/条件生成与运动重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 动态形状生成工作（如 HyperDiffusion）存在时序一致性差、渲染速度慢的问题，原因是运动表示未与形状表示解耦。本文旨在弥合这一差距，同时提升生成质量和效率。

Method: 提出一种神经变形表示，通过预测多个部分的蒙皮权重和刚性变换来建模运动；将其与条件神经有符号距离场结合，构建形状与运动潜空间解耦的 4D 表示架构；在此基础上训练扩散模型，利用解耦的形状和运动特征作为数据点进行生成。

Result: 在无条件生成、条件生成和运动重定向实验中，该方法在 4D 动态形状生成上优于先前工作，并展示了多种潜在应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦式神经变形表示有效提升了 4D 动态形状生成的时序一致性和效率，且具有广泛的应用前景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 4D 动态形状生成（3D 物体随时间变形），属于多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation），该子主题明确涵盖动态 3D/4D 场景生成与重建。

Importance reason: Focuses on 4D dynamic shape generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's configured direction weights and boost keywords. Signals: 4D dynamic shape generation, neural deformation representation, diffusion model, motion retargeting, conditional generation

Abstract: Recent developments in 3D shape representation opened new possibilities for generating detailed 3D shapes. Despite these advances, there are few studies dealing with the generation of 4D dynamic shapes that have the form of 3D objects deforming over time. To bridge this gap, we focus on generating 4D dynamic shapes with an emphasis on both generation quality and efficiency in this paper. HyperDiffusion, a previous work on 4D generation, proposed a method of directly generating the weight parameters of 4D occupancy fields but suffered from low temporal consistency and slow rendering speed due to motion representation that is not separated from the shape representation of 4D occupancy fields. Therefore, we propose a new neural deformation representation and combine it with conditional neural signed distance fields to design a 4D representation architecture in which the motion latent space is disentangled from the shape latent space. The proposed deformation representation, which works by predicting skinning weights and rigid transformations for multiple parts, also has advantages over the deformation modules of existing 4D representations in understanding the structure of shapes. In addition, we design a training process of a diffusion model that utilizes the shape and motion features that are extracted by our 4D representation as data points. The results of unconditional generation, conditional generation, and motion retargeting experiments demonstrate that our method not only shows better performance than previous works in 4D dynamic shape generation but also has various potential applications.

</details>


### [91] [Effective Multi-sensor Conditioning for Street-view Novel-view Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01590)
*Zhengfei Kuang, Adam Sun, Liyuan Zhu, Tong Wu, Shengqu Cai, Jonathan Tremblay, Iro Armeni, Ehsan Adeli, Lior Yariv, Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StreetNVS 是一个基于视频扩散模型的街景新视角合成框架，通过多传感器（LiDAR、多相机图像、位姿）联合条件控制，利用参考增强相机注意力模块和两阶段课程训练策略，在稀疏 LiDAR 条件下显著优于现有方法，并能合成极端偏离轨迹的连贯视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有街景新视角合成方法仅利用了车辆多传感器数据中的部分信号（如仅用图像或仅用 LiDAR），当目标轨迹偏离原始驾驶路径时质量严重下降。作者认为这本质上是多传感器融合问题，需要同时利用稀疏 LiDAR 的精确几何、环绕参考图像的密集外观以及相机位姿的几何关联。

Method: 提出 StreetNVS 视频扩散框架：(1) 设计参考增强相机注意力模块（Reference-Enhanced Camera Attention），基于相对射线级位置编码（relative ray-level positional encoding）联合条件化 LiDAR 重投影、环绕视图参考图像和相机位姿；(2) 提出两阶段课程训练策略，逐步让模型适应越来越稀疏的 LiDAR 输入。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 上，StreetNVS 在稀疏 LiDAR 条件下大幅超越 SOTA 基线，性能可媲美使用稠密点云（10-100 倍密度）的方法；还能合成沿极端偏离轨迹（如抬升、变道、拉远、旋转）的连贯视频。

Conclusion: 多传感器融合信号（LiDAR + 多视图图像 + 位姿）能显著提升街景新视角合成的质量和鲁棒性，特别是在稀疏传感器设置和极端轨迹下。该工作为自动驾驶场景的神经渲染提供了更实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究街景新视角合成（novel-view synthesis），属于动态驾驶场景的生成与重建，与 canonical 子主题 "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong in multimodal generation but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: video diffusion, novel-view synthesis, multi-sensor conditioning, street-view, LiDAR

Abstract: Modern vehicle platforms are equipped with a rich sensor suite, including LiDAR, calibrated multi-camera rigs, and accurate ego-motion, that in principle offers strong signal for re-rendering a driving scene from novel viewpoints. A growing line of recent work leverages video diffusion models for this task, using their generative priors to synthesize plausible novel views from sparse vehicle observations. In practice, however, existing methods exploit only a fragment of this signal, and their quality tends to degrade as the target trajectory departs from the recorded driving path. We argue that this is fundamentally a multi-sensor fusion problem: sparse LiDAR reprojections supply accurate but incomplete metric geometry, surround-view reference imagery supplies dense appearance but no metric depth, and camera poses tie the two together across views. We introduce StreetNVS, a video diffusion framework that jointly conditions on all three signals through a Reference-Enhanced Camera Attention module based on a relative ray-level positional encoding. We develop a two-stage curriculum training strategy that gradually exposes the model to increasingly sparse LiDAR. On the Waymo Open Dataset, StreetNVS substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under sparse LiDAR conditioning, matches methods that rely on 10-100 times denser point clouds. We further show capabilities of synthesizing coherent videos along extreme out-of-trajectory paths such as elevation, lane-shift, pullback, and rotation. Our website:

</details>


### [92] [WebSpline: Structure-Informed Splines for Real-Time 3D Gaussians from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02096)
*Jongmin Park, Jeonghwan Yun, Minh-Quan Viet Bui, Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WebSpline 提出了一种结构感知样条（SIS）表示方法，结合结构代理图（SPG），从单目视频中重建动态 3D 高斯场景，实现高保真重建与快速渲染，在 iPhone 和 NVIDIA 数据集上达到 SOTA 效果且渲染速度比第二名快 10 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单目视频进行动态场景重建的方法难以在全局结构一致性与局部细节之间取得平衡，且渲染速度较慢。作者希望提出一种既能保持结构连贯性又能高效渲染的动态 3D 高斯框架。

Method: 提出 WebSpline 框架，核心是结构感知样条（SIS）表示，每个动态高斯轨迹用可学习的立方 Hermite 样条建模，并通过辅助结构代理图（SPG）组织运动结构。两阶段优化：第一阶段从 2D 点轨迹初始化 SPG 并用时序刚性正则化细化；第二阶段从 SPG 初始化 SIS 并在空间和结构邻域约束下优化。推理时仅需计算 SIS 即可获得高斯运动。

Result: 在 iPhone 和 NVIDIA 两个单目动态场景基准上，WebSpline 达到了 SOTA 渲染质量，且在 iPhone 数据集上渲染速度比第二名方法 WorldTree 快 10 倍以上。

Conclusion: WebSpline 通过结构感知样条表示有效平衡了动态场景重建中的全局结构一致性与局部细节，同时实现了实时渲染速度，为单目动态场景重建提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WebSpline，从单目视频重建动态 3D 场景，使用 4D 高斯泼溅（4DGS）框架，属于动态场景生成与重建，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel structure-informed spline representation for dynamic 3D Gaussians, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive/diffusion generation focus. Signals: dynamic 3D gaussians, monocular video reconstruction, real-time rendering, structure proxy graph, spline representation

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular videos remains highly challenging, as existing methods often struggle to balance global structural coherence and local fine-grained details under limited multi-view cues. To address this challenge, we propose WebSpline, a novel dynamic 3D Gaussian framework that enables structurally coherent and high-fidelity reconstruction from monocular videos with fast rendering. The core of WebSpline is the Structure-Informed Spline (SIS) representation, which models each dynamic Gaussian trajectory using a learnable cubic Hermite spline whose motion is structurally organized with an auxiliary Structural Proxy Graph (SPG). The proposed framework is optimized in two stages: (i) in the first stage, the SPG is initialized from 2D point tracks and refined with temporal rigidity regularization to establish structural coherence for moving objects across the sequence; and (ii) in the second stage, the SIS representation is initialized from the refined SPG and optimized under both spatial and structural neighborhood constraints. At inference, Gaussian motion is obtained solely by evaluating the learned SIS, enabling fast rendering. Extensive experiments on the challenging monocular dynamic scene benchmarks, iPhone and NVIDIA, demonstrate that our WebSpline achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while rendering over 10 times faster than WorldTree, the second-best method on the iPhone dataset.

</details>


### [93] [MORPHOS: Autoregressive 4D Generation with Temporal Structured Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02491)
*Minkyung Kwon, Jinhyeok Choi, Youngjin Shin, Jaeyeong Kim, JongMin Lee, Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

TL;DR: MORPHOS 提出了一种自回归框架，通过时序结构化潜变量（T-SLAT）从视频生成动态3D资产，支持网格、3D高斯和辐射场等多种表示，利用因果注意力保证时序一致性，并在多个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3D生成方法通常局限于单一表示（如仅网格或仅3DGS），难以建模拓扑变化，且在长视频上时序一致性差。作者希望提出一个统一的4D表示和自回归框架，能够跨多种表示生成动态3D资产，同时处理拓扑变化并保持长时序一致性。

Method: 提出 Temporal Structured Latents (T-SLAT)，一种统一的4D表示，沿时间维度联合编码几何与外观。基于T-SLAT，MORPHOS 采用因果注意力机制自回归生成动态3D资产，每帧以其历史帧为条件。还提出时序结构增强（temporal-structural augmentation）来缓解自回归生成中的误差累积。

Result: 在多个基准上，MORPHOS 在外观方面取得SOTA，在几何方面取得有竞争力的结果，展示了跨多种表示的优越泛化能力和长时序生成的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MORPHOS 通过统一的4D潜变量表示和自回归框架，有效解决了动态3D生成中表示单一、拓扑变化和时序一致性的挑战，为4D生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从视频生成动态3D资产（4D生成），属于多模态生成方向。现有 canonical subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成 (dynamic_4d_generation)" 完全匹配，直接复用。

Importance reason: Autoregressive 4D generation with temporal structured latents directly advances the user's high-priority continuous multimodal and autoregressive video generation interests. Signals: autoregressive 4D generation, temporal structured latent, causal attention for temporal consistency, unified representation for mesh, 3D Gaussian, radiance field, state-of-the-art appearance

Abstract: We present MORPHOS, a novel autoregressive framework that generates dynamic 3D assets from videos across diverse representations, including meshes, 3D Gaussians, and radiance fields. Existing methods are typically limited to a single representation, struggle to model topological changes, or fail to maintain temporal consistency over long videos. To address these limitations, we introduce the Temporal Structured Latents (T-SLAT), a unified 4D representation that jointly encodes geometry and appearance along the temporal dimension. Leveraging T-SLAT, MORPHOS autoregressively generates dynamic 3D assets via causal attention, conditioning each frame on its preceding history to ensure temporal consistency while handling evolving topologies. We also propose a temporal-structural augmentation to mitigate error accumulation in autoregressive generation. MORPHOS achieves state-of-the-art performance in appearance and competitive results in geometry across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating superior generalization across various representations and robustness in long-horizon generation.

</details>

### [94] [UniVerse: A Unified Modulation Framework for Segmentation-Free,Disentangled Multi-Concept Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00351)
*Quynh Phung, Sandesh Ghimire, Minsi Hu, Chung-Chi Tsai, Jia-Bin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成
