TL;DR: UniVerse 提出一种统一调制框架，在扩散 Transformer 中实现无需分割掩码的多概念个性化生成，能够从复杂场景中解耦并精确提取多个目标概念，在定位精度和视觉保真度上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化视觉理解方法在面对多物体图像时难以定位和提取特定概念，严重依赖分割监督且组合泛化能力差，无法准确解耦和操控单个概念。

Method: UniVerse 在扩散 Transformer 中引入统一调制框架，学习将复杂场景分解为概念特定的表征，并以统一方式组合这些表征，实现无需显式分割掩码的可组合/可分解概念提取与细粒度定位。

Result: 在多个基准上，UniVerse 在定位精度和视觉保真度上显著优于现有最先进方法，能够从杂乱场景中精确提取目标概念。

Conclusion: UniVerse 为更灵活、可解释和个性化的视觉生成与理解铺平了道路，展示了无需分割监督的多概念解耦个性化生成的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniVerse，一种无需分割的多概念个性化生成框架，属于多概念定制化/个性化生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "主体与概念驱动生成" (customization_generation) 高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的统一多模态模型。

Importance reason: Strong on controllable generation and diffusion transformer, but lacks direct alignment with top-priority continuous language/multimodal or world model subtopics. Signals: diffusion transformer, controllable generation, subject-driven generation, multi-concept personalization, segmentation-free

Abstract: Personalized visual understanding has advanced significantly, yet existing approaches struggle to localize and extract specific concepts when input images contain multiple objects. Many prior methods rely heavily on segmentation-based supervision or exhibit poor compositional generalization, limiting their ability to accurately disentangle and manipulate individual concepts. In this work, we propose UniVerse, a Unified Modulation Framework for segmentation-free, disentangled multi-concept personalization in diffusion transformers. Our method allows for composable and decomposable concept extraction, enabling fine-grained localization and representation of target objects without explicit segmentation masks. UniVerse learns to decompose complex scenes into concept-specific representations and then compose them in a unified manner, enabling robust personalization across diverse visual contexts. Through extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, we demonstrate that UniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both localization accuracy and visual fidelity. Qualitative and quantitative results show that our approach can precisely extract target concepts in cluttered scenes, paving the way for more flexible, interpretable, and personalized visual generation and understanding.

</details>


### [95] [Polaris: Scaling Up Instruction-Guided Image Generation Towards Millions of Personalized Style Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01858)
*Zhi-Kai Chen, Jun-Peng Jiang, Jun-Jie Tao, De-Chuan Zhan, Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Polaris 是一个智能检索框架，通过索引超过 6500 个 checkpoint 和 75000 个 adapter，根据用户指令自动检索并集成最相关的预训练模块，实现无需额外训练的可扩展、可控且对齐的个性化风格图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对图像生成模型的个性化需求日益多样化（如特定风格或特征），传统方法依赖微调，成本高且难以扩展。社区已积累了大量微调模块和适配器，但如何系统性地利用这些现有资源来满足新需求仍是一个开放问题。

Method: Polaris 构建了一个包含 6500+ checkpoints 和 75000+ adapters 的大规模模型库索引，基于用户输入和指令检索最相关的组件，并通过智能集成机制将它们对齐用于指令驱动的生成和编辑，整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: Polaris 能够从海量候选模块中准确找到最相关的组件，实现可扩展、可控且与用户指令对齐的个性化图像生成，无需任何额外训练。

Conclusion: Polaris 验证了通过检索和集成现有微调模块库来满足多样化个性化生成需求的可行性，为大规模个性化图像生成提供了一种无需训练的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Polaris，一个智能检索框架，从大量微调模型库（6500+ checkpoints 和 75000+ adapters）中自动选择和集成最相关的模型，以根据用户指令实现个性化风格图像生成。这属于主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）方向，因为核心是面向个性化风格需求的定制化生成，且无需额外训练。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval-based style generation, not directly on priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Instruction-guided generation, Retrieval framework, Personalized style, No additional training, Large-scale module library

Abstract: Users increasingly expect image generation models to quickly adapt to highly diverse and personalized requirements, such as producing images with distinctive styles or characteristics. Traditional approaches rely on fine-tuning, which is costly and difficult to scale. To cope with these limitations, the community has accumulated a growing library of fine-tuned modules and adapters, where each component targets specific generation needs and collectively serves as a foundation for handling new demands. This naturally raises a question: instead of repeatedly training new models, can we systematically exploit this expanding ecosystem to better fulfill user instructions? To this end, we present Polaris, an intelligent retrieval framework that automatically selects and integrates suitable models from the model library based on a user's instructions. The key insight is that harnessing such a massive and heterogeneous pool requires not only finding the most relevant modules among thousands of candidates, but also aligning them effectively for instruction-driven generation and editing. Polaris addresses this challenge by indexing over 6,500 checkpoints and 75,000 adapters, and retrieving the most relevant components given a user's input and instruction. In doing so, it delivers scalable, controllable, and well-aligned generation -- without any additional training.

</details>


### [96] [Equilibrated Diffusion: Frequency-aware Textual Embedding for Equilibrated Image Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02129)
*Liyuan Ma, Xueji Fang, Guo-Jun Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Equilibrated Diffusion，一种频率感知的文本嵌入方法，通过将概念在频率空间中分解为低频（主体内容）和高频（风格）并分别优化，实现图像定制中主体身份与风格的解耦，结合掩码引导扩散和残差参考注意力（RRA）提升主体保真度和文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像定制方法（如 DreamBooth、Textual Inversion）将概念的所有属性（主体、风格、背景）打包到统一嵌入中，导致属性纠缠，难以消除风格和背景等无关干扰，影响定制生成的质量和文本对齐。

Method: 1) 频率分解：利用图像频率分量与语义的固有联系，将概念分解为低频（主体内容）和高频（风格），分别优化独立的嵌入。2) 多频率嵌入合并：合并多频率嵌入以保持模型原有的空间定制能力。3) 掩码引导扩散：限制无关背景变化，增强文本对齐。4) 残差参考注意力（RRA）：插入到空间注意力中，保留主体结构和身份一致性。

Result: 实验表明 Equilibrated Diffusion 在主体保真度和文本遵循度上超过主流基线方法（如 DreamBooth、Textual Inversion、Custom Diffusion 等），验证了频率解耦策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 频率感知的嵌入解耦方法能有效分离图像定制中的主体内容与风格，实现更平衡的定制生成和更一致的文本-视觉匹配，为个性化图像生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于参考概念图像的个性化图像定制生成（image customization），属于主体与概念驱动生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "主体与概念驱动生成"（customization_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on image customization with frequency decomposition, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or video generation. Signals: frequency-aware embedding, subject-style disentanglement, mask-guided diffusion, residual reference attention, image customization

Abstract: Image customization learns target subjects from reference concept images and generates conditioned images per text prompts, mainly modifying styles or backgrounds. Prevailing methods adopt fine-tuning to pack diverse concept attributes into a unified latent embedding, yet entangled attributes hinder elimination of irrelevant disturbances from style and background. To address this issue, we propose Equilibrated Diffusion, a frequency-driven approach that disentangles tangled concept features for balanced customization and consistent text-visual matching. Unlike conventional methods learning full concepts with shared embeddings and unified tuning, our work utilizes the inherent link between image frequency components and semantics: low frequencies represent subject content and high frequencies correspond to styles. We decompose concepts in frequency space and optimize each embedding independently. This separate optimization enables the denoiser to capture style detached from subject identity and generalize better to unseen stylistic prompts. Merging multi-frequency embeddings preserves the model's original spatial customization ability. We further deploy mask-guided diffusion to restrict irrelevant background changes and boost text alignment. Residual Reference Attention (RRA) is inserted into spatial attention to retain subject structure and identity consistency. Experiments prove Equilibrated Diffusion exceeds mainstream baselines on subject fidelity and text adherence, verifying our method's superiority.

</details>


### [97] [Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Reference Conditioning for Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02441)
*Yuheng Chen, Teng Hu, Yuji Wang, Qingdong He, Lizhuang Ma, Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 高优先级 (80.33) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ST-DRC，一种空间-时间解耦的参考条件注入框架，用于身份保持的文生视频生成。通过潜空间上下文特征拼接、TASS-RoPE 位置编码、外观不变性增强和三分支无分类器引导，在轻量级 LTX-2.3 基础上实现了强身份保持、提示对齐和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份保持视频生成方法难以平衡高层语义控制与低层身份保真度，存在身份细节丢失或过度复制参考图像的问题。

Method: 1) 潜空间上下文特征注入：将参考图像经视频 VAE 编码后与噪声视频潜变量拼接，无需额外适配器即可获取丰富低层身份细节。2) TASS-RoPE：时间相邻-空间偏移的 RoPE 方案，参考 token 在时间上靠近视频序列但在空间上偏移，抑制像素级复制捷径。3) 外观不变性参考增强 + 人脸引导身份目标，防止捷径学习并增强身份监督。4) 三分支参考无分类器引导，独立控制文本遵循度和参考保真度。

Result: 在面部身份保持视频生成赛道中排名靠前，实验证明 ST-DRC 在身份保持、提示对齐、时间一致性和视频质量上均取得优异表现，且模型轻量。

Conclusion: 空间-时间解耦的参考条件注入策略能有效平衡身份保真度与语义控制，为身份保持视频生成提供了一种轻量高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究身份保持的视频生成（identity-preserving video generation），属于参考图引导的个性化/主体驱动生成，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "主体与概念驱动生成"（customization_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong identity-preserving video generation with novel spatial-temporal decoupling, but not directly aligned with top-priority continuous/multimodal latent or world model subtopics. Signals: Identity-preserving video generation, spatial-temporal decoupled conditioning, TASS-RoPE position encoding, three-stream CFG, lightweight LTX-2.3 backbone

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation (IPVG) aims to synthesize high-fidelity videos that follow text prompts while faithfully preserving a reference identity. Despite recent progress, existing IPVG methods still struggle to balance high-level semantic control and low-level identity fidelity. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-DRC, an effective Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Reference Conditioning framework for identity-preserving text-to-video generation. At the framework level, ST-DRC performs latent in-context feature injection by encoding the reference image with the video VAE and concatenating it with noisy video latents, enabling rich low-level identity details to be accessed without additional adapters. To separate identity-aware reference retrieval from appearance copying, we introduce TASS-RoPE, a Temporal-Adjacent Spatial-Shifted RoPE scheme that places reference tokens near the video sequence in time but shifts them in space, allowing reference information to flow through spatio-temporal attention while suppressing pixel-level copy-paste shortcuts. To further prevent shortcut learning and strengthen the otherwise diluted identity supervision in the diffusion objective, we combine appearance-invariant reference augmentation with face-guided identity objectives, encouraging the model to preserve identity under variations in color, pose, and layout. At inference time, we introduce a three-stream reference classifier-free guidance strategy that independently controls text adherence and reference fidelity. Experiments demonstrate that ST-DRC achieves strong identity preservation, prompt alignment, temporal consistency, and video quality with a lightweight design built on LTX-2.3. Our method ranks among the top submissions in the facial identity-preserving video generation track, validating the effectiveness of spatial-temporal decoupled reference conditioning.

</details>

### [98] [VDSB-GWSyn: Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge for Controllable and Anatomically Feasible Guidewire Synthesis in Coronary Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00109)
*Haoyuan Tang, Zhuo Zhang, Jialin Li, Shuai Xiao, Jiachen Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (37.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出VDSB-GWSyn框架，基于扩散Schrödinger桥模型在冠脉造影图像上可控合成高保真导丝图像，通过形状先验、掩码约束和SPADE条件生成，有效缓解了标注数据稀缺问题，显著提升了导丝端点定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠脉导丝端点定位是计算机辅助PCI的关键能力，但标注CAG图像稀缺且现有导丝合成模型适应性有限，成为导丝端点定位的主要瓶颈。

Method: VDSB-GWSyn框架包含三个步骤：(1) 形状先验算法学习基本导丝几何形状；(2) 在血管分割掩码约束下生成导丝掩码并输出对应端点坐标；(3) 使用SPADE条件化的扩散Schrödinger桥模型在真实CAG图像上合成逼真导丝样本。

Result: 合成样本在ROI-FID和ROI-KID上表现良好，IPR分数高。合成数据预训练+真实数据微调将MPE从16.01px降至7.71px，PCK@3px从52.63%提升至86.27%。

Conclusion: VDSB-GWSyn有效解决了导丝标注数据稀缺问题，显著提升了导丝端点定位的临床可靠性，其可控设备合成设计理念可推广至其他介入设备感知任务。

Classification reason: 该论文使用扩散Schrödinger桥在冠脉造影图像上可控合成导丝图像，属于医学图像中的可控生成/编辑任务。虽然具体应用是医学图像合成，但其核心是条件图像生成（在真实CAG图像上合成导丝），与"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑、Inpainting等概念高度吻合，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Medical domain paper with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: medical imaging, coronary angiography, guidewire synthesis, diffusion Schrödinger bridge, controllable synthesis

Abstract: Coronary guidewire endpoint localization is a fundamental capability for computer-assisted PCI, and its importance increases as robot-assisted PCI is progressively adopted to reduce operator radiation exposure. However, the scarcity of annotated CAG images with guidewires and the limited adaptability of existing guidewire synthesis models remain key bottlenecks for guidewire endpoint localization. To address this issue, we propose VDSB-GWSyn, a Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge (DSB) model-based framework, enabling synthesis of controllable, high-fidelity guidewire samples under complex anatomical backgrounds. VDSB-GWSyn first uses our shape prior algorithm to learn the basic guidewire geometry. It then generates guidewire masks under constraints imposed by the vessel segmentation masks and outputs the corresponding endpoint coordinates. Finally, it synthesizes realistic guidewire samples on real CAG images using DSB conditioned with SPADE. Experimental results show that the guidewire samples synthesized by VDSB-GWSyn achieve favorable ROI-FID and ROI-KID, as well as high IPR scores. In addition, incorporating our synthesized data for synthetic pre-training followed by real fine-tuning substantially improves downstream guidewire endpoint localization, reducing MPE from 16.01~px to 7.71~px and increasing PCK at 3~px from 52.63\% to 86.27\%, leading to more clinically reliable deployment of robot-assisted guidewire delivery systems. Moreover, the core design philosophy of controllable device synthesis with strict background preservation and anatomical feasibility constraints has the potential to transfer to other interventional device perception tasks where annotated data are scarce.

</details>


### [99] [Real2SAM2Real: Generative 3D Caches as Complementary Context for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00299)
*Jiayi Wu, Haoming Cai, Cornelia Fermuller, Christopher Metzler, Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (71.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Real2SAM2Real 提出利用 SAM3D 等 3D 提升模型提取显式可编辑的 3D 缓存，作为视频扩散模型的几何骨架，通过软空间对齐注入和最小化微调策略，实现对相机轨迹和多实体运动的精确解耦控制，克服了高动态运动和大遮挡下的结构崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在合成高保真视频时，面对大动态运动或复杂遮挡时容易出现结构崩溃，因为现有方法过度依赖隐式扩散先验来生成未观测区域。作者希望引入显式的 3D 几何先验来提供可靠的 3D 感知引导，从而实现对相机轨迹和多实体运动的精确解耦控制。

Method: 1) 利用 3D 提升模型（如 SAM3D）从输入视频中提取前景实体的完整 3D 体积缓存（3D cache），而非仅可见外壳；2) 设计软空间对齐注入（Soft Spatial-Aligned Injection）机制，将 3D 缓存的空间先验注入视频扩散模型；3) 采用最小化微调策略，在保持预训练先验的同时有效利用 3D 引导；4) 使用掩码法线图作为跨模态桥梁，构建无需 3D 真值的数据构建与扰动流水线。

Result: 实验表明，Real2SAM2Real 实现了对相机轨迹和多实体运动的精确解耦控制，在大相机位移和严重遮挡下仍能保持优异的时空一致性，克服了过度依赖扩散先验导致的结构崩溃问题，消除了由结构空洞和错误立面引起的视角歧义以及反射折射带来的误导线索。

Conclusion: 通过将几何与外观解耦，利用生成式 3D 缓存作为互补上下文，Real2SAM2Real 有效提升了视频扩散模型在复杂动态场景下的可控性和一致性，为视频生成中的精确几何控制提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出利用3D缓存（3D cache）为视频扩散模型提供几何先验，实现相机轨迹和多实体运动的解耦控制，属于视频生成中的可控编辑与引导生成，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel 3D cache injection for video diffusion control, but tangential to user's core priorities on continuous models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D cache for video diffusion, camera and motion control, soft spatial alignment, masked normal maps, decoupled geometry and appearance

Abstract: While Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) excel at synthesizing high-fidelity videos, enabling precise camera and scene control remains challenging. Existing methods predominantly rely on implicit diffusion priors to generate unobserved regions, inevitably leading to structural collapse during high-dynamic movements or complex occlusions. To address this challenge, we propose Real2SAM2Real, a framework that leverages 3D lifting models (e.g., SAM3D) to extract an explicitly editable 3D cache, serving as a robust geometric scaffold for the VDM. By capturing the entire 3D volume of foreground entities rather than just their visible shells, this cache injects holistic spatial priors into the VDM, providing dependable 3D-aware guidance for complex scene dynamics. To effectively leverage this 3D guidance while preserving pre-trained priors, we design a Soft Spatial-Aligned Injection mechanism alongside a minimally invasive fine-tuning strategy tailored for VDMs. Furthermore, we employ masked normal maps as a cross-modal bridge to construct a 3D-free data curation and perturbation pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Real2SAM2Real enables precise, decoupled control over both camera trajectories and multi-entity motions. By utilizing the complementary context from generative 3D caches, our framework overcomes typical breakdowns caused by over-reliance on diffusion priors, maintaining exceptional spatiotemporal consistency under large camera shifts and severe occlusions. Crucially, by decoupling geometry from appearance, our VDM-tailored 3D cache eradicates perspective ambiguities caused by structural holes and erroneous facades, as well as misleading cues from reflections and refractions. Project website is available at

</details>


### [100] [VICR: Visual In-Context Restoration for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00704)
*Qichang Zhang, Hailong Wang, Baiang Li, Linhao Wang, Rong Fu, Erkang Cheng, Simon James Fong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (55.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VICR提出了一种基于扩散Transformer（DiT）的视觉上下文恢复框架，将真实图像超分辨率（Real-ISR）重新建模为图像补全任务。通过解耦的视觉先验注入机制（局部线索恢复结构、全局线索保证语义一致性），并引入推理时智能体对退化严重区域的语义提示进行精炼，仅用127M参数即达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式Real-ISR方法通常使用纠缠的条件机制（如将低质量图像直接作为条件输入），导致结构漂移或语义不一致的细节生成。作者希望解决这一问题，提出一种解耦的条件注入方式，将超分辨率任务转化为更可控的图像补全问题。

Method: VICR基于DiT架构，核心创新包括：(1) 解耦的视觉先验注入机制——从低质量图像中提取局部线索（恢复结构、辅助高频细节合成）和全局线索（引导整体生成、促进语义一致性）；(2) 将Real-ISR重新表述为图像补全任务；(3) 对于严重退化区域的模糊语义，引入推理时智能体（inference-time agent），利用低质量输入中的视觉证据精炼语义提示，同时保持模型参数固定。

Result: 在多个Real-ISR基准上，VICR以仅127M可训练参数取得了SOTA性能，在结构保真度和语义一致性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: VICR通过解耦视觉先验注入和推理时语义精炼，有效解决了生成式Real-ISR中的结构漂移和语义不一致问题，验证了将超分辨率建模为图像补全任务的有效性，为真实图像超分辨率提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于DiT的图像超分辨率方法VICR，将Real-ISR建模为图像补全（image completion）任务，属于图像恢复/增强类工作。这与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的图像编辑、inpainting等概念高度相关，因为超分辨率本质上是一种图像恢复/编辑任务，且论文明确将问题表述为image completion。

Importance reason: Novel DiT-based in-context restoration for super-resolution, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive or continuous modeling focus. Signals: Diffusion Transformer, Real-ISR as image completion, decoupled visual prior injection, inference-time agent for semantic refinement, 127M parameters SOTA

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) requires balancing structural fidelity to degraded observations with realistic detail synthesis. However, existing generative Real-ISR methods often rely on entangled conditioning mechanisms, leading to structural drift or semantically inconsistent details. To address this issue, we propose Visual In-Context Restoration (VICR), a Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based framework that formulates Real-ISR as image completion. Specifically, we introduce a decoupled visual prior injection mechanism that derives local and global cues from the low-quality (LQ) image: local cues help recover image structures and support high-frequency detail synthesis, while global cues guide overall generation and promote semantic consistency. For ambiguous regions under severe degradation, VICR employs an inference-time agent to refine semantic prompts using visual evidence from the LQ input while keeping model parameters fixed. Experiments show that VICR achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple Real-ISR benchmarks with only 127M trainable parameters.

</details>


### [101] [COLLAR: Cascaded Object-Level Latent Refinement for High-Fidelity Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00954)
*Xinlong Zhang, Jia Wei, Xiaoyu Zhang, Teng Zhou, Chengyu Lin, Yongchuan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (54.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COLLAR提出了一种无需训练的级联目标级潜空间精炼框架，通过跨尺度语义对齐（CSSA）和循环特征注入（CFI）模块，在扩散Transformer中实现高保真度的目标级条件生成，在COCO-MIG和COCO-POS基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散Transformer在引入深度图、Canny图等结构先验进行目标级条件生成时，常出现视觉伪影，且难以在局部小区域保持精确控制。现有方法缺乏对目标级特征的精细优化机制。

Method: 提出COLLAR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 跨尺度语义对齐（CSSA）模块，通过注意力机制将目标级特征注入扩展视场（FoV）分支，弥合空间-语义差距；(2) 循环特征注入（CFI）模块，利用基于频率的自适应策略，通过背景反馈机制选择性地用上下文对齐的局部信息更新全局骨干网络。扩展FoV分支作为特征优化枢纽，确保目标级特征融入全局生成过程。

Result: 在COCO-MIG和COCO-POS基准上，COLLAR在语义对齐、图像质量和空间保真度方面一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: COLLAR通过级联目标级潜空间精炼和FoV扩展策略，有效解决了扩散Transformer中目标级条件生成的视觉伪影和局部控制精度问题，是一种无需训练的通用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出COLLAR框架，在扩散Transformer中实现目标级别的条件生成控制（如深度图、Canny图引导），属于可控图像生成/编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on object-level control in diffusion transformers, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: training-free framework, object-level conditional generation, diffusion transformer, COCO-MIG benchmark, cascaded latent refinement

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity object-level control in Diffusion Transformers remains a significant challenge despite the introduction of structural priors like depth and Canny maps. Current object-level conditional generation methods frequently suffer from visual artifacts and struggle to maintain precise control over objects within small localized regions. To address these limitations, we propose Cascaded Object-Level Latent Refinement (COLLAR), a training-free framework that progressively optimizes object-level features via the Field-of-View (FoV) expansion. First, we propose the Cross-Scale Semantic Alignment (CSSA) module to address spatial-semantic gaps by injecting object-level features into extended-FoV branches via attention mechanisms. To further optimize these features, the Cyclic Feature Injection (CFI) module introduces a reciprocal background feedback mechanism. It leverages a frequency-based adaptive strategy to selectively update the global backbone with context-aligned local information. Finally, the extended-FoV branch serves as a hub for feature optimization, ensuring that object-level features are integrated into the global generation process without compromising final image quality. Extensive experiments on the COCO-MIG and COCO-POS benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across semantic alignment, image quality, and spatial fidelity.

</details>


### [102] [Decoupled Residual Denoising Diffusion Models for Unified and Data Efficient Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01048)
*Ziyue Lin, Jiahe Hou, Hongyu Xia, Xinrui Xie, Feifei Wang, Yuyin Zhou, Wei Wang, Jiawei Liu, Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦残差去噪扩散模型（DRDD），将扩散过程解耦为两个独立阶段：随机噪声扩散（用于域对齐和流形提升）和确定性残差扩散（学习核心语义映射）。该方法在统一图像到图像翻译任务中表现出色，且数据效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像到图像翻译中，噪声注入除了传统的流形提升作用外，还具有隐式的域对齐（domain harmonization）效果。然而，现有方法将噪声和残差在同一个耦合扩散过程中同时去除，过早地破坏了这种域对齐效应，导致统一I2I翻译效果受限。

Method: DRDD将扩散过程解耦为两个顺序独立的阶段：(1) 随机噪声扩散阶段——仅对目标域图像添加和去除噪声，实现域对齐和流形提升，该阶段仅需无配对的目标域图像训练；(2) 确定性残差扩散阶段——在固定噪声域中学习源域到目标域的语义映射。两个阶段独立训练，推理时串联执行。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明，DRDD与主流扩散模型广泛兼容，在多种I2I翻译任务（如风格迁移、图像修复、超分辨率等）上均取得稳健的统一翻译效果，尤其在有限配对数据场景下显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DRDD通过解耦噪声扩散和残差扩散，有效保留了域对齐效应，简化了统一I2I翻译的学习难度，同时利用无配对数据提升了数据效率，为统一图像翻译提供了一种新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出解耦残差去噪扩散模型（DRDD）用于统一且数据高效的图像到图像翻译（I2I），属于图像编辑/翻译任务。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing），因为I2I翻译是图像编辑的核心子任务。

Importance reason: Novel decoupling of diffusion process for unified I2I translation, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion model, image-to-image translation, domain harmonization, data efficiency, decoupled diffusion

Abstract: We propose Decoupled Residual Denoising Diffusion models (DRDD) for unified and data-efficient image-to-image (I2I) translation. While diffusion models have advanced I2I translation in terms of quality and diversity, we uncover a previously under-explored property in diffusion models. Crucially, beyond its conventional role of manifold lifting (i.e., moving data off low-dimensional manifolds), injecting Gaussian noise facilitates domain harmonization by implicitly aligning feature distributions across domains, a property particularly advantageous for unified I2I translation. However, existing diffusion models prematurely erode this harmonization effect, as noise and residuals are simultaneously removed in a single coupled diffusion process. To address this, DRDD decouples the diffusion process into two sequential and independent diffusion stages: (1) a stochastic noise diffusion for domain harmonization and manifold lifting, and (2) a deterministic residual diffusion that learns the core semantic mapping entirely within the fixed-noise domain. This decoupling preserves harmonization and manifold lifting effects throughout the transformation, substantially simplifying the learning of unified mappings across diverse tasks and domains. Notably, the noise diffusion stage is trained exclusively on abundant, unpaired target-domain images, greatly improving data efficiency. Comprehensive theoretical and empirical analysis demonstrates that DRDD is broadly compatible with mainstream diffusion models and consistently delivers robust, unified I2I translation, even under limited paired data. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [103] [Chameleon: Style-Content Disentangled Framework for Cross-Domain Object Compositing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01079)
*Sukhun Ko, Soo Ye Kim, Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (55.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chameleon，一个两阶段训练框架用于跨域物体合成（cross-domain object compositing）。通过构建首个大规模跨域合成数据集 ChameleonDataset，第一阶段使用联合硬对比学习（JHCL）训练 ChameleonEncoder 以解耦风格与内容表征，第二阶段在扩散 Transformer 中引入时空注意力门控（STAG）自适应调节风格注入，在合成合理性和风格保真度上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像合成方法主要针对同域（如自然图像）场景，跨域合成（前景与背景来自不同域，如卡通物体插入真实照片）缺乏研究。由于缺少大规模配对训练数据，现有方法多采用无训练混合策略，仅能进行色调级对齐，容易产生风格不一致或过度风格化的问题。

Method: (1) 构建 ChameleonDataset：通过可扩展的数据构建流水线生成大规模跨域合成配对数据及评测基准。(2) 第一阶段：提出联合硬对比学习（JHCL）训练 ChameleonEncoder，有效解耦风格与内容表征。(3) 第二阶段：在扩散 Transformer（DiT）中引入时空注意力门控（STAG），自适应地沿空间和时间维度调控来自第一阶段的风格 token 注入，实现有效的风格化合成。

Result: 在跨域合成任务上，Chameleon 在合成合理性和风格保真度两方面均优于现有的域内/跨域合成模型、顺序流水线方法以及商业模型，取得了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过大规模数据构建和两阶段训练框架（内容-风格解耦 + 可控风格注入）可以有效解决跨域物体合成这一挑战性任务，为跨域图像编辑提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究跨域图像合成（cross-domain object compositing），即将前景物体插入背景图像并使其风格与背景匹配，属于图像编辑与合成范畴。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Cross-domain compositing is a niche editing task, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: cross-domain compositing, style-content disentanglement, diffusion transformer, dataset construction, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Image compositing aims to seamlessly insert a foreground object into a background image, and recent advances in diffusion models have significantly enhanced the quality, especially when the foreground and background images come from the same domain (e.g., natural images). However, cross-domain compositing, where the foreground and background come from different domains, is relatively underexplored and remains challenging because the model must preserve the foreground object's identity while stylizing it to match the background domain. Existing cross-domain compositing approaches largely rely on training-free blending and refinement strategies. This is partly due to the lack of large-scale paired datasets for cross-domain compositing, limiting the development of training-based solutions. As a result, they are limited to tone-level alignment and often produce style-inconsistent or overstylized results. To overcome such limitations, we construct ChameleonDataset, the first large-scale training dataset for cross-domain compositing, with a comprehensive evaluation benchmark, built through a scalable data construction pipeline. Building on this, we propose Chameleon, a novel two-stage training-based cross-domain compositing framework. In the first stage, we propose Joint Hard Contrastive Learning (JHCL) to train ChameleonEncoder, which effectively disentangles style and content representations. In the second stage, we introduce Spatio-Temporal Attention Gating (STAG) into a diffusion transformer for effective stylization, adaptively regulating how style tokens from the first-stage encoder are injected across spatial and temporal dimensions. Our method outperforms state-of-the-art in-domain and cross-domain compositing models, sequential pipelines and commercial models, achieving improvements in both compositional plausibility and stylistic fidelity.

</details>


### [104] [Training-free image inversion for one-step diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01380)
*Tao Wu, Senmao Li, Yaxing Wang, Shiqi Yang, Kai Wang, Joost van de Weijer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (70.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TFinv，一种无需训练的框架，用于单步扩散模型的真实图像反演与编辑。通过迭代噪声对齐（iterNA）缩小初始噪声与高斯分布的差距，以及后缀学习（suffL）增强文本-图像对齐，实现了高效精确的图像反演和局部编辑，在 PIE-Bench 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步扩散模型（如 SD-Turbo、LCM）在真实图像反演和编辑方面存在两大挑战：(1) 初始噪声与理想高斯分布之间的差距导致编辑性差；(2) 文本描述与图像表征之间的对齐不足（Caption Gap）。现有方法多依赖多步扩散模型，效率低。

Method: 提出 TFinv 框架，包含两个关键技术：(1) 迭代噪声对齐（iterNA）——通过迭代优化最小化初始噪声与标准高斯分布之间的分布差距；(2) 后缀学习（suffL）——引入可学习的后缀提示 token 来增强文本-图像对齐。此外，提出基于掩码的局部编辑技术以保持背景完整性。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 数据集上的实验表明，TFinv 在单步扩散编辑任务中达到 SOTA 性能，并且在效率上显著优于现有的多步方法。

Conclusion: TFinv 有效解决了单步扩散模型在真实图像反演和编辑中的关键瓶颈，实现了无需额外训练的高效图像编辑，为单步扩散模型的实际应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的单步扩散模型图像反演与编辑框架（TFinv），核心任务是真实图像的反演与编辑，属于图像编辑范畴。因此映射到 canonical 子主题「图像与视频编辑」(visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Focuses on one-step diffusion inversion, which is tangentially related to user's priority on efficient inference but not directly on core continuous language or video generation models. Signals: training-free inversion, one-step diffusion, image editing, iterative noise alignment, suffix learning

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a novel training-free inversion (TFinv) framework for one-step diffusion models,addressing key challenges in real image inversion and editing. We first identify two critical factors hamperingreal-image inversion and editing: (1) Initial Latent Editability, which is related to the distance between theinitial noise and the ideal Gaussian distribution, and (2) Caption Gap, which means the alignment betweentext captions and image representations. Both factors influence inversion efficiency and the editability ofone-step diffusion models. Then, we propose two novel techniques: iterative noise alignment (iterNA), whichminimizes the distribution gap to align with the normal Gaussian distribution, and suffix learning (suffL),which enhances text-to-image caption alignment by introducing learned suffix prompt tokens. These techniquesenable precise inversion of input images into their initial noise representations and facilitate image , we propose a mask-based editing technique for localized edits while preserving backgroundintegrity. Comprehensive experiments on the PIE-Bench dataset validate that our method TFinv not onlyachieves state-of-the-art performance in one-step diffusion editing, but also significantly outperforms existingmultistep approaches in efficiency. The code is available at .

</details>


### [105] [PAI-Studio: Cinematic Video Background Replacement with Camera-Aware Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01399)
*Heyuan Gao, Bangxun Tang, Yiren Song, Guian Fang, Zijian He, Jie Yang, Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (57.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PAI-Studio 提出了一种新的参考条件视频合成任务——电影级视频背景替换。该方法基于 Diffusion Transformer 视频骨干网络，通过双向注意力机制在统一架构中联合建模前景动态与背景参考信息，同时保证前景身份保持、背景场景匹配、全局光照一致性和前景重光照。还构建了 30K 规模的高质量电影/视频数据集。实验表明该方法显著优于现有开源和商业 API 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源系统和商业 API 无法同时保证运动一致的背景生成、高保真前景重光照和前景身份保持，常常导致静态背景、不一致边界和明显的合成伪影。因此需要一种能够同时解决这些挑战的统一方法。

Method: 基于 Diffusion Transformer 视频骨干网络，将问题重新表述为上下文条件生成任务。通过双向注意力机制，模型在统一架构中联合捕获前景动态和背景参考信息。同时构建了一个 30K 规模的高质量电影和在线视频数据集来支持该任务。

Result: 大量评估表明，该方法在运动一致的背景生成、前景重光照和前景身份保持方面显著优于现有开源和商业 API 解决方案。

Conclusion: PAI-Studio 成功解决了电影级视频背景替换中长期存在的挑战，通过统一架构同时实现了运动一致的背景生成、高保真前景重光照和前景身份保持，为视频编辑领域提供了新的有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究参考条件视频背景替换（video background replacement），属于视频编辑任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Video background replacement with camera-aware motion is a niche editing task, not aligned with top priority continuous multimodal or world model directions. Signals: video diffusion transformer, reference-conditioned video generation, foreground-background joint modeling, 30K film dataset, outperforms commercial APIs

Abstract: We present PAI-Studio, a new reference-conditioned video synthesis task that addresses a long-standing challenge in cinematic background replacement: generating dynamic backgrounds aligned with foreground motion while preserving foreground identity, matching reference scene appearance, and achieving globally consistent illumination with realistic foreground relighting. Existing open-source systems and commercial APIs cannot simultaneously ensure motion-consistent background generation, high-fidelity foreground relighting and foreground identity preservation, often resulting in static backgrounds, inconsistent boundaries, and noticeable compositing artifacts. To bridge this gap, we build upon a Diffusion Transformer video backbone and reformulate the problem as an in-context conditional generation task. Through bidirectional attention, our model jointly captures foreground dynamics and background reference information within a unified architecture. We further construct a 30K-scale dataset sourced from high-quality films and online videos to support this task. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing open-source and commercial API solutions.

</details>


### [106] [Hist2Style: Histogram-Guided Stylization with Bilateral Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01819)
*Dekel Galor, Adam Pikielny, Zhoutong Zhang, Ke Wang, Laura Waller, Jiawen Chen, Ilya Chugunov*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.IV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Hist2Style 提出一种基于双边网格的快速边缘感知风格化方法，通过将大图像编辑模型蒸馏为轻量网络，以直方图嵌入作为可解释接口，实现实时、高分辨率、用户可控的照片级颜色与色调风格迁移，同时避免幻觉并保持内容结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型风格迁移方法计算开销大、可能产生幻觉、用户控制有限，不适合高分辨率实时工作流。作者希望设计一种快速、保真、可交互的风格化方法。

Method: 将风格化操作约束为双边空间中的局部仿射变换，利用双边网格实现边缘感知；将大图像编辑模型蒸馏为轻量网络，在大规模监督语料（由语言和视觉语言模型生成）上训练；网络以风格目标的直方图嵌入为条件，提供可解释的用户控制接口。

Result: Hist2Style 在保持内容结构、避免幻觉的同时，支持实时高分辨率照片级风格化，用户可通过调整目标颜色分布交互式控制输出风格。

Conclusion: 双边网格公式结合直方图条件嵌入能够高效实现高质量、可控的照片级风格迁移，为实时图像编辑提供了一种轻量且保真的替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究的是基于直方图引导的双边网格实现照片级风格迁移（photorealistic style transfer），属于图像编辑/风格化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on stylization and image editing, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: stylization, bilateral grid, real-time, high-resolution, user control

Abstract: Photorealistic style transfer aims to match the color and tone of an input image to that of a style target while preserving the content and details of the original scene. Although existing large image models can facilitate these kinds of appearance edits, their high computational demands, potential for hallucinations, and limited user control make them unsuitable for high-resolution, real-time workflows. We introduce Hist2Style, a bilateral-grid formulation for fast, edge-aware stylization that preserves visual fidelity by constraining operations to locally affine transforms in bilateral space. Our model distills a large image editing model into a lightweight network by training on a large supervised corpus generated with language and vision-language models, targeting spatially varying color edits. The network conditions on a histogram-based embedding of the style target to provide an interpretable interface for adjusting the output style by modifying the target color distribution. Overall, Hist2Style maintains content structure by construction, avoids hallucinations, and supports real-time, high-resolution photorealistic stylization with interactive user-controllable color and tone adjustments.

</details>


### [107] [MT-EditFlow: Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Image Editing with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01985)
*Jiahui Huang, Yasi Zhang, Tianyu Chen, Shu Wang, Jianwen Xie, Oscar Leong, Mingyuan Zhou, Nanzhu Wang, Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (88.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MT-EditFlow 是一个基于流匹配（flow matching）的强化学习框架，专为多轮图像编辑设计。它通过多奖励公式和优势融合策略，解决单轮编辑模型在多轮交互中因曝光偏差导致的错误累积问题，显著提升多轮编辑的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令式图像编辑模型主要针对单轮编辑训练，在多轮交互场景中表现不佳。问题根源在于：(1) 单轮失败会导致整个序列失败（all-or-nothing）；(2) 曝光偏差导致编辑错误逐轮累积。因此需要一种面向多轮编辑的优化框架。

Method: 提出 MT-EditFlow 框架，将多轮图像编辑建模为序列决策问题，结合流匹配（flow matching）与强化学习（GRPO 和 NFT）。设计了多奖励公式（multi-reward formulation），包括轮次级聚合策略、VLM 推理模式以平衡奖励偏差与方差，以及优势融合级别以防止奖励黑客。通过将聚合优势广播到整个编辑轨迹，弥合局部规划与全局多轮任务成功之间的差距。

Result: 在多种基础模型上显著提升多轮编辑性能。在 FLUX.1-Kontext-dev 上，第3轮整体性能提升 6.85 分，超越了 Qwen-Image-Edit 等开源模型。同时保持了较高的边际成功率并减少了曝光偏差。

Conclusion: MT-EditFlow 为多轮图像编辑提供了一种有效的强化学习优化框架，能够缓解错误传播问题，为更可靠、更自然的人机协作视觉内容创作奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于指令的多轮图像编辑（multi-turn image editing），属于图像编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong flow matching RL for multi-turn editing, but subtopic is outside top priority continuous/multimodal generation. Signals: flow matching, reinforcement learning, multi-turn image editing, reward optimization, exposure bias reduction

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in instruction-based image editing have captured significant attention, as models are now capable of handling real-world editing demands with the practicality required by everyday users. However, editing models trained primarily for single-turn edits often break down in multi-turn editing--the natural interactive setting where a user iteratively refines an image based on the model's own previous outputs. This failure stems from the all-or-nothing requirement, where a single failed turn compromises the entire sequence, and error propagation, where exposure bias leads to compounding editing errors. To address these challenges, we introduce MT-EditFlow, a flow-matching reinforcement learning framework designed to optimize reward signals for sequential image editing. MT-EditFlow integrates a multi-turn perspective with a multi-reward formulation to provide a unified structure applicable to both GRPO and NFT-based reinforcement learning methods. We systematically analyze and optimize the reward signal by investigating effective scoring strategies for turn-level aggregation, VLM reasoning modes to trade off reward bias and variance, and advantage fusion levels to prevent reward hacking. Our findings reveal that broadcasting the aggregated advantage across the entire editing trajectory effectively bridges the gap between local planning and global multi-turn task success. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MT-EditFlow significantly improves performance across diverse base models. Notably, it boosts FLUX.1-Kontext-dev by 6.85 points in turn-3 overall performance, surpassing state-of-the-art open-source models such as Qwen-Image-Edit. By maintaining high marginal success rates and reducing exposure bias, MT-EditFlow provides a foundation for more reliable and natural human-AI collaboration in visual content creation.

</details>


### [108] [Order within Chaos: Capturing Intrinsic Energy Anomalies for AI-Manipulated Image Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02178)
*Yiming Wang, Baiqi Wu, Qingming Li, Jiahao Chen, Tong Zhang, Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAME框架，利用扩散过程对局部高频方差的抑制特性（统计能量差）来检测和定位AI编辑图像伪造区域，并引入EditStream自动化数据合成流水线以跟上生成模型发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像伪造定位方法依赖物理噪声特征，无法有效检测AI生成/编辑的逼真伪造图像。需要一种不依赖物理噪声、能捕捉生成模型固有统计异常的新方法。

Method: 理论上证明扩散过程会抑制局部高频方差，产生与自然光学成像不同的统计能量差。提出FLAME框架：使用LAD（Local Anomaly Detection）图捕获内在异常，结合参数高效适配器（Adapter）适配SAM实现像素级伪造定位。同时提出EditStream自动化流水线，基于指令持续合成训练数据。

Result: 在多个AI生成伪造数据集上达到SOTA，显著优于此前方法，并能有效泛化到未见过的生成架构。

Conclusion: 扩散过程的内在统计特性可被利用来检测AI编辑图像，FLAME框架结合LAD图与SAM适配器实现了鲁棒的伪造定位，EditStream解决了训练数据滞后问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI生成图像伪造定位（forgery localization），属于图像编辑检测与定位任务，与[图像与视频编辑]（visual_editing）这一规范子主题紧密相关，因为该子主题涵盖图像编辑及其逆向检测任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on image forgery detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: AI-manipulated image forgery, diffusion process energy gap, SAM adapter, automated data synthesis, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative AI have led to image editing models capable of producing realistic forgeries that evade traditional image forgery localization methods, as these approaches depend on physical noise absent in synthetic data. To address this challenge, we theoretically demonstrate that the diffusion process inherently suppresses local high-frequency variance, creating a statistical energy gap that is distinguishable from the natural entropy of optical imaging. Guided by this insight, we propose FLAME, a unified framework that utilizes a LAD map to capture these intrinsic anomalies, coupled with a parameter-efficient adapter for SAM to achieve precise, pixel-level forgery localization. Furthermore, to bridge the lag between forensic benchmarks and evolving generative models, we introduce EditStream, an automated pipeline for continuous, instruction-based training data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FLAME establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming previous methods on AI-generated forgery datasets while effectively generalizing to unseen generative architectures. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [109] [AlbedoEdit: Unified Instance-Level Video Editing with Albedo Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01362)
*Xilong Zhou, Bao-Huy Nguyen, Zheng Zeng, Jacob Munkberg, Jon Hasselgren, Thomas Leimkühler, Nima Kalantari, Miloš Hašan, Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AlbedoEdit 是一个统一的实例级视频编辑框架，利用光照无关的反照率图（albedo map）作为用户编辑引导，通过微调视频基础模型，联合支持物体插入、物体移除和纹理编辑三种任务，并在合成数据集上训练以隐式学习编辑后的光照效果（如高光、阴影、反射）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法要么只提供粗粒度的语义控制，要么为每种编辑任务设计专用框架，缺乏统一的细粒度实例级编辑方案。作者希望利用反照率图（albedo）光照不变且不含镜面反射、阴影等干扰的特性，提供一个用户友好的统一编辑机制。

Method: 基于视频基础模型（video foundation model），微调使其将源 RGB 视频转换为编辑后的 RGB 视频，以用户编辑的第一帧反照率图作为条件。构建了一个包含三种编辑任务的配对合成数据集进行训练，使模型隐式学习编辑后产生的复杂光照效果（如高光、软阴影、镜面反射）。

Result: 在物体插入、移除和纹理编辑三个任务上，AlbedoEdit 在定性和定量指标上均优于现有的最先进视频编辑方法。

Conclusion: 反照率图作为编辑引导是一种有效且用户友好的机制，能够统一多种实例级视频编辑任务，并隐式模拟编辑后的真实光照效果，为视频编辑提供了更灵活通用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一的实例级视频编辑框架 AlbedoEdit，支持物体插入、移除和纹理编辑，属于视频编辑任务。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）完全覆盖该工作。

Importance reason: Video editing with albedo guidance is novel but not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: Unified video editing, Albedo guidance, Object insertion/removal/texture, Synthetic training data, Lighting effects simulation

Abstract: Video generative models have achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing photorealistic video sequences. However, enabling broader and more creative downstream applications requires fine-grained instance-level video editing, including object insertion, object removal, and texture editing, which has emerged as a prominent yet challenging problem. Existing approaches either propose unified generative frameworks with only coarse semantic control, or design task-specific frameworks for individual editing tasks, limiting their flexibility and applicability across diverse real-world scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose AlbedoEdit, a unified generative video editing framework that jointly supports object insertion, object removal, and texture editing. Our key insight is that the intrinsic albedo map, which is invariant to lighting and contains no specularity, shadowing and inter-reflection effects, provides an effective and user-friendly mechanism for specifying fine-grained appearance edits. Built upon video foundation models, AlbedoEdit is fine-tuned to translate source RGB videos into edited RGB videos, conditioned on a user-edited first-frame albedo. Trained on a new paired synthetic dataset covering all three editing tasks, AlbedoEdit implicitly learns to harmonize edited contents and simulate complex real-world visual effects triggered by editing operations, including specular highlights, soft shadows, and mirror reflections. AlbedoEdit demonstrates superior performance over state-of-the-art video editing approaches, both qualitatively and quantitatively. Project webpage is .

</details>


### [110] [Single-Line Drawing Generation via Semantics-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01910)
*Tanguy Magne, Alexandre Binninger, Ruben Wiersma, Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (65.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个语义驱动的单线画自动生成方法，通过分数蒸馏采样优化均匀有理B样条曲线参数，生成连续单笔矢量线条画，支持文本或图像引导，并可直接用于刺绣、激光雕刻等下游制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单线画是一种高度抽象的艺术形式，现有文本到图像模型无法直接生成连续单笔线条画，且缺乏对线条风格和细节的精细控制。作者希望实现自动化的、语义驱动的单线画生成，并保持矢量格式以支持下游制造。

Method: 使用均匀有理B样条（URBS）曲线作为连续单笔画的参数化表示，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）优化曲线控制点，结合文本或图像引导的语义损失以及额外的风格损失项，实现语义驱动且风格可控的单线画生成。

Result: 在美学质量和风格忠实度上优于现有文本到图像模型和优化管线，生成的矢量线条画可直接用于刺绣、激光雕刻和线材弯曲等制造流程。

Conclusion: 基于语义驱动的URBS曲线优化方法能有效生成高质量、风格可控的单线画，且矢量输出天然支持下游数字制造，为艺术风格化生成开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文通过语义驱动优化生成单线画（single-line drawing），属于图像风格化生成与编辑范畴，与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的风格化、可控编辑方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel semantics-driven single-line drawing generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: single-line drawing, semantics-driven optimization, score distillation sampling, vector format, text/image-guided generation

Abstract: Line drawings are a highly expressive art form that requires the artist to abstract and distill the essence of their subject. We present the first semantics-driven method for automatically generating single-line drawings in vector format, guided either by a text prompt describing the concept or an input image depicting it. Our approach leverages score distillation sampling to optimize the parameters of a uniform rational B-spline (URBS) curve, ensuring that the drawing consists of a single continuous stroke by design. This representation provides fine-grained control over the level of detail, while additional loss terms allow us to steer the final artistic style. We demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-image models and optimization pipelines for this task, producing results that are both more aesthetically pleasing and more faithful to the style of continuous line drawing artists. Furthermore, because our method generates a vectorized curve, it directly supports downstream fabrication processes such as embroidery, laser engraving and wire bending. Our code and results are available at .

</details>

### [111] [Cross-Axis Feature Fusion with Joint-Wise Motion Difference Prediction for Text-Based 3D Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01014)
*Gyojin Han, Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于文本的3D人体动作编辑方法，通过跨轴特征融合（joint轴与time轴的双Transformer架构）和辅助任务（Soft-DTW距离回归）来同时理解编辑的时间位置和关节位置，在MotionFix数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D人体动作编辑方法主要关注何时（时间维度）进行编辑，而忽略了哪些关节需要被修改。本文旨在构建一个同时理解编辑的时间位置和具体关节位置的模型，以更精确地保留源动作的风格和结构。

Method: 提出双轴锚定Transformer架构：一个沿关节维度提取特征（joint-anchored transformer），一个沿时间维度提取特征（time-anchored transformer），并通过跨轴融合块（cross-axis fusion block）整合两种表征。此外引入辅助任务：训练joint-anchored transformer回归源动作与目标动作关节旋转之间的Soft-DTW距离，使其学会区分哪些关节需要修改、哪些需要保留。

Result: 在MotionFix数据集上的实验表明，该方法在文本指令对齐、源动作保持和生成动作保真度方面均显著优于现有方法，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模关节维度的编辑信息，结合跨轴特征融合与辅助任务，可以有效提升文本驱动的3D人体动作编辑的语义对齐质量和动作保真度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于文本的3D人体动作编辑，属于人体动作生成与编辑范畴，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow 3D motion editing task with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: 3D human motion editing, cross-axis feature fusion, Soft-DTW auxiliary task, MotionFix dataset, text-based editing

Abstract: We address text-based 3D human motion editing, where the goal is to preserve the style and structure of a source motion while applying edits described in natural language. The release of the MotionFix dataset has spurred active research into training-based diffusion models that directly generate an edited motion from a source motion and a text instruction. While previous works have focused primarily on learning when an edit should occur temporally, our goal is to create a model that understands not only this temporal aspect but also which specific joints are responsible for the change. Targeting this, we propose a novel architecture and a complementary auxiliary task to aid its training. Our architecture consists of two axis-anchored transformers, which extract distinct features along the joint and time dimensions respectively, and a cross-axis fusion block that integrates these representations. We further introduce an auxiliary task that trains the joint-anchored transformer to regress the Soft-DTW distance between source and target joint rotations. This objective teaches the module to understand which joints to modify and which to preserve. Through comprehensive experiments on the MotionFix dataset, we demonstrate that our method significantly improves semantic alignment with both the text instruction and the source motion, as well as the overall fidelity of the generated motion, achieving state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [112] [MotionDreamer: Universal Skeletal Motion Generation for 3D Rigged Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01518)
*Ye Tao, Yuxin Yao, Kendong Liu, Dapeng Wu, Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MotionDreamer 是一个基于扩散模型的通用骨骼运动生成框架，能从2D视频引导生成3D绑定模型的动画。通过构建约20,000个带纹理、骨骼和动画序列的3D模型数据集，并提出结构-语义注入机制将2D视觉运动线索映射到异构3D骨骼结构，实现了跨类别（从生物到幻想生物）的高保真动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模板化方法受限于特定拓扑结构，无法泛化到不同形态；而逐案例优化计算成本高、易陷入局部最优且对视角歧义敏感。需要一种类别无关的通用骨骼动画生成方法。

Method: 1) 构建大规模动态数据集（约20,000个3D模型，含完整纹理、骨骼绑定和动画序列）；2) 提出结构-语义注入机制，将纹理和语义属性直接整合到骨骼关节表示中，使模型能将2D视觉动态映射到特定关节层级和功能角色；3) 基于扩散模型框架生成骨骼动画。

Result: 在大量未见类别（从现有生物到幻想生物）上，MotionDreamer 生成的动画在解剖一致性和高保真度方面显著优于现有方法，树立了鲁棒高效的4D资产生成新标杆。

Conclusion: MotionDreamer 证明了通过结构-语义注入机制弥合2D视觉运动线索与异构3D骨骼结构之间的运动学鸿沟是可行的，为类别无关的通用骨骼动画生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MotionDreamer，一个基于扩散的框架，从2D视频引导生成类别无关的3D骨骼动画（rigged shapes 的骨骼运动生成）。这属于多模态生成方向下的"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）子主题，该子主题涵盖虚拟人、人体动作、动画生成等，与本文的骨骼运动生成高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D skeletal motion generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: diffusion-based framework, 3D rigged shape animation, 2D video guidance, large-scale dynamic dataset, structural-semantic injection

Abstract: Motion generation for rigged shapes is vital for scalable 4D asset production. However, template-based methods are limited by specific topologies and fail to generalize across diverse morphologies. Conversely, per-case optimization is computationally expensive, susceptible to local optima, and highly sensitive to viewpoint-induced ambiguities. In this paper, we present MotionDreamer, a diffusion-based framework designed for category-agnostic skeletal animation generation from 2D video guidance. To overcome the scarcity of high-quality training data, we have curated a large-scale dynamic dataset comprising approximately 20,000 diverse 3D models, each featuring complete textures, skeletal rigging, and a wide array of comprehensive animation sequences. To bridge the kinematic gap between 2D visual motion cues and heterogeneous 3D skeletal structures, we propose a structural-semantic injection mechanism. Our model integrates texture and semantic attributes directly into skeletal joint representations. This allows it to map perceived visual dynamics to specific joint hierarchies and their functional roles. This enables MotionDreamer to synthesize high-fidelity animations that maintain anatomical consistency across a vast range of unseen categories, from existing biological species to fantastical beings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing methods, setting a new state-of-the-art benchmark for robust and efficient 4D asset generation. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [113] [Real-Time Generation of Streamable Talking Portrait Video with Reference-Guided Deep Compression VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01620)
*Sicheng Xu, Yu Deng, Shoukang Hu, Yichuan Wang, Yizhong Zhang, Zhan Chen, Jiaolong Yang, Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向流式传输的说话人像视频实时生成框架，包含因果视频VAE（支持参考图像引导的深度潜空间压缩）和基于Rectified Flow Transformer的自回归潜空间去噪模型，实现了高质量、低延迟的说话人像视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在说话人像视频生成上效果显著，但计算开销大，难以用于实时交互场景。本文旨在设计一个可流式传输、实时生成的说话人像视频框架。

Method: 提出因果视频VAE，通过引入可变数量的参考图像作为引导，使网络聚焦于动态信息而非静态外观，提升压缩效率与重建质量；扩展残差自编码范式以改善时空因果性。生成器基于Rectified Flow Transformer架构，以块级自回归方式生成视频潜变量，支持流式传输。

Result: 方法实现了实时高质量说话人像视频生成，速度显著快于基线模型，在真实感、生动性和视频质量上与大型模型持平甚至更优。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计的因果VAE压缩与自回归流匹配生成相结合，可以在保持高质量的同时实现实时流式说话人像视频生成，为交互式数字人应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于语音和参考图像的说话人像视频实时生成，属于数字人/说话头/肖像动画方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and efficient inference priorities, but lacks direct connection to top-weighted continuous language/multimodal models. Signals: Real-time generation, Causal video VAE with reference-guided compression, Rectified Flow Transformer, Autoregressive latent denoising, Streamable talking portrait video

Abstract: Video diffusion models have significantly advanced portrait video generation, yet their high computational demands limit their use in interactive applications. This work presents a framework for streamable talking portrait video generation conditioned on speech audio and reference images. Designed meticulously for streaming scenarios, it features a causal video VAE for deep latent compression and an autoregressive latent denoising model. Our causal VAE integrates a variable number of reference images as guidance, allowing the network to focus on dynamic information rather than static appearance, thereby enhancing compression efficacy and reconstruction quality. Additionally, we extend the residual auto-encoding paradigm to improve spatial-temporal causality handling in our VAE. The generator is based on a Rectified Flow Transformer architecture and produces video latents in a blockwise auto-regressive manner. Our method enables the real-time generation of high-quality talking portrait videos, achieving speeds significantly faster than baseline models. Furthermore, comprehensive experiments demonstrate that it is on par with or even outperforms these large models in realism, vividness, and video quality.

</details>


### [114] [Conditional Collapse in Sign Language Production: A Diagnostic and a Scaling Argument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01643)
*Rui Hong, Jana Košecká*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对手语生成任务中的"条件坍塌"问题进行了诊断分析，提出三级评估框架（初始姿态条件、输出多样性、目标忠实度），发现现有模型在忠实度上始终不达标，且FID与忠实度不相关，指出句子级配对数据集规模是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成评估指标（FID、BT BLEU）在指标提升的同时，生成的动作可能并未忠实表达手语手势，存在条件坍塌问题。需要更细粒度的诊断性评估方法。

Method: 提出三级评估框架：τ1（初始姿态条件）、τ2（输出多样性）、τ3（目标忠实度），使用冻结的运动自编码器（MoAE）的潜表征计算成对距离比。在How2Sign数据集上评估14个SLP模型检查点，并在ASL3DWord孤立词汇数据集上进行对比实验。

Result: 所有模型在τ3忠实度上均未达标；FID变化近两个数量级但与忠实度不相关；在孤立词汇数据集ASL3DWord上可达到良好的τ3，表明句子级配对数据集规模是瓶颈。

Conclusion: 现有SLP模型存在条件坍塌问题，FID等常用指标无法反映忠实度，需要更大规模的句子级配对数据集来缓解该问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究手语生成（Sign Language Production），即从文本生成手语动作，属于虚拟人/人体动作生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on sign language production, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks the user's boost keywords. Signals: Sign language production, conditional collapse, evaluation framework, dataset bottleneck, not aligned with user's direction or subtopics

Abstract: Sign Language Production (SLP) is the task of generating avatar sign language motion from natural language text. The quality of the generated motion is typically evaluated by a motion-space Fréchet distance (FID) and back-translation (BT) BLEU score on benchmarks such as How2Sign. Both metrics can improve substantially while the underlying generator fails to faithfully represent the sign language gestures. In this work we propose to evaluate the generated motion at three independent levels: ({\tau}1) initial-pose conditioning, ({\tau}2) output diversity, and ({\tau}3) target faithfulness. We compute these as pairwise-distance ratios using latent representations of a frozen motion autoencoder (MoAE). We evaluate 14 SLP model checkpoints on the How2Sign dataset, including a re-implemented Neural Sign Actors (NSA), and show that {\tau}3 faithfulness is never attained, while FID varies by nearly two orders of magnitude and is uncorrelated with faithfulness. We show that on the isolated gloss dataset ASL3DWord favorable {\tau}3 can be attained, hence isolating the size of the sentence-level paired-dataset as the bottleneck.

</details>


### [115] [Towards 3D-Aware Video Diffusion Models: Render-Free Human Motion Control with Mesh Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02000)
*Jingyun Liang, Min Wei, Shikai Li, Yizeng Han, Hangjie Yuan, Lei Sun, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.IV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需渲染的3D感知视频扩散框架，通过将3D人体网格压缩为token，与视频token联合在DiT架构中处理，实现人体运动控制视频生成，避免了传统2D引导方法中的视角依赖伪影和轨迹-姿态不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在人体运动控制任务中通常依赖渲染的2D运动引导视频（如骨架、深度图），这些2D引导存在视角依赖、轨迹-姿态不匹配等问题，且无法让模型真正理解3D结构。作者希望探究视频扩散模型是否能感知3D结构，并提出一种直接利用3D网格token进行条件生成的无渲染框架。

Method: 提出Mesh Tokenization方法，将3D人体网格压缩为紧凑的token序列，与视频token一起输入DiT（Diffusion Transformer）架构进行联合建模。该框架无需显式渲染2D引导，直接在token空间中融合外观、3D结构和相机视角信息，实现端到端的运动控制视频生成。

Result: 在人体运动控制基准上取得了强性能，相比依赖2D引导的方法，有效减少了视角依赖伪影和轨迹-姿态不匹配问题，生成的视频在3D结构一致性和运动控制精度上表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过网格token化，视频扩散模型能够更好地捕捉复杂3D人体结构及其与环境的交互，表明视频扩散模型具备感知3D结构的能力，为3D感知视频生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于3D人体网格token的人体运动控制视频生成，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及视频扩散模型与3D结构感知，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel mesh tokenization for 3D-aware video diffusion aligns with user's priority on video generation and world models. Signals: video diffusion, 3D-aware generation, human motion control, mesh tokenization, DiT architecture

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown remarkable success in video generation. However, whether such models are truly aware of the 3D structure underlying visual observations, rather than simply reproducing plausible 2D projections, remains an open question. In this work, we investigate this question through human motion control, a task that requires precise modelling of 3D human geometry, motion, camera viewpoint, and scene context. Unlike prior methods that rely on rendered 2D motion guidance videos, we propose a render-free framework that conditions video generation directly on compressed 3D human mesh tokens. This representation preserves full 3D geometric information while enabling a unified token-based generation pipeline that processes video tokens jointly with motion tokens in a DiT-based architecture. This design requires the model to reason jointly about appearance, 3D structure, and camera viewpoint during video generation. Experimental results demonstrate strong performance on human motion control benchmarks, while reducing artifacts induced by view-dependent 2D guidance and trajectory-pose mismatches during editing. These findings suggest that video diffusion models, when equipped with mesh tokenization, can better capture complex 3D human structures and their interactions with the surrounding environment.

</details>

### [116] [Diffusion Image Generation with Explicit Modeling of Data Manifold Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00094)
*Duoduo Xue, Zhiyu Zhu, Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (85.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIND（Data Manifold-aware Image diffusioN moDel），通过在连续扩散模型的得分函数中集成离散 patch tokenization，显式建模数据流形几何。引入可微分的 soft top-k 聚合机制、双分支高频特征嵌入层以及多阶段过渡采样策略。在 ImageNet 256×256 上，MIND-B（130M 参数）在 guidance 下 FID 达 2.06，超越 LlamaGen-3B（3.1B 参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像扩散模型（如 DiT、SiT）在建模数据流形时缺乏对几何结构的显式利用，导致生成质量受限。离散 token 方法（如 VQ-VAE）擅长结构化量化但缺乏连续扩散的灵活性。作者希望结合两者的优势，显式建模数据流形几何以提升生成质量。

Method: MIND 框架的核心创新包括：(1) 在连续扩散模型的得分函数中引入离散 patch tokenization，通过可微分的 soft top-k 聚合机制实现端到端训练；(2) 设计双分支高频特征嵌入层，缓解 Transformer 骨干在低维输入上的频谱偏差；(3) 提出多阶段过渡采样策略，根据时间步动态调整采样方案。整体架构基于 DiT 改进。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，80 epoch 训练后，基础模型无 guidance 时 FID 为 22.73，相比 DiT-B/2 基线的 43.47 降低近一半。相比 DiT 和 SiT 基线平均降低 FID 15.95 和 9.06。有 guidance 时 MIND-B（130M 参数）FID 达 2.06，超越 LlamaGen-3B（3.1B 参数）的 2.18；MIND-XL（715M 参数）FID 进一步降至 1.95。

Conclusion: MIND 通过显式建模数据流形几何，成功融合离散 token 的结构量化能力与连续扩散的并行生成灵活性，为扩散图像生成提供了新视角。实验表明该方法在参数效率上显著优于现有方法，有望推动该领域的进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MIND 框架，在连续扩散模型中引入离散 patch tokenization 以显式建模数据流形几何，核心是改进图像扩散生成（基于 DiT 架构）。属于 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion），该子主题涵盖图像扩散模型与 DiT 等基础图像生成方法。

Importance reason: Integrates discrete tokenization into continuous diffusion for manifold modeling, directly advancing continuous language/multimodal models and image generation. Signals: continuous diffusion with discrete tokens, soft top-k aggregation, manifold geometry modeling, surpasses LlamaGen-3B with 130M params, spectral bias mitigation

Abstract: Image generative models aim to sample data points from the underlying data manifold, a task that requires learning and decoding a dense, low-dimensional, and compact parameterization space. To achieve this, we propose the Data Manifold-aware Image diffusioN moDel (MIND), a novel framework that explicitly models manifold geometry by integrating discrete patch tokenization into the score function of a continuous diffusion model. This approach successfully leverages both the structural quantification capabilities of discrete tokens and the parallel generation flexibility of continuous diffusion. Moreover, we enable end-to-end differentiable training via a novel soft top-$k$ aggregation mechanism and introduce dual-branch high-frequency feature embedding layers to alleviate the spectral bias of transformer backbones on low-dimensional inputs. Furthermore, for inference, we design a multi-stage transition sampling scheme that dynamically adjusts the sampling scheme based on timestep. Extensive experiments on ImageNet 256$\times$256 demonstrate the effectiveness of MIND. After 80-epoch training, our base model achieves an FID of 22.73 without guidance, nearly halving the 43.47 FID of the vanilla DiT-B/2 baseline. The proposed method reduces FID by 15.95 and 9.06 on average compared with the baselines DiT and SiT, respectively. For image generation on ImageNet-256$\times$256 with guidance, the proposed MIND-B with only 130M parameters achieves an FID of 2.06, superpassing the LlamaGen-3B with 3.1B parameters. The proposed MIND-XL with 715M parameters further reduces the FID to 1.95. Our MIND introduces a fresh perspective on diffusion-based image generation, paving the way for future research and innovation in this community. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [117] [Versatile Framework with Semantic and Structural guidance for Image Reconstruction from Brain Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00121)
*Yizhuo Lu, Changde Du, Qiongyi Zhou, Liuyun Jiang, Huiguang He*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindDiffuser，一种两阶段图像重建框架，利用 CLIP 文本嵌入和 Stable Diffusion 从脑活动（fMRI/EEG/MEG）中重建视觉刺激图像，并通过浅层 CLIP 视觉特征反向传播对齐结构信息，在三个模态数据集上超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑活动图像重建方法虽能恢复语义信息（概念、物体），但在细粒度结构信息（位置、朝向、大小）上与原刺激一致性差，影响模型的可控性和可解释性。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1 将从脑信号解码的 CLIP 文本嵌入输入 Stable Diffusion 生成含语义的初步图像；Stage 2 利用解码的浅层 CLIP 视觉特征作为监督信号，通过反向传播迭代优化 Stage 1 的特征向量以对齐结构信息。

Result: 在 fMRI、EEG、MEG 三种模态的视觉刺激脑响应数据集上，MindDiffuser 显著优于先前 SOTA 方法，空间和时间可视化结果支持其神经生物学合理性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升了脑活动图像重建的语义与结构一致性，且跨模态通用性强，为未来神经解码研究提供了指导。

Classification reason: 该论文利用 Stable Diffusion（文生图扩散模型）从脑活动信号重建图像，属于图像扩散模型的应用。虽然任务特殊（脑解码），但核心方法基于文生图扩散模型，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散" 合理相关。

Importance reason: While technically solid, the paper's focus on brain decoding from fMRI/EEG/MEG is outside the user's core priority subtopics and is flagged by negative keywords. Signals: brain decoding, image reconstruction, CLIP, Stable Diffusion, fMRI

Abstract: Reconstructing visual stimuli from brain recordings has been a meaningful and challenging task in brain decoding. Especially, the achievement of precise and controllable image reconstruction bears great significance in propelling the progress and utilization of brain-computer interfaces. Recent methods, leveraging advances in the power of text-to-image generation models, have reconstructed images that closely approximate complex natural stimuli in terms of semantics (e.g., concepts and objects). However, they struggle to maintain consistency with the original stimuli in fine-grained structural information (e.g., position, orientation and size), which undermines both the controllability and interpretability of the models. To address the aforementioned issues, we propose a two-stage image reconstruction framework, termed MindDiffuser. In Stage 1, Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) text embeddings decoded from brain responses are input into Stable Diffusion, generating a preliminary image containing semantic information. In Stage 2, we use decoded shallow CLIP visual features as supervisory signals, iteratively refining the feature vectors from Stage 1 via backpropagation to align structural information. We conducted extensive experiments on brain response datasets across three modalities (fMRI, EEG, MEG) elicited by visual stimuli, demonstrating that our framework significantly enhances the performance of previous state-of-the-art models, highlighting the effectiveness and versatility of our approach. Spatial and temporal visualization results further support the neurobiological plausibility of our framework, providing guidance for future neural decoding efforts across different brain signal modalities.

</details>


### [118] [PathAR: Structure-First Autoregressive Synthesis of Multimodal Pathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01543)
*Yuan Zhang, Jiahao Xia, Junzhang Huang, Meng Wang, Feng Chen, Guanyu Yang, Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 普通相关 (52.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PathAR 提出了一种结构优先的自回归病理图像合成框架，通过 Dual-VQ tokenizer 将结构（mask）与外观解耦，并使用交错自回归 Transformer 实现结构到外观的条件依赖，从而在模态转换下保持形态学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态病理图像数据稀缺，现有统一生成模型将结构与外观耦合在统一的 token 流中，导致模态变化时结构可控性差，无法保证解剖结构的一致性。

Method: 提出 PathAR 框架：(1) Dual-VQ tokenizer 将病理图像分解为 mask 引导的结构 token 和外观 token；(2) 交错自回归 Transformer（IAR）采用非对称注意力可见性，强制结构到外观的依赖关系；(3) 支持模态标签条件生成，实现空间对齐的图像-掩码对生成。

Result: 实验表明 PathAR 在结构一致性和模态保真度上优于基线方法，保持样本多样性，支持数据稀缺场景下的下游分割任务，并可扩展到更细粒度的器官标签变化。

Conclusion: PathAR 通过显式解耦结构与外观，有效提升了多模态病理图像生成的结构可控性，为数据稀缺的医学图像分析提供了可行的统一生成方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出病理图像的自回归生成框架 PathAR，属于多模态生成方向。虽然论文聚焦于医学病理图像这一特定领域，但其核心方法是自回归视觉生成（结构优先的自回归合成），与 [CANONICAL] 的"文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）在图像生成范式上最为接近。考虑到该 subtopic 描述涵盖"图像扩散模型、DiT 等基础图像生成方法"，PathAR 的自回归生成范式可归入此范畴。其他 subtopic 如"自回归视觉生成"目前 paper count 为 0 且非 canonical，而 text_to_image_diffusion 是 canonical 且可合理涵盖图像生成方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical pathology synthesis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes a negative keyword. Signals: pathology image synthesis, structure-first autoregressive, dual-VQ tokenizer, interleaved autoregressive transformer, modality translation

Abstract: Data scarcity in multimodal pathology motivates unified generative models that synthesize modality-specific appearance while preserving anatomically coherent structure. Although modalities differ in appearance statistics, morphological structures such as cellular topology and tissue boundaries are largely preserved across acquisition protocols. However, existing methods often model these factors within a homogeneous token stream, implicitly coupling structure with appearance and weakening structural controllability under modality shifts. To address this, we propose pathology Autorgressive modeling (PathAR), a structure-first autoregressive synthesis framework that explicitly factorizes structure and appearance for modality-label-conditioned pathology employs a dual vector quantization (Dual-VQ) tokenizer to decompose samples into mask-grounded structure and appearance tokens, and an interleaved autoregressive (IAR) transformer with asymmetric attention visibility to enforce structure-to-appearance dependence. PathAR stabilizes morphology under heterogeneous modality-specific appearances and enables spatially aligned image--mask pair generation. Extensive experiments show that PathAR improves structural consistency and modality fidelity over baselines, maintains sample diversity, supports downstream segmentation in data-scarce regimes, and demonstrates extensibility to finer-grained intra-modality organ-label variation.

</details>


### [119] [FocusDiT: Masking Queries in Diffusion Transformers for Fine-grained Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02090)
*Xueji Fang, Liyuan Ma, Jianhao Zeng, Jinjin Cao, Mingyuan Zhou, Guo-Jun Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (77.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FocusDiT 提出一种查询掩码机制，在 Diffusion Transformer 的 FFN 层中仅让关键查询 token 通过，从而聚焦于复杂细节的生成，提升细粒度图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 中的 FFN 层充当解码视觉内容的键值词汇表，但并非所有查询 token 对生成质量同等重要。作者认为，聚焦于对应更复杂细节的关键查询 token，并鼓励模型改进这些 token，对于细粒度视觉生成至关重要。

Method: 提出 FocusDiT，在 DiT 的 FFN 层前引入一个掩码机制，选择性地只让关键查询 token 进入 FFN 层。被掩码的查询 token 可以从 FFN 词汇表中检索视觉 token 并用于解码其视觉细节，从而提升对复杂区域的生成质量。

Result: 在文本到图像生成任务上的大量实验验证了 token 掩码机制在提升生成性能方面的有效性，生成的图像在细粒度细节上表现更优。

Conclusion: FocusDiT 通过查询掩码策略有效提升了 DiT 在细粒度图像生成上的表现，证明了在扩散 Transformer 中聚焦关键 token 的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FocusDiT，在 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 中通过查询掩码机制提升细粒度图像生成质量，属于文本到图像生成与图像扩散模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: FocusDiT improves fine-grained image generation via query masking in DiT, aligning with user's interest in efficient inference and controllable generation. Signals: diffusion transformer, query masking, fine-grained generation, text-to-image, FFN vocabulary

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) has been widely adopted in the generative diffusion field, advancing the denoising of query tokens through attention and Feed-Forward (\text{FFN}) layers. FFN actually acts as the key-value vocabulary for decoding visual contents where the value embeds the visual semantical knowledge. We present that focusing on critical query tokens corresponding to more complex details and encouraging the model to improve these tokens is essential for fine-grained visual generation. To this end, we propose FocusDiT, which applies a Masking scheme to focus on critical query tokens that are exclusively fed into FFN. The masked queries can retrieve visual tokens from the FFN vocabularies, and use them to decode their visual details. Extensive text-to-image experiments validate the effectiveness of token masking in enhancing generative performance.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-text_to_video_diffusion'></div>
