### [120] [Auteur: Language-Driven Cinematographic Framing for Human-Centric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01900)
*Muhammed Burak Kizil, Enes Sanli, Niloy J. Mitra, Xuelin Chen, Erkut Erdem, Aykut Erdem, Duygu Ceylan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Auteur 提出了一种语言驱动的、以人为中心的电影化镜头构图方法，通过定义以人为中心的相机参数化、领域特定语言（DSL）和微调的多模态大语言模型，将自然语言描述映射为稀疏 DSL 关键帧并插值为连续相机轨迹，输入视频生成器实现可控的电影化构图。在 34K 对齐数据上训练，提出新的构图评测指标，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频模型虽然实现了高视觉保真度和时序一致性，但有意地控制相机运动仍然困难。现有方法将相机运动视为像素合成的副产品，产生的轨迹随机、空间不一致且不关注驱动场景的人类主体。专业电影摄影师是以相对于演员的构图（景别、角度、构图）来构思镜头的，而非世界空间轨迹，因此需要一种以人为中心的、语言驱动的相机构图方法。

Method: 1) 提出以人为中心的相机参数化，将相机参数定义为人体姿态和运动的函数；2) 设计领域特定语言（DSL），可转换为标准 6-DoF 相机参数；3) 微调多模态大语言模型作为虚拟导演，将自然语言描述和粗略人体运动映射为稀疏 DSL 关键帧；4) 通过确定性插值将稀疏关键帧转换为连续相机轨迹；5) 将相机轨迹作为输入提供给视频生成器。同时构建了包含 34K 对齐文本、人体运动和 DSL 标注相机轨迹的数据集。

Result: Auteur 在多种以人为中心的场景中实现了电影化的镜头构图，在提出的构图聚焦指标上持续优于现有方法，能够生成具有可控景别、角度和构图的视频，这是先前生成模型所不具备的能力。

Conclusion: Auteur 通过语言驱动的、以人为中心的相机参数化方法，成功实现了生成式视频中的有意相机控制，弥合了自然语言描述与电影化镜头构图之间的鸿沟，为可控视频生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出语言驱动的电影化镜头构图方法，用于以人为中心的视频生成，核心是视频生成中的可控相机轨迹控制，属于文生视频与视频扩散方向。同时涉及多模态大语言模型作为虚拟导演进行语言到相机参数的映射，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-weighted continuous multimodal and video generation priorities, with a novel human-centric camera control approach. Signals: human-centric camera framing, language-driven video generation, domain-specific language for camera control, fine-tuned MLLM for cinematography, new framing metrics

Abstract: Generative video models have achieved remarkable visual fidelity and temporal coherence, yet intentional camera control remains elusive. Existing frameworks treat camera motion as a byproduct of pixel synthesis, producing trajectories that are stochastic, spatially inconsistent, and indifferent to the human subject driving the scene. In this work, we present Auteur, a method for language-driven, human-centric camera framing in generative video. Our core insight is that professional filmmakers conceive shots not as world-space trajectories but as framings defined relative to the actor, encoding shot size, angle, and composition as functions of human pose and motion. We formalize this intuition as a human-centric camera parameterization and introduce a Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that is convertible to standard 6-DoF camera parameters. A fine-tuned multimodal large language model then acts as a virtual director, mapping natural language descriptions and coarse human motion to sparse DSL keyframes that are deterministically interpolated into continuous camera trajectories, which are then provided as input to video generators. We train and evaluate Auteur on a new dataset of 34K aligned text, human motion, and DSL-annotated camera trajectories drawn from procedural synthesis and real-world movie footage from the CondensedMovies dataset. Auteur enables cinematographic framing of human-centered scenes, a capability largely absent in prior generative models. To assess this behavior, we propose new framing-focused metrics, and our experiments show that Auteur consistently outperforms existing methods

</details>


### [121] [Retrieve What's Missing: Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval for Consistent Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02479)
*Minseok Joo, Dogyun Park, Taehoon Lee, Kyujin Lee, Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (86.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COVRAG 提出了一种基于深度图的覆盖最大化检索增强框架，用于长自回归视频生成中的历史帧检索，以提升长程几何一致性。该方法利用预训练3D先验构建目标视图覆盖图作为轻量级3D记忆证据，并通过最大化残差覆盖增益迭代选择记忆帧，同时引入滑动窗口深度缓存提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长自回归视频生成中保持长程几何一致性是一个挑战。现有记忆增强方法要么依赖相机位姿或视场重叠（过于粗糙，无法推理像素级可见性），要么使用显式3D重建（成本高昂）。需要一种轻量级且有效的记忆检索方法。

Method: 提出 COVRAG 框架：1) 使用预训练深度先验构建目标视图覆盖图作为轻量级3D记忆证据；2) 通过最大化残差覆盖增益迭代选择记忆帧，优先选取能解释目标视图中未被当前上下文或已选记忆覆盖区域的帧；3) 引入滑动窗口深度缓存以高效估计几何信息。

Result: 在 RealEstate10K 和 DL3DV10K 数据集上，COVRAG 相比基线方法在长程几何一致性上取得显著提升，同时保持较低的延迟。

Conclusion: 基于深度图的覆盖最大化检索策略能够有效提升长视频生成的几何一致性，且计算开销可控，为长时序自回归视频生成中的记忆管理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注长视频生成中的几何一致性，属于视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses long-term geometric consistency in autoregressive video generation, a key challenge for the user's high-priority subtopics. Signals: autoregressive video generation, video generation, world model, efficient inference, coverage-maximizing retrieval

Abstract: Maintaining long-term geometric consistency remains challenging for long-horizon autoregressive video generation. Memory-augmented generative models address this by retrieving historical frames, but their effectiveness depends on two key design choices: what 3D-geometric evidence should represent past observations, and how memory frames should be selected from this evidence. Existing methods often rely on camera poses or field-of-view overlap, which are lightweight but too coarse to reason about pixel-wise visibility, or use explicit 3D reconstruction, which provides fine-grained evidence but is costly to maintain over long rollouts. We propose Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (COVRAG), a depth-based memory retrieval framework that uses pretrained 3D priors to construct a target-view coverage map as lightweight 3D memory evidence. For frame selection, COVRAG maximizes residual coverage gain, iteratively retrieving frames that explain target-view regions not covered by the current context or previously selected memories. To improve scalability in long-video generation, we introduce sliding-window depth caching for efficient geometry estimation. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV10K show that COVRAG improves long-horizon geometric consistency while maintaining low latency compared to baselines.

</details>


### [122] [LongLive-RAG: A General Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02553)
*Qixin Hu, Shuai Yang, Wei Huang, Song Han, Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LongLive-RAG 提出将长视频生成建模为检索增强生成问题，通过从历史生成潜变量中检索相关上下文来替代滑动窗口注意力，从而减少误差累积和身份漂移，提升自回归视频扩散的长视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中因滑动窗口注意力机制导致误差累积和身份漂移问题——当前窗口一旦出现外观错误，后续生成只能基于退化轨迹继续偏离。现有方法缺乏对历史信息的有效利用。

Method: 提出 LongLive-RAG 框架：将已生成的潜变量作为动态可搜索的历史记忆库，在每个新块生成时使用查询嵌入检索相关历史潜变量作为非局部上下文条件。引入 Window Temporal Delta Loss 抑制冗余局部相似性，使嵌入捕捉有意义的时序变化。

Result: 在多个自回归视频扩散骨干网络和不同生成长度上验证，长视频质量显著提升，获得 VBench-Long 平均排名最佳。检索步骤仅增加少量计算开销。

Conclusion: LongLive-RAG 是首个将自生成潜变量历史作为内容可寻址检索记忆用于开放式自回归长视频生成的方法，有效缓解了滑动窗口注意力导致的误差累积问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长视频生成，属于视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度相关。虽然论文聚焦于自回归视频扩散中的长视频生成问题，但核心仍是视频扩散生成方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation error accumulation with a novel RAG approach, highly relevant to your priority on video generation and world models. Signals: autoregressive video generation, retrieval-augmented generation, long video generation, error accumulation, identity drift

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion enables variable-length synthesis, but long-horizon generation often suffers from accumulated errors and identity drift. For efficiency, existing methods commonly adopt sliding-window attention during generation. This creates an irreversible generation trajectory: once the active window accumulates appearance errors, subsequent generations can only condition on this degraded trajectory and drift further away. We address this limitation by formulating long video generation as a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) problem. Rather than relying solely on the recent window, we treat previously generated latents as a dynamic, searchable history. We propose LongLive-RAG, a general retrieval framework for AR video generation. At each new block, LongLive-RAG uses a query embedding to retrieve relevant historical latents. This lightweight retrieval step adds only a small overhead relative to generation and lets the generator condition on non-local context instead of only the recent window. To make retrieval more discriminative, we introduce the Window Temporal Delta Loss that suppresses redundant local similarity and encourages embeddings to capture meaningful temporal changes. Together, these components help reduce error accumulation caused by sliding-window attention. Experiments across multiple AR backbones and generation lengths show improved long-video quality and the best average VBench-Long rank. To our knowledge, among open-ended AR long video generation methods, LongLive-RAG is the first to formulate self-generated latent history as content-addressable retrieval memory. Code is available at .

</details>

### [123] [Collaborative Few-Step Distillation and Low-Bit Quantization for Wan2.2 Dual-Expert Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00658)
*Jinyang Du, Shenghao Jin, Ziqian Xu, Ruihao Gong, Shiqiao Gu, Yang Yong, Jinyang Guo, Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对 Wan2.2-T2V-A14B 视频扩散模型，提出了一种结合少步分布匹配蒸馏与低比特量化的部署导向压缩流水线。该方法利用模型的双专家去噪路径，分别校准高噪声和低噪声分支，保护敏感入口层，并采用 HiF4 格式提升动态范围覆盖。量化在蒸馏后的少步学生模型上校准，减少推理时的激活分布不匹配。实验表明，联合设计在 8 步和 20 步设置下平均超越原始全精度基线，20 步配置取得最佳质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型虽然视觉质量高，但部署成本高昂——每个样本需要大量去噪步骤且参数量巨大。现有方法通常单独研究蒸馏或量化，缺乏针对视频扩散模型的联合压缩方案，且未充分利用模型的双专家去噪结构特性。

Method: 提出联合少步蒸馏与低比特量化的压缩流水线：(1) 利用 Wan2.2 的双专家去噪路由，分别校准高噪声和低噪声分支；(2) 保护敏感入口层；(3) 采用 HiF4 格式的低比特表示提升动态范围覆盖；(4) 量化在蒸馏后的少步学生模型上校准，而非原始长步轨迹，以减少推理时的激活分布不匹配。

Result: 联合设计的量化模型在相同步数下接近全精度模型，并在 8 步和 20 步设置下平均超越原始全精度基线。20 步配置在测试配置中取得最佳质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 蒸馏与量化的协同设计能有效压缩视频扩散模型，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低部署成本，为大规模视频生成模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频扩散模型的少步蒸馏（few-step distillation）与低比特量化（low-bit quantization）联合压缩方法，属于生成模型效率优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and acceleration for video diffusion, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel co-design of distillation and quantization. Signals: video diffusion, efficient inference, distillation, quantization, Wan2.2

Abstract: Large video diffusion models achieve strong visual quality but remain expensive to deploy because each sample requires many denoising steps and a large resident parameter footprint. This paper studies a deployment-oriented compression pipeline for Wan2.2-T2V-A14B by combining few-step distribution-matching distillation with low-bit quantization. The pipeline follows the model's dual-expert denoising route, calibrates the high-noise and low-noise branches separately, protects sensitive entrance layers, and uses HiF4-style low-bit representation to improve dynamic-range coverage. Quantization is calibrated on the distilled few-step student rather than on the original long-step trajectory, reducing activation-distribution mismatch during inference. The proposed co-design keeps the quantized model close to the same-step full-precision model and surpasses the original full-precision baseline at 8 and 20 steps on average. The 20-step setting gives the best quality-efficiency trade-off in the tested configurations.

</details>


### [124] [DASH: Dual-Branch Score Distillation for Guidance-Calibrated Compact Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00798)
*Abdullah Al Shafi, Kazi Saeed Alam, Sk Imran Hossain, Engelbert Mephu Nguifo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (64.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DASH 提出一种双分支蒸馏框架，独立监督条件与无条件两个分数分支，解决类条件扩散模型蒸馏中无监督无条件分支导致引导失效的问题，并引入 TIRT Transfer 复制教师的时间步重要性课程。在 CIFAR-10/100 上实现 5.9 倍压缩，FID 仅损失 4 点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类条件扩散模型的输出级蒸馏中，无条件分数分支缺乏监督，导致学生模型中无分类器引导（CFG）的差距被低估，两个分支趋向一致，引导失效。需要一种能同时监督两个分支的蒸馏方法以保持引导保真度。

Method: 提出 DASH 框架：(1) 双分支蒸馏——独立监督条件分支和无条件分支，用锚定项（anchor term）将条件预测正则化到真实噪声；(2) TIRT Transfer——将教师模型收敛后的每时间步重要性课程（per-timestep importance curriculum）作为冻结先验复制给学生，避免在有限蒸馏预算中重新学习。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 和 CIFAR-100 上，5.9 倍压缩的模型在 50 步 DDIM 采样下 FID 与教师模型差距在 4 点以内，显著优于从头训练。消融实验表明无条件监督贡献超过 60% 的总蒸馏增益，课程迁移和锚定正则化提供互补收益。

Conclusion: 双分支约束对于保持引导保真度的压缩至关重要，无条件分支的独立监督是蒸馏成功的主要因素。DASH 为类条件扩散模型的高效压缩提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型的参数压缩与蒸馏（输出级蒸馏），属于生成模型效率优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel dual-branch distillation method for compact diffusion models, but focuses on class-conditional image generation, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion model compression, score distillation, classifier-free guidance, CIFAR-10/100, model efficiency

Abstract: Parameter compression of class-conditional diffusion models reveals an underexplored limitation in output-level distillation: the unconditional score branch remains unsupervised, leaving the classifier-free guidance gap underdetermined in the student. This gap, amplified at every denoising step, admits degenerate solutions where both branches collapse toward identical predictions, rendering guidance ineffective despite low output-level training loss. This paper introduces DASH, a dual-branch distillation framework that independently supervises both score branches, uniquely specifying target branch outputs for each training sample through independent branch constraints, with an anchor term regularising conditional predictions toward ground-truth noise. The framework further introduces TIRT Transfer, which copies the teacher's converged per-timestep importance curriculum into the student as a frozen prior, eliminating the need to relearn it within limited distillation budgets. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 demonstrate that 5.9x compression maintains quality within 4 FID points of the teacher at 50-step DDIM sampling, considerably outperforming training from scratch with guidance fidelity well preserved. Ablation studies confirm that unconditional supervision is the dominant contribution, accounting for over 60% of total distillation gain. Curriculum transfer and anchor regularisation provide complementary benefit, together validating dual-branch constraints as empirically essential for guidance-preserving compression.

</details>


### [125] [Boundary-Protection W8A8 HiFloat8 Quantization for Large-Scale Text-to-Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00957)
*Yiming Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (89.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对14B参数文生视频扩散Transformer（Wan2.1-T2V-14B）的边界保护W8A8 HiFloat8后训练量化方法。通过分析40个注意力块的激活分布异质性，发现首尾边界块与中间块统计特性差异显著，因此保留前2块和后3块为BF16，其余35块量化为W8A8 HiF8。该方法在VBench五个维度上匹配或略超BF16基线，验证了数据驱动的边界保护策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文生视频扩散Transformer模型参数量巨大（如14B），推理部署成本高。现有均匀量化策略无法有效处理不同Transformer块之间激活分布的异质性，导致精度损失。因此需要一种针对视频DiT模型特点的、能保护边界敏感块的高效量化方法。

Method: 对Wan2.1-T2V-14B中40个WanAttentionBlock进行逐块激活分布分析，发现首尾边界块与中间块的统计特性存在根本差异。基于此提出边界保护策略：保留前2个和后3个块为BF16精度，其余35个块采用W8A8 HiFloat8量化。同时探索了量化感知训练（QAT）作为补充微调阶段，并分析了其在单卡硬件上未能超越纯PTQ的条件。

Result: 在VBench五个维度上，所提PTQ方法匹配或略超BF16基线，在5个prompt的评估集上无可测量的精度损失。消融实验验证了完整边界保护配置取得最高平均VBench分数。QAT在单卡硬件上未能超越纯PTQ。

Conclusion: 边界保护策略是量化大规模视频DiT模型的有效方法，通过数据驱动的块选择保留关键边界块精度，可在W8A8 HiF8量化下实现无损推理。该工作为大规模文生视频模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生视频扩散Transformer的W8A8 HiFloat8后训练量化方法，属于生成模型效率优化（量化加速），与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for large video diffusion transformers, a high-priority subtopic, with a practical boundary-protection quantization method. Signals: W8A8 HiFloat8 quantization, 14B text-to-video diffusion transformer, boundary-protection strategy, activation distribution analysis, Ascend 910B NPU deployment

Abstract: We present a post-training quantization (PTQ) approach for Wan2.1-T2V-14B, a 14-billion-parameter text-to-video diffusion transformer, targeting the W8A8 HiFloat8 (HiF8) format on Ascend 910B NPUs. A central challenge in quantizing video DiT models is the heterogeneous activation distribution across transformer blocks: boundary blocks (the first and last few blocks) exhibit fundamentally different statistical properties from middle blocks, making uniform quantization ineffective. We conduct a systematic per-block activation analysis across all 40 WanAttentionBlocks and use the findings to motivate a boundary-protection strategy that retains the first two and last three blocks in BF16 while quantizing the remaining 35 blocks with W8A8 HiF8. The proposed PTQ method matches or marginally exceeds the BF16 baseline on all five VBench dimensions evaluated, indicating no measurable accuracy loss within the 5-prompt evaluation set. An ablation study over four protection configurations confirms that full boundary protection yields the highest average VBench score, validating the data-driven block selection. We additionally investigate quantization-aware training (QAT) as a complementary fine-tuning stage and analyze the conditions under which it fails to outperform plain PTQ on single-card hardware.

</details>


### [126] [Strong Stochastic Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01086)
*Sam McCallum, Zander W. Blasingame, Timothy Herschell, Niklas Rindtorff, Alexander Tong, James Foster*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (85.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Strong Stochastic Flow Maps (SSFMs)，一种学习加性噪声 SDE 强解映射的新框架，将确定性流映射推广到随机场景，通过布朗运动的多项式近似实现无模拟训练，在图像生成和分子系统少步采样上优于现有随机流映射方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流映射（flow maps）通过直接学习微分方程的解映射实现少步采样，但现有方法仅限于 ODE 的解映射近似。扩散模型本质上是 SDE 过程，需要大量网络评估进行数值积分。作者希望将流映射推广到随机 SDE 场景，学习 SDE 的强解映射（路径级收敛而非仅边际分布收敛），从而在保持生成质量的同时大幅减少采样步数。

Method: 提出 SSFM 框架：(1) 将确定性流映射推广到加性噪声 SDE，学习 SDE 的强解映射（路径级收敛）；(2) 引入布朗运动的多项式近似，证明其路径级收敛性；(3) 基于此设计无模拟（simulation-free）训练目标，直接学习扩散模型的解映射，避免推理时的数值积分。

Result: 在图像生成任务上，SSFMs 优于现有随机流映射方法；在分子系统少步采样上也展示了有效性。实验表明该方法在极少数步数下即可达到高质量生成。

Conclusion: SSFMs 成功将流映射从 ODE 推广到 SDE，实现了加性噪声 SDE 的强解映射学习，为扩散模型的高效少步采样提供了新范式，并在图像和分子生成上验证了有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Strong Stochastic Flow Maps (SSFMs)，旨在通过学习 SDE 的强解映射实现少步采样，属于生成模型加速与高效化的核心范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Strongly advances continuous flow mapping for generative models, directly aligning with your priority on continuous language/multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: continuous flow mapping, simulation-free training, few-step sampling, additive-noise SDE, polynomial Brownian motion approximation

Abstract: Flow and diffusion models generate high-quality samples in many modalities; however, many network evaluations are required during inference due to numerical integration of an underlying differential equation. Flow maps alleviate this problem by learning the solution map of the differential equation directly, enabling few-step sampling. Yet, current methods are restricted to approximating the solution map of ODEs. These methods can be used to learn the transition kernel of an SDE, thereby obtaining a solution map that recovers the marginal distributions of the process (weak convergence) rather than the solution path (strong convergence). We propose Strong Stochastic Flow Maps (SSFMs) as a novel framework for learning the strong solution map of additive-noise SDEs, directly generalizing deterministic flow maps to the stochastic setting. Further, a polynomial approximation to Brownian motion is introduced and shown to converge pathwise. These results enable a simulation-free training objective for the solution map of diffusion models. We demonstrate that SSFMs outperform previous stochastic flow map methods on image generation and enable few-step sampling of molecular systems.

</details>


### [127] [Restoring Initial Noise Sensitivity in Text-to-Image Distillation via Geometric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01651)
*Huayang Huang, Ruoyu Wang, Jinhui Zhao, Wei Deng, Daiguo Zhou, Jian Luan, Yu Wu, Ye Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (79.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到图像生成蒸馏会导致对初始噪声的敏感性退化，提出几何感知蒸馏（GAD），通过匹配雅可比-向量积来对齐师生模型的局部几何结构，从而恢复噪声敏感性、提升多样性并保持高视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成蒸馏方法主要优化效率和输出保真度，但忽略了原始轨迹中对初始噪声敏感性的关键属性。这种敏感性的退化会损害依赖噪声优化和操控的下游控制方法（如图像编辑、风格化等）。

Method: 提出几何感知蒸馏（GAD）框架，通过匹配教师和学生模型关于输入噪声的雅可比-向量积（Jacobian-vector products），对齐局部函数行为，从而恢复教师模型的微分响应特性，保留输入-输出景观的局部几何结构。

Result: 在多种 T2I 范式和噪声驱动控制任务上的大量实验表明，GAD 显著恢复了噪声敏感性并提升了生成多样性，同时保持了高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: GAD 通过几何对齐有效恢复了蒸馏模型对初始噪声的敏感性，为生成蒸馏中保留关键轨迹属性提供了新思路，有助于提升下游噪声驱动控制任务的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出几何感知蒸馏（GAD）方法，用于加速文生图扩散模型的少步采样，属于生成模型蒸馏与加速方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and generation quality in text-to-image distillation, aligning with high-priority subtopics on efficient inference and image generation. Signals: generative distillation, text-to-image, noise sensitivity, geometric alignment, diversity improvement

Abstract: Generative distillation significantly accelerates text-to-image (T2I) generation by compressing multi-step trajectories into few-step student models while preserving perceptual quality. However, existing methods primarily optimize efficiency and output fidelity, often neglecting critical properties of the original trajectory. In this work, we identify a key missing property: sensitivity to initial noise, whose degradation impairs downstream control methods relying on noise-based optimization and manipulation. We trace this issue to standard distillation objectives that enforce pointwise output alignment, inadvertently flattening the input-output landscape and suppressing the teacher's local geometric structure. To address this, we propose Geometry-Aware Distillation (GAD), a sensitivity-preserving framework that aligns the local functional behavior of teacher and student models. Specifically, GAD matches Jacobian-vector products with respect to input noise, enabling the student to reproduce the teacher's differential response to perturbations. Extensive experiments across multiple T2I paradigms and noise-driven control tasks demonstrate that GAD significantly restores sensitivity and improves diversity while maintaining high visual fidelity. Code is available at .

</details>


### [128] [Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02133)
*Yangtian Zhang, Zhe Wang, Arthur Gretton, Rex Ying, David van Dijk, Michalis K. Titsias, Jiaxin Shi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (52.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Insertion Process (IP)，一个基于变分学习的插入式生成框架，通过将插入轨迹与排列建立双射对应，联合学习插入位置、插入内容和终止条件，支持变长生成，在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成任务上优于固定顺序的自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非单调序列生成方法（如掩码扩散模型）虽然提供了比从左到右自回归建模更灵活的替代方案，但大多数是顺序无关的且依赖固定长度网格，无法支持变长生成和自适应插入顺序。本文旨在提出一个概率框架来学习变长插入模型中的插入顺序。

Method: 本文形式化了插入轨迹与排列之间的双射对应关系，从而将数据似然精确重参数化为排列上的求和。基于此，提出 Insertion Process (IP)——一种随机生成模型，通过基于排列的变分推理联合学习插入位置、插入内容和终止条件。IP 原生支持变长生成，并学习数据驱动的插入顺序偏好。

Result: 在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成实验上，学习插入顺序提高了建模质量和泛化能力，特别是在没有规范从左到右结构的领域中表现优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了学习插入顺序对于非单调序列生成的有效性，IP 框架为变长生成和自适应插入顺序提供了统一的概率解决方案，在缺乏自然顺序的领域具有显著优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于变分学习的插入式生成框架（Insertion Process），属于非单调序列生成方法，与掩码扩散模型相关。虽然论文主要关注文本/序列生成，但其核心思想（学习插入顺序、变分推理、非自回归生成）与多模态生成中的高效生成范式（如扩散加速、非自回归生成）高度相关。映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation），因为该框架本质上是一种非自回归/非单调的高效生成方法。

Importance reason: Novel insertion-based generation framework but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Insertion-based generation, Variational learning, Variable-length generation, Non-monotonic generation, Molecular string generation

Abstract: Non-monotonic sequence generation methods, such as masked diffusion models, provide a flexible alternative to left-to-right autoregressive modeling by allowing tokens to be generated in non-fixed and prescribed orders. Despite their practical advantages, most existing non-monotonic models are order-agnostic and rely on a fixed-length grid, limiting their ability to support variable-length generation and adaptive insertion order. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic framework for learning insertion order in variable-length insertion models. We formalize a bijective correspondence between insertion trajectories and permutations, which enables an exact reparameterization of the data likelihood as a sum over permutations. Building on this result, we propose the Insertion Process (IP), a stochastic generative model that jointly learns where to insert, what to insert, and when to terminate, trained via permutation-based variational inference. Unlike prior fixed-canvas approaches, IP natively supports variable-length generation and learns data-driven preferences over insertion orders. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation demonstrate that learning insertion order improves both modeling quality and generalization in domains without a canonical left-to-right structure.

</details>


### [129] [Low-Pass Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02177)
*Francesco M. Ruscio, T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Low-Pass Flow Matching，一种基于算子调制插值路径的 Flow Matching 变体，通过引入时变谱偏置（从源谱过渡到频率衰减偏置），使生成过程更符合自然数据的频谱特性。在无条件图像生成任务（包括科学数据集 Galaxy10）上，该方法与自适应 ODE 求解器结合时，能在保持或提升样本质量的同时显著降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Flow Matching 使用白噪声作为源分布，但自然数据的功率谱通常随频率衰减，白噪声的平坦频谱与数据分布不匹配，导致生成路径效率低下。本文旨在通过设计更符合数据频谱特性的插值路径来提升 Flow Matching 的采样效率。

Method: 提出 Low-Pass Flow Matching，核心是使用算子调制插值路径（operator-modulated interpolant），该路径在时间演化过程中引入时变谱偏置：初始阶段保持源分布（如白噪声）的频谱特性，随着路径接近数据分布，逐渐过渡到频率衰减的谱偏置。这种设计使得生成过程更自然地匹配自然数据的低频主导特性。

Result: 在无条件图像生成任务上，包括标准基准和科学数据集 Galaxy10，Low-Pass Flow Matching 与自适应 ODE 求解器结合时，相比标准 Flow Matching 基线，在保持或提升样本质量（FID 等指标）的同时，显著减少了所需的采样步数和计算成本。

Conclusion: Low-Pass Flow Matching 通过引入与数据频谱特性对齐的时变谱偏置，有效提升了 Flow Matching 的采样效率，尤其适用于自适应 ODE 求解器场景。该方法为生成模型中的源分布设计提供了新的视角，未来可进一步探索更复杂的谱偏置设计及其在其他生成任务中的应用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Low-Pass Flow Matching，通过算子调制插值路径引入时变谱偏置，在保持或提升样本质量的同时显著降低自适应 ODE 求解器的采样成本，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly improves sampling efficiency for flow matching, a core technique in the user's priority subtopics of continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Flow matching acceleration, spectral bias for natural data, adaptive ODE solver compatibility, Galaxy10 scientific dataset validation, operator-modulated interpolant

Abstract: Flow Matching typically relies on white noise sources, a choice often misaligned with the power spectra of natural data, which tend to decay with frequency. To address this, we introduce Low-Pass Flow Matching, a variant of Flow Matching based on an operator-modulated interpolant. This formulation induces a time-varying spectral bias that transitions from the source spectrum to a frequency-decaying bias as the path approaches the data. We validate our method on unconditional image generation tasks, including the scientific Galaxy10 dataset. Empirically, we show that our method is particularly effective when paired with adaptive ODE solvers, where it improves or preserves sample quality while substantially reducing sampling cost compared to standard baselines.

</details>


### [130] [Why Are DMD Students Lazy? Understanding the Copying Behavior in Few-Step Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02237)
*Shucheng Li, Iolo Jones, Alexander Tong, Michael M. Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (57.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）中少步学生模型的一种意外行为——复制（copying），即学生模型自发地复现教师模型的原始噪声-数据配对。实验表明，复制并非来自对抗目标或教师记忆，而是高维蒸馏中学生模型几何自由度受限导致的涌现现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）通过对齐所有尺度的噪声分布来压缩预训练扩散模型为少步生成器。理论上，分布级监督不依赖于特定的噪声-数据配对，学生应能自由重映射潜噪声。然而在高维设置中，学生模型却自发复制了教师的原始配对，这一现象需要被理解和解释。

Method: 通过系统实验分析高维蒸馏中的复制行为，排除对抗目标和教师记忆等可能原因，从几何自由度的角度论证复制是高维蒸馏中模型容量受限导致的涌现现象。

Result: 证明了复制行为在高维蒸馏中普遍存在，且不是由对抗目标或教师记忆引起；证据表明复制是学生模型在高维空间中几何自由度受限的涌现结果。

Conclusion: 揭示了少步蒸馏中复制行为的本质原因，为理解和改进分布匹配蒸馏方法提供了理论洞见。

Classification reason: 该论文研究分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）中少步蒸馏的复制行为，属于扩散模型蒸馏与少步采样加速方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Reveals a fundamental emergent copying behavior in few-step distillation that impacts model efficiency and generation quality. Signals: Few-step distillation, emergent copying behavior, high-dimensional geometry, DMD analysis, model efficiency insight

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) compresses pretrained diffusion models into efficient few-step generators by aligning their noised distributions across all scales. In principle, such distribution-level supervision remains agnostic to specific noise-data pairings of the teacher; this provides the student the freedom to remap latent noise, a behavior consistently observed in low-dimensional settings. Surprisingly, we find that in high-dimensional settings, distilled students spontaneously reproduce the original noise-data pairings of the teacher, a phenomenon we term copying. We demonstrate that copying is neither a byproduct of adversarial objectives nor a result of teacher memorization. Instead, our evidence suggests that copying is an emergent property arising from the limited geometric freedom of the student model during high-dimensional distillation.

</details>


### [131] [Self-Regulating Annealing in Heavy-Tailed Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01645)
*Keito Wakatsuki, Hideaki Shimazaki*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (75.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SDE的重尾扩散模型采样器，通过状态依赖的扩散系数实现自调节退火机制，有效提升重尾数据分布上的采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯扩散模型在处理重尾数据集时效果不佳，虽然已有重尾扩散模型（HTDM）用Student's t分布替代高斯分布，但其基于SDE的采样方法尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一种显式包含状态依赖扩散系数的SDE采样器，该状态依赖性通过自适应调节有效噪声尺度，自然引入自调节退火机制，并从理论上分析了该机制的工作原理。

Result: 实验验证了该自调节退火机制对于从重尾分布中生成样本的必要性，表明所提SDE采样器能够有效提升重尾数据上的生成质量。

Conclusion: 状态依赖扩散系数带来的自调节退火机制是重尾扩散模型SDE采样的关键，为处理重尾数据分布的生成建模提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于SDE的重尾扩散模型采样器，引入状态依赖扩散系数实现自调节退火机制，属于扩散模型采样效率与方法的改进，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on heavy-tailed diffusion sampling, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Heavy-tailed diffusion, SDE sampler, self-regulating annealing, state-dependent diffusion, not autoregressive

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a leading framework for deep generative modeling. While the standard Gaussian formulation is theoretically convenient, its suitability for heavy-tailed datasets remains unclear. To address this, heavy-tailed diffusion models (HTDMs) extend the standard formulation by replacing the Gaussian distribution with a Student's t-distribution, thereby improving tail fidelity on heavy-tailed datasets. Although stochastic differential equation (SDE)-based sampling is possible in HTDMs, it has not been fully explored. In this paper, we propose an SDE-based sampler for HTDMs that explicitly incorporates a state-dependent diffusion coefficient. This state dependence naturally induces a self-regulating annealing mechanism by adaptively modulating the effective noise scale. We theoretically explore this mechanism and experimentally verify its necessity for reproducing samples from a heavy-tailed distribution.

</details>

### [132] [Improving Visual Representation Alignment Generation with GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00583)
*Shentong Mo, Sukmin Yun*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (65.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VRPO（生成表征策略优化），用强化学习替代扩散Transformer中静态的表征对齐损失（如REPA），将表征对齐建模为奖励引导过程，动态优化生成保真度、感知质量和语义一致性，在ImageNet-256×256上实现最高+1.8 FID提升和2.3倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征对齐框架（如REPA）使用固定的余弦对齐或对比学习损失，缺乏自适应性，无法在训练和推理过程中动态平衡表征一致性与生成质量，导致判别性收益有限且无法以任务自适应方式优化对齐。

Method: 提出VRPO（Generative Representation Policy Optimization），将表征对齐建模为奖励引导的强化学习过程：扩散模型在去噪过程中，其内部特征与预训练视觉编码器（如DINOv2）的嵌入之间的对齐程度通过生成保真度、感知质量和语义一致性奖励来引导，使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行优化，无缝集成到SiT和DiT架构中。

Result: 在ImageNet-256×256上，VRPO-Alignment相比REPA在相同计算预算下实现最高+1.8 FID提升和2.3倍训练加速，且引入的计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: VRPO通过强化学习动态优化表征对齐，有效提升了扩散Transformer的收敛速度和生成质量，为生成模型中的表征学习提供了新的自适应优化范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VRPO，用强化学习（GRPO）替代扩散Transformer中静态的表征对齐损失，将表征对齐视为奖励引导过程以提升生成质量。这属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化范畴，与[生成模型对齐与奖励]（generation_alignment）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly improves diffusion transformer training efficiency and generation quality via RL-based representation alignment, aligning with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: diffusion transformer, representation alignment, reinforcement learning, training acceleration, FID improvement

Abstract: Recent diffusion transformers have demonstrated strong image synthesis capabilities but remain inefficient to train due to weak alignment between generative and discriminative representations. While representation alignment frameworks such as REPA improve convergence by aligning noisy denoising features with pretrained visual encoders, their externally supervised alignment loss is static and lacks adaptivity during training and inference. Existing methods rely on fixed cosine alignment or contrastive objectives, which cannot dynamically balance representation consistency and generation quality, resulting in limited discriminative benefit and failing to optimize alignment in a task-adaptive manner. To address this, we propose VRPO, a reinforcement-based optimization strategy that replaces REPA's static alignment loss with a generative representation policy optimization objective. Instead of enforcing a fixed similarity constraint, VRPO treats representation alignment as a reward-guided process: the model receives adaptive rewards based on generation fidelity, perceptual quality, and semantic coherence between the diffusion features and pretrained visual embeddings. This formulation enables the generator to continuously refine its internal representations toward semantically meaningful directions while improving image quality. Our VRPO-driven training seamlessly integrates into diffusion transformers, introducing negligible computation cost and preserving full compatibility with SiT and DiT architectures. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-256x256 demonstrate that our VRPO-Alignment substantially enhances both convergence and fidelity, achieving up to +1.8 FID improvement and 2.3x faster training compared to REPA under identical compute budgets.

</details>


### [133] [TrustLDM: Benchmarking Trustworthiness in Language Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00023)
*Yichuan Mo, Yukun Jiang, Yanbo Shi, Mingjie Li, Michael Backes, Yang Zhang, Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrustLDM，一个针对语言扩散模型（LDM）的可信度评测基准，系统评估了不同 LDM 架构在安全、隐私和公平性三个维度的表现，并提出了自动评估框架 TrustLDM-Auto 以识别脆弱配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言扩散模型（LDM）因其灵活的任意顺序解码策略在语言处理中展现出潜力，但这种灵活性也可能带来新的可信度风险。现有工作缺乏对 LDM 可信度的系统性评估，因此需要构建专门的评测基准来理解其潜在风险。

Method: 构建 TrustLDM 基准，涵盖安全、隐私、公平性三个维度，使用多种恶意 post context 对 LDM 进行评测。进一步提出 TrustLDM-Auto 自动评估框架，利用 LDM 解码灵活性系统性地识别脆弱配置。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用用户提示时 LDM 表现出较强的可信度，但当恶意 post context 附加到掩码响应上时，对齐行为显著下降。较长的上下文不一定带来更强的影响，解码顺序和生成长度均影响评测结果。TrustLDM-Auto 揭示了所有被评估模型在多个维度上的可信度弱点。

Conclusion: 语言扩散模型在可信度方面存在显著脆弱性，尤其是在恶意上下文注入场景下。TrustLDM 和 TrustLDM-Auto 为构建更可信的 LDM 提供了系统化的评测工具和洞察。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦语言扩散模型（LDM）的可信度评测，涵盖安全、隐私、公平性等对齐维度，属于生成模型的对齐与评测范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on trustworthiness benchmarking for language diffusion models, which is tangentially related to your priority on continuous language models but lacks direct alignment with your core subtopics. Signals: Trustworthiness benchmark, Language diffusion models, Safety, privacy, fairness evaluation, Automatic vulnerability identification, Not directly on continuous token or video generation

Abstract: The rapid development of Language Diffusion Models (LDMs) challenges the dominant position of auto-regressive competitors in language processing. However, their flexible, any-order decoding strategies not only enable fast decoding speed but also potentially bring new trustworthiness challenges. To better understand the risks behind their pipelines, we introduce a comprehensive trustworthiness benchmark tailored to LDMs (TrustLDM), evaluating safety, privacy, and fairness across different LDM architectures with multiple categories of static post contexts. Our empirical results show that although LDMs generally exhibit strong trustworthiness with only the user prompts, their alignment behavior degrades noticeably when the malicious post contexts are attached to the masked responses. We further observe that longer contexts do not necessarily induce stronger effects, and both decoding order and generation length affect the evaluation outcomes. Finally, we propose TrustLDM-Auto, an automatic evaluation framework that leverages LDM decoding flexibility to systematically identify vulnerable configurations, revealing substantial trustworthiness weaknesses across all evaluated models and dimensions. Our work may potentially help the community build more trustworthy LDMs. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [134] [Geometric Erasure by Contrastive Velocity Matching in Rectified Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00140)
*Jonas Henry Grebe, Tobias Braun, Anna Rohrbach, Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GEM（Geometric Erasure by Matching），一种针对 Rectified Flow Transformer 的高效概念擦除框架。通过将基于轨迹的遗忘信号转化为教师引导的 flow-matching 目标，结合吸引与排斥信号实现几何引导，在抑制有害概念的同时保持良性生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态生成模型广泛应用，有害内容合成、深度伪造和版权侵权风险日益突出。概念擦除作为一种安全防护手段，但现有方法主要针对 U-Net 扩散模型，未能跟上 Rectified Flow Transformer 的快速发展。

Method: 提出 GEM 框架，建立基于轨迹的遗忘（源自生成流网络）与经典教师引导擦除之间的桥梁。将轨迹信号转化为教师引导的 flow-matching 设置：教师模型提供互补的吸引（attraction）和排斥（repulsion）信号，组合成统一的几何引导目标，实现对目标概念的选择性抑制。

Result: 在 Rectified Flow 模型上验证了 GEM 的有效性，能够有效擦除有害概念同时保持良性内容的生成质量，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GEM 为 Rectified Flow 模型提供了一种简单而高效的概念擦除框架，弥合了轨迹遗忘与教师引导擦除之间的理论鸿沟，为生成模型的安全防护提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Rectified Flow 模型中的概念擦除（concept erasure）方法，属于生成模型的可控性与对齐范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel erasure framework for Rectified Flows, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous models and generation. Signals: Rectified Flow Transformer, concept erasure, teacher-guided flow-matching, geometric guidance, generative flow networks

Abstract: While the rapid adoption of multimodal generative models offers immense potential, it has also increased the risks of harmful content synthesis, deepfakes, and copyright infringements. To address these challenges, concept erasure has emerged as a prospective safeguard. However, as the field gradually transitions from U-Net-based diffusion models to Rectified Flow Transformers, erasure research has struggled to keep pace. In this work, we introduce GEM, a simple but highly effective erasure framework for Rectified Flow models. As part of our contribution, we establish a principled bridge between trajectory-based unlearning grounded in Generative Flow Networks and classic teacher-guided erasure: we translate trajectory-based signals into a teacher-guided flow-matching setup that unifies the strengths of both paradigms. Concretely, a teacher provides complementary attraction and repulsion signals that we combine into a single geometric guidance objective, yielding targeted suppression of unwanted concepts while preserving benign generation.

</details>


### [135] [LithoGRPO: Fast Inverse Lithography via GRPO Reinforced Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00228)
*Yao Lai, Xuyuan Xiong, Zeyue Xue, Guojin Chen, Jing Wang, Xihui Liu, Rui Zhang, Robert Mullins, Bei Yu, Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LithoGRPO 将 flow matching 与 GRPO 强化学习相结合，用于半导体逆光刻掩码优化，通过显式物理奖励函数驱动生成，并提出了快速 shot-counting 算法加速评估，在掩码生成质量和效率上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆光刻技术（ILT）依赖优化方法或纯生成模型，但优化方法计算成本高，而生成模型难以利用 ILT 中显式定义的物理度量（如光刻保真度、可制造性）作为优化目标。作者希望将生成模型的效率与基于物理奖励的强化学习相结合，实现高效且高质量的掩码生成。

Method: 提出 LithoGRPO 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 基于 flow matching 的生成模型预训练，学习从目标布局到掩码的映射；(2) 使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行强化学习微调，利用显式定义的物理奖励函数（包括光刻保真度和可制造性指标）优化生成策略。同时提出快速 shot-counting 算法，将可制造性评估加速 130 倍以上。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，LithoGRPO 在掩码保真度和可制造性指标上均超越现有优化方法和学习方法，同时保持高效的生成速度。快速 shot-counting 算法在加速 130 倍的同时保持了与传统指标一致的掩码排序。

Conclusion: 首次将 flow matching 与强化学习统一用于逆光刻掩码优化，验证了基于物理奖励的 RL 微调能有效提升生成模型在特定物理任务上的性能，为将生成模型应用于物理约束下的工业优化问题提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文将 flow matching 生成范式与 GRPO 强化学习结合，利用显式物理奖励函数优化光刻掩码生成，属于生成模型与奖励/对齐方法的结合。虽然应用领域特殊（逆光刻），但其核心方法（flow matching + RL fine-tuning with reward）与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies flow matching and GRPO to a niche semiconductor domain, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: flow matching + GRPO, inverse lithography, fast shot-counting algorithm, domain-specific physical reward, no continuous language/video focus

Abstract: In semiconductor manufacturing, lithography projects circuit layouts onto silicon wafers through an optical mask. As circuit features shrink below the wavelength of light, optical diffraction causes the printed patterns to deviate from their intended layouts. Inverse Lithography Technology (ILT) addresses this challenge by generating optimized masks that enhance the fidelity of pattern transfer onto wafers. While ILT resembles an image synthesis task, its reliance on explicit physical metrics for mask evaluation limits the applicability of existing generative models. We introduce LithoGRPO, an ILT framework that integrates the flow-matching paradigm with GRPO-based reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning, enabling efficient exploration of diverse masks for a given target layout. Unlike purely generative or optimization-based approaches, RL in LithoGRPO exploits the explicitly defined, physics-based reward function of ILT, enabling optimization under complex, process-aware constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework that unifies flow matching and RL for mask optimization. To improve RL sampling efficiency, we propose a fast shot-counting algorithm for manufacturability evaluation, achieving over 130x speedup while preserving the mask ranking of the traditional shot-count metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LithoGRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance over both optimization-based and learning-based methods, while maintaining efficient mask generation.

</details>


### [136] [DiffCrossGait: Trajectory-Level Alignment for 2D-3D Cross-Modal Gait Recognition via Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00153)
*Zhiyang Lu, Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DiffCrossGait 提出了一种基于潜扩散的2D-3D跨模态步态识别方法，通过共享高斯噪声驱动两种模态在潜空间中进行轨迹级对齐，并采用三阶段对齐策略（身份锚定、动态一致性、跨模态结构可恢复性）来学习模态不变特征，在SUSTech1K和FreeGait基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态2D-3D步态识别方法仅对齐最终嵌入向量，忽略了2D轮廓与3D LiDAR距离视图之间固有的域差异，无法充分捕捉模态间的细粒度对应关系。

Method: 提出DiffCrossGait框架，将跨模态匹配重新定义为身份相关的潜扩散空间中的轨迹级对齐。通过共享高斯噪声驱动两种模态在潜空间中经历相同的去噪演化过程，并设计三阶段对齐策略（Tri-Phase Alignment Strategy），利用不同噪声强度分别实现身份锚定、动态一致性和跨模态结构可恢复性。扩散机制仅作为训练目标，推理时无需迭代去噪，保持高效。

Result: 在SUSTech1K和FreeGait两个基准数据集上，DiffCrossGait取得了最先进的跨模态步态识别性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过潜扩散空间中的轨迹级对齐而非简单的最终嵌入对齐，可以有效弥合2D和3D模态间的域差异，学习到更具判别力的模态不变步态特征，且该方法在推理时无额外计算开销。

Classification reason: 该论文使用潜空间扩散过程对齐2D和3D步态特征，属于生成模型中的跨模态对齐方法，与[生成模型对齐与奖励]（generation_alignment）子主题中的"对齐"概念高度吻合。虽然不涉及RLHF，但核心是跨模态表征对齐，且扩散机制作为训练目标用于对齐，符合该子主题的广义对齐范畴。

Importance reason: Cross-modal gait recognition is a niche biometrics task with limited overlap with your priority subtopics on continuous language/video generation and world models. Signals: latent diffusion for alignment, trajectory-level alignment, 2D-3D cross-modal, gait recognition, decoupled generative alignment

Abstract: Cross-modal 2D-3D gait recognition is impeded by inherent domain discrepancies between 2D silhouette and 3D LiDAR range-view representations. While prior methods align only final embeddings, we propose DiffCrossGait, which reformulates cross-modal matching as trajectory-level alignment in an identity-relevant latent diffusion space, rather than assuming full equivalence between 2D and 3D observations. By driving both modalities with shared Gaussian noise within a latent space, we enable continuous alignment throughout the generative evolution. We introduce a Tri-Phase Alignment Strategy that exploits varying noise intensities to enforce identity anchoring, dynamics consistency, and cross-modal structural recoverability, thereby constraining both modalities to share denoising dynamics and bottleneck structure, which promotes modality-invariant gait features. Crucially, our framework decouples generative alignment from the discriminative backbone; the diffusion mechanism serves exclusively as a training objective, ensuring high inference efficiency by eliminating the computational overhead of iterative denoising. Extensive experiments on the SUSTech1K and FreeGait benchmarks demonstrate that DiffCrossGait achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [137] [APE: Agentic Prompt Enhancer for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00204)
*Zijian Huang, Jay Zhangjie Wu, Zian Wang, Tianshi Cao, Jiasi Chen, Sanja Fidler, Huan Ling, Xuanchi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (71.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 APE（Agentic Prompt Enhancer），一个轻量级框架，通过后训练小语言模型作为提示增强智能体，支持单智能体（SAPE）和多智能体（MAPE）两种模式，利用任务感知奖励优化提示质量，在不修改下游视觉模型的前提下提升图像生成与编辑的视觉对齐和指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的图像生成与编辑系统对提示措辞高度敏感，语义相似的请求因表述不同可能产生差异很大的输出。现有强大的提示增强器依赖 ChatGPT 或 Gemini 等大型闭源 LLM，带来成本、延迟和部署依赖问题。因此需要一种轻量级、可训练的提示增强方案。

Method: 提出 APE 框架，后训练小语言模型（SLM）作为提示增强智能体。单智能体版本 SAPE 一次性重写提示；多智能体版本 MAPE 将增强过程分解为路由器（router）、重写器（rewriter）和组合器（composer）三阶段，分别处理对象、属性、空间关系和编辑等组合约束。采用任务感知奖励（task-aware rewards）和后训练协议优化模型。

Result: 在图像生成和编辑基准上，后训练的小提示增强器可靠地优于其基础版本，缩小了与闭源提示增强器的差距；MAPE 在复杂组合任务上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 轻量级、可训练的提示增强框架 APE 能够有效提升图像生成与编辑的提示质量和对齐效果，无需依赖大型闭源 LLM，为实际部署提供了更高效、低成本的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Agentic Prompt Enhancer (APE)，通过后训练小语言模型作为提示增强智能体，利用任务感知奖励来提升图像生成与编辑中的视觉对齐和指令跟随能力。这属于生成模型中对齐与奖励优化的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on prompt enhancement for image generation, which is a secondary tool rather than a core advance in the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Prompt enhancement, small language model, image generation and editing, task-aware reward, multi-agent decomposition

Abstract: Natural language has become a powerful interface for image generation and editing, yet text-guided visual systems remain highly sensitive to prompt formulation. Semantically similar requests can produce different outputs depending on wording, specificity, and how explicitly visual constraints are stated, motivating prompt enhancement as a trainable component rather than a peripheral user choice. Existing strong enhancers often rely on large, proprietary LLMs such as ChatGPT or Gemini, adding cost, latency, and deployment dependence to the visual generation pipeline. We propose Agentic Prompt Enhancer (APE), a lightweight framework that post-trains small language models (SLMs) as prompt-enhancement agents. APE supports both single-agent rewriting and role-specialized multi-agent enhancement. Its single-agent instantiation, SAPE, rewrites the prompt in one pass, while its multi-agent instantiation, MAPE, decomposes enhancement into a router--rewriter--composer process for handling compositional constraints over objects, attributes, spatial relations, and edits. With task-aware rewards and post-training protocols, APE improves visual alignment and prompt following without modifying the downstream visual model. Experiments on challenging image generation and editing benchmarks demonstrate that post-trained small prompt enhancers reliably outperform their base counterparts, narrowing the gap to closed-source prompt enhancers; in addition, MAPE proves particularly strong on complex compositional tasks within these benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [Score-Control for Hallucination Reduction in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00377)
*Mahesh Bhosale, Naresh Kumar Devulapally, Abdul Wasi, Chau Pham, Vishnu Suresh Lokhande, David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方差引导的分数调制（VSM）策略，通过控制扩散模型中的分数Jacobian来减少分数平滑性，从而缓解图像生成中的幻觉问题，在合成和真实数据集上实现了约25%的幻觉降低，同时保持高保真度和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像生成中常产生幻觉（即位于真实数据分布支撑集之外的不可信样本），影响可靠性和信任度。先前研究提出分数平滑性可能导致幻觉的假设，但缺乏系统性验证和缓解方法。本文旨在实证验证该假设并从密度角度形式化幻觉与分数Lipschitz常数的关系，进而提出有效的幻觉缓解策略。

Method: 1) 首先通过实验验证了分数平滑性导致幻觉的假设，并从密度角度提供了理论解释；2) 形式化地将幻觉概率质量与学习到的分数函数的Lipschitz常数联系起来；3) 提出方差引导的分数调制（Variance-Guided Score Modulation, VSM）策略，通过控制分数Jacobian来减少分数平滑性，使其更好地逼近真实分数函数，从而减少幻觉；4) 构建了两个具有极端语义变化的新基准数据集用于系统评估幻觉。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集（CIFAR-10、CelebA、ImageNet）上的实验表明，VSM策略可将幻觉减少约25%，同时保持生成样本的高保真度和多样性。两个新构建的基准数据集为幻觉的系统性评估提供了标准化测试平台。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实践上验证了分数平滑性与扩散模型幻觉之间的因果关系，提出的VSM策略为缓解幻觉提供了一种原则性的有效方法，推动了更可靠的扩散图像生成的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型中的幻觉缓解问题，提出基于分数Jacobian控制的方差引导分数调制（VSM）策略，属于生成模型的可控性与可靠性优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中关于guidance与可控性的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in diffusion models but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: diffusion model hallucination, score smoothness, variance-guided modulation, image generation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the backbone of modern generative AI, powering advances in vision, language, audio and other modalities. Despite their success, they suffer from hallucinations, implausible samples that lie outside the support of true data distribution, which degrade reliability and trust. In this work, we first empirically confirm previously proposed hypothesis that score smoothness causes hallucinations in Image Generation diffusion models and provide a density-based perspective. We further formalize this notion by linking the hallucinations probability mass to lipschitz constant of the learned score function. Motivated by this, we introduce a Variance-Guided Score Modulation (VSM) strategy that controls the score Jacobian, in turn reducing score smoothness and better approximating the ground truth score that decreases hallucinations. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach reduces hallucinations (up to ~25%) while maintaining high fidelity and diversity, providing a principled step toward more reliable diffusion-based image generation. We also propose two benchmark datasets with extreme semantic variation for systematic hallucination evaluation. Code and Datasets are publicly available at .

</details>


### [139] [Generate in Reconstruction Space, Match in Semantic Space: Transport Geometry for One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00514)
*Hugues Van Assel, Edward De Brouwer, Saeed Saremi, Gabriele Scalia, Aviv Regev*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (89.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出在语义SSL特征空间中使用Sinkhorn散度作为单步生成模型的训练目标，通过将生成样本与真实样本在特征空间中进行分布匹配，显著提升了生成质量（ImageNet FID降低39倍），并揭示了SSL特征空间几何结构对分布匹配可处理性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模与自监督表征学习（SSL）优化不同的目标：生成训练追求分布保真度，SSL追求语义一致性。尽管已有工作发现SSL特征能改善生成训练，但其协同机制尚不明确。本文旨在理解SSL在生成建模中的作用，特别是在单步生成框架中。

Method: 使用冻结的SSL特征提取器，在特征空间中计算生成样本与真实样本之间的Sinkhorn散度，作为Wasserstein距离的可计算替代，并将其作为单步生成模型的训练目标。通过理论分析和大量实验，研究不同SSL特征族（如DINO、CLIP、MAE等）对生成性能的影响。

Result: 在ImageNet上实现FID降低39倍；发现语义SSL特征通过抑制噪声重建细节产生更紧凑的几何结构，使分布匹配更易处理；最佳训练SSL特征不必与评测指标使用的特征一致；使用Inception作为特征提取器可改善FID但降低匹配稳定性和样本质量，揭示了一种"指标黑客"现象。

Conclusion: SSL特征空间的结构化几何特性是提升生成模型性能的关键，匹配稳定性可作为选择SSL特征的有效定量标准。该工作为理解生成模型与表征学习之间的协同提供了理论框架和实践指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究生成模型中使用SSL特征空间进行分布匹配（Sinkhorn散度）以提升单步生成质量，属于生成模型训练中的对齐与优化方法，与[生成模型对齐与奖励]（generation_alignment）中关于guidance、可控性和训练优化的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel SSL feature-space distribution matching objective for one-step generation, achieving dramatic FID reduction and revealing geometric insights into representation learning. Signals: Sinkhorn divergence in SSL feature space, 39x ImageNet FID reduction, reveals metric hacking with Inception, connects SSL geometry to generation tractability, strong empirical results on ImageNet

Abstract: Generative modeling and self-supervised representation learning (SSL) optimize structurally different objectives: generative training rewards distributional fidelity, while SSL rewards semantic coherence. Yet recent work repeatedly finds that SSL features improve generative training, though the mechanism of this synergy remains unclear. Here, we study the benefits of SSL in generative modeling in the framework of one-step generation where the role of representation is explicit: frozen SSL features are used to match generated samples to real data. We use the Sinkhorn divergence in that feature space, providing a tractable surrogate for the Wasserstein distance, the population-level discrepancy approximated by Fréchet-style evaluation metrics (such as FID). We find that this objective becomes highly effective when computed in a semantically structured SSL feature space (a 39$\times$ reduction in ImageNet FID). We trace this behavior primarily to matching estimation: semantic SSL features that suppress nuisance reconstruction details induce a more compact geometry, making distribution matching more tractable. As a consequence, the best training SSL features need not match the features used by the evaluation metric. In particular, we show that using Inception as the feature extractor can improve FID while degrading matching stability and sample quality, revealing a form of metric hacking. Using extensive experiments on ImageNet, we identify which SSL feature families lead to best generation performance and show that matching stability is a quantitative criterion for selecting them. Code is available at .

</details>


### [140] [OctoT2I: A Self-Evolving Agentic Text-to-Image Router](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01803)
*Xu Jiang, Bin Chen, Gehui Li, Yule Duan, Ronggang Wang, Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OctoT2I 是一个自演化的智能体文本到图像路由框架，通过多轮状态化路由策略和自演化机制（PSEL 循环），在多个 T2I 模型间自适应选择最优工具，在 GenEval 上达到 0.96 的同时实现 90.3% 推理加速和 56.6% 能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型（从大规模到轻量实时）面临单模型扩展的边际收益递减问题。智能体 T2I 方法虽尝试使用多模型缓解瓶颈，但存在三大挑战：依赖昂贵的人工先验或标注、僵化的单路径决策机制、忽视推理效率。因此需要一种无需人工监督、能自适应路由且兼顾效率与质量的框架。

Method: OctoT2I 框架包含：(1) 状态化多轮路由策略，基于知识库和记忆自适应选择最合适的 T2I 工具；(2) 自演化机制，从零开始自主定义基础概念维度（如风格、颜色、数量），然后通过迭代的"提出-求解-评估-学习"(PSEL) 循环智能探索维度组合，高效发现每个工具的能力边界，实现持续改进而无需外部指导。

Result: 在 GenEval 上达到 0.96 的竞争性能，同时相比领先基线 Flow-GRPO 实现 90.3% 推理加速和 56.6% 能效提升，在性能与效率之间取得了出色的平衡。

Conclusion: OctoT2I 通过自演化智能体路由策略有效解决了多 T2I 模型协作中的效率与质量权衡问题，无需人工标注即可自动发现各模型能力边界，为多模型协同生成提供了一种高效、可扩展的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个智能体框架 OctoT2I，通过自演化机制在多个 T2I 模型间进行路由选择，以联合优化生成质量和推理效率。这属于多模态生成方向下的生成模型对齐与可控性（路由选择本质上是对齐不同模型的能力与任务需求），与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）相关，因为其核心是通过自演化学习各模型的能力边界并进行最优路由决策，涉及偏好优化与可控性。

Importance reason: Novel agentic routing for T2I with self-evolution and efficiency focus, but outside core priority subtopics. Signals: agentic text-to-image routing, self-evolving mechanism, inference efficiency optimization, GenEval 0.96, 90.3% speedup

Abstract: The explosive growth of Text-to-Image (T2I) models, from large-scale versions to lightweight, real-time ones, now faces diminishing marginal returns from single-model scaling. Agentic T2I methods emerged to alleviate this bottleneck by using multiple models. However, existing agentic T2I methods suffer from three key challenges: reliance on expensive handcrafted priors or human annotations, rigid single-path decision mechanisms, and a neglect of inference efficiency. To address these challenges, we introduce OctoT2I, a novel agentic framework that reformulates the T2I task as a joint optimization of generation quality and inference efficiency. OctoT2I implements a stateful, multi-round routing strategy that adaptively selects the most suitable tool based on its knowledge and memory. This strategy is enabled by a knowledge base built from scratch by our novel Self-Evolving Mechanism. This mechanism, which requires no human supervision, first autonomously defines foundational Conceptual Dimensions (eg, style, color, count) and then intelligently explores their combinations via an iterative" Propose--Solve--Evaluate--Learn"(PSEL) loop. The PSEL loop efficiently discovers each tool's capability frontier, driving continuous improvement without external guidance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OctoT2I achieves competitive performance (0.96) on GenEval while delivering a 90.3% inference speedup and a 56.6% energy-efficiency gain over the leading baseline (Flow-GRPO), striking an exceptional balance between performance and efficiency. Code and models will be made available.

</details>


### [141] [KG-FairDiff: Knowledge Graph-Guided Prompt Refinement for Demographically Fair Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01282)
*Farbod Davoodi, Seyed Reza Tavakoli Shiyadeh, Pooria Safaei, Sana Harighi, Parsa Gholami, Amirali Amini, Kimia Vanaei, Emad Firoozi, Parham Abed Azad, Babak Khalaj, Siavash Ahmadi, Amir Hossein Payberah, Mohammad Hossein Rohban, Soheil Kolouri, Ali Diba*

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (69.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KG-FairDiff 是一个模型无关的推理时框架，通过知识图谱引导的提示词优化来缓解文生图模型中的性别、种族、年龄等人口统计偏见，将公平感知提示优化形式化为约束优化问题，并证明了优化循环的有限终止性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图系统在部署时会产生人口统计层面的偏见（如对女性、有色人种、老年人等群体的刻板印象或欠表征），而现有缓解方法要么需要昂贵的重新训练（对闭源模型不可行），要么依赖忽略文化上下文的固定模板。因此需要一种无需重新训练、模型无关、且能保留语义保真度的推理时公平性优化方法。

Method: 提出 KG-FairDiff 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 一个包含约1200条文化与偏见相关三元组的知识图谱，用于检索结构化上下文；(2) 一个 LLM 重写器，基于检索到的知识提出提示词优化建议；(3) 一个验证器，仅接受能降低基于散度的公平性损失且保留原始语义的优化提示。将公平感知提示优化形式化为约束优化问题，并证明了优化循环的有限终止性。同时提出了一个数学一致的评测套件，将 Bias-P/Bias-W 与目标分布的散度关联，将 ENS 与 KL 散度关联。

Result: 在八个广泛部署的骨干生成器上进行了审计，KG-FairDiff 显著降低了性别、种族、年龄及交叉维度上的不公平性，同时保留了提示的语义保真度。

Conclusion: KG-FairDiff 提供了一种实用、可部署的推理时公平性优化方案，无需重新训练即可有效缓解文生图模型中的多种人口统计偏见，为更公平的生成式 AI 提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文关注文生图系统中的公平性偏差缓解，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性问题。论文提出知识图谱引导的提示词优化框架以减少人口统计偏见，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的对齐、可控性、偏好优化等主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on demographic fairness in text-to-image generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with the user's core interests in continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: model-agnostic inference-time framework, knowledge graph for bias mitigation, constrained optimization for prompt refinement, finite-termination proof, evaluation on gender, race, age disparities

Abstract: Text-to-Image (TTI) systems are now everyday infrastructure for journalism, education, advertising, and public communication, and the demographic and cultural stereotypes they inherit from training data (rendering women, people of colour, older adults, and non-Western cultures as under-represented or caricatured) become a population-level harm at deployment scale. Existing mitigations either require costly retraining, infeasible for the closed-source backbones that dominate consumer products, or rely on fixed demographic templates that ignore cultural context. We present KG-FairDiff, a model-agnostic, inference-time framework that formalises fairness-aware prompt refinement as a constrained optimisation problem and operationalises it as a closed-loop pipeline: a knowledge graph of ~1,200 culture- and bias-related triples retrieves structured context, an LLM rewriter proposes refinements, and a validator accepts only prompts that reduce a divergence-based fairness loss while preserving semantic fidelity to the user's original intent. We prove a finite-termination bound for the refinement loop, contribute a mathematically consistent evaluation suite linking Bias-P/Bias-W to divergence from target distributions and ENS to KL divergence, and audit eight widely-deployed backbone generators. KG-FairDiff substantially reduces gender, race, age, and intersectional disparities while preserving prompt semantics, offering a practical, deployment-ready route to more equitable generative AI.

</details>


### [142] [Pave-GRPO: Beyond Instantaneous Guidance through Principled Average Velocity Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01636)
*Pengyang Ling, Jiazi Bu, Yujie Zhou, Yibin Wang, Zhenyu Hu, Zihan Zhang, Yi Jin, Huaian Chen, Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (57.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Pave-GRPO 提出基于平均速度分解的 GRPO 目标函数改进，在不增加生成成本的前提下，将稀疏的奖励反馈传播到更多中间去噪阶段，实现更细粒度的流模型偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 GRPO 的流模型后训练对齐方法因迭代去噪成本高，只能使用极少的去噪步数进行组采样，导致奖励反馈仅能覆盖少数时间步，大部分中间去噪阶段缺乏直接监督，对齐粒度不足。

Method: Pave-GRPO 保持高效少步组采样，但通过平均速度分解将每个粗粒度的过渡步骤等价分解为多个中间时间步的细粒度子轨迹集合，从而在不增加生成开销的情况下将奖励信号传播到更密集的时间阶段，实现零成本的优化范围扩展和全面的时间监督。

Result: 大量实验表明，Pave-GRPO 在不同奖励设置下均有效提升了偏好对齐效果，实现了全面的性能提升。

Conclusion: Pave-GRPO 通过平均速度分解策略，在不增加计算成本的前提下显著增强了流模型偏好对齐的细粒度与全面性，为流生成模型的后训练对齐提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Pave-GRPO，通过平均速度分解改进流匹配生成模型的后训练对齐（GRPO），属于生成模型对齐与偏好优化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a principled method to improve GRPO alignment for flow models, relevant to continuous language/multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: flow matching alignment, GRPO improvement, zero-cost horizon expansion, preference optimization, diffusion language model

Abstract: Post-training via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning flow-based generative models with human preferences. However, the iterative denoising nature of flow models incurs substantial costs when generating group rollouts for policy-gradient updates, compelling existing methods to train with extremely few denoising steps. This temporal sparsity severely restricts preference optimization: reward feedback can only reach a handful of stages per trajectory, leaving the vast majority of intermediate denoising steps without direct supervision and thus compromising alignment granularity. To address this, we propose Pave-GRPO, which reformulates the GRPO objective through Principled average velocity decomposition. Rather than generating expensive high-step rollouts, we maintain efficient few-step group sampling but decompose each coarse transition into an equivalent ensemble of finer sub-trajectories spanning multiple intermediate timesteps. This propagates reward feedback to a denser set of temporal stages for more comprehensive preference alignment without additional generation cost. This design offers two benefits: (i) zero-cost horizon expansion: through the direct reuse of piece-wise group samples and their associated rewards, Pave-GRPO significantly broadens the effective optimization scope under fixed sampling budgets; and (ii) comprehensive temporal supervision: by equivalently decomposing an instantaneous velocity target into a multi-timestep ensemble, it distributes reward signals across more intermediate stages of the denoising process, enabling finer-grained and more thorough preference optimization. Extensive experiments validate that Pave-GRPO effectively advances preference alignment across different reward settings, offering comprehensive performance enhancement.

</details>


### [143] [Hallucination-Aware Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems via Robust Prior Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02331)
*Pengfei Jin, Yiqi Tian, Kailong Fan, Bingjie Qi, Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (47.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散模型逆问题求解器中的"测量条件幻觉"问题，指出幻觉内容源于先验更新步骤，并提出 Robust Prior Update (RPU) 模块——通过探测先验更新的局部稳定性并重新锚定位移来提升实例忠实性。在 FFHQ 和 ImageNet 上的实验表明，RPU 在 PSNR、LPIPS 和人类偏好评估上均优于 DPS 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型逆问题求解器虽然能生成逼真的重建结果，但逼真性并不保证恢复的细节与测量数据一致。现有方法缺乏对"测量条件幻觉"（即视觉上合理但与测量实例不一致的内容）的系统性分析和缓解手段。

Method: 将基于贝叶斯规则的扩散逆问题求解器分解为先验更新和测量条件化两个步骤，指出幻觉内容在测量校正之前通过先验侧提议进入。提出 RPU 模块：探测扩散先验更新的局部稳定性，将得到的位移重新锚定到当前迭代点，同时保持测量更新不变。在 DPS 框架中实例化 RPU。

Result: 在 FFHQ 上，RPU 在框内修复、高斯去模糊和运动去模糊任务中均提升了 PSNR 和 LPIPS。人类评估中，RPU 在 FFHQ 框内修复上获得 91.9% 的盲选非平局多数偏好和 91.1% 的辅助非平局偏好。ImageNet 高斯去模糊实验中平局较多，但非平局情况下 RPU 占优。

Conclusion: 鲁棒化先验更新可以有效提升扩散逆问题求解器的实例忠实性，尤其是在先验主导弱约束内容时效果显著。RPU 作为一个求解器级别的模块，无需重新训练即可集成到现有方法中。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型逆问题求解中的幻觉问题，提出 Robust Prior Update (RPU) 模块来提升生成结果与测量数据的一致性（instance faithfulness），属于生成模型的可控性与对齐范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题"生成模型对齐与奖励"。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion inverse problem hallucination, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: diffusion inverse problems, hallucination analysis, robust prior update, instance faithfulness, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Diffusion-based inverse problem solvers can produce realistic reconstructions, but realism alone does not ensure that the recovered details are supported by the measurement. We study this failure as measurement-conditioned hallucination: visually meaningful content that is either implausible or inconsistent with the measured instance. Our analysis separates Bayes-rule-based diffusion inverse solvers into a prior update and a measurement-conditioning step, showing that hallucinated content can enter through the prior-side proposal before the measurement correction is applied. Motivated by this view, we propose Robust Prior Update (RPU), a solver-level module that probes the local stability of the diffusion prior update, re-anchors the resulting displacement at the current iterate, and leaves the measurement update unchanged. We instantiate RPU in DPS and evaluate it on FFHQ and ImageNet inverse problems using automatic metrics and human faithfulness studies. On FFHQ, RPU improves PSNR and LPIPS over DPS across box inpainting, Gaussian deblurring, and motion deblurring. In human judgments, RPU receives 91.9% of blind non-tie majority preferences and 91.1% of ground-truth-assisted non-tie preferences on FFHQ box inpainting, while the ImageNet Gaussian reader study is tie-heavy but favors RPU among non-tie cases. These results support a targeted claim: robustifying the prior update can improve instance faithfulness in diffusion inverse solvers, especially when the prior shapes weakly constrained content.

</details>


### [144] [Drifting Preference Optimization for One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02521)
*Zhou Jiang, Yandong Wen, Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (73.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Drifting Preference Optimization (DrPO)，一种面向确定性单步文生图生成器的在线偏好微调方法。DrPO 通过从当前生成器中采样候选图像，用目标奖励排序后合成特征空间更新方向（非参数偶极偏好场 + 参考漂移），无需奖励模型反向传播或策略似然，在 SD-Turbo 和 SDXL-Turbo 上显著提升对齐效果并降低训练计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步文生图生成器（如 SD-Turbo）在部署中具有高效优势，但偏好微调困难：标准对齐方法通常依赖策略似然、去噪轨迹、可微奖励梯度或测试时优化，这些方法不适用于确定性单步生成器。因此需要一种无需奖励梯度、无需策略似然的在线偏好微调方法。

Method: DrPO 包含三个关键组件：(1) 从当前生成器采样多个候选图像，用目标奖励函数（黑盒/不可微均可）排序；(2) 构造非参数偶极偏好场（dipole preference field），将高/低分样本的特征差异作为更新方向；(3) 引入参考漂移项（reference drift），从冻结的基础生成器估计，防止偏离预训练分布。最终通过解耦的特征空间回归目标进行优化，推理时仍为单次前向传播。

Result: 在 SD-Turbo 和 SDXL-Turbo 上使用 HPSv3 和 GenEval 等奖励/基准评估，DrPO 在无需奖励梯度的单步偏好基线中取得最佳对齐效果；在匹配有效批大小设置下，通过去除奖励模型反向传播，将 HPSv3 训练计算量降低 3.51 倍。

Conclusion: DrPO 为单步确定性生成器提供了一种高效、无需奖励梯度的在线偏好微调方案，实验证明其对齐效果优于现有基线，且计算开销显著降低。初步离线实验还表明，基于样本的梯度合成方法可推广到在线奖励排序之外的场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DrPO，一种针对单步文生图生成器的在线偏好微调方法，属于生成模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel online preference optimization for one-step generators, but the subtopic is not in the user's priority list. Signals: One-step generative models, Preference optimization, Reward-free gradient, SD-Turbo, SDXL-Turbo

Abstract: One-step text-to-image generators are attractive for deployment because they generate an image with a single forward pass, but preference finetuning them remains difficult: standard alignment methods often rely on policy likelihoods, denoising trajectories, differentiable reward gradients, or test-time optimization. We propose Drifting Preference Optimization (DrPO), an online preference-finetuning method for deterministic one-step generators. For each prompt, DrPO samples candidates from the current generator, ranks them with a target reward, and uses high- and low-scoring samples to synthesize a feature-space update direction. The update is a non-parametric dipole preference field plus a reference drift estimated from the frozen base generator, and is optimized through a detached feature-space regression target. The target reward is used only for ranking, so DrPO can train with large, black-box, or non-differentiable rewards while inference remains a single generator call. We evaluate DrPO on SD-Turbo and SDXL-Turbo with multiple target rewards and benchmarks, including HPSv3 and GenEval. DrPO improves alignment over reward-gradient-free one-step preference baselines and reduces HPSv3 training computation by $3.51\times$ under the matched effective-batch setting by removing reward-model backpropagation. Initial offline experiments suggest that sample-based gradient synthesis can also be used beyond online reward ranking.

</details>


### [145] [Initialization is Half the Battle: Generating Diverse Images from a Guidance Potential Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02453)
*Xiang Li, Dianbo Liu, Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出标准高斯初始化常导致生成模型陷入主导模式，提出 DivIn 方法，从 guidance potential posterior 中采样初始噪声，利用 Langevin 动力学引导初始噪声远离坍缩区域，同时锚定在有效数据流形上。该方法是一种推理时多样性增强技术，兼容扩散模型和 flow matching 模型，且与轨迹级方法正交，可显著扩展多样性-质量帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽保真度高，但常出现模式坍缩（mode collapse）。已有增强多样性的方法主要聚焦于生成轨迹中的干预，而忽略了标准高斯初始化对 guidance potential landscape 的不可知性，导致轨迹容易坍缩到主导模式。

Method: 将初始噪声的选择建模为从 guidance potential posterior 中采样，该后验有效重新加权先验，使其偏向多样性丰富的区域。为高效采样，提出 DivIn（Diversity-inducing Initialization），利用 Langevin 动力学主动导航初始化 landscape，引导初始噪声远离坍缩区域，同时将其锚定在有效数据流形上。该方法作为推理时增强，兼容扩散模型和 flow matching 模型。

Result: 在 class-to-image 和 text-to-image 场景下的大量实验表明，DivIn 在多样性方面表现优越。与轨迹级方法结合时，能显著扩展多样性-质量帕累托前沿，超越单独使用任一方法的效果。

Conclusion: 初始化选择是影响生成多样性的关键因素，DivIn 通过 guidance potential posterior 采样初始噪声有效缓解模式坍缩，且与现有轨迹级方法正交互补，为提升生成模型多样性提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DivIn 方法，通过 Langevin 动力学从 guidance potential posterior 中采样初始噪声，以提升扩散模型和 flow matching 模型的生成多样性。这属于生成模型中对 guidance 和可控性的优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中 guidance 与可控性的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly addresses diversity-quality Pareto frontier in diffusion/flow matching, aligning with user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models and flow matching. Signals: inference-time diversity enhancement, guidance potential posterior, Langevin dynamics initialization, compatible with diffusion and flow matching, orthogonal to trajectory methods

Abstract: Despite the remarkable fidelity of generative models, they frequently suffer from mode collapse. Existing strategies for enhancing diversity predominantly focus on intervening during the generation trajectory. We identify a critical oversight that the standard Gaussian initialization often causes trajectories to collapse into dominant modes because it is agnostic to the guidance potential landscape. In this work, we formulate selecting the initial noise from a guidance potential posterior, which effectively re-weights the prior towards diversity-rich regions. To sample from this distribution efficiently, we introduce Diversity-inducing Initialization (DivIn), which leverages Langevin dynamics to actively navigate the initialization landscape, steering initial noise away from collapsing regions while anchoring them to the valid data manifold. Our method serves as an inference-time diversity enhancement compatible with both diffusion and flow matching models. Extensive experiments show that DivIn exhibits a superior performance in both class-to-image and text-to-image scenarios. Furthermore, we highlight that as DivIn is orthogonal to trajectory-based methods, combining them significantly expands the diversity-quality Pareto frontier beyond what either achieves in isolation.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-generation_benchmark'></div>
