### [146] [Temporally-Aligned Evaluation for Audio-Driven Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01031)
*Zhicheng Zhang, Lei Wang, Yu Zhang, Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.GR, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出音频驱动说话头生成的现有帧级评测指标因假设严格时序对应而存在缺陷，提出将评测重新定义为序列对齐问题，引入Soft Dynamic Time Warping实现时序对齐的评测框架，在20种方法、7个数据集上验证了该方法的鲁棒性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话头生成的评测协议依赖帧级指标，假设生成视频与参考视频之间存在严格的逐帧时序对应。然而语音驱动的面部运动天然包含轻微时序偏移、不同语速和风格变化，导致传统指标将无害的时序差异误判为质量错误，难以公平比较不同方法。

Method: 将动态生成模型的评测重新表述为序列对齐问题，提出将Soft Dynamic Time Warping（Soft DTW）集成到现有评测管线中。通过对齐特征轨迹同时保持时序顺序，该框架对有限时序错位具有鲁棒性，且不改变底层的感知、身份或同步编码器。帧级评测被视为刚性对齐下的特例。

Result: 在7个数据集上对20种方法进行大规模基准测试，实验表明时序对齐的指标对时序差异更鲁棒，跨数据集结果更一致，并能更好揭示不同建模范式之间的系统权衡（如同步性与真实感、表现力与稳定性之间的权衡）。

Conclusion: 时序对齐的评测框架比传统帧级评测更适用于音频驱动说话头生成任务，能够提供更稳定、更公平的评估结果，并有助于理解不同方法的设计权衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于时序对齐（Soft DTW）的音频驱动说话头生成评测框架，属于生成模型的评测方法创新，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a principled evaluation reformulation for talking head generation, but the topic is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: evaluation reformulation, temporal alignment, Soft Dynamic Time Warping, talking head generation, benchmark

Abstract: Audio-driven talking-head generation has advanced rapidly, yet existing evaluation protocols mainly rely on frame-wise metrics that assume strict temporal correspondence between generated and reference videos. This assumption does not match speech-driven facial motion, which naturally includes slight timing shifts, different speaking speeds, and stylistic variations. As a result, conventional metrics may treat harmless timing differences as quality errors, making it harder to fairly compare methods and understand their trade-offs. In this work, we argue that evaluation of dynamic generative models should be formulated as a sequence-alignment problem rather than independent frame comparison. We introduce a unified sequence-level reformulation that integrates Soft Dynamic Time Warping into established evaluation pipelines. By aligning feature trajectories while preserving temporal order, the proposed framework provides robustness to bounded temporal misalignments without altering the underlying perceptual, identity, or synchronization encoders. We show that frame-wise evaluation can be viewed as a special case under rigid alignment, while sequence-level alignment provides improved stability, lower sensitivity to timing differences, and clearer separation between modeling paradigms. Building on this principled formulation, we conduct a large-scale benchmark of 20 methods across seven datasets spanning canonical, in-the-wild, and style-diverse scenarios under standardized protocols. Extensive experiments show that temporally aligned metrics are more robust to timing differences, provide more consistent results across datasets, and better reveal systematic trade-offs between modeling paradigms, such as synchronization versus realism and expressiveness versus stability.

</details>


### [147] [CV-Arena: An Open Benchmark for Instructional Computer Vision Problem Solving with Human-AI Collaborative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00931)
*Fangzhou Lin, Peiran Li, Lingyu Xu, Wenjing Chen, Qianwen Ge, Shuo Xing, Mingyang Wu, Xiangbo Gao, Siyuan Yang, Kazunori Yamada, Ziming Zhang, Haichong Zhang, Zhen Dong, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CV-Arena 是一个面向指令式计算机视觉问题解决（即指令引导图像编辑）的开放基准，包含12K高分辨率真实图像指令对，覆盖16种任务类型。论文还提出了 Active Elo 人机协作偏好评估协议，利用逻辑门控的多维 VLM 评估器（CV-Judge）自动过滤明显失败案例，将高质量对比交由专家评判，并通过可靠性加权 Elo 更新聚合。对21个系统的评估揭示了指令遵循、物理推理、结构控制和细节保持方面的持续差距，并展示了闭环推理（CV-Agent）作为专业级视觉编辑的 promising 方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准大多聚焦于狭窄的外观编辑任务，无法全面覆盖专业工作流中真实图像任务的多样性。缺乏一个能够评估系统在保持性、几何性、物理性和可用性约束下执行指令式图像编辑的大规模、高质量基准。

Method: 1) 构建 CV-Arena 基准：通过 CogRetriever 双轨检索-筛选流水线（结合定向网络搜索、智能体查询优化、验证和可追溯性）收集12K高分辨率真实图像指令对，覆盖16种指令式视觉任务类型。2) 提出 Active Elo 评估协议：使用 CV-Judge（逻辑门控的多维 VLM 评估器）自动拒绝明显失败案例并解决高置信度比较，将接近的高质量比较路由给专家评分者，通过可靠性加权 Elo 更新聚合人机监督。3) 开发 CV-Agent：一个轻量级智能体模型，结合规划、编辑和验证的闭环推理。

Result: 对21个系统（包括闭源、开源和智能体模型）的综合评估显示：现有模型在指令遵循、物理推理、结构控制和细粒度细节保持方面存在持续差距。CV-Agent 展示了闭环推理在专业级指令遵循视觉编辑中的潜力。

Conclusion: CV-Arena 为指令式计算机视觉问题解决提供了全面的评测基准，Active Elo 协议有效结合了自动评估与人类判断。实验揭示了当前模型的不足，并表明闭环推理是提升专业级图像编辑能力的有前景方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向指令式图像编辑的开放基准 CV-Arena，包含12K高分辨率真实图像指令对，覆盖16种视觉任务类型，并提出了 Active Elo 人机协作偏好评估协议。这属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）范畴，是 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark for instruction-guided image editing, but not directly aligned with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Benchmark for instruction-guided image editing, Human-AI collaborative preference protocol, Active Elo evaluation, CV-Agent closed-loop reasoning, Not in priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing is becoming a general interface for visual work, yet existing benchmarks still focus largely on narrow appearance edits and do not fully capture the diversity of real-image tasks in professional workflows. Here, we define instructional computer vision problem solving as a broader formulation of image editing: given a real input image and a natural-language instruction, a system must produce an edited output that realizes the requested transformation while satisfying explicit preservation, geometric, physical, and usability constraints. We introduce CV-Arena, an open benchmark designed to evaluate this capability at professional scales. CV-Arena contains 12K high-resolution real-image instruction pairs spanning 16 instruction-based visual task types, constructed using CogRetriever, a dual-track retrieval-and-curation pipeline that combines targeted web search, agentic query refinement, verification, and traceability. To evaluate models at scale while preserving human fidelity, we propose Active Elo, a human-AI collaborative preference protocol that leverages CV-Judge, a logic-gated, multi-dimensional VLM evaluator, to reject clear failures and resolve high-confidence comparisons; and to route close, high-quality comparisons to expert raters. Mixed human and AI supervision is then aggregated through reliability-weighted Elo updates. Our comprehensive evaluation of 21 systems, including proprietary, open-source, and agentic models, on CV-Arena reveals persistent gaps in instruction adherence, physical reasoning, structural control, and fine-grained detail preservation. We further develop CV-Agent, a lightweight agentic model that combines planning, editing, and verification, and demonstrate that closed-loop reasoning is a promising direction for professional-grade instruction-following visual editing.

</details>


### [148] [WorldCoder-Bench: Benchmarking Physically Grounded 3D World Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01869)
*Shuo Lu, Yinuo Xu, Kecheng Yu, Siru Jiang, Yongcan Yu, Yubin Wang, Haitao Yang, Yuxiang Zhang, Bin Wang, Ran He, Jian Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WorldCoder-Bench 是一个用于评测 LLM 从自然语言生成可交互 3D 世界（基于 Three.js）的基准，包含 2026 个专家任务和 StateProbe 执行级验证协议。最佳模型仅达到 27.8% 的验证覆盖率，主要失败原因是状态模式漂移和交互链断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页生成评测仅关注像素或 DOM 节点，无法评估隐藏在 canvas 内部的 3D 世界物理约束、状态同步和交互逻辑。需要一种能够验证生成程序运行时状态和行为的评测方法。

Method: 构建了包含 2026 个专家任务的 WorldCoder-Bench 基准，涵盖 Simulation、Rendering、Application 三类场景，并提供可选的 .glb 资源和隐藏行为合约。提出 StateProbe 执行级验证协议，在沙箱浏览器中探测生成程序并验证隐藏的、抗篡改的运行时状态合约。引入 Return on Automation (RoA) 和 Time Efficiency Multiplier (TEM) 指标衡量成本与时间效益。

Result: 在 9 个前沿模型上，最佳系统在 WorldCoder-Core 上仅达 27.8% 验证覆盖率，在 WorldCoder-Robust 上为 19.9%。失败主要由状态模式漂移和交互链断裂导致，而非缺少场景元素。廉价或快速模型在简单领域仍能提供可观价值。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在生成物理约束的 3D 交互世界方面能力有限，状态管理和交互逻辑是主要瓶颈。WorldCoder-Bench 为这一方向提供了系统化的评测框架，有助于推动 LLM 从静态代码生成向动态世界构建发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于物理约束3D世界合成的评测基准 WorldCoder-Bench，包含任务集和执行级验证协议 StateProbe，属于生成模型评测范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Benchmarks for 3D world generation are valuable but this paper's focus on Three.js and interactive worlds is tangential to your core interests in continuous language/video models and efficient inference. Signals: 3D world synthesis benchmark, LLM-generated interactive environments, execution-based verification, low model performance, not directly related to continuous tokens or video generation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly asked not only to write static interfaces, but to construct executable interactive worlds from natural language. Browser-native 3D, commonly built with , is a natural next frontier: generated programs must integrate assets, obey spatial and physical constraints, and keep user-facing controls synchronized with hidden runtime state. Existing web-generation benchmarks and evaluators, however, largely observe only pixels or DOM nodes, while the mechanics of a world unfold inside an opaque <canvas>. We introduce WorldCoder-Bench, a benchmark for autonomous, physically grounded 3D world synthesis. WorldCoder-Bench contains 2,026 expert-curated tasks across Simulation, Rendering, and Application scenarios, with optional .glb assets and hidden behavioral contracts. We further propose StateProbe, an execution-based protocol that probes generated programs in a sandboxed browser and verifies hidden, mutation-hardened contracts over runtime states and transitions. Beyond verification coverage, we report Return on Automation and Time Efficiency Multiplier to measure correctness-adjusted cost and time savings. Across nine frontier models, the best system reaches only 27.8% verification coverage on WorldCoder-Core and 19.9% on WorldCoder-Robust, with failures dominated by state-schema drift and broken interaction chains rather than missing scene elements. Utility metrics further show that cheap or fast models can still provide substantial value on easier domains. WorldCoder-Bench is available at .

</details>


### [149] [ProductWebGen: Benchmarking Multimodal Product Webpage Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01022)
*Zhihong Liu, Siqi Kou, Zheng Li, Ye Ma, Quan Chen, Peng Jiang, Kai Yu, Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (68.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProductWebGen，一个用于系统评测多模态商品网页生成能力的基准，包含500个测试样本和13个商品品类。任务是从源商品图像、视觉内容指令和网页指令生成包含多张一致图像的展示网页。论文设计了基于编辑（LLM+图像编辑模型）和基于统一多模态模型（UM）两种评测流程，实验表明编辑方法在网页指令遵循和内容吸引力上领先，而UM方法在视觉内容指令遵循上更有优势。同时构建了 ProductWebGen-1k 微调数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商品展示网页的自动生成在营销、广告和电商领域具有重要实用价值，但现有工作缺乏对该任务的系统性评测。该任务要求严格的视觉一致性（多张产品图像之间）和高保真的指令遵循（生成可渲染的HTML代码），对多模态生成模型的可控性和指令跟随能力提出了挑战。

Method: 构建 ProductWebGen 基准（500样本，13品类），每个样本包含源图像、视觉内容指令和网页指令。设计两种评测流程：(1) 基于编辑的方法：LLM生成HTML代码，图像编辑模型生成产品图像；(2) 基于统一多模态模型（UM）的方法：单个UM同时生成HTML和图像，图像生成依赖前文多模态上下文。同时构建 ProductWebGen-1k 数据集（1000组真实产品图像+LLM生成的HTML代码），用于微调开源UM模型BAGEL。

Result: 基于编辑的方法在网页指令遵循和内容吸引力上取得领先结果；基于UM的方法在视觉内容指令遵循上表现更好。在开源UM BAGEL上的微调实验验证了 ProductWebGen-1k 数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: ProductWebGen 为多模态商品网页生成任务提供了系统化的评测基准，揭示了编辑方法和统一多模态方法各自的优势，并为该方向提供了有价值的基准数据和微调数据集。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 ProductWebGen，一个用于评测多模态商品网页生成能力的基准数据集（500个样本，13个品类），并构建了 ProductWebGen-1k 微调数据集。这属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一 canonical 子主题，因为核心贡献是新的评测基准和数据集。

Importance reason: Focuses on webpage generation benchmark, not directly on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal generation benchmark, E-commerce application, Editing vs. UM workflows, Limited to 500 samples, No direct connection to continuous language/video generation

Abstract: Crafting a product display webpage from a source product image, along with layout and visual content instructions, holds significant practical value for domains such as marketing, advertising, and E-commerce. Intuitively, this task demands strict visual consistency across product displays and high-fidelity instruction following to jointly generate renderable HTML code. These requirements on controllability and instruction-following are closely aligned with the core features of advanced multimodal generative models, such as image editing models and unified models. To this end, this paper introduces ProductWebGen to systematically benchmark the product webpage generation capacities of these models. We organize ProductWebGen with 500 test samples covering 13 product categories; each sample consists of a source image, a visual content instruction, and a webpage instruction. The task is to generate a product showcase webpage including multiple consistent images in accordance with the source image and instructions. Given the mixed-modality input-output nature of the task, we design and systematically compare two workflows for evaluation -- one uses large language models and image editing models to separately generate HTML code and images (editing-based), while the other relies on a single UM to generate both, with image generation conditioned on the preceding multimodal context (UM-based). Empirical results show that editing-based approaches achieve leading results in webpage instruction following and content appeal, while UM-based ones may display more advantages in fulfilling visual content instructions. We also construct a supervised fine-tuning dataset, ProductWebGen-1k, with 1,000 groups of real product images and LLM-generated HTML code. We verify its effectiveness on the open-source UM BAGEL. The data and code are available at .

</details>


### [150] [TextFake: Benchmarking AI-Generated Image Detection on Text-Rich Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01050)
*Yuning Zhang, Changtao Miao, Mingyu Liao, Tingyu Liu, Xinghao Wang, Tao Gong, Qi Chu, Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TextFake 是一个包含 20,000 张图像、覆盖 28 种语言和 4 类主题的文本丰富型 AI 生成图像检测基准。通过四阶段流水线生成伪造图像，消除协变量捷径。零样本评估 14 个专用检测器和 3 个前沿 VLM API，发现所有方法准确率均未超过 80%，部分方法相比自然图像基准下降超 60%。诊断评估揭示了三种失败模式：文本密度诅咒、渲染保真度伪装和阈值崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成图像检测器在自然图像基准上表现良好，但在富含文本的伪造图像（如伪造截图、文档、新闻页面）上的表现尚未被系统测试。这类文本丰富的伪造图像在虚假信息传播中非常普遍，因此需要专门的基准来评估检测器的实际能力。

Method: 构建 TextFake 基准：包含 20,000 张图像，覆盖 28 种语言、4 类主题（新闻、社交媒体、文档、广告）和 2 种场景模态（纯文本、图文混合）。通过四阶段流水线生成伪造图像：先标注真实图像的三维控制属性（文本密度、渲染质量、布局复杂度），再通过分布对齐的结构化提示生成对应伪造图像，从而消除协变量捷径。在 14 个专用检测器和 3 个前沿 VLM API 上进行零样本评估。

Result: 所有方法在 TextFake 上准确率均未超过 80%，部分方法相比自然图像基准下降超 60%。诊断评估发现三种失败模式：(1) 文本密度诅咒——密集文字使低级检测器失效；(2) 渲染保真度伪装——更强的文本渲染抑制生成伪影；(3) 阈值崩溃——常规扰动使检测器性能降至随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有 AI 生成图像检测器在文本丰富图像上存在系统性缺陷，亟需针对文本渲染伪影和密集文字场景的专用检测方法。TextFake 基准为未来研究提供了评估平台和诊断方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 TextFake 基准，专门用于评测 AI 生成图像检测器在富含文本图像（如伪造截图、文档、新闻页面）上的表现，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时，该工作也涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack），因为其核心是检测 AI 生成图像，与对抗检测相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI-generated image detection for text-rich images, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Benchmark for text-rich AIGI detection, 28 languages, 4 topics, 14 detectors + 3 VLMs evaluated, identifies three failure modes, no method exceeds 80% accuracy

Abstract: Recent AI-generated image (AIGI) detectors perform well on natural-image benchmarks, but their behavior on text-rich forgeries, such as fabricated screenshots, documents, and news pages prevalent in misinformation, remains untested. We introduce TextFake, a 20,000-image benchmark for text-rich AIGI detection spanning 28 languages, 4 topic categories, and 2 scene modalities. Fake images are synthesized via a four-stage pipeline that annotates real images along three controlled dimensions and generates counterparts through distribution-aligned structured prompting, ruling out covariate shortcuts. Zero-shot evaluation of 14 specialized detectors and 3 frontier VLM APIs reveals a large systematic gap: no method exceeds 80% accuracy, with some dropping over 60% from natural-image benchmarks. Diagnostic evaluations identify three failure modes: the Text Density Curse, where dense glyphs overwhelm low-level detectors; Cloaking via Rendering Fidelity, where stronger text rendering suppresses enerative artifacts; and Threshold Collapse, where routine perturbations drive detectors toward chance-level performance.

</details>


### [151] [3DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agentic Procedural 3D Modeling Via Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01057)
*Yipeng Gao, Lei Shu, Genzhi Ye, Xi Xiong, Ameesh Makadia, Meiqi Guo, Laurent Itti, Jindong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 3DCodeBench是一个系统性的基准，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体通过代码进行程序化3D建模的能力。它包含多模态提示数据集、评测协议和基于人类偏好的排名平台3DCodeArena。实验发现主要失败源于API不匹配，成功渲染的模型仍存在几何组件断开或漂浮的问题，而测试时扩展（如更高推理预算和多轮优化）能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化3D建模能生成确定性、引擎就绪且可精确编辑的资产，但需要深厚的3D软件API、参数化设计和代码级几何推理专业知识。现有神经3D生成器缺乏这些特性，而VLM作为程序化3D建模智能体的能力尚未被系统评估，因此需要一个全面的基准来评测和推动这一方向。

Method: 构建了3DCodeBench基准，包含大规模多模态（文本/图像）提示、程序化代码和3D对象三元组数据集；设计了评测协议；开发了3DCodeArena排名平台，基于成对人类偏好对生成的3D输出进行排序。对12种先进VLM进行了系统评估，分析了失败模式并研究了测试时扩展策略（更高推理预算、多轮优化）的效果。

Result: 12种VLM在程序化3D建模任务上表现差异显著；主要失败源于API不匹配；成功渲染的模型仍存在几何组件断开或漂浮问题；测试时扩展（更高思考预算和多轮优化）能整体提升性能；揭示了高质量程序化编码数据对商业VLM的重要性。

Conclusion: 程序化3D建模对当前VLM仍具挑战性，需要高质量程序化编码数据和提供高保真反馈的鲁棒执行环境来支持迭代优化。3DCodeBench和3DCodeArena为探索基于VLM的程序化3D建模提供了基础工具包。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了3DCodeBench，一个评估VLM智能体进行程序化3D建模的基准，包含数据集、评测协议和基于人类偏好的排名平台3DCodeArena。这属于多模态生成方向下的"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）规范子主题，因为其核心贡献是评测基准与数据集。同时该工作也涉及VLM作为3D建模智能体，与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D code generation benchmark, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D procedural modeling, VLM agent benchmark, code generation, human preference ranking, test-time scaling

Abstract: Procedural 3D modeling through code is emerging as a versatile paradigm, offering deterministic, engine-ready, and precisely editable assets that neural 3D generators inherently lack. Authoring such procedural content, however, demands deep expertise in 3D software APIs, parametric design, and code-level geometric reasoning. In this paper, we propose 3DCodeBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating vision-language model (VLM) agents for procedural 3D generation in 3D modeling software. Specifically, 3DCodeBench evaluates how effectively 12 advanced VLMs can serve as procedural 3D modelers by translating text and image references into procedural code for 3D modeling software. Recognizing that automated metrics may not fully capture the perceptual quality of 3D shapes, we build 3DCodeArena, a ranking platform based on pairwise human preferences over generated 3D outputs. From extensive evaluations and results, we observe that: (1) Failures mostly arise from API mismatches, while successful renders still suffer from disconnected or floating 3D geometric components. (2) Test-time scaling, such as higher thinking budgets and multi-turn refinement, improves performance overall. Our findings highlight a critical need for high-quality procedural coding data to advance commercial VLMs. Furthermore, effective procedural 3D modeling requires a robust execution environment that provides high-fidelity feedback for iterative refinement. We release 3DCodeBench, including the curated large-scale dataset of multimodal (text/image) prompts, procedural code, 3D object triplets, evaluation protocol, and the public 3DCodeArena platform as a foundational toolkit for exploring VLM-based procedural 3D modelers.

</details>


### [152] [PairedGTA: Generating Driving Datasets for Controlled Photometric Shift Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01192)
*Andrea Chianese, Giulio Rossolini, Alessandro Biondi, Marco Cococcioni, Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PairedGTA，一个基于GTA游戏引擎的自动驾驶数据集生成框架，通过软件API控制光照和天气条件，生成完美像素对齐的配对图像，用于系统分析光度偏移对语义分割模型性能的影响，排除了几何和语义变化的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实自动驾驶数据集难以提供同一场景在不同天气/光照条件下的完美配对图像，因为相机位姿、交通流和动态物体位置会随时间变化，导致只能获得粗略配对的图像，无法将光度偏移的影响与几何/语义变化分离开来。

Method: 基于GTA游戏引擎的高保真数据生成框架，通过软件API与游戏引擎通信，在保持场景几何、相机位姿和动态物体身份与位置不变的前提下，修改光照和天气条件，为每个采样位置程序化实例化动态实体并渲染像素对齐的图像。

Result: 通过语义分割模型的系统分析，证明了该框架生成的配对数据能够更直接地将模型输出退化归因于光度偏移而非不受控的几何或语义因素，为评估自动驾驶感知系统在不同环境条件下的鲁棒性提供了可靠工具。

Conclusion: PairedGTA框架能够生成完美配对的驾驶场景图像，有效支持对视觉感知系统在多种不利条件下性能退化的可控分析，为自动驾驶感知系统的鲁棒性评估提供了有价值的数据生成工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于GTA游戏引擎的自动驾驶数据集生成框架，用于生成完美配对的图像以分析光照和天气变化对语义分割模型的影响。这属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on controlled dataset generation for photometric shift analysis, which is not aligned with user's priority on generative models and world models. Signals: Driving dataset generation, controlled photometric shift, GTA engine, semantic segmentation evaluation, not generative model

Abstract: Evaluating the performance of visual perception systems for autonomous driving is essential to ensure reliable operation across diverse environmental scenarios. Ideally, a balanced and fair analysis across different adverse conditions would require perfectly paired images of the same scene under different weather or illumination changes. This would allow evaluating the effect of photometric shifts independently of geometry and semantic changes. Unfortunately, real-world datasets rarely provide images of the same scene under different environmental conditions, because, normally, camera pose, traffic, and locations of dynamic objects (vehicles, pedestrians, etc.) vary over time, thus yielding only coarsely paired data. To address this challenge, this work introduces a data generation framework based on a high-fidelity game engine for extracting perfectly paired images. By leveraging software APIs that communicate with the GTA game engine, the framework modifies illumination and weather conditions while preserving scene geometry, camera pose, and the identity and placement of dynamic objects. For each sampled location, it procedurally instantiates dynamic entities and renders pixel-aligned images under diverse adverse conditions. The benefit of the proposed generation framework in driving scenarios is demonstrated through a systematic analysis of semantic segmentation models, whose output degradation can be attributed more directly to photometric shifts rather than to uncontrolled semantic or geometric factors.

</details>


### [153] [TECCI: Tricky Edits of Collected and Curated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01213)
*Aishwarya Agrawal, Roy Hirsch, Yasumasa Onoe, Sherry Ben, Jason Baldridge*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TECCI 是一个针对文本引导图像编辑的挑战性评测基准，包含 7550 对图像与编辑指令，覆盖 7 类图像和 5 类编辑类型。人工评估和基于 Gemini 的自动评估表明，现有最佳模型整体成功率不超过 22%，在位置、运动、视角、缩放和创意编辑等"棘手"编辑上表现尤其困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导图像编辑方法在指令跟随、最小化编辑和视觉质量方面仍存在诸多困难，尤其对于涉及位置、运动、视角、缩放和创意编辑等挑战性任务。缺乏一个系统性地针对这些"棘手"编辑场景的评测基准来揭示现有方法的真实局限性。

Method: 构建 TECCI 基准：1) 收集并发布全新的图像集，涵盖 7 个类别（建筑、自然、动物、食物、人物、物体、艺术），特意针对现有方法弱点设计；2) 使用 Gemini 自动生成每张图像的 5 类编辑指令（颜色/外观、位置/布局、运动/动态、推理、创意），并额外人工编写 530 张图像的挑战性指令；3) 对 5 个领先的图像编辑模型进行人工评估（指令跟随、编辑最小性、视觉质量三维度）；4) 使用 Gemini 构建自动评分器，达到 74.7% 的人类评估匹配准确率。

Result: 1) 所有模型整体成功率均低于 22%；2) Nano Banana Pro 是表现最好的模型；3) 模型在指令跟随上显著优于编辑最小性和视觉质量；4) 模型在建筑和自然图像上表现最差，这些图像需要强空间布局理解和精细视觉细节；5) 推理和创意编辑最难，颜色和外观编辑最容易。

Conclusion: TECCI 揭示了现有图像编辑模型在处理"棘手"编辑时的系统性不足，为社区提供了一个具有挑战性的评测基准。自动评分器展示了替代昂贵人工评估的潜力。该基准有望推动图像编辑方法在指令跟随、编辑最小性和视觉质量方面的全面进步。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个全新的图像编辑评测基准 TECCI，包含新数据集和人工/自动评估，属于生成模型评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark) 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Benchmark reveals severe limitations in image editing, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Image editing benchmark, low model success rate, Gemini auto-rater, 7 image categories, 5 edit types

Abstract: Despite tremendous recent progress, current text-guided image editing methods still struggle with many aspects of editing involving instruction following, minimally editing the source image, and ensuring high visual quality. These problems are especially apparent when the requested edit is challenging, such as those that involve position, motion, viewpoint, scale and creative edits. To systematically test generative image editors, we propose a novel image editing benchmark -- TECCI: Tricky Edits of Collected and Curated Images. TECCI consists of a completely new set of images we are releasing. The images in TECCI span 7 image categories. The images and these categories were curated intentionally to target weaknesses of existing methods. The edit instructions in TECCI are automatically generated by Gemini, covering 5 edit types per source image. We also curated a set of 530 images for which we created challenging manually written edit instructions. Overall, TECCI contains 7550 pairs of images and edit instructions. We conduct human evaluations of five leading image editing models on TECCI. Humans judge outputs along three dimensions: 1) instruction following, 2) minimality of the edits, and 3) visual quality. To scale-up the evaluation, we also build an auto-rater using Gemini that achieves 74.7% accuracy in matching human evaluations. Our evaluations reveal that: 1) none of the models exceed a 22% overall success rate, demonstrating the challenging nature of TECCI, 2) Nano Banana Pro is the best performing model overall, 3) models perform significantly better at instruction following compared to minimal edits and visual quality, 4) models struggle with editing architecture and nature images which require strong understanding of spatial layout and intricate visual details. 5) reasoning and creative edits are the most difficult, whereas color and appearance edits are the easiest.

</details>


### [154] [Knowledge-Intensive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01285)
*Chenxu Wang, Mingda Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出知识密集型视频生成（KIVI）任务，要求模型根据简短的信息寻求型提示生成事实准确、有用的视频。作者构建了KIVI-Bench基准（1,080个prompt）并提出自动评测指标，实验表明现有SOTA视频生成模型在事实性和信息呈现上远落后于人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型在视觉质量上进步显著，但缺乏对生成视频事实性和实际有用性的系统评估。作者希望推动视频生成从"好看"走向"有用"，即生成能解释、演示或指导用户完成任务的视频。

Method: 定义知识密集型视频生成（KIVI）任务，构建KIVI-Bench基准（1,080个prompt，涵盖解释、流程、演示三类），提出自动评测指标（事实性评分和有用性评分），并在7个SOTA视频生成模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 现有模型在事实性和有用性上显著落后于人类表现，尤其在视觉属性、操作步骤和清晰信息呈现方面存在明显不足。作者提出的自动指标与人类标注的一致性显著优于现有替代指标。

Conclusion: KIVI是一个具有挑战性的新方向，揭示了当前视频生成模型在事实性和实用性方面的严重局限，为未来研究提供了评测基准和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了知识密集型视频生成（KIVI）任务，并构建了KIVI-Bench基准（1,080个prompt）以及事实性和有用性的自动评测指标，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel benchmark for factual video generation but lacks direct alignment with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: New benchmark for factual video generation, Evaluates factuality and helpfulness, Weak match to user's priority subtopics, No direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models, Limited to evaluation, not core generation methods

Abstract: Text-to-video generation has advanced rapidly in visual quality, but remains under-evaluated for factuality and practical usefulness. We introduce knowledge-intensive video generation (KIVI), where models generate videos from short information-seeking prompts that ask for explanations, procedures, or demonstrations. To evaluate this setting, we construct KIVI-Bench, a benchmark of 1,080 prompts, and propose automatic metrics for factuality and helpfulness. Human evaluation shows that our metrics significantly better align with human annotations than existing alternatives. Experiments on seven state-of-the-art video generation models show that current systems still lag behind human performance, especially on visual properties, procedural operations, and clear information presentation. These results highlight KIVI as a challenging direction for factual and instructionally useful video generation.

</details>


### [155] [SafeGen-Bench: Benchmarking Safety in Image-Conditioned Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01481)
*Yingzi Ma, Xiaogeng Liu, Yawen Zheng, Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeGen-Bench 是一个专门评估图像条件文生视频（T2V）模型安全性的基准。它定义了10个恶意类别，使用安全文本提示+安全起始帧的组合来测试模型是否仍会生成有害视频。实验表明现有模型难以一致避免生成恶意内容，且单模态护栏（文本或图像）不足以提供鲁棒防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频安全基准主要关注恶意文本提示，忽略了"安全文本+安全图像"组合仍可能生成有害视频这一常见且棘手的问题。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 定义了10个恶意类别（聚焦时序序列和行为相关风险），从多种图像/视频源中精心挑选起始帧，配以对应文本提示构建基准。在多种条件T2V模型上评估不安全分数，并测试了基于文本和基于图像的护栏的有效性。

Result: 当前条件T2V模型在SafeGen-Bench上不安全分数高达44.5，尤其在高质量生成条件下更难避免恶意内容。单模态护栏在7个恶意类别上失败率达80%，不足以提供鲁棒防御。

Conclusion: SafeGen-Bench揭示了现有条件T2V模型在安全性方面的显著不足，呼吁开发更安全、更可控的条件T2V模型，并需要多模态联合的防御机制。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SafeGen-Bench，一个专门评估图像条件文生视频模型安全性的基准，属于生成模型的安全评测。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配，同时涉及多模态安全（multimodal_safety_attack）方向。

Importance reason: Safety benchmark for image-conditioned video generation is useful but not aligned with user's priority on advancing generation capabilities. Signals: Safety evaluation, image-conditioned T2V, multimodal guardrails, 10 malicious categories, high failure rate

Abstract: With the rapid advancements in text-to-image diffusion models, generative video models (T2V models) like Sora can now produce short synthetic videos from a text prompt or an initial image. However, synthetic video generation -- especially when guided by an initial image -- often poses risks, including the potential creation of illegal, politically sensitive, or unethical content. Existing benchmarks have started to consider the safety of generated videos, but they primarily focus on testing models with malicious text prompts, ignoring the scenario where text prompt and image combination may still lead to harmful video content. In practice, this is a common and challenging issue: videos generated from safe text and image inputs can nonetheless convey harmful information. To bridge this gap, we introduce SafeGen-Bench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the safety of conditional T2V models. Our benchmark defines 10 malicious categories, concentrating on risks related to both temporal sequences and depicted behaviors. SafeGen-Bench consists of carefully selected start frames from diverse image and video sources, paired with corresponding text prompts to simulate realistic inputs. We evaluate a variety of conditional T2V models on SafeGen-Bench, and the results indicate that current models struggle to consistently avoid generating malicious content with unsafety scores reaching up to 44.5, especially under conditions requiring high quality. Furthermore, we assess the effectiveness of both text-based and image-based guardrails on our benchmark, finding that unimodal guardrails alone were insufficient to provide a robust defense, with an 80\% failure rate across seven malicious categories. We hope that SafeGen-Bench will foster the development of safer and more controllable conditional T2V models.

</details>


### [156] [Compliance-Scored Best-of-N Guardrail Orchestration for Multimodal Document Generation in Payments Dispute Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01513)
*Nataraj Agaram Sundar, Tejas Morabia*

Main category: cs.DC, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (36.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向企业级多模态文档生成的合规评分护栏编排层，通过多候选并行生成、加权合规评分与早退机制，在支付争议防御场景中实现了91%的合规率与显著的胜率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的金融争议叙述、合规通知等企业文档生成需要严格的模式正确性、策略合规性和低延迟大规模运行。现有生产系统将PII脱敏、内容审核、格式验证等步骤拼凑在一起，导致逻辑碎片化、请求路径缓慢和运营成本高昂。

Method: 提出一个统一的护栏编排层，支持文本和图像输入。框架运行可配置的并行生成头，对候选输出进行加权合规评分（包括PII检测、内容审核、模式约束和领域规则），返回得分最高的输出及其选择元数据，并支持早退机制。

Result: 运营数据显示5次尝试在20秒内完成，合规率达91%。在支付争议防御摘要中，变量队列整体胜率高于对照组（301/659 vs 536/1548），提升+11.0个百分点（95% CI [6.6, 15.5], p<0.001）；调整后的未收物品案件提升+7.5个百分点（95% CI [0.2, 15.7], p=0.045）。还报告了770份生成证据审查和70例OCR切片的校准信号。

Conclusion: 合规评分护栏编排层能有效提升企业级多模态文档生成的合规性和胜率，同时保持低延迟。该方法在金融争议防御场景中取得了统计显著的改进，为高风险文档生成提供了可复现的工程框架。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦企业级多模态文档生成（金融争议叙述等）的合规性评测与护栏编排，提出了基于合规评分的多候选生成与早退机制，并报告了合规率、胜率等运营指标。核心贡献在于生成质量评测与合规性评估框架，与 [CANONICAL] 生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on enterprise guardrail orchestration for payments, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation research. Signals: enterprise document generation, compliance scoring, best-of-n guardrail, payments dispute, not continuous language model

Abstract: High-stakes enterprise document generation, including financial dispute narratives, compliance notices, and audit summaries, demands schema correctness, policy compliance, and low-latency operation at scale. Prior to a unified guardrail layer, production systems often stitched together separate PII redaction, content moderation, and format validation steps, leading to fragmented logic, slower request paths, and higher operational cost. We present a guardrail orchestration layer for text and image inputs that couples multi-candidate generation with an explicit compliance score used for early exit. The framework runs configurable parallel generation heads, scores candidates against weighted guardrails including PII detection, content moderation, schema constraints, and domain rules, and returns the best-scoring output with selection metadata. The available operational readout reports 5 attempts within 20 seconds and 91 percent compliance. For payments dispute defense summaries, we analyze aggregate operational scenario readouts rather than a randomized A/B test. Variable cohorts show higher count win rates than controls overall, 301/659 versus 536/1548, corresponding to +11.0 percentage points with 95 percent confidence interval [6.6, 15.5] and p < 0.001, and for adjusted item-not-received cases, +7.5 percentage points with 95 percent confidence interval [0.2, 15.7] and p = 0.045. Fraud and local evidence-ranking deltas are directionally positive but not statistically significant from the aggregate count data. We also report reviewer-calibrated Responsible-AI evidence-quality signals from 770 generated-evidence reviews and a 70-case OCR slice, and document the reproducibility boundary through the request interface, scoring logic, pseudocode, and operational evidence boundary.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Categories of Caste: Examining Caste Bias and Morality in Text-to-Image AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00039)
*Divyanshu Kumar Singh, Dipto Das, Deepika Rama Subramanian, Koustuv Saha, Stephen Voida, Bryan Semaan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.HC, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (56.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文超越将种姓视为身份类别的传统视角，转而关注种姓的关系性维度，通过算法审计与批判话语分析，揭示文生图模型如何延续和强化种姓偏见，并提出反种姓的公平AI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将种姓视为简单的身份类别（如高种姓 vs 低种姓），未能捕捉种姓歧视的关系性机制。本文旨在更细致地理解文生图模型如何通过关系性维度延续种姓偏见。

Method: 结合算法审计（algorithmic audit）与批判话语分析（critical discourse analysis），以挑战婆罗门规范性（Brahminical Normativity）为概念框架，分析文生图模型输出中的种姓偏见。

Result: 揭示了文生图模型中的种姓偏见超越了简单的上/下种姓二元对立，呈现出更复杂的关系性歧视模式。

Conclusion: 需要超越将种姓视为类别的研究范式，采用反种姓（anti-caste）的方法来应对AI系统中的种姓偏见与公平性问题。

Classification reason: 该论文对文生图模型进行算法审计，分析种姓偏见与道德问题，属于生成模型的评测与公平性分析，与 [CANONICAL] 生成模型评测与数据集 方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on caste bias in T2I models, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: caste bias, text-to-image, algorithmic audit, critical discourse analysis, anti-caste approach

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models have shown promising utility across various domains. However, such models are also amplifying harmful societal biases in their outputs. In the context of South Asia, recent work has shown caste biases and stereotypes are being perpetuated through Generative AI (GenAI) systems. While this research offers extremely relevant insight into invisibilized narratives of caste discrimination through the GenAI system, they often treat caste as an identity category. Therefore, in this work we shift our ontology to focus on the relational aspect of caste. This enables us to develop a more nuanced understanding of the mechanics of caste discrimination by and through T2I models. Combining an algorithmic audit with critical discourse analysis, we draw on a conceptual frame challenging Brahminical Normativity to show how caste biases are perpetuated beyond the simple binaries of upper vs lower-caste categories. Our contributions are two-fold. Beyond challenging the categorical understanding of caste as a category, we propose an anti-caste approach to tackle the issue of caste bias and fairness in AI systems.

</details>


### [158] [Pool-Select-Refine: Allocation-Aware Generative Dataset Distillation with Soft-Label-Guided Latent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01920)
*Wenmin Li, Shunsuke Sakai, Zhongkai Zhao, Tatsuhito Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Pool-Select-Refine框架，一种分配感知的生成式数据集蒸馏方法。该方法先构建过完备候选池并选择紧凑子集，再通过教师模型的软标签监督在潜空间中对所选样本进行精炼，从而解耦生成、选择和精炼三个阶段，更有效地利用蒸馏预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的数据集蒸馏方法采用僵硬的"生成即用"策略，在固定每类图像预算下直接使用生成样本作为最终蒸馏集，导致候选生成与预算分配紧密耦合，可能造成预算浪费或样本信息不足。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 构建过完备候选池并基于目标预算选择紧凑子集；2) 在潜空间中利用教师模型的软标签监督对所选样本进行精炼，提升语义对齐同时保持生成先验。

Result: 在大规模和细粒度图像分类基准上，该方法相比基于扩散的基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 在精炼之前引入筛选阶段是改进基于扩散的数据集蒸馏的一种简单而有效的方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于扩散模型的数据集蒸馏方法（Pool-Select-Refine），核心贡献在于通过候选池构建、子集选择和潜空间精炼来改进生成式数据集蒸馏。这属于生成模型在数据集压缩/蒸馏方面的应用，与"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题相关，因为该主题涵盖生成模型相关的数据集构建与评测方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on dataset distillation, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Dataset distillation, diffusion-based, soft-label refinement, not autoregressive, not video/image generation

Abstract: Diffusion-based dataset distillation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for condensing large-scale datasets into compact synthetic sets. By leveraging pretrained generative priors, these methods can produce realistic class-conditional samples more efficiently than traditional matching-based approaches. However, most existing diffusion-based methods still adopt a rigid ``Generate-and-Use'' strategy, where the generated samples are directly treated as the final distilled set under a fixed images-per-class budget. Such a design tightly couples candidate generation with final budget allocation, which may result in redundant waste of the limited budget or insufficiently informative samples. In this paper, we propose ``Pool-Select-Refine'', a two-stage framework for allocation-aware generative dataset distillation. First, instead of directly using a fixed number of generated samples, we construct an over-complete candidate pool and select a compact subset under the target budget. Second, we refine the selected samples in latent space using soft-label supervision derived from the teacher model, improving semantic alignment while preserving the generative prior. This design explicitly decouples generation, selection, and refinement, enabling more effective use of the distillation budget. Experiments on large-scale and fine-grained image classification benchmarks show that the proposed framework delivers consistent gains over diffusion-based baselines. The results suggest that introducing a curation stage before refinement is a simple yet effective way to improve diffusion-based dataset distillation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究拓扑优化中代理模型的分布外泛化问题，提出灵敏度条件化的Bernoulli流匹配生成器，通过信息论分析证明灵敏度场是最优条件信号，并引入伪灵敏度概念刻画不同物理场的泛化能力，在结构TO和CFD-TO数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化代理模型在负载或边界条件变化时表现出高度可变的分布外泛化能力，其来源尚不明确。本文旨在从信息论角度解释这一现象，并寻找最优的条件信号以提升泛化性能。

Method: 将拓扑优化流程建模为因果马尔可夫链，利用数据处理不等式证明灵敏度场是信息论最优的条件信号。提出伪灵敏度概念形式化描述哪些物理场能近似灵敏度从而促进泛化。设计灵敏度条件化的Bernoulli流匹配生成模型，在结构TO基准和新提出的CFD-TO数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 灵敏度条件化在结构TO和CFD-TO数据集上均取得最优的OOD泛化性能；随着条件信号从灵敏度逐渐退化为原始参数，泛化性能逐步下降，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 灵敏度场是拓扑优化代理模型泛化的信息论最优条件信号；伪灵敏度框架可有效预测不同物理场的泛化能力；灵敏度条件化的流匹配生成器为拓扑优化提供了一种鲁棒的代理建模方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究拓扑优化中基于灵敏度条件的Bernoulli流匹配生成模型的泛化能力，属于生成模型在工程优化中的应用与评测。虽然涉及flow matching生成方法，但核心贡献在于分析不同条件信号对OOD泛化的影响，并引入新的CFD-TO数据集进行评测，因此映射到[生成模型评测与数据集]（generation_benchmark）最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on engineering optimization generalization, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: topology optimization, Bernoulli flow matching, sensitivity conditioning, OOD generalization, CFD-TO dataset

Abstract: Surrogate models for topology optimization (TO) exhibit highly variable out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization under distribution shifts such as changing loads or boundary conditions, yet the source of this variability remains unclear. We hypothesize that OOD performance is governed by how much information the conditioning signal preserves about the adjoint sensitivity (reduced gradient) that drives classical TO. Modeling the TO pipeline as a causal Markov chain, the Data Processing Inequality establishes that, under this abstraction, the sensitivity field is an information-theoretically optimal conditioning signal for topology prediction. However, computing exact adjoint sensitivities can be expensive or unavailable in practice; we observe that certain physical fields can approximate sensitivities through monotone transformations. To formalize this, we introduce \textbf{pseudo-sensitivities} to characterize which fields enable generalization versus those that are information-poor. We then show that a sensitivity-conditioned Bernoulli flow-matching generator empirically confirms these predictions: conditioning on sensitivities yields state-of-the-art OOD performance, while increasingly distant physical fields degrade toward raw parameter conditioning. Results hold across structural TO benchmarks under load shifts and our new CFD-TO dataset under boundary-condition shifts such as multi-outlet configurations. Code and datasets are available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市轨迹生成研究使用不同的数据集、预处理流程、轨迹表示和评估指标，导致方法间难以公平比较，无法判断性能差异来自生成机制本身还是实验协议的不一致。

Method: 构建统一基准框架 CityTrajBench，标准化数据摄入、轨迹归一化、特征构建、模型适配、地图感知后处理、模型选择和多级评估协议。支持统计基线、VAE、GAN、扩散和流匹配等异构生成器，在三个真实城市轨迹数据集上从全局空间真实性、行程级分布保真度、轨迹级几何相似性、条件移动一致性和效率五个维度进行评估。

Result: DiffTraj 在轨迹级几何保真度上最强，DiffRNTraj 在结构敏感的全局真实性上有竞争力，TrajFlow 在真实性、质量、条件一致性和效率之间取得良好平衡，而简单的马尔可夫基线在粗粒度行程和局部移动统计上仍有竞争力。

Conclusion: 城市轨迹生成质量本质上是多目标的，没有单一模型在所有标准上占优；CityTrajBench 为未来城市移动生成研究提供了可复现的基准协议和测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了城市尺度车辆轨迹生成的统一评测基准 CityTrajBench，标准化了数据预处理、评估指标和实验协议，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on vehicle trajectory generation, which is not a priority subtopic and is penalized by negative keywords. Signals: Benchmark for trajectory generation, Not in priority subtopics, Negative keyword "trajectory prediction" implied, No boost keywords matched, Low personal relevance

Abstract: Urban trajectory generation is a fundamental task for transportation simulation, urban planning, and mobility analytics. However, systematic comparison across trajectory generation methods remains difficult because existing studies often rely on different datasets, preprocessing pipelines, trajectory representations, and evaluation metrics. This fragmentation makes it unclear whether reported performance differences arise from the generation mechanism itself or from inconsistent experimental protocols. To address this issue, we present CityTrajBench, a unified benchmark framework and protocol for city-scale vehicle trajectory generation. CityTrajBench standardizes data ingestion, trajectory normalization, feature construction, model adaptation, map-aware post-processing, model selection, and multi-level evaluation under a common setting. It supports heterogeneous generators, including statistical baselines, VAE-based, GAN-based, diffusion-based, and flow-matching-based models, and evaluates them on three real-world urban trajectory datasets. The benchmark measures global spatial realism, trip-level distribution fidelity, trajectory-level geometric similarity, conditional mobility consistency, and efficiency. Experiments reveal clear trade-offs across model families: DiffTraj is strongest on trajectory-level geometric fidelity, DiffRNTraj is competitive on structure-sensitive global realism, and TrajFlow provides a strong balance across realism, quality, conditional consistency, and efficiency. Meanwhile, a simple Markov baseline remains competitive on coarse-grained trip and local-movement statistics. These findings show that urban trajectory generation quality is inherently multi-objective, that no single model dominates all criteria equally, and that CityTrajBench provides a reproducible benchmark protocol and testbed for future research on urban mobility generation.

</details>


### [161] [Improving Combined Detection and Classification of TEM Defects via Mask-Conditioned Latent Diffusion Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02532)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于掩码条件潜扩散模型（LDM）的生成式数据增强方法，用于合成透射电子显微镜（TEM）缺陷图像及其自动标注的多类缺陷掩码。通过将合成数据与少量真实标注数据结合训练Mask R-CNN模型，在检测和分类F1分数的调和均值上最高提升0.02，展示了生成模型在数据稀缺的显微图像分析任务中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透射电子显微镜（TEM）图像中微观结构缺陷的分析，特别是在辐照金属合金中，常受限于高质量标注数据的稀缺性。传统数据增强方法（如几何变换）无法生成多样化的真实缺陷形态，因此需要一种能够生成逼真且带有自动标注缺陷掩码的合成图像的方法来扩充训练数据。

Method: 提出一种掩码条件潜扩散模型（mask-conditioned LDM），从实验掩码中学习分布并采样生成合成掩码，再以掩码为条件生成对应的逼真TEM图像，从而自动获得图像-掩码对。将生成的合成数据与少量（10、50、100张）真实标注图像混合，训练Mask R-CNN模型进行缺陷检测与分类。

Result: 生成式数据增强在整体模型性能上带来小幅提升，检测与分类F1分数的调和均值最高提升0.02。但检测和分类性能的相对提升幅度依赖于具体的训练/测试数据划分，表明增强效果存在一定的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 掩码条件潜扩散模型能够有效生成逼真的TEM缺陷图像及自动标注掩码，在数据稀缺场景下对深度学习模型性能有积极但有限的提升作用。该工作展示了目标生成模型在显微图像量化任务中的数据增强潜力，为未来在更广泛的数据稀缺科学图像分析中应用生成式增强提供了参考。

Classification reason: 该论文使用掩码条件潜扩散模型（LDM）合成TEM图像进行数据增强，属于生成模型在数据稀缺场景下的应用。其核心贡献是生成合成数据并评测其对下游检测分类任务的提升效果，与[生成模型评测与数据集]（generation_benchmark）这一规范子主题高度相关，因为该子主题涵盖生成模型评测、新数据集及数据增强方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on TEM defect detection with diffusion augmentation, far from user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: TEM defect detection, mask-conditioned latent diffusion, data augmentation, microscopy, low relevance to user's core topics

Abstract: Analyzing microstructural defects in transmission electron microscopy (TEM) images, particularly in irradiated metal alloys, is often limited by the availability of high-quality, labeled data. To address this, we introduce a generative data augmentation approach using a mask-conditioned latent diffusion model (LDM) for synthesizing realistic TEM images with controllable, automatically labeled multi-class defect masks. Without requiring manual annotations for generation, our method enables the creation of synthetic image-mask pairs by sampling distributions learned from experimental masks. These generated data were used to augment small experimental datasets of varying sizes (10, 50, and 100 labeled experimental images) to train a Mask Regional Convolutional Neural Network (R-CNN) model for defect detection and classification. Our results show that generative augmentation yields small overall model performance improvements, with up to a 0.02 gain in the harmonic mean of detection and classification F1 scores. However, we also find that the relative contributions to detection and classification improvement depend on the specific train/test data split. These findings highlight the potential of targeted generative models to enhance deep learning performance in data-scarce microscopy-based image quantification tasks.

</details>


### [162] [LL-Bench: Rethinking Low-Level Vision Evaluation in the Era of Large-Scale Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02535)
*Lu Liu, Huiyu Duan, Chenxin Zhu, Jintong Lu, Haoyun Jiang, Liu Yang, Qiang Hu, Guangtao Zhai, Xiaoyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出LL-Bench，一个用于评估大规模生成模型在低层视觉任务（如图像恢复、增强等16种退化任务）上表现的综合性基准，包含2,469张退化图像、28,919张恢复图像、152,020个人类偏好标注和28,334个质量评分。基于该基准，系统诊断了生成模型与传统恢复模型的性能边界与失败模式，并提出了LL-Score——一个基于MLLM的图像质量评估器，能同时评估恢复质量和幻觉存在性，优于现有指标并可作为奖励模型用于训练生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生成模型在图像生成和编辑任务上表现出色，但它们在需要像素级控制的低层视觉任务（如图像去噪、超分辨率、去模糊等）上的性能尚未被充分研究。现有基准缺乏对生成模型在低层视觉任务上的系统性评估，且缺乏与人类偏好对齐的质量评估指标。

Method: 1) 构建LL-Bench基准：收集2,469张真实退化图像，覆盖16种低层视觉退化任务；使用10个大规模生成模型和21个传统恢复模型生成28,919张恢复图像；通过专家级人工标注获得152,020个成对偏好和28,334个质量评分。2) 提出LL-Score：基于MLLM（多模态大语言模型）的图像质量评估器，同时评估恢复质量和幻觉存在性，通过指令微调使其与人类偏好对齐。

Result: LL-Bench系统诊断揭示了大规模生成模型在低层视觉任务中的性能边界和独特失败模式（如幻觉问题），与传统恢复模型形成对比。现有质量评估指标与人类评分存在显著差异。LL-Score在图像质量评估上优于现有指标，并且作为奖励模型可以有效指导生成模型在低层视觉任务上的训练。

Conclusion: LL-Bench为评估生成模型在低层视觉任务上的表现提供了全面的基准和人类偏好数据；LL-Score作为与人类偏好对齐的评估器和奖励模型，有望推动生成模型在低层视觉任务上的进一步发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了LL-Bench基准，包含低层视觉退化任务的图像数据集、人工偏好标注和质量评分，并提出了LL-Score评估指标。这完全符合[多模态生成]方向下的[生成模型评测与数据集]（generation_benchmark）规范，即生成模型评测基准、新数据集、自动评测指标和人类偏好评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-level vision evaluation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Low-level vision benchmark, generative model evaluation, image restoration, MLLM-based quality assessment, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Large-scale generative models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across image generation and editing tasks. However, their performance in low-level vision tasks, which require pixel-wise control, remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{LL-Bench}, a comprehensive \textbf{Benchmark} for evaluating the capabilities of large-scale generative models on \textbf{L}ow-\textbf{L}evel vision tasks. The benchmark comprises 2,469 real-world degraded images covering 16 low-level degradation tasks, and 28,919 restored images produced by 10 state-of-the-art large-scale generative models and 21 conventional restoration models, which are annotated with 152,020 expert-level pairwise human preferences and 28,334 quality scores. Built upon LL-Bench, we present a systematic diagnosis that reveals the performance boundaries and unique failure modes of large-scale generative models across diverse low-level vision tasks, compared with conventional representative restoration approaches. Moreover, we investigate the effectiveness of current quality evaluation metrics on LL-Bench, which exhibit significant discrepancy with human ratings. To better align restored-image quality assessment with human preferences, we further propose \textbf{LL-Score}, an MLLM-based evaluator that captures both restoration quality and hallucination existence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LL-score not only outperforms existing image quality assessment metrics, but also serves as a promising reward model for training generative models on low-level vision tasks.

</details>


### [163] [PaintBench: Deterministic Evaluation of Precise Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00188)
*Kai Xu, Ellis Brown, Shrikar Madhu, Rob Fergus, He He, Saining Xie*

TL;DR: PaintBench 是一个动态可扩展的精确视觉编辑评测基准，覆盖20种基础编辑操作（几何变换、结构操作、颜色变化、符号推理），通过程序化生成实现无限且抗污染的评测集，并采用确定性像素级评估。在11个图像编辑模型上的测试显示最高仅17.1% mIoU，揭示了当前模型在精确编辑任务上的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型在开放式视觉编辑上表现良好，但在需要精确单一答案的编辑任务上存在显著障碍。缺乏一个可扩展、抗污染、确定性评估的基准来系统衡量和推动精确视觉编辑的进展。

Method: 通过程序化生成构建 PaintBench 基准，包含20种基础精确编辑操作（几何变换、结构操作、颜色变化、符号推理四类），支持可配置复杂度实现无限扩展。采用确定性像素级评估指标（mIoU等），无需依赖有偏见的评判模型。同时构建 TinyGrafixBench 用于验证 PaintBench 分数与实际应用任务的相关性。

Result: 在11个图像编辑模型上，最高分仅17.1% mIoU。几何变换、大部分结构操作和基于公式的颜色变化是最具挑战性的类型。场景变化（物体数量、背景复杂度、配色方案、编辑区域大小）会显著影响性能。PaintBench 分数与 TinyGrafixBench 实际应用任务表现呈强线性相关（R²=0.91, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: PaintBench 为精确视觉编辑提供了严谨的评测基础，揭示了当前模型在该任务上的严重不足，并为推动该领域进展提供了可靠工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PaintBench，一个用于精确视觉编辑的确定性评测基准，属于生成模型评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for precise visual editing, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models. Signals: Precise visual editing benchmark, Deterministic pixel-level evaluation, Reveals severe model limitations, Strong correlation to applied tasks, Not directly on priority subtopics

Abstract: While current multimodal models are proficient at open-ended visual editing, executing precise single-answer edits remains an important obstacle. To probe this challenge, we introduce PaintBench, a dynamically scalable benchmark targeting 20 fundamental precise visual editing operations across four categories: geometric transformation, structural manipulation, color change, and symbolic reasoning. Procedural generation with configurable complexity enables an effectively infinite, contamination-resistant evaluation suite, and deterministic pixel-level evaluation eliminates reliance on bias-prone judge models. Across 11 image editing models, we find overall low performance, with the current highest-performing industry leader scoring only 17.1% (mIoU). Task decomposition reveals especially challenging operation types (geometric transformation, most structural manipulation, formula-based color change) and model-specific specializations. Fine-grained benchmark diagnostics further show performance degradations induced by scene variations in object count, background complexity, color scheme, and edit-region size. To test generalization of PaintBench scores to applied task performance, we create a procedural, deterministic evaluation for data visualization editing (TinyGrafixBench) and find strong linear correlation with PaintBench scores ($R^2 = 0.91$, $p < 0.001$). Altogether, PaintBench provides a rigorous foundation for measuring and driving progress in precise multimodal visual editing.

</details>


### [164] [Flow Matching for Convective-Scale Precipitation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00281)
*Tom Wetherell*

Main category: physics.ao-ph, cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文训练了一个 flow matching 模型用于对流尺度降水降尺度（8km→2km），以新加坡为研究区域，并与扩散模型 CPMGEM 进行对比。Flow matching 在空间技能评分（FSS、SAL）上持续优于扩散模型，但低估了降水分布的上尾，导致气候平均偏干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水预测对气候适应至关重要，动力降尺度计算成本高昂，生成式机器学习（尤其是扩散模型）已成为有前景的替代方案。本文探索 flow matching 这一新兴生成框架在降水降尺度中的潜力，并系统评估其与扩散模型的性能差异。

Method: 训练一个 flow matching 模型，将每日降水从 8 km 分辨率映射到 2 km 分辨率，覆盖新加坡附近的对流尺度区域。使用分数技能评分（FSS）和 SAL（结构、振幅、位置）评分作为评测指标，与基于分数的扩散模型 CPMGEM 进行对比。

Result: Flow matching 在所有降水阈值和邻域尺度上均取得更高的 FSS 分数，SAL 评分的结构和振幅分量更紧凑，位置技能相当。但 flow matching 低估了降水分布的上尾，导致气候平均存在干偏差。

Conclusion: Flow matching 是对流尺度降水降尺度任务中具有竞争力的生成框架，尤其擅长捕捉空间结构，但在极端降水量级上存在系统性低估，需要进一步改进。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 flow matching 模型在降水降尺度（downscaling）生成任务上的表现，并与扩散模型（CPMGEM）进行对比评测，属于生成模型在特定科学领域的应用与评测。最匹配的现有 canonical subtopic 是"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark），因为论文核心是对比 flow matching 与扩散模型的空间技能评分（FSS、SAL）等评测指标。

Importance reason: Focuses on precipitation downscaling, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Flow matching for downscaling, spatial skill improvement, dry bias in upper tail, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Generative machine learning is an increasingly important complement to dynamical downscaling for producing high-resolution precipitation projections, with diffusion models currently the leading approach. Flow matching is a related generative framework that has recently achieved strong results across image, video and other domains, and shown early promise for downscaling. We train a flow matching model to map daily precipitation from 8 km to 2 km over a convective-scale domain centred on Singapore, and benchmark it against CPMGEM, a score-based diffusion model. Flow matching achieves consistently better spatial skill: higher fractions skill score at every precipitation threshold and neighbourhood scale tested, and tighter structure and amplitude components of the SAL score with comparable location skill. However, flow matching underestimates the upper tail of the precipitation distribution, resulting in a dry bias in the climatological mean. These results suggest that flow matching is a competitive generative framework for convective-scale precipitation downscaling, particularly well suited to capturing spatial structure.

</details>

## 自回归视觉生成 [[Back]](#toc)

自回归图像与视频生成，包括 next-token、MaskGIT、VAR、MAR、LlamaGen 等范式。

### [165] [Where to Refine, When to Stop: Rethinking Redundancy via Latent Discrepancy for Efficient Visual Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00310)
*Changwang Mei, Peisong Wang, Zekun Li, Changsheng Li, Shuang Qiu, Qinghao Hu, Gang Li, Yifan Zhang, Zhihui Wei, Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LD-Pruning，一种基于潜空间差异度量的无训练框架，通过解码无关的区域选择和自适应无条件分支跳过，有效去除 VAR 模型中的冗余计算，实现高达 2.35x 的推理加速同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR 模型在高分辨率图像生成中推理延迟严重，现有加速方法多依赖启发式度量进行 token 剪枝，对复杂语义敏感且适应性差，亟需更准确的冗余识别方法。

Method: 提出 Latent Discrepancy（潜差异度）作为统一度量，通过测量生成过程中模型状态的变化量化 token 贡献；基于此设计 LD-Pruning 框架，包含解码无关的区域选择（decoding-free region selection）和自适应无条件分支跳过（adaptive unconditional-branch skipping）两个模块。

Result: 在 Infinity-8B 模型上实现最高 2.35x 加速，在多个分辨率下保持与原始模型相当的生成质量，优于现有启发式剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 潜空间/像素空间的信号能更准确地识别 VAR 生成中的冗余，LD-Pruning 是一种高效且通用的训练免费加速方案，可推广到其他自回归生成模型。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于 Visual Autoregressive (VAR) 模型的推理加速，属于自回归视觉生成范式下的效率优化工作，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "自回归视觉生成" (autoregressive_visual_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive image generation, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel training-free pruning method. Signals: autoregressive image generation, efficient inference, training-free acceleration, latent discrepancy, visual autoregressive model

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models deliver high-quality image generation but suffer from significant inference latency at high resolutions. Recent acceleration approaches most rely on heuristic measures with layer features to prune tokens. Such heuristics are sensitive to complex contextual semantics, leading to inaccurate identification of redundant computation and poor adaptability across prompts. We rethink redundancy in VAR from the perspective of its impact on pixel-space generation and introduce Latent Discrepancy. This unified metric quantifies a token's contribution by measuring the change in model states during generation. Our analysis shows that redundancy is more accurately identified when guided by image latent or pixel-space signals. We further observed that in classifier-free guidance (CFG), the convergence trend of the discrepancy between conditional and unconditional branches exhibits high dynamics with different prompts. Based on these findings, we propose LD-Pruning (Latent Discrepancy Pruning), a training-free framework that removes redundancy via latent discrepancy by integrating decoding-free region selection and adaptive unconditional-branch skipping. Extensive experiments show that LD-Pruning substantially reduces inference latency while maintaining high generation quality, achieving up to 2.35x speedup on Infinity-8B.

</details>


### [166] [Residual Decoder Adapter: ID-Preserving Tokenizer Adaption for Autoregressive Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01911)
*Dongxing Mao, Jinpeng Wang, Jiahao Tang, Kevin Qinghong Lin, Linjie Li, Zhengyuan Yang, Lijuan Wang, Min Li, Jingru Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出残差解码器适配器（RDA），在不重新训练视觉 tokenizer 和自回归模型的前提下，通过引入配对码本和平行残差分支来增强 tokenizer 的解码输出，显著提升 AR 模型的文本渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归模型在整体图像生成上表现优异，但在文本渲染方面存在模糊笔画和字母形状扭曲的问题。作者追溯该问题到视觉 tokenizer 难以重建细粒度细节。直接改进 tokenizer 需要同时重新训练 tokenizer 和 AR 模型，成本高昂。因此，作者希望在不重新训练现有 tokenizer 和 AR 模型的情况下提升文本渲染性能。

Method: 提出 Residual Decoder Adapter (RDA)，以 post-hoc 方式升级现有 tokenizer 而不改变其 token 空间。包含两个新组件：(1) 配对码本（paired codebook），与原始码本共享 token 分布；(2) 并行分支，学习重建图像与真实图像在像素空间中的微小差异（残差）。这种残差设计使得 tokenizer 的增强是非侵入式的，保持与已有 AR 模型的兼容性。

Result: RDA 大幅提升了文本渲染效果。在 TextAtlas 基准上，微调后的 Janus-Pro OCR 准确率从 24.52% 提升至 58.26%（TextVisionBlend），从 12.75% 提升至 36.81%（StyledTextSynth）。

Conclusion: RDA 通过轻量级、非侵入式的 tokenizer 解码器适配，有效解决了视觉自回归模型在文本渲染上的不足，无需重新训练整个模型，具有良好的实用性和兼容性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于视觉自回归（AR）模型的文本渲染能力提升，属于自回归视觉生成范式。现有 canonical subtopic "自回归视觉生成"（autoregressive_visual_generation）完美匹配。同时，论文涉及视觉 tokenizer 的改进，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly improves autoregressive text rendering via a non-invasive tokenizer adapter, aligning with high-priority continuous visual token and autoregressive image generation subtopics. Signals: Autoregressive image generation, continuous visual token enhancement, residual decoder adapter, text rendering improvement, no retraining required

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (AR) models generate images by predicting discrete tokens that are decoded by a visual tokenizer. Despite demonstrating strong overall image generation ability, they still underperform on text rendering with blur strokes and disrupt letter shapes. In this work, we trace this limitation to the visual tokenizer, which struggles to reconstruct fine-grained detail. Improving the tokenizer is straightforward but expensive, as it necessitates retraining both the tokenizer and the AR model. Can we improve text rendering performance of AR models without retraining the existing tokenizer and AR model? To achieve this, we propose the Residual Decoder Adapter(RDA) that upgrades an existing tokenizer post-hoc without changing its token space. Specifically, it refines the decoder output of the visual tokenizer by introducing two novel components: (i) a paired codebook that shares the token distribution with the original one; (ii) a parallel branch to learn the tiny differences (residual) between the reconstructed image and the ground-truth images in the pixel space. This residual design allows us to enhance the tokenizer non-invasively while preserving compatibility with prior AR models. RDA substantially improves text rendering significantly by a large margin. For instance, we boost finetuned Janus-Pro OCR accuracy rises from 24.52% to 58.26% (TextVisionBlend), from 12.75% to 36.81% (StyledTextSynth) on competitive TextAtlas benchmark. The code is available at

</details>

### [167] [Sparse Autoencoders for Interpretable Emotion Control in Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01479)
*Hongfei Du, Jiacheng Shi, Sidi Lu, Gang Zhou, Ye Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析基于LLM的TTS模型语义隐状态中的情感相关变化，识别出稀疏潜在特征，并提出一种特征级干预框架，通过操控少量潜在特征实现双向情感诱导与抑制，无需修改骨干参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的TTS系统虽然提升了语音表现力，但情感控制的可解释性不足。已有方法依赖外部条件或全局激活引导，对情感控制背后的内部表征缺乏深入理解。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从LLM-based TTS的语义隐状态中提取稀疏潜在特征，分析情感变化在多个稀疏特征上的分布；提出特征级干预框架，通过调整少量关键特征实现情感诱导与抑制，无需修改模型骨干参数。

Result: 实验表明，情感变化分布在多个稀疏潜在特征上，干预少量子集即可实现可解释的情感控制；不同潜在特征与特定声学属性（如音高）相关；该方法在情感诱导与抑制上达到或优于全局引导及现有TTS基线。

Conclusion: 稀疏自编码器为LLM-based TTS的情感控制提供了可解释的潜在特征分析工具，特征级干预框架在保持模型参数不变的前提下实现了有效的情感调控，揭示了情感表达源于多个潜在特征的协同贡献而非单一全局偏移。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于稀疏自编码器的可解释情感控制文本到语音（TTS）生成，属于音频生成方向，与现有规范子主题"音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS emotion control via SAE, which is outside user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-adjacent. Signals: Sparse autoencoders for interpretability, emotion control in TTS, feature-level intervention, no backbone modification, acoustic attribute association

Abstract: Integrating large language models (LLMs) into text-to-speech (TTS) systems has improved speech expressiveness, yet interpretable emotional control remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily rely on external conditioning or global activation steering, offering limited insight into the internal representations underlying emotional control. In this work, we analyze emotion-related variation in the semantic hidden states of LLM-based TTS models using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to identify sparse latent features. Our analysis shows that emotional variation is distributed across multiple sparse latent features, while intervening on a small subset enables interpretable emotion control. Building on this observation, we introduce a feature-level intervention framework for bidirectional emotion induction and suppression without modifying backbone parameters. We further show that distinct latent features are associated with specific acoustic attributes (e.g., pitch), suggesting that emotional expression arises from coordinated latent contributions rather than a single global shift. Empirically, steering these sparse latent features achieves comparable or superior emotion induction and suppression performance relative to global steering and existing TTS baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Sympatheia: Emotionally Adaptive Voice Assistant with Continuous Affect Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00851)
*Sukru Samet Dindar, Riki Shimizu, Xilin Jiang, Nima Mesgarani*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.SD, cs.HC, cs.LG, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (54.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Sympatheia 是一个情感自适应的语音到语音对话框架，通过从用户语音中推断的连续效价-唤醒度（VA）信号以及多模态传感模块提供的显式情感规格来条件化语音生成，构建了 Sympatheia-18k 情感条件合成对话语料库，在语义内容和语音表达上均实现了情感适配的响应生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常语音中的情感线索往往微弱、中性或模糊，现有情感对话系统难以准确推断用户情绪并做出恰当响应。需要一种能够处理弱情感信号、并融合多模态情感信息的语音对话框架。

Method: 提出 Sympatheia 框架，采用连续效价-唤醒度（VA）作为情感条件控制信号，条件化语音到语音对话生成。构建 Sympatheia-18k 情感条件合成对话语料库，包含12个情感锚点，分为情感子集（学习情感语音行为）和中性子集（隔离显式情感控制）。支持从语音、面部表情、生物信号和文本情感描述等多种传感模块获取 VA 估计。

Result: Sympatheia 在生成语义内容和语音表达均情感适配的响应方面优于语音对话基线。当语音本身提供有限情感证据时，融合多模态传感模块的 VA 接口能显著改善响应对齐。

Conclusion: 连续情感条件化是构建情感自适应语音助手的有效实用步骤，多模态情感融合能弥补语音情感线索不足的问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出情感自适应的语音对话框架，涉及语音生成与情感条件控制，属于音频生成范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）涵盖语音生成、TTS、跨模态条件音频生成，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on emotional voice assistants, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: continuous affect conditioning, valence-arousal control, speech-to-speech dialogue, emotion-adaptive generation, synthetic dialogue corpus

Abstract: Empathetic spoken dialogue systems must infer a user's emotional state to respond appropriately, yet everyday speech often carries weak, neutral, or ambiguous affective cues. To address this, we introduce Sympatheia, a speech-to-speech dialogue framework conditioned on affect inferred from the user's speech and, when available, explicit affect specifications provided as a continuous valence--arousal (VA) control signal by a multimodal sensing module or user interface. To train our model, we construct Sympatheia-18k, an emotion-conditioned synthetic spoken dialogue corpus with 12 emotion anchors. This dataset includes an emotional split for learning affective speech behavior, and a neutral split that pairs emotionally neutral queries with multiple emotion-conditioned responses to isolate explicit emotion control in emotionally ambiguous cases. Empirical results show that Sympatheia outperforms speech conversational baselines in generating responses whose semantic content and spoken delivery are both emotionally appropriate. We further show that the same VA interface can integrate emotion estimates from diverse sensing modules, including facial expression, biosignals, and textual affect descriptions, improving response alignment when speech alone provides limited emotional evidence. These results suggest that continuous affect conditioning is an effective practical step for building emotionally adaptive voice assistants.

</details>


### [169] [JenBridge: Adaptive Long-Form Video Soundtracking across Scene Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01703)
*Jiashuo Yu, Yao Yao, Boyu Chen, Alex Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: JenBridge 是一个面向长视频配乐生成的模块化框架，采用基于 flow matching 的 Transformer 生成模型，通过两阶段训练（文本-音频预训练 + 视频域适配）和 LLM Agent 驱动的自适应过渡机制，实现跨场景切换的高保真、连贯配乐生成，并提出了 LVS Benchmark 用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 音乐系统主要针对短片段设计，缺乏处理长视频中场景切换时配乐连贯性的机制，导致生成的配乐在场景过渡处不自然、叙事连贯性差。

Method: JenBridge 采用两阶段范式：1）在大规模文本-音频语料上预训练基于 flow matching 的 Transformer 生成模型，建立音乐先验；2）通过双文本-视觉条件对齐适配到视频域。核心创新是自适应过渡机制（Adaptive Transition Mechanism），包含多种过渡风格工具包（含生成式过渡方法），并由 LLM Agent 作为"导演"智能选择最适合每个叙事转折的过渡方式。

Result: 在提出的 LVS Benchmark 上，JenBridge 在客观和主观指标上均显著优于现有方法，尤其在过渡自然度和整体叙事连贯性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: JenBridge 展示了模块化、可解释的长视频配乐生成框架的有效性，为全自动、专业级视频配乐生成迈出了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长视频配乐生成（video soundtracking），属于音频与多模态联合生成任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video soundtracking with flow matching, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: flow matching, long-form video, LLM agent, transition mechanism, LVS benchmark

Abstract: We address the challenge of generating high-fidelity, long-form soundtracks that remain coherent across scene transitions. Existing AI music systems are mainly designed for short, isolated clips and lack mechanisms to ensure narrative continuity. We present JenBridge, a modular and interpretable framework for adaptive long-form video soundtracking that ensures both high-fidelity audio generation and transition naturalness. The core architecture is a Transformer-based generative model trained with a flow-matching objective, following a two-stage paradigm: pretraining on large-scale text-audio corpora to establish robust musical priors, then adapting to the video domain with dual text-visual conditioning for precise cross-modal alignment. Crucially, to achieve long-form coherence across diverse scene changes, JenBridge incorporates a novel adaptive transition mechanism. This system features a versatile toolkit of transition styles, including a generative transition method, and uniquely employs a Large Language Model (LLM) Agent that acts as a director to select the most appropriate transition for each narrative shift intelligently. To rigorously assess this task, we propose the LVS Benchmark, a new benchmark that includes a curated dataset and novel evaluation metrics focusing on holistic and transition-aware assessment. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate that JenBridge significantly outperforms existing methods in both objective and subjective metrics, particularly in terms of transition naturalness and overall narrative coherence. JenBridge represents a significant step towards fully automated, professional-quality video soundtracking.

</details>
