### [170] [A Multi-Domain Red Teaming Framework for Safety, Robustness, and Fairness Evaluation of Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00027)
*Andrei Marian Feier, Veysel Kocaman, Yigit Gul, Ahmet Korkmaz, Alexander Thomas, Aleksei Zakharov, Jay Gil, Mehmet Butgul, David Talby*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (68.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多领域红队测试框架，在690个临床场景下评估了11个当代LLM的安全性、鲁棒性和公平性，发现平均性能掩盖了关键安全场景中的完全失败，并揭示了人口统计修改导致的10-20%错误放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉临床实践中常见的对抗性或伦理复杂条件下的模型行为，需要更全面的安全评估框架。

Method: 构建了涵盖9个领域、150多个子类别的690个临床场景，引入对抗性变换，采用七维度评分标准结合LLM辅助评分和人工验证的混合评估方法。

Result: 模型平均得分0.791-0.984，但高性能系统在个别安全关键场景中完全失败；公平性相关任务在人口统计修改后错误放大10-20%；人工评审发现了自动评估遗漏的临床相关失败。

Conclusion: 性能方差和最坏情况失败比平均准确度更能反映临床可靠性，结合自动化与临床专家监督的混合评估方法对可信安全评估至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多领域红队测试框架，评估医疗大语言模型在对抗性场景下的安全性、鲁棒性和公平性，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical LLM safety, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domain. Signals: Medical domain red teaming, safety/robustness/fairness evaluation, clinical LLM benchmark, demographic bias amplification, hybrid human-AI evaluation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across healthcare, yet existing benchmarks fail to capture model behavior under adversarial or ethically complex conditions common in clinical practice. We developed a multi-domain red teaming framework evaluating eleven contemporary LLMs across 690 clinically grounded scenarios spanning nine domains and over 150 subcategories. Scenarios incorporated adversarial transformations, and responses were assessed using a seven-dimension rubric with LLM-assisted scoring and human-in-the-loop validation. Results revealed substantial performance variance, with mean scores ranging from 0.791 to 0.984. Critically, several high-performing systems produced complete failures in individual safety-critical scenarios, demonstrating that aggregate accuracy masks clinically meaningful risk. The highest-performing systems (X-BAI, GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.1) achieved scores above 0.97 with low variance, while performance varied significantly across domains. Equity-related tasks showed 10-20% error amplification with demographic modifications, and human reviewers identified clinically relevant failures missed by automated evaluation. Our findings demonstrate that performance variance and worst-case failures provide more clinically meaningful reliability indicators than mean accuracy alone, and that hybrid evaluation approaches combining automation with clinician oversight are essential for credible safety assessment.

</details>


### [171] [Visual-Noise Guided In-Context Distillation for Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00105)
*Junkai Chen, Yuhao He, Junxiang You, Ruiqi Liu, Chenyu Wang, Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VGID（Visual-Noise Guided In-Context Distillation），一种基于蒸馏的 MLLM 机器遗忘框架。通过视觉扰动与文本上下文遗忘的双模态干预构建教师分布，引导学生模型实现参数级遗忘，无需外部教师模型或显式不良响应标注，在遗忘效果与模型效用之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 可能记忆并泄露敏感或受限知识，带来隐私与安全风险。现有训练式遗忘方法难以平衡遗忘效果与模型效用，而免训练上下文遗忘方法无法在参数层面移除记忆知识，易受逆向工程攻击，且在视觉输入强条件信号下效果不足。

Method: VGID 框架通过双模态干预（视觉噪声扰动 + 文本上下文遗忘提示）从冻结基座模型动态构建面向遗忘的教师分布，然后利用该分布作为蒸馏信号，指导学生模型进行参数级遗忘，无需外部教师模型或显式不良响应标注。

Result: 在代表性设置下，VGID 将遗忘集 ROUGE-L 降低 0.371，而保留集 ROUGE-L 仅下降 0.055，在遗忘效果与模型效用之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: VGID 通过蒸馏框架有效实现了 MLLM 的参数级遗忘，兼顾了遗忘效果与模型通用能力保持，为多模态模型安全提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的机器遗忘（unlearning），旨在移除模型中的敏感或受限知识以提升隐私与安全性，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" 涵盖安全评测、越狱、对抗攻击与防御，unlearning 作为安全防御的一种形式可合理归入该主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM unlearning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: MLLM unlearning, distillation-based framework, visual perturbation, in-context unlearning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress on vision-language tasks, but they may also memorize and expose sensitive or restricted knowledge, raising concerns about privacy and broader safety risks. Machine Unlearning (MU) provides a promising way to remove targeted undesirable knowledge from trained models without retraining from scratch while preserving general model utility. Nevertheless, effective unlearning in MLLMs remains particularly challenging. Existing training-based methods often struggle to balance unlearning effectiveness and model utility. In contrast, training-free methods such as in-context unlearning preserve model utility by avoiding parameter updates, but they do not remove memorized knowledge at the parameter level and may remain vulnerable to reverse-engineering attacks. More importantly, in-context unlearning is insufficient in multimodal settings, where visual inputs can provide strong conditioning signals and induce undesirable outputs. To address these challenges, we propose Visual-Noise Guided In-Context Distillation (VGID), a distillation-based framework for MLLM unlearning. VGID dynamically constructs an unlearning-oriented teacher distribution from the frozen base model through dual-modal intervention that combines visual perturbation with textual in-context unlearning. The resulting intervention-induced distribution serves as a teacher signal for distillation, guiding the student model toward parameter-level unlearning without requiring external teacher models or explicit undesirable response annotations. Experimental results show that VGID achieves strong unlearning effectiveness while preserving competitive model utility, reducing forget set ROUGE-L by 0.371 with only a 0.055 drop in retain set ROUGE-L in a representative setting.

</details>


### [172] [Adversarially Robust Control of Conditional Value-at-Risk via Rockafellar-Uryasev Conformal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00320)
*Catherine Chen, Jingyan Shen, Zhun Deng, Lihua Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线、无分布假设的CVaR（条件风险价值）控制框架，通过结合共形推断、在线学习与Rockafellar-Uryasev变分表示，在非平稳和对抗性环境下为非线性尾部风险函数提供可证明的安全保证，并在投资组合风险管理和LLM毒性缓解任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典风险控制方法依赖于平稳性或期望线性性假设，无法应对现代高风险部署场景中数据分布漂移或策略性对抗变化的情况。尾部风险（如CVaR）在金融风控和LLM安全等场景中至关重要，但缺乏在对抗环境下的在线控制方法。

Method: 利用共形尾部风险控制、在线学习与Rockafellar-Uryasev CVaR变分表示之间的深层联系，设计了一种在线CVaR控制算法。该方法无需对数据生成过程做任何假设，提供对抗性遗憾保证，并证明经验CVaR渐近受控于目标水平，且控制渐近紧致。

Result: 在投资组合风险管理中有效控制了尾部风险；在LLM毒性缓解任务中，该方法能在对抗性提示下将毒性CVaR控制在目标水平以下，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的对抗鲁棒CVaR控制框架为高风险场景下的尾部风险管理提供了理论保证和实用工具，尤其适用于LLM安全等需要应对对抗攻击的领域。

Classification reason: 论文提出了一种对抗鲁棒的CVaR控制框架，并在LLM毒性缓解（toxicity mitigation）上进行了应用验证，属于多模态/语言模型的安全与可靠性方向。映射到[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）这一规范子主题，因为论文的核心应用场景之一是对抗环境下LLM的毒性控制，属于安全防御范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial CVaR control for LLM safety, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: conformal inference, adversarial robustness, CVaR control, LLM toxicity mitigation, distribution-free

Abstract: We present an online, distribution-free framework for controlling the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), extending conformal tail risk control to non-stationary and adversarial environments. Unlike classical risk control methods, which rely on stationarity or linearity of expectation, our approach provides provable safety guarantees for a nonlinear tail risk functional under arbitrary data-generating processes that may drift or shift strategically over time. By leveraging deep connections between conformal tail risk control, online learning, and the variational representation of CVaR introduced by Rockafellar and Uryasev, we develop a novel procedure for online CVaR control with adversarial regret guarantees. The proposed method operates without assumptions on the underlying data-generating process, making it broadly applicable in modern high-stakes deployment settings. We prove that the realized empirical CVaR is asymptotically controlled at the target level, and that the resulting control is asymptotically tight up to a finite-sample conservatism gap. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on portfolio risk management and toxicity mitigation for Large Language Models (LLMs), where rare but catastrophic failures dominate system risk.

</details>


### [173] [TukaBench: A Culturally Grounded Jailbreak Benchmark for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01322)
*Victor Akinode, Senyu Li, Wassim Hamidouche, Waqas Zamir, Inbal Becker-Reshef, David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TUKABENCH 是一个面向七种非洲语言的越狱安全评测基准，通过人工翻译、文化适配、人工策划和代码混合四种设置扩展了 JailbreakBench，发现使用非洲语言可降低模型拒绝率，且文化适配提示拒绝率最低，同时揭示了模型理解失败和 LLM-as-a-judge 在低资源语言中可靠性下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 安全评测高度以英语为中心，低资源语言（尤其是非洲语言）的安全评估严重缺失，亟需一个文化适配的多语言越狱基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于 JailbreakBench (JBB) 构建 TUKABENCH，包含四种设置：(1) JBB 提示的人工翻译；(2) 英语提示适配非洲文化背景后人工翻译；(3) 通过与 GPT-5.2 交互验证的人工策划提示；(4) 英语与非洲语言混合的代码切换提示。在多个闭源和开源模型上进行评测，引入 Deflection（偏离）作为新的分类标签，并使用人工标注验证 LLM-as-a-judge 的可靠性。

Result: 使用非洲语言提示相比英语降低了模型拒绝率；文化适配的提示拒绝率最低；低资源语言和较少支持的脚本中 LLM-as-a-judge 与人工标注的一致性下降。

Conclusion: 多语言越狱安全评测至关重要，文化适配因素显著影响模型安全行为，现有 LLM-as-a-judge 方法在低资源语言中可靠性不足，需要更鲁棒的评估方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多语言（非洲语言）LLM越狱安全评测，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴下的安全评测与越狱攻击方向，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on African language jailbreak safety, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Low-resource language safety, cultural adaptation, LLM-as-a-judge reliability, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Safety evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains heavily English-centric, leaving Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), particularly African ones, critically underexplored. We introduce TUKABENCH, a jailbreak benchmark for seven African languages that extends JailbreakBench (JBB) beyond direct translation through four settings: human translation of JBB prompts, English adaptation to African contexts followed by human translation, human-curated prompts validated through interactions with GPT-5.2, and code-switched prompts combining English and African languages, isolating the effect of language, cultural grounding, and prompt evasiveness on model safety. Across closed and open models, prompting in African languages reduces refusal relative to English, with culturally adapted prompts leading to least refusal. The evaluation also surfaces two structural limitations: model comprehension failures and reduced LLM-as-a-judge reliability in LRLs. To capture the first, we introduce Deflection alongside Refused and Jailbroken; to assess the second, we validate outputs with human annotations, showing that judge-human agreement drops in lower-resource languages and less commonly supported scripts.

</details>


### [174] [MESA: Improving MoE Safety Alignment via Decentralized Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00651)
*Yitong Sun, Yao Huang, Teng Li, Ranjie Duan, Yichi Zhang, Xingjun Ma, Hui Xue, Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MESA 提出了一种针对 MoE 架构大语言模型的安全对齐框架，通过最优传输理论将安全责任分散到多个专家上，避免安全能力集中在少数专家导致的脆弱性，同时通过动态路由精炼确保精确激活安全模块，在保持模型有用性的同时提升防御有害攻击的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构虽然高效，但存在"安全稀疏性"问题——安全能力集中在少数专家中，容易被对抗性绕过。同时，传统的对齐方法均匀调整所有参数，忽略了不同专家的功能差异，反而损害了模型性能。因此需要一种针对 MoE 的、能分散安全责任同时最小化对有用性干扰的对齐方法。

Method: MESA 基于最优传输理论，包含两个核心机制：(1) 专家容量重分配（Expert Capacity Reallocation）——构建传输成本矩阵，将安全职责分配给成本效益最高的专家；(2) 动态路由精炼（Dynamic Routing Refinement）——约束路由模块精确激活这些分散的安全专家模块。

Result: 实验表明，MESA 在多种有害基准测试上取得了鲁棒的防御性能，同时保持了模型的有用性（helpfulness），有效解决了 MoE 的安全稀疏性问题。

Conclusion: MESA 通过去中心化的专家安全对齐策略，为 MoE 大模型提供了一种兼顾安全性与有用性的对齐框架，验证了最优传输理论在模型安全对齐中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MoE 大语言模型的安全对齐问题，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴。虽然论文主要针对文本 LLM，但安全对齐与攻防是该 canonical subtopic 的核心主题，且 MoE 安全稀疏性问题是当前多模态安全领域的重要关注点。

Importance reason: Focuses on MoE safety alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: MoE safety, optimal transport, safety sparsity, adversarial robustness, LLM alignment

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale Large Language Models (LLMs) efficiently, enabling greater capacity with reduced computational cost by dynamically routing inputs to relevant experts, yet introduce a critical vulnerability: Safety Sparsity, where safety capabilities concentrate in few experts, making them susceptible to adversarial bypassing. Meanwhile, conventional alignment methods uniformly adapt all parameters, ignoring their functional differences and inadvertently degrading performances. To address these challenges, we propose MESA (MoE Safety Alignment), a targeted alignment framework for MoE-based LLMs that strategically decentralizes safety responsibility to maximize coverage while minimizing interference with utility. Based on Optimal Transport (OT) theory, MESA operates through two mechanisms: (1) Expert Capacity Reallocation uses a transport cost matrix to distribute safety duties to the most cost-effective experts, and (2) Dynamic Routing Refinement constrains the router to precisely activate these decentralized modules. Experiments show that MESA achieves robust defensive performance against varied harmful benchmarks while preserving helpfulness. Code is available at .

</details>


### [175] [RoleCDE:Benchmarking and Mitigating Role-Alignment Trade-offs in Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01552)
*Huayi Lai, Shichao Song, Simin Niu, Hanyu Wang, Jiawei Yang, Zhouxing Wang, Zhiqiang Yin, Xun Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出RoleCDE，首个在角色特定价值观与对齐约束之间结构化冲突下评估角色扮演智能体的基准，包含约8000个角色配置和近24000个困境实例。实验发现"角色价值解耦"现象：模型在冲突时系统性地偏向对齐/道德决策而非角色价值观。基于RoleCDE的微调可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演智能体评测主要关注表面角色一致性（如语言风格、知识），缺乏对角色价值观与对齐约束（安全、道德）冲突下决策能力的评估。实际应用中，角色扮演智能体常面临角色特定价值观与通用对齐约束之间的矛盾，现有基准无法系统评测这种价值冲突下的决策能力。

Method: 构建RoleCDE基准：将角色感知决策形式化为认知困境场景，联合评估角色-场景定位、价值冲突解决和决策倾向。覆盖约8000个角色配置和近24000个困境实例，分三个难度级别和八个角色类别。基于该基准对主流LLM进行评测，并设计基于RoleCDE的微调方法缓解角色价值解耦现象。

Result: 主流LLM在角色价值观与对齐约束冲突时表现出"角色价值解耦"现象——系统性地偏向对齐/道德一致决策而非角色特定价值观。该行为对困境难度不敏感，但在不同角色类别间差异显著。基于RoleCDE的微调有效缓解了解耦，同时保持了一般角色扮演保真度和通用推理能力。

Conclusion: 角色扮演智能体存在角色价值解耦问题，即模型在冲突时优先考虑对齐约束而非角色价值观。RoleCDE基准为评估和缓解这一问题提供了有效工具，基于该基准的微调可改善价值权衡推理能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究角色扮演智能体在角色特定价值观与对齐约束（安全/道德）之间的冲突决策问题，属于多模态安全与对齐的范畴。虽然论文主要聚焦LLM而非多模态，但其核心关注的是角色-对齐价值冲突下的安全与对齐评测，与"多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）这一规范子主题在安全评测、对齐约束冲突方面高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on role-playing agent alignment, not core user priorities like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: role-playing agents, alignment trade-offs, benchmark, value decoupling, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Role-playing agents(RPAs) are widely used to steer large language models(LLMs) toward role-consistent behavior, yet existing benchmarks mainly evaluate surface-level fidelity and offer limited insight into decision making under role-alignment value conflicts. To address this gap, we introduce RoleCDE, the first benchmark designed to evaluate RPAs under structured conflicts between role-specific values and alignment-oriented constraints. RoleCDE formulates role-aware decision making as cognitive dilemma scenarios, jointly evaluating role-scenario grounding, value conflict resolution, and decision tendencies. The benchmark is constructed at scale, covering approximately 8k diverse role profiles and scenarios and nearly 24k dilemma instances across three difficulty levels and eight role categories. Evaluation of several mainstream LLMs reveals a "Role Value Decoupling" phenomenon, where agents systematically default to alignment-and morality-consistent decisions rather than role-specific values when the two conflict, even under explicit role conditioning. This behavior is largely invariant to dilemma difficulty but varies substantially across role categories. We further show that RoleCDE-based fine-tuning effectively mitigates this decoupling by improving value trade-off reasoning, while preserving general role-playing fidelity and general reasoning performance. Code is available at: .

</details>


### [176] [SafeSteer: Localized On-Policy Distillation for Efficient Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02530)
*Hao Li, Jingkun An, Zijun Song, Pengyu Zhu, Rui Li, Hao Wang, Wendi Feng, Yesheng Liu, Lijun Li, Jin-Ge Yao, Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeSteer提出一种基于安全token的局部化on-policy蒸馏方法，通过激活操控构建安全教师模型，仅对安全相关token施加反向KL惩罚，在仅使用100个有害样本且无需通用数据的情况下，实现了安全性与通用能力之间的优越权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐方法（如RLHF）往往导致对齐税（alignment tax），即通用能力下降。已有缓解方法依赖大量通用数据或辅助奖励模型，成本高昂。作者认为安全特征在输出分布中本质上是稀疏的，对齐只需要局部修改而非全局权衡，因此提出一种轻量级、局部化的安全对齐方法。

Method: SafeSteer包含三个步骤：(1) 通过激活操控（activation steering）构建安全教师模型；(2) 设计安全token选择算法，识别与安全相关的token；(3) 在训练时仅对选中的安全token施加反向KL散度惩罚（on-policy蒸馏），其余token保持原样，从而保留通用能力。仅需100个有害样本，无需通用数据。

Result: 在7个安全基准上取得强安全性能，在5个通用能力基准上仅有极小退化。相比基线方法，所需有害样本不到1%，且无需任何通用数据，显著降低了对齐成本。

Conclusion: SafeSteer验证了安全对齐可以通过局部化修改实现，安全特征的稀疏性使得仅对安全token进行蒸馏即可有效对齐，同时大幅降低对齐成本和通用能力损失。该方法为高效安全对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注LLM的安全对齐（safety alignment），属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴下的安全对齐方法。虽然论文主要针对文本LLM，但安全对齐方法可迁移至多模态LLM，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖安全对齐、防御等主题，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: safety alignment, on-policy distillation, activation steering, reverse KL penalty, efficient alignment

Abstract: Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values often degrades their general capabilities, termed the alignment tax. Existing methods mitigate this by balancing dual objectives, which heavily rely on massive general-purpose data or auxiliary reward models. In this paper, we argue that, because safety features are inherently sparse within the output distribution, alignment requires localized modifications rather than global trade-offs. To this end, we propose SafeSteer, which performs on-policy distillation confined to safety tokens. First, we construct a safety teacher via activation steering. Based on this teacher, we develop a safety token selection algorithm. Consequently, SafeSteer restricts the reverse KL penalty to these tokens during training to preserve general capabilities. Experimental results across diverse models show that our SafeSteer achieves a superior trade-off between safety and general capability compared with existing methods, attaining strong safety performance on seven safety benchmarks with only minimal degradation on five general capability benchmarks. Notably, SafeSteer requires only 100 harmful samples without using any general-purpose data, less than 1% of what previous baselines used, considerably reducing alignment cost. More details are on our project page at .

</details>


### [177] [What to Test Next: Interpretable Coverage Gap Discovery in Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01624)
*Abhishek Aich, Sparsh Garg, Vijay Kumar BG, Turgun Yusuf Kashgari, Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.SE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SliceScorer，一种用于驾驶VLM缺失测试切片推荐的确定性评分规则，结合暴露先验和邻域失败先验来发现高风险覆盖缺口；并嵌入SliceNav，一个LLM编排的验证流水线，支持从开发者查询到定向评估的端到端工作流。实验表明该方法在发现高风险覆盖缺口方面优于先前的切片发现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶VLM需要在多样化的Operational Design Domains (ODDs)条件下准确理解场景，但现有验证方法存在大量未测试的切片（missing slices），导致经验性故障率不可靠。需要一种可解释、可审计且保守的方法来推荐缺失但高风险的条件切片进行测试。

Method: 提出SliceScorer，一种确定性评分规则，包含两个先验：(i) 基于暴露的覆盖先验，优先考虑罕见、测试不足的区域；(ii) 邻域失败先验，从相似已测试条件传播风险。将SliceScorer嵌入SliceNav，一个LLM编排的验证流水线，模型解释开发者查询并选择相关算子（分诊、评分、获取、评估）和词汇扩展，组合验证工作流，同时保持所有评分确定性和可审计性。

Result: 在三个驾驶VLM（WiseAD、DriveMM、Cosmos-Reason2-2B）上的实验表明，SliceNav比先前的切片发现方法更有效地发现高风险覆盖缺口，同时在条件空间中保持多样化的推荐。消融实验验证了两个评分组件均有贡献，定性分析展示了从开发者查询到定向评估的端到端工作流。

Conclusion: SliceScorer和SliceNav为驾驶VLM的安全关键验证提供了一种可解释、可审计且保守的缺失切片推荐方法，能够有效发现高风险覆盖缺口，有助于提升驾驶VLM在多样化条件下的可靠性和安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注驾驶VLM在Operational Design Domains (ODDs)下的覆盖缺口发现与安全验证，提出SliceScorer和SliceNav用于高覆盖风险区域的检测与推荐。这属于多模态模型的安全验证与可靠性评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度相关，因此复用该subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving VLM safety validation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Driving VLM safety, Coverage gap discovery, Interpretable scoring, LLM-orchestrated verification, Not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Driving vision-language models (VLMs) must accurately understand scenes across diverse conditions defined by Operational Design Domains (ODDs), yet verification remains sparse: many slices are missing, making empirical failure rates unreliable. We propose SliceScorer, a deterministic scoring rule for missing-slice recommendation that combines (i) an exposure-based coverage prior to prioritize rare, under-tested regions, and (ii) a neighbor-failure prior that propagates risk from similar tested conditions. SliceScorer is deliberately simple - interpretable, auditable, and conservative - properties essential for safety-critical validation. For stress testing beyond the declared ODD, we embed SliceScorer within SliceNav, an LLM-orchestrated verification pipeline where the model interprets developer queries to select relevant operators (triage, scoring, acquisition, evaluation) and vocabulary extensions, composing verification workflows while keeping all scoring deterministic and auditable. Experiments on three driving VLMs (WiseAD, DriveMM, Cosmos-Reason2-2B) show that SliceNav surfaces high-risk coverage gaps more effectively than prior slice-discovery methods while maintaining diverse recommendations across the condition space. Ablations confirm both scoring components contribute, and qualitative analysis demonstrates end-to-end workflows from developer query to targeted evaluation.

</details>


### [178] [Jailbreaking Multimodal Large Language Models using Multi-Clip Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02111)
*Choongwon Kang, Seungjong Sun, Hyunmin Jun, Jang Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multi-Clip Video (MCV) SafetyBench 数据集，系统研究多片段视频输入如何影响 MLLM 的安全对齐漏洞。实验表明，视频片段数量越多、内容越多样、动态性越强，攻击成功率越高；视频模态比图像模态更脆弱。基于图像模态相对鲁棒的特性，提出了一种防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究已表明 MLLM 的安全对齐可通过视觉输入被绕过，但视频输入的哪些特性（如片段数量、多样性、动态性）会诱发漏洞尚不清楚。本文旨在系统性地探究这一问题。

Method: 构建 MCV SafetyBench 数据集（2,920 个视频），每个视频由多个描述有害查询相关上下文的短视频片段组成。在 8 个代表性视频 MLLM 上进行系统实验，分析片段数量、视频动态性、上下文多样性对攻击成功率的影响，并提出基于图像模态鲁棒性的防御策略。

Result: 攻击成功率随视频片段数量增加而持续上升；视频模态比图像模态更脆弱；动态视频比静态视频更脆弱；视频包含更多样化的上下文时更脆弱。

Conclusion: 视频输入的多片段、动态性和多样性是导致 MLLM 安全漏洞的关键因素，利用图像模态的相对鲁棒性可设计有效防御。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型（MLLM）的视频越狱攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM safety and video jailbreaking, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Video modality vulnerability, Multi-clip attack success, Defense via image robustness, MLLM safety alignment, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced to process video inputs, concerns have emerged about their potential for malicious misuse. Prior jailbreak studies have shown that safety alignment in MLLMs can be bypassed through visual inputs, yet it remains unclear which properties of video inputs induce this vulnerability. To address this gap, we introduce Multi-Clip Video (MCV) SafetyBench, a dataset of 2,920 videos designed to evaluate how the diversity of video inputs affects the vulnerability of MLLMs. Each video consists of multiple short clips depicting diverse contexts related to a harmful query. Experiments on eight representative video MLLMs show that attack success consistently increases with the number of clips. Our results further indicate that the video modality is (1) more vulnerable than the image modality, (2) more vulnerable to dynamic videos than to static videos, and (3) more vulnerable when videos contain more diverse contexts. Building on these findings, we propose a defense strategy that leverages the relative robustness of the image modality.

</details>


### [179] [Cross-modal linkage risk in clinical vision-language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02276)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh, Mahshad Lotfinia, Sven Nebelung, Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (40.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了临床视觉语言模型（VLM）中图像与放射学报告之间的跨模态重链接隐私风险，发现模型临床专业化程度越高，重链接能力越强；并提出了一种无需重新训练、仅对投影头进行差分隐私微调的防御方法，在显著降低重链接风险的同时基本保持图像表征的临床效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床场景中，胸部X光片和放射学报告在采集后通常被刻意分离存储（如图像数据共享或报告受控访问），但VLM在训练时学习到的共享嵌入空间可能通过余弦相似度将匿名图像与其原始报告重新关联，构成隐私泄露风险。现有研究缺乏对此类跨模态重链接风险的系统量化评估和有效防御方法。

Method: 将隐私风险形式化为图像到报告的检索任务（image-to-report retrieval），使用MIMIC-CXR和CheXpert Plus两个公开配对数据集作为基准。评估了从通用到临床专业化的多个VLM（包括CLIP、BioViL、CXR-BERT等）。防御方法：冻结图像和文本编码器，仅对定义对齐层的投影头应用差分隐私优化（DP-SGD），设置epsilon=0.34, delta=6×10⁻⁶。

Result: 最强VLM在N=100候选池中检索正确报告的概率是随机水平的15倍，在N=10,000时为50倍，在全数据库规模下仍显著高于随机水平。在病理匹配的困难负样本下信号依然存在。DP微调后，Recall@1在N=10,000时降低61.8%，且零样本迁移到CheXpert Plus；图像侧线性探测分类的宏AUROC仅从79.63%微降至79.43%。

Conclusion: 临床VLM存在显著的跨模态重链接隐私风险，且随模型专业化程度增强而加剧。对共享对齐层进行有针对性的差分隐私微调可以在不显著损害图像表征临床效用的前提下，大幅降低这种风险，为临床多模态模型的隐私保护提供了一种实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究临床VLM中跨模态链接的隐私风险（图像-报告重识别攻击）并提出差分隐私防御方法，属于多模态安全与隐私保护范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on clinical VLM privacy risks, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords like clinical. Signals: Clinical VLM privacy, Cross-modal re-linkage risk, DP finetuning defense, Not in priority subtopics, Contains negative keyword clinical

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) trained on paired chest radiographs and radiology reports learn a shared embedding space that can preserve instance-level image-report correspondence. This poses a privacy risk in settings where radiographs and reports are deliberately kept separate after acquisition, such as image-only data sharing or access-controlled reports, because a de-identified image may be re-linked to its original narrative report through cosine similarity alone. We formalized this as image-to-report retrieval and used public paired cohorts, in which the true pairing is known by design, as ground-truth benchmarks to audit the risk rather than as the privacy scenario. Evaluating VLMs of increasing clinical specialization on 406,241 paired examples from 126,804 patients across MIMIC-CXR (43,793 held-out pairs) and external CheXpert Plus (29,296 pairs), we found that re-linkage rose systematically with specialization: the strongest VLM retrieved the correct report at 15 times chance at a candidate pool of N = 100, 50 times chance at N = 10,000, and well above chance at full-database scale. The signal persisted under pathology-matched hard negatives that removed disease-label shortcuts, indicating correspondence beyond broad diagnostic categories. To reduce it without retraining, we froze both encoders and applied differentially private optimization only to the projection heads defining the alignment layer (epsilon = 0.34, delta = 6x10-6). This reduced Recall@1 by 61.8% at N = 10,000 on MIMIC-CXR and transferred to CheXpert Plus without retraining, while image-side utility was largely preserved: macro AUROC for linear-probe classification across 14 labels shifted only from 79.63% to 79.43%. Targeted DP finetuning of the shared alignment layer can substantially reduce cross-modal re-linkage without materially degrading the image representations that make these models clinically useful.

</details>


### [180] [Auditing Asset-Specific Preferences in Financial Large Language Models: Evidence from Bitcoin Representations and Portfolio Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02528)
*Wenbin Wu*

Main category: q-fin.GN, cs.LG, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三级审计协议，系统检测金融大语言模型对比特币是否存在内置偏好。行为审计发现模型对比特币的排名具有框架依赖性；通过稀疏自编码器在Gemma 3中定位到一个主导的比特币选择性特征，放大/抑制该特征可因果性地改变模型输出；该特征扰动可影响下游投资组合分配决策，但影响幅度有限（有界行为杠杆）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正被用于金融顾问和交易代理，但它们是否对特定资产存在内置偏见尚未被系统检验。本文旨在回答三个问题：LLM是否系统性偏好某些金融工具？能否识别出对偏好有因果影响力的内部表征？该表征是否影响下游金融决策？

Method: 设计三级审计协议：(1) 行为审计——对8个前沿LLM进行比特币排名测试，包括不同框架（可靠货币、危机、自主代理）下的排名和属性交换实验；(2) 内部表征审计——在Gemma 3的数千个稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征中搜索比特币选择性特征，并通过放大/抑制进行因果验证；(3) 下游影响审计——测试特征扰动对投资组合分配的影响，并验证有界行为杠杆。

Result: 比特币在"可靠货币"框架下排名约5/8，但在危机和自主代理框架下排名靠前；属性交换实验确认排名追踪功能属性而非名称。在Gemma 3中定位到一个主导的比特币选择性SAE特征。放大该特征使比特币投资组合份额增加5.2个百分点，抑制则降低4.6个百分点；放大在加密货币内部重新分配，抑制则减少总加密资产敞口。

Conclusion: LLM确实存在资产特定偏好，且可通过内部SAE特征进行因果性操控，但影响有界。该框架将内部表征与外部推荐行为联系起来，为建立"了解你的代理"（KYA）标准提供了第一步，即了解代理的偏好及其可移动范围。

Classification reason: 该论文对金融大语言模型进行行为审计，检测其对特定资产（比特币）的内置偏好，并利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）识别内部表征中的因果特征，属于多模态/大语言模型的安全与可靠性审计范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）中的安全评测、内部表征分析方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on financial LLM bias auditing, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include financial. Signals: Financial LLM bias, Sparse autoencoder feature analysis, Bounded behavioral leverage, Portfolio allocation impact, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language models now power robo-advisors and trading agents, yet whether they carry built-in biases toward specific assets is largely untested. We ask three questions: do LLMs systematically prefer certain financial instruments; can an internal representation with causal leverage over those preferences be identified; and does that representation affect downstream financial decisions? We develop a three-level audit protocol and apply it to Bitcoin. First, a behavioral audit of eight frontier LLMs shows that Bitcoin's ranking among money-like instruments is frame-dependent: models place it around rank 5 of 8 as "reliable money" but near the top under crisis and autonomous-agent frames, and an attribute-swap experiment confirms rankings track functional properties, not names. Second, we open a model's internals: a search across thousands of sparse-autoencoder features in Gemma 3 identifies a dominant Bitcoin-selective feature. Amplifying it shifts the model toward the asset and suppressing it shifts the model away, even when "Bitcoin" never appears in the prompt. Third, we test financial consequences: amplification raises Bitcoin's portfolio share by 5.2 percentage points while suppression lowers it by 4.6 pp, with amplification reallocating within crypto and suppression cutting total crypto exposure. We characterize this as bounded behavioral leverage (leverage meaning causal influence over outputs, not financial leverage): an identifiable internal feature can be perturbed to move financial choices, but only within measurable limits. The framework links internal representations to external recommendations, validated with random controls and mechanism boundaries. As LLMs become autonomous financial agents, this is a first step toward a behavioral layer for emerging know-your-agent (KYA) standards: knowing what an agent prefers, and how far that preference can be moved.

</details>

### [181] [Citation Grounding: Detecting and Reducing LLM Citation Hallucinations via Legal Citation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00898)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.DL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出引用接地（Citation Grounding, CG）指标，基于1.008亿份乌克兰法院判决构建的引用图（5.02亿条边，21,736个法规节点）来检测LLM生成法律引用的幻觉。CG分解为引用精确度、相关性和时效性三个维度。进一步提出CG-DPO方法，通过四种策略破坏真实判决中的已验证引用来构造偏好对，对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行LoRA微调，达到98.5%的验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统性地生成法律引用幻觉——编造法规引用、引用已废止条款、混淆司法管辖区——但目前缺乏自动化方法来大规模测量或减少这一行为。

Method: 1) 构建引用接地（CG）指标，包含精确度（引用是否存在）、相关性（上下文是否合适）、时效性（引用在相关日期是否有效）三个维度。2) 从1.008亿份乌克兰法院判决中提取引用图作为ground-truth。3) 提出CG-DPO方法，通过四种策略（替换、删除、修改、混淆）破坏真实判决中的已验证引用来构造偏好对，使用DPO对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行LoRA微调。

Result: 在100个乌克兰法律查询上评估5个系统（4个商业LLM + 1个RAG系统），CG得分范围为0.791-0.873，13-21%的引用存在幻觉。CG-DPO微调后的模型在区分正确与错误引用上达到98.5%的平均验证准确率，奖励边际+14.9，标准差<0.3 pp（3个种子）。

Conclusion: 引用接地（CG）能有效检测LLM法律引用幻觉，CG-DPO无需人工标注即可显著降低幻觉。引用图、评估框架和CG-DPO数据集已开源。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于LLM在法律场景中的引用幻觉（citation hallucination）检测与缓解，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）范畴。虽然论文主要处理文本法律引用而非视觉模态，但其核心问题——幻觉检测、事实性验证、DPO偏好优化——与多模态幻觉缓解的方法论高度一致，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖事实性与可靠性提升，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on legal citation hallucination detection, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Legal domain, citation grounding metric, DPO for hallucination reduction, large citation graph, low overlap with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language models systematically hallucinate legal citations -- fabricating statute references, citing repealed provisions, and confusing jurisdictions -- yet no automated method exists to measure or reduce this behavior at scale. We propose citation grounding (CG), a metric that verifies LLM-generated legal citations against a ground-truth citation graph extracted from 100.8 million Ukrainian court decisions (502 million edges, 21,736 unique statute nodes). CG decomposes into three components -- citation precision (does the cited provision exist?), citation relevance (is it contextually appropriate?), and citation temporality (was it valid at the relevant date?) -- enabling differential diagnosis of hallucination types. Empirical evaluation on 100 Ukrainian legal queries across five systems -- four commercial LLMs via AWS Bedrock (Claude Haiku 4.5, Mistral Pixtral Large, Amazon Nova Pro/Lite) and one RAG-augmented production system -- reveals CG ranging from 0.791 to 0.873, with 13-21% of citations hallucinated. To reduce hallucinations without human annotation, we introduce Citation Grounding DPO (CG-DPO): a method that constructs preference pairs algorithmically by corrupting verified citations from real court decisions via four targeted strategies. On a dataset of 2,244 court decisions, a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model fine-tuned with LoRA achieves 98.5% mean validation accuracy in distinguishing correct from corrupted citations (rewards margin +14.9, std < 0.3 pp across 3 seeds). The citation graph, evaluation framework, and CG-DPO dataset are released as open resources.

</details>


### [182] [TIGER: Traceable Inference with Graph-Based Evidence Routing for Mitigating Hallucinations in Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00232)
*Kaixiang Zhao, Tianrun Yu, Shawn Huang, Porter Jenkins, Yushun Dong, Amanda Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TIGER 是一个推理时框架，通过从输入和输出中分别提取观测图与声明图，计算图条件风险分数，对高风险声明进行局部修复，从而缓解多模态生成中的事实级幻觉。在图像→文本、图像+文本→文本、音频→文本、视频→文本四种跨模态路径上均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时修复方法存在两个问题：(1) 输出中的幻觉声明会偏置模型对输入的解读；(2) 自由形式的反馈无法在事实级别进行排序或调度。因此需要一种能够独立提取事实证据、对声明进行风险评分并局部修复的框架。

Method: TIGER 从输入中提取观测图（observation graph），从当前输出中提取声明图（claim graph），然后为每个声明分配基于图条件的风险分数（支持与冲突）。模型在保持主干冻结的情况下，仅修复高风险的声明。论文还提供了收敛性分析，证明期望总风险在温和假设下几何递减到显式渐近界。

Result: 在图像→文本、图像+文本→文本、音频→文本、视频→文本四种跨模态路径上，TIGER 减少了不支持的生成内容，同时保持了任务质量。在多个主干模型上均有效，CrisisFACTS 案例研究表明该修复机制可改善多源场景中的接地性。

Conclusion: TIGER 通过图基证据路由实现了事实级别的幻觉修复，是一种不依赖模型微调、可跨模态泛化的推理时框架，在多种多模态生成任务中均能有效提升事实性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态生成中的幻觉缓解（fact-level repair），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题。论文提出推理时图基证据路由框架来减少生成中的不实内容，核心是幻觉缓解与事实性提升。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in multimodal generation, but the graph-based evidence routing approach is not aligned with the user's priority on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal hallucination mitigation, inference-time repair, graph-based evidence routing, cross-modal fact-level repair, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: We study fact-level repair for multimodal generation, where a fluent output may contain specific facts that are not supported by the input. Existing inference-time repair methods often generate feedback by jointly conditioning on the input and the current output. This design has two limitations: hallucinated claims in the output can bias the model's interpretation of the input, and free-form feedback cannot be ranked or scheduled at the fact level. We present TIGER, an inference-time framework that redesigns feedback for localized repair. TIGER independently extracts an observation graph from the input and a claim graph from the current output, then assigns each claim a graph-conditioned risk score based on support and conflict. The model repairs selected high-risk claims while keeping the backbone frozen. We provide a convergence analysis showing that the expected total risk decreases geometrically to an explicit asymptotic bound under mild assumptions. Experiments across four cross-modal paths, including image-to-text, image+text-to-text, audio-to-text, and video-to-text, show that TIGER reduces unsupported content while preserving task quality. The gains hold across multiple backbones, and a CrisisFACTS case study suggests that the same repair mechanism can improve grounding in multi-source settings.

</details>


### [183] [FLaG: Fine-Grained Latent Grouping for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00301)
*Wentao Ye, Liyao Li, Zhiqing Xiao, Muzhi Zhu, Jiaqi Hu, Zhanming Shen, Xiaomeng Hu, Sean Du, Haobo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FLaG 提出了一种轻量级的幻觉检测框架，通过将幻觉检测建模为机制感知的证据聚合问题，利用一组潜在证据组和基于能量的路由机制来捕捉 LLM 中异质的幻觉模式，无需修改底层模型即可实现 SOTA 检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 中的幻觉源于多种异质的失败机制（如知识缺失、注意力分散、解码错误等），单一的全局不确定性分数难以可靠检测所有类型的幻觉。现有方法缺乏对异质错误机制的建模能力。

Method: FLaG 将幻觉检测建模为机制感知的证据聚合问题。它维护一组潜在证据组，每个实例通过基于能量的路由机制软关联到多个组，然后通过对数边缘聚合（log-marginal aggregation）组合组条件可靠性信号。该框架作为冻结模型的头部运行，无需修改底层语言模型，计算开销极小。理论上证明了贝叶斯最优检验统计量必然具有对数边缘形式，FLaG 是其可计算近似。

Result: 在多个基准和 LLM 骨干网络上，FLaG 持续达到 SOTA 性能，展现出跨数据集和跨模型的鲁棒迁移能力，且在有限监督下仍然有效。

Conclusion: FLaG 通过细粒度潜在分组有效捕捉了 LLM 中异质的幻觉模式，为幻觉检测提供了一种轻量、高效且理论完备的解决方案，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM 幻觉检测问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）子主题。虽然论文主要针对文本 LLM，但其提出的细粒度潜在分组幻觉检测框架 FLaG 可直接迁移至多模态 LLM 的幻觉检测场景，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解与可靠性提升，与论文核心问题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on hallucination detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: hallucination detection, latent evidence groups, energy-based routing, frozen-model head, SOTA detection

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) arise from heterogeneous failure mechanisms, making reliable detection difficult for any single global uncertainty score. In this work, we formulate hallucination detection as a mechanism-aware evidence aggregation problem, where diverse representation- and token-level signals must be interpreted under multiple latent explanations. We propose FLaG, a lightweight hallucination detection framework that models correctness through a set of latent evidence groups. Each instance is softly associated with multiple groups via an energy-based routing mechanism, and group-conditional reliability signals are combined through a principled log-marginal aggregation. This design enables FLaG to capture heterogeneous hallucination patterns while remaining invariant to decision thresholds and evaluation metrics. The framework operates as a frozen-model head, requires no modification to the underlying language model, and incurs minimal computational overhead. We further provide a theoretical perspective that connects FLaG to optimal evidence aggregation under heterogeneous error mechanisms, showing that the Bayes-optimal test statistic necessarily admits a log-marginal form and that FLaG constitutes a tractable approximation with a controllable error bound. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and LLM backbones demonstrate that FLaG consistently achieves SOTA performance, while exhibiting robust transfer across datasets and models, and remaining effective under limited supervision.

</details>


### [184] [Detect Before You Leap: Mirage Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00435)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury, Md. Shaown Miah*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Text-Conditioned Layer-wise Internal Alignment (TC-LIA)方法，通过探测CLIP ViT视觉编码器各层patch token与问题文本的对齐轨迹，结合像素统计检测、零样本域路由和VLM自评估，构建集成系统来检测VLM是否应在缺乏视觉证据时回答（即mirage检测）。在5个VQA域、3种输入条件和12个VLM骨干上达到约94.6-94.7%的三类检测准确率，mirage率低于3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在视觉证据缺失、空白或不相关时仍会给出看似自信的回答，这种"mirage"现象在医疗和文档VQA等高风险场景中尤为危险，可能误导用户认为回答基于图像证据。现有研究缺乏在模型生成回答前检测mirage的通用方法。

Method: 提出TC-LIA方法：将CLIP ViT-H/14各层图像patch token投影到最终CLIP嵌入空间，计算与问题嵌入的相似度，追踪视觉证据在各层是否出现。提取最终图像-文本余弦相似度、后层top-k patch-文本对齐、早层到后层增益和层间斜率等特征，结合像素统计空白/噪声检测、零样本域路由和结构化VLM自评估，构建集成分类器进行三类检测（正常/空白/不相关）。

Result: 在5个VQA域（含医疗和文档）、3种输入条件（正常/空白/不相关）和12种VLM骨干上，最佳系统达到约94.6-94.7%的三类检测准确率，mirage率低于3%，而基线mirage率在21.7%至66.6%之间。

Conclusion: TC-LIA是一种有效的模型无关mirage预检测方法，能够在VLM生成回答前判断是否应弃权，显著降低mirage率。该方法不依赖特定VLM架构，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在缺乏视觉证据时产生看似合理但无根据回答的"幻影"(mirage)问题，并提出检测方法来判断模型应回答还是弃权。这属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM reliability and hallucination detection, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: VLM reliability, mirage detection, CLIP internal alignment, model-agnostic, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can produce confident visual answers even when the required visual evidence is missing, blank, or unrelated to the question. This failure mode, known as mirage (Asadi et al. 2026), is especially concerning in medical and document visual question answering, where plausible but visually ungrounded responses may be mistaken for image-based evidence. We study pre-release mirage detection: given an image-question pair, the goal is to determine whether a VLM should answer or abstain before producing a response. We propose Text-Conditioned Layer-wise Internal Alignment (TC-LIA), a model-agnostic method that probes patch-token representations across the layers of a CLIP ViT-H/14 vision encoder. TC-LIA projects layer-wise image patch tokens into the final CLIP embedding space and measures their similarity to the question embedding, allowing the method to track whether question-relevant visual evidence emerges across vision layers. The resulting alignment trajectory is summarized using final image-text cosine similarity, late-layer top-k patch-text alignment, early-to-late gain, and layer-wise slope. These features are combined with pixel-statistic blank/noise detection, zero-shot domain routing, and structured VLM self-assessment in an ensemble. Across five VQA domains, three input conditions, and twelve VLM backbones, the best systems achieve approximately 94.6-94.7% three-class detection accuracy with mirage rates below 3%, while baseline mirage rates range from 21.7% to 66.6%.

</details>


### [185] [Med-HEAL: Analyzing and Mitigating Hallucinations in Medical LLMs with Hallucination-Aware In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01301)
*Yiming Liao, Zeno Franco, Jose Eduardo Lizarraga Mazaba, Keke Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (71.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Med-HEAL提出了一个系统框架，用于识别、分析和缓解医疗大语言模型中的幻觉问题。基于EHRNoteQA基准和MIMIC-IV数据构建幻觉数据集，通过LLM-as-a-Judge与人工审核双重标注，并探索了自批评流水线和检索增强上下文学习两种缓解策略。实验表明自批评策略在5个开源模型中的3个上显著提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗LLM中的幻觉对临床决策支持构成严重风险，但现有基准缺乏真实的临床上下文，且对幻觉缓解的实践指导有限。需要系统性的框架来识别和分析医疗场景下的幻觉，并探索有效的缓解策略。

Method: 基于EHRNoteQA基准（源自MIMIC-IV出院小结），在BioMistral-7B上构建幻觉数据集。采用LLM-as-a-Judge（GPT-4o）与医学生人工审核的双重标注流程，对模型输出进行正确性判断和推理错误标注。提出两种缓解策略：(1) 自批评流水线——模型自我审查答案并重新生成；(2) 检索增强上下文学习（RA-ICL）——向模型展示幻觉和修正示例。

Result: 在BioMistral、Llama-3.1、DeepSeek、Qwen2.5和Qwen3五个开源模型上的实验表明，自批评策略在3个模型上显著提升了准确性（p < 0.05），且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: Med-HEAL提供了可复用的医疗LLM幻觉数据集和实用的幻觉缓解框架，支持AI系统在临床环境中的安全部署。自批评策略是一种无需参数更新的有效缓解手段。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医疗LLM中的幻觉分析与缓解，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）子主题。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本医疗LLM而非视觉多模态，但幻觉缓解的核心问题与该子主题高度一致，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解的通用方法（如自批评、检索增强上下文学习），因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical hallucination mitigation, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword domain. Signals: medical LLM hallucination, self-critique pipeline, retrieval-augmented in-context learning, clinical EHR reasoning, no parameter update mitigation

Abstract: Hallucinations in medical large language models (LLMs) pose serious risks for clinical decision support, particularly when models must reason over complex electronic health records (EHRs). However, existing benchmarks often lack a realistic clinical context and provide limited insight into how hallucinations can be mitigated in practice. We introduce Med-HEAL, a framework for systematically identifying, analyzing, and mitigating hallucinations in medical LLMs using clinically grounded data. Building on the EHRNoteQA benchmark derived from MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, we construct a hallucination dataset by evaluating BioMistral-7B on open-ended clinical question answering tasks. Model outputs are labeled through a dual evaluation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge assessment (GPT-4o) with human auditing by medical student reviewers, producing correctness judgments and annotations of reasoning errors via a custom web-based evaluation system. We then leverage this dataset to investigate mitigation strategies: a self-critique pipeline, in which the test model reviews its own answers to detect potential errors and regenerates responses for flagged cases, and retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RA-ICL), which exposes the model to hallucinated and corrected examples. Experiments across five open-source LLMs-BioMistral, Llama-3.1, DeepSeek, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3, show that the self-critique strategy improves accuracy for three of five models (p < 0.05) without requiring parameter updates. Med-HEAL provides both a reusable hallucination dataset and a practical framework for studying and mitigating hallucinations in medical LLMs, supporting safer deployment of AI systems in clinical environments. Our code and data are publicly available at .

</details>


### [186] [MM-Snowball: Evaluating and Mitigating Hallucination Snowballing in Multimodal Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00622)
*Yue Jiang, Xue Jiang, Lihua Zhang, Zhiqiang Wang, Yuhang Lu, Peng Wang, Bo Han, Feng Zheng, Dingkang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (79.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-Snowball，首个针对多模态多轮对话中幻觉雪崩现象（hallucination snowballing）的细粒度诊断基准，并提出了训练无关的冲突感知视觉修正方法（CAVR），通过表示级视觉刷新和logit级输出分布修正双重机制有效缓解幻觉累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在交互式多轮对话中会出现幻觉雪崩现象——初始错误在对话轮次间不断放大，导致模型逐渐忽视视觉信息而过度依赖被污染的历史文本。现有基准局限于单轮VQA，无法捕捉长程交互中的错误传播动态。

Method: 提出 Conflict-Aware Visual Rectification (CAVR)，一种训练无关的方法，包含双重协同机制：(1) 在表示层面刷新视觉grounding，重新锚定模型到视觉事实；(2) 在logit层面修正输出分布，纠正被污染文本历史导致的偏差。

Result: MM-Snowball基准对先进MLLMs构成显著挑战，现有单轮VQA缓解方法在多轮场景下效果不佳。CAVR方法取得了最先进的性能，有效缓解了多轮对话中的幻觉雪崩。

Conclusion: 幻觉雪崩是多模态多轮对话中一个关键但被忽视的问题，CAVR提供了一种有效的训练无关缓解方案，为构建更可靠的交互式AI系统提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型在多轮对话中的幻觉雪崩现象（hallucination snowballing），并提出缓解方法。这属于多模态幻觉与可靠性问题，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a critical reliability issue in multimodal dialogue but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal hallucination, Multi-turn dialogue, Training-free mitigation, Visual grounding, Error propagation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) demonstrate remarkable visual understanding, yet their reliability in interactive settings is severely undermined by hallucination snowballing: a phenomenon where initial errors amplify across conversational turns, leading to a collapse in coherence. This failure reveals a fundamental vulnerability where models progressively neglect visual grounding in favor of over-relying on polluted textual history. Existing benchmarks are predominantly confined to single-turn VQA, which fail to capture the complex dynamics of error propagation in long-horizon interactions. To address this, we introduce MM-Snowball, the first benchmark for fine-grained diagnosis of hallucination snowballing within dialogues. Extensive evaluation shows that our benchmark poses a significant challenge even to advanced MLLMs and reveals the inefficacy of existing mitigation methods designed for single-turn VQA. To counteract this degradation, we propose Conflict-Aware Visual Rectification (CAVR). This training-free method mitigates snowballing through a synergistic dual-mechanism that refreshes visual grounding at the representation level and rectifies output distributions at the logit level, effectively re-anchoring the model to visual facts. Experiments demonstrate that CAVR achieves state-of-the-art performance, offering a promising path toward more reliable interactive AI. Data and code are available at:

</details>


### [187] [Ask4VG: Risk-Aware Question Selection for Reducing Prior-Driven Answers in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01044)
*Xiaorong Zhu, Qiang Li, Zibo Xu, Weijie Wang, Weizhi Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ask4VG，一种无标签的风险感知问题选择框架，通过反事实视觉探测（原始图像、扰动图像、空白图像、不匹配图像）估计问题诱导的幻觉风险，并利用学习到的风险估计器对候选问题改写进行重排序，选择对视觉证据缺失/不匹配更敏感的问题，从而减少医学 VQA 中的先验驱动回答。在 VQA-RAD 上，该方法将反事实风险从 0.658 降至 0.623，精确准确率从 0.337 提升至 0.356。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学 VQA 中许多问题是模板化或高度相似的，这容易让模型学习到"问题-答案捷径"而非基于图像证据的推理，导致幻觉回答。现有方法主要关注回答层面的幻觉缓解，而忽略了问题本身可能诱导幻觉的风险。

Method: 提出 Ask4VG 框架，包含：(1) 反事实视觉探测——对同一问题在原始图像、扰动图像、空白图像、不匹配图像下提问，利用答案关系构建弱监督信号；(2) 反事实风险估计器——基于弱监督信号训练，估计问题诱导幻觉的风险；(3) 问题改写重排序——对候选问题改写按预测风险排序，选择对视觉证据缺失最敏感的问题用于最终回答生成。

Result: 在 VQA-RAD 上使用 Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct，仅提示改写使反事实风险上升，而预测风险重排序将风险从 0.658 降至 0.623，精确准确率从 0.337 提升至 0.356。在 PMC-VQA 的 300 样本外部验证中，风险降低方向一致且准确率略有提升。

Conclusion: 问题选择是回答层面幻觉缓解的有力补充，通过选择对视觉证据更敏感的问题可以有效降低医学 VQA 中的幻觉风险，提升回答可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学 VQA 中的幻觉缓解问题，通过反事实视觉探测估计问题诱导的幻觉风险并重新排序问题，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VQA hallucination, which is outside user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical VQA, hallucination mitigation, counterfactual probing, question selection, risk-aware reranking

Abstract: Medical visual question answering requires models to ground their responses in image evidence, because visually unsupported answers can mislead downstream interpretation. However, many medical VQA questions are generic, template-like, or highly similar in form, which can encourage models to learn question-answer shortcuts instead of image-dependent reasoning and thereby increase the risk of hallucinated responses. We propose Ask4VG, a label-free pilot framework for risk-aware question selection. Ask4VG estimates question-induced hallucination risk through counterfactual visual probing: the same question is asked under the original image, a perturbed image, a blank image, and a mismatched image, and the resulting answer relations are converted into weak supervision for a counterfactual risk estimator. The learned estimator then reranks candidate question rewrites to favor intent-preserving questions that are less invariant to missing or mismatched visual evidence before final answer generation. On VQA-RAD with Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct, prompt-only rewriting increases counterfactual risk, whereas predicted-risk reranking reduces held-out risk from 0.658 to 0.623 and improves exact accuracy from 0.337 to 0.356. A 300-sample PMC-VQA external check shows the same direction of risk reduction with a small accuracy gain. These results suggest that question selection is a promising complement to response-level hallucination mitigation for reliable medical VQA.

</details>


### [188] [Self-Improving Small Object Grounding in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01612)
*Tianze Yang, Yucheng Shi, Ruitong Sun, Ninghao Liu, Jin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (85.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现LVLM的内部注意力结构编码了定位质量信息，通过训练轻量级IoU回归器（仅基于注意力图）可实现强IoU预测（Pearson r>0.67）。基于此提出ACS框架（含学习版ACS-Learned和无训练版ACS-Free），通过从多个候选框中选取最优框来改进小目标定位，在COCO和Objects365上实现高达19%的自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在细粒度视觉任务（如小目标定位）上存在可靠性不足的问题，而现有方法通常需要额外训练或外部模块。本文旨在探索能否仅利用LVLM内部的注意力结构来提升小目标定位的可靠性，无需微调模型。

Method: 1) 训练一个轻量级IoU回归器，仅以LVLM的注意力图为输入，预测预测框与真实框的IoU。2) 提出ACS-Learned框架：对同一图像多次采样生成多个候选框，用IoU回归器评分并选取最优框。3) 分析回归器学到的知识，识别出对定位最关键的Transformer层和注意力头。4) 基于此提出ACS-Free：无需任何学习组件，仅根据这些关键头的注意力熵对候选框排序，选择熵最低的框。

Result: 在COCO和Objects365数据集上，ACS方法在小目标定位上实现了高达19%的自改进（self-improvement）。ACS-Free在所有无训练方法中排名最佳，且与有训练方法性能相当。

Conclusion: LVLM的注意力结构本身编码了定位质量信息，利用这一结构可以显著提升小目标定位的可靠性和可解释性，无需微调模型。这为提升LVLM在细粒度视觉任务上的可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注LVLM中小目标定位的可靠性提升，通过注意力结构分析实现自改进定位，属于多模态模型可靠性/忠实性提升范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Improves small object grounding in LVLMs via attention-based self-improvement, but focuses on reliability rather than the user's priority of continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: LVLM attention encodes grounding quality, IoU regressor from attention maps, up to 19% improvement on small objects, training-free ACS-Free variant, strong on COCO and Objects365

Abstract: Can internal attention patterns in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) identify reliable small-object boxes without fine-tuning? In this work, we provide an affirmative answer. Attention structure in LVLMs encodes grounding quality-a lightweight IoU regressor trained solely on attention maps achieves strong IoU prediction (Pearson r > 0.67). This regressor powers the regressor-based variant of our Attention-based Candidate Selection (ACS) framework, called ACS-Learned, which selects the best box from multiple sampled candidates to improve object grounding. By analyzing what the regressor learns, we reveal which transformer layers and heads are most critical and derive ACS-Free: a training-free selector that ranks candidates by attention entropy on these discriminative heads, with no learned component at inference. Experiments on COCO and Objects365 demonstrate up to 19% self-improvement on small object localization, with ACS-Free ranking best among all training-free methods, demonstrating that useful attention structure improves both localization reliability and interpretability in LVLMs.

</details>


### [189] [Density-Aware Translation of Spurious Correlations in Zero-Shot VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01710)
*Afsaneh Hasanebrahimi, Hanxun Huang, Christopher Leckie, Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出密度感知翻译（DAT）方法，利用CLIP嵌入空间的几何特性（各向异性壳结构），通过局部密度项重新校准图像-文本相似度分数，抑制虚假相关性导致的过度自信预测，提升零样本分类的鲁棒性和最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM（如CLIP）在零样本分类中易受虚假相关性影响，即上下文线索主导预测而忽略语义内容。现有方法依赖微调或提示工程，前者破坏预训练优势，后者易产生幻觉。作者观察到CLIP嵌入存在模态间隙且呈各向异性壳分布：常见模式聚集在均值附近，稀有模式被推向外部，导致对齐不均匀，虚假相关性被放大而稀有语义被边缘化。

Method: 提出Density-Aware Translation (DAT)，基于局部几何密度项重新校准图像-文本相似度分数。具体地，利用组参考集计算嵌入密度，采用相对度量（relative measure）根据密度重新缩放相似度：抑制稀疏区域（diffuse regions）的过度自信分数，同时保留密集区域中语义一致的匹配。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，DAT在保持平均准确率的同时，显著提升了最差组准确率（worst-group accuracy），验证了密度感知翻译作为一种简单有效的校准机制在零样本分类中的可靠性。

Conclusion: DAT通过利用CLIP嵌入空间的几何特性进行密度感知校准，有效缓解了零样本VLM中的虚假相关性问题，是一种无需微调或提示工程的轻量级校准方法，为多模态模型的可靠分类提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本VLM分类中虚假相关性（spurious correlations）导致的预测偏差问题，提出密度感知翻译（DAT）方法校准图像-文本相似度分数，属于多模态模型可靠性/忠实性提升范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM robustness via geometric calibration, but is not aligned with user's priority on generative models and continuous representations. Signals: zero-shot classification, spurious correlation, CLIP embedding geometry, density calibration, worst-group accuracy

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, achieve powerful zero-shot classification. However, their predictions remain sensitive to spurious correlations, where contextual cues dominate over semantic content. Earlier solutions typically rely on fine-tuning or prompt engineering, which either undermine the advantages of pre-trained models or are prone to hallucination. In this work, we propose Density-Aware Translation (DAT) that refines image-text similarity scores using a local geometric density term derived from group reference sets. Our approach is motivated by the phenomenon that CLIP embeddings exhibit a modality gap and lie on an anisotropic shell in the feature space: common patterns cluster near the mean, while rare patterns are pushed outward. This geometry creates uneven alignment, where spurious correlations are amplified while semantically meaningful but rare cues are marginalised. To address this, we employ a relative measure to rescale similarities based on embedding density, suppressing overconfident scores in diffuse regions while preserving dense, semantically consistent matches. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in worst-group and average accuracy, highlighting density-aware translation as a simple and effective calibration mechanism for reliable zero-shot classification using multimodal models.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_rl_alignment'></div>

## 多模态强化学习与对齐 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 RL、RLHF、DPO、偏好学习、多模态对齐。

### [190] [Skill or Skip? Learning Selective Skill Invocation in Agentic Tasks via Dual-Granularity Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00510)
*Chishui Chen, Jiaye Lin, Te Sun, Junxi Wang, Yi Yang, Cong Qin, Yangen Hu, Lu Pan, Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SelSkill，一种双粒度偏好学习框架，用于智能体任务中选择性技能调用。通过预测不确定性优先筛选决策点，构建受控的 invoke-skip 偏好对，结合回合级结果偏好与步骤级调用偏好进行学习。在 ALFWorld 和 BFCL 上分别提升任务成功率 10.9 和 5.7 个百分点，执行精度提升约 29 个百分点，并展现出跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能使用方法主要关注选择相关技能或改进技能本身，但忽略了在某个决策点上即使技能相关也不一定应该被调用。不恰当的技能调用会引入无关上下文，干扰原本正确的执行流程。因此需要一种选择性技能调用机制。

Method: 提出 SelSkill 框架，将技能使用建模为 skill-or-skip 二选一决策。首先利用预测不确定性（predictive uncertainty）对候选决策点进行优先级排序；然后从共享轨迹前缀中构建受控的 invoke-skip 偏好对；最后结合回合级（episode-level）结果偏好与步骤级（step-level）调用偏好进行双粒度偏好学习。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上使用 Qwen3-8B，任务成功率提升 10.9 个百分点，执行精度提升 29.1 个百分点。在 BFCL 上任务成功率提升 5.7 个百分点，执行精度提升 29.5 个百分点。在 Tau-bench 和 PopQA 上的零样本结果进一步表明学到的调用策略可迁移到包含未见技能的新领域。

Conclusion: 选择性技能调用是提升智能体任务执行质量的关键。SelSkill 通过双粒度偏好学习有效区分何时调用技能、何时跳过，显著提升了任务成功率和执行精度，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SelSkill，一种双粒度偏好学习框架，用于智能体任务中选择性技能调用。核心方法是通过偏好学习（preference learning）优化技能调用的决策，属于多模态/智能体场景下的偏好对齐与强化学习范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on agentic skill invocation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: agentic tasks, skill invocation, preference learning, ALFWorld, BFCL

Abstract: Agent skills are callable procedural modules that provide reusable knowledge and execution policies for complex agentic tasks. However, existing methods mainly focus on selecting relevant skills or improving the skills themselves, while overlooking whether a relevant skill should actually be invoked at the current decision point. Unhelpful invocations may introduce irrelevant context and disrupt an otherwise correct execution process. To address this issue, we propose SelSkill, a dual-granularity preference-learning framework for selective skill invocation. SelSkill formulates skill use as a skill-or-skip decision, uses predictive uncertainty to prioritize candidate decision points, and constructs controlled invoke-skip preference pairs from shared trajectory prefixes. It further combines episode-level outcome preferences with step-level invocation preferences to capture both overall trajectory quality and the local effectiveness of skill invocation. On ALFWorld with Qwen3-8B, SelSkill improves task success by 10.9 percentage points and execution precision by 29.1 percentage points. On BFCL, it improves task success by 5.7 percentage points and execution precision by 29.5 percentage points. Zero-shot results on Tau-bench and PopQA further suggest that the learned invocation policy transfers to new domains with previously unseen skills.

</details>


### [191] [From Demonstrations to Rewards: Test-Time Prompt Optimization for VLM Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00083)
*Christian Gumbsch, Leonardo Barcellona, Lennard Schünemann, Platon Karageorgis, Andrii Zadaianchuk, Zehao Wang, Sergey Zakharov, Fabien Despinoy, Rahaf Aljundi, Efstratios Gavves*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Demo2Reward 提出一种测试时提示优化方法，利用少量专家演示（3-10条轨迹）自动优化 VLM 奖励模型的语言指令，在不额外训练模型的情况下减少假阳性奖励，提升下游机器人策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖准确的奖励函数，但在机器人等实际应用中，奖励函数往往需要手工设计或难以获取。现有 VLM 作为零样本奖励模型存在假阳性预测问题，会严重损害下游策略学习。而机器人领域通常有少量专家演示数据可用，这为在策略训练前优化奖励模型提供了机会。

Method: Demo2Reward 是一种测试时适应技术，基于少量专家演示轨迹（3-10条），通过优化 VLM 奖励模型的语言指令（prompt）来减少假阳性奖励，同时保持真阳性。该方法不需要额外的模型训练或在策略学习期间增加计算资源。

Result: 在多个模拟机器人任务和不同策略骨干网络上，Demo2Reward 一致优于现有的零样本和少样本 VLM 奖励模型。此外，该方法成功迁移到真实机器人学习场景，无需手工设计奖励函数即可实现策略学习。

Conclusion: 利用少量演示数据在测试时优化 VLM 奖励模型的提示，是一种有效且实用的方法，能够显著提升奖励质量并促进下游策略学习，为机器人领域提供了一种无需手工设计奖励函数的可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 VLM 作为奖励模型，通过测试时提示优化来改进奖励信号，用于机器人强化学习。这属于多模态 RL 与对齐方向（multimodal_rl_alignment），同时与具身世界模型（embodied_world_model）相关，因为涉及机器人策略学习与演示数据。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM reward optimization for robotics, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: VLM reward models, test-time prompt optimization, robotics, few-shot demonstrations, false positive reduction

Abstract: Reinforcement learning relies on accurate reward functions, which are often hand-crafted or even unavailable in real-world applications, such as robotics. Recent work has explored the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models. However, without careful prompt engineering, these approaches tend to produce suboptimal rewards, where false positive predictions can severely degrade downstream policy learning. In robotics, limited datasets comprising expert demonstrations are often collected to bootstrap policy learning. This scenario provides an opportunity to optimize a reward model prior policy training. We propose Demo2Reward a test-time adaptation technique to optimize the language instruction of a reward model based on a few demonstrations (3-10 trajectories) to reduce false positives while preserving true positives. Crucially, this requires no additional model training or computation resources during policy learning. We show that Demo2Reward consistently outperforms existing zero- and few-shot VLM reward models across a range of simulated robotic tasks and policy backbones. Finally, we demonstrate that Demo2Reward effectively transfers to a real-world robotic learning scenario, enabling policy learning without manually engineering a reward function.

</details>


### [192] [Internalize the Temperature: On-Policy Self-Distillation as Policy Reheater for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00755)
*Xuewei Yang, Jiachen Yu, Jie Wu, Shaoning Sun, Junjie Wang, Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-OPSD（温度缩放在线自蒸馏），一种轻量级的策略"回热"方法，通过在熵坍塌的RL检查点上对模型自身logits施加高温缩放构造自教师，再将平滑后的分布蒸馏回学生模型，从而将温度的探索效应内化到模型参数中。实验表明该方法在Qwen3-4B/8B上比标准继续RL和rollout级温度回热产生更强的初始化效果，且主要降低输出尖锐度而保持中间表征和推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能提升LLM的推理能力，但常导致熵坍塌——策略越来越集中，降低了rollout多样性和有效学习信号。现有方法（如熵正则化、调整采样温度）都是模型外部的干预，无法内化到模型参数中。作者希望将温度的探索效应内化到模型参数中，作为一种轻量级的后坍塌干预手段。

Method: TS-OPSD方法：从熵坍塌的RL检查点出发，对模型自身的logits施加高温缩放（temperature scaling）构造一个"自教师"，然后将教师平滑后的分布通过KL散度蒸馏回学生模型。该过程不需要外部教师、特权数据或额外推理成本，是一种on-policy自蒸馏方法。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上的实验表明：TS-OPSD策略回热产生的初始化比标准继续RL和rollout级温度回热更强。进一步分析显示，TS-OPSD主要降低输出尖锐度（output sharpness），同时保持中间表征、top候选集和推理能力。

Conclusion: 熵恢复可以作为面向推理的RL训练中一种简单有效的后坍塌干预手段。TS-OPSD通过将温度探索效应内化到模型参数中，在不增加推理成本的前提下有效缓解熵坍塌，为继续RL提供了更强的初始化。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提升LLM推理能力，并提出TS-OPSD方法解决熵坍塌问题。虽然论文主要聚焦于语言模型推理的RL训练，但其方法（on-policy self-distillation、temperature scaling）与多模态RL对齐方向（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的RL训练、偏好优化、对齐等技术高度相关，且该方向涵盖多模态RLHF/DPO等广义RL训练方法。论文的RL训练与熵恢复技术可迁移至多模态场景，因此归入该现有子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL entropy collapse for LLM reasoning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: RL for reasoning, entropy collapse, on-policy self-distillation, temperature scaling, Qwen3 experiments

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but often suffers from entropy collapse, in which increasingly concentrated policies reduce rollout diversity and useful learning signals. Existing remedies either constrain the RL objective (e.g., entropy regularization) or adjust sampling temperature during rollout collection, but these interventions remain external to the model parameters. We propose Temperature-Scaled On-Policy Self-Distillation (TS-OPSD), a lightweight policy reheating method that internalizes the exploratory effect of temperature into model parameters. Starting from an entropy-collapsed RL checkpoint, TS-OPSD constructs a self-teacher by applying high-temperature scaling to the model's own logits, then distills the resulting smoother distribution back into the student. This policy reheating requires no external teacher, privileged data, or additional inference cost. Experiments on Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base show that policy reheating yields a stronger initialization for continued RL than both standard continued RL and rollout-level temperature reheating. Further analyses show that TS-OPSD mainly reduces output sharpness while preserving intermediate representations, top candidate sets, and reasoning capability. These results suggest that entropy restoration can serve as a simple post-collapse intervention for extending reasoning-oriented RL.

</details>


### [193] [CAST: Non-Privileged Clipped Asymmetric Self-Teaching with Advantage Flipping for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00172)
*Yang Li, Gongle Xue, Yijia Guo, Yuheng Yuan, Liwen Hu, Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CAST 提出一种无答案自蒸馏方法，通过 stop-gradient 自教师模型和双向局部优势符号翻转，为 GRPO 风格的可验证奖励强化学习提供密集的 token 级监督信号，解决了组相对优势在全部正确/全部错误时消失的问题，在数学推理任务上提升了 RLVR 训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 等 RLVR 方法中，outcome-level 奖励仅提供稀疏监督，且当所有采样轨迹全部正确或全部错误时，组相对优势会消失（零梯度），导致无法有效学习。OPSD 虽提供密集 token 级指导，但其 token 偏好与轨迹正确性不一定对齐，且需要特权教师上下文（参考解条件评分）。因此需要一种无需参考解、能根据轨迹正确性提供有效 token 级信号的自蒸馏方法。

Method: CAST 在保持 GRPO 目标的基础上，引入一个 stop-gradient 自教师模型来根据轨迹正确性塑造 token 级优势。核心设计包括：(1) 使用自教师 log-probability gap 作为 token 级信号；(2) 双向局部优势符号翻转：正确轨迹中教师负 token 获得负优势，错误轨迹中教师正 token 获得有界正优势；(3) 对零方差的全部正确/全部错误组，分配有界符号约束的基础优势，使其能贡献验证器签名的 token 反馈。整个训练过程无需参考解条件评分。

Result: 在数学推理任务上的实验表明，CAST 相比基线 GRPO 和 OPSD 方法显著提升了 RLVR 训练效果，特别是在处理全部正确/全部错误的零方差组时表现出优势，同时保持了轻量级、基于验证器的轨迹级目标。

Conclusion: CAST 通过无答案自蒸馏和优势翻转机制，有效解决了 GRPO 中稀疏监督和零方差组梯度消失的问题，为 RLVR 训练提供了一种更高效、更鲁棒的 token 级信号塑造方法，且无需特权教师上下文。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CAST，一种用于 GRPO 风格 RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）的无答案自蒸馏方法，旨在改进大语言模型的推理能力。虽然论文主要聚焦于纯文本数学推理，但其方法（GRPO、RLVR、自蒸馏、token 级优势塑造）与多模态强化学习与对齐方向的核心技术（RLHF、DPO、偏好学习、对齐）高度相关，属于该方向的方法论扩展。因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Focuses on GRPO self-distillation for math reasoning, not on the user's priority continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: GRPO, self-distillation, token-level advantage, math reasoning, RLVR

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), especially Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has been widely used to improve reasoning in large language models. However, outcome-level rewards provide only sparse supervision, and group-relative advantages vanish when all sampled trajectories for a prompt are either correct or incorrect. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) offers dense token-level guidance, but its token preferences are not necessarily aligned with trajectory correctness; empirical diagnostics show that OPSD signals behave differently on correct and incorrect rollouts, with teacher-positive and teacher-negative gap signals exhibiting different noise profiles. These diagnostics are conducted under an OPSD-style privileged teacher context for analysis only, whereas CAST training uses answer-free self-teacher by these observations, this work proposes CAST, an answer-free self-distillation method for GRPO-style RLVR. CAST keeps the verifier-grounded GRPO objective, but uses a stop-gradient self-teacher to shape token-level advantages according to trajectory correctness. Unlike prior self-distilled RLVR methods, CAST does not require reference-solution-conditioned teacher scoring, keeps the self-teacher log-probability gap active throughout training, and applies bidirectional local advantage sign reversal: teacher-negative tokens in correct trajectories can receive negative token-level advantages, while teacher-positive tokens in incorrect trajectories can receive bounded positive local advantages. For zero-variance all-correct and all-wrong groups, CAST assigns bounded sign-constrained base advantages, so these otherwise zero-gradient groups can contribute verifier-signed token feedback. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that CAST improves RLVR training while retaining a lightweight, verifier-grounded trajectory-level objective.

</details>


### [194] [Weak Critics Make Strong Learners: On-Policy Critique Distillation for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00424)
*Can Jin, Jiakang Li, Rui Wu, Eddy Zhang, Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出弱批评者强监督（Weak-Critic Strong Oversight）范式，利用弱模型作为批评者而非标注者来指导强模型。通过渐进式on-policy批评蒸馏（OPCD），筛选高质量批评并蒸馏为强模型的自适应自教师信号，在推理和对齐基准上持续提升强模型性能，为可扩展监督提供有效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能力增强，弱监督者难以提供可靠的标签、偏好或最终判断，限制了弱到强泛化（weak-to-strong generalization）和可扩展监督（scalable oversight）。现有方法要求弱模型作为标注者或裁判，但弱模型在复杂任务上能力不足。本文探索一种更易实现的弱监督形式：让弱模型作为批评者（critic），仅提供非误导性的修订方向，帮助强模型更好地利用自身知识。

Method: 1) 首先验证弱批评可以在推理时提升冻结的强模型性能，并证明批评质量是关键因素。2) 提出渐进式on-policy批评蒸馏（OPCD）：在训练过程中，强模型生成回答，弱批评者提供批评，筛选高质量批评后，通过自适应自教师信号将批评引导的行为蒸馏到强模型中。该方法在训练轮次中持续改进强模型。

Result: 在推理和对齐基准上的实验表明，OPCD方法能够随着训练轮次持续提升强模型性能，验证了弱批评者监督的有效性。弱批评者提供的修订方向帮助强模型更好地利用自身知识，实现了可扩展的弱监督。

Conclusion: 本文证明了弱批评者强监督是一种有效的可扩展监督范式。弱模型作为批评者比作为标注者或裁判更易实现，且能通过OPCD方法持续提升强模型能力。这为弱到强泛化和AI对齐提供了新的可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究弱模型作为批评者（critic）来监督强模型，通过渐进式on-policy批评蒸馏（OPCD）提升强模型在推理和对齐任务上的表现。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好学习范畴，与现有[多模态强化学习与对齐]子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel weak-to-strong oversight method using critique distillation, relevant to scalable alignment for multimodal models. Signals: weak-critic strong oversight, on-policy critique distillation, scalable oversight, weak-to-strong generalization, alignment improvement

Abstract: As large language models become stronger, weak supervisors may fail to provide reliable labels, preferences, or final judgments for complex outputs, limiting both weak-to-strong generalization and scalable oversight. We study a more tractable form of weak supervision: using a weak model as a critic rather than as a labeler or judge. Instead of solving the task or selecting the correct answer, the weak critic only needs to provide a non-misleading revision direction that helps the strong model better use its own knowledge. We call this setting *weak-critic strong oversight*. We first show that weak critiques can improve frozen strong models at inference time, and that critique quality is key to this improvement. We then propose progressive on-policy critique distillation (**OPCD**), which filters high-quality critiques and distills critic-guided behavior into the strong model through adaptive self-teacher signals. Experiments on reasoning and alignment benchmarks show that our method improves strong models over training epochs, suggesting an effective path for scalable oversight with weak supervision.

</details>


### [195] [ARCA: Adapter-Residual Credit Assignment When Token Signals Degenerate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00257)
*Rodney Lafuente-Mercado*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出在 LoRA 参数高效微调下，LLM-RL 中常用的 token 级信用分配信号（如 surprisal、熵减、策略散度）会因归一化而退化（趋于均匀或集中在任务无关位置）。作者提出 ARCA（Adapter-Residual Credit Assignment），利用 adapter 隐藏状态残差作为 token 重要性信号，无需学习奖励模型或价值头。在 MATH/Qwen3-1.7B GRPO 实验中，ARCA 展现出非退化的信用分布，并与基线方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-RL 的 token 级信用分配方法假设策略完全可训练，但实际中常用 LoRA 等参数高效微调。这种分离导致在 LoRA 约束下，策略被限制在参考模型的低秩邻域内，使得基于输出分布差异的信用信号（surprisal、熵减、策略散度）在轨迹内归一化后退化，无法有效分配信用。

Method: 作者首先形式化分析了 LoRA 下信用信号的退化行为，提出用权重基尼系数和有效 token 比率等浓度诊断指标来量化退化程度。然后提出 ARCA 方法，通过计算 adapter 隐藏状态残差 $\|h^{\text{adapted}}_t - h^{\text{base}}_t\|_2$ 来衡量每个 token 的重要性，即关注 adapter 实际改变了模型哪些位置，而非输出分布的不确定性或偏移。ARCA 无需学习奖励模型、价值头或构建搜索树。

Result: 在 MATH/Qwen3-1.7B GRPO 实验中，ARCA 展现出预测的非退化中间区域信用分布，在匹配的 rollout 预算下与 rank 匹配的基线方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: LoRA 微调下的 LLM-RL 存在 token 级信用信号退化的结构性问题，ARCA 通过 adapter 残差提供了一种轻量级、非退化的替代方案，为参数高效 RL 中的信用分配提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM-RL 中 LoRA 微调下的 token 级信用分配问题，提出 ARCA 方法。虽然论文主要讨论语言模型 RL，但其方法（基于 adapter 残差的 token 重要性分配）与多模态 RL/对齐方向中的 RLHF、偏好学习、GRPO 等技术高度相关，且论文实验使用 MATH/Qwen3-1.7B GRPO 框架，属于多模态强化学习与对齐范畴。因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Addresses a niche RL credit assignment issue under LoRA, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: LoRA credit assignment, token signal degeneration, adapter residual, GRPO, LLM-RL efficiency

Abstract: Token-level credit assignment for language-model reinforcement learning is usually formulated as if the policy were fully trainable, while practical LLM-RL pipelines often rely on parameter-efficient fine-tuning, especially LoRA. We argue that this separation hides a structural failure mode. Under LoRA, the policy is restricted to a low-rank neighborhood of the reference model, so the per-token output-distribution differences used by common intrinsic credit signals, surprisal, entropy reduction, and policy divergence, can become degenerate after within-trajectory normalization, either approaching uniform weights or concentrating on a small set of task-agnostic positions. We formalize this behavior and propose measuring it directly with concentration diagnostics such as weight Gini and effective-token ratio. We then introduce \emph{Adapter-Residual Credit Assignment} (ARCA), a lightweight alternative that derives token salience from the adapter's own hidden-state residual, $\|h^{\text{adapted}}_t - h^{\text{base}}_t\|_2$. ARCA asks where the adapter actually changes the model, rather than where the output distribution appears uncertain or shifted, and requires no learned reward model, value head, or tree construction. In a compact MATH/Qwen3-1.7B GRPO sweep, ARCA exhibits the predicted non-degenerate middle-regime credit distribution under matched rollout budgets and remains competitive with rank-matched baselines.

</details>


### [196] [Expected Value Alignment for Generative Reward Modeling in Formal Mathematics Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01160)
*Shihao Ji, Haotao Tan, Zihui Song, Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (50.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Expected Value Alignment (EVA)，一种用于形式化数学验证的生成式奖励建模方法。EVA 在保持离散文本输出的同时，通过计算 token logits 的期望值提取连续评分，解决了生成式奖励模型在连续回归任务中的离散化伪影问题。基于该方法实现的 Leibniz 奖励模型在 Lean 4 形式化验证任务上优于零样本和传统奖励建模基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型设计存在两难：值头模型提供连续分数但修改了生成模型接口，生成式奖励模型保留文本解释但与连续浮点回归不匹配（数值被分割到多个 token 上）。需要一种既能保持离散文本输出又能提取连续评分的方法。

Method: EVA 方法让模型以结构化 JSON 格式输出整数分数，然后通过对相应锚点 token 的 logits 计算期望值来得到连续分数。训练结合因果语言建模目标与这些期望值的辅助均方误差损失。基于此实现了 Leibniz 奖励模型用于 Lean 4 形式化验证。

Result: 在 Lean 4 形式化验证任务上的评估表明，基于 logits 的连续评分显著减少了离散化伪影，同时保留了生成式批评的可解释性，优于零样本和传统奖励建模基线。

Conclusion: EVA 有效地弥合了生成式奖励模型与连续评分之间的鸿沟，为形式化数学验证中的过程奖励建模提供了一种兼具可解释性和评分精度的新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Expected Value Alignment (EVA) 方法，将生成式奖励模型与连续评分对齐，属于多模态/LLM 的奖励建模与对齐方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on formal math verification, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: generative reward modeling, formal verification, Lean 4, expected value alignment, token logit scoring

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used with formal interactive theorem provers such as Lean 4. Scaling these systems with reinforcement learning or search methods requires process reward models (PRMs) that can evaluate intermediate reasoning steps. Existing reward-model designs expose a practical trade-off. Value-head models provide continuous scores but modify the generative model interface, while generative reward models preserve textual rationales but are poorly matched to continuous floating-point regression because numeric values are split across tokens. We introduce Expected Value Alignment (EVA), a reward-modeling procedure that keeps the surface output discrete while extracting continuous scores from the model's token distribution. The model emits integer scores in a structured JSON format, and EVA computes a continuous score as the expectation over the logits of the corresponding anchor tokens. Training combines the causal language modeling objective with an auxiliary mean squared error loss on these expected values. We instantiate EVA in \textit{Leibniz}, a reward model for Lean 4 formal verification, and evaluate it against zero-shot and reward-modeling baselines. The evaluation demonstrates that continuous logit-based scoring significantly reduces discretization artifacts while retaining the interpretability of generative critiques.

</details>
