### [197] [S-SPPO: Semantic-Calibrated Self-Play Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01561)
*Xiwen Chen, Wenhui Zhu, Jingjing Wang, Peijie Qiu, Zhipeng Wang, Huayu Li, ZhengXiao He, Xuanzhao Dong, Prayag Tiwari, Mingkun Xu, Yujian Xiong, Feng Luo, Abolfazl Razi, Brendan Hogan Rappazzo, Anderson Schneider, Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 S-SPPO，一种语义校准的自对弈偏好优化方法，通过语义门控校准胜率目标和潜空间排斥防止表征坍缩，解决了 SPPO 在语义不可区分响应上过度自信导致策略退化的问题，在 AlpacaEval 2.0 上取得 52.19% 胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 DPO 基于 Bradley-Terry 模型，难以建模人类偏好中常见的非传递性。SPPO 通过自对弈生成胜负对来迭代优化策略，但存在关键不稳定性：当偏好裁判对语义上不可区分的响应给出过度自信的胜率时，策略会退化。本文旨在解决这一稳定性问题。

Method: 提出 S-SPPO 双空间语义校准框架：(1) 监督校准（Supervision Calibration）——通过语义门控，根据响应间的语义重叠程度将胜率目标向最大熵基线退火；(2) 表征校准（Representation Calibration）——通过潜空间排斥力（latent repulsion）强制选择样本与拒绝样本在表征空间中的几何多样性，防止流形坍缩。理论上证明该校准保持了常数和博弈结构，促进收敛到纳什均衡。

Result: 在 AlpacaEval 2.0 上，使用 Llama-3-8B 达到 52.19% 胜率和 47.46% 长度控制胜率，无需额外人工标注偏好数据，避免了 SPPO 的性能退化。

Conclusion: S-SPPO 通过语义校准有效缓解了 SPPO 的策略退化问题，在偏好对齐任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能，且理论保证了博弈结构的稳定性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 S-SPPO，一种基于语义校准的自对弈偏好优化方法，用于改进 LLM 与人类偏好对齐。虽然论文主要聚焦于文本 LLM 的对齐，但其方法（DPO/SPPO 改进、偏好优化、语义校准）直接属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题，该子主题涵盖 RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法。论文的语义校准框架（语义门控+潜空间排斥）可推广到多模态场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM alignment, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM alignment, preference optimization, semantic calibration, SPPO improvement, AlpacaEval benchmark

Abstract: Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences is often formulated via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). However, the standard Bradley-Terry instantiation of DPO is limited in modeling common departures from transitivity in human preferences. To address this, recent work has introduced Self-Play Preference Optimization (SPPO), which iteratively refines the policy by training on self-generated win-lose pairs. Our investigation, however, reveals a critical instability in SPPO: the optimization is prone to policy degeneration when the preference oracle assigns overly confident wins to semantically indistinguishable responses. To mitigate this, we propose S-SPPO, a dual-space semantic calibration framework comprising: i) Supervision Calibration via semantic gating, which anneals win rate targets toward the maximum-entropy baseline as semantic overlap increases; and ii) Representation Calibration via latent repulsion to enforce geometric diversity to prevent manifold collapse and maintain latent diversity between chosen and rejected samples. Theoretically, we show that the calibration preserves the constant-sum game structure, facilitating convergence to a Nash Equilibrium. Empirically, S-SPPO avoids the performance degradation seen in prior methods, achieving 52.19% win rate and 47.46% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 with Llama-3-8B, without using additional human-annotated preferences during training. The code will be available at .

</details>


### [198] [TRON: Targeted Rule-Verifiable Online Environments for Visual Reasoning RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01599)
*Tianze Yang, Yucheng Shi, Ruitong Sun, Jingyuan Huang, Ninghao Liu, Jin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (63.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TRON 提出了一种面向视觉推理强化学习的在线环境框架，通过可控生成器-验证器程序按需生成无限训练样本（图像-问题-答案三元组），并支持课程学习难度控制。在 Qwen3-VL-4B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 MiMo-VL-7B-SFT 上的 RL 后训练显著提升了十个外部多模态推理基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 RL 后训练依赖静态 curated 数据集，样本数量受限于收集预算，无法提供可扩展、可验证且可控的训练信号。需要一种能按需生成无限可验证训练样本的在线环境来支持视觉推理强化学习。

Method: TRON 设计了一个可控生成器-验证器程序作为在线环境：每次 rollout 采样新的潜视觉状态、渲染图像、提出问题并精确验证答案。包含 520 个环境，分为空间、数学、图表、模式/逻辑和计数五个能力桶，支持课程学习难度控制。支持单模型全桶训练和按桶的能力专家模型。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-4B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 MiMo-VL-7B-SFT 上使用 TRON 进行 RL 后训练，在十个外部多模态推理基准上持续提升性能。同时提供了生成可靠性、实例与难度多样性、跨环境近重复检测和基模型通过率等分析。

Conclusion: TRON 提供了一个可扩展、可验证、可控的在线环境框架，有效解决了视觉推理 RL 训练数据受限的问题，展示了在线生成训练样本相比静态数据集的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TRON，一个面向视觉推理强化学习的在线环境框架，通过可验证规则生成无限训练样本。核心贡献在于为多模态 RL 提供可扩展、可验证的训练信号，属于多模态强化学习范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Proposes a scalable online RL environment for visual reasoning, directly enabling controllable generation and curriculum learning for multimodal RL post-training. Signals: visual reasoning RL, online environment, controllable generation, curriculum learning, multimodal reasoning benchmark improvement

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for visual reasoning needs scalable, verifiable, and controllable training signals. Existing visual RL post-training trains on static curated datasets, with fixed image-question-answer samples bounded by their collection budget. In this work, we introduce TRON (Targeted, Rule-verifiable Online eNvironments), an online environment substrate: a training rollout is generated on demand by a controllable generator-verifier program that samples a fresh latent visual state, renders an image, asks a question, and exactly verifies the answer. A single run can therefore draw an unbounded stream of fresh instances at the difficulty level required by the current curriculum. The current TRON suite contains 520 environments organized into five ability buckets (spatial, mathematical, diagram, pattern/logic, and counting); the same substrate supports both a single full model trained on all buckets and per-bucket ability-specialist models, with no additional data collection. We also introduce a substrate analysis covering generation reliability, instance and level diversity, cross-environment near-duplicates, and base-model pass rate by difficulty level. RL post-training with METHOD consistently improves performance on ten external multimodal reasoning benchmarks across Qwen3-VL-4B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MiMo-VL-7B-SFT.

</details>


### [199] [TriAlign: Towards Universal Truth Consistency in Personalized LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01755)
*Thi-Nhung Nguyen, Linhao Luo, Rollin Omari, Junae Kim, Thuy-Trang Vu, Dinh Phung*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TriAlign 提出首个离线多智能体强化学习框架，用于解决个性化LLM中的通用真值一致性问题，通过联合优化真值准确性、跨群体一致性和个性化，减少不同社会群体间的真值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM会根据用户偏好和社会属性调整回复，但这可能导致不同社会群体在客观任务上接收到系统性不准确的回复，造成通用真值不一致。现有对齐方法要么忽略个性化，要么主要关注主观偏好对齐，忽视了通用真值的公平性和一致性。

Method: 提出 TriAlign 框架，采用离线多智能体强化学习（MARL），将每个社会群体建模为一个智能体。通过公平性感知目标和显式不一致惩罚项，联合优化三个目标：通用真值准确性、跨群体真值一致性和个性化。

Result: 在多个基准上，TriAlign 相比强基线方法在三个目标之间取得了更好的平衡，减少了跨社会群体的通用真值差异，同时提升了客观任务性能和个性化质量。

Conclusion: TriAlign 有效解决了个性化LLM中的真值一致性问题，证明了多智能体强化学习框架在平衡准确性、一致性和个性化方面的优势，为公平可靠的个性化LLM对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究个性化LLM的对齐问题，提出TriAlign框架通过多智能体强化学习（MARL）实现跨社会群体的通用真值一致性。虽然论文聚焦于文本LLM而非多模态，但其核心问题——对齐、偏好学习、公平性——与多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题高度契合，该子主题涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法。论文的MARL框架和公平性目标是对齐领域的重要扩展，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM alignment fairness, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM alignment, multi-agent RL, truth consistency, personalization, fairness

Abstract: Personalized large language models adapt responses to users' preferences and social attributes, but can introduce substantial universal truth inconsistencies across social groups, where some groups systematically receive less accurate responses on objective tasks. Existing alignment methods either ignore personalization or mainly focus on subjective preference alignment, largely overlooking fairness and consistency in universal truths. To address this gap, we study Truth-Invariant Alignment (TIA), an alignment problem for personalized LLMs that aims to ensure universal truths remain consistent across social groups while preserving personalization. We propose TriAlign, the first offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for TIA, where each social group is modeled as an agent interacting. TriAlign jointly optimizes universal truth accuracy, cross-group truth consistency, and personalization through a fairness-aware objective and an explicit inconsistency penalty. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that TriAlign achieves a stronger balance among these three objectives than strong baselines, reducing universal truth disparities across social groups while improving both objective task performance and personalization quality.

</details>


### [200] [OmniOPD: Logit-Free On-Policy Distillation via Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01476)
*Yuhang Zhou, Lizhu Zhang, Yifan Wu, Mingyi Wang, Peng Bo, Jiayi Liu, Xiangjun Fan, Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (85.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniOPD 提出了一种无需 logits 的在线蒸馏框架，通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 和 chunk 级语义相似度替代传统的 token 级 logit 匹配，结合峰值熵调度器和贝叶斯先验，在数学等基准上超越标准 OPD 方法，并支持黑盒教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）需要访问教师模型的 token 级 logits，这排除了许多闭源强模型作为教师；同时 token 级 logit 信号本身脆弱，依赖师生模型间狭窄的 next-token 重叠，容易放大重复循环等退化模式。本文旨在解决这两个耦合的限制。

Method: OmniOPD 用蒙特卡洛 rollout 替代确定性 logit 匹配，通过连续语义相似度度量近似教师对多 token chunk 的局部偏好；引入峰值熵调度器（peak-entropy scheduler）仅在学生高不确定性的推理分叉处进行监督；使用 Dirichlet-Multinomial 贝叶斯先验和基模型 KL 锚点来约束离散采样的方差并防止策略崩溃。

Result: 在多个竞争性基准上，OmniOPD 在数学任务上比标准 OPD 方法提升高达 +28.64%；与更强的黑盒教师（如 Claude-4.5-Haiku 和 Gemini-2.5-Flash）配合时，在数学任务上额外获得 +9.54% 的相对提升，使学生模型超越自我探索 RL 的性能。

Conclusion: Chunk 级语义验证比 token 级 logit 匹配提取了更可靠的学习信号，后者虽然信息密度高但被显著的噪声和脆弱性抵消。OmniOPD 框架有效支持黑盒教师模型，为在线蒸馏提供了更实用和鲁棒的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniOPD，一种无需教师 logits 的在线蒸馏方法，通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 和语义相似度进行 chunk 级监督，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好学习范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel logit-free distillation method with chunk-level verification, but focuses on LLM alignment rather than the user's priority multimodal generation or continuous models. Signals: On-policy distillation, logit-free supervision, chunk-level semantic similarity, Monte Carlo rollout, black-box teacher support

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its own generative trajectories under dense token-level feedback from a stronger teacher, mitigating both the off-policy distribution shift of Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and the sparse credit assignment of Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, standard OPD faces two coupled limitations. First, it requires direct access to the teacher's token-level logits, excluding a broad class of capable proprietary models from serving as teachers. Second, the token-level logit signal itself is brittle, depending on a narrow overlap of plausible next tokens between teacher and student, and prone to amplifying degenerate patterns such as repetition loops. In this paper, we introduce OmniOPD, a novel framework that addresses both limitations through a logit-free, chunk-level supervision signal. OmniOPD replaces deterministic logit matching with Monte Carlo rollouts that approximate the teacher's local preferences through a continuous semantic similarity metric over multi-token chunks, and concentrates this supervision via a peak-entropy scheduler that audits the student only at its high-uncertainty reasoning forks. A Dirichlet-Multinomial Bayesian prior and a base-model KL anchor further bound the variance of discrete sampling and prevent policy collapse across unaudited tokens. Across competitive benchmarks, OmniOPD surpasses the standard OPD approach by up to +28.64% on math, confirming that chunk-level semantic verification extracts a more reliable learning signal than token-level logit matching, whose high information density is offset by significant noise and brittleness. Furthermore, when paired with stronger black-box teachers such as Claude-4.5-Haiku and Gemini-2.5-Flash, OmniOPD achieves an additional +9.54% relative on math over its open-weight teacher counterpart, advancing the student past the performance of self-exploratory RL.

</details>


### [201] [Mitigating Perceptual Judgment Bias in Multimodal LLM-as-a-Judge via Perceptual Perturbation and Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02578)
*Seojeong Park, Jiho Choi, Junyong Kang, Seonho Lee, Jaeyo Shin, Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型作为自动评估器时存在"感知判断偏差"——当视觉证据与文本线索冲突时，MLLM 评估器倾向于奖励看似合理但不正确的回答。作者构建了感知扰动判断数据集（PPJD），并提出结合结构化 GRPO 奖励与批排序目标的统一训练框架，显著提升了评估器的感知忠实度、排序一致性和与人类评价的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 作为自动评估器（MLLM-as-a-Judge）时，当视觉证据与文本线索冲突，评估器倾向于奖励看似合理但不正确的回答，而非基于自身视觉感知进行判断。这种"感知判断偏差"导致评估结果不一致且不可验证，亟需解决。

Method: 1) 构建 Perceptually Perturbed Judgment Dataset (PPJD)，通过最小编辑生成反事实回答，隔离感知错误并提供可验证监督信号。2) 提出统一训练框架，结合结构化 GRPO 奖励（对感知忠实度进行细粒度评分）与批排序目标（实现全局排序一致性，无需显式成对标签）。

Result: 在多个 MLLM-as-a-Judge 基准上，该方法显著提升了感知忠实度、排序一致性和与人类评价的对齐。实验表明该方法具有可扩展性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为训练感知忠实、可解释且对视觉-推理冲突鲁棒的多模态评估器提供了一条可扩展的通用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 作为自动评估器时的感知判断偏差问题，并提出基于 GRPO 奖励建模和排序目标的训练框架来对齐人类偏好。这属于多模态对齐与偏好学习范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a critical bias in MLLM-as-a-judge, but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM evaluation bias, perceptual judgment bias, GRPO reward, batch-ranking, multimodal alignment

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong reasoning ability, yet their reliability as automated evaluators remains limited by a critical weakness: when visual evidence conflicts with textual cues, MLLM judges tend to reward plausible narratives over perceptually correct answers. We identify and systematically analyze this phenomenon, which we term Perceptual Judgment Bias. Through controlled visual perturbations, existing multimodal judges frequently anchor on the response text instead of their own visual perception, leading to inconsistent and non-verifiable evaluations. To address this issue, we introduce the Perceptually Perturbed Judgment Dataset, which constructs minimally edited counterfactual responses that isolate perceptual errors and enable verifiable supervision. Building on this dataset, we develop a unified training framework that combines a structured GRPO-based reward with a batch-ranking objective, achieving coherent global ordering without explicit pairwise labels. Experiments across diverse MLLM-as-a-Judge benchmarks show that our approach substantially improves perceptual fidelity, ranking coherence, and alignment with human evaluation. Our results establish a scalable and generalizable pathway for training multimodal judges that are perceptually grounded, interpretable, and robust to visual-reasoning conflicts.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_reasoning_cot'></div>

## 多模态推理与思维链 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 CoT、视觉推理、数学/代码/科学推理、推理时计算扩展。

### [202] [TimeLogic Challenge @ CVPR 2026: Strong MLLMs Meet Evidence-Seeking Agents for Temporal-Logic Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01631)
*Zhaoyang Xu, Xusheng He, Wei Liu, Zhenyang Li, Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeLogic Challenge（CVPR 2026），针对时序逻辑视频问答任务，设计了一个无需训练的证据搜索智能体。该智能体通过Think-Act-Observe循环，结合多粒度采样工具包和绝对时间戳，将时序关系简化为数值比较。系统使用轻量级分类器将问题路由到不同时序类别，每类有定制策略、迭代深度和提示，采样预算自适应调整。结合Gemini 3.1 Pro，在官方测试集上达到77.13 AvgAcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常对固定均匀采样的帧进行单次推理，难以处理需要精确定位动作边界或跨多个分散事件的时序逻辑视频问答（如before/after/until/overlap等关系）。需要一种能够主动搜索证据而非被动处理固定帧的方法。

Method: 提出证据搜索智能体，采用Think-Act-Observe循环：1) Think：LLM分析当前观察并决定下一步动作；2) Act：使用多粒度采样工具包（包括跳帧采样、关键帧采样、密集采样等）获取新帧；3) Observe：将每个观察与绝对时间戳交错，使时序关系简化为共享时间轴上的数值比较。轻量级分类器将问题路由到时序类别（如before/after/until/since/overlap），每类有定制策略、迭代深度和提示，采样预算根据语料特征和片段长度自适应调整。

Result: 在TimeLogic官方测试集上达到77.13 AvgAcc，显著优于基线方法。消融实验验证了多粒度采样、时序分类路由和自适应预算策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 证据搜索范式有效弥补了标准VLM在时序逻辑推理上的不足，将时序关系转化为数值比较的思路简洁有效。无需训练、即插即用的特性使其具有实用价值，为时序视频推理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注时序逻辑视频问答（Temporal-Logic VideoQA），涉及多事件时序关系推理（before/after/until/overlap等），属于多模态推理范畴。论文提出的证据搜索智能体通过Think-Act-Observe循环进行主动探索，结合多粒度采样和时序推理策略，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on temporal-logic video QA with an evidence-seeking agent, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: temporal-logic video QA, evidence-seeking agent, multi-granular sampling, Gemini 3.1 Pro, training-free system

Abstract: Temporal-logic video question answering requires a model to reason about when actions occur relative to one another, such as before, after, until, since, overlap, and multi-event chains, rather than merely what is present in a video. Standard vision-language models typically answer such questions in a single pass over a fixed, uniformly sampled set of frames, which is poorly matched to evidence that is often localized to narrow action boundaries or dispersed across several distant events. We present an evidence-seeking agent that treats temporal-logic VideoQA as active exploration. The agent follows a Think-Act-Observe loop driven by a multi-granular sampling toolkit, where every observation is interleaved with its absolute timestamp so that temporal relations reduce to numerical comparisons on a shared time axis. Its behavior is shaped by benchmark structure: a lightweight classifier routes each question to a temporal category, each with a tailored policy, iteration depth, and prompt, while sampling budgets adapt to corpus characteristics and clip length. The resulting training-free system couples Gemini 3.1 Pro with a temporal-reasoning policy and achieves 77.13 AvgAcc on the official TimeLogic test set.

</details>


### [203] [Distilling Neuro-Symbolic Programs into 3D Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01215)
*Wentao Mo, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: APEIRIA 通过三阶段课程学习（3D 感知对齐、CoT 监督微调、CoT 强化学习），将神经符号 3D 推理程序的可解释推理模式蒸馏到 3D 多模态大语言模型中，实现了透明推理与开放词汇灵活性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 空间推理方法存在根本性权衡：神经符号方法（NS3D）通过组合式程序实现可解释推理，但受限于封闭概念词汇和简单程序；端到端 3D MLLM 能处理复杂自然语言和开放词汇概念，但缺乏显式空间验证的黑盒推理。作者希望桥接两种范式，既保留符号推理的透明性和模块化，又获得 MLLM 的灵活性。

Method: 提出 APEIRIA，采用三阶段课程学习：(a) 3D 感知对齐——将物体视觉-几何特征对齐到 LLM 嵌入空间；(b) CoT-SFT——利用符号程序轨迹进行监督微调，学习查询分解和逐步验证；(c) CoT-RL——通过强化学习将推理模式扩展到开放集概念和深层嵌套指令。核心思想是迁移推理模式而非概念特定知识，保持规划与感知组件的模块化可互换性。

Result: 在 3D 定位、问答和描述生成任务上，APEIRIA 超越了先前的神经符号 3D 方法，并在 3D 空间推理数据集上达到与最先进 3D MLLM 相当的性能，成功统一了符号方法的系统推理与 MLLM 的灵活性。

Conclusion: 通过将神经符号程序的推理模式蒸馏到 MLLM 中，可以在保持透明推理和模块化设计的同时，获得处理开放词汇和复杂指令的能力，为 3D 空间推理提供了一种兼具可解释性和灵活性的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 APEIRIA，通过将神经符号程序的推理模式蒸馏到 3D MLLM 中，结合自然语言思维链（CoT）进行 3D 空间推理。核心贡献在于将符号推理的透明性与 MLLM 的灵活性结合，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D spatial reasoning with neuro-symbolic distillation, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: 3D MLLM, neuro-symbolic distillation, chain-of-thought, spatial reasoning, curriculum learning

Abstract: Current 3D spatial reasoning methods face a fundamental trade-off: neuro-symbolic 3D (NS3D) concept learners achieve interpretable reasoning through compositional programs but are constrained to closed-set concept vocabularies and simple programs; end-to-end 3D multi-modal LLMs (3D MLLMs) could handle complex natural language and open-vocabulary concepts but suffer from black-box reasoning without explicit spatial verification. We introduce APEIRIA, a neuro-symbolic 3D MLLM to bridge two paradigms by distilling symbolic reasoning patterns into MLLMs with natural language chain-of-thought. Our three-stage curriculum progressively builds reasoning capabilities: a) 3D perception alignment grounds object visual-geometric features to the LLM, b) CoT-SFT teaches query decomposition and stepwise verification from symbolic program traces, and c) CoT-RL extends reasoning patterns to open-set concepts and deeply nested instructions. By transferring reasoning patterns rather than concept-specific knowledge, APEIRIA preserves key NS3D virtues: transparent reasoning and modular interchangeability of planning and perception components. Evaluations on grounding, question answering, and captioning show that APEIRIA surpasses prior NS3D methods and matches state-of-the-art 3D MLLMs on 3D spatial reasoning datasets, unifying symbolic methods' systematic reasoning with MLLMs' flexibility. Code is available at .

</details>


### [204] [Evaluating Interactive Reasoning in Large Language Models: A Hierarchical Benchmark with Executable Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00103)
*Mingyuan Fan, Weiguang Han, Daixin Wang, Cen Chen, Zhiqiang Zhang, Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多轮交互式推理评测框架，将推理建模为主动证据获取与信念更新的过程。LLM 仅获知任务规则，需主动向隐藏环境发出查询、整合部分观测并决定何时提交答案。基于该框架构建了包含 474 个可执行游戏的基准，评估了多个前沿 LLM，结果表明该基准具有高度区分性，且上下文扰动和反事实修订会显著降低模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理评测多关注单步或静态推理，缺乏对多轮交互、主动信息获取和信念更新能力的评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，设计一个能评估 LLM 在动态环境中主动推理能力的基准。

Method: 设计了一个多轮交互式推理框架：LLM 仅接收任务规则，需主动向隐藏环境发出查询，逐步整合部分观测，并自主决定何时提交最终答案。基于该框架构建了 474 个可执行游戏，每个游戏在 5 个难度级别下评估。除成功率与交互效率外，还评估了上下文鲁棒性（受控扰动）和元认知适应能力（反事实修订与必要性判断）。

Result: 该基准具有高度区分性，能暴露不同 LLM 在成功率和交互效率上的巨大差异。上下文扰动导致中等但一致的性能下降，而反事实修订和必要性判断则导致更大幅度的性能下降。

Conclusion: 多轮交互式推理是评估 LLM 推理能力的重要维度，现有模型在主动信息获取和信念更新方面仍有显著不足，尤其是在反事实推理场景下表现脆弱。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个多轮交互式推理评测框架，将推理视为主动证据获取与信念更新的过程，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的推理评测方向。虽然论文仅涉及纯文本 LLM，但其交互式推理框架与多模态推理评测高度相关，且该 canonical subtopic 涵盖推理评测。

Importance reason: Evaluates LLM interactive reasoning but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Interactive reasoning benchmark, LLM evidence acquisition, contextual robustness, counterfactual revision, executable games

Abstract: We introduce a multi-turn interactive framework for reasoning evaluation that treats reasoning as active evidence acquisition and belief updating. Wherein, LLMs receive only the task rules, must issue targeted queries to a hidden environment, integrate partial observations over time, and decide when to submit a final answer. Beyond standard success rate and interaction efficiency, we evaluate contextual robustness under controlled contextual perturbations, and metacognitive adaptation through counterfactual revision and necessity judgment. We instantiate the framework as a benchmark of 474 executable games, each evaluated under five fixed configuration search spaces corresponding to five difficulty levels, and evaluate a broad set of frontier LLMs. Results show that the benchmark is highly discriminative, exposing large differences not only in success rate but also in interaction efficiency. Moreover, we empirically show that contextual perturbations cause moderate but consistent declines, whereas counterfactual revision and necessity judgment lead to much larger drops.

</details>


### [205] [Structured Visual Evidence Decomposition for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Screening of Obstructive Sleep Apnea-Hypopnea Syndrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00087)
*Chen Zhan, Yingchen Wei, Xiaoyu Tan, Jingjing Huang, Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (42.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EviOSAHS 是一个面向阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停综合征（OSAHS）筛查的证据驱动多模态推理框架。它将正面面部图像分解为七个固定解剖学查询，生成结构化证据卡，再结合临床资料由大语言模型进行二分类筛查判断。在642名受试者上达到88.47%准确率、94.86%灵敏度和5.14%假阴性率，优于直接多模态提示和朴素两阶段流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多导睡眠监测（PSG）前筛查需要结合临床风险因素和可见的头面部/颈部线索，但直接使用通用多模态基础模型进行医疗二分类判断会产生不稳定、校准不佳的输出。现有方法缺乏可审计、高灵敏度的结构化推理流程。

Method: 提出 EviOSAHS 框架，将筛查分为两个阶段：(1) 图像证据获取阶段：将正面面部图像分解为七个固定解剖学查询（颈部、下巴、嘴、面/颈脂肪、下颌、中面部、鼻子），通过视觉模型生成结构化证据卡，记录目标解剖部位、可见性、风险方向、证据强度、置信度和摘要；(2) 临床裁决阶段：将结构化证据卡与清洗后的临床资料结合，由大语言模型进行平衡的二分类筛查判断。

Result: 在642名受试者（正常→筛查阴性，轻/中/重度OSAHS→筛查阳性）上，EviOSAHS 达到88.47%准确率、94.86%灵敏度、93.74% F1分数、5.14%假阴性率。消融实验表明七问题视觉分解和平衡最终裁决对高灵敏度至关重要。4,494个视觉输出的问题级审计显示100%结构化解析率和93.88%高可见性率。

Conclusion: EviOSAHS 提供了一个可审计、高灵敏度的 OSAHS 预筛查工作流，可作为分诊辅助工具而非诊断系统。未来需要前瞻性验证、外部测试和校准的操作点控制才能进行临床部署。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于结构化视觉证据分解的多模态推理框架（EviOSAHS），将面部图像分解为七个解剖学查询，生成结构化证据卡，再结合临床信息由LLM进行最终筛查判断。这属于多模态推理（视觉推理+结构化证据链）范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Niche medical screening application with no alignment to user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: medical screening, OSAHS, structured evidence decomposition, low false-negative rate, auditable workflow

Abstract: Effective pre-polysomnography screening for obstructive sleep apnea-hypopnea syndrome (OSAHS) requires combining clinical risk factors with visible craniofacial and neck cues. Directly prompting general-purpose multimodal foundation models for medical yes/no decisions can yield unstable, poorly calibrated outputs. We propose EviOSAHS, an evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning framework that separates image-only anatomical evidence acquisition from final clinical adjudication. Each frontal facial image is decomposed into seven fixed anatomical queries covering the neck, chin, mouth, face/neck fat, lower jaw, midface, and nose. Visual responses are converted into structured evidence cards recording target anatomy, visibility, risk direction, evidence strength, confidence, and a concise summary. These cards are combined with a cleaned clinical profile only in the final stage, where a large language model performs balanced binary screening adjudication. We evaluated EviOSAHS on a 642-subject cohort, mapping normal subjects to screening-negative and mild, moderate, or severe OSAHS subjects to screening-positive. EviOSAHS achieved 88.47% accuracy, 94.86% sensitivity, 93.74% F1-score, and a 5.14% false-negative rate, outperforming clinical-only prompting, direct multimodal prompting, and naive two-stage pipelines under a unified protocol. Ablations showed that seven-question visual decomposition and balanced final adjudication were critical to the high-sensitivity operating point. A question-level audit of 4,494 visual outputs showed a 100% structured parse rate and 93.88% high-visibility rate. EviOSAHS provides an auditable, high-sensitivity workflow for binary pre-polysomnography OSAHS screening, but should be viewed as a triage assistant rather than a diagnostic system. Prospective validation, external testing, and calibrated operating-point control are needed before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [206] [Towards Understanding Modality Interaction in Multimodal Language Models via Partial Information Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00959)
*Wanlong Fang, Tianle Zhang, Wen Tao, Alvin Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (79.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文引入部分信息分解（PID）作为决策级框架，将多模态大模型中感官输入和语言输入的贡献分解为独特、冗余和协同三部分。在视觉-语言基准上发现：推理和定位任务呈现高协同性，专家和知识任务更依赖语言独有信息。进一步扩展为 Sensory PID 处理三模态系统，揭示视觉信息主导了音视频融合任务。PID 引导的重加权初步证明可提升多模态推理和定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注多模态表征对齐或最终任务性能，缺乏对模态间交互机制（如信息如何协同、冗余或独特贡献）的深入理解。作者希望从决策层面系统分析不同模态在 MLLM 中的具体作用模式。

Method: 提出 Partial Information Decomposition (PID) 框架，将多模态输入的信息贡献分解为 Unique（模态独有）、Redundant（冗余）和 Synergistic（协同）三部分。在视觉-语言任务上分析不同基准的模态使用模式，并扩展为 Sensory PID 处理视频-音频-语言三模态系统，将语言作为控制变量分解音视频信息增益。

Result: 推理和定位任务呈现高协同性，专家和知识任务更依赖语言独有信息。这些模态使用模式在不同模型家族中具有泛化性，并能预测对模态级干预的敏感性。Sensory PID 揭示视觉信息在音视频融合任务中占据主导地位，形成"感官协同瓶颈"。PID 引导的重加权初步提升了多模态推理和定位性能。

Conclusion: PID 框架为理解 MLLM 中模态交互提供了系统性的决策级分析工具，揭示了不同任务类型的模态使用模式，并为改进多模态推理提供了可操作的指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型中不同模态（视觉、语言、音频）的交互机制，使用部分信息分解（PID）框架分析模态间的独特、冗余和协同贡献，属于多模态推理与理解范畴。与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度相关，因为论文核心关注推理任务中的模态交互模式（如高协同性出现在推理和定位任务中），并探索通过 PID 引导重加权来提升多模态推理性能。

Importance reason: Provides a novel decision-level framework for understanding modality interaction, directly relevant to your priority on multimodal understanding and reasoning. Signals: Partial Information Decomposition, modality interaction analysis, synergy in reasoning tasks, PID-guided reweighting improves performance, extends to tri-modal systems

Abstract: Understanding modality interaction in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is central to reliable deployment. We introduce Partial Information Decomposition (PID) as a decision-level framework that separates unique, redundant, and synergistic contributions of sensory and linguistic inputs, beyond representation alignment and outcome-based evaluation. Across vision--language benchmarks, PID reveals recurring modality-use profiles: reasoning and grounding-oriented tasks tend to exhibit high synergy, whereas expert and knowledge-oriented tasks show stronger language-unique reliance. These profiles generalize across model families and predict sensitivity to modality-level interventions. We further extend PID to tri-modal systems with Sensory PID, treating language as a control variable to decompose video--audio information gain. Applied to omni-modal models, Sensory PID reveals a sensory synergy bottleneck dominated by visual information even on audio--visual fusion tasks. Finally, PID-guided reweighting provides initial evidence for improving multimodal reasoning and grounding performance.

</details>


### [207] [Decomposed On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Reasoning: Steering Gradients for Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00564)
*Hee Suk Yoon, Eunseop Yoon, Jaehyun Jang, SooHwan Eom, Ji Woo Hong, Mark Hasegawa-Johnson, Qi Dai, Chong Luo, Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Visual Gradient Steering (VGS) 方法，通过将 VLM 蒸馏损失分解为语言先验和视觉定位两个正交分量，并动态重定向梯度以优先优化视觉子空间，从而显著提升小模型在多模态推理任务中的视觉定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLM 蒸馏方法将教师模型的监督信号视为单一整体，忽略了语言先验与视觉定位两个目标在梯度空间中的正交性，导致优化过程被动地在两者之间折中。作者假设视觉定位是多模态推理的主要瓶颈，因此需要一种能够主动优先优化视觉子空间的蒸馏方法。

Method: 首先从数学上分解 on-policy 蒸馏损失为语言先验分量和视觉定位分量，并证明其梯度向量近似正交。基于此发现，提出 Visual Gradient Steering (VGS)：在每次更新时，计算两个分量的梯度，通过动态调整权重重新定向更新向量，使其更偏向视觉定位子空间，从而在保持语言能力的同时强化视觉感知。

Result: 在多个蒸馏设置和复杂多模态基准上，VGS 显著优于标准 on-policy 蒸馏方法，在视觉定位相关任务上取得明显提升，且训练开销极小。

Conclusion: VLM 蒸馏中的语言先验和视觉定位目标在梯度空间中是正交的，标准优化会陷入次优折中。VGS 通过主动引导梯度优先优化视觉子空间，能够有效提升小模型的视觉定位能力，为多模态推理蒸馏提供了新的优化视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的蒸馏与推理优化，核心关注视觉定位（visual grounding）在多模态推理中的瓶颈问题，属于多模态推理范畴。现有 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）涵盖视觉推理与多模态推理，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel gradient decomposition for VLM reasoning distillation, but not directly aligned with top priority continuous/multimodal generation or world model subtopics. Signals: VLM distillation, gradient steering, visual grounding, on-policy distillation, multimodal reasoning

Abstract: While on-policy distillation offers dense supervision for training small reasoning models, its optimization dynamics in the multimodal domain remain under-explored. In this work, we challenge the standard monolithic view of Vision-Language Model (VLM) distillation by mathematically decomposing the loss into two distinct components: the language prior and visual grounding. Our analysis uncovers that gradient vectors for these components are nearly orthogonal, indicating that the objective of aligning with the teacher's language distribution is geometrically independent from the objective of matching its visual perception. Consequently, standard optimization passively follows a suboptimal compromise trajectory that implicitly balances the two objectives. Hypothesizing that visual grounding constitutes the primary bottleneck for vision-language reasoning, we introduce Visual Gradient Steering (VGS), a method that dynamically reorients the update vector to prioritize the visual subspace. Experimental results on multiple distillation settings and complex multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that VGS significantly outperforms the standard monolithic formulation of on-policy distillation, achieving superior grounding with minimal training overhead.

</details>


### [208] [Off-the-Shelf LLMs as Process Scorers: Training-Free Alternative to PRMs for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01682)
*Atoosa Chegini, Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Chunk-Level Guided Generation，一种无需训练的推理时方法，利用现成大语言模型作为过程评分器，通过固定长度块（chunk）的似然评分引导小模型逐步生成，避免错误传播，在多个数学推理基准上超越多数投票并匹配甚至超越 PRM 引导搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM（过程奖励模型）引导搜索需要带步骤级标签的奖励模型训练，成本高昂；而简单的多数投票方法在小模型已陷入错误推理路径时失效。本文希望在不训练奖励模型的前提下，利用现成的大语言模型作为过程评分器来引导小模型生成，实现训练自由的推理时引导。

Method: 提出 Chunk-Level Guided Generation 框架：小模型每步生成 k 个固定长度的候选块（chunk），大模型通过计算每个候选块的似然（log-probability）进行评分，无需生成文本。两种选择规则：LGS（选择长度归一化后大模型对数概率最高的块）和 CGS（减去小模型对数概率，偏好大模型与小模型分歧大的块）。固定长度块避免了变长推理步骤中存在的长度偏差问题。

Result: 在 GSM8K、MATH、Minerva Math、AMC23、AIME24 上，CGS 相比多数投票提升最高 28 个百分点；在匹配的引导预算下，CGS 在多数基准上匹配或超越 Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-72B 引导搜索，且无需奖励模型训练。Qwen2.5-7B 由 Qwen2.5-72B 引导时，MATH 达 81.8%，Minerva Math 达 63.6%（k=16），超过多数投票 4-6 个百分点。生成的推理轨迹比 PRM 引导搜索更短。

Conclusion: Chunk-Level Guided Generation 是一种有效的训练自由替代方案，利用现成大语言模型作为过程评分器，在数学推理任务上取得了与 PRM 引导搜索相当甚至更优的性能，同时生成更短的推理轨迹，具有实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦数学推理中的过程评分与引导生成，属于推理时计算扩展（inference-time scaling）范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free inference-time method for math reasoning, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: training-free process scoring, chunk-level guidance, length bias analysis, math reasoning benchmarks, PRM alternative

Abstract: Selecting the best response from multiple small-model samples using a stronger scorer is a simple inference-time strategy, but fails when the small model has already committed to incorrect reasoning paths. PRM guided search avoids this by scoring candidate continuations during generation, but requires a reward model trained with step-level labels. We propose Chunk-Level Guided Generation, a training-free alternative that uses an off-the-shelf large language model as a process scorer. At each step, a small model samples k fixed-length candidate chunks, while the larger model scores the candidates using likelihoods without generating any text. The selected chunk is committed before the next step, steering generation before errors can propagate. We instantiate this framework with two selection rules: Likelihood-Guided Selection (LGS), which selects the chunk with the highest length-normalized large-model log-probability, and Contrastive-Guided Selection (CGS), which subtracts the small model's log-probability to favor chunks where the large model's preference diverges from the small model's. We show that scoring variable-length reasoning steps with large-model likelihoods is unreliable due to a systematic length bias that persists even after length normalization, and that fixed-length chunks avoid this confound. On GSM8K, MATH, Minerva Math, AMC23, and AIME24 with Qwen2.5-1.5B guided by Qwen2.5-32B and Llama-3.2-1B guided by Llama-3.1-70B, CGS outperforms majority voting by up to 28 pp and, under matched guidance budgets, matches or outperforms Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-72B guided search on most benchmarks without reward-model training. With Qwen2.5-7B guided by Qwen2.5-72B, CGS reaches 81.8% on MATH and 63.6% on Minerva Math at k=16, surpassing majority voting by 4--6 pp. Finally, Chunk-Level Guided Generation produces substantially shorter reasoning traces than PRM guided search.

</details>


### [209] [Pause and Think: A Dataset and Benchmark for Video-Grounded Assistive Action Suggestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00616)
*Shivam Singh, Saptarshi Majumdar, Pratik Prabhanjan, Zicheng Liu, Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 pause-and-think-T 数据集和 pause-and-think-B 基准，通过结构化推理训练使 4B 参数的紧凑 VLM 在视频场景理解与目标规划任务上达到 58.0% 准确率，以 59 倍更少的参数匹配 Qwen3-VL-235B（58.9%），并在场景理解上超越 GPT-4o，同时在 EgoThink 和 TempCompass 上展现出强大的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在视频场景中缺乏接地推理、时序一致性和上下文感知规划能力，难以提供人类可用的、基于场景的辅助建议。作者希望通过结构化推理训练来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建 pause-and-think-T 推理训练数据集，引导模型在生成答案前先暂停并推理视觉证据；基于该数据集微调一个 4B 参数的紧凑 VLM；设计 pause-and-think-B 基准评估上下文理解与目标规划能力。

Result: 4B 模型在 pause-and-think-B 上达到 58.0% 准确率，以 59 倍更少参数匹配 Qwen3-VL-235B（58.9%），场景理解超越 GPT-4o；在 EgoThink 和 TempCompass 上展现出显著的分布外泛化能力，在 affordance、assistance、attribution、situated reasoning 和 temporal order 等维度均有提升。

Conclusion: 针对性的推理监督训练使紧凑模型能够提供可执行的、视觉接地指导，并在无需大规模模型扩展的情况下泛化到训练数据之外，验证了结构化推理训练的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 pause-and-think-T 推理训练数据集和 pause-and-think-B 基准，专注于视频场景下的结构化推理（pause, reason over visual evidence），属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video-grounded reasoning and compact VLM efficiency, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Video-grounded reasoning, compact VLM efficiency, structured reasoning dataset, temporal consistency, scene understanding

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) struggle with grounded reasoning, temporal consistency, and context aware planning in videos. We introduce pause-and-think-T, a reasoning-centric training dataset that encourages models to pause, reason over visual evidence, and produce concise, actionable responses. The dataset promotes structured reasoning prior to answer generation, guiding models toward human-like, scene-grounded assistance. We fine-tune a compact 4B-parameter model and evaluate it on our pause-and-think-B benchmark targeting contextual understanding and goal planning tasks. The model achieves 58.0% accuracy at 59x fewer parameters than Qwen3-VL-235B (58.9%), matching GPT-5.2 on scene understanding and surpassing GPT-4o. Beyond our benchmark, it also shows strong out-of-distribution performance on EgoThink and TempCompass, with substantial gains in affordance, assistance, attribution recognition, situated reasoning, and temporal order, without benchmark-specific training. Our results indicate that targeted reasoning supervision enables compact models to deliver actionable, visually grounded guidance while generalizing beyond training data, without requiring large-scale model expansion.

</details>


### [210] [CASTLE2026 Team WDL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00712)
*Zhengyang Li, Zhenglin Du, Yi Wen, Fang Liu, Shuo Li, Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Qwen的证据感知多模态推理流水线，用于CASTLE Challenge @ EgoVis 2026中的长视频问答任务。系统通过解析问题线索、检索ASR片段、附加辅助图像、采样候选视频帧，并将问题路由到静态视觉、语音/文本、时序和混合类型四种专用提示模板中处理，最终通过置信度加权投票聚合多次推理结果。LoRA微调将得分从0.21提升至0.50，更多帧采样进一步提至0.58，最终排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CASTLE Challenge @ EgoVis 2026要求模型在600+小时的多视角长视频中回答四选一问题，每个问题需要综合视频、转录文本、辅助照片、人物、日期、房间和时序上下文等多种证据。现有方法难以有效整合这些异构证据进行长视频推理，因此需要设计专门的证据感知多模态推理系统。

Method: 基于Qwen构建证据感知多模态推理流水线：(1) 解析问题线索提取关键信息；(2) 检索ASR转录片段获取语音/文本证据；(3) 附加辅助图像（如人物、房间照片）；(4) 均匀采样候选视频帧；(5) 将问题路由到静态视觉、语音/文本、时序和混合类型四种专用提示模板；(6) 多次推理后通过置信度加权投票聚合结果。使用LoRA进行高效微调。

Result: LoRA微调将得分从0.21提升至0.50；增加采样帧数进一步将得分提升至0.58。最终系统在CASTLE Challenge @ EgoVis 2026中排名第一。

Conclusion: 证据感知的多模态推理流水线结合LoRA微调和多帧采样，能够有效处理长视频问答任务中异构证据的整合与推理，在CASTLE Challenge中取得了最优性能。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长视频问答中的多模态推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范子主题。论文构建了基于Qwen的证据感知多模态推理流水线，处理静态视觉、语音/文本、时序和混合类型问题，与子主题定义高度吻合。

Importance reason: A challenge-winning technical report on egocentric video QA, but lacks novelty in core methods and is far from the user's priority subtopics. Signals: egocentric video QA, long-form video, evidence-aware pipeline, Qwen-based, LoRA fine-tuning

Abstract: The CASTLE Challenge @ EgoVis 2026 evaluates long-form egocentric video question answering over 600+ hours of multi-perspective recordings. Each four-choice question requires evidence from videos, transcripts, auxiliary photos, people, days, rooms, and temporal context. We propose an evidence-aware multimodal reasoning pipeline based on Qwen. Our system parses question hints, retrieves ASR chunks, attaches auxiliary images, samples candidate video frames, and routes questions into static visual, speech/text, temporal, and mixed types with specialized prompts. Multiple inference passes are aggregated by confidence-weighted voting and converted into the official Codabench format. In ablation, LoRA improves the score from 0.21 to 0.50, and more sampled frames further raise it to 0.58. Our final system ranks first in the CASTLE Challenge @ EgoVis 2026.

</details>


### [211] [Mechanistic Diagnostics of Spatial Lexical Bias in Multimodal Large Language Model Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01914)
*Chuang Ma, Qianying Liu, Tomoyuki Obuchi, Fei Cheng, Wang Yang, Sudong Cai, Shuyuan Zheng, Akiko Aizawa, Sadao Kurohashi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (78.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现 MLLM 在空间多项选择题中存在"空间词汇偏差"：添加一个空间关系词到选项会吸引模型选择该选项。通过机械可解释性工具，作者发现该偏差主要源于语言侧而非视觉侧，并证明仅用轻量级 LLM 侧 DPO 微调即可有效缓解该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将 MLLM 空间推理失败归因于视觉信息关注不足，但本文发现存在另一种互补的失败模式——空间词汇偏差，即选项中的空间关系词本身会误导模型决策，需要深入理解其根源。

Method: 使用 9 个开源 MLLM 进行系统实验，构造"二元稳定但三元脆弱"的诊断示例；利用视觉注意力分析、残差流探针、激活修补（activation patching）和稀疏组件干预等机械可解释性工具定位偏差来源；提出轻量级 LLM 侧 DPO 微调方法，使用单物体对合成数据进行训练。

Result: 空间词汇偏差广泛存在于多个 MLLM 中；偏差主要源于语言侧而非视觉侧；轻量级 LLM 侧 DPO 微调在合成数据上将四路鲁棒准确率提升最多 100 个百分点，在 WhatsUp、SpatialMQA-Direct 和 VSR 数据集上分别提升 68.0、32.6 和 20.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: MLLM 空间推理失败的一个重要来源是语言侧的空间词汇偏差，而非单纯的视觉信息缺失；通过针对性的语言侧微调可以有效缓解该问题，为提升 MLLM 空间推理可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 在空间多项选择题中的空间词汇偏差（spatial lexical bias），属于多模态空间推理的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides mechanistic diagnostics and a lightweight fix for spatial lexical bias in MLLMs, but the subtopic is not a priority. Signals: MLLM spatial reasoning, mechanistic interpretability, DPO fine-tuning, bias mitigation, language-side failure

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain unreliable on spatial multiple-choice questions, and their failures are often attributed to poorly attended visual information. In this work, we identify a complementary failure mode, spatial lexical bias: adding a spatial relation word to the answer options can attract the model's decision and make the newly added option likely to be selected. Using nine open-weight MLLMs, we show that this phenomenon is widely observed. In particular, models can answer a binary spatial question correctly, yet consistently select an incorrect third spatial option once it is added to the answer set. We isolate such binary-stable but ternary-fragile cases as diagnostic examples and leverage mechanistic interpretability tools, revealing that a substantial part of the failure instead originates on the language side rather than the visual side: visual attention analyses and residual-stream probes show the correct spatial relation remains internally available on these failures, while irrelevant-option controls, activation patching, and sparse component interventions trace the bias to specific LLM-side channels and neurons. Based on this finding, we show that a lightweight LLM-only DPO update on tiny single-object-pair synthetic data mitigates the bias, lifting four-way robust accuracy by up to 100 points on synthetic data, and by 68.0, 32.6, and 20.1 points on broader evaluation datasets WhatsUp, SpatialMQA-Direct, and VSR.

</details>


### [212] [PlanarBench: Evaluating LLM Spatial Reasoning via Planar Graph Drawing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02010)
*Oleksandr Nikitin*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PlanarBench 是一个评估 LLM 空间推理能力的基准，要求模型根据边列表以 ASCII 艺术形式绘制平面图。该基准通过置换边顺序、边方向和节点标签来防止记忆化，在 91 个模型上测试了 199 个最简单的非异构连通平面图（2-7 个顶点），发现边数是比节点数更准确的难度预测因子（r=-0.85）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 图推理基准通常只使用节点数作为难度指标，且缺乏对空间推理（如平面图绘制）这一难以通过记忆解决的复杂任务的系统评估。作者希望构建一个能真正测试 LLM 空间推理能力、且能抵抗记忆化的基准。

Method: 构建 PlanarBench 基准，包含 199 个最简单的非异构连通平面图（2-7 个顶点）。任务要求 LLM 根据给定的边列表，以 ASCII 艺术形式输出平面图。通过随机置换边顺序、边方向和节点标签来生成多个变体，防止模型依赖记忆。在 91 个模型上进行评估，分析边数、节点数等特征与任务难度的相关性。

Result: 边数是任务难度的主导预测因子（r=-0.85），这一发现是先前 LLM 图基准未报告的。91 个模型在平面图绘制任务上的表现差异显著，揭示了当前 LLM 在空间推理方面的局限性。

Conclusion: PlanarBench 提供了一个有效的空间推理评估工具，边数比节点数更能预测任务难度。当前 LLM 在平面图绘制这一空间推理任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文评估LLM在平面图绘制任务上的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理（空间推理）范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for spatial reasoning but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: LLM spatial reasoning benchmark, planar graph drawing, ASCII art task, edge count difficulty predictor, 91 models tested

Abstract: PlanarBench tests whether LLMs can draw planar graphs as ASCII art given only an edge list -- a spatial reasoning task that resists memorization because edge order, edge orientation, and node labels are all permutable. We evaluate 91 models on the 199 simplest non-isomorphic connected planar graphs (2 - 7 vertices). Edge count is the dominant difficulty predictor ($r = -0.85$) -- a finding not reported in prior LLM graph benchmarks, which use only node count as the difficulty axis.

</details>


### [213] [An Enigma of Artificial Reason: Investigating the Production-Evaluation Gap in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01462)
*Mingzhong Sun, Teresa Yeo, Armando Solar-Lezama, Tan Zhi-Xuan*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型（LRM）存在显著的"生产-评估差距"：模型在生成数学推理链时近乎完美，但在评估含有推理缺陷但答案正确的解答时表现极差（低至48%）。通过思维链分析和线性探针实验，揭示了答案确认偏差是主要原因——模型倾向于验证最终答案而非仔细检查推理步骤，甚至会在发现异常推理时编造合理化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类研究表明人们通常更擅长评估推理而非从头生成推理，但当前LRM训练范式侧重于生成长链推理来解决复杂问题。作者希望探究LRM在评估推理方面的能力，以及是否存在与人类不同的"生产-评估差距"。

Method: 构建VAIR数据集（Valid-Answer-Invalid-Reasoning），包含答案正确但推理有缺陷的数学问题。对比人类与LRM在求解和评估上的表现差异，并通过思维链分析、线性探针和因果干预（causal patching）等方法探究模型内部表征和决策机制。

Result: 人类在评估与求解之间仅差6%，而前沿LRM在评估VAIR解答时得分低至48%，尽管求解近乎完美。线性探针显示模型激活能编码有效推理，但无法稳健地将VAIR解答表征为无效。因果干预表明最终答案的表征直接影响模型判断，证实了答案确认偏差。

Conclusion: 当前主导的推理训练方法激励LRM生成并确认指向正确答案的推理，但未能使其学会稳健地评估推理过程本身。这种"生产-评估差距"是LRM的重要局限性，需要新的训练范式来弥补。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大型推理模型（LRM）在数学推理中的"生产-评估差距"，属于多模态推理与思维链方向下的推理评估与CoT分析，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Reveals a critical reasoning evaluation gap in LRMs, but focuses on math reasoning rather than the user's priority multimodal or continuous models. Signals: production-evaluation gap, answer confirmation bias, reasoning evaluation failure, VAIR dataset, causal patching analysis

Abstract: Studies of human reasoning have shown that people are typically stronger at evaluating reasoning than producing it from scratch. In contrast, large reasoning models (LRMs) are trained to excel at producing long chains of reasoning to solve complex problems. How then do LRMs perform at evaluating reasons? We investigate this with the Valid-Answer-Invalid-Reasoning (VAIR) dataset: math problems and solutions with trivial reasoning flaws but valid answers, designed to isolate reasoning evaluation from the confound of reasoning production. Unlike humans, who we find are only 6% worse at grading than solving such problems, we find a substantial production-evaluation gap in LRMs: frontier models score as low as 48% when evaluating VAIR solutions, despite near-perfect solution production. Why this enigma? Through chain-of-thought (CoT) analysis, we find evidence of an answer confirmation bias: LRMs often produce then check for the correct answer instead of carefully verifying each step, fabricating rationalizations even when noticing anomalous reasoning. Linear probes corroborate this, showing that while LRM activations encode some representation of valid reasoning, they fail to robustly represent VAIR solutions as invalid. Causal patching of the final answer's representations causes LRM verdicts and activations to flip, demonstrating that answer validity is responsible for models' confirmation biases. These findings indicate an outstanding limitation in dominant approaches to reasoning training, which incentivize LRMs to produce and confirm reasoning towards correct answers, but not to robustly evaluate the underlying reasons.

</details>


### [214] [Attention-guided Fine-tuning of Multimodal Large Language Models Improves Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01558)
*Sanchit Sinha, Guangzhi Xiong, Bohan Liu, Zhenghao He, Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (78.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多模态大语言模型中思维链推理的失败模式（过早答案承诺和视觉token访问不足），发现标准CoT监督微调只能部分缓解这些问题，进而提出Attentive-CoT（Att-CoT），一种注意力引导的微调目标，通过延迟答案承诺和维持视觉token访问来提升CoT推理性能，在三个视觉推理基准和六个MLLM上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示在多模态大语言模型中的效果不稳定，在多个视觉推理基准上CoT反而会降低性能。作者希望系统分析CoT在MLLM中的失败原因，并提出有效的改进方法。

Method: 首先对三种现代MLLM系列进行系统分析，识别出两种主要失败模式：过早答案承诺（premature answer commitment）和推理过程中视觉token访问不足。然后提出Attentive-CoT（Att-CoT），一种注意力引导的微调目标函数，在不改变模型架构的前提下，鼓励CoT轨迹延迟答案承诺并维持持续的视觉token访问。

Result: 在三个视觉推理基准上对六个MLLM进行实验，Att-CoT相比标准CoT监督微调显著提升了CoT推理性能，有效缓解了识别出的两种失败模式。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的微调可以有效改善MLLM中CoT推理的质量，通过延迟答案承诺和增强视觉token访问来提升视觉推理能力，且该方法可作为插件集成到现有CoT微调流程中。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型中的思维链（CoT）推理，分析CoT在视觉推理中的失败模式并提出注意力引导的微调方法Att-CoT来提升CoT性能。这直接对应[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot），属于多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Addresses CoT failure modes in MLLMs with a practical fine-tuning method, but is not in the user's priority subtopics. Signals: MLLM reasoning, chain-of-thought, attention-guided fine-tuning, visual token access, premature answer commitment

Abstract: The effectiveness of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remains uncertain: across several visual reasoning benchmarks, CoT prompting often degrades performance compared to direct prompting. In this paper, we provide a systematic analysis of CoT behavior in three modern MLLM families across model scales on datasets requiring step-wise visual evidence. Our analysis identifies two recurring failure modes: premature answer commitment and limited direct visual-token access during rationale generation. We further find that standard CoT-style Supervised Fine-Tuning (CoT-SFT) can mitigate these issues only partially, while often increasing reliance on textual priors and reducing counterfactual visual dependence. Motivated by these findings, we propose Attentive-CoT (Att-CoT), an attention-guided fine-tuning objective that encourages CoT trajectories to delay answer commitment while maintaining sustained visual-token access. Att-CoT can be plugged into any CoT-SFT training run without architectural changes. Experiments on three visual reasoning benchmarks across six MLLMs show that Att-CoT enhances CoT performance over standard fine-tuning.

</details>


### [215] [Disentanglement-Based Equivariant Learning for Compositional VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02168)
*Zhou Du, Zhaoquan Yuan, Xiao Wu, Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DEAL 框架，通过因果启发的解耦学习和等变约束，在仅使用答案监督的条件下提升组合式 VQA 的推理能力，在 CLEVR-CoGenT 和 GQA-SGL 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合式 VQA 方法未能有效解耦底层概念，且依赖额外线索（如问题模板）进行训练，不适用于真实场景。本文旨在仅利用答案监督实现组合推理能力的提升。

Method: 提出 DEAL 框架：1）在重编码框架中通过因果干预解耦视觉和文本输入中的概念；2）基于等变性原理，对推理输入施加组合变换，并在输出上施加等变约束，增强组合推理能力。

Result: 在 CLEVR-CoGenT 和 GQA-SGL 基准上，DEAL 在视觉泛化和语言泛化设置下均超越现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 解耦学习与等变约束相结合能有效提升组合式 VQA 的泛化能力，且无需额外监督信号，具有实际应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究组合式视觉问答（compositional VQA），属于多模态视觉推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on compositional VQA with disentanglement and equivariance, which is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Compositional VQA, Disentanglement, Equivariant learning, Answer-only supervision, CLEVR-CoGenT

Abstract: Compositional visual question answering (VQA) represents a challenging yet fundamental task that requires models to comprehend novel combinations of previously learned concepts. The current methods often overlook the disentanglement of underlying concepts and are restricted in terms of their ability to effectively capture the compositional variation mechanism. Moreover, the state-of-the-art techniques depend on additional clues for training, which is not feasible in real-world VQA scenarios. To address these issues, in this paper, we introduce a novel Disentanglement-based EquivAriant Learning (DEAL) framework for compositional VQA, which is guided exclusively by ground-truth answers. In DEAL, we employ causality-inspired interventions to disentangle concepts derived from visual and textual inputs within a re-encoding framework. Based on the principle of equivariance, we subsequently perform a compositional transformation on the inference input and impose the equivariant constraint on the output to augment the compositional reasoning capacity of the model. Comprehensive experiments conducted on the benchmark CLEVR-CoGenT and GQA-SGL datasets validate the superiority of our proposed DEAL approach over the existing state-of-the-art methods for compositional VQA tasks in both visual and linguistic generalization settings.

</details>


### [216] [Active Exploring like a Pigeon: Reinforcing Spatial Reasoning via Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02459)
*Wei Deng, Xianlin Zhang, Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (66.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 受鸽子导航行为启发，提出一种基于智能体VLM的空间推理管线，通过动态认知地图和空间断言代码（SAC）提供密集奖励信号，结合监督微调和强化微调，在MindCube基准上达到80.5%准确率，在Rotation子集上相对提升53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理中通常作为被动观察者，难以应对真实世界应用；而强化学习方法依赖稀疏奖励，对复杂推理任务效果有限。受鸽子构建和利用认知地图进行导航的启发，作者希望让VLM主动探索环境并利用结构化记忆进行空间推理。

Method: 提出智能体空间推理管线：(1) 动态认知地图（Dynamic Cognitive Map），将场景布局参数化为物体位置和朝向，作为持久化记忆；(2) 空间断言代码（Spatial Assertion Codes, SAC），用Python表达式程序化描述空间关系，与动态认知地图协作验证中间推理步骤，提供密集奖励信号。通过监督微调（SFT）和强化微调（RL）优化模型。

Result: 在MindCube基准上达到80.5%总体准确率，在最具挑战性的Rotation子集上以29.5个准确率点（相对提升53.2%）超越此前最佳方法。

Conclusion: 受鸽子认知地图启发的主动探索式空间推理方法显著提升了VLM的空间推理能力，动态认知地图与SAC结合提供的密集奖励信号有效克服了稀疏奖励问题，验证了智能体式VLM在复杂空间推理任务中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于通过强化学习增强VLM的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel spatial reasoning pipeline with dense rewards and cognitive maps, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: spatial reasoning, reinforcement finetuning, cognitive map, VLM agent, MindCube benchmark

Abstract: Enabling Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to perform spatial reasoning remains challenging. Existing approaches treat VLMs as passive observers, which is difficult for real-world applications. Moreover, reinforcement learning methods rely on sparse rewards, limiting their effectiveness for complex reasoning tasks. Inspired by pigeons' building and exploiting cognitive maps for navigation, we propose a novel agentic pipeline for spatial reasoning. First, we introduce a new \emph{dynamic cognitive map} parameterizing scene layout as object positions and orientations, serving as persistent memory for new observations. Second, we propose a novel \emph{Spatial Assertion Codes (SAC)}, Python expressions programmatically describing spatial relationships. By collaborating with the dynamic cognitive map, SAC enables verification of intermediate reasoning steps, providing dense reward signals. We optimize the model via supervised and reinforcement finetuning. Experiments on the MindCube benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance with \emph{80.5\%} overall accuracy, outperforming the best current method by \emph{29.5} accuracy points (a relative improvement of \emph{53.2\%}) on the challenging \textsc{Rotation} subset. Our code and data are open-sourced at .

</details>


### [217] [Question-Aware Evidence Ledgers for Video Relational Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02506)
*Yilin Ou, Mengshi Qi, Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于问题感知证据账本（Question-Aware Evidence Ledgers）的测试时推理流水线，用于视频关系推理问答（VRR-QA）。该方法以 GPT-5.5 视频问答求解器为核心，通过路由账本显式提取目标、计数单位、参考帧及时空范围等证据，并利用开放词汇检测、深度线索、场景图等外部工具作为证据源，最终通过保守门控机制融合答案，在测试集上达到 92.95% 的总体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频关系推理问答（VRR-QA）中，答案往往依赖于隐式的空间关系、事件边界、目标身份和对话上下文，而非单一显著帧。现有方法难以有效捕获和利用这些多维度证据，导致推理能力受限。

Method: 提出一个测试时推理流水线：(1) 基于强 GPT-5.5 视频 QA 求解器生成初始答案；(2) 设计问题感知证据账本（routed ledgers），针对计数、空间、端点、视角和对话推理等不同问题类型，显式提取所需的目标、计数单位、参考帧及时空范围；(3) 利用开放词汇检测、深度线索、成对裁剪、ASR 和场景图等外部工具作为证据源；(4) 采用保守门控机制，仅在独立证据唯一支持不同选项时才更新答案。

Result: 在 VRR-QA 挑战测试集上，最终证据门控流水线达到 92.95% 的总体准确率和 93.79% 的宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: 问题感知证据账本能够有效增强视频关系推理中多维度证据的显式利用，保守门控机制在保持初始答案稳定性的同时，通过可靠证据提升推理准确性。该方法展示了结合大模型与结构化证据提取在复杂视频推理任务中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦视频关系推理（VRR-QA），涉及空间关系、事件边界、目标识别和对话上下文等复杂多模态推理任务，属于多模态推理范畴。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on video relational reasoning with GPT-5.5, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: video relational reasoning, GPT-5.5, question-aware evidence ledgers, test-time reasoning, external tools

Abstract: The VRR-QA challenge evaluates visual relational reasoning in videos, where answers often depend on implicit spatial relations, event boundaries, target identity, and dialogue context rather than a single salient frame. We present a test-time reasoning pipeline built around a strong GPT-5.5 video QA solver and a set of question-aware evidence ledgers. The initial solver answers each question from a uniform video representation, while routed ledgers are prompted to make the required targets, count units, reference frames, and temporal or spatial scope explicit for counting, spatial, endpoint, viewpoint, and dialogue reasoning. External tools such as open-vocabulary detection, depth cues, pair crops, ASR, and scene-graph ledgers are used only as evidence sources. A conservative gate keeps the current answer unless independent evidence uniquely supports a different option. The final evidence-gated pipeline achieves 92.95% overall accuracy and 93.79% macro accuracy on the challenge test split.

</details>


### [218] [VLMs are Good Teachers for Video Reasoning via Adaptive Test-Time Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02564)
*Junhao Cheng, Liang Hou, Tianxiong Zhong, Xin Tao, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (92.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的视频推理范式：将VLM作为"教师"，通过提取任务规则构建可微奖励函数，在测试时在线优化VGM（视频生成模型）的轻量LoRA模块，从而引导VGM生成符合任务逻辑的视觉轨迹。在VBVR-Bench和RULER-Bench上平均提升16.7个点，大幅超越VLM-as-Solver和Best-of-N基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"Reasoning with Video"范式使用视频生成模型（VGM）生成视觉轨迹来完成推理任务，但VGM难以理解和遵循任务特定规则，导致逻辑失败。已有工作尝试用VLM作为预求解器生成文本指导，但文本描述无法捕捉复杂时空细节，VGM也难以忠实执行细粒度或长尾指令。作者观察到VLM虽然作为求解器能力有限，但具备强大的感知能力来评估过程约束满足和最终目标达成，因此提出将VLM角色转变为"教师"。

Method: 1) VLM教师从任务描述中提取任务特定规则，构建可微奖励函数；2) 在测试时，通过在线优化VGM中的轻量LoRA模块，使VGM生成的视觉轨迹最大化VLM奖励；3) 该方法实现了自适应测试时优化，将推理能力扩展到VGM固有边界之外。

Result: 在符号推理（VBVR-Bench）和通用推理（RULER-Bench）基准上，该方法平均提升16.7个点，远超VLM-as-Solver范式（+0.4点）和Best-of-N缩放（+2.2点），且测试时计算成本相当。

Conclusion: 将VLM作为测试时教师为视频推理提供了一种有前景的新范式，通过在线优化实现了可泛化的视频推理能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频推理任务，利用VLM作为"教师"通过测试时优化引导视频生成模型进行推理，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot），该子主题涵盖视觉推理与推理时计算扩展，与本工作高度契合。

Importance reason: Novel VLM-as-teacher paradigm for video reasoning aligns with user's high-priority multimodal understanding and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: video reasoning, test-time optimization, VLM teacher, VGM reasoner, LoRA adaptation

Abstract: The recent "Reasoning with Video" paradigm utilizes Video Generation Models (VGMs) to generate temporally coherent visual trajectories to complete reasoning tasks. Although state-of-the-art VGMs excel at visual quality, they often struggle to understand and follow task-specific rules, leading to logical failures across diverse reasoning scenarios. Existing efforts try to utilize Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as problem pre-solvers to produce or refine textual guidance for the VGM. However, textual descriptions fail to capture intricate spatiotemporal details, and VGMs often struggle to faithfully execute fine-grained or long-tail instructions even with a valid plan. While VLMs struggle as solvers, they possess strong perception capabilities to evaluate process-constraint satisfaction and final-goal achievement. Leveraging this strength, we introduce a paradigm shift that transitions the role of VLMs to "teachers". Specifically, a VLM teacher extracts task-specific rules to formulate differentiable rewards, guiding a VGM Reasoner via test-time online optimization of a lightweight LoRA module. This strategy enables adaptive test-time optimization and extends the reasoning capabilities beyond the VGM's intrinsic boundaries. Evaluations on symbolic (VBVR-Bench) and general-purpose (RULER-Bench) video reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed method yields a 16.7-point average performance gain, outperforming the VLM-as-Solver paradigm (+0.4 points) and Best-of-N scaling (+2.2 points) by a large margin at comparable test-time cost. These findings reveal that integrating VLMs as test-time teachers offers a promising paradigm for achieving generalizable video reasoning. Project Page:

</details>
