### [219] [ROGLE: Robust Global-Local Alignment with Automated Region Supervision for Text-Based Person Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01825)
*Zequn Xie, Xibei Jia, Sihang Cai, Shulei Wang, Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ROGLE 提出一种鲁棒的全局-局部对齐框架，通过自动化的区域-句子匹配（RSM）策略生成伪区域级标注，结合全局对比学习和局部对齐的多粒度学习，用于文本到行人图像检索。同时构建了 P-VLG 基准数据集，包含超过10万个标注区域和长描述文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 CLIP 的 TBPS 模型由于训练于短描述文本，存在全局表征偏差和语义稀疏性问题，导致细粒度对齐能力弱，且缺乏区域级标注数据。

Method: 提出 ROGLE 框架，包含：(1) 自动化的 Region-to-Sentence Matching (RSM) 策略，自动挖掘伪区域-句子对以提供细粒度监督；(2) 多粒度学习策略，融合全局对比学习与区域级局部对齐；(3) 构建 P-VLG 基准数据集，包含10万+标注区域和长描述文本。

Result: 大量实验表明 ROGLE 显著优于现有方法，尤其在长描述查询上表现突出。

Conclusion: ROGLE 通过自动化区域监督和多粒度对齐有效解决了 TBPS 中的细粒度理解问题，P-VLG 基准为未来研究提供了更全面的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于文本的行人检索（Text-Based Person Search），属于跨模态检索任务，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on text-based person search, a niche retrieval task not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: text-based person search, global-local alignment, automated region supervision, CLIP fine-tuning, benchmark dataset

Abstract: Text-Based Person Search (TBPS) aims to retrieve pedestrian images using natural language queries. However, existing TBPS models, especially those based on CLIP, struggle with fine-grained understanding due to global representational bias and semantic sparsity inherited from training on short captions. This results in weak fine-grained alignment, exacerbated by the scarcity of region-level annotations. To address this, we propose ROGLE (Robust Global-Local Embedding), a unified framework that overcomes reliance on costly manual annotations through an automated Region-to-Sentence Matching (RSM) strategy. RSM automatically mines pseudo region-sentence pairs for scalable fine-grained supervision. Furthermore, ROGLE employs a multi-granular learning strategy that fuses global contrastive learning with region-level local alignment. We also introduce the P-VLG Benchmark, a large-scale dataset constructed by curating and enriching images from established public benchmarks. It features over 100,000 annotated regions and rich long-form captions, making it the first TBPS benchmark to support both global and local assessment protocols. Extensive experiments show that ROGLE significantly outperforms existing approaches, particularly on challenging long-form queries. Code and the P-VLG benchmark will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [220] [T-CLIP: Enabling Thermal Perception for Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00673)
*Tayeba Qazi, Ayush Maheshwari, Prerana Mukherjee, Brejesh Lall*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (72.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出T-CLIP，通过解耦的双LoRA框架将CLIP扩展到热红外感知领域，解决热图像与文本的跨模态对齐问题。同时引入IR-Cap，首个物理感知的热红外图像描述生成管道与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在低光照和恶劣天气下具有优势，但现有视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）无法对齐热红外图像与文本描述，存在热感知鸿沟。具体挑战包括：缺乏带标注的热红外数据集、标准LLM无法推理热现象、全局场景上下文与物体级热信号在单一嵌入空间中冲突。

Method: 提出IR-Cap管道，利用物理知识生成全局和细粒度热描述，构建三个公开基准上的描述数据集。提出T-CLIP，采用解耦的双LoRA框架，分别独立适配CLIP用于场景级和物体级热红外理解，避免全局与局部特征冲突。

Result: 在三个热红外基准上的跨模态检索任务中，T-CLIP在所有基线方法上取得一致改进。同时展示了其在文本条件热图像生成中的适用性。

Conclusion: T-CLIP有效弥合了热红外感知与视觉-语言模型之间的鸿沟，解耦的双LoRA设计成功解决了全局与局部热信号表征冲突问题，为热红外多模态理解与生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是将CLIP扩展到热红外图像-文本跨模态检索，属于多模态检索方向。同时涉及文本条件热图像生成，也匹配多模态生成方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on thermal-specific CLIP adaptation, which is niche and not aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Thermal perception gap, Decoupled dual-LoRA, IR-Cap dataset, Cross-modal retrieval, Not autoregressive or diffusion-based

Abstract: Thermal imaging offers a powerful alternative to visible-spectrum vision under challenging conditions such as low illumination and adverse weather, yet foundational vision-language models like CLIP fail to align thermal images with textual descriptions due to a fundamental thermal perception gap. We identify three major challenges: the lack of captioned thermal datasets, the inability of standard LLMs to reason about thermal phenomena, and a key representational challenge in thermal imaging where global scene context and object-level heat signatures conflict when learned together in a single embedding space. To address these, we introduce IR-Cap, the first physics-aware thermal captioning pipeline and dataset providing complementary global and fine-grained thermal descriptions across three public benchmarks, and T-CLIP, a decoupled dual-LoRA framework that independently adapts CLIP for scene-level and object-level thermal understanding. T-CLIP achieves consistent improvements over all baselines across three thermal benchmarks in cross-modal retrieval, and we provide an exploratory demonstration of its applicability to text-conditioned thermal image generation.

</details>


### [221] [Reason, Retrieve, Re-rank: A Zero-Shot Reasoning-Aware Framework for Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00910)
*Ali Alavi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: R3-CoVR 是一个零样本组合视频检索框架，利用冻结的 MLLM (Qwen3-VL-8B) 推理编辑后的视频状态变化并生成描述，结合 SigLIP-2 进行初检索，再通过约束感知重排序提升精度，在 CVPR 2026 VidLLMs 挑战赛上达到 91.9% R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索 (CoVR) 需要根据参考视频和文本修改找到目标视频，现有方法依赖大量标注数据训练，缺乏零样本能力。本文旨在构建一个无需训练、完全基于冻结基础模型的零样本 CoVR 框架。

Method: 提出 R3-CoVR 三阶段流水线：(1) Reason：用 Qwen3-VL-8B 推理编辑后的"后效"（状态变化、动作阶段、场景、镜头、节奏），生成简洁的后编辑描述；(2) Retrieve：用 SigLIP-2 对比视频-文本编码器对描述和候选视频进行初检索；(3) Re-rank：用同一 MLLM 作为评判器，对短列表候选按约束条件重新排序。

Result: 在挑战测试集上达到 91.9% R@1 和 98.2% R@10。关键发现：(i) 将描述长度匹配到对比编码器的文本窗口使 R@1 从 67.5 提升至 72.7；(ii) 约束感知重排序使 R@1 从 72.7 提升至 91.9，是最大的单一增益来源。

Conclusion: R3-CoVR 证明了零样本组合视频检索的可行性，通过推理驱动的描述生成和约束感知重排序，无需任何训练即可取得优异性能。该框架简洁、模块化，易于复现和扩展。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Composed Video Retrieval (CoVR)，即组合视频检索任务，属于跨模态/多模态检索范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索" (multimodal_retrieval) 完全匹配该方向。

Importance reason: Zero-shot composed video retrieval with frozen MLLMs is solid engineering but not a core advance for the user's priority subtopics. Signals: composed video retrieval, zero-shot, frozen MLLM, re-ranking, CVPR workshop challenge

Abstract: Composed Video Retrieval (CoVR) seeks the target video that results from applying a free-form textual modification to a reference video. We address the \emph{Reason-Aware} CoVR (CoVR-R) challenge at the CVPR~2026 VidLLMs workshop, where retrieval is strictly zero-shot. We present \textbf{R3-CoVR} (\emph{Reason, Retrieve, Re-rank}), a training-free pipeline built entirely from frozen foundation models. A multimodal large language model (Qwen3-VL-8B) reasons about the \emph{after-effects} an edit implies -- state transitions, action phases, scene, camera and tempo -- and verbalises a concise post-edit description; a contrastive video--text encoder (SigLIP-2) embeds this description and the gallery for first-stage retrieval; finally a constraint-aware re-ranking stage uses the same multimodal model as a judge that scores each shortlisted candidate against the intended edited result. On the challenge test set, R3-CoVR attains \textbf{91.9\% R@1} and \textbf{98.2\% R@10}. Two findings drive these results: (i)~matching the description length to the contrastive encoder's text window lifts \Rk{1} from $67.5$ to $72.7$; and (ii)~the constraint-aware re-ranker, which reorders only the shortlist, lifts \Rk{1} from $72.7$ to $91.9$ -- the single largest gain. We analyse the re-ranker's behaviour, the retrieve/re-rank blend, and the shortlist depth, and we release a clean three-layer implementation.

</details>


### [222] [TrafficRAG: A Multimodal RAG Framework for Traffic Accident Liability Determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01737)
*Xu Li, Zedong Fu, Xinyi Li, Xun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (47.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TrafficRAG 是一个多模态检索增强框架，通过 VLM 将事故视频转化为结构化文本查询，结合稀疏与稠密检索获取法律知识，再由 LLM 生成标准化的交通事故责任分析报告，在多个指标上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通事故责任分析方法效率低、主观性强、结果不一致；大语言模型受限于噪声视频输入和缺乏法律领域知识，难以生成可靠的责任分析报告。

Method: 首先使用视觉语言模型（VLM）将事故视频转化为结构化文本描述作为检索查询；然后采用 BM25 稀疏检索与稠密嵌入检索相结合的混合检索策略，获取相关交通法规和相似历史案例；最后 LLM 结合检索到的法律知识与多模态事故证据进行综合推理，生成标准化的责任分析报告。

Result: TrafficRAG 在法律规范适应性（77.32%）、事实忠实性（81.71%）和责任比例 MAE（5.48%）等指标上一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过检索增强将多模态事实证据与法律条款相结合，可以有效提升交通事故责任判定的可靠性和准确性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TrafficRAG，一个多模态检索增强框架（RAG），用于交通事故责任判定。它使用 VLM 生成结构化文本描述作为检索查询，结合 BM25 和稠密检索获取交通法规和历史案例，再由 LLM 进行推理。核心是多模态检索增强生成，属于多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on legal RAG for traffic accidents, far from user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Multimodal RAG, traffic accident liability, legal domain, BM25+ dense retrieval, VLM for structured queries

Abstract: Traffic accident liability analysis is a critical yet challenging task in intelligent transportation and legal assistance. Existing methods often suffer from low efficiency, subjective judgment, and inconsistent analysis results. Meanwhile, large language models are constrained by noisy video inputs and insufficient legal domain knowledge. To address these issues, this work presents TrafficRAG, a multimodal retrieval-augmented framework for automated traffic accident analysis and report generation. Specifically, the proposed framework first adopts a vision-language model to produce structured textual descriptions of accident scenarios, which serve as accurate retrieval queries. Based on these textual queries, a hybrid retrieval strategy integrating BM25 sparse retrieval and dense embedding retrieval is employed to fetch relevant traffic regulations and similar historical cases. Finally, the large language model incorporates retrieved legal knowledge and multimodal accident evidence for comprehensive reasoning, and generates standardized, legally grounded liability analysis reports. Extensive experiments show that TrafficRAG consistently outperforms baseline methods, achieving 77.32% Legal Norm Adaptation Accuracy, 81.71% Factual Faithfulness, and a Liability Ratio MAE of 5.48%. The results validate that integrating multimodal factual evidence with legal clauses via retrieval augmentation can effectively improve the reliability and accuracy of traffic accident liability determination.

</details>


### [223] [Dual-Route Top-K Retrieval with 1v1 VLM Reranking for the CoVR-R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01097)
*Yuyang Sun, Yongliang Wu, Xingyu Zhu, Yuxia Chen, Zhenxiang Jiang, Yangguang Ji, Wenbo Zhu, Yanxi Shi, Jay Wu, Shuo Wang, Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双路 Top-K 检索结合 1v1 VLM 重排序的方法，用于组合视频检索（CoVR-R）挑战。方法将任务解耦为候选集构建和候选替换决策两个子问题，通过 VLM slot selector 改进文本种子、DFN 视觉嵌入构建视觉路线，合并后使用 VLM 进行保守的 1v1 重排序，在隐藏测试集上达到 95.28 R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索（CoVR-R）任务需要根据参考视频和文本修改描述检索目标视频，现有方法在候选集召回和最终排序之间存在耦合问题，难以同时保证高召回率和精确排序。

Method: 方法包含两条路线：(1) 文本路线：使用 VLM slot selector 从现有候选中提取关键信息改进文本种子；(2) 视觉路线：使用 DFN-H/DFN-L 提取 contact-sheet 嵌入。两条路线合并为 top-10 候选集后，使用 VLM 进行保守的 1v1 比较（当前 top-1 与每个挑战者逐一对比），决定是否替换。

Result: 在隐藏测试集上，R@1 达到 95.28，R@5 达到 97.47，R@10 达到 98.48，R@50 达到 99.66。

Conclusion: CoVR-R 任务中，召回-选择解耦（recall-selection decoupling）比广泛的文本重排序或直接多候选 VLM 分类更有效。

Classification reason: 该论文解决的是组合视频检索（Composed Video Retrieval, CoVR-R）任务，属于跨模态/组合检索范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific video retrieval reranking task with limited connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: composed video retrieval, dual-route retrieval, VLM reranking, CoVR-R challenge, recall-selection decoupling

Abstract: We describe \emph{Dual-Route Top-K Retrieval with 1v1 VLM Reranking} for the CoVR-R challenge. The method treats composed video retrieval as two coupled problems: finding a sufficiently complete top-k candidate set, and then safely deciding whether any candidate should replace a strong current top-1. We first improve the reasoning/text seed with a VLM slot selector over existing candidates, without introducing DFN visual retrieval. We then add a visual route from contact-sheet embeddings using DFN-H/DFN-L. The routes are merged into a top-10 candidate set, after which a VLM final reranker performs conservative 1v1 comparisons between the current top-1 and each challenger. On the hidden test split, the final system reaches 95.28 R@1, 97.47 R@5, 98.48 R@10, and 99.66 R@50. The main lesson is that CoVR-R benefits more from recall-selection decoupling than from broad text reranking or direct multi-candidate VLM classification.

</details>


### [224] [HOLA: Holistic Multi-Modal Alignment for Open-Set 3D Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01334)
*Koby Aharonov, Oren Shrout, Ayellet Tal*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HOLA 提出了一种整体多模态对齐方法用于开放集3D识别，通过解耦多正例对比损失将每个点云与多视角图像和多文本描述对齐，在长尾基准上取得SOTA零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集3D识别方法通常将每个点云与单张图像或单条文本对齐，导致表征局限于部分视角，无法捕捉3D物体的整体理解。

Method: 提出解耦多正例对比损失（decoupled multi-positive contrastive loss），将点云与多视角图像和多文本描述联合对齐，同时分离正例聚合与负例竞争；并设计轻量文本适配器减少网络文本与人工标注之间的域差距。

Result: 在长尾开放词汇基准上取得SOTA零样本性能，同时保持高帧率。

Conclusion: 多视角多文本的对齐策略能显著提升3D开放集识别的泛化能力，解耦对比损失有效避免了多正例场景下的"聚光灯拥挤"问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态对齐方法用于开放集3D识别，核心是跨模态（点云-图像-文本）对比学习对齐，属于多模态检索/跨模态对齐范畴，映射到现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"。

Importance reason: Strong open-set 3D recognition method but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: open-set 3D recognition, multi-modal alignment, decoupled multi-positive contrastive loss, zero-shot long-tail benchmarks, lightweight text adapter

Abstract: Open-set 3D recognition requires models that generalize to rare or unseen categories. Recent approaches address this by distilling language-vision knowledge into 3D encoders, typically relying on heavy 2D ViTs and aligning each point cloud with a single image or caption, thus anchoring representations to partial views. We propose aligning each point cloud with multiple images and textual descriptions to capture a more holistic understanding of 3D objects. To realize this idea, it is essential to design a loss function capable of jointly aligning a 3D instance with multiple matched signals, multi-view images and multiple texts, while separating positive aggregation from negative competition. We introduce such a function, termed the decoupled multi-positive contrastive loss. Our formulation enhances the loss's hardness-aware focus on challenging negatives, avoiding the "spotlight crowding" that occurs when many positives share the same softmax with all the negatives. Complementing this, we present a lightweight text adapter applied only to web captions, reducing the domain gap to curated annotations and enabling effective use of large-scale unsupervised text. Our model demonstrates state-of-the-art open-vocabulary performance on long-tail benchmarks, yielding substantial zero-shot improvements while sustaining high frame rates.

</details>


### [225] [Training-Free Composed Video Retrieval via Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02321)
*Yang Liu, Qianqian Xu, Peisong Wen, Siran Dai, Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的组合视频检索框架，利用冻结的 DINOv3 模型进行视觉候选筛选，再通过视频大语言模型进行推理评估，在 CVPR 2026 挑战赛上取得 48.78% Recall@1 和 51.48% Recall@5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频检索主要基于文本查询，但用户有时需要通过视觉示例（参考视频）加文本指令来指定检索目标。组合视频检索（Composed Video Retrieval）场景需要同时理解视觉参考和修改指令，现有方法通常需要大量训练数据，本文旨在探索无需训练的解决方案。

Method: 提出 Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning 框架：1) 使用冻结的 DINOv3 模型提取视觉表示，从候选库中筛选出紧凑的视觉相关候选集；2) 利用视频大语言模型（Video-LLM）评估每个候选是否满足修改指令；3) 对 top 候选进行基于推理的精炼，提升首位预测准确率。整个流程无需训练。

Result: 在 CVPR 2026 Reason-Aware Composed Video Retrieval Challenge 测试集上，Recall@1 达到 48.78%，Recall@5 达到 51.48%。

Conclusion: 无需训练的组合视频检索框架是可行的，通过视觉表示筛选与 LLM 推理相结合可以有效完成该任务。未来可通过更强的视频-LLM 以及视觉表示与语言推理的更深度融合进一步提升检索精度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究组合视频检索（Composed Video Retrieval），属于跨模态/多模态检索任务，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on composed video retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic, and uses training-free methods that lack novelty in the user's key areas. Signals: composed video retrieval, training-free, DINOv3, video-LLM reasoning, CVPR challenge

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models have expanded video retrieval from simple text-based search to more flexible scenarios, where users may specify the desired result through both visual examples and textual instructions. In the CVPR 2026 Reason-Aware Composed Video Retrieval Challenge, the system is required to retrieve a target video according to a reference video and a modification instruction. To address this task, we develop Visual Representation-Guided Video-LLM Reasoning for Training-Free Composed Video Retrieval. Our framework first uses frozen DINOv3 models to obtain a compact set of visually relevant candidates, and then applies large vision-language models to evaluate whether each candidate satisfies the modification instruction. A final reasoning-based refinement is further performed on the top candidates to improve the first-ranked prediction. Without training, our system achieves 48.78 Recall@1 and 51.48 Recall@5 on the test set. Future work may further improve retrieval accuracy through stronger video-LLMs and detailed integration between visual representations and language reasoning.

</details>


### [226] [Multi-modal Video Representation Alignment for Robust Self-supervised Driver Distraction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02352)
*David J. Lerch, Livien Majer, Zeyun Zhong, Manuel Martin, Frederik Diederichs, Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督多模态全局对齐框架，通过软目标（cycle-consistency）和加权机制处理多模态视频中的噪声负样本和不可靠正样本，在 Drive&Act 驾驶员分心检测数据集上优于现有方法，并展现出跨视角泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对比学习目标（如 InfoNCE）假设所有负样本同等信息量、所有正样本可靠，但在多模态视频数据中，由于视角变化、遮挡或跨模态语义重叠，这一假设常被违反。现有方法缺乏对噪声负样本和不可靠正样本的联合建模。

Method: 提出多模态全局对齐框架：1）利用 cycle-consistency 分数生成软目标，放松硬负样本假设；2）基于相似度分布设计加权机制，缓解噪声正样本影响；3）将传统成对对齐扩展到全局多模态设置，聚合所有模态对的对齐信息。

Result: 在 Drive&Act 数据集上，该方法在 RGB、IR、Depth、Skeleton 四种模态上一致优于成对对齐和现有全局对齐基线；跨视角消融实验表明模型对未见相机视角具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为自监督全局多模态表征学习提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案，能够实现可靠的驾驶员分心检测，并开创了真实世界多模态视频理解的新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态视频表征的对齐学习，属于多模态表征学习与跨模态对齐范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）中的跨模态表征对齐方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on driver distraction detection, which is outside your priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords like autoregressive or world model. Signals: self-supervised multi-modal alignment, driver distraction detection, cycle-consistency, cross-view generalization, Drive&Act dataset

Abstract: Robust self-supervised learning of multi-modal video representations is critical for real-world applications such as driver distraction detection, where multiple sensors provide complementary but noisy signals. Conventional contrastive objectives, such as InfoNCE, assume all negatives are equally informative and all positives are reliable. However, this assumption is frequently violated in multi-modal data due to viewpoint changes, occlusions, or semantic overlap across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel framework for multi-modal global alignment that addresses these challenges by jointly modeling faulty negatives and unreliable or faulty positives. We introduce soft targets derived from cycle-consistency scores to relax the hard-negative assumption, and a weighting mechanism based on similarity distributions to mitigate the impact of noisy or faulty positives. Our approach extends traditional pairwise alignment to a principled global multi-modal setting, aggregating alignment information across all modality pairs. We evaluate our method on the Drive&Act dataset, demonstrating that it consistently outperforms both pairwise and existing global alignment baselines across RGB, IR, Depth, and Skeleton modalities. Cross-view ablation studies further show strong generalization to unseen camera perspectives, highlighting the robustness of our representations. Overall, our framework provides a scalable and effective solution for self-supervised global multi-modal representation learning, enabling reliable driver distraction detection and pioneering in real-world multi-modal video understanding. Our code will be published on GitHub.

</details>


### [227] [Reason-Then-Retrieve for CoVR-R with Structured Edit Prompts and Dense-Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02450)
*DongQing Liu, MengShi Qi, HongWei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (48.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本的"先推理后检索"流水线用于组合视频检索（CoVR-R）。利用 Qwen3.5-27B 对参考视频和编辑指令进行编辑推理，生成目标视频的结构化描述，并基于生成 token 的隐藏状态构建稠密嵌入，同时结合 TF-IDF 稀疏检索分支进行稠密-稀疏融合排序，在验证集和测试集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索（CoVR-R）任务中，目标视频并非直接描述，而是需要通过参考视频和编辑指令推理出细粒度的变化（如物体身份、动作顺序、最终状态、手部交互、场景转换等），现有方法难以有效处理这种推理需求。

Method: 构建零样本"推理-检索"流水线：1）使用 Qwen3.5-27B 对参考视频和编辑指令进行编辑推理，生成目标视频的结构化描述；2）对每个图库视频生成检索导向的结构化描述和稠密嵌入（通过对生成 token 的隐藏状态进行 token 依赖加权池化）；3）对查询端，模型先进行编辑推理，再生成目标视频描述，其隐藏状态作为查询嵌入；4）引入 TF-IDF 稀疏检索分支与稠密检索分支进行融合排序，不同数据划分使用不同的融合权重。

Result: 在验证集上达到 R@1 80.81、R@5 94.86、R@10 97.11、R@50 98.59；在盲测集上达到 R@1 89.73、R@5 95.79、R@10 96.63、R@50 97.98，均为当前最佳提交结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的零样本推理-检索流水线在 CoVR-R 任务上取得了显著效果，验证了利用大语言模型进行编辑推理并结合稠密-稀疏融合检索的有效性，为组合视频检索提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究的是基于推理的组合视频检索（CoVR-R），属于多模态检索范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow video retrieval task with no connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: composed video retrieval, zero-shot pipeline, dense-sparse fusion, no generative modeling, no continuous representation

Abstract: CoVR-R studies reason-aware composed video retrieval: given a reference video and an edit instruction, the system must retrieve the target video that satisfies the edit. The main difficulty is that the target is not described directly; it must be inferred from fine-grained changes in object identity, action order, final state, hand interaction, and scene transition. We build a zero-shot reason-then-retrieve pipeline around Qwen3.5-27B. For each gallery video, the model generates a retrieval-oriented structured description and a dense embedding by pooling generated-token hidden states with token-dependent weights. For each query, the model first performs edit reasoning over the reference video and instruction, then generates a target-video description whose hidden states serve as the query embedding. We complement dense retrieval with a TF-IDF branch over the generated texts and fuse the two rankings with split-specific weights. On validation, the current best submission reaches 80.81 at R@1, 94.86 at R@5, 97.11 at R@10, and 98.59 at R@50. On the blind test split, it reaches 89.73 at R@1, 95.79 at R@5, 96.63 at R@10, and 97.98 at R@50.

</details>

### [228] [HLL: Can Agents Cross Humanity's Last Line of Verification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02449)
*Xinhao Song, Su Su, Sirui Song, Hongliang Wu, Wen Shen, Zhihua Wei, Gongshen Liu, Linfeng Zhang, Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HLL（Humanity's Last Line of Verification）基准，通过交互式 CAPTCHA 验证任务评测多模态智能体能否在受保护的真实工作流中替代人类。实验表明，当前最先进的多模态智能体在定位、动作校准、状态跟踪和过程一致性方面存在显著不足，性能随验证类型和界面复杂度剧烈波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体被期望代表用户操作界面，但服务方通过 CAPTCHA 等机制刻意防止自动化。现有评测缺乏对智能体能否真正跨越"人类验证边界"的系统评估，因此需要构建一个专门测试智能体在受保护工作流中替代人类能力的基准。

Method: 构建 HLL 基准，涵盖多种 CAPTCHA 交互类型（如滑块、拼图、文字识别等），在闭环 GUI 环境中引入现实压力因素（杂乱网页、困难变体、轨迹条件验证）。评估了 8 个前沿多模态智能体在定位、动作执行、状态跟踪和过程一致性方面的表现。

Result: 当前多模态智能体在 CAPTCHA 验证任务上表现脆弱：不同验证类型间性能差异大，真实界面条件下性能下降，且当正确答案需要有效动作轨迹支持时性能进一步降低。揭示了智能体在定位、动作校准、状态跟踪和过程一致性方面的明显不足。

Conclusion: HLL 提供了一个具体的测试平台来衡量多模态智能体在受保护真实工作流中替代人类的程度。当前智能体距离真正的人类替代仍有显著差距，特别是在需要精确交互和过程一致性的场景中。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 HLL 基准，用于评测多模态智能体在 CAPTCHA 验证场景中替代人类的能力，属于多模态智能体评测，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Evaluates multimodal agents on a practical human-substitution task, but the CAPTCHA focus is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous models and generation. Signals: multimodal agent benchmark, CAPTCHA verification, human substitution, GUI interaction, process consistency

Abstract: Multimodal agents are increasingly expected to operate interfaces on behalf of users, raising a central deployment question: can they truly substitute for humans in workflows that services deliberately protect against automation? CAPTCHA verification makes this question concrete. It is not merely a visual puzzle, but a human-verification boundary placed before account creation, content access, form submission, and other protected actions. We introduce \textbf{Humanity's Last Line of Verification (HLL)}, a controlled benchmark that uses interactive CAPTCHA verification to evaluate whether agents can cross this boundary through grounded, human-like interaction rather than recognition alone. HLL covers diverse CAPTCHA interactions and exposes agents to controlled realism stressors, including cluttered webpages, harder task variants, and trace-conditioned validation of the solving process. We evaluate eight frontier multimodal agents in a closed-loop GUI environment. The results show that current agents remain brittle at this human-substitution boundary: performance varies sharply across verification types, degrades under realistic interface conditions, and drops further when correct answers must be supported by valid action traces. By exposing gaps in localization, action calibration, state tracking, and process consistency, HLL provides a concrete testbed for measuring how close multimodal agents are to acting as human substitutes in protected real-world workflows. Our code is available at

</details>


### [229] [DraDDP: A Multimodal Multi-Party Dialogue Discourse Parsing Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00012)
*Shannan Liu, Peifeng Li, Yaxin Fan, Qiaoming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个公开的英文多模态多方对话语篇解析数据集 DraDDP，基于美剧包含495个对话片段、6374个话语和9.1小时视频，并建立了多模态基准评测，实验表明多模态信息有助于捕捉对话结构和关系类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话语篇解析研究大多局限于文本模态或两人对话，无法满足多模态和多方对话场景的需求，缺乏公开的多模态多方对话语篇解析数据集。

Method: 基于美国电视剧构建多模态多方对话语篇解析数据集 DraDDP，包含495个对话片段、6374个话语和9.1小时并行视频内容。在数据集上建立多模态基准评测，分析不同模态对对话结构和关系类型识别的影响。

Result: 构建了首个公开的英文多模态多方对话语篇解析数据集，实验证明多模态信息（视频+文本）相比纯文本能更有效地捕捉对话结构和关系类型。

Conclusion: 多模态信息对多方对话语篇解析任务具有重要价值，DraDDP数据集将为多模态对话理解研究提供基础资源。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了首个面向多模态多方对话语篇解析的英文数据集 DraDDP，属于多模态理解与评测方向。该数据集包含视频+文本多模态信息，并建立了基准评测，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 子主题。

Importance reason: Dataset is for multimodal dialogue parsing, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal dialogue parsing, dataset construction, English TV drama, benchmark, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multi-party dialogue discourse parsing aims to identify dependency structures and relation types between utterances in conversations. Previous studies are mostly limited to textual modality or two-party dialogue, failing to meet the multimodal and multi-party settings. In this paper, we construct the first publicly available English multimodal dataset DraDDP for multi-party dialogue discourse parsing, based on American TV dramas. DraDDP contains 495 dialogue segments with 6,374 utterances and 9.1 hours of parallel video content, covering rich multi-party interaction scenarios. Moreover, we establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating this task on DraDDP and conducting in-depth analysis on the impact of different modalities. Experimental results demonstrate the value of multimodal information in capturing dialogue structures and relation types. We will publicly release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and code to promote future research in multimodal dialogue understanding.

</details>


### [230] [Do Text Edits Generalize to Visual Generation? Benchmarking Cross-Modal Knowledge Editing in UMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00477)
*Xin Gao, Cheng Yang, Chufan Shi, Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniKE，首个面向统一多模态模型（UMM）的跨模态知识编辑基准，包含 2971 个编辑样本。实验发现文本侧编辑成功率可达 92%，但图像生成侧 VQA 准确率仅 18.5%，揭示显著模态差距。作者提出推理增强参数编辑方法（RPE），通过显式激活编辑知识提升图像生成效果，最高提升 18.6 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMM）在实际部署中需要有效更新内部知识。虽然文本模型的知识编辑已较成熟，但文本编辑能否成功迁移到图像生成尚不清楚。现有知识编辑方法仅关注文本输出，缺乏对跨模态迁移效果的评估。

Method: 1) 构建 UniKE 基准：包含 2971 个编辑样本，覆盖属性编辑和关系编辑两种类型，使用 VQA 进行视觉验证。2) 提出推理增强参数编辑（RPE）：在生成前显式激活已编辑的知识，通过推理时干预增强编辑表征对视觉生成条件的引导。3) 进行机制分析，探究模态差距的根源。

Result: 文本侧编辑成功率约 92%，而直接图像生成下最佳 VQA 准确率仅 18.5%。RPE 方法在所有模型-编辑器组合上均提升 VQA 准确率，最高提升 18.6 个百分点。机制分析表明，模态差距与编辑后的文本表征与视觉生成条件路径之间的部分对齐有关。

Conclusion: 文本知识编辑不能保证可靠的跨模态迁移，需要模态感知的编辑方法。UniKE 基准和 RPE 方法为跨模态知识编辑提供了评估框架和初步解决方案，揭示了统一多模态模型中知识编辑的关键挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 UniKE，首个面向统一多模态模型（UMM）跨模态知识编辑的评测基准，包含 2971 个编辑样本和 VQA 验证协议，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Directly benchmarks cross-modal knowledge editing in unified multimodal models, revealing a critical modality gap that aligns with your priority on multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: First cross-modal editing benchmark for UMMs, reveals 92% text vs 18.5% image VQA accuracy, proposes RPE method with up to 18.6% gain, mechanistic analysis of modality gap, strong fit to unified multimodal and multimodal understanding_generation directions

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose multimodal intelligence. As they are deployed in real-world applications, effectively updating internal knowledge becomes critical. While knowledge editing has matured for text-only models, it remains unclear whether edits that successfully modify textual outputs also transfer to image generation in UMMs. To study this question, we introduce UniKE, the first benchmark for cross-modality knowledge editing in UMMs, comprising 2,971 edit subjects spanning attribute and relation edits. Using VQA-based visual verification, we reveal a striking modality gap: text-side efficacy can reach approximately 92%, whereas the best overall VQA accuracy under direct image generation is only 18.5%. We further propose Reasoning-augmented Parameter Editing, which explicitly activates edited knowledge before generation and improves overall VQA accuracy for all evaluated model-editor pairs, with gains up to 18.6 percentage points. Mechanistic analysis shows that this gap is associated with partial alignment between edited textual representations and the conditioning pathways for visual generation, where edits sufficient for text outputs may remain too weak or misaligned to steer image synthesis. These findings show that textual knowledge edits do not guarantee reliable cross-modality transfer and motivate modality-aware editing methods. Our code and data are available at .

</details>


### [231] [SafeVLA-Bench: A Benchmark for the Success-Safety Gap in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00773)
*Jialiang Fan, Weizhe Xu, Oleg Sokolsky, Insup Lee, Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeVLA-Bench 是一个针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的安全评测基准，通过信号时序逻辑（STL）形式化任务相关的安全约束，并引入两个不安全成功指标（SBU 和 VSI），揭示高任务成功率下隐藏的安全违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 基准仅以任务成功率为指标，忽略了成功轨迹中可能存在的安全隐患（如过度接触力、干扰无关物体、物体失稳、机器人自接触等），缺乏对安全性的系统评估。

Method: 提出 SafeVLA-Bench 后验安全评估框架，将任务感知的安全需求形式化为信号时序逻辑（STL）规约，定义两个不安全成功指标：Succ-But-Unsafe (SBU) 表示成功但违反安全的轨迹比例，Violation Severity Index (VSI) 表示有界的最坏违规深度分数。在 LIBERO 和 RoboCasa-365 两个基准上实例化该框架，评估了 9 个策略模型。

Result: 高任务成功率并不等同于安全执行：桌面操作中高成功率的基线仍有 13-15% 的不安全轨迹率；RoboCasa-365 中 36-56% 的成功 rollout 违反了至少一条安全条款。

Conclusion: SafeVLA-Bench 揭示了 VLA 模型中成功与安全之间的显著差距，为未来安全感知的 VLA 策略开发提供了系统化的评估工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SafeVLA-Bench，一个针对 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的安全评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。虽然论文聚焦于机器人操作任务的安全评估，但其核心贡献是构建评测框架和基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Safety evaluation for VLA is valuable but not aligned with user's priority on generative models and continuous representations. Signals: VLA safety benchmark, STL formalization, unsafe-success metrics, robotics manipulation, not generative model focus

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) benchmarks measure whether a policy completes a requested manipulation task, but binary success can hide safety-relevant trajectory behavior: reaching the goal while applying excessive contact, disturbing bystander objects, destabilizing the held object, or entering robot self-contact. We present SafeVLA-Bench, a post-hoc safety-evaluation framework for existing simulator-based VLA benchmarks. It formalizes task-aware safety requirements as Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and reports native success with two unsafe-success metrics: Succ-But-Unsafe (SBU), the fraction of rollouts that both succeed and violate safety, and Violation Severity Index (VSI), a bounded worst-violation depth score. We instantiate SafeVLA-Bench on LIBERO and RoboCasa-365, evaluating nine policy-benchmark entries across tabletop and kitchen manipulation tasks. High task success does not imply safe execution: high-SR tabletop baselines still leave 13 to 15 percent unsafe-episode rates,and 36 to 56 percent of successful RoboCasa-365 rollouts violate at least one active safety clause. Project page: .

</details>


### [232] [Planktonzilla: Multimodal dataset and models for understanding plankton ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00080)
*Alan Gerson Contreras Montanares, Luis Valenzuela, Luis Martí, Nayat Sanchez-Pi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.NE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Planktonzilla-17M，一个包含1740万张图像、覆盖13种成像系统、602个分类类别的统一浮游生物多模态数据集，并基于该数据集对比了监督学习与CLIP风格训练在浮游生物分类上的表现，发现监督分类器在利用分类学文本时能达到或超过CLIP训练效果，同时揭示了现有生物基础模型（BioCLIP/BioCLIP2）在海洋成像领域的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮游生物分类模型在单一数据集上表现良好，但由于训练数据孤立、标签不一致，无法跨仪器和跨环境泛化。缺乏统一的大规模数据集和标准化评测基准，阻碍了海洋生态系统监测和气候变化研究。

Method: 收集并整合公开的浮游生物图像数据集，统一分类学标签和地理环境元数据，构建 Planktonzilla-17M 数据集（1740万图像，602个分类类别）。在共享 ViT 骨干上对比监督分类训练与 CLIP 风格图文对比训练，并评估 BioCLIP/BioCLIP2 的零样本和少样本性能。

Result: Planktonzilla-17M 是目前最大最全面的浮游生物图像数据集。监督分类器在使用分类学文本作为标签时，性能达到或超过 CLIP 风格训练。BioCLIP 和 BioCLIP2 在零样本和少样本设置下对浮游生物分类表现较差。

Conclusion: 统一的大规模数据集能显著提升浮游生物分类性能，监督学习在利用分类学层级信息时仍具竞争力，而现有生物基础模型在海洋成像领域泛化能力不足，需要针对该领域进行专门训练或微调。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 Planktonzilla-17M 大规模浮游生物图像数据集（含1740万图像、602个分类类别），并进行了监督学习与CLIP风格对比实验。核心贡献是数据集与基准评测，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴。

Importance reason: Specialized marine biology dataset with no alignment to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Plankton classification dataset, CLIP vs supervised comparison, BioCLIP limitations, domain-specific marine imaging, no autoregressive or diffusion video/image generation

Abstract: Marine plankton underpin aquatic food webs and play a key role in global CO2 sequestration, making reliable species identification critical for understanding ocean health and climate feedbacks. Existing classification models perform well on individual collections but fail to generalize across instruments and environments due to isolated training datasets and inconsistent labels. To address this, we introduce Planktonzilla-17M, a unified dataset consolidating publicly available plankton image collections spanning thirteen imaging systems. It comprises 17.4 million images with standardized taxonomy and geo-environmental metadata, including 3.74 million plankton images spanning over 602 taxonomic classes, of which 201 are identified at the species level, making it the largest and most comprehensive plankton image dataset to date. Using this large-scale dataset, we perform a controlled comparison between supervised and CLIP-style image--text training on a shared ViT backbone. We find that a supervised classifier matches or exceeds CLIP-style training when trained using taxonomic lineage as text. We further observe that BioCLIP and BioCLIP2 perform poorly on plankton in zero-shot and few-shot settings. Leveraging Planktonzilla-17M improves plankton classification performance, highlighting the limitations of current biological foundation models in marine imaging domains.

</details>


### [233] [CardioLens: Revealing the Clinical Reality Gap of MLLMs via Multi-Sequence Cardiac MRI Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00123)
*Zixian Su, Hongkai Zhang, Fan Gao, Encheng Su, Taiping Qu, Jingwei Guo, Nan Zhang, Hui Wang, Zhen Zhou, Kairui Bo, Yan Chen, Yue Ren, Shuai Li, Lei Xu, Henggui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (67.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CardioLens 是一个基于私有医院数据构建的心脏多序列 MRI 评测基准，包含 47 万+切片和 1.3 万+ QA 对，覆盖图像理解、报告生成和疾病诊断三个阶段。对 24 个 SOTA MLLM 的评测揭示了显著的临床现实差距：模型整体表现差，沿真实 CMR 工作流性能递减，存在类别坍塌失败模式，且切片选择策略和显式推理提示均无法有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 MLLM 评测多基于公开数据集，任务形式简化（如孤立输入、识别式任务），无法反映真实临床场景的复杂性和数据泄露风险。心脏 MRI 涉及多序列、多视角、多时相的综合分析，现有评测无法评估模型在真实临床工作流中的表现。

Method: 从私有医院档案构建 CardioLens 基准，包含 4D Cine、LGE、灌注和 T2 加权四种序列，通过严格的报告到 QA 构建与验证流程生成 13,494 个 QA 对。设计三阶段评测（图像理解、报告生成、疾病诊断），对 24 个 SOTA MLLM 进行系统评估，并设计消融实验分析切片选择策略和推理提示的影响。

Result: MLLM 在 CardioLens 上整体表现较差，性能沿 CMR 工作流（理解→报告→诊断）递减。混淆分析显示模型存在类别坍塌失败模式，倾向于预测常见异常类别而非区分临床不同的发现。不同切片选择策略下性能变化仅约 1%，显式推理提示反而使模型更保守而非改善视觉证据利用。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLM 在心脏 MRI 临床解读上远未达到可靠水平，临床决策需要跨序列、跨视角、跨时相整合分布式证据。CardioLens 为开发面向真实临床部署的下一代 MLLM 提供了临床基础的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 CardioLens，一个针对心脏 MRI 的多模态大模型临床评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Strong medical benchmark but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical MLLM benchmark, clinical reality gap, category-collapse failure, multi-sequence MRI, private hospital data

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong performance on public medical benchmarks, yet existing evaluations often remain weak proxies for clinical use, relying on isolated inputs and simplified recognition-style tasks. We introduce CardioLens, a leakage-resistant evaluation testbed for multi-sequence Cardiovascular Magnetic Resonance (CMR), constructed from private hospital archives through a rigorous report-to-QA construction and verification pipeline. CardioLens contains 473,896 slices and 13,494 verified QA pairs across 4D Cine, LGE, perfusion, and T2-weighted imaging, and evaluates three stages of CMR interpretation: image understanding, report generation, and disease diagnosis. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, CardioLens reveals a substantial clinical reality gap: models perform poorly overall, with performance degrading along the real CMR workflow. Confusion analysis further shows a category-collapse failure mode, where models default to frequent abnormal categories rather than distinguishing clinically distinct findings. To rule out MLLM-compatible input construction as the primary cause, we compare random, clinically motivated, and data-driven slice selection protocols under different slice budgets; performance changes only marginally, typically by about 1%. Explicit reasoning prompts also fail to rescue performance, often making models more conservative rather than improving visual evidence use. These results show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable CMR interpretation, where clinical decisions require integrating distributed evidence across sequences, views, and temporal phases. CardioLens provides a clinically grounded testbed for developing next-generation MLLMs toward real-world clinical deployment.

</details>


### [234] [StemBind: When MLLMs Get Lost Between Rules and Instances in Abstract Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00148)
*Xixiang He, Baiqi Wu, Xingming Li, Ao Cheng, Qiyao Sun, Xuanyu Ji, Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (77.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StemBind 是一个共享主干的诊断性抽象视觉推理基准，通过将感知、规则和完整任务对齐到同一视觉主干上，系统性地定位 MLLM 在 AVR 中的失败环节。实验发现模型存在"规则-完整任务鸿沟"和"绑定缺口"，瓶颈在于规则到实例的映射（S3阶段），且更大的模型和思维链模式无法可靠解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AVR 基准将感知、规则归纳和答案选择压缩为单一的对错信号，无法区分模型在哪个子步骤失败。作者观察到 MLLM 经常"知道规则但选错答案"，因此需要一个能精确定位失败环节的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建 StemBind 基准：包含 2,298 个共享视觉主干的 stem，每个 stem 对应三个对齐问题——感知（P）、规则（R）和完整任务（F），共 19,533 个任务。每个完整任务还标注了 Sternberg 的四个推理阶段（S1 编码、S2 推断、S3 映射、S4 应用）。评估了 24 种前沿 MLLM 配置，并设计了阶段级刺激增强（Stage-wise Stimulus Augmentation）来定位瓶颈。

Result: (i) 22/24 模型上规则准确率高于完整任务准确率（R-F 鸿沟）；(ii) 即使 P 和 R 都正确，模型仍有 51.2% 的概率答错 F（绑定缺口）；(iii) 瓶颈在 S3 规则到实例映射阶段；(iv) 更大的模型和思维链模式无法可靠缩小差距，思维链甚至降低了规则和完整任务准确率。

Conclusion: StemBind 将 AVR 评估从最终答案排名转向定位推理断裂点，识别出规则到实例的绑定（rule-to-instance binding）是视觉推理的具体瓶颈，为未来研究提供了明确方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 StemBind，一个用于抽象视觉推理（AVR）的诊断性评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准通过共享主干设计将感知、规则归纳和答案选择解耦，以定位 MLLM 在 AVR 任务中的失败环节。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong diagnostic benchmark for MLLM reasoning but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM evaluation, abstract visual reasoning, rule-to-instance binding, diagnostic benchmark, reasoning stages

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often know the rule but pick the wrong answer: on abstract visual reasoning (AVR) tasks, a model can describe what it sees and name the underlying pattern, yet still fail to choose the matching candidate. Existing AVR benchmarks cannot detect this because they collapse perception, rule induction, and answer selection into a single right-or-wrong signal. We introduce StemBind, a shared-stem diagnostic benchmark that probes the same visual stem with three aligned questions: Perception (what is in the image), Rule (what pattern governs it), and Full (which option completes it), so a final-answer error can be attributed to a specific sub-step on the same evidence. StemBind contains 2,298 curated knowledge-light stems across nine auditable visual operations, totaling 19,533 P/R/F tasks, with each full item annotated by Sternberg's four reasoning stages (S1 Encode, S2 Infer, S3 Map, S4 Apply). Evaluating 24 frontier MLLM configurations yields four findings. (i) The R-F chasm: rule accuracy exceeds full-item accuracy on 22 of 24 models, so most failures happen after the rule is identified. (ii) A persistent binding gap: even when P and R are both correct on the same stem, models still answer F incorrectly 51.2% of the time. (iii) The bottleneck is S3: process diagnostics and Stage-wise Stimulus Augmentation localize the dominant failure to rule-to-instance mapping. (iv) Scaling and thinking do not help: neither larger models nor explicit thinking mode reliably closes the gap, and thinking even lowers rule and full-item accuracy. StemBind reframes AVR evaluation from final-answer ranking to locating where abstract visual reasoning breaks down, identifying rule-to-instance binding as a concrete next target for vision-grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [235] [PolySpeech-100: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Speech Understanding Across 100+ Languages and Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01016)
*Sicheng Yang, Shulan Ruan, Shiwei Wu, Yu Liu, Lu Fan, Zhi Li, You He*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PolySpeech-100 是一个覆盖 110 种语言变体的大规模语音理解基准，通过混合构建管道（人工录音+指令驱动合成语音）覆盖 19 种中文方言和 80+ 低资源语言。评估 22 个 SOTA 模型发现：开源 E2E 模型在重方言上优于级联系统；开源模型在低资源语言上性能严重下降；CoT 在零样本设置下反而降低语音理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Speech-LLM 评测存在三大局限：偏向高资源语言、仅关注低级 ASR 而非语义推理、忽视区域方言。亟需一个覆盖多语言多方言、评估语义理解能力的基准。

Method: 提出混合构建管道：1) 黄金标准人工录音；2) 指令驱动的合成语音生成，覆盖 19 种中文方言和 80+ 低资源语言。构建 110 种语言变体的评测集，评估 22 个 SOTA 模型（包括 Gemini-3、GPT-Audio、Qwen2.5-Omni 等）。

Result: 1) 开源 E2E 模型在重方言上优于级联（ASR+LLM）系统，证明直接音频处理保留副语言线索和韵律特征；2) 商业模型保持鲁棒性，但开源模型在低资源语言上性能严重下降；3) 零样本 CoT 反而降低大多数模型的语音理解性能，揭示模态对齐差距。

Conclusion: PolySpeech-100 为下一代包容性、全能型 Speech-LLM 建立了严格的评测标准，揭示了当前模型在多语言方言理解和模态对齐方面的关键不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PolySpeech-100，一个覆盖 100+ 语言和方言的大规模语音理解评测基准，属于多模态（语音-语言）评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for multilingual speech understanding but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Speech-LLM evaluation, low-resource language coverage, dialect robustness, CoT degrades performance, hybrid synthetic data pipeline

Abstract: While End-to-End (E2E) Speech-Large Language Models (Speech-LLMs) are rapidly evolving, their evaluation methodologies remain limited to the era of simple transcription. Existing benchmarks suffer from three critical limitations: a pronounced bias towards high-resource languages, a focus on low-level recognition (ASR) rather than semantic reasoning, and a neglect of regional dialects. To bridge this gap, we introduce PolySpeech-100, a massive-scale benchmark designed to assess `native-level' speech comprehension across 110 linguistic variants. We employ a novel hybrid construction pipeline that augments gold-standard human recordings with instruction-driven synthetic speech, allowing us to cover 19 distinct Chinese dialects and over 80 low-resource languages. Extensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art models (including Gemini-3, GPT-Audio, and Qwen2.5-Omni) yields pivotal insights. First, we demonstrate that open-source E2E models outperform Cascade (ASR+LLM) systems on heavy dialects, proving that direct audio processing preserves critical paralinguistic cues and prosodic features (e.g., intonation, stress) that are often lost in standard transcription. Second, we reveal a significant performance gap: while commercial models maintain robustness, open-source models suffer catastrophic degradation on low-resource languages. Finally, counter-intuitively, we observe that under standard zero-shot settings, Chain-of-Thought prompting frequently degrades speech understanding performance for most evaluated models, revealing a potential modality alignment gap in current architectures. PolySpeech-100 establishes a rigorous standard for the next generation of inclusive, omni-capable Speech-LLMs. The data, demo, and code are publicly available at .

</details>


### [236] [Dr. DocBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Expert-Level and Difficult Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01393)
*Minglai Yang, Xinyan Velocity Yu, Pengyuan Li, Xinyu Guo, Zhenting Qi, Konwoo Kim, Longtian Ye, Xiaolong Luo, Jinhe Bi, Henry Zhang, Haris Riaz, Xuan Zhang, Yunze Xiao, Bangya Liu, Tom Tang, Yunfei Zhao, Qunshu Lin, Zihan Wang, Minghao Liu, Michael Lingzhi Li, Yilun Du, Jesse Thomason, Rogerio Feris, Alex Pentland, Zexue He*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dr. DocBench，一个面向专家级困难文档解析的难度感知基准，涵盖 52 个 BISAC 学科领域、4,514 页长文档标注，包含化学公式、乐谱、复杂表格等专业内容，揭示了现有 SOTA 解析器在该基准上的显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OCR 和文档解析基准在覆盖面和难度上日益受限：多聚焦于常见文档类型或均匀采样页面，现代解析器已表现良好；缺乏对化学公式、乐谱、复杂表格、跨页布局等专家领域结构的标注。需要更具挑战性的基准来推动文档智能发展。

Method: 从大规模多语言书籍语料库出发，覆盖 52 个 BISAC 学科领域；采用基于解析器失败的采样策略，选取多个 SOTA 系统均难以处理的困难文档；构建了 4,514 页长文档（平均约 100 页）的 65k 高质量页面级和块级标注，涵盖布局、阅读顺序、层级关系和领域特定视觉内容。

Result: 对流水线解析器和通用 VLM 的评估表明，在现有基准上的强性能并不能迁移到专家级文档解析任务。分析揭示了跨学科、内容类型和结构属性的显著失败模式。

Conclusion: Dr. DocBench 可作为诊断和推进文档智能的综合性测试平台，填补了现有基准在专家级困难文档解析方面的空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向专家级困难文档解析的综合评测基准 Dr. DocBench，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Benchmark for expert document parsing, but outside user's core focus on generation and continuous models. Signals: Expert-level document parsing benchmark, 52 BISAC domains, parser-failure sampling, 4,514 annotated pages, reveals SOTA failures

Abstract: Document parsing and recognition are fundamental capabilities for vision-language models (VLMs) and document processing systems. However, existing Optical Character Recognition (OCR) and document parsing benchmarks are increasingly limited in coverage and difficulty: many focus on common document genres or uniformly sampled pages where modern parsers already perform strongly, while offering limited annotation for expert-domain structures such as chemical formula, music notation, complex tables, and cross-page layouts. We introduce Dr. DocBench, a difficulty-aware benchmark for expert-level document parsing. Built from a large-scale multilingual book corpus, Dr. DocBench spans 52 BISAC subject domains and selects challenging documents through parser-failure-based sampling, targeting cases where multiple state-of-the-art systems struggle. It contains 4,514 annotated pages from long documents averaging around 100 pages, with 65k high-quality page- and block-level annotations for layout, reading order, hierarchical relations, and domain-specific visual contents. Evaluations of pipeline-based parsers and general-purpose VLMs show that strong performance on existing benchmarks does not transfer to our expert-level document parsing. Our analysis reveals substantial failures across subjects, content types, and structural attributes, highlighting Dr. DocBench as a comprehensive testbed for diagnosing and advancing document intelligence.

</details>


### [237] [Benchmarking LLM-as-a-Judge for Long-Form Output Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01629)
*Junjie Chen, Yuxi Dong, Haitao Li, Weihang Su, Yujia Zhou, Min Zhang, Yiqun Liu, Qinyao Ai*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LongJudgeBench，一个用于系统评估 LLM-as-a-Judge 在长文本输出评测中可靠性的综合基准。实验发现当前 LLM 裁判在不同场景下表现不稳定，评分标准和参考信息虽有帮助但不足以完全解决长文本评测的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-as-a-Judge 的元评测基准主要关注短文本输出，而长文本输出评测不仅涉及长度增加，还涉及更复杂的文档级需求。长文本生成场景日益增多，但 LLM 裁判在长文本评测中的可靠性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建 LongJudgeBench 基准，覆盖多种真实场景和评判协议，系统评估多种 LLM 裁判（涵盖多个基础模型和评判设置），分析评分标准（rubrics）和参考信息对评测可靠性的影响。

Result: 当前 LLM 裁判在不同场景下表现不稳定，存在显著的可靠性差距；评分标准和参考信息有助于提升评测质量，但并非在所有情况下都足够。

Conclusion: LongJudgeBench 揭示了 LLM-as-a-Judge 在长文本评测中的可靠性挑战，为未来开发更鲁棒、上下文感知且与人类对齐的 LLM 裁判方法提供了基准支持。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 LongJudgeBench，一个用于评估 LLM-as-a-Judge 在长文本输出评测中可靠性的基准。虽然论文聚焦于 LLM 长文本评测而非多模态，但其核心是"评测与基准"这一主题，与 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）在评测方法论上高度一致，且该子主题涵盖自动评估协议。论文属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测相关工作。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation reliability, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM-as-a-judge, long-form evaluation, benchmark, reliability gap, not aligned with user's direction weights or priority subtopics

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for long-form generation, reliably evaluating long-form outputs has become a critical challenge. LLM-as-a-judge offers a scalable alternative to human evaluation, yet its reliability in long-form output evaluation remains underexamined: existing meta-evaluation benchmarks focus mainly on short-form outputs. Compared with short-form evaluation, long-form evaluation is not merely a matter of output length; it often requires judges to handle more complex document-level demands. In this work, we introduce LongJudgeBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLM judges on long-form outputs across diverse real-world scenarios and judging protocols. We systematically evaluate a broad range of LLM judges, covering multiple base models and judging settings. Our results reveal a substantial reliability gap: current LLM judges remain unstable across scenarios, and rubrics or references are helpful but not always sufficient. We hope LongJudgeBench will support future research on more robust, context-aware, and human-aligned LLM-as-a-judge methods. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [238] [Through the PRISM: Principle-Aware, Interpretable, and Multi-Scale Evaluation of Visual Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00592)
*Mona Gandhi, KJ Joseph, Srinivasan Parthasarathy, Sayan Nag*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRISM 基准，包含 10 万扰动训练样本和 1 万验证样本，用于系统评估多模态模型对视觉设计原则（可读性、对比度、对齐、重叠、连贯性）的理解。研究发现现有模型（如 Qwen-2.5-VL、GPT-4o-mini）对设计原则退化不敏感，GPT-4o 有全局意识但缺乏细粒度解耦。作者进一步提出多尺度评估框架，结合轻量评分器、指令微调 VLM 和提示方法，提供可解释的设计失败分析并指导布局改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类设计师能够整体推理多个设计原则，而机器智能通常将这些原则压缩为单一启发式评分，缺乏可解释性和诊断精度。现有多模态模型对视觉设计质量的细粒度理解能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 1) 构建 PRISM 基准：基于 Crello 数据集的专业布局，沿可读性、对比度、对齐、重叠、连贯性五个设计原则进行系统扰动，生成 10 万训练样本和 1 万验证样本。2) 提出多尺度评估框架：集成轻量评分器进行定量评估、指令微调 VLM 进行局部反馈、基于提示的方法进行全局推理，提供可解释的设计失败分析。

Result: Qwen-2.5-VL 和 GPT-4o-mini 对目标设计原则退化不敏感；GPT-4o 表现出全局意识但缺乏细粒度解耦。多尺度评估框架能够提供可解释的设计失败解释，并基于局部洞察进行针对性布局改进。

Conclusion: PRISM 基准和多尺度评估框架为构建可解释的、具备设计素养的多模态推理系统奠定了基础，揭示了当前多模态模型在视觉设计原则理解方面的不足，并为未来研究提供了评估工具和改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PRISM 基准，系统性地扰动专业布局以评估多模态模型对视觉设计原则的理解能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal evaluation for design principles, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal evaluation benchmark, Design principle understanding, Limited model sensitivity, Multi-scale evaluation framework, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Effective visual communication stems from the harmony of multiple design principles, such as readability, contrast, alignment, overlap, and coherence, which collectively govern clarity and intent of the communicator. While human designers reason holistically over these principles, machine agents typically condense them into a single heuristic score, offering limited interpretability and diagnostic precision. To address this gap, we introduce PRISM (PRinciple-aware, Interpretable, and Structure-guided Design Modifications), a benchmark that systematically perturbs professional layouts from the Crello dataset along measurable design principles. The benchmark comprises 100K perturbed training samples and 10K perturbed validation designs, each isolating a specific principle violation for controlled analysis of multimodal reasoning about design quality. We show that models like Qwen-2.5-VL and GPT-4o-mini are largely insensitive to targeted principle degradations, whereas GPT-4o exhibits global awareness without fine-grained disentanglement. Building on these insights, we propose a multi-scale evaluation framework that integrates lightweight scorers for quantitative assessment, instruction-tuned vision-language models for localised feedback, and prompt-based methods for global reasoning. Our framework provides interpretable explanations of design failures. Using these localised insights, we show targeted refinements that improve layout quality. Together, PRISM and our framework lay the foundation for interpretable design-literate multimodal reasoning systems.

</details>


### [239] [What to Format and How: A Benchmark and Workflow Approach for Document Formatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01936)
*Shihao Rao, Liang Li, Jiapeng Liu, Tong Lin, Bing Li, Xiyan Gao, Peng Fu, Jing Huang, Can Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DocFormBench 基准和 DocFormFlow 工作流方法，用于解决文档格式化中的内容感知目标定位与修改执行问题。DocFormBench 扩展了文本到格式的评估场景，DocFormFlow 将格式化过程解耦为"格式化什么"和"如何格式化"两个阶段，在多个 LLM 和多模态模型上验证了其提升准确率并降低 token 消耗的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在文档格式化方面虽有进展，但真实场景中的格式化往往需要根据文档内容识别格式化目标（内容感知），这一设定缺乏专门的评测基准，且现有方法存在冗余文档阅读问题。

Method: 提出 DocFormBench 基准，涵盖多种格式化需求并设计准确率和效率指标；提出 DocFormFlow 工作流方法，将格式化过程解耦为"what to format"（目标定位）和"how to format"（修改执行）两个阶段，减少冗余阅读。

Result: 在多个 LLM 和多模态模型上的实验表明，DocFormFlow 相比基线方法一致提升了格式化准确率，同时降低了 token 消耗。进一步分析表明，精确的目标定位是影响格式化性能的主要因素。

Conclusion: DocFormBench 和 DocFormFlow 为更智能、更可靠的文档格式化研究提供了基准和方法基础，揭示了目标定位在格式化任务中的关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DocFormBench（文档格式化评测基准）和 DocFormFlow（工作流方法），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题，因为其核心贡献之一是构建了面向文档格式化的评测基准和评估指标。

Importance reason: Focuses on document formatting, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Document formatting benchmark, Workflow decoupling, Multimodal evaluation, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have opened up new possibilities for automated document formatting. However, real-world formatting often requires identifying targets based on document content. This content-aware setting remains challenging and underexplored, primarily due to the lack of dedicated evaluation enable evaluation in realistic content-aware scenarios, we introduce DocFormBench, a benchmark that extends Text-to-Format evaluation to diverse formatting requirements, along with metrics for both accuracy and mitigate redundant document reading in existing methods during formatting, we propose DocFormFlow, a workflow formatting method that decouples target localization from modification execution into what to format and how. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and multimodal models show that DocFormFlow consistently improves formatting accuracy while reducing token consumption compared to representative baselines. Further analysis reveals that precise target localization is the primary factor influencing formatting performance. We hope DocFormBench and DocFormFlow will facilitate future research toward more intelligent and reliable document formatting.

</details>


### [240] [SuperMemory-VQA: An Egocentric Visual Question-Answering Benchmark for Long-Horizon Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00825)
*Samiul Alam, Shakhrul Iman Siam, Michael J. Proulx, James Fort, Richard Newcombe, Hyo Jin Kim, Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MA, cs.ET, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SuperMemory-VQA 是一个面向 AI 眼镜的第一视角长时程记忆 VQA 基准数据集，包含 52.9 小时日常活动记录（RGB、音频、眼动、IMU、SLAM）和 4,853 个人工验证的问答对，涵盖物体/位置记忆、意图回忆、场景回忆、时间线重建、对话记忆和上下文检索等任务，并包含"不可回答"选项以测试幻觉鲁棒性。评测表明现有系统在真实记忆任务上远不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一视角数据集主要关注短片段的行为识别或通用问答，评测的是感知能力而非人类真实的记忆需求。AI 眼镜作为个性化记忆助手需要处理长时程视频流中的记忆缺口，但缺乏相应的评测基准。

Method: 收集了 52.9 小时 AI 眼镜日常活动视频（含 RGB、音频转录、眼动、IMU、SLAM 轨迹），通过人工验证的标注流程构建了 4,853 个多项选择问答对，涵盖 6 类记忆任务（物体/位置记忆、意图回忆、视觉场景回忆、时间线重建、对话记忆、上下文检索），并设置"不可回答"选项。评测了多种智能体框架和 LLM 骨干模型。

Result: 现有领先的智能体框架和 LLM 骨干在真实世界记忆任务上表现远不可靠，尤其在需要长时程跨片段检索和推理的任务上性能显著下降。参与者调查表明问题设计符合日常记忆需求。

Conclusion: 现有 AI 系统在长时程第一视角记忆任务上远未达到可靠水平，需要新的架构来实现有依据的 AI 记忆，仅在证据充分时才能回答。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向长时程第一视角视频记忆的VQA评测基准（SuperMemory-VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测数据集和基准。

Importance reason: Benchmark for long-horizon memory in egocentric VQA, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: egocentric VQA benchmark, long-horizon memory, AI glasses, hallucination robustness, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: AI glasses present a compelling platform for AI agents to serve as personalized memory assistants. To be genuinely useful, such systems must move beyond short-term video comprehension and address memory gaps that humans experience for practical, personal, or social purposes over longitudinal egocentric video streams. However, existing egocentric datasets predominantly focus on action recognition or generic QAs from short clips, measuring perceptual capabilities rather than realistic human memory needs. We introduce SuperMemory-VQA, an egocentric visual question answering (VQA) dataset for evaluating AI assistants on practical, long-horizon memory tasks. It contains 52.9 hours of everyday activities recorded with AI glasses, including synchronized RGB video, audio transcription, eye gaze, IMU, and SLAM trajectories. Through a human-verified annotation pipeline, we construct grounded 4,853 question-answer pairs that span object and location memory, intent recall, visual scene recall, timeline reconstruction, conversational memory, and in-context retrieval. Each question is posed as multiple-choice with an explicit "unanswerable" option to test hallucination robustness. Benchmarking leading agentic frameworks and LLM backbones reveals that existing systems remain far from reliable on real-world memory tasks, highlighting the need for new architectures for grounded AI memory that can answer only when evidence is sufficient. A participant survey further supports that our questions are realistic, useful, and aligned with everyday memory needs.

</details>


### [241] [RoboStressBench: Benchmarking VLM Robustness to Physical Visual Stress in Embodied Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00828)
*Leyi Wu, Yifan Zhao, Jinjie Zhang, Suzeyu Chen, Wosong Chen, Zhifei Chen, Tianshuo Xu, Qingchun He, Hongxin Hu, Haojian Huang, Yangkai Wei, Wenqian Li, Yinchuan Li, Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RoboStressBench 是一个基于逆图形学与物理渲染方程设计的基准，将具身场景中的视觉压力分解为材质、视角、光照、几何四个物理维度，系统评估 VLM 在真实物理压力下的视觉识别、推理和规划能力，并引入压力感知的智能体求解器来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测基准多使用干净图像或孤立扰动，未能覆盖真实物理场景中由材质、视角、光照、几何等因素引起的多样化视觉压力，缺乏对物理视觉压力的系统性定义和评测框架。

Method: 从逆图形学视角出发，受物理渲染方程启发，将视觉压力分解为四个物理维度：材质（M）、视角（V）、光照（L）、几何（G），构建 RoboStressBench 基准。通过控制变量实验评估 VLM 在各维度压力下的识别、推理和规划能力，并提出压力感知智能体求解器，先检测视觉压力源再调用视觉编辑技能进行预处理，最后进行推理。

Result: 对多个 SOTA VLM 的评估揭示了不同物理压力维度对不同具身能力的差异化退化模式，这些模式在聚合准确率中往往被掩盖。压力感知智能体求解器在高压力场景下有效提升了 VLM 的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RoboStressBench 提供了一个原则性的评测框架，用于诊断和提升 VLM 在真实物理压力下的感知能力，有助于开发更可靠的具身 AI 系统。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RoboStressBench，一个评估 VLM 在具身场景中面对物理视觉压力（材质、视角、光照、几何）的鲁棒性的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_benchmark"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM robustness benchmarking, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: VLM robustness, physical visual stress, embodied scenes, benchmark, inverse graphics

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong visual understanding and are increasingly deployed in embodied AI systems, where reliable perception under real conditions is essential. However, existing benchmarks assess VLMs using clean images or isolated perturbations rather than stresses caused by physical scene formation. This design has two limitations: it covers only a narrow subset of everyday visual stresses, and some perturbations rarely appear in realistic embodied scenes. This gap raises a fundamental question: how can we define visual stress in a principled way that captures the diverse factors encountered in physical environments? To address this question, we formulate visual perception from an inverse graphics perspective and introduce RoboStressBench, a benchmark for evaluating VLM robustness to physical visual stress in embodied scenes. Inspired by the physical rendering equation, RoboStressBench decomposes visual stress into four physically grounded dimensions: Material (M), Viewpoint (V), Lighting (L), and Geometry (G). This design enables RoboStressBench to cover a broad range of visual stresses in real-world environments, while allowing controlled analysis of their effects on VLM capabilities such as visual recognition, reasoning, and planning. Through comprehensive evaluations of state-of-the-art VLMs, we identify stress-specific failure modes and reveal that different physical factors degrade different embodied capabilities, which are often obscured by aggregate accuracy. We further introduce a stress-aware agentic solver that detects visual stressors and invokes visual-editing skills before reasoning, improving robustness in high-stress scenarios. Overall, RoboStressBench provides a principled evaluation framework for diagnosing and improving VLM perception under real-world physical stress, supporting the development of more reliable embodied AI systems.

</details>


### [242] [Benchmarks for Vision-Language Models in Urban Perception Should Be Reliability-Aware and Negotiated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00871)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文论证了面向城市感知任务的VLM基准评测应将标注者间不一致性和弃权视为测量结果，报告标注者间信度与模型对齐度，并将标签空间和评分策略视为可协商的构件。基于蒙特利尔100个街景、30个维度、12名参与者的标注数据，对7个VLM进行了零样本评估，发现模型与人类共识的一致性随维度级人类信度变化，且存在分布不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM越来越多地用于街道级图像的结构化描述（如街景审计、制图、公众咨询），但这些任务涉及主观判断和标注者间分歧。现有基准未充分考虑标注不一致和弃权响应，导致评测结果不可靠，可能误导城市治理决策。

Method: 构建了蒙特利尔100个街景图像在30个维度上的标注数据集，由12名来自7个社区组织的参与者标注。采用确定性零样本评估方法，对7个VLM进行评测，分析模型与人类共识的一致性、标注者间信度以及分布匹配情况。

Result: 模型与人类共识的一致性随维度级人类信度变化；在"总体印象"维度上，模型与标注者存在分布不匹配，包括"不适用"（Not applicable）率的不同。

Conclusion: 面向城市感知的VLM基准评测应将不确定性和基准假设在评测报告中透明化，将标注不一致和弃权作为测量结果处理，并建议基准创建者、模型开发者和机构采取相应行动。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向城市感知场景的VLM评测基准，关注标注者间不一致性、弃权响应等可靠性问题，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on urban perception VLM benchmarks, not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: VLM benchmark, urban perception, inter-annotator reliability, distributional mismatch, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to generate structured descriptions of street-level imagery for tasks such as streetscape auditing, mapping, and public consultation. These uses combine observable attributes with appraisal categories, and the human targets are often distributions of judgments with disagreement and explicit non-response. This paper argues that benchmarking VLMs for urban perception should treat disagreement and abstention as measurement outcomes, report inter-annotator reliability alongside model alignment, and treat the label space and scoring policy as negotiable artifacts when outputs are intended to inform urban governance. We ground the argument in a benchmark of 100 Montreal street scenes annotated along 30 dimensions by 12 participants from seven community organizations, and in a deterministic zero-shot evaluation of seven VLMs. Across dimensions, model agreement with human consensus co-varies with dimension-level human reliability, and for the appraisal dimension Overall Impression models and annotators exhibit distributional mismatch including different rates of Not applicable. We close with actions for benchmark creators, model developers, and institutions to make uncertainty and benchmark assumptions visible in evaluation reports.

</details>


### [243] [MMDG-Bench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00891)
*Qianshan Zhan, Qian Wang, Da Li, Xiao-Jun Zeng, Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMDG-Bench是一个面向多模态领域泛化（MMDG）的综合评测基准，提出了D2M（先领域泛化后多模态学习）和M2D（先多模态学习后领域泛化）两种框架，统一了视频-音频-光流动作识别和RGB-Depth-IR人脸防伪等任务的评估协议，通过10个基线实验验证了结构化组合优于现有方法，并揭示了模态稳定性与框架选择的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习（MML）和领域泛化（DG）各自取得了显著进展，但两者的系统集成（即多模态领域泛化MMDG）研究不足。现有MMDG研究局限于动作识别任务，缺乏标准化的评估协议和统一基准，阻碍了该领域的系统发展。

Method: 提出了MMDG-Bench基准，包含D2M（先DG后MML）和M2D（先MML后DG）两种框架，统一了视频-音频-光流动作识别和RGB-Depth-IR人脸防伪等任务的实验协议。通过将统一MML配置与5种DG技术配对，实例化了10个MMDG基线，并在多种骨干网络上进行系统评估。

Result: 结构化组合（D2M/M2D+DG技术）在多个任务上持续优于现有SOTA方法。关键发现：(1) DG技术在不同骨干网络上提供一致的泛化提升，而非DG方法对骨干网络变化高度敏感；(2) 当模态关系跨域稳定时D2M更优，反之M2D更鲁棒；(3) 更强的骨干网络在结构化框架中带来更大的性能增益。

Conclusion: MMDG-Bench为多模态鲁棒性研究提供了原则性基础和可操作的设计指南，强调了统一基准的必要性，并为未来MMDG研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MMDG-Bench，一个面向多模态领域泛化的综合评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Provides a structured benchmark for multimodal domain generalization, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal domain generalization benchmark, D2M and M2D frameworks, Action recognition and face anti-spoofing, Modality stability analysis, No direct link to continuous language or video generation

Abstract: Multi-modal Domain Generalization (MMDG) seeks to leverage complementary modalities to enhance model robustness on unseen domains. Despite extensive progress in Multi-modal Learning (MML) and Domain Generalization (DG) as individual fields, their systematic integration remains under-explored. Current MMDG research is largely confined to action recognition and lacks standardized evaluation protocols. To address this, we introduce MMDG-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring two foundational frameworks: DG then MML (D2M) and MML then DG (M2D). We provide unified experimental protocols across diverse tasks, including video-audio-flow action recognition and RGB-Depth-IR face anti-spoofing. By instantiating ten MMDG baselines through pairing a unified MML configuration with five DG techniques under both D2M and M2D orderings, we demonstrate that these structured combinations frequently outperform existing state-of-the-art methods, underscoring the necessity of a unified benchmarking effort. Our analysis yields three key insights: (1) Integrating DG techniques provides consistent generalization gains across various backbones, whereas non-DG methods are highly sensitive to backbone shifts; (2) The optimal framework choice depends on inter-modal stability: D2M excels when modal relations are stable across domains, while M2D is more robust to cross-domain relational variance; (3) Stronger backbones yield amplified performance dividends when integrated into our structured frameworks. MMDG-Bench provides a principled foundation and actionable design guidelines for future research in multi-modal robustness. Code is released at .

</details>


### [244] [PaSBench-Video: A Streaming Video Benchmark for Proactive Safety Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02443)
*Yusong Zhao, Yuejin Xie, Youliang Yuan, Junjie Hu, Jitian Guo, Yujiu Yang, Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PaSBench-Video 是一个包含740个视频的流式安全预警评测基准，覆盖驾驶、医疗、日常生活和工业生产四个领域，要求多模态大模型在危险发生前及时发出预警。测试13个模型发现，最严格指标下无模型超过20%，且召回率与误报率高度相关（Pearson 0.64），模型依赖场景级活动线索而非推理潜在危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法测试多模态大模型在危险发生前的预警能力：它们依赖静态输入、忽略时间精度、且未包含安全场景的误报测量。实际应用中，从危险信号出现到事故发生之间存在可干预的时间窗口，需要模型能在此窗口内发出及时且准确的预警。

Method: 构建 PaSBench-Video 基准，包含740个视频（481个风险视频和259个无风险视频），覆盖驾驶、医疗、日常生活和工业生产四个领域。风险视频标注了帧级风险起始边界和事故边界。评估协议要求模型以因果方式观察视频，并生成时间校准且内容正确的预警。

Result: 测试13个MLLM，最严格指标下无模型超过20%。召回率与误报率Pearson相关系数为0.64，即更高的检测率必然以在大多数安全片段上触发预警为代价。不同领域表现差异显著：日常生活领域（风险本质上是异常的）模型在低误报率下达到中等召回率；驾驶领域（常规与危险场景相似）模型则不加区分地触发预警。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型依赖场景级活动线索而非推理潜在危害来进行安全预警，导致检测率与误报率之间存在强耦合。PaSBench-Video 揭示了现有模型在时序安全预警任务上的严重不足，为未来研究提供了新的评测方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向视频流的安全预警评测基准 PaSBench-Video，属于多模态大模型在安全场景下的评测，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety warning benchmark, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Safety benchmark, MLLM temporal warning, high false-positive correlation, domain performance gap, no model exceeds 20%

Abstract: Between the first visible sign of danger and the moment an accident occurs, there is often a window where intervention remains possible. Video-capable multimodal large language models (MLLMs) could serve as always-on safety monitors that issue warnings during this window. Yet current benchmarks do not test this ability: they rely on static inputs, ignore timing precision, and omit false-positive measurement on safe scenes. We present PaSBench-Video, a 740-video benchmark with 481 risk and 259 no-risk videos across four domains: driving, healthcare, daily life, and industrial production. Risk videos are annotated with frame-level risk onset and accident boundaries. A model must observe the video causally and produce a warning that is both temporally calibrated and content-correct. Testing 13 MLLMs, we find that no model exceeds 20.0% on our strictest metric, and recall is tightly coupled with false-positive rate, with Pearson correlation 0.64: higher detection comes only at the cost of triggering warnings on the majority of safe clips. Performance splits sharply by domain: models achieve moderate recall at low false-positive rates in daily life, where risks are inherently anomalous, yet fire indiscriminately in driving, where routine and hazardous scenes look alike. These results indicate that current models rely on scene-level activity cues rather than reasoning about emerging harm.

</details>


### [245] [FigSIM: A Dataset for Fine-grained Suicide Severity and Figurative Language in Suicide Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02523)
*Liuliu Chen, Elise R. Carrotte, Brian E. Chapman, Jo Robinson, Mike Conway*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FigSIM，首个用于自杀模因细粒度分析的数据集，包含1049个模因，标注了自杀严重程度、比喻现象和自杀相关内容，并对16个单模态和多模态模型进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自杀模因在社交媒体上日益常见，但缺乏对其特征的深入理解和合适的内容审核策略。目前缺少标注数据集来开发和评估自动化审核方法，这是该领域的关键障碍。

Method: 构建了包含1049个自杀模因的FigSIM数据集，每个模因标注了：(1)细粒度自杀严重程度等级，(2)比喻现象（如隐喻），(3)自杀相关内容（如自杀方法描述）。在三个任务（比喻语言检测、自杀严重程度分类、自杀相关内容检测）上对16个单模态和多模态模型进行了基准测试。

Result: 实验表明自杀模因对建模和内容审核都提出了独特挑战。分析揭示了模型偏差，例如对较高自杀严重程度等级的预测不足，尤其是在包含比喻表达的模因中更为明显。

Conclusion: FigSIM数据集填补了自杀模因细粒度分析领域的空白，揭示了现有模型在处理此类敏感内容时的局限性，为未来开发更有效的自动化内容审核方法提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FigSIM，首个面向自杀模因的细粒度多模态数据集，并对多种单模态和多模态模型进行了基准评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Dataset focuses on niche suicide meme analysis, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: suicide memes, fine-grained severity, figurative language, multimodal benchmark, social media content moderation

Abstract: Suicide memes are memes used to express suicide-related thoughts or comment on suicide-related issues. Suicide memes are increasingly common on social media, yet remain poorly understood and potentially harmful. There is an urgent need to better understand their characteristics and to develop appropriate content moderation strategies that limits users' exposure to potentially harmful content. Currently, the absence of annotated datasets of suicide memes remains a key barrier to developing and evaluating automated moderation approaches. In this paper, we introduce FigSIM, the first dataset designed for fine-grained analysis of suicide memes. The dataset consists of 1049 memes, each annotated for (1) fine-grained suicide severity levels, (2) figurative phenomena (e.g., metaphors), and (3) suicide-related content (e.g., suicide method depiction). We benchmark 16 unimodal and multimodal models across three tasks: figurative language, suicide severity, and suicide-related content detection. Overall, FigSIM demonstrates that suicide memes pose unique challenges for both modeling and content moderation. Analysis revealed biases, such as underprediction of higher suicide severity levels, especially for figurative memes. The dataset (including splits used for analyses) is publicly available. Content Warning: This paper contains suicide-related content that may be triggering.

</details>


### [246] [SMH-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Environment-Grounded Reasoning and Action in Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01912)
*Kuan Li, Shuo Zhang, Huacan Wang, Fangzhou Yu, Zecheng Sheng, Yi Gu, Weipeng Ming, Lei Xue, Chen Liu, Sen Hu, Ronghao Chen, Siyue Lin, Yuqing Hou, Xiaofeng Mou, Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SMH-Bench是一个基于可执行可验证的智能家居模拟器HomeEnv构建的综合基准，包含1100个高质量任务，覆盖7大类22个子类，用于评估LLM智能体在智能家居环境中的推理、交互和行动能力。实验表明前沿LLM在显式控制和查询任务上表现良好，但在自动化调度、歧义处理和个人化推理方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能家居基准多聚焦于静态指令到API的映射或有限模拟，无法评估LLM在真实家庭场景中的推理、交互和可靠行动能力。因此需要更全面的基准来推动可靠、上下文感知、可实际部署的智能家居智能体发展。

Method: 构建了HomeEnv可执行可验证的智能家居模拟器，并基于此设计了SMH-Bench基准，包含1100个任务，涵盖7大类（设备控制、查询、自动化、歧义处理、个人化推理等）和22个子类，任务按简单/中等/复杂三种家庭规模分层（从公寓到含135个设备的多房间环境）。

Result: 前沿LLM在显式控制和查询任务上表现强劲，但在自动化任务调度、歧义处理和个人化推理方面表现显著较弱，且随着家庭复杂度增加性能下降明显。

Conclusion: SMH-Bench揭示了当前LLM智能体在复杂智能家居场景中的关键短板，为开发更可靠、上下文感知的智能家居智能体提供了有价值的评测平台和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SMH-Bench，一个用于评估LLM智能体在智能家居环境中进行环境基础推理与行动能力的综合基准。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题，因为该子主题涵盖多模态/智能体评测基准，SMH-Bench正是针对智能家居场景的评测基准。

Importance reason: Focuses on smart-home agent benchmarking, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Smart home benchmark, LLM agent evaluation, environment-grounded reasoning, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Smart homes are evolving toward complex state-dependent living environments, requiring Large Language Models (LLMs) to reason over user intent, preferences, and multi-device interactions. However, existing smart-home benchmarks often focus on static instruction-to-API mapping or limited simulations, failing to evaluate whether LLMs can reason, interact, and act reliably in realistic household scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce SMH-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLMs in smart-home environments. Built upon HomeEnv, an executable and verifiable smart-home simulator, SMH-Bench contains 1,100 high-quality tasks spanning 7 categories and 22 fine-grained subcategories. It further stratifies tasks across simple, medium and complex homes, ranging from small apartments to dense multi-room environments with 135 devices. Experiments show that although frontier LLMs achieve strong performance on explicit control and query tasks, they still exhibit significant weaknesses in automation task scheduling, ambiguity handling and personalized reasoning, especially as home complexity increases. We hope SMH-Bench will facilitate the development of more reliable, context-aware, and practically deployable smart-home agents.

</details>


### [247] [BADGER: Bridging Agentic and Deterministic Evaluation for Generative Enterprise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02109)
*Shannon Serrao, Soumitra Chatterjee, Dorina Strori, Abhishek Sharma, Nathan Miller*

Main category: cs.AI
