Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BADGER 是一个面向企业级 AI 系统的统一评测框架，整合了 Text-to-SQL 评估与智能体行为评估。它提出 LLM 辅助的 SQL 组件提取方法、混合执行准确率指标 Hybrid-EX（在人工标注数据上达到 Cohen's kappa=0.717），以及企业智能体评估套件，可在客户数据环境中运行，作为持续评估基础设施而非一次性质量门禁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 评测（如 Spider、BIRD）和智能体评测（如 G-Eval、RAGAS）各自独立，缺乏统一的企业级生产化评测框架，无法同时评估 SQL 生成准确性和多步智能体推理行为，且现有指标在 CTE 复杂 SQL、列别名、数值容差等方面存在脆弱性。

Method: BADGER 包含三个核心贡献：(1) LLM 辅助的 SQL 组件提取方法，扩展 Spider 方法论以处理 CTE 密集和方言特定的 SQL；(2) 混合执行准确率指标 Hybrid-EX，先用 LLM 推断结构对齐，再进行确定性单元格级评分，解决列别名和数值容差问题；(3) 企业智能体评估套件，整合 RAGAS、G-Eval 和智能体基准指标，新增 Excess Tool Usage 指标，统一为可配置的评测流水线。

Result: 在 150 条人工标注的企业查询上验证，Hybrid-EX 达到 Cohen's kappa=0.717（95% CI: 0.600-0.822，高度一致性）和 87.3% 平衡准确率，优于所有六个对比框架（Delta-kappa: 0.322-0.502，所有 p<=0.001）。

Conclusion: BADGER 提供了一个统一、可配置、在企业数据环境中运行的生产级评测框架，能够作为持续评估基础设施而非一次性质量门禁，支持快速原型化客户特定的评测指标和 LLM 评判后端。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BADGER，一个面向企业级 Text-to-SQL 和智能体推理的统一评测框架，属于多模态评测与基准方向。虽然不直接涉及视觉模态，但其核心贡献是评测框架、指标和协议，与 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 的评测协议、自动评估等主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on enterprise Text-to-SQL and agent evaluation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Enterprise AI evaluation, Text-to-SQL, Agentic behavior, Hybrid-EX metric, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Enterprise AI systems that translate natural language into SQL queries and orchestrate multi-step agentic reasoning pipelines require evaluation approaches fundamentally different from academic benchmarks. Spider and BIRD established execution-accuracy protocols; G-Eval and RAGAS advanced LLM-based assessment; and recent work such as Spider 2.0, BEAVER, and BIRD-Interact has begun to address enterprise and agentic dimensions. No single framework unifies text-to-SQL assessment with agentic behavior evaluation into a production-grade pipeline calibrated against human expert judgment. We present BADGER, developed at Merkle, a unified evaluation framework integrating text-to-SQL assessment with agentic behavior evaluation. BADGER offers three contributions. First, LLM-assisted SQL component extraction extending Spider methodology to handle CTE-heavy, dialect-specific SQL. Second, a hybrid execution accuracy metric (Hybrid-EX) resolving column-aliasing and numeric-tolerance brittleness by using an LLM to infer structural alignments before deterministic cell-level scoring. Validated on 150 human-annotated industry queries, Hybrid-EX achieves Cohen's kappa=0.717 [95% CI: 0.600-0.822] (Substantial agreement) and 87.3% balanced accuracy, outperforming all six competing frameworks (Delta-kappa: 0.322-0.502, all p<=0.001). Third, an enterprise agentic evaluation suite assembling RAGAS, G-Eval, and agent benchmark metrics into a unified pipeline; Excess Tool Usage is the sole novel element. BADGER runs entirely within the client's governed data environment, supports configurable LLM judge backends, and enables rapid prototyping of client-specific judges and metrics, serving as a continuous evaluation backbone rather than a one-time quality gate.

</details>


### [248] [ChartArena: Benchmarking Chart Parsing across Languages, Scenarios, and Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01348)
*Shangpin Peng, Gengluo Li, Xingyu Wan, Chengquan Zhang, Hao Feng, Binghong Wu, Huawen Shen, Weinong Wang, Ziyi Cai, Zhuotao Tian, Han Hu, Can Ma, Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ChartArena 是一个全面的双语图表解析评测基准，覆盖 8 类图表家族（含流程图、思维导图等图示结构）和数字渲染、打印照片、手绘照片三种视觉场景。通过人机协同标注和格式无关的评估协议，对 26 个主流 MLLM 进行评测，发现闭源模型领先但开源模型快速追赶，文档解析模型在数值图表上表现尚可但在图示结构上大幅落后，专家图表解析器局限于窄类图表，雷达图和手绘场景对所有模型仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表解析评测基准存在三大局限：1）仅覆盖窄类图表类型，忽略了流程图、思维导图等图示结构；2）模型输出格式不兼容，难以公平比较；3）数据集很少包含实际场景中遇到的打印或手绘图像。因此需要一个全面、统一、公平的评测基准。

Method: 1）构建 ChartArena 数据集：采用人机协同标注流程，覆盖 8 类图表家族（数值图表+图示结构），每种图表包含数字渲染、打印照片、手绘照片三种视觉场景，经多阶段人工验证确保标注可靠性。2）设计格式无关的评估协议：将异构输出映射到两种规范语义空间（归一化三元组视图和有向图视图），使用结构感知指标进行评分。

Result: 对 26 个主流 MLLM 的评估发现：1）Gemini 3.1 Pro 等前沿闭源模型整体领先，但最强开源系统正在快速缩小差距；2）文档解析模型在数值图表上表现尚可，但在图示结构上大幅落后；3）专家图表解析器局限于窄类图表。雷达图和手绘场景对所有模型仍是最具挑战的任务。

Conclusion: ChartArena 揭示了当前 MLLM 在图表解析任务上的能力差距，为未来研究提供了统一的评测基础。该基准填补了现有评测在图表类型多样性、输出格式兼容性和视觉场景覆盖方面的空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 ChartArena，一个面向图表解析的综合双语评测基准，涵盖多种图表类型、视觉场景和输出格式，并对 26 个主流 MLLM 进行了系统评估。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for chart parsing but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Chart parsing benchmark, Bilingual evaluation, Format-agnostic protocol, Hand-drawn scenarios, MLLM evaluation

Abstract: Charts are a primary medium for conveying quantitative and relational information, yet systematically evaluating chart parsing models remains difficult. Existing benchmarks focus on narrow chart types and leave diagrammatic structures such as flowcharts and mind maps largely unaddressed, while models produce outputs in incompatible formats, and datasets rarely include the printed or hand-drawn images encountered in practice. To address these issues, we introduce ChartArena, a comprehensive bilingual benchmark covering eight chart families spanning both numeric charts and diagrammatic structures, each evaluated across three visual scenarios: digital renderings, printed photos, and hand-drawn photos. The dataset is built via a human-agent collaborative annotation pipeline with multi-stage human verification to ensure annotation reliability. To enable fair cross-model comparison, we further design a format-agnostic evaluation protocol that maps heterogeneous outputs into two canonical semantic spaces, a normalized triple view and a directed graph view, and scores them with structure-aware metrics. Through extensive evaluation of 26 leading MLLMs, we observe three consistent findings: (i) frontier proprietary models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro lead overall, yet the strongest open-source systems are rapidly closing the gap; (ii) document parsing models handle numeric charts reasonably but fall sharply behind on diagrammatic structures; and (iii) expert chart parsers remain limited to narrow chart families. Across all models, radar charts and hand-drawn scenarios stay especially challenging. These findings show that ChartArena exposes clear capability gaps and provides a unified foundation for future progress. ChartArena is publicly available at .

</details>


### [249] [Perception First: A Frontier Native-Video Model with Self-Consistency for Implicit Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01485)
*Ali Alavi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提交至 CVPR 2026 VRR Challenge，系统评估了多种开源 Video-LMM（Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、InternVL3 等）在 ImplicitQA 基准上的表现。核心发现是：该基准受限于感知能力而非推理能力——推理增强策略（CoT、问题分解等）效果中性甚至有害，而基础模型的感知能力和轻量测试时去噪才是可靠杠杆。错误分析表明相对深度、视角和计数是最难类别，因果和社会推理已接近解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答基准多关注单帧可见信息，缺乏对隐式推理（需跨帧推断空间布局、运动、深度、视角、因果和社会上下文）的评测。作者希望通过系统性实验揭示当前 Video-LMM 在隐式视频问答中的真实瓶颈。

Method: 在 ImplicitQA/VRR-QA 基准上，对 Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、InternVL3、Gemma-3 以及 RL 微调的视频推理模型 Video-R1、VideoChat-R1.5 进行零样本评测。测试多种推理增强策略：思维链、问题分解、描述-推理级联、音频转录、空间状态提示、自一致性、多模型集成和类别路由。同时进行逐类别错误分析，并尝试注入单目深度线索。

Result: 推理增强策略对性能提升效果中性甚至有害；基础模型感知能力是决定性因素。错误分析显示相对深度、视角和计数是最难类别，因果和社会推理已接近解决。显式注入深度线索反而使测试准确率下降 5.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: ImplicitQA 基准是感知受限而非推理受限的；当前 Video-LMM 的瓶颈在于低层视觉感知（深度、视角、计数），而非高层推理。提升模型感知能力比改进推理流程更为关键。

Classification reason: 该论文围绕 VRR Challenge / ImplicitQA 基准进行系统性评测，分析多种 Video-LMM 在隐式视频问答上的表现，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on video QA benchmarking and perception limitations, not on core generative or continuous model advances. Signals: perception-bound benchmark, reasoning augmentation neutral/harmful, depth/counting hardest, not generative model, no new architecture

Abstract: We describe our submission to the VRR Challenge @ CVPR 2026, built on the \emph{ImplicitQA} / \emph{VRR-QA} benchmark~\cite{implicitqa}: multiple-choice video question answering in which answers are deliberately \emph{not} observable in any single frame and must be inferred from spatial layout, motion, depth, viewpoint, causality, and social context across discontinuous frames of creative video. We conduct a systematic, training-free study spanning open-source Video-LMMs (Qwen2.5-VL~\cite{qwen25vl}, Qwen3-VL~\cite{qwen3vl}, InternVL3, Gemma-3, and the RL-tuned video reasoners Video-R1~\cite{videor1} and VideoChat-R1.5~\cite{videochatr15}) and a battery of inference-time strategies (chain-of-thought, question decomposition, describe-then-reason cascades, audio transcripts, spatial state prompting, self-consistency~\cite{selfconsistency}, multi-model ensembling, and category routing). Our central finding is that this benchmark is \emph{perception-bound rather than reasoning-bound}: reasoning-side augmentations are neutral-to-harmful, whereas base-model perceptual capability and lightweight test-time denoising are the only reliable levers. A per-category error analysis localizes the difficulty to low-level perception -- relative depth, viewpoint, and counting are the hardest categories, while causal and social reasoning are nearly solved -- and a prompt that explicitly injects monocular depth cues to attack the weakest category \emph{lowers} test accuracy by $5.8$ points, confirming that the model needs a better \emph{percept}, not a better \emph{procedure}.

</details>


### [250] [The Image Reconstruction Game: Drawing Common Ground Through Iterative Multimodal Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01901)
*Sherzod Hakimov, Mattia D'Agostini, Ivan Samodelkin, David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了Image Reconstruction Game（图像重建游戏），一个全自动的多轮对话基准，让VLM（描述者）向图像生成器（生成者）发出修正指令，通过多轮迭代使累积的共同理解直接可视化为重建图像。实验发现描述者是重建质量的主导因素，生成者决定迭代是否有效；数学和几何图像最具挑战性；描述者的token预算显著影响收敛行为；人类验证表明自动评测与人类偏好的一致性仅为轻微到中等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测多为单轮问答，无法评估多轮交互中共同理解（common ground）的建立过程。作者希望设计一个可观测、可量化的多轮交互基准，通过图像重建这一可视化任务，直接观察VLM与生成模型之间如何通过迭代对话逐步达成共识。

Method: 设计Image Reconstruction Game框架：一个VLM作为"描述者"（Describer），一个文本到图像生成模型作为"生成者"（Generator）。给定目标图像，描述者首先生成初始描述，生成者据此生成图像；然后描述者观察生成图像与目标图像的差异，发出修正指令；如此迭代多轮。使用自动评测指标（CLIPScore、DreamSim、LPIPS、MSE）和人类评估来衡量重建质量。交叉组合两种描述者（LLaVA-NeXT-Interleave-Qwen-7B、GPT-4o）和两种生成者（FLUX.1-dev、SDXL），在7个图像类别上评测。

Result: 1) 描述者是重建质量的主导因素，GPT-4o作为描述者显著优于LLaVA；2) 生成者决定迭代是否有效——FLUX.1-dev受益于迭代，SDXL则可能退化；3) 数学和几何图像类别最具挑战性；4) 短token预算导致初始重建稀疏但迭代改进空间大，长token预算初始质量高但改进空间小；5) 强描述者使用更丰富的修正词汇（空间、数值、结构类），弱描述者集中于表面属性且倾向于提前停止；6) 最佳自动评测指标与人类偏好仅达到轻微到中等一致性。

Conclusion: Image Reconstruction Game提供了一个可观测多轮交互中共同理解建立过程的有效基准。研究表明描述者能力对多轮交互质量至关重要，且自动评测需要人类校准才能可靠使用。该基准为评估VLM的多轮交互能力和共同理解建立提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个全自动的多轮对话基准（Image Reconstruction Game），用于评测VLM通过迭代指令引导图像生成器重建图像的能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及多模态生成（图像生成器作为评测对象），因此也匹配multimodal_generation方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal dialogue benchmark, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: multimodal dialogue benchmark, iterative image reconstruction, VLM and generator interaction, token budget effects, human validation of automated metrics

Abstract: We introduce the Image Reconstruction Game, a fully automated benchmark in which a vision-language model issues corrective instructions to an image generator across multiple turns, making accumulated common ground directly observable as a rendered image. Benchmarking two Describer models crossed with two Generator models across seven image categories, we find that the describer is the dominant factor in reconstruction quality, while the generator determines whether iterative refinement helps or hurts. Mathematical and geometric images pose the greatest challenge. The describer's token budget strongly affects convergence: shorter budgets yield sparser first renderings with more room for visible improvement, while longer budgets raise absolute quality but leave less to fix. Stronger describers use a richer correction vocabulary spanning spatial, numeric, and structural categories, while weaker describers concentrate on surface properties and tend to stop after a few turns. Human validation shows that the best automated judge reaches only slight-to-fair agreement with human preferences, and automated scores require human recalibration to be used reliably.

</details>


### [251] [A Structured Benchmark for Text-Guided Anomaly Detection: When Language Stops Conditioning the Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01992)
*Stefano Samele, Eugenio Lomurno, Teodora Jovanovic, Sanjay Shivakumar Manohar, Alberto Crivellaro, Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TGAD基准，通过三个逐步增加语言功能角色的场景（提示敏感性、组件级标注、组装面板数据集APD）系统评估文本引导的工业异常检测系统。实验表明，当前多模态异常检测方法中文本接口仅表面性地影响决策，标准基准高估了文本引导能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测方法虽声称支持文本引导的零样本/少样本检测，但其评测协议继承自单模态基准，文本条件保持不变，无法衡量语言是否真正影响决策。报告的性能提升可能来自预训练视觉特征而非文本引导，需要更严格的评测协议。

Method: 构建TGAD结构化基准，包含三个场景：(1) 在MVTec AD上控制提示词敏感性；(2) 扩展MVTec AD添加组件标签，要求模型仅评估指定部件；(3) 提出新的Assembled Panel Dataset (APD)，结合缺陷类型和组件位置知识。评估三种代表性范式：生成式大视觉语言模型、免训练判别式模型、嵌入自适应判别式模型。

Result: 三种范式下文本接口均仅表面性影响决策：生成模型在去除物体名词时I-AUROC从97.4降至82.6；组件级指令在引入外部缺陷后从90.3降至66.3；APD上图像级判别低于MVTec水平，最低仅31.5（低于随机）。

Conclusion: 标准基准高估了当前多模态异常检测系统的文本引导能力，本文提出的结构化评测协议是确保模型可通过语言可靠控制以用于工业部署的必要前提。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于文本引导异常检测的结构化基准（TGAD），包含新的数据集APD和评测协议，旨在评估多模态视觉语言模型在工业异常检测中是否真正受文本条件控制。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Rigorous benchmark reveals that current multimodal anomaly detection systems fail at true text-guided decision, challenging inflated claims in a niche application area. Signals: Exposes superficial text conditioning in anomaly detection, introduces structured TGAD benchmark with APD dataset, strong negative results for generative and discriminative models, high relevance for evaluation methodology but low for user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection has historically been a unimodal task. Recent multimodal vision-language models have produced systems that admit textual input alongside the image and are presented as enabling text-guided zero- and few-shot inspection. Yet these methods are evaluated with protocols inherited from unimodal benchmarks that hold the textual condition constant and therefore cannot measure whether language conditions the decision; whether reported gains reflect text guidance or strong pretrained visual features remains open. We introduce Text-Guided Anomaly Detection (TGAD), a structured benchmark that progressively increases the functional role of language across three scenarios: a controlled prompt-sensitivity setting on MVTec AD; a component-tagged extension of MVTec AD that requires the model to restrict its assessment to an instructed part; and the new Assembled Panel Dataset (APD), a realistic industrial setting that requires both defect-type and component-location knowledge. We evaluate one representative model per paradigm: generative large vision-language, training-free discriminative, and embedding-adaptive discriminative. In all three, the textual interface conditions the decision only superficially: prompt content is absorbed unless the object noun is removed (the generative model's I-AUROC drops from 97.4 to 82.6); component-level instructions do not constrain the decision once defects outside the instructed part are admitted as normal (from 90.3 to 66.3); and when both combine on APD, image-level discrimination collapses below the MVTec level, in one case below chance (71.2, 50.5, 31.5). These results suggest that standard benchmarks overstate the text-guided capabilities of current multimodal anomaly detection systems, and that a protocol of this kind is a prerequisite for models that can be reliably controlled through language for industrial deployment.

</details>


### [252] [Distortion-Aware Fusion of Statistical and Vision-Language Features for Blind Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02002)
*Bishr Omer Abdelrahman Adam, Xu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种失真感知融合框架，将统计特征（NSS）与视觉语言模型嵌入（SigLIP, CLIP-H）通过乘法门控机制动态融合，用于盲图像质量评估，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典NSS描述符与现代VLM嵌入从不同角度解决BIQA问题，但两者是否互补以及如何根据输入图像动态加权其贡献尚未被探索。

Method: 提出失真感知融合框架：提取138维NSS特征 + SigLIP和CLIP-H两种VLM嵌入，通过乘法门控机制学习每个输入的流权重，使用混合损失（MSE + Pearson线性相关 + 成对排序）训练，无需微调VLM骨干。

Result: KonIQ-10k (SROCC=0.9142, PLCC=0.9279), KADID-10k (SROCC=0.9715, PLCC=0.9733, 超越SOTA), LIVE Challenge (SROCC=0.8527, PLCC=0.8802)。门控权重与独立消融实验的NSS贡献正相关（rho=0.33）。

Conclusion: NSS与VLM特征互补，门控融合优于静态拼接；模型能自主发现失真-流亲和模式，NSS对噪声和颜色偏移贡献最大，对感知失真贡献最小。

Classification reason: 该论文研究盲图像质量评估（BIQA），使用VLM嵌入（SigLIP, CLIP-H）与NSS特征融合，属于多模态视觉语言模型在下游任务中的应用。最相关的规范子主题是"多模态评测与基准"，因为论文核心是图像质量评估（一种视觉评测任务），且涉及VLM特征的使用和基准测试。

Importance reason: Focuses on BIQA with NSS and VLM fusion, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Blind image quality assessment, Distortion-aware fusion, NSS + VLM, No end-to-end fine-tuning, SOTA on KADID-10k

Abstract: Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) aims to predict perceived image quality without access to a reference image. Classical natural scene statistics (NSS) descriptors and modern vision-language model (VLM) embeddings address this problem from fundamentally different perspectives, yet whether combining them yields complementary benefits and how to weight their contributions per input image remains unexplored. We propose a distortion-aware fusion framework that integrates a 138-dimensional NSS descriptor with two complementary VLM embeddings, SigLIP and CLIP-H, through a multiplicative gating mechanism that learns per-input stream weights conditioned on image content. Unlike static concatenation fusion, the proposed gating network suppresses or amplifies each stream's contribution based on the input, producing weights that correlate positively (Spearman rank correlation rho=0.33) with the per-distortion NSS contribution measured by independent ablation on KADID-10k. The framework requires no end-to-end fine-tuning of the VLM backbones and is trained with a hybrid loss combining mean squared error, Pearson linear correlation, and pairwise ranking objectives. We evaluate on three standard benchmarks: KonIQ-10k (SROCC=0.9142, PLCC=0.9279), KADID-10k (SROCC=0.9715, PLCC=0.9733, surpassing recent state-of-the-art methods), and LIVE Challenge in-the-Wild (SROCC=0.8527, PLCC=0.8802 with cross-dataset pretraining and fine-tuning). A per-distortion analysis on KADID-10k reveals that NSS features contribute most on noise and color-shift distortions where pixel statistics are directly affected, and least on perceptual distortions such as color saturation changes. The learned gate values validate these findings, confirming that the model autonomously discovers distortion-stream affinity patterns consistent with the manual per-distortion study.

</details>


### [253] [InsightVQA: High-Dimensional Emotion-Cognitive Visual Question Answering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02171)
*Shiyu Wang, Ziyu Liu, Chaoyi Yu, Yujie Yin, Zhongqian Mao, Jing Chen, Jiaqi Song, Yunshi Lan, Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 InsightVQA，一个大规模层次化视觉问答基准数据集，专注于情感理解与认知推理。数据集包含 138K 图像和 725K QA 对，分为感知 QA、基础理解 QA 和认知 QA 三个层次。同时提出了 InsightVQA-Bench（30K 样本评测集）和 InsightNet（情感调优基线模型），实验表明该基准对现有 MLLM 构成显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感理解基准主要关注情感识别，缺乏对情感产生原因（grounded understanding）和更高层次认知推理（如响应意图预测、序列洞察推理）的支持，无法全面评估模型的情感认知能力。

Method: 从 6 个公开来源收集 351K 图像，经过多阶段过滤得到 138K 高置信度图像。采用三级层次化标注：感知 QA（情感与效价识别）、基础理解 QA（通过约束引导生成提取视觉触发因素）、认知 QA（响应意图预测与序列洞察推理）。共构建 725K QA 对，并从中精选 30K 样本作为评测基准。同时提出 InsightNet 作为情感调优基线 MLLM。

Result: InsightVQA 对现有 MLLM 构成显著挑战，特别是在基础理解 QA 和认知 QA 层次上，模型表现远低于人类水平。InsightNet 在情感调优后性能有所提升，但仍与人类存在较大差距。

Conclusion: InsightVQA 填补了情感认知推理评测的空白，揭示了当前多模态大模型在深层情感理解与认知推理方面的不足，为未来研究提供了有价值的基准和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 InsightVQA，一个面向情感认知推理的视觉问答基准数据集，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on emotion-cognitive VQA benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: emotion understanding benchmark, cognitive reasoning VQA, not continuous or generative, no autoregressive or diffusion focus, no world model connection

Abstract: Visual emotion understanding requires models not only to recognize emotional states, but also to why they arise and perform higher-level cognitive reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on emotion recognition, offering limited support for grounded understanding and response-oriented analysis. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{InsightVQA}, a large-scale dataset for hierarchical visual question answering on emotion understanding and cognitive reasoning. Building from 351K images collected from six public sources, we apply a rigorous multi-stage filtering pipeline to curate 138K high-confidence images. Each image is annotated at three hierarchical levels: perception QA for emotion and valence recognition, grounded understanding QA constructed from visual trigger extraction through constraint-guided generation, and cognition QA centered on response intent prediction and sequential insight reasoning. In total, InsightVQA contains 725K QA pairs. We further present \textbf{InsightVQA-Bench}, a high-quality evaluation benchmark comprising 30K samples for fine-grained evaluation. To support evaluation, we introduce \textbf{InsightNet}, an emotion-tuned baseline for MLLMs. Results demonstrate that InsightVQA poses significant challenges for grounded emotion understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [254] [Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs on Code Generation for Complex Interactive Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00154)
*Fan Wu, Lishuai Dong, Cuiyun Gao, Yujia Chen, Yiming Huang, Yang Xiao, Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 WebIGBench，首个针对复杂交互式网页代码生成的多模态大模型评测基准，涵盖 103 个真实网页、5 种交互类型和 871 个交互动作，并设计了自动化交互一致性评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估静态网页的代码生成，忽略了现代 Web 应用中频繁的用户-页面交互行为，且评估标准局限于视觉保真度和代码结构，缺乏对交互一致性的考量。

Method: 通过人工设计的交互路径结合 UI 自动化，从真实网站收集 103 个复杂网页，覆盖点击、输入等 5 种交互类型共 871 个交互动作；提出自动化评估流程，衡量生成网页与参考网页之间的交互一致性。

Result: 在多个代表性 MLLM 上的实验揭示了当前模型在交互式网页代码生成任务中的性能边界。

Conclusion: WebIGBench 填补了交互式网页代码生成评测的空白，为未来 MLLM 在动态 Web 开发中的应用提供了有价值的基准和评估方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 WebIGBench，一个用于评估 MLLM 在复杂交互式网页上代码生成能力的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow benchmark for interactive webpage code generation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: multimodal LLM benchmark, interactive webpage code generation, interaction consistency evaluation, not matching priority subtopics, lacks boost keywords

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in multimodal reasoning and code generation, catalyzing a new paradigm for front-end development. In particular, these models can directly transform visual designs into executable code, significantly improving the efficiency and adaptability of web development. Modern web applications are dynamic and interactive, featuring frequent user-page interactions. However, existing benchmarks largely evaluate the code generation of static webpages, ignoring the complex interactive behaviors in real-world applications. Besides, their evaluation criteria remain confined to visual fidelity and code structure, overlooking the interaction consistency between the generated and the reference webpages. To address these limitations, we introduce WebIGBench, the first benchmark designed to evaluate code generation for interactive webpages with complex interactions. By combining manually designed interaction paths with UI automation, we collected 103 complex webpages from real-world websites. This benchmark covers 5 popular interactive action types (e.g., click, input) involving 871 distinct interactive actions. Moreover, we propose a novel evaluation pipeline to address the gap in automated assessment of interactive actions. Extensive experiments on several representative MLLMs reveal the performance boundaries of current models in interactive webpage code generation using WebIGBench. The proposed benchmark is available at .

</details>


### [255] [Ego-METAS: Egocentric online Multimodal Energy-efficient Temporal Action Segmentation benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02246)
*Maria Santos-Villafranca, Jesus Bermudez-cameo, Alejandro Perez-Yus, Giovanni Maria Farinella, Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ego-METAS 是首个面向第一视角在线多模态节能时序动作分割的基准，包含 100+ 小时未裁剪视频、5 种模态、硬件能量预算和多种路由策略基线，揭示了最优传感器路由高度依赖场景，现有策略学习方法难以适应连续未裁剪环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要在"始终在线"模式下感知环境，但能量感知感知（energy-aware perception）研究不足，大多数先前工作假设无限计算资源。本文旨在填补这一空白，为资源受限设备上的在线多模态感知提供标准化评测平台。

Method: 构建 Ego-METAS 基准：从 EgoExo4D、CMU-MMAC、CaptainCook4D 三个数据集收集 100+ 小时未裁剪第一视角视频，涵盖 RGB、音频、注视、IMU 和单色相机 5 种模态；定义在线时序动作分割任务，要求模型在每时间步动态选择激活哪些传感器，并严格遵守硬件代表性能量预算；提供统一划分、清洗标注、预提取特征和多种基线路由策略。

Result: 最优路由高度依赖场景；现有策略学习方法（主要为裁剪片段设计）难以适应连续未裁剪环境；即使简单的随机路由动态融合互补模态，对于在严格能量预算下平衡预测精度也至关重要。

Conclusion: Ego-METAS 为自主始终在线具身 AI 的鲁棒、成本感知策略开发提供了标准化基础，揭示了在线多模态节能感知中传感器动态路由的重要性和挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了首个面向第一视角在线多模态节能时序动作分割的基准 Ego-METAS，包含多模态数据、能量预算和评测协议，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark contribution for embodied AI efficiency, but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: first egocentric online multimodal energy-aware benchmark, 100+ hours untrimmed video, 5 modalities, hardware energy budgets, routing policy baselines

Abstract: To operate in the physical world, embodied agents must perceive their environment in an "always-on" fashion, selectively accessing the most informative sensors to balance energy constraints and task accuracy. Despite its importance for resource-constrained devices, energy-aware perception remains under-explored, with most prior work assuming unlimited compute. To address this, we introduce Ego-METAS: the first Egocentric online Multimodal Energy-efficient Temporal Action Segmentation benchmark. Ego-METAS provides a unified testbed of more than 100 hours of untrimmed egocentric video from EgoExo4D, CMU-MMAC, and CaptainCook4D, spanning 5 modalities (RGB, audio, gaze, IMU, and monochrome camera). We formulate an online temporal action segmentation task where models must dynamically select which sensors to activate at each timestep while strictly adhering to hardware-representative energy budgets. Alongside the benchmark, we release unified splits, cleaned annotations, pre-extracted features, and a diverse suite of baseline routing policies. Our evaluations show that optimal routing is highly scenario-dependent, and that existing policy-learning methods, designed primarily for trimmed clips, struggle to adapt to continuous, untrimmed environments. However, even simple dynamic fusion of complementary modalities (e.g., via random routing) proves critical for balancing predictive accuracy against strict energy budgets. Ultimately, Ego-METAS provides a standardized foundation to develop robust, cost-aware policies for autonomous, always-on embodied AI.

</details>


### [256] [Explainable Forensics of Manipulated Segments in Untrimmed Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02402)
*Yue Feng, Jingjing Li, Qijia Lu, Wei Ji, Jingrou Zhang, Fei Shen, Xiao Li, Yizhen Jia, Qiang Chen, Limin Wang, Wentong Li, Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出长视频中AI生成片段时序定位与解释（TASLE）任务，构建了包含12,472个未修剪视频的大规模基准数据集，并提出MSLoc方法——结合边界敏感提案生成模块与MLLM精炼模块，实现粗到细的伪造片段定位与可解释分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频取证方法主要针对短片段独立操作，无法处理真实场景中AI生成内容稀疏嵌入在长视频中的情况。缺乏针对长视频中局部AI生成片段定位与解释的基准和方法。

Method: 提出MSLoc基线方法，采用粗到细两阶段策略：1）边界敏感提案生成模块（BSPG）高效扫描长视频生成候选片段；2）基于MLLM的精炼模块对候选片段进行精确边界定位和可解释推理。同时构建TASLE基准数据集，包含12,472个未修剪长视频，涵盖多种伪造模式和丰富标注信号。

Result: 实验验证了MSLoc基线方法的有效性，在长视频AI生成片段定位与解释任务上取得了良好性能，证明了片段级可解释取证对于长视频分析的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了长视频AI生成片段取证的研究空白，提出的TASLE基准和MSLoc方法为长视频伪造检测提供了新的任务范式与基线，推动了视频取证领域向更真实场景的拓展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了TASLE基准数据集（12,472个未修剪长视频，含时间边界、真实性标签和片段级解释），用于长视频中AI生成片段的时序定位与可解释性分析，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时，论文也涉及视频生成内容的检测，与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on video forensics, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: video forensics, long video analysis, MLLM refinement, temporal localization, not generation or world model

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI-driven video generation has transformed content creation, while simultaneously increasing the risk of misinformation through localized manipulations in long-form videos. Existing video forensic methods predominantly operate on short, independent clips, and thus fail to capture realistic scenarios where AI-generated content is sparsely embedded within otherwise authentic footage. To bridge this gap, we formulate the task of Temporal AI-Generated Segment Localization and Explanation, which targets authenticity detection, temporal localization, and interpretable analysis of manipulated segments in untrimmed long videos. We further introduce TASLE, a large-scale benchmark comprising 12,472 untrimmed videos with diverse manipulation patterns and rich annotation signals, including temporal boundaries, authenticity labels, and segment-level rationales. In addition, we propose MSLoc, a coarse-to-fine forensic baseline that combines a boundary-sensitive proposal generation module for efficient long-video scanning with an MLLM-based refinement module for precise boundary localization and interpretable reasoning. Experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed baseline, highlighting the importance of segment-level explainable forensics for long-form AI-generated video analysis. Our dataset and code are publicly available at .

</details>


### [257] [X-Stream: Exploring MLLMs as Multiplexers for Multi-Stream Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02482)
*Peiwen Sun, Xudong Lu, Huadai Liu, Yang Bo, Dongming Wu, Huankang Guan, Minghong Cai, Jinpeng Chen, Xintong Guo, Shuhan Li, Rui Liu, Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: X-Stream 是首个面向多流视频流式理解的基准，包含4220个QA对和932个视频，覆盖多窗口、多视角、多设备场景下的11个子任务。实验表明当前最先进的MLLM在多流并发理解上仅能达到约50%的准确率，暴露了现有模型在跨流推理方面的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准局限于单流范式，无法评估模型在真实场景（如体育直播、自动驾驶、多屏协作）中所需的在线、跨流推理能力。因此需要构建一个专门评估多流流式理解的基准。

Method: 构建了X-Stream基准数据集（4220个QA对，932个视频），采用双验证流水线防止模型过度依赖单一流。将MLLM概念化为"多路复用器"（multiplexer），基于信号多路复用理论系统评估其多流理解性能，设计了11个子任务涵盖多窗口、多视角、多设备场景。

Result: 最先进的MLLM在多流并发理解任务上仅达到约50%的得分，且主动推理能力较差。实验揭示了当前多路复用方案的权衡，为下一代多流智能体提供了经验指导。

Conclusion: X-Stream填补了多流流式理解评测的空白，暴露了现有MLLM在多流并发场景下的严重不足，为未来多流智能体的发展提供了评测协议和实证指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了X-Stream，首个面向多流视频流式理解（multi-stream streaming understanding）的基准评测，包含多窗口、多视角、多设备场景下的11个子任务。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel multi-stream video understanding benchmark but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: First multi-stream video benchmark, MLLMs as multiplexers, 11 subtasks, 50% accuracy ceiling, dual-verification pipeline

Abstract: While video streaming understanding has made significant strides, real-world applications, such as live sports broadcasting, autonomous driving, and multi-screen collaboration, inherently demand continuous, multi-stream interactions. However, existing benchmarks are confined to single-stream paradigms, leaving a critical gap in evaluating online, cross-stream reasoning. To bridge this, we introduce X-Stream, the first benchmark dedicated to multi-stream streaming understanding. Comprising 4,220 rigorously curated QA pairs across 932 videos, X-Stream evaluates 11 subtasks across multi-window, multi-view, and multi-device scenarios. Crucially, our dataset is constructed using a novel dual-verification pipeline that prevents over-reliance on a single stream. Furthermore, we pioneer the conceptualization of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) as naive multiplexers, systematically evaluating their performance through the lens of Signal Multiplexing Theory. Our extensive online inference experiments reveal a stark reality: state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle significantly with concurrent streams, achieving only about 50% score and exhibiting poor proactive ability. Ultimately, X-Stream exposes the trade-off of current multiplexing schemes, providing both a practical evaluation protocol and empirical guidance for next-generation multi-stream agents.

</details>


### [258] [Moment-Video: Diagnosing Temporal Fidelity of Video MLLMs on Momentary Visual Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02522)
*Xiaolin Liu, Yilun Zhu, Xiangyu Zhao, Xuehui Wang, Yan Li, Xin Li, Haoyu Cao, Xing Sun, Shaofeng Zhang, Xu Yang, Zhihang Zhong, Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Moment-Video 是一个专门用于诊断视频多模态大模型在瞬时视觉事件上时间保真度的基准，包含1000个人工验证的视频问答对，覆盖7个领域和4种任务类型。评测33个模型发现最佳模型仅39.6%准确率，开源模型普遍低于25%，揭示了当前视频MLLM在捕捉短暂但关键视觉证据方面的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频MLLM在通用和长视频理解上取得了快速进展，但它们保留短暂但关键的视觉证据（如仅持续几帧的局部动作或状态变化）的能力尚未被充分探索。稀疏帧采样、视觉token压缩和粗粒度时间聚合可能导致这些瞬时证据被跳过或稀释，而语言侧推理无法可靠恢复。

Method: 构建 Moment-Video 基准，包含1000个视频问答对，每个问题都基于局部的、视觉可观察的、对采样敏感的瞬时事件。覆盖7个领域和25个细分子类别，包含四种任务类型：时间发生（Temporal Occurrence）、时间计数（Temporal Counting）、动作描述（Action Description）和时间推理（Temporal Reasoning）。评测了33个专有和开源MLLM。

Result: 最佳模型 Seed-2.0-Pro 仅达到39.6%总体准确率，大多数开源模型低于25%。诊断分析表明，更密集的帧采样能改善部分模型但无法消除瓶颈，更长的视频带来更强的时间定位挑战。

Conclusion: 当前视频MLLM仍然缺乏时间保真的表征来捕捉、保留和使用短暂但决定性的视觉证据，在瞬时视觉事件理解上存在显著差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Moment-Video 基准，专门用于诊断视频 MLLM 在瞬时视觉事件理解上的时间保真度，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Diagnoses temporal fidelity in video MLLMs but focuses on evaluation, not on the user's priority of continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: video MLLM benchmark, temporal fidelity, momentary events, low model accuracy, evaluation-focused

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress on general and long-form video understanding, yet their ability to preserve brief answer-critical visual evidence remains underexplored. Many practical questions are determined by momentary visual events: localized actions or state transitions that may last only a few frames. Such evidence can be skipped by sparse frame sampling, suppressed by visual-token compression, or diluted by coarse temporal aggregation, causing failures that language-side reasoning cannot reliably recover. We introduce Moment-Video, a benchmark for diagnosing the temporal fidelity of video MLLMs through momentary visual event understanding. Each question is grounded in a localized, visually observable, and sampling-sensitive event, requiring models to notice, count, describe, or reason about transient evidence rather than rely on persistent objects, global scene context, or language priors. Moment-Video contains 1,000 human-verified video-QA pairs across 7 domains and 25 fine-grained subcategories, covering four task types: Temporal Occurrence, Temporal Counting, Action Description, and Temporal Reasoning. We evaluate 33 proprietary and open-source MLLMs on Moment-Video. The best-performing model, Seed-2.0-Pro, achieves only 39.6% overall accuracy, while most open-source models remain below 25%, revealing a substantial gap in momentary visual event understanding. Diagnostic analyses show that denser frame sampling improves some models but does not eliminate the bottleneck, and longer videos introduce stronger temporal-localization challenges. These findings suggest that current video MLLMs still lack temporally faithful representations for capturing, preserving, and using brief but decisive visual evidence.

</details>


### [259] [Evolving to the Aesthetics of a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00112)
*Stephen James Krol, Jon McCormack*

Main category: cs.NE, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文探索了两种使用视觉语言模型（VLM）评估设计美学的方法：一是基于CLIP-IQA直接预测美学分数，二是通过VLM进行成对比较并使用Glicko评分系统估计种群排名。在自定义生成系统案例中，将结果与艺术家的美学排名及其他评估技术进行了比较，并分析了两种方法的优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化系统在创意领域取得了显著成果，但设计能够有效捕捉抽象输出美感的适应度函数仍然是一个开放问题。现有方法难以量化美学这种主观概念，需要探索利用VLM进行美学评估的新途径。

Method: 提出了两种基于VLM的美学评估方法：(1) CLIP-IQA方法，直接为每个设计预测美学分数；(2) 成对比较方法，通过VLM使用用户自定义提示对候选设计进行两两比较，然后利用Glicko评分系统从比较结果中估计种群排名。在自定义生成系统上进行案例研究，并与艺术家的美学排名及其他评估技术进行对比。

Result: 记录了两种方法在进化设计中的表现，与艺术家的美学排名进行了比较，分析了CLIP-IQA和VLM成对比较方法各自的优势和局限性。

Conclusion: VLM可以作为一种有效的设计美学评估工具，但两种方法各有优劣：CLIP-IQA直接高效但灵活性有限，VLM成对比较方法更灵活但计算成本更高。未来工作可进一步探索VLM在创意进化系统中的美学评估应用。

Classification reason: 该论文探索使用VLM（视觉语言模型）进行美学评估，属于多模态理解与生成方向。论文提出了两种基于VLM的美学评价方法（CLIP-IQA和VLM pairwise comparison + Glicko评分），本质上是对VLM作为美学评估工具的能力进行评测和比较。这与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）子主题高度相关，因为该子主题涵盖多模态评测、自动评估等方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-based aesthetic evaluation for evolutionary design, not on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM aesthetic evaluation, evolutionary design, CLIP-IQA, Glicko ranking, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Evolutionary systems have demonstrated remarkable results in creative domains, with recent applications in generative typography, design, and music. However, an open problem remains in designing fitness functions that effectively capture the desired aesthetics of abstract outputs. In this work, we explore two methods for evaluating the aesthetics of a population using Vision-Language Models (VLMs). The first method uses CLIP-IQA to predict an aesthetic score for each design. The second method instead pits candidates against each other, with winners determined by a VLM using a custom prompt specified by the user. The outcomes of these pairwise comparisons are then used to estimate a population ranking via the Glicko rating system. We present these methods in the context of a case study using a custom generative system and compare the resulting rankings with an artist's aesthetic ranking and those produced by other aesthetic evaluation techniques. Additionally, we document the artist's experience using these approaches to evolve designs, critically analysing the strengths and weaknesses of both methods.

</details>

### [260] [ETC: Extreme Token Compression via Task-aware Visual Information Distillation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00543)
*Yiling Gao, Hongchen Wei, Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ETC框架通过变分信息蒸馏，在VLM中将高分辨率图像产生的视觉token压缩至极少数（甚至单个）token，同时保持任务性能，显著降低KV-cache开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像在VLM中产生大量视觉token，导致推理时高计算成本和KV-cache开销。现有token压缩方法在极端压缩率下性能严重下降，亟需一种能在极低token数下仍保持任务性能的压缩方法。

Method: 从信息论角度出发，证明最小化任务损失需要紧凑表示保留指令感知的充分统计量。ETC利用文本到图像的交叉注意力对原始视觉特征加权，近似潜在指令感知预测统计量；再通过变分信息蒸馏，使紧凑表示保留恢复该预测统计量的必要信息。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen3-VL-2B上实验，ETC在极端压缩（甚至单token）下仍保持有效，大幅减少KV-cache开销的同时保留强任务性能。

Conclusion: ETC通过任务感知的变分信息蒸馏实现了VLM中视觉token的极端压缩，为高效多模态推理提供了新思路，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于VLM中视觉token的极端压缩以减少KV-cache开销，属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in VLMs via extreme token compression, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: extreme token compression, variational information distillation, KV-cache reduction, task-aware compression, LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen3-VL-2B evaluation

Abstract: In Vision-Language Models (VLMs), high-resolution images produce a large number of visual tokens, resulting in high computational costs and KV-cache overhead during inference. To address this problem, we propose an Extreme Token Compression (ETC) framework that minimizes task loss when reducing the number of input tokens based on the principle of variational information distillation. Specifically, from an information-theoretic perspective, we show that minimizing task loss requires the compact representation to preserve the instruction-aware sufficient statistic of the task-relevant visual information for prediction. In practice, ETC leverages text-to-image cross-attention to weight the original visual features to approximate the latent instruction-aware predictive statistic. Moreover, ETC introduces a variational information distillation, enabling the compact representation to preserve the essential information to recover this predictive statistic. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen3-VL-2B show that ETC remains effective even under single-token compression, substantially reducing KV-cache overhead while retaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [261] [LASER: Loss-Aware Singular-value Decomposition and Rank Allocation for Efficient Low-Precision Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00573)
*Haiyu Wang, Yutong Wang, Leshu Li, Yihui Ren, Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (78.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LASER 提出了一种损失感知的 SVD 低秩分解框架，利用 Kronecker 分解 Fisher 信息矩阵指导分解，并引入跨层秩分配策略，将低秩压缩扩展到 FFN 层，在低精度推理下实现 2.3 倍以上解码加速且保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）计算开销大、参数量多，难以部署在资源受限设备上。现有低秩分解方法仅优化局部矩阵重建误差、使用均匀或启发式秩分配，且主要关注注意力投影而忽略前馈网络（FFN），缺乏对下游任务损失的感知。

Method: LASER 从模型损失的二阶近似出发，推导出曲率加权的 SVD 目标，使用 Kronecker 分解的 Fisher 信息矩阵引导分解以关注下游性能而非单纯重建误差；提出基于校准梯度的损失感知跨层秩分配策略，实现各层参数预算的有效分配；最后通过 SVD 与量化的混合方案将低秩压缩扩展到 FFN 层。

Result: 在多个 VLM 上的实验表明，LASER 在低精度推理下相比此前方法实现 2.3 倍以上的解码加速，同时保持较强的模型精度。

Conclusion: LASER 通过损失感知的 SVD 分解和跨层秩分配，有效压缩 VLM 模型，在显著加速推理的同时保持精度，为资源受限设备上的 VLM 部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出低秩分解压缩框架 LASER，通过损失感知的 SVD 和跨层秩分配来压缩 VLM，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for multimodal LLMs, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel loss-aware SVD method that achieves significant speedup. Signals: efficient inference, low-rank compression, vision-language model, SVD, Fisher information

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) deliver strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their large computational cost and high parameter counts make deployment challenging on resource-constrained devices. Low-rank decomposition has emerged as a promising compression technique, yet existing methods often optimize local matrix reconstruction error, rely on uniform or heuristic rank allocation, and focus mainly on attention projections while leaving feed-forward networks underexplored. In this paper, we propose~\textit{LASER} (\textbf{L}oss-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{S}ingular-value d\textbf{E}composition and \textbf{R}ank allocation), a low-rank compression framework for efficient low-precision VLM inference. LASER derives a curvature-weighted SVD objective from a second-order approximation of the model loss and uses Kronecker-factored Fisher information to guide decomposition toward downstream performance rather than reconstruction alone. We further introduce a loss-aware cross-layer rank allocation strategy based on calibration gradients, enabling more effective parameter budgeting across layers. Finally, we extend low-rank compression to FFN layers through a hybrid scheme that combines SVD with quantization. The evaluation results show that LASER achieves more than $2.3\times$ decoding speedup over previous work while preserving strong accuracy under low-precision inference.

</details>


### [262] [On the Limits of Token Reduction for Efficient Unified Vision Language Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01503)
*Siyi Chen, Weiming Zhuang, Jingtao Li, Lingjuan Lv*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (84.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了 token 缩减在统一视觉语言模型训练中的可行性与局限性。通过逐层注意力分析，发现视觉理解在深层存在大量冗余，而视觉生成对图像 token 的依赖贯穿所有层。基于此设计了任务特定的加速器，但发现统一训练中任务特定的 token 丢弃会导致参数路径分歧，消除联合优化的协同增益，表明高效统一建模需要保留跨任务共享结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉语言模型（VLM）将视觉理解与视觉生成集成在单一自回归骨干中，但其联合训练计算开销极大，而效率优化方面的研究尚不充分。本文旨在探索 token 缩减方法加速统一 VLM 训练的可行性及其边界。

Method: 首先通过逐层注意力分配分析，揭示视觉理解与视觉生成在图像 token 依赖上的不对称性。然后设计任务特定的加速器，分别针对理解任务和生成任务选择性减少图像 token 计算。最后在统一训练场景下评估这些加速器的效果，分析协同损失的原因。

Result: 任务特定的 token 缩减在孤立训练中能显著提升效率，但在统一训练中会导致协同损失——任务特定的 token 丢弃迫使模型采用不同的参数路径，消除了联合优化中通常存在的相互性能增益。

Conclusion: 高效统一建模需要保留跨任务共享结构，亟需协同感知的加速策略来替代简单的任务特定 token 缩减方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究统一视觉语言模型（VLM）训练中的 token 缩减加速方法，属于多模态模型效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses token reduction limits in unified VLM training, revealing a critical synergy loss that challenges naive acceleration for multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: Unified VLM training efficiency, Token reduction asymmetry, Synergy loss in joint optimization, Task-specific accelerator design, Multimodal understanding vs generation redundancy

Abstract: Unified vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual understanding and visual generation within a single autoregressive backbone, but their joint training is computationally expensive and largely overlooked from an efficiency perspective. In this work, we study the feasibility and limits of token-reduction-based acceleration for unified VLM training. Through a systematic analysis of layerwise attention allocation, we uncover a fundamental asymmetry: visual understanding exhibits substantial late-layer visual redundancy, whereas visual generation maintains persistent dependence on image tokens across depth. Guided by this observation, we design task-specific accelerators that selectively reduce image-token computation for each objective. While these methods achieve significant efficiency gains in isolated settings, we observe a consistent synergy loss under unified training -- task-specific token dropping necessitates divergent parameter pathways and eliminates the mutual performance gains typically observed in joint optimization. Our findings suggest that efficient unified modeling requires preserving shared cross-task structures, highlighting the need for synergy-aware acceleration strategies. Project page: .

</details>


### [263] [Improving Visual Token Reduction via Rectifying Distortions for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01711)
*Hyeonwoo Cho, DongHyeon Baek, Yewon Kim, Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RESTORE 框架，通过校正位置和注意力扭曲来改进多模态大模型中的视觉 token 缩减（VTR）方法，在保持效率的同时提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 中大量视觉 token 导致二次复杂度计算瓶颈，现有 VTR 方法忽略了缩减序列与完整序列之间的位置和注意力一致性，导致表征扭曲，影响下游任务性能。

Method: RESTORE 包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于相对距离的注意力权重校准方法，用于恢复缩减后丢失的视觉注意力；(2) 独特的锚点选择策略用于 token 合并，减少特征平均过程中的信息损失。

Result: 在多个基准上，RESTORE 一致提升了多种 VTR 方法的准确率，实现了最先进的性能，同时保持了计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过显式校正位置和注意力扭曲，可以显著提升视觉 token 缩减的质量，为高效 MLLM 推理提供了一种简单有效的改进方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于通过视觉 token 缩减（VTR）来提升多模态大模型（MLLM）的推理效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化方向。现有 canonical subtopic "efficient_multimodal"（多模态高效化与轻量化）明确涵盖 token 压缩与高效推理，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient MLLM inference via visual token reduction, aligning with user's high priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: visual token reduction, efficient multimodal inference, attention distortion correction, token merging, MLLM acceleration

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in vision-language tasks, yet the quadratic computational complexity arising from the vast number of visual tokens incurs significant memory and latency bottlenecks. While visual token reduction (VTR) strategies have been explored to mitigate this burden, existing methods overlook the positional and attentional consistency between the full and reduced sequences, resulting in a distorted representation. To this end, we propose RESTORE, a novel VTR framework that rectifies the positional and attentional distortions while maintaining efficiency. Specifically, we present a simple yet effective calibration method that restores lost visual attention by augmenting attention weights based on relative distances. We also introduce a distinctive anchor selection for token merging to mitigate information loss during feature averaging. Experimental results on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves the accuracy of various reduction methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance while maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [264] [EvoCut: Multi-Layer Evolution-Aware Visual Token Compression for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01756)
*Hongyu Lu, Feng Zhang, Wenwei Jin, Huanling Hu, Pengfei Zhang, Yao Hu, Jiawei Li, Shikai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EvoCut 是一种无需训练和无需注意力的视觉 token 压缩方法，通过分析视觉编码器中 token 的多层演化方向，利用信息 token 偏离群体演化方向的特征来估计 token 重要性，在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上仅保留 11.1% 的 token 即可保持 94.4% 的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 token 压缩方法通常仅从特定层的注意力分数或表征属性估计 token 重要性，忽略了 token 在视觉编码器各层之间的演化过程，导致重要性估计不完整，压缩后性能损失较大。

Method: 首先分析视觉编码器各层中 token 的演化方向，发现 token 形成多个群体演化方向；进一步观察到信息性 token 倾向于持续偏离这些群体演化方向。基于此，提出 EvoCut，利用多层演化偏差（multi-layer evolution deviation）来估计 token 重要性，无需训练和注意力计算。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上，仅保留 11.1% 的视觉 token 即可保持 94.4% 的平均性能，在多个图像和视频理解基准上验证了方法的有效性和效率-精度平衡能力。

Conclusion: EvoCut 通过捕捉 token 在视觉编码器中的多层演化特征，提供了一种更全面的 token 重要性估计方法，在无需额外训练和注意力计算的前提下实现了高效的视觉 token 压缩，为 LVLMs 的推理加速提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉 token 压缩方法 EvoCut，旨在提升大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的推理效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化方向。该方向已有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal），涵盖 token 压缩等效率优化技术，因此直接复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in multimodal models, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel training-free compression method. Signals: visual token compression, training-free, efficient inference, LVLM acceleration, token evolution analysis

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) achieve strong performance on image and video understanding tasks, but their inference efficiency is constrained by the large number of visual tokens produced by vision encoders. Most existing visual token compression methods estimate token importance from attention scores or representation properties at specific layers, overlooking how visual tokens evolve across the vision encoder. Such layer-specific criteria may provide incomplete importance estimates and limit performance preservation after compression. To address this issue, we analyze layer-wise visual token evolution directions and observe that tokens form multiple group evolution directions across vision-encoder layers. Our analysis further shows that informative tokens tend to exhibit persistent deviations from common group evolution directions. Based on this observation, we propose EvoCut, a training-free and attention-free visual token compression method that estimates token importance from multi-layer evolution deviation. Experimental results show that EvoCut can retain only 11.1\% of the visual tokens on LLaVA-1.5-7B while preserving 94.4\% of the average performance, demonstrating its effectiveness in balancing efficiency and accuracy.

</details>


### [265] [STaR-KV: Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Re-weighting for KV Cache Compression in GUI Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01790)
*Yuhang Han, Wenzheng Yang, Yujie Chen, Xiangqi Jin, Yaojie Zhang, Siteng Huang, Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: STaR-KV 提出了一种无需训练的 KV 缓存压缩框架，通过子空间感知评分、时间稳定性折扣和熵驱动温度自适应调整 token 重要性，在 GUI VLM 上以极低开销实现近 40% 的峰值 GPU 内存削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 视觉语言模型在交互过程中 KV 缓存随步骤线性增长，例如 UI-TARS-1.5-7B 仅处理五张截图就消耗 76 GB GPU 内存，接近主流 80 GB 加速器的上限。现有 KV 压缩方法存在两个错误的结构性假设：将视觉 token 重要性聚合为单一共享显著性图，以及对融合后的分数分布施加固定的 top-B 截断。实验表明注意力子空间存在空间特化且跨层迁移，分数分布形状沿轨迹漂移，因此需要更精细的压缩策略。

Method: STaR-KV 从三个维度校准 token 重要性：(i) 子空间感知评分——通过在线空间互信息识别注意力子空间级别的 token 重要性；(ii) 时间稳定性折扣——抑制持续被关注的子空间中冗余缓存条目；(iii) 熵驱动温度——自适应重塑分数分布形状以适应其动态漂移。该方法无需训练，直接应用于现有 VLM 的 KV 缓存压缩。

Result: 在四个 GUI 基准上，STaR-KV 在相同压缩预算下达到最强平均准确率，优于 GUIKV、SnapKV 等 SOTA 方法。压缩阶段 FLOPs 开销几乎为零（-0.07%），在 20% KV 缓存预算下峰值 GPU 内存降低近 40%。

Conclusion: STaR-KV 通过打破现有 KV 压缩方法的两大错误假设，提出时空自适应重加权框架，在 GUI VLM 上实现了高效且低开销的 KV 缓存压缩，显著降低了部署门槛。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 GUI 视觉语言模型中的 KV 缓存压缩，属于多模态模型推理效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free KV cache compression method for GUI VLMs, aligning with efficient inference and multimodal understanding priorities. Signals: KV cache compression, GUI VLM, training-free, efficient inference, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Vision-language-model-based graphical user interface (GUI) agents have shown broad automation capabilities, yet deployment is bottlenecked by a key-value (KV) cache that grows linearly with interaction steps. For instance, UI-TARS-1.5-7B consumes 76 GB of GPU memory on merely five screenshots, approaching the capacity of mainstream 80 GB accelerators. Existing KV compression methods share two structural assumptions: aggregating visual-token importance into a single shared saliency map, and applying a fixed top-B cutoff to the fused score distribution. Pilot measurements refute both: spatial specialization lives at the attention-subspace level and migrates across layers, while the score distribution drifts in shape along a trajectory. We propose STaR-KV (Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Re-weighting), a training-free KV cache compression framework that calibrates token importance along three axes: (i) subspace-aware scoring driven by online spatial mutual information; (ii) a temporal stability discount that suppresses redundant cache entries from persistently attended subspaces; and (iii) an entropy-derived temperature that adaptively reshapes the score distribution. Across four GUI benchmarks, STaR-KV achieves the strongest average accuracy among state-of-the-art KV compression methods (e.g., GUIKV, SnapKV) at matched budgets, with no compression-stage FLOPs overhead (-0.07%) and cutting peak GPU memory by nearly 40% at a 20% KV-cache budget. Code is available at .

</details>


### [266] [InfoMerge: Information-aware Token Compression for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02161)
*Xinxin Liu, Shiwei Gan, Xiao Liu, Yafeng Yin, Lei Xie, Sanglu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (78.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: InfoMerge 是一种训练无关的视觉 token 压缩方法，通过时序指纹差分（Temporal Fingerprint Difference）进行段级二阶时序冗余估计，并结合内容感知预算分配（CABA）动态分配 token 预算，在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 上减少 85% 视觉 token 的同时保持 98.8% 的平均性能，实现 4.24 倍预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video-LLM 因大量视觉 token 导致计算开销巨大。已有的训练无关压缩方法依赖局部相邻帧相似性估计时序冗余，或按片段长度分配 token 预算，对帧级噪声敏感，无法捕捉真实视频中非均匀的信息分布。

Method: 提出 InfoMerge，包含两个核心模块：(1) 时序指纹差分（Temporal Fingerprint Difference），一种段级二阶时序冗余估计策略，建模同一空间位置 token 在片段内的时序相似性结构；(2) 内容感知预算分配（CABA），基于片段独特性和谱熵表征丰富度动态分配段级 token 预算，减少冗余静态区域的重复保留，将更多 token 分配给信息丰富的片段。

Result: 在多个基准和骨干网络上实现强效率-精度权衡。在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 上，减少 85% 视觉 token 时保持 98.8% 原始平均性能，预填充阶段实现 4.24 倍加速。在更激进的压缩下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: InfoMerge 通过鲁棒的冗余估计和内容感知预算分配，在训练无关条件下有效提升了 Video-LLM 的 token 利用效率和推理速度，为视频大语言模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出训练无关的视觉 token 压缩方法，旨在提升 Video-LLM 的推理效率，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）明确包含 token 压缩，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on efficient inference and multimodal understanding, offering a training-free token compression method with high practical speedup. Signals: training-free token compression, temporal fingerprint difference, content-aware budget allocation, 85% token reduction, 4.24x prefill speedup

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) achieve strong performance in video understanding, but their excessive visual tokens bring substantial computational overhead. Existing training-free compression methods improve inference efficiency by reducing visual tokens, yet they often rely on local adjacent-frame similarity for temporal redundancy estimation or allocate token budgets mainly according to segment length. Such designs are sensitive to frame-level noise and fail to capture the non-uniform information distribution of real-world videos. To address these challenges, we propose InfoMerge, a training-free visual token compression method that improves token utilization through robust redundancy estimation and content-aware budget allocation. Specifically, we propose the Temporal Fingerprint Difference: a segment-level second-order temporal redundancy estimation strategy, which models the temporal similarity structure of tokens at the same spatial positions within each segment. We further introduce Content-Aware Budget Allocation (CABA), which dynamically allocates segment-level token budgets based on segment uniqueness and spectral-entropy-based representational richness. By reducing repeated preservation of redundant static regions and allocating more tokens to informative segments, InfoMerge makes better use of the limited token budget while maintaining strong performance. Extensive experiments show that InfoMerge achieves strong efficiency--accuracy trade-offs across multiple benchmarks and backbones, with more pronounced advantages under aggressive compression. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, InfoMerge retains 98.8\% of the original average performance while reducing 85\% of visual tokens and achieving a 4.24-fold speedup in the prefill stage.

</details>


### [267] [AdaCodec: A Predictive Visual Code for Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02569)
*Haowen Hou, Zhen Huang, Zheming Liang, Qingyi Si, Chenglin Li, Shuai Dong, Kele Shao, Ruilin Li, Dianyi Wang, Nan Duan, Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (90.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AdaCodec 是一种面向视频 MLLM 的预测性视觉编码方法，利用帧间冗余，仅在必要时发送完整参考帧，否则用紧凑的 P-token 编码帧间变化（运动与预测残差），在 1/7 token 预算下超越全帧 RGB 基线，并将首 token 延迟从 9.26s 降至 1.62s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 MLLM 将每个采样帧独立编码为 RGB 图像，导致相邻帧的视觉 token 大量重复，造成计算和存储浪费。作者希望设计一种更直接的视频接口，利用帧间预测来消除冗余，在保持性能的同时大幅降低 token 数量。

Method: AdaCodec 提出预测性视觉编码框架：仅当当前帧无法从先前上下文（参考帧）中良好预测时（即条件预测代价高），才发送完整参考帧（全视觉 token）；否则，编码帧间变化（包括运动信息和预测残差）作为紧凑的 P-token。该框架可即插即用地替换现有视频 MLLM 的帧编码模块。

Result: 在 11 个基准上，AdaCodec 在匹配 token 预算下超越 Qwen3-VL-8B 的逐帧 RGB 基线。在 1/7 token 预算（32k vs 224k）下，AdaCodec 在所有长视频基准上超越基线；在 5 个通用视频基准上平均分数提升，同时首 token 时间从 9.26s 降至 1.62s。

Conclusion: AdaCodec 验证了预测性视觉编码在视频 MLLM 中的有效性，通过利用帧间冗余实现了显著的 token 压缩和推理加速，为构建更高效的视频多模态系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AdaCodec，通过预测性视觉编码（仅在全帧预测代价高时发送完整参考帧，否则发送紧凑的帧间变化 P-token）来大幅压缩视频 MLLM 的视觉 token 数量，属于 MLLM 的 token 压缩与高效推理范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient video MLLM inference via predictive visual coding, aligning with user's high-priority continuous multimodal and efficient inference subtopics. Signals: predictive visual code, video MLLM efficiency, inter-frame redundancy, token budget reduction, time-to-first-token improvement

Abstract: Video is temporally redundant: adjacent frames usually share most objects, background, and layout. Yet existing video multimodal large language models (video MLLMs) usually encode each sampled frame as an independent RGB image, causing visual tokens to repeat content already present in earlier frames. This suggests a more direct video interface: send a full reference frame only when the scene cannot be predicted well from prior context, and otherwise transmit a compact description of inter-frame changes. We call this interface a \emph{predictive visual code}, and instantiate it for video MLLMs as \textbf{AdaCodec}. AdaCodec spends full visual tokens on a reference frame only when its conditional predictive cost is high; otherwise, it encodes inter-frame changes, including motion and prediction residuals, as compact P-tokens. Across all eleven benchmarks, AdaCodec improves over the Qwen3-VL-8B per-frame RGB baseline at a matched visual-token budget. Even at $1/7$ the budget, AdaCodec with 32k tokens surpasses the 224k baseline on all long-video benchmarks; on five general-video benchmarks, it raises the average score while substantially cutting time-to-first-token from 9.26s to 1.62s.

</details>


### [268] [Massive Spikes in LLMs are Bias Vectors: Mechanistic Uncovering and Spike-Free Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02288)
*Yung-Chin Chen, Chung Peng Lee, Ze-Wei Liou, Naveen Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (70.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文揭示 LLM 中的大规模激活尖峰本质上是结构化的向量偏置（而非标量偏置），并基于此机制提出 INSERTQUANT 后训练量化框架，通过钳制尖峰并利用预计算模板向量恢复功能，实现无尖峰的低比特量化，且可推广到 ViT 等多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 中的大规模激活尖峰严重损害量化精度，现有方法对其成因理解不足。本文旨在从机制层面揭示尖峰的本质，并据此设计更优的量化方案。

Method: 通过几何分析证明激活尖峰是结构化的向量偏置：W_K 对比放大向量、W_Q 将语义 token 对齐到该向量、W_V 将其投影到谱零空间。模型利用低频带和相干通道对将偏置定位在"旋转稳定区"以抵抗 RoPE 扰动。基于此提出 INSERTQUANT，通过钳制尖峰并插入预计算模板向量来恢复功能，实现无尖峰量化。

Result: INSERTQUANT 在 LLM 上达到与 SOTA 逐张量量化方法相当的性能，并唯一地泛化到 ViT 等其他模态，实现鲁棒的低比特量化。

Conclusion: 激活尖峰本质上是结构化的向量偏置，理解这一机制可以设计出更有效的量化方法。INSERTQUANT 证明了机制驱动的量化方案在跨模态泛化上的优势。

Classification reason: 论文提出 INSERTQUANT 后训练量化框架，通过消除 LLM 中的激活尖峰实现低比特量化，并推广到 ViT 等多模态模型。属于多模态高效化与轻量化（量化方向），因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and quantization for LLMs and ViTs, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: mechanistic insight into activation spikes, spike-free quantization framework, generalizes to ViTs, enables low-bit quantization, addresses attention sink and value-state drain

Abstract: Massive activation spikes in Large Language Models (LLMs) severely degrade quantization by stretching dynamic ranges. While prior hypotheses characterize these as high-level scalar biases, we argue that they are merely the scalar intermediates of rigid, structural vector biases in the spike-carrying tokens. We show that these tokens converge to constant vectors after normalization that drive the attention sink and value-state drain mechanisms. We geometrically substantiate this by analyzing the coordination of projection weights: $W_K$ contrastively amplifies the vector, $W_Q$ aligns semantic tokens toward it, and $W_V$ projects it into the spectral null-space. Furthermore, we reveal that the model actively preserves these structural biases against Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) perturbations by localizing them in "zones of rotational stability" utilizing low-frequency bands and coherent channel pairs. Leveraging this, we propose INSERTQUANT, a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that clamps spikes and restores their function via pre-computed template vectors. This renders activations strictly spike-free, enabling robust low-bit quantization with high fidelity. INSERTQUANT achieves parity with state-of-the-art per-tensor quantization methods on LLMs and uniquely generalizes beyond text to other modalities such as ViTs.

</details>

### [269] [CoCoVideo: The High-Quality Commercial-Model-Based Contrastive Benchmark for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00101)
*Huidong Feng, Wentao Chen, Jie Chen, Xinqi Cai, Ruolong Ma, Yinglin Zheng, Yuxin Lin, Ming Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoVideo-26K数据集，包含13个主流商业视频生成模型的高质量真伪视频对，并设计了CoCoDetect检测框架，结合对比学习与置信门控MLLM推理，在AI生成视频检测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC视频检测数据集主要依赖开源生成模型，质量远低于商业系统；即使包含商业样本的数据集也常带有可见水印，影响真实性。缺乏高质量、无水印、语义对齐的真伪视频对数据集，限制了模型对高保真AIGC视频的泛化能力。

Method: 构建CoCoVideo-26K数据集：收集13个主流商业视频生成模型（如Sora、Runway等）的生成视频，并与真实视频进行语义对齐配对。提出CoCoDetect检测框架：使用R3D-18骨干网络提取时空表征进行对比学习，同时引入置信门控机制，将低置信度样本路由到MLLM进行物理合理性及场景一致性的推理判断。

Result: 在CoCoVideo-26K及多个公开基准上取得SOTA性能，验证了框架的鲁棒性和泛化能力。数据集和代码已开源。

Conclusion: 高质量商业模型AIGC视频检测需要更真实的数据集和结合对比学习与MLLM推理的混合检测框架，本文提出的方案有效提升了检测性能与泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向AI生成视频检测的对比基准数据集CoCoVideo-26K，并构建了检测框架。核心贡献是数据集与评测基准，属于多模态生成方向下的"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）规范子主题。同时涉及MLLM推理检测，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on AIGC video detection, not on generation or world models, and lacks direct connection to priority subtopics. Signals: AI-generated video detection, commercial model benchmark, contrastive learning, MLLM reasoning, not generation or world model

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of artificial intelligence generated content (AIGC) technologies, video forgery has become increasingly prevalent, posing new challenges to public discourse and societal security. Despite remarkable progress in existing deepfake detection methods, AIGC forgery detection remains challenging, as existing datasets mainly rely on open-source video generation models with quality far below that of commercial AIGC systems. Even datasets containing a few commercial samples often retain visible watermarks, compromising authenticity and hindering model generalization to high-fidelity AIGC videos. To address these issues, we introduce CoCoVideo-26K, a contrastive, commercial-model-based AIGC video dataset covering 13 mainstream commercial generators and providing semantically aligned real-fake video pairs. This dataset enables deeper exploration of the differences between authentic and high-quality synthetic videos and establishes a new benchmark for highly realistic video forgery detection. Building on this dataset, we propose CoCoDetect, a detection framework integrating contrastive learning with confidence-gated multimodal large language model (MLLM) inference. An R3D-18 backbone extracts spatio-temporal representations, while a confidence gate routes uncertain cases to an MLLM for reasoning about physical plausibility and scene consistency. Extensive experiments on CoCoVideo-26K and public benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating the framework's robustness and generalizability. Our code and dataset are available at .

</details>

### [270] [Hierarchical Semantic-Augmented Navigation: Optimal Transport and Graph-Driven Reasoning for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01565)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HSAN（层次化语义增强导航）框架，通过构建动态层次化语义场景图、基于最优传输的拓扑规划器以及图感知强化学习策略，解决了连续环境视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）中的长程任务规划与场景理解难题，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN-CE 方法在长程导航任务中因场景理解有限、规划效率低和缺乏鲁棒决策框架而表现不佳，静态地图和启发式规划器难以应对复杂 3D 室内空间的动态导航需求。

Method: HSAN 框架包含三个核心创新：(1) 利用视觉语言模型构建动态层次化语义场景图，从物体到区域再到区域层级捕获多级环境表征；(2) 基于 Kantorovich 对偶的最优传输拓扑规划器，平衡语义相关性与空间可达性来选择长期目标；(3) 图感知强化学习策略，实现精确的低层控制与子目标导航，同时鲁棒地避开障碍物。

Result: 在多个 VLN-CE 数据集上的大量实验表明，HSAN 达到了最先进的性能，在导航成功率和未知环境泛化能力方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过融合谱图理论、最优传输和先进多模态学习，HSAN 有效克服了静态地图和启发式规划器的局限性，为连续环境中的视觉语言导航提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究连续环境中的视觉语言导航（VLN-CE），涉及空间推理、场景图构建和强化学习规划，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）子主题。同时，论文中的世界建模和规划控制部分也与世界模型方向相关。

Importance reason: Strong VLN-CE paper but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks key boost keywords. Signals: VLN-CE, hierarchical semantic scene graph, optimal transport planning, graph-aware RL, state-of-the-art on multiple benchmarks

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) poses a formidable challenge for autonomous agents, requiring seamless integration of natural language instructions and visual observations to navigate complex 3D indoor spaces. Existing approaches often falter in long-horizon tasks due to limited scene understanding, inefficient planning, and lack of robust decision-making frameworks. We introduce the \textbf{Hierarchical Semantic-Augmented Navigation (HSAN)} framework, a groundbreaking approach that redefines VLN-CE through three synergistic innovations. First, HSAN constructs a dynamic hierarchical semantic scene graph, leveraging vision-language models to capture multi-level environmental representations, from objects to regions to zones, enabling nuanced spatial reasoning. Second, it employs an optimal transport-based topological planner, grounded in Kantorovich's duality, to select long-term goals by balancing semantic relevance and spatial accessibility with theoretical guarantees of optimality. Third, a graph-aware reinforcement learning policy ensures precise low-level control, navigating subgoals while robustly avoiding obstacles. By integrating spectral graph theory, optimal transport, and advanced multi-modal learning, HSAN addresses the shortcomings of static maps and heuristic planners prevalent in prior work. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging VLN-CE datasets demonstrate that HSAN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in navigation success and generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


### [271] [Reasmory: 3D Reconstruction as Explicit Memory for VLMs Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00963)
*Jixuan He, Xueting Li, Chieh Hubert Lin, Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (62.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Reasmory 提出将3D重建作为VLM的显式空间记忆，通过领域特定语言（DSL）约束VLM对3D记忆的查询、变换和渲染操作，实现结构化程序执行式的空间推理，在多视图和视频空间推理基准上提升6-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在需要精确空间理解的任务（如视角推理、方向比较、距离估计）上仍不可靠。多视图图像和单目视频中的空间线索稀疏且分散，难以组织和利用。重建模型虽能聚合观测为显式空间记忆（如点云），但直接作为自由形式工具使用时，VLM可能错误调用工具、跳过必要变换或误用中间结果。

Method: Reasmory 框架包含三个核心组件：1）显式3D记忆构建——通过重建模型将多视图/视频观测聚合为3D点云；2）语义实例增强——将3D点云与语义分割的物体实例关联；3）轻量级领域特定语言（DSL）——约束VLM如何查询物体和相机、变换视角、渲染观测。生成的程序经过解析和验证后再执行，确保与空间记忆的可靠交互。

Result: 在多视图图像和视频空间推理基准上，Reasmory 相比 GPT-5-mini 和 Gemini-3-flash 等强基线取得6-18%的一致提升，表明通过受约束、经过验证的操作访问显式3D记忆比自由形式工具调用更有效。

Conclusion: 显式3D记忆在通过受约束、经过验证的操作访问时对VLM空间推理最有用，Reasmory 的结构化程序执行范式为提升VLM空间推理可靠性提供了有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何利用3D重建作为显式记忆来增强VLM的空间推理能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，完全匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal understanding and spatial reasoning, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or generation priorities. Signals: 3D reconstruction as explicit memory, DSL-constrained spatial reasoning, VLM spatial reasoning benchmark gains, multimodal understanding generation, no direct continuous token or generation focus

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit emerging spatial reasoning capabilities, yet they remain unreliable on tasks requiring precise spatial understanding, such as viewpoint reasoning, directional comparison, and distance estimation. In multi-view images and monocular videos, relevant spatial cues are often sparse and distributed across redundant observations, making them difficult to organize and exploit. Reconstruction-based Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer a natural way to aggregate such observations into explicit spatial memory, such as point clouds. However, simply exposing reconstruction models as free-form tools is brittle, VLMs may invoke tools incorrectly, skip required spatial transformations, or misuse intermediate results. We propose \textbf{Reasmory}, a framework that formulates spatial reasoning as structured program execution over reconstructed spatial memory. Reasmory constructs explicit 3D memory, augments it with semantically grounded 3D object instances, and introduces a lightweight Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that constrains how VLMs query objects and cameras, transform viewpoints, and render observations during reasoning. Generated programs are parsed and validated before execution, enabling more reliable interaction with spatial memory than unconstrained tool use. Experiments on multi-view image and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains of 6--18\% over strong baselines, including GPT-5-mini and Gemini-3-flash, indicating that explicit 3D memory is most useful when accessed through constrained, validated operations rather than free-form tool calls.

</details>


### [272] [MASER: Modality-Adaptive Specialist Routing for Embodied 3D Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02463)
*Hilton Raj, Vishnuram AV*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MASER 提出一种轻量级模态自适应路由框架，为共享 VLM 主干训练五个不同模态适配器，并通过学习一个基于问题语义的神经路由策略，在推理时选择最优适配器进行3D空间问答，在 Open3D-VQA 上验证了路由策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在 3D 环境中通常只针对单一模态（如点云或 RGB）进行微调，忽略了问题语义可能更适合另一种模态的事实。不同问题类型可能从不同模态中获得最佳答案，但现有方法无法根据问题自适应选择最优模态。

Method: MASER 基于共享 VLM 主干，为五种模态（RGB、点云、深度图、RGB+点云、RGB+深度）分别训练轻量适配器。同时，使用 frozen sentence transformer 编码问题文本，通过一个小型 MLP 学习路由策略，该 MLP 在 oracle 适配器准确率标签上训练。推理时仅调用一次所选适配器，实现高效推理。

Result: 在 Open3D-VQA 基准上，点云模态在 51.5% 的问题上表现最佳，说明没有单一模态普遍最优。MASER 路由策略达到 51.3% 的 oracle 一致性，优于随机森林消融实验的 43.5%，且每次推理仅调用一个适配器。

Conclusion: MASER 证明了基于问题语义进行模态自适应路由的有效性，为 3D 具身空间智能中的多模态融合提供了一种轻量级、高效的解决方案，未来可扩展到更多模态和更复杂的路由策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身智能体在3D环境中基于多模态（RGB、点云、深度图等）进行空间问答推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，该子主题涵盖空间推理、3D视觉定位与具身推理。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied 3D spatial VQA routing, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D spatial reasoning, modality routing, VLM adapters, Open3D-VQA, lightweight framework

Abstract: In 3D environments, Embodied Agents answer spatially relevant questions through reasoning from a mixture of modalities including natural language, RGB images, point clouds, depth maps and camera poses. Existing Vision-Language models (VLMs) are fine-tuned over a single modality. This completely ignores the question semantics which may favor a different modality than the finetuned modality. To address this, we propose MASER (Modality-Adaptive SpEcialist Routing), a lightweight framework that trains five different modality adapters of a shared VLM backbone and learns a neural routing policy that selects the best adapter based on the question during inference. We encode each question with a frozen sentence transformer and pass the embedding through a small Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) trained on oracle adapter-accuracy labels. We evaluate our methodology over the Open3D-VQA benchmark and our evaluations show that no single modality is universally optimal -- point-cloud answers are best in 51.5% of cases. MASER routes with 51.3% oracle agreement, outperforming a Random-Forest ablation (43.5%), with only a single adapter call per question.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [273] [Sandboxed Coding Agents are Competitive Omni-modal Task Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00579)
*Dongping Chen, Xuanao Huang, Zhihan Hu, Qingyuan Shi, Dianqi Li, Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (93.07) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文证明，仅具备文本+图像访问能力的编码智能体（coding agent）配合沙盒工具接口，可以在多个音视频基准上匹敌甚至超越SOTA原生全模态模型。通过将全模态任务转化为检索和信息处理问题，编码智能体展现出强大的竞争力。论文还提出了Code-X训练方案、OmniCoding轨迹数据集和TerminalBench-O过程级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"原生全模态模型是解决视频/音频任务的必要条件"这一普遍假设，探索更轻量、更通用的替代方案。

Method: 使用仅支持文本+图像的编码智能体，通过沙盒环境编写代码并编排工具（如转录、帧提取等），将全模态任务转化为检索和信息处理问题。提出Code-X训练方案，包含OmniCoding轨迹数据集和可验证奖励，并在Qwen-3.5-9B和Qwen-3.6-27B上建立基线。

Result: 编码智能体在多个音视频基准上匹配或超越SOTA原生全模态模型；通过技能注入（人工编写和自蒸馏）可显著提升性能；Code-X训练方案在开源模型上取得有效基线。

Conclusion: 编码智能体是原生全模态模型的有力竞争者，将全模态任务转化为检索与信息处理问题是一种有效范式。未来方向是"多模态处理"（many-modality processing），而非简单的多模态感知。

Classification reason: 该论文研究的是使用编码智能体（coding agent）结合沙盒工具接口来解决多模态任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型与MLLM范畴。论文提出的Code-X训练方法、OmniCoding轨迹数据集以及TerminalBench-O基准均围绕多模态任务求解展开，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）高度相关。

Importance reason: Shows coding agents can match native omnimodal models, directly relevant to your interest in unified multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: unified multimodal, coding agent, multimodal understanding, tool-use, efficient inference

Abstract: As multimodal LLMs increasingly target video and audio, it is often assumed that such tasks require native omnimodal models. We show that this is not always the case: coding agents with only text+image access and a sandboxed tool-use interface can match, and in several settings outperform, SOTA native omnimodal models and predefined multimodal agent scaffolds across multiple audio-video benchmarks. Our trajectory analysis suggests that their strength comes from writing code and orchestrating tools to extract relevant evidence from transcripts, frames, and other modality signals, thereby converting omnimodal tasks into retrieval and information-processing problems rather than ingesting entire media streams. We further characterize their limitations through a failure taxonomy and process-level trace analysis, and show that simple skill injection, including human-written and self-distilled skills, substantially improves performance. To explore open-source elicitation, we introduce Code-X, a training recipe with the OmniCoding trajectory dataset and verifiable reward, and provide baselines on Qwen-3.5-9B and Qwen-3.6-27B. Finally, we argue that the next frontier is many-modality processing, and introduce TerminalBench-O, a process-level benchmark for real-world omnimodal processing tasks. Code will be available at .

</details>


### [274] [MLLM-Microscope: Unlocking Hidden Structure Within Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00909)
*Ravil Mussabayev, Rustam Mussabayev*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLLM-Microscope 系统，用于分析多模态大语言模型（LLaVA-NeXT 和 OmniFusion）中隐藏表征的线性度、内在维度和各向异性，揭示了不同模态融合策略对模型内部工作方式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）虽然性能优异，但其内部工作机制尚不清晰。理解隐藏表征的结构（如线性度、维度、各向异性）有助于指导未来模型的设计与优化。

Method: 提出 MLLM-Microscope 分析系统，在 ScienceQA 数据集上评估 LLaVA-NeXT 和 OmniFusion 两个 SOTA MLLM。系统分析各 Transformer 层中多模态 token 嵌入的线性度、内在维度和各向异性，分别考察主流（main stream）和残差流（residual stream）中不同模态 token 的行为。

Result: 两种模型的主流和残差流中，各模态 token 均表现出高度线性行为。LLaVA-NeXT 的图像 token 线性度略有下降，而 OmniFusion 保持稳定。OmniFusion 的图像 token 维度在各层中始终高于 LLaVA-NeXT。OmniFusion 的各向异性在各层中保持较低水平。
