Conclusion: MLLM 的内部工作机制高度依赖于 LLM 之前执行的模态融合方式。MLLM-Microscope 系统能够提供新的洞察，有助于理解 MLLM 内部运作并指导未来模型的设计与优化。

Classification reason: 该论文分析多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的内部表征结构，属于通用 MLLM 分析与理解范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides mechanistic analysis of MLLM representations but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM interpretability, representation linearity, intrinsic dimension, anisotropy, modality fusion analysis

Abstract: This work presents MLLM-Microscope, a novel system designed for analyzing the hidden representations within Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Our system evaluates the linearity, intrinsic dimension, and anisotropy of multimodal token embeddings across transformer layers. Utilizing the ScienceQA dataset, we evaluate two state-of-the-art MLLMs, LLaVA-NeXT and OmniFusion. We find that both the main and residual streams for tokens of both modalities exhibit highly linear behaviors across transformer layers. However, LLaVA-NeXT's image tokens reveal a slight decline in linearity, whereas OmniFusion's remain consistent. Image token dimensions in OmniFusion remain consistently higher across layers compared to LLaVA-NeXT. Also, the OmniFusion's anisotropy is observed to stay consistently low throughout the layers. These findings suggest that the inner workings of MLLMs highly depend on the nature of modality fusion performed before passing the token sequence into LLM. This and other new potential insights obtainable from our system are surely capable of enhancing our understanding of the inner workings of MLLMs, informing future model design and optimization.

</details>


### [275] [Hyperbolic and Evidence-Prioritized Experts for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00275)
*Zijie Zhou, Dandan Zhu, Hangxiangpan Wang, Heng Zhang, Huishen Jiao, Yi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AsyMoE，一种非对称混合专家架构，通过双曲空间跨模态专家和证据优先语言专家，显式建模视觉与语言模态之间的层次关系，缓解深层语言专家依赖参数记忆而丢失上下文证据的问题，在多个 LVLM 基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 方法对视觉和语言模态采用对称架构，忽略了二者处理方式的固有不对称性：文本查询通常描述视觉场景的部分方面，二者是层次包含关系而非平行关系；且深层语言专家逐渐从基于证据的处理转向参数记忆依赖，丢失了对输入视觉和语言信息的 grounding。

Method: 提出 AsyMoE 架构，包含三类专家：1）模态内专家（Intra-modality experts）处理各自模态的特定特征；2）双曲跨模态专家（Hyperbolic inter-modality experts）利用负曲率几何捕获跨模态层次包含关系；3）证据优先语言专家（Evidence-priority language experts）抑制参数记忆激活，在整个网络深度中保持上下文 grounding。

Result: 在多个 LVLM 基准上，AsyMoE 相比基线方法平均提升 1.5%，在幻觉敏感任务上提升高达 3.8%，同时相比稠密模型激活参数减少 25.45%。

Conclusion: 显式建模视觉与语言模态之间的不对称性（层次包含关系与证据依赖）能够有效提升 LVLM 的性能和效率，特别是在缓解幻觉方面具有显著优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AsyMoE，一种面向大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的新型 MoE 架构，属于通用视觉语言模型架构改进，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel asymmetric MoE with hyperbolic geometry for cross-modal hierarchy and evidence-priority experts addresses key LVLM grounding issues. Signals: MoE for LVLMs, hyperbolic space for cross-modal hierarchy, evidence-priority experts reduce hallucination, consistent gains on multiple benchmarks, parameter efficiency

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance on multimodal tasks through scaled architectures and extensive training. Recent studies introduce Mixture of Experts (MoE) into LVLMs for improved computational efficiency. However, existing MoE approaches treat visual and linguistic modalities with symmetric architectures, overlooking the inherent asymmetry in how these two modalities are processed. This asymmetry causes two critical issues. First, text and vision form hierarchical rather than parallel relationships, as text queries typically describe partial aspects of complete visual scenes. Euclidean expert space struggles to encode such containment structures. Second, language experts in deeper layers progressively shift from evidence-based processing to parametric memory dependence, losing grounding in the provided visual and linguistic information. To address these issues, we propose AsyMoE, a novel architecture that explicitly models this asymmetry through three specialized expert groups. Intra-modality experts handle modality-specific processing. Hyperbolic inter-modality experts capture hierarchical cross-modal relationships through negative curvature geometry. Evidence-priority language experts suppress parametric memory activation and maintain contextual grounding throughout network depth. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AsyMoE achieves consistent improvements over baseline methods, with average gains of 1.5\% over MoE variants and up to 3.8\% on hallucination-sensitive tasks. AsyMoE activates 25.45\% fewer parameters compared to dense models.

</details>


### [276] [Zamba2-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00390)
*Hassan Shapourian, Kasra Hejazi, Olabode M. Sule, Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (90.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Zamba2-VL 是基于 Zamba2 混合架构（Mamba2 SSM + 共享 Transformer 块）的视觉语言模型系列，在图像理解、推理、OCR、定位等基准上与同规模 Transformer VLM 竞争，同时凭借近线性预填充计算和小的循环状态，在时延上实现约一个数量级的 TTFT 降低，特别适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 架构的 VLM 在长序列推理时存在二次计算复杂度和高时延问题，限制了其在边缘设备上的部署。作者希望利用 SSM 架构（Mamba2）的线性复杂度优势，构建兼具高性能与高效率的 VLM。

Method: 基于 Zamba2 混合语言骨干（Mamba2 SSM 层 + 少量共享 Transformer 块），采用视觉编码器 + 投影层将图像特征映射到语言空间，构建 1.2B、2.7B 和 7B 三个规模的 VLM。继承 Zamba2 的近线性预填充计算和小循环状态特性，实现低 TTFT。

Result: 在图像理解、推理、OCR、定位、计数等基准上与 Molmo2、Qwen3-VL、InternVL3.5 等 Transformer VLM 竞争，显著优于 VL-Mamba、Cobra、mmMamba 等先前 SSM/混合 VLM。TTFT 比 Transformer 基线低约一个数量级，小规模模型优势最明显。

Conclusion: Zamba2-VL 证明了混合 SSM-Transformer 架构在 VLM 中的有效性，在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低推理时延，为边缘设备上的高效多模态部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Zamba2-VL，一套基于混合 SSM-Transformer 架构的视觉语言模型，属于通用 VLM 架构设计，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal models with a hybrid SSM-Transformer VLM achieving near-linear prefill and order-of-magnitude TTFT reduction, highly aligned with user's priority on efficient continuous VLM and MLLM. Signals: Hybrid Mamba2-Transformer VLM, Near-linear prefill compute, Order-of-magnitude TTFT reduction, Edge-deployment focus, Competes with leading Transformer VLMs

Abstract: We present Zamba2-VL, a suite of vision-language models built on Zamba2, a hybrid language-model architecture combining Mamba2 state-space layers with a small number of shared transformer blocks. Across a broad range of image understanding, reasoning, OCR, grounding, and counting benchmarks, Zamba2-VL is competitive with leading Transformer-based open-weight VLMs of comparable scale, including the Molmo2, Qwen3-VL, and InternVL3.5 families, and substantially outperforms prior SSM-based and hybrid VLMs such as VL-Mamba, Cobra, and mmMamba. Inheriting the near-linear prefill compute and small, near-constant recurrent state of its Zamba2 backbone, Zamba2-VL delivers roughly an order of magnitude lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) than these Transformer baselines at matched parameter scale, with the efficiency gap most pronounced at the smaller 1.2B and 2.7B scales most relevant to on-device and edge deployment. We release three models -- 1.2B, 2.7B, and 7B -- together with inference code at .

</details>


### [277] [V-LynX: Token Interface Alignment for Video+X LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00508)
*Jungin Park, Jiyoung Lee, Kwanghoon Sohn*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (89.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: V-LynX 发现 Video LLM 中视觉 token 会形成连续流形（token interface），并利用这一特性设计轻量级辅助通路，通过注意力响应和统计分布对齐，将音频、3D 等新模态集成到冻结的 Video LLM 中，无需配对数据或重编码器，在音视频 QA、3D 推理、高帧率和多视角视频理解上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video LLM 集成新模态（如音频、3D）通常需要重训练或添加重编码器，成本高且依赖配对数据。作者发现 Video LLM 中视觉 token 并非简单映射为文本嵌入，而是形成连续流形（token interface），可作为独立实体操作。基于此发现，提出利用该接口高效集成新模态的方法。

Method: V-LynX 在冻结的视觉编码器旁添加轻量级辅助通路，使用未配对的单模态数据，通过两种对齐方式将新模态 token 与视频 token 对齐：(1) 注意力响应对齐（attention response alignment），使新模态 token 在 LLM 中的注意力模式与视频 token 一致；(2) 统计分布对齐（statistical distribution alignment），确保新模态 token 的分布与视频 token 流形兼容。整个过程无需配对数据，保持 Video LLM 参数冻结。

Result: 在音视频 QA、3D 推理、高帧率视频理解、多视角视频理解等多个基准上达到 SOTA，且参数量和计算开销远低于需要重编码器或微调的方法。消融实验验证了注意力对齐和分布对齐各自的有效性。

Conclusion: V-LynX 验证了 Video LLM 中 token interface 的存在及其作为通用多模态集成入口的可行性，为高效、可扩展的多模态 LLM 架构提供了新范式，未来可推广到更多模态。

Classification reason: 论文提出 V-LynX 框架，通过 token interface alignment 将新模态（音频、3D 等）集成到 Video LLM 中，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构改进。同时论文发现 Video LLM 中视觉 token 形成连续流形（token interface），与连续语言模型与多模态方向相关，但主要贡献在 MLLM 架构扩展。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal token interface alignment, a top-priority subtopic, with efficient integration of new modalities into frozen Video LLMs. Signals: continuous manifold token interface, token interface alignment, lightweight auxiliary pathway, unpaired unimodal data integration, SOTA audio-visual QA and 3D reasoning

Abstract: This study introduces an intriguing phenomenon in Video LLMs: rather than merely translating frames into textual embeddings, Video LLMs establish a continuous manifold, token interface, allowing visual tokens to operate as standalone entities within the architecture. Exploiting this discovery, we propose V-LynX, a scalable framework that integrates novel modalities into Video LLMs by repurposing the internalized interface. Departing from conventional paradigms that necessitate heavy modality-specific encoders or paired supervision, V-LynX employs a lightweight auxiliary pathway in parallel with the frozen vision encoder. Our method integrates new sensory inputs with intrinsic video priors by aligning both attention responses and statistical distributions using unpaired unimodal data sets. This ensures manifold compatibility while preserving the integrity of the Video LLMs. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that V-LynX achieves SOTA and efficiency across audio-visual QA, 3D reasoning, high-frame-rate, and multi-view video understanding. The code is available at .

</details>


### [278] [MMG2Skill: Can Agents Distill In-the-Wild Guides into Self-Evolving Skills?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01993)
*Xinyu Che, Junqi Xiong, Yunfei Ge, Xinping Lei, Shihao Li, Hang Yan, Han Li, Yuanxing Zhang, Zhiqi Bai, Jinhua Hao, Ming Sun, Han Li, Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMG2Skill 框架，将网络上的多模态指南转化为智能体可执行的技能，并通过轨迹级根因反馈闭环优化技能。同时构建了首个 guide-to-skill 学习基准 MMG2Skill-Bench。在 GUI 控制、开放游戏和策略卡牌三个领域、六种 VLM 骨干上，该方法一致优于基线，宏平均提升 12.8-25.3 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络上存在大量多模态、异构、含噪声的程序性知识（如教程、指南），但这些知识面向人类执行者，难以直接被智能体用作可执行技能。现有方法要么依赖人工标注技能，要么无法有效利用这些非结构化指南。因此需要一种自动将网络指南转化为智能体技能并持续改进的方法。

Method: 提出 MMG2Skill 闭环框架：(1) 将多模态指南编译为结构化、可编辑的技能（skill）；(2) 固定 VLM 智能体在执行时依赖这些技能进行决策；(3) 执行后从轨迹级根因反馈中自动修订技能，无需使用基准分数。同时构建了 MMG2Skill-Bench 基准，覆盖 GUI 控制、开放游戏和策略卡牌三类任务。

Result: 在六个 VLM 骨干（含不同规模和类型）上，MMG2Skill 在所有模型-领域设置中一致优于基线，宏平均提升 12.8-25.3 个百分点。消融实验表明，直接用原始指南提示智能体会降低性能，而结构化技能构建和轨迹驱动修订两者缺一不可。在可推断成功的任务中，基于分析器的早停机制可防止后期性能退化并节省 25%-53% 的尝试次数。

Conclusion: MMG2Skill 有效弥合了人类指南与智能体技能之间的鸿沟，证明了从网络指南中蒸馏可执行技能并闭环优化的可行性。该框架不依赖基准分数，仅通过轨迹级反馈即可持续改进技能，为智能体利用网络知识解决长程任务提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何将多模态网络指南转化为智能体可执行的技能，并利用 VLM 作为执行骨干进行闭环优化。核心涉及 VLM 智能体的技能学习与执行，属于通用视觉语言模型应用范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent skill learning from web guides, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal agent skill learning, Guide-to-skill benchmark, Closed-loop self-improvement, VLM-based execution, Not aligned with core priority subtopics

Abstract: Abundant procedural knowledge on the Web holds great potential for helping agents solve long-horizon tasks. However, such knowledge is often multimodal, heterogeneous, noisy, and implicitly assumes human executors, making it difficult to use directly as the skills required by agents. To bridge the gap between human-oriented guides and agent-executable skills, we formalize this problem as guide-to-skill learning: converting in-the-wild guides into executable skills and continuously improving them from trajectories observable to the agent. To evaluate the capability of existing agents on this task, we introduce MMG2Skill-Bench, the first benchmark designed for this problem. We further propose MMG2Skill, a closed-loop framework that compiles guides into editable skills, conditions a fixed vision-language model (VLM) agent on these skills during execution, and revises the skills from trajectory-level root-cause feedback without using benchmark scores. Across GUI control, open-ended gameplay, and strategic card play with six VLM backbones, MMG2Skill consistently outperforms vanilla baseline agents in every model-domain setting, achieving macro-average gains of +12.8 to +25.3 percentage points across backbones. Ablation studies show that directly prompting agents with raw guides can degrade performance, while both structured skill construction and trajectory-driven revision are necessary for the observed improvements. On success-inferable tasks, analyzer-based early stopping further prevents late-stage performance regressions and saves 25%-53% of attempts when the success signal is properly calibrated.

</details>


### [279] [Feature Alignment Determines Fusion Strategy: A Comparative Study of Cross-Attention and Concatenation in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01207)
*Zhiqiang Zhou, Xuezhen Xie*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验证明，特征对齐质量而非数据规模是多模态融合中交叉注意力与拼接策略选择的主要决定因素。当特征已通过视觉-语言预训练对齐时，拼接在样本效率上显著优于交叉注意力（4.1-5.1个百分点），因为拼接只需 O(d_v+d_t) 样本学习融合投影，而交叉注意力需要 O(d_v*d_t) 样本学习双线性注意力权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合中交叉注意力与拼接的选择长期依赖实践者直觉而非原理性理解，缺乏理论指导的决策框架。

Method: 在 Flickr8k 数据集上使用 ResNet18 和 CLIP ViT-B/32 两种特征提取骨干进行受控实验，结合样本复杂度分析从理论上解释两种融合策略的差异，并通过对齐退化实验验证特征对齐质量的影响。

Result: 拼接在特征预对齐时在所有测试规模（2048-16384样本）上优于交叉注意力4.1-5.1个百分点；交叉注意力需要超过256倍样本才能达到与拼接相当的融合投影学习效果；特征对齐退化时拼接的优势从1.3%增长到2.8%。

Conclusion: 特征对齐质量是决定多模态融合策略选择的首要因素，为多模态系统设计提供了基于原理的决策框架，对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的架构设计具有直接指导意义。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态融合策略（交叉注意力 vs 拼接），直接涉及多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的架构设计，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: Provides a principled, sample-complexity-based framework for choosing between cross-attention and concatenation in multimodal fusion, directly informing MLLM design. Signals: Feature alignment determines fusion strategy, Concatenation more sample-efficient than cross-attention, O(d_v+d_t) vs O(d_v*d_t) sample complexity, Directly relevant to unified multimodal model design, Controlled experiments with CLIP and ResNet backbones

Abstract: The choice between cross-attention and concatenation for multimodal fusion remains governed by practitioner intuition rather than principled understanding. In this paper, we demonstrate that feature alignment quality, not data scale alone, is the primary determinant of which fusion strategy excels. Through controlled experiments on Flickr8k using two feature extraction backbones (ResNet18 and CLIP ViT-B/32), we show that concatenation outperforms cross-attention by 4.1-5.1 percentage points across all tested scales (2048-16384 samples) when features are pre-aligned by a vision-language pretraining objective. We provide a theoretical explanation grounded in sample complexity analysis: concatenation requires O(d_v + d_t) samples to learn its fusion projection, while cross-attention requires O(d_v * d_t) samples to learn bilinear attention weights, over 256 times as many for 512-dimensional CLIP features. When features are already aligned, the approximation error gap between the two methods vanishes, and concatenation's sample efficiency dominates at all practical dataset sizes. An alignment degradation study confirms a monotonic trend: as feature alignment degrades, concatenation's advantage grows from 1.3% to 2.8%. These findings provide a principled decision framework for fusion method selection in multimodal systems, with direct implications for the design of Multimodal Large Language Models.

</details>


### [280] [Agent Skills Should Go Beyond Text: The Case for Visual Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01414)
*Binxiao Xu, Ruichuan An, Bocheng Zou, Hang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文论证了现有智能体技能学习仅依赖文本（指令、推理轨迹等）的局限性，提出多模态技能范式（Visual Skills），将静态先验、动态先验和交错视觉技能三种形式与文本逻辑结合，并设计了自动构建系统SYSTEM。实验表明，在GUI等视觉密集型任务中，多模态技能显著优于纯文本技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能学习方法将可复用经验存储为纯文本资产（如指令、推理轨迹），但许多视觉密集型任务（如GUI操作）中的可复用知识依赖于空间布局、视觉定位、细粒度外观和局部状态变化，纯文本范式无法有效编码这些视觉信息，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出多模态技能范式，包含三种形式：(1) 静态先验——稳定的空间约定；(2) 动态先验——原位视觉工作记忆；(3) 交错视觉技能——将有序文本步骤与源帧、截图或页面区域绑定。同时提出自动构建系统SYSTEM，从任务轨迹中保留文本推理、空间参考、视觉边界和交互模式，自动转换为可复用的多模态技能。

Result: 在GUI及其他视觉密集型任务上的实验表明，多模态技能一致性地优于纯文本技能，尤其是在需要空间对应、视觉证据和状态感知交互的任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: 可复用的智能体技能应超越纯文本，成为多模态资产，以支持未来多模态智能体的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态技能（visual skills）范式，将视觉信息（空间布局、视觉边界、截图等）与文本逻辑结合，用于增强智能体能力。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型与MLLM范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）相关，因为论文核心是让智能体技能从纯文本扩展到多模态视觉资产，涉及多模态指令与交互。

Importance reason: Proposes visual skills beyond text for agents, aligning with multimodal understanding and generation priorities. Signals: multimodal skill paradigm, visual-centric tasks, GUI tasks, static/dynamic priors, automatic skill construction

Abstract: Reusable skills are a key mechanism for extending agent capabilities, allowing agents to accumulate experience and solve increasingly complex tasks. Yet most existing skill-learning methods store reusable experience as text-only assets, such as instructions, reasoning traces, or summarized trajectories. We argue that this text-only paradigm creates a fundamental bottleneck for visual-centric tasks, where reusable knowledge often depends on spatial layout, visual grounding, fine-grained appearance, and localized state changes. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{\NAME}, a multimodal skill paradigm that combines declarative textual logic with explicit visual support. We distinguish three reusable forms: static priors for stable spatial conventions, dynamic priors for in-situ visual working memory, and interleaved visual skills that bind ordered text steps to the source frames, screenshots, or page regions that justify them. Rather than only describing what to do, visual skills also encode where to look, how to inspect, and how to verify visual outcomes. To scale visual-skill construction, we introduce \textbf{\SYSTEM}, an automatic system that converts agent experience into reusable multimodal skills by preserving textual reasoning, spatial references, visual boundaries, and interaction patterns from task trajectories. Experiments on GUI and other visual-centric tasks show that visual skills consistently outperform text-only skills, particularly when success requires spatial correspondence, visual evidence, and state-aware interaction. These results support our central position: reusable agent skills should go beyond text and become multimodal assets for future multimodal agents.

</details>


### [281] [Beyond Low-Rank: Low-Rank Sparse Prompting via Spiking Neural Network and Prompt Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01945)
*Yumiao Zhao, Bo Jiang, Beibei Wang, Xixi Wan, Xiao Wang, Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoRSP（Low-Rank Sparse Prompting），一种结合脉冲神经网络（SNN）的低秩稀疏视觉提示框架，通过低秩分解构造提示因子并利用 SNN 的脉冲发放机制生成实例级稀疏提示，在多个视觉骨干网络上实现高效且鲁棒的迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示方法通常使用密集像素级提示，存在冗余扰动、泛化能力有限和能效低的问题。受大脑启发的脉冲神经元具有离散脉冲发放和稀疏输出的特性，作者希望将这种生物机制引入视觉提示学习，以实现更紧凑、鲁棒且节能的提示适配。

Method: LoRSP 首先通过低秩分解构造一组提示因子（prompt factors）来捕获不同的提示子空间，然后将这些因子输入脉冲神经网络（SNN）架构，通过 integrate-and-fire 过程发放脉冲，最终生成稀疏的视觉提示。低秩约束与 SNN 的稀疏发放机制共同保证了提示的紧凑性和实例级选择性。

Result: 在五种异构视觉骨干网络（如 ViT、Swin、ConvNeXt 等）和多个基准数据集上的实验表明，LoRSP 在保持更少可调参数的同时，取得了与现有 VP 方法相当或更优的性能，验证了低秩稀疏提示的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LoRSP 成功将脉冲神经网络的稀疏发放机制引入视觉提示学习，通过低秩分解与 SNN 的结合实现了实例级选择性稀疏提示，为高效、鲁棒的视觉模型适配提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉提示（Visual Prompting）方法，属于视觉语言模型/预训练视觉模型高效适配的下游任务，与 VLM/MLLM 通用架构中的提示学习范式紧密相关，因此归入 canonical subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: Combines SNN with low-rank prompting, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Low-rank sparse prompting, Spiking neural network, Visual prompt learning, Instance-specific adaptation, Energy-efficient adaptation

Abstract: Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as an efficient paradigm for adapting large-scale pre-trained vision models to downstream tasks by incorporating learnable prompts at the input level. However, existing VP methods typically employ dense pixel-level prompts, which often suffer from redundant perturbations, limited generalization and energy inefficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose to integrate brain-inspired spiking learning into visual prompt learning tasks. As we know that spiking neuron can perform inexpensive information processing by transmitting the input data into discrete spike trains and return sparse outputs. Inspired by this, we propose \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank visual \textbf{S}pike \textbf{P}rompting (LoRSP), a novel framework that learns dynamic low-rank sparse visual prompts naturally via a Spiking neuron learning mechanism. The core idea of LoRSP is to exploit the brain-inspired sparse firing mechanism of spiking neurons to generate pixel-level sparse prompt for each instance. To be specific, we first construct a series of prompt factors via low-rank factorization to capture distinct prompt subspaces. These prompt factors are then fed into an SNN architecture, which performs the integrate-and-fire process to emit spikes. As a result, our LoRSP generates a \emph{sparse} visual prompt while maintaining the low-rank constraint. This design enables instance-specific selective prompting, leading to more compact and robust adaptation across diverse downstream tasks. Extensive experiments on five heterogeneous vision backbones and multiple benchmarks demonstrate that LoRSP achieves competitive performance while requiring fewer tunable parameters compared to existing VP methods.

</details>


### [282] [ToolFG: Towards Well-Grounded Fine-Grained Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02518)
*Yu Xue, Haoxuan Qu, Zhuoling Li, Yihang Lou, Yan Bai, Hossein Rahmani, Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ToolFG，首个面向细粒度图像分类的工具集成 MLLM 框架，通过 MCTS 引导的工具使用知识蒸馏和模型-工具协同进化机制，使 MLLM 能自主调用外部工具获取可靠视觉线索，显著提升细粒度分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度图像分类（FGIC）需要区分高度相似的类别，传统方法依赖模型内部知识，缺乏可靠的外部验证机制。现有 MLLM 在 FGIC 上表现有限，且缺乏灵活使用外部工具来获取可验证视觉线索的能力。

Method: ToolFG 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 工具集，包含多个可调用的外部视觉工具（如目标检测、OCR、颜色识别等）；(2) MCTS 引导的工具使用知识蒸馏机制，从高级闭源 MLLM 中挖掘工具使用和 FGIC 相关知识来训练模型；(3) 模型-工具协同进化机制，联合优化工具集和模型的工具使用策略，使其相互适应并专精于 FGIC 任务。

Result: 在多个细粒度图像分类基准上的大量实验表明，ToolFG 框架显著优于现有方法，验证了工具集成 MLLM 在 FGIC 任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: ToolFG 首次将工具集成引入细粒度图像分类，通过让 MLLM 自主调用外部工具获取可靠视觉线索，实现了更可靠、更扎实的分类结果。MCTS 知识蒸馏和模型-工具协同进化机制为未来 MLLM 工具使用研究提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ToolFG，一个基于 MLLM 的工具集成细粒度图像分类框架，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构的范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on fine-grained classification via tool-augmented MLLM, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: MLLM tool use, fine-grained classification, MCTS knowledge distillation, model-tool co-evolution, not autoregressive or continuous token

Abstract: Fine-grained image classification (FGIC) has broad applications and has attracted significant research attention. In this paper, we explore a novel paradigm for solving FGIC by proposing \textbf{ToolFG}, the first tool-integrated MLLM-based framework tailored to FGIC. ToolFG enables MLLMs to autonomously and flexibly use external tools during the reasoning process, actively interact with images, and collect verifiable visual cues for distinguishing highly similar categories in a more \textit{reliable} and \textit{well-grounded} manner. To equip the model with such tool-use ability, we design a novel \textbf{MCTS-guided tool-use knowledge distillation mechanism}, which effectively mines tool-use- and FGIC-relevant knowledge from advanced proprietary MLLMs for model training. Furthermore, we propose a \textbf{model-tool co-evolution mechanism} that jointly refines the toolset and the model's tool-use policy, driving them toward a mutually adapted and FGIC-specialized state. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [283] [ProtoAda: Prototype-Guided Adaptive Adapter Expansion and Geometric Consolidation for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02576)
*Yu-Cheng Shi, Zhen-Hao Xie, Jun-Tao Tang, Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ProtoAda 提出了一种原型引导的自适应适配器扩展与几何整合框架，用于多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）。通过格式感知的任务原型解决传统基于图文相似度路由导致的"格式盲"任务分配问题，并利用几何感知的方式整合格式兼容的更新，有效缓解了任务间梯度干扰，在多个基准上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCIT 方法使用 Mixture of LoRA Experts 结合图文相似度路由，但不同任务即使语义相似也可能具有不同的输出结构（如 VQA 输出短文本 vs. 接地任务输出坐标），导致路由错误、梯度干扰和专家协作低效。需要一种能同时感知任务语义和输出格式的任务分配机制。

Method: ProtoAda 包含两个核心组件：(1) 格式感知任务原型（Format-Aware Task Prototypes），为每个任务学习一个原型向量，同时编码任务语义和输出结构信息，用于指导路由和任务分配；(2) 几何感知整合（Geometry-Aware Consolidation），在参数空间中利用格式兼容性进行几何约束的更新整合，重用并逐步精炼已有专家参数。

Result: 在多个 MCIT 基准上，ProtoAda 显著优于现有方法，尤其是在输出结构容易被顺序微调破坏的任务（如接地任务）上表现突出，有效缓解了格式盲分配导致的性能退化。

Conclusion: ProtoAda 通过引入格式感知的原型引导机制和几何感知的参数整合，有效解决了多模态持续指令微调中因任务输出结构差异导致的梯度干扰问题，为 MLLM 的持续学习提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的持续指令微调（Continual Instruction Tuning），属于 MLLM 架构与训练范式的改进，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses multimodal continual instruction tuning with a novel prototype-guided method, highly relevant to user's priority on unified multimodal models and MLLMs. Signals: multimodal continual instruction tuning, prototype-guided routing, format-aware task prototypes, geometric consolidation, MLLM

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, but real-world deployment requires them to continually acquire new vision-language capabilities, making Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) essential. To reduce inter-task interference and promote collaboration, recent methods often employ sparse architectures like Mixture of LoRA Experts with image-text similarity routing. However, tasks with distinct response structures could share highly similar visual-linguistic semantics and thus be wrongly routed to the same expert; image-text similarity alone is insufficient for reliable task assignment. For example, an expert in a grounding task requiring coordinate prediction may be biased toward producing short textual answers after learning semantically similar VQA tasks. This format-blind task assignment integrates heterogeneous response types into shared parameters, inducing gradient interference and ineffective expert collaboration. To address this problem, we propose ProtoAda, a prototype-guided adaptive tuning framework. ProtoAda introduces format-aware task prototypes to align task assignment and routing with both task semantics and output structure, and further consolidates format-compatible updates in a geometry-aware manner to effectively reuse and progressively refine existing parameters. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ProtoAda achieves superior performance, especially on tasks whose answer structures are easily corrupted by sequential tuning.

</details>


### [284] [Thinking in Blender: Staged Executable Inverse Graphics with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02580)
*Guangzhao He, Rundong Luo, Wei-Chiu Ma, Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SEIG框架，利用预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）从单张图像逐步重建可编辑的3D Blender场景，涵盖几何、材质、构图和光照，无需专用3D模型或可微渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆图形（inverse graphics）是一个长期存在且高度欠约束的问题，传统方法依赖专用2D/3D基础模型、可微渲染或多视角监督。本文探索能否仅用通用预训练VLM直接从单张图像重建可编辑的3D场景。

Method: 提出Staged Executable Inverse Graphics (SEIG)框架，将逆图形任务分解为多个阶段（几何、材质、构图、光照），在每个阶段通过VLM逐步生成和细化Blender Python代码，直接在可执行的Blender代码空间中重建3D场景。

Result: 在多种场景上的实验表明，分阶段重建（staged reconstruction）显著优于端到端方法，在像素级、感知级和语义级重建指标上均有提升。重建的Blender场景支持渲染、重光照和编辑等多种下游应用。

Conclusion: 通用预训练VLM能够通过任务分解实现可执行的逆图形重建，分阶段策略对提升重建质量至关重要，为无需专用模型的可编辑3D场景重建提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）从单张图像重建可编辑的3D场景（Blender程序），属于通用VLM的应用与能力探索，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"（vlm_mllm_general）。同时论文涉及3D场景生成，也与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel staged inverse graphics framework using VLMs, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM-based 3D reconstruction, staged executable inverse graphics, Blender program generation, single-image 3D scene editing, no specialized 3D models needed

Abstract: Inverse graphics is a longstanding and highly underconstrained problem that seeks to reconstruct images as editable 3D scenes which can be rendered, relit, and manipulated. In this work, we investigate whether pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) can perform executable inverse graphics directly from a single image by reconstructing a scene as an editable Blender program, without relying on specialized 2D or 3D foundation models, differentiable rendering, or multi-view supervision. We introduce Staged Executable Inverse Graphics (SEIG), an agentic framework that reconstructs a 3D scene from a single image by progressively refining scene factors including geometry, materials, composition, and lighting directly in executable Blender code space. We evaluate our framework across diverse scenes using a range of reconstruction metrics spanning pixel-level, perceptual, and semantic fidelity. Our experiments show that staged reconstruction substantially improves reconstruction fidelity, highlighting the importance of task decomposition for executable inverse graphics with general-purpose VLMs. Finally, we showcase various downstream applications enabled by the reconstructed editable Blender scenes.

</details>


### [285] [MOSS-Audio Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01802)
*Chen Yang, Chufan Yu, Hanfu Chen, Jie Zhu, Jingqi Chen, Ke Chen, Wenxuan Wang, Yang Wang, Yaozhou Jiang, Yi Jiang, Zhengyuan Lin, Ziqi Chen, Zhaoye Fei, Chenghao Liu, Jun Zhan, Kang Yu, Kexin Huang, Mingshu Chen, Qinyuan Cheng, Ruixiao Li, Shimin Li, Songlin Wang, Yang Gao, Yiyang Zhang, Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (78.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MOSS-Audio 是一个统一的音频-语言模型，支持语音、环境音和音乐的理解，涵盖音频描述、时间感知问答、带时间戳的转录和音频推理。模型采用音频编码器+模态适配器+大语言模型架构，核心设计包括 DeepStack 跨层特征注入和时间标记机制，并在大规模音频-语言数据上预训练后经过多阶段后训练，发布 4B 和 8B 两种规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-语言模型通常只关注单一音频类型（如仅语音或仅环境音），缺乏统一的音频理解框架。同时，音频理解中的时间定位（如时间戳转录、时间感知问答）能力不足。作者希望构建一个统一的音频-语言模型，能够同时处理语音、环境音和音乐，并具备时间感知的推理能力。

Method: MOSS-Audio 由三部分组成：音频编码器（产生 12.5Hz 时序表征）、模态适配器（将音频表征投影到语言模型空间）和大语言模型（自回归生成文本）。两个关键设计：(1) DeepStack 跨层特征注入——将编码器多个深度的声学信息暴露给解码器；(2) 时间标记——在音频 token 流中插入显式的时间戳标记。数据层面设计了事件保持的音频标注流水线，在连贯事件边界分割音频，分别对语音、音乐和通用音频进行分支标注，合并为统一描述用于预训练，并保留分支标注用于构建 SFT 数据。模型在大规模音频-语言数据上预训练（含时间感知目标），然后进行多阶段后训练以增强指令跟随和音频推理能力。

Result: MOSS-Audio 在通用音频理解、语音描述、ASR 和带时间戳的 ASR 等任务上取得了强性能，发布 4B 和 8B 两种规模的 Instruct 和 Thinking 配置版本。

Conclusion: MOSS-Audio 作为一个统一的音频-语言模型，通过 DeepStack 跨层特征注入和时间标记机制，在多种音频理解任务上表现出色，为未来的语音代理提供了一个有前景的理解基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MOSS-Audio，一个统一的音频-语言模型，支持语音、环境音和音乐的理解与推理。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴（音频作为另一种模态），因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: While technically solid, this audio-language model paper is only tangentially related to your core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Audio-language model, DeepStack cross-layer injection, Time markers, Event-preserving annotation, Unified audio understanding

Abstract: MOSS-Audio is a unified audio-language model for speech, environmental sound, and music understanding, supporting audio captioning, time-aware question answering, timestamped transcription, and audio-grounded reasoning. MOSS-Audio couples a dedicated audio encoder with a modality adapter and a large language model: the encoder produces 12.5 Hz temporal representations, the adapter projects them into the decoder space, and the decoder generates autoregressive text outputs. Two design choices are central to the system: \textbf{DeepStack cross-layer feature injection}, which exposes the decoder to acoustic information from multiple encoder depths, and \textbf{time markers}, which provide explicit temporal cues by inserting timestamp markers into the audio-token stream. At the data level, we design an event-preserving audio annotation pipeline that segments raw audio at coherent event boundaries, applies branch-specific annotation to speech, music, and general audio, and merges the results into unified captions for pretraining. The intermediate branch-specific captions are further retained to support the construction of task-oriented SFT data. The model is pretrained on large-scale audio-language data, with time-aware objectives incorporated to support temporal grounding, and then undergoes multi-stage post-training to enhance instruction following and audio-grounded reasoning. We release 4B and 8B variants in both Instruct and Thinking configurations. MOSS-Audio achieves strong performance across general audio understanding, speech captioning, ASR, and timestamped ASR, positioning it as a promising understanding foundation for future voice agents.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [286] [Turing Patterns for Multimedia: Reaction-Diffusion Multi-Modal Fusion for Language-Guided Video Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01615)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Wei Ji, Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出反应-扩散多模态融合（RDMF）框架，受图灵斑图理论启发，将视频-语言对齐建模为反应-扩散过程，用于视频时刻检索与高亮检测。视频特征通过扩散捕获时序上下文，文本-视频交互通过非线性反应增强相关特征并抑制噪声，利用 Gray-Scott 模型实现高效融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型依赖静态交叉注意力或提示调优机制，无法自适应建模模态间动态演化的关系，导致对齐不佳且泛化有限。作者受系统生物学中图灵斑图形成原理启发，希望将视频-语言对齐重新构想为反应-扩散过程，以更好地捕获时序视频与文本语义之间的非线性动态交互。

Method: 提出 RDMF（Reaction-Diffusion Multimodal Fusion）框架：1）视频特征在时间维度上扩散以捕获时序上下文；2）文本-视频交互建模为非线性反应，放大相关特征并抑制噪声；3）采用 Gray-Scott 反应-扩散模型设计高效融合模块；4）利用图灵不稳定性判据进行稳定性与收敛性的数学分析；5）结合预训练编码器和 DETR 风格头部实现时刻检索与显著性预测。

Result: 初步实验表明 RDMF 在识别视频显著时刻方面有潜力超越现有方法，论文尚未给出完整定量结果，但提供了理论分析和初步实验验证。

Conclusion: RDMF 是一种开创性的跨学科方法，将系统生物学与多媒体研究相结合，为视频-语言任务中的多模态融合提供了新范式。理论上有严格的数学基础，实践上兼容标准组件，有望在视频时刻检索和高亮检测任务上取得更好性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言引导的视频时刻检索（Video Moment Retrieval），属于视频问答与视频理解范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 中 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）涵盖 VQA 任务，视频时刻检索是 VQA 在视频领域的延伸；同时 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）也相关。但视频时刻检索更贴近 VQA 任务范式（给定查询检索对应视频片段），因此映射到 multimodal_vqa_doc。

Importance reason: Novel interdisciplinary approach but low alignment with user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Novel reaction-diffusion fusion, Video moment retrieval, Low priority subtopic, No autoregressive/diffusion generation, No continuous language model

Abstract: Video-language models are pivotal for tasks such as moment retrieval and highlight detection, yet they often struggle to capture the dynamic, non-linear interactions between temporal video sequences and textual semantics. Existing approaches, relying on static cross-attention or prompt-tuning mechanisms, fail to adaptively model the evolving relationships between modalities, leading to suboptimal alignment and limited generalization. Inspired by systems biology, we propose \textbf{Reaction-Diffusion Multimodal Fusion (RDMF)}, a novel framework that reimagines video-language alignment as a reaction-diffusion (RD) process, drawing on the principles of pattern formation introduced by Alan Turing. In RDMF, video features diffuse across time to capture temporal context, while text-video interactions are modeled as non-linear reactions that amplify relevant features and suppress noise, forming emergent patterns akin to biological systems. Leveraging the Gray-Scott RD model, we design a computationally efficient fusion module that integrates video and text representations, supported by rigorous mathematical analysis of stability and convergence using Turing instability criteria. Our framework is theoretically grounded, employing advanced mathematical tools to ensure stable pattern formation, and is practically viable, incorporating standard components like pretrained encoders and DETR-style heads for moment retrieval and saliency prediction. RDMF represents a pioneering interdisciplinary approach, bridging systems biology and multimedia research to address the limitations of conventional multimodal fusion. Preliminary experiments demonstrate its potential to outperform existing methods in identifying salient video moments, offering a new paradigm for video-language tasks.

</details>


### [287] [Ryze: Evidence-Enriched Data Synthesis from Biomedical Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00902)
*Yeqi Huang, Yue Chen, Yanwei Ye, Guanhao Su, Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ryze 是一个全自动系统，能从原始生物医学论文中提取图表、表格、标题和文本等完整证据结构，合成高质量QA训练数据，并通过SFT+RL的分阶段后训练策略，以不到200美元的成本将Qwen3-VL-8B微调为BioVLM-8B，在LAB-Bench上达到48.0%加权准确率，超越基座模型12.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在生物医学研究中不可靠，因为科学论文中的正确答案依赖于分散在图表、表格、标题和引用文本中的证据。现有后训练流程受限于昂贵的人工标注和丢失证据结构的合成数据。

Method: Ryze 包含三个核心组件：(1) 证据增强数据合成——从论文PDF中提取视觉元素（图表）、标题、结构化数据（表格）和引用段落，构建包含完整证据的QA对；(2) 布局与OCR错误减少——通过图表/表格感知的提取和LLM清洗来修正错误；(3) 进度门控后训练——先进行SFT，再结合RL（强化学习）进行分阶段训练。

Result: 从Qwen3-VL-8B出发，Ryze以不到200美元的成本训练出BioVLM-8B，在LAB-Bench上达到48.0%加权准确率，比基座模型提升12.6个百分点，并超越GPT-5.2达3.8个百分点。

Conclusion: Ryze 证明了全自动、低成本的证据增强数据合成可以有效提升VLM在专业领域（生物医学）的问答能力，且开源了系统和模型。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于从生物医学论文（含图表、表格、标题、文本）中合成证据增强的QA数据以训练领域专用VLM，属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/文档/图表理解）范畴，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_vqa_doc" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on biomedical VLM data synthesis, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: biomedical domain, evidence-enriched data synthesis, VLM post-training, low-cost fine-tuning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: General-purpose VLMs remain unreliable for biomedical research because valid answers in scientific papers depend on evidence split across figures, tables, charts, captions, and referring text. Existing post-training pipelines are bottlenecked by costly expert annotation and by synthetic data that drops this evidence structure. We present Ryze, a fully automated system that converts raw biomedical papers into an evidence-enriched training set and a domain-specialized VLM. Ryze synthesizes QA pairs with complete supporting evidence (visual element, caption, extracted structure, and referring paragraphs), reduces layout and OCR errors via chart/table-aware extraction and LLM-based cleansing, and applies a progress-gated post-training strategy combining supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Starting from Qwen3-VL-8B, Ryze produces BioVLM-8B at under USD 200, achieving 48.0% weighted accuracy on LAB-Bench, outperforming the base model by +12.6 percentage points (pp) and surpassing GPT-5.2 by +3.8 pp. We release Ryze as open source together with the trained BioVLM-8B model.

</details>


### [288] [ASAP: Advancing Medical Volumetric Representation Learning with Anatomy-aware Semantically-adaptive Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00602)
*Rongsheng Wang, Fenghe Tang, Zihang Jiang, Yingtai Li, Xu Zhang, Haoran Lai, Wenxin Ma, Wei Wei, Zhiyang He, Xiaodong Tao, Rui Yan, Qingsong Yao, Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (68.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ASAP 是一个面向胸部CT体积扫描的解剖感知语义自适应视觉-语言预训练框架，通过器官级结构先验注入、句子级-区域级选择性对齐和双模态掩码建模，在15个数据集22个下游任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学体积扫描（如CT）具有复杂的解剖结构，而放射学报告提供的监督信号弱且异构，导致学习可迁移且可解释的表征非常困难。现有方法缺乏对解剖结构先验的有效利用和细粒度的视觉-语言对齐机制。

Method: ASAP 包含三个核心组件：(1) 解剖感知知识注入模块，利用现成分割工具引入器官级结构先验；(2) 语义自适应选择性对齐机制，动态关联句子级发现与局部体积区域；(3) 语义自适应融合模块，在双模态掩码建模范式下实现解剖感知视觉特征与文本线索的有效交互。

Result: 在15个数据集、22个下游任务（包括异常分类、分割、疾病预后预测、报告生成、词汇分类、跨模态检索和VQA）上，ASAP 一致达到SOTA性能，尤其在有限监督和分布偏移场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: ASAP 通过解剖感知先验和语义自适应对齐，有效学习了可迁移且具有临床意义的医学体积表征，验证了视觉-语言预训练在医学影像领域的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出医学CT体积扫描的视觉-语言预训练框架，涉及跨模态检索、视觉问答、报告生成等下游任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向。虽然论文聚焦医学影像，但其核心是视觉-语言对齐与多模态理解，与现有 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）在任务类型（VQA、跨模态检索、报告生成）上高度匹配，故复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on medical volumetric pre-training, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and is flagged by negative keywords. Signals: medical volumetric, chest CT, radiology reports, anatomy-aware, negative keyword match

Abstract: Learning transferable and interpretable representations from medical volumetric scans remains challenging due to complex anatomical structures and weak, heterogeneous supervision provided by radiology reports. In this paper, we propose Anatomy-aware Semantically-Adaptive Pre-training (ASAP), a principled vision-language pre-training framework for fine-grained medical volumetric representation learning from large-scale chest CT scans and their corresponding radiology reports. ASAP integrates three key components: (1) an anatomy-aware knowledge injection module that incorporates organ-level structural priors via off-the-shelf segmentation tool to encourage anatomically coherent representations; (2) a semantically-adaptive selective alignment mechanism that dynamically associates sentence-level findings with localized volumetric regions; and (3) a semantically-adaptive fusion module for effective interaction between anatomically informed visual features and grounded textual cues under dual-modal masked modeling paradigm. Beyond methodological contributions, we establish a comprehensive benchmark for medical volumetric vision-language pre-training on chest CT, covering 15 datasets and 22 downstream tasks spanning abnormality classification, segmentation, disease prognosis prediction, report generation, vocabulary classification, cross-modal retrieval and visual question answering. This benchmark provides standardized evaluation protocols to systematically assess representation quality under diverse clinical settings and data regimes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASAP consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across tasks and datasets, with particularly pronounced gains under limited supervision and distribution shift, validating its effectiveness in learning transferable and clinically meaningful volumetric representations.

</details>


### [289] [An Open-Source Benchmark and Baseline for Multi-temporal Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00987)
*Bingyu Li, Da Zhang, Tao Huo, Zhiyuan Zhao, Junyu Gao, Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出多时序指代分割（MTRS）新任务，构建了首个基准数据集 MTRefSeg-21K（含21K高质量三元组），并提出变化感知 LVLM 框架 MTRefSeg-R1，通过两阶段训练策略实现语言描述的时间变化分割，实验表明该方法优于现有 LVLM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 在单帧图像理解上表现优异，但对多时序图像中语言描述的时间变化进行分割的能力尚未被探索。传统指代分割和变化检测任务各自独立，缺乏同时需要时序对应推理、语言定位和像素级掩码预测的统一任务。

Method: 提出 CRAFT-Agent 自动化数据构建管线（含人工审核），构建 MTRefSeg-21K 基准。提出 MTRefSeg-R1 框架：第一阶段从20K纯视觉双时序样本学习通用时间变化感知；第二阶段在 MTRefSeg-21K 上微调，实现细粒度语言引导的时间定位。模型显式建模跨时序视觉差异，对齐语言指令与时间变化，预测指代变化掩码。

Result: 在 MTRefSeg-21K 基准上对多种 VLM 和 LVLM 模型进行评测，直接推理表现较差，任务特定微调仍有局限。MTRefSeg-R1 在多个指标上显著优于现有 LVLM 基线，展示了 MTRS 任务的挑战性和潜力。

Conclusion: MTRS 是一个具有挑战性的新任务，MTRefSeg-R1 通过显式建模时序变化和两阶段训练策略有效提升了多时序指代分割性能，为未来研究提供了基准和方法基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多时序指代分割（MTRS）新任务，涉及视觉语言模型对多时序图像的指代理解与分割，属于多模态理解范畴。虽然任务新颖，但核心是视觉语言模型在特定场景（多时序变化）下的语言引导分割能力，与现有 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）在 VLM 驱动的视觉理解与语言指代定位方面有合理关联，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: The paper introduces a niche multi-temporal segmentation task with limited direct alignment to the user's priority subtopics on continuous language models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: New task and benchmark for temporal segmentation, change-aware LVLM framework, low overlap with user's priority subtopics and boost keywords, no autoregressive, diffusion, or continuous representation focus

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown strong visual understanding and language-guided grounding abilities, yet their capacity for multi-temporal visual reasoning remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{Multi-temporal Referring Segmentation (MTRS)}, a new task that aims to segment language-described temporal changes from multi-temporal images. MTRS extends conventional referring segmentation and change detection by jointly requiring temporal correspondence reasoning, language grounding, and pixel-level mask prediction. We propose \textbf{CRAFT-Agent}, an automated data construction pipeline with human auditing, and build \textbf{MTRefSeg-21K}, the first MTRS benchmark, containing 21K high-quality multi-temporal image-text-mask triplets across diverse scenes, viewpoints, and domains. Benchmarking a broad set of VLM- and LVLM-based models reveals that direct inference performs poorly, while task-specific fine-tuning remains limited. To address this, we propose \textbf{MTRefSeg-R1}, a change-aware LVLM framework trained with a two-stage strategy. It first learns general temporal-change perception from 20K vision-only bi-temporal samples, and is then fine-tuned on MTRefSeg-21K for fine-grained language-guided temporal localization. MTRefSeg-R1 explicitly models cross-temporal visual differences, aligns language instructions with temporal variations, and predicts referred change masks. Extensive experiments show that MTRefSeg-R1 achieves strong and often superior performance compared with existing LVLM baselines, demonstrating the challenge and potential of MTRS.

</details>


### [290] [HakushoBench: A Japanese Chart and Table VQA Benchmark from Governmental White Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01132)
*Issa Sugiura, Shuhei Kurita, Yusuke Oda, Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HakushoBench 是一个基于日本政府白皮书构建的日文图表VQA基准，包含2,053张图像和人工标注的QA对，覆盖10余种图表类型。实验表明该基准对开源VLM具有挑战性（最佳开源模型仅58.6%准确率），开源与闭源模型间存在34.9个百分点的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英文图表理解基准已快速发展，但非英文（尤其是日文）的图表VQA基准严重匮乏，难以评估VLM在非英文场景下的泛化能力。现有非英文基准构建面临数据收集困难的问题。

Method: 利用日本政府白皮书作为可扩展的数据源，从中提取自然出现的多样化图表和表格图像，构建HakushoBench基准。包含2,053张图像、10余种图像类型，并人工标注了QA对，设计上注重评估对图表的深层整体理解而非局部视觉线索。

Result: 在多种VLM上的实验表明：最佳开源模型仅达58.6%准确率；开源与闭源模型（如GPT-4o）之间存在34.9个百分点的显著差距，说明复杂图表理解仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: HakushoBench 是一个具有挑战性的日文图表VQA基准，揭示了当前VLM在非英文图表理解方面的不足，为未来研究提供了有价值的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个日文图表VQA基准，属于视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范子主题，该子主题明确涵盖VQA、图表/表格理解等内容。

Importance reason: Focuses on a Japanese-specific VQA benchmark, which is narrow and not aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Japanese chart VQA, non-English benchmark, limited to document understanding, no connection to continuous tokens or world models, low overlap with boost keywords

Abstract: Understanding chart and table images is essential for applying vision-language models (VLMs) to real-world document understanding. While English benchmarks have advanced rapidly, non-English counterparts remain scarce, leaving it unclear whether this progress generalizes across languages. A key obstacle is the difficulty of collecting realistic and diverse non-English chart and table images at scale. To address this, we leverage governmental white papers as a scalable source for benchmark construction beyond English, as they contain naturally occurring charts and tables across diverse formats and domains and are freely accessible in many countries. As a first instantiation, we introduce HakushoBench, a challenging Japanese chart and table VQA benchmark built from 33 governmental white papers. HakushoBench contains 2,053 images spanning over 10 image types, with manually annotated QA pairs, designed to assess deep and holistic understanding of charts and tables, rather than local visual cues alone. Experiments across a broad range of VLMs demonstrate that HakushoBench remains challenging for open-weight models: the best open-weight model achieves only 58.6% accuracy, and a 34.9-point gap between open-weight and proprietary models highlights substantial room for improvement in complex chart and table understanding. We release our dataset and code.

</details>


### [291] [Beyond Text and Tables: Vision-Language Model Integration in ComProScanner for Extracting Materials Data from Scientific Figures with High Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00065)
*Aritra Roy, Enrico Grisan, Chiara Gattinoni, John Buckeridge*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文扩展了ComProScanner多智能体框架，集成VLM实现从科学图表中自动提取材料成分-属性数据，在压电陶瓷数据集上达到0.97的准确率和F1分数，成为首个支持文本、表格和图表全模态自动提取的材料科学文献挖掘平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料科学文献挖掘框架仅能处理文本和表格内容，大量定量属性数据仅以图表形式呈现，无法被自动提取。作者希望填补这一空白，实现从科学图表中自动恢复成分-属性数据对。

Method: 在ComProScanner框架中新增FigureExtractor工具（基于标题和关键词过滤图表）和GraphExtractorTool智能体（将图表传递给可配置VLM以提取成分-属性对）。选取四种VLM（基于LMArena Diagram排行榜和成本标准）进行评估，并引入基于范围的数值误差阈值参数替代精确值匹配。

Result: 在50篇压电陶瓷文章的d33测试语料上，Gemini-3-Flash-Preview取得最佳性能：成分准确率0.97，归一化F1分数0.97，同时是四种模型中成本最低的。

Conclusion: VLM集成的ComProScanner是首个能够从文本、表格和图表中统一提取结构化成分-属性数据的全自动多模态材料科学文献挖掘平台，为材料信息学提供了更全面的数据自动化工具。

Classification reason: 该论文使用VLM从科学图表中提取材料成分-属性数据，属于图表理解与文档智能范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on materials science figure extraction, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: domain-specific materials science, VLM for figure extraction, not autoregressive or diffusion generation, no continuous language model, no world model

Abstract: Automated extraction of materials composition-property data from scientific literature has advanced considerably with the development of large language model-based pipelines; however, existing frameworks remain limited to textual and tabular content, overlooking the substantial proportion of quantitative property data reported exclusively in scientific figures. Here, we extend ComProScanner, a fully end-to-end multi-agent framework for automated composition-property database construction, with a native vision-language model (VLM) based figure extraction capability. The extension introduces a FigureExtractor utility for caption-keyword-based figure filtering across all supported publishers, and a GraphExtractorTool agent that passes extracted figures to a configurable VLM to recover composition-property pairs from scientific charts and plots. Four VLMs are selected for evaluation on the basis of the LMArena Diagram leaderboard with an input cost criterion of less than \$1.50 per million tokens. Benchmarking on 50 piezoelectric ceramic articles from the established $d_{33}$ test corpus demonstrates that Gemini-3-Flash-Preview achieves the highest performance with a composition accuracy of 0.97 and a normalised F1 score of 0.97, whilst remaining the most cost-effective model among the four evaluated. We additionally introduce a range-based value error threshold parameter into the evaluation framework, providing a more physically meaningful assessment of numeric property values extracted from figures than exact value matching. These contributions establish VLM-integrated ComProScanner as the first materials-specific, fully automated, multimodal literature mining platform capable of extracting structured composition-property data from text, tables, and figures within a single unified pipeline.

</details>


### [292] [TLG: Temporal-Logic Grounding for Video Question Answering via Source-Annotation Reconstruction and Category-Targeted Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01591)
*Ali Alavi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TLG 提出三阶段时序逻辑问答系统，通过从公开源数据集重建动作时间线、解析问题为时序逻辑程序并确定性执行，结合 VLM 回退和分类路由，在 TimeLogic Challenge 上将准确率从 46.9% 提升至 71.37%，揭示了时序定位是当前 VLM 的不可约瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视频语言模型在需要形式化时序逻辑推理（如 before/after/until 等 16 种时序算子）的视频问答任务上表现接近随机，因为它们将视频视为帧的集合，无法定位动作发生的具体时间点。作者旨在通过显式的时序逻辑接地来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: TLG 包含三个层级：(i) 从公开源数据集标注重建视频动作时间线，将每个问题解析为时序逻辑程序并确定性执行；(ii) 在无标注时回退到强开源 VLM；(iii) 仅将 VLM 经验上最弱的问类别路由到前沿推理模型（如 GPT-4o）。

Result: TLG 将测试准确率从 VLM 基线 46.9% 提升至 71.37%（+24.5 绝对提升），接近排行榜第一名（仅差 3 个百分点）。消融实验表明三种基于模型的时间线重建变体均不如整体 VLM，证明时序定位是当前不可约的瓶颈，真实标注而非更大模型才是提升准确率的关键。

Conclusion: TLG 通过显式时序逻辑接地有效解决了视频问答中的时序推理难题，揭示了当前 VLM 在时序定位方面的根本局限，并表明利用已有标注进行符号化推理比单纯扩大模型规模更为有效。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频问答（VideoQA）中的时序逻辑推理，属于视觉问答范畴，与 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on temporal-logic video QA with a specific benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: temporal-logic reasoning, video question answering, VLM bottleneck, source-annotation reconstruction, category-targeted reasoning

Abstract: The TimeLogic Challenge evaluates formal temporal-logic reasoning over video - 16 operators (before, after, until, since, always, co-occur, ordering, ...) in boolean and 4-way multiple-choice form. End-to-end video-language models (VLMs) hover near chance on this task because they treat video as a bag of frames and cannot localize when actions occur. We present TLG (Temporal-Logic Grounding), a three-tier system that (i) reconstructs each video's action timeline from the public source-dataset annotations the benchmark was generated from, parses every question into a temporal-logic program, and executes it deterministically; (ii) falls back to a strong open VLM where no annotation exists; and (iii) routes only the question categories where the VLM is empirically weakest to a frontier reasoning model. TLG raises test accuracy from a 46.9% VLM baseline to 71.37%, a +24.5 absolute gain, reaching within 3 points of the leaderboard top. We report extensive ablations, including three model-based timeline-reconstruction variants that all underperform a holistic VLM, isolating temporal grounding as the irreducible bottleneck and showing that real annotations - not larger models - drive accuracy.

</details>


### [293] [Vision-language Models for Driver Monitoring Systems: A Driver Activity Description Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02273)
*David J. Lerch, Sarath Mulugurthi, Manuel Martin, Frederik Diederichs, Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (48.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对驾驶员监控系统中VLM难以识别细粒度驾驶员行为的问题，构建了Drive&Act描述数据集（将原始动作标签转为自然语言描述），评测了三个VLM在该基准上的表现，并微调VLM使其在驾驶员活动描述任务上显著提升（ACCR分数从66提升至76），跨数据集泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在通用数据集上训练，难以准确识别驾驶员行为中的细微差异，缺乏针对驾驶员活动理解的细粒度描述数据集。

Method: 基于Drive&Act数据集构建细粒度自然语言描述数据集；使用LLM评分方法评测三个VLM；在描述数据集上微调VLM并在Drive&Act和DMD数据集上评估。

Result: 微调后的VLM在ACCR评分上达到76，超过零样本基线（66），且跨数据集泛化到DMD表现良好。

Conclusion: 用丰富描述的驾驶员动作数据微调VLM能显著提升其对驾驶员行为的理解能力，同时表明需要更多样化的数据集以支持更广泛的泛化。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了驾驶员活动描述数据集并对VLM进行微调与评测，属于视觉问答与细粒度视觉理解范畴，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）方向一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow driver monitoring application with no alignment to user's priority subtopics like continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Driver monitoring dataset, VLM fine-tuning, low priority subtopic, no autoregressive or diffusion video generation, no continuous language/multimodal models

Abstract: Understanding subtle driver actions is essential for building reliable driver monitoring systems. Existing visionlanguage models (VLMs) are trained on general datasets and struggle to recognize fine distinctions in driver behaviors. This paper addresses this limitation by creating a detailed natural language version of the Drive&Act dataset. We evaluate three VLMs on our new benchmark using LLM-based scoring methods. Their performance on the new benchmark shows that they cannot reliably generate accurate fine-grained driver activity descriptions. Based on the labeled Drive&Act dataset we create a new Drive&Act description dataset containing finegrained descriptions to train VLMs on driver activity understanding. Cross dataset evaluation on the Driver Monitoring Dataset (DMD) shows that the VLM fine-tuned on our new Drive&Act description dataset generalizes well to actions in the DMD dataset. The VLM fine-tuned on our Drive&Act description dataset achieves an ACCR score of 76 outperforming the zero-shot VLM baseline with an ACCR score of 66. These findings demonstrate that adapting VLMs with richly described driver actions can significantly improve their ability to interpret driver behavior while also highlighting the need for more diverse datasets to support broader generalization in future applications. Our Drive&Act description dataset and code will be publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

视频 LLM、视频问答、时序定位、长视频理解、第一视角视频。

### [294] [FlowNar: Scalable Streaming Narration for Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00620)
*Zeyun Zhong, Manuel Martin, Chengzhi Wu, David Schneider, Frederik Diederichs, Juergen Gall, Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (82.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowNar 提出了一种可扩展的流式长视频叙述框架，通过动态上下文管理策略和 Cross Linear Attentive Memory (CLAM) 模块，在保持有界视觉内存和计算复杂度的同时，显著提升了长视频流式叙述的质量和效率，在 Ego4D 等数据集上支持 10 倍更长视频处理并实现 3 倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模多模态模型（LMMs）主要面向离线场景，不适合流式视频的动态需求。虽然已有在线适配方法改善了实时处理能力，但资源需求随视频时长线性增长，面临严重的可扩展性瓶颈。本文旨在解决流式长视频叙述中的可扩展性问题。

Method: FlowNar 包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态上下文管理策略，用于移除历史视觉上下文；(2) CLAM（Cross Linear Attentive Memory）模块，用于保留流式视觉历史信息。同时提出了自条件化评估协议和补充评估指标，以在部署条件下评估流式叙述模型。

Result: 在 Ego4D、EgoExo4D 和 EpicKitchens100 数据集上的实验表明，FlowNar 在叙述质量上显著优于强基线方法，同时支持处理 10 倍更长的视频，实现了 3 倍更高的吞吐量（FPS），且计算复杂度有界。

Conclusion: FlowNar 通过动态上下文管理和 CLAM 记忆模块，成功实现了可扩展的流式长视频叙述，在保持高质量叙述的同时大幅提升了效率和可扩展性，为流式视频理解提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FlowNar，面向流式长视频的在线叙述生成框架，属于视频理解与流式处理方向。虽然涉及流式处理，但核心是视频理解与叙述生成，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 video_understanding_temporal（视频理解与时序推理）最为合适。

Importance reason: Strong streaming video narration work but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: streaming video narration, dynamic context management, CLAM module, bounded memory, Ego4D benchmark

Abstract: Recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), primarily designed for offline settings, are ill-suited for the dynamic requirements of streaming video. While recent online adaptations improve real-time processing, they still face critical scalability challenges, with resource demands typically growing at least linearly with video duration. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose FlowNar, a novel framework for scalable streaming video narration. The core of FlowNar is a dynamic context management strategy for historical visual context removal, combined with our CLAM (Cross Linear Attentive Memory) module for streaming visual history retention, ensuring bounded visual memory usage and computational complexity, crucial for efficient streaming. We also introduce a realistic self-conditioned evaluation protocol and complementary evaluation metrics to assess streaming narration models under deployment-like conditions. Experiments on the Ego4D, EgoExo4D, and EpicKitchens100 datasets demonstrate that FlowNar substantially improves narration quality over strong baselines while being highly efficient, supporting processing of 10$\times$ longer videos and achieving 3$\times$ higher throughput (FPS). The code is available at .

</details>


### [295] [FROST-STA: Frozen Dense Features for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00694)
*Chaoyang Wang, Lexuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (45.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FROST-STA 提出了一种基于冻结 V-JEPA 2.1 ViT-G 骨干的密集特征方法，用于 Ego4D 第一视角视频的短期物体交互预测（STA），通过物体中心解码、多头预测和集成策略，在 EgoVis 2026 挑战赛中获得第二名（Overall Top-5 mAP 5.13）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频中的短期交互预测需要系统推断用户将接触哪个物体、执行什么动作以及接触时间，这对具身智能和人机交互至关重要。现有方法在物体级交互预测方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 ViT-G 骨干，提取两路密集 token 流：查询前短片段视频 token（384px）和最后一帧高分辨率图像 token。通过注意力探针和帧引导时序池化模块将视频表示对齐到最后一帧空间参考系，再与图像特征融合。融合后的特征由 Faster R-CNN 风格的 STA 头解码出物体框、名词、动词、接触时间和交互质量。训练25轮，集成8个预测头和15-25轮检查点。

Result: 在 Ego4D STA 挑战赛官方测试集上取得 Overall Top-5 mAP 5.13，排名第二，验证了冻结密集图像-视频特征在物体级交互预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 冻结的密集图像-视频特征可以作为物体级交互预测的强基线，未来可探索更高效的对齐模块和更丰富的时序建模。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于Ego4D第一视角视频中的短期物体交互预测（STA），属于第一视角视频理解与时序推理任务，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on a specific video understanding challenge (Ego4D STA) using frozen features, which is not aligned with the user's priority on generative models, continuous representations, or world models. Signals: Ego4D challenge, frozen backbone, object interaction anticipation, no generative modeling, no continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: Short-term anticipation in egocentric video requires more than recognizing the current scene: a system must infer which object the camera wearer will contact, which action will follow, and how soon the contact will happen. This report describes FROST-STA, our submission to the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation (STA) Challenge at EgoVis 2026. For each query time, the model produces a ranked set of structured hypotheses containing an active-object box, noun label, verb label, time-to-contact (TTC), and confidence. FROST-STA builds on the V-JEPA 2.1 STA evaluation protocol, but adapts it to the challenge by using object-centric decoding, multi-head prediction, and a submission-oriented training and ensembling recipe. We keep the V-JEPA 2.1 ViT-G backbone fixed and extract two dense token streams: video tokens from a short clip resized to 384 pixels before the query, and image tokens from the last observed high-resolution frame. A compact alignment module, consisting of an attentive probe and frame-guided temporal pooling, maps the clip representation onto the spatial reference of the final frame before fusing it with image features. The fused maps are decoded by Faster R-CNN-style STA heads that estimate box offsets, nouns, verbs, TTC values, and interaction quality. For the final leaderboard entry, we train for 25 epochs with the official training split plus additional permitted validation annotations, and combine predictions across eight heads and checkpoints from epochs 15-25. FROST-STA obtains 5.13 Overall Top-5 mAP on the official test server, ranking second in the challenge and showing that frozen dense image-video features can serve as a strong basis for object-level interaction forecasting.

</details>


### [296] [Adaptive Dense Evidence Refinement for Video Relational Reasoning for VRR-QA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01104)
*Yuyang Sun, Yongliang Wu, Xingyu Zhu, Yuxia Chen, Zhenxiang Jiang, Yangguang Ji, Wenbo Zhu, Yanxi Shi, Jay Wu, Shuo Wang, Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应密集证据精炼系统用于视频关系推理问答，通过轻量级视图识别不稳定问题，仅对困难问题路由到高预算密集证据模块进行时序帧观察、关系特定探针和候选验证，在VRR-QA测试集上达到90.07%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频问答中空间、时序、视角、深度和可见性关系往往无法通过单帧解决，现有方法难以区分"找到合理备选答案"和"决定何时应更改当前答案"这两个问题。

Method: 提出推理时自适应计算系统：先用直接视频语言模型回答所有问题，再用多个轻量级视图检测不稳定问题，仅将困难问题路由到高预算密集证据模块，该模块构建带时间戳的帧观察、关系特定探针、候选验证和保守时序聚合。

Result: 在VRR-QA测试集上获得90.07%平均准确率和87.81%宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: 通过分离"寻找备选答案"和"决定是否更改答案"两个子问题，自适应路由策略能有效提升视频关系推理问答的性能，且计算效率更高。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于视频关系推理问答（VRR-QA），属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video relational reasoning QA, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: video relational reasoning, adaptive dense evidence, VRR-QA challenge, test-time computation, multimodal understanding

Abstract: VRR-QA evaluates whether video-language systems can infer spatial, temporal, viewpoint, depth, and visibility relations that are not always resolved by a single frame. We present an inference-only system built around adaptive test-time computation. The system first answers each question with a direct video-language model pass, then uses multiple lightweight views to find unstable questions. Only these difficult questions are routed to a high-budget dense evidence module that constructs timestamped frame observations, relation-specific probes, candidate verification, and conservative temporal aggregation. This design separates two problems that are often confused in video question answering: finding plausible alternative answers and deciding when a current answer should actually be changed. On the test split, the final system obtains 90.07 average accuracy and 87.81 macro average accuracy. The report focuses on the final test system and the implementation settings required to reproduce the adaptive dense verifier.

</details>


### [297] [Temporal Evidence Routing with Structured Visual Evidence for TimeLogicQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01106)
*Yuyang Sun, Yongliang Wu, Xingyu Zhu, Yuxia Chen, Zhenxiang Jiang, Yangguang Ji, Wenbo Zhu, Yanxi Shi, Jay Wu, Shuo Wang, Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视觉证据路由流水线（Temporal Evidence Routing），将感知与符号时序推理分离，用于解决 TimeLogicQA 视频时序推理任务。系统通过 MLLM 提取结构化视觉证据，结合程序化验证器和确定性时序规则推理器，在测试集上达到 81.8% AvgAcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答系统在时序推理任务上表现不佳，尤其是涉及事件存在性、顺序、持续性、边界条件和重叠等复杂时序关系的推理。TimeLogicQA 基准揭示了这一挑战，本文旨在通过分离感知与符号推理来提升时序推理能力。

Method: 提出视觉证据路由流水线：1) 问题解析器将问题分解为事件目标、答案模式、候选选项和时序算子；2) 根据视频时长和算子难度路由视频，短片使用全帧证据，长视频使用事件聚焦候选窗口；3) MLLM 生成结构化视觉证据；4) 程序化验证器恢复密集动作区间；5) 确定性归约器应用算子特定的时序规则得到最终答案；6) 保守融合机制仅在视觉证据、时序程序和置信度检查一致时接受答案。

Result: 在 TimeLogicQA 官方测试集上，最终系统达到 AvgAcc 81.8%，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将感知与符号时序推理分离的流水线方法能有效提升视频时序推理能力，结构化视觉证据与程序化验证的结合减少了噪声答案翻转，为视频问答中的复杂时序推理提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频问答中的时序推理（TimeLogicQA），属于视频理解与时序推理这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，与现有 subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific video QA benchmark with symbolic reasoning, not on core generative or continuous multimodal model advances. Signals: Video temporal reasoning, MLLM-based evidence extraction, Symbolic temporal logic, Benchmark-specific pipeline, No generative or continuous model contribution

Abstract: TimeLogicQA evaluates whether video question answering systems can reason over temporal relations such as event existence, ordering, persistence, boundary conditions, and overlap. We address this task with a visual evidence routing pipeline that separates perception from symbolic temporal reasoning. The system first parses each question into event targets, answer mode, candidate options, and temporal operators. It then routes videos according to duration and operator difficulty, using ordered full-frame evidence for short clips and event-focused candidate windows for long videos. A multimodal large language model produces structured visual evidence for the relevant events, while programmatic verifiers recover dense action intervals and a deterministic reducer applies operator-specific temporal rules to produce the final answer. Conservative fusion accepts an answer only when the visual evidence, temporal program, and confidence checks agree, reducing noisy answer flips. On the official test evaluation, our final system achieves an AvgAcc of 81.8.

</details>


### [298] [3rd Place at CVPR 2026 CASTLE Challenge: Agentic Multi-View Long-Context Video Understanding via Hierarchical Knowledge Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01933)
*Raghad Albusayes, Munirah Alyahya*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的多智能体框架，通过视频知识图谱和分层检索机制，在 CVPR 2026 CASTLE 挑战赛（600+小时多视角视频数据）中取得第三名，实现了长上下文多视角视频的零样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解方法难以处理大规模、长上下文、多视角同步视频流中的复杂时空推理问题，亟需一种无需训练、可扩展的框架来应对极端规模的多模态视频数据。

Method: 提出一个无需训练的智能体框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 视频知识图谱，建模静态/动态实体及其时间关系和交叉事件，支持多跳关系推理；(2) 自适应智能体工作流，通过分层检索和索引机制解析复杂查询。

Result: 在 CASTLE 2026 挑战赛（600+小时、15个摄像头同步拍摄的多视角视频）中取得全球第三名，展示了高精度的零样本推理能力。

Conclusion: 基于知识图谱的智能体检索框架能有效应对大规模长上下文多视角视频理解任务，无需额外训练即可实现复杂时空推理，为极端规模视频理解提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于大规模多视角长视频理解，涉及视觉计数、动作定位、多视角跟踪和说话人时序推理等任务，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-view long-context video understanding, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or generation. Signals: Multi-view video understanding, Knowledge graph retrieval, Zero-shot reasoning, No training required, Challenge solution

Abstract: This paper presents our winning methodology for the CASTLE 2026 Challenge at the CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop, where our team secured third place globally. The challenge tasks participants with answering highly complex visual, spatiotemporal, and verbal questions, including visual counting, action localization, multi-view tracking and speaker temporal reasoning, within massive, multimodal video streams. The underlying dataset consists of over 600 hours synchronized footage captured by 15 ego and exo camera sources. To tackle the extreme scale and long-context demands of this environment, we introduce a training-free agentic framework optimized for long-form video understanding. Our framework introduces two core architectural components: i) a Video Knowledge Graph that maps static and dynamic entities, their temporal relationships, and intersecting events to enable multi-hop relational reasoning, and ii) an adaptive agentic workflow that resolves complex queries through a hierarchical retrieval and indexing. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework achieves high zero-shot reasoning accuracy on long-context multi-view streams. Our code will be released at .

</details>

### [299] [FlowOVD: Learning Generative Latent Flows for Zero-shot Open-vocabulary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00782)
*Yao Wei, Andrea Cavallaro, Changjae Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowOVD 提出了一种基于 rectified flow 的文本条件查询生成框架，将开放词汇目标检测中的解码器查询生成建模为潜空间中的连续传输过程，避免了启发式离散查询构建，在 COCO 和 LVIS 上分别达到 49.5 AP 和 31.5 AP，超越 GroundingDINO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测方法通常将检测建模为判别式预测问题，解码器查询要么是静态的，要么从编码器特征初始化，限制了查询的多样性和灵活性。作者希望引入生成式视角，通过连续潜空间传输过程生成更灵活、语义对齐更好的解码器查询。

Method: FlowOVD 基于 rectified flow 构建文本条件查询生成框架。它将文本无关的初始查询（从噪声或可学习嵌入出发）通过连续潜空间中的常微分方程（ODE）逐步变换为文本引导的查询。该过程由文本特征条件化的速度场网络驱动，使得生成的查询能够与目标类别语义对齐。整个框架集成到 VLM 检测器中，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在 COCO 数据集上达到 49.5 AP，在 LVIS 数据集上达到 31.5 AP，分别超越 GroundingDINO 1.2 AP（+2.5%）和 4.1 AP（+15.0%）。在长尾分布的 LVIS 基准上提升更为显著，验证了连续查询生成对开放词汇泛化的有效性。

Conclusion: FlowOVD 证明了将解码器查询生成建模为连续潜空间传输过程是一种有效的开放词汇检测范式，能够生成更具表达力和语义对齐的查询，尤其在长尾分布场景下优势明显。

Classification reason: 该论文将开放词汇检测中的解码器查询生成建模为潜空间中的连续传输过程（rectified flow），属于连续视觉语言模型与潜空间流方法。同时该方法基于VLM检测器进行开放词汇检测，也属于多模态理解与生成方向。因此映射到 canonical subtopic "连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流"。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent flow for open-vocabulary detection, a top-priority subtopic with strong empirical gains. Signals: rectified flow for query generation, continuous latent dynamics in VLM detector, outperforms GroundingDINO on COCO and LVIS, aligns with continuous language/multimodal model priority, no extra training data needed

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) has achieved remarkable progress through large-scale vision-language pre-training. Existing methods, however, typically formulate OVD as a discriminative prediction problem, where decoder queries are either static or initialized from encoder features, thus limiting their diversity and flexibility. In this paper, we introduce a generative perspective by modeling decoder query generation as a continuous transport process in latent space. We propose FlowOVD, a text-conditioned query generation framework based on rectified flow that progressively transforms text-agnostic queries into text-guided queries. By introducing continuous latent query dynamics into a vision-language model (VLM) based detector, our method avoids heuristic discrete query construction and enables more expressive semantic alignment for open-vocabulary detection. Without requiring additional training data, FlowOVD achieves 49.5 AP on COCO and 31.5 AP on LVIS, outperforming GroundingDINO by +1.2 AP (+2.5 %) and +4.1 AP (+15.0 %), respectively. The larger gain on the challenging long-tailed LVIS benchmark further highlights the effectiveness of continuous query generation for open-vocabulary generalization.

</details>
